Understanding whether GenAI changes or shifts developers' cognitive demands during everyday development is important for a developer-centered evaluation of GenAI-supported software development. It can inform organizations in designing and evaluating effective AI-supported workflows. In this work, we study how GenAI use and task context relate to professional developers' perceived cognitive load and whether wearable-derived physiological characteristics provide additional information beyond this context. In a four-day industrial field study at two SAP sites, 21 developers documented their tasks, task duration, GenAI use, and perceived cognitive load while wearing an EmbracePlus wristband. The results show that perceived cognitive load is associated with both GenAI use and task context, while physiological measures provide only limited additional information. These findings suggest that developers' perceived cognitive load during GenAI-supported software development should be evaluated in relation to the concrete work context, with wearable physiological data used as complementary rather than standalone information.

</details>


### [33] [Retrieve, Reproduce, Reveal: Dissecting Retrieval-Augmented Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37669)
*Sabrina Kaniewski,Tim Krämer,Julius Bächle,Markus Enzweiler,Michael Menth,Tobias Heer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对六个开源RAG4SVD系统进行复现与统一基准评测，并开展组件级分析，揭示了可复现性差异、性能不可迁移性及流水线各阶段对齐的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的软件漏洞检测系统多用专有模型评测，阻碍开放科学与可复现性；且研究间数据集、知识库、骨干模型和指标各异，难以进行有意义的跨系统比较。此外，系统通常仅作为整体端到端评测，缺乏对内部组件的分析。

Method: 研究六个开源RAG4SVD系统，采取三项措施：(i)在开放权重设定下复现其实验设置；(ii)使用统一数据集、指标套件和开放权重模型池构建统一基准；(iii)进行组件级分析，将代表性流水线分解为输入抽象、知识检索和检测三个组件。

Result: 各系统的可复现性差异显著；在统一基准下，已发表的RAG4SVD性能在受控开放权重评测中无法迁移，且强烈依赖所用模型。组件分析显示，有效RAG4SVD依赖流水线各阶段间的对齐，例如oracle知识可将检索提升至接近最优，但检测性能仍低（成对准确率0.51），说明仅靠检索有效性不足以实现可靠检测。

Conclusion: 应不仅在端到端层面，还应在流水线组件层面评测RAG4SVD，本研究为更标准化、具备RAG意识的评测实践提供了基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly used to enhance Large Language Model (LLM)-based software vulnerability detection by grounding predictions in retrieved vulnerability knowledge, such as vulnerability reports. However, existing RAG-based software vulnerability detection (RAG4SVD) systems are often evaluated using proprietary models, which challenges open science and reproducibility. Further, studies use different datasets, custom knowledge bases, different backbone models, and diverse metrics, which hinders meaningful cross-system comparison. In this work, we study six open-source RAG4SVD systems and address these reproducibility and comparability challenges through (i) reproduction of their experimental settings under an open-weight setting, and (ii) a unified benchmark using a common dataset, metric suite, and pool of open-weight models. Further, RAG4SVD systems typically consist of multiple components, yet are often evaluated only as a whole system, i.e., end-to-end. Therefore, we perform (iii) a component-level analysis that decomposes representative RAG4SVD pipelines into input abstraction, knowledge retrieval, and detection. Our results demonstrate that reproducibility varies substantially across systems. Under the presented unified benchmark, published RAG4SVD performance does not transfer under a controlled open-weight evaluation and depends strongly on the used model. The component analysis shows that effective RAG4SVD depends on the alignment between pipeline stages. For example, oracle knowledge raises retrieval to near-optimal, yet performance remains low (0.51 pairwise accuracy), demonstrating that retrieval effectiveness alone is insufficient for reliable detection. These findings motivate evaluating RAG4SVD not only end-to-end, but at the level of pipeline components, and provide a basis for more standardized, RAG-aware evaluation practices.

</details>


### [34] [Is manual software optimization a thing of the past?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37849)
*Pavlin G. Poličar,Martin Špendl,Tomaž Hočevar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究探讨LLM智能体能否自主优化科学软件，在t-SNE、ssGSEA和graphlet counting三个问题上取得最高两个数量级的加速，且正确性经代码维护者验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学软件需处理更大数据集并保持可接受执行时间，但传统软件优化需大量编程、算法和数值方法专业知识。近期LLM进展提供了自动化该过程的可能，因此研究LLM智能体能否在已高度优化的成熟科学软件上自主实现显著性能提升。

Method: 由人类定义范围、正确性标准和验证机制，随后LLM智能体自主工作（部分情况下持续数小时），针对t-SNE、ssGSEA和graphlet counting三个计算问题进行软件优化，最终由代码维护者审查并验证正确性。

Result: 在所有测试配置中优化实现均更快，最高比现有最快工具快两个数量级。优化包括底层代码优化、数学重构，以及为graphlet counting设计的全新算法。

Conclusion: 软件优化可日益委托给自主智能体，人类角色从实现优化转向决定优化哪个软件、定义目标、提供验证机制并确保最终软件正确性。对于范围明确、可验证的问题，人工软件优化或将成为过去。

Abstract: Scientific software is increasingly required to process larger datasets while maintaining acceptable execution times. Software optimization traditionally requires substantial expertise in programming, algorithms, and numerical methods. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) offer the possibility of automating much of this process. We investigate whether LLM-based agents can autonomously achieve substantial performance improvements in scientific software, including mature implementations that have already been extensively optimized by human developers. We tasked an LLM-based agent with optimizing software for three computational problems: t-SNE, single-sample gene set enrichment analysis (ssGSEA), and graphlet counting. Humans defined the scope, correctness criteria, and a verification mechanism, after which the agent worked autonomously, in some cases for several hours. Code maintainers reviewed each resulting implementation and verified its correctness. The optimized implementations were faster in all tested configurations, by up to two orders of magnitude over the fastest existing tools. The improvements included low-level code optimizations, mathematical reformulations, and an entirely new algorithm for graphlet counting. Software optimization can increasingly be delegated to autonomous agents, with the human role shifting from implementing optimizations to deciding which software to optimize, defining objectives, providing verification mechanisms, and ensuring the correctness of the final software. For well-scoped, verifiable problems, we argue that manual software optimization may be a thing of the past.

</details>


### [35] [AgentBug-Smith: Automatically Reproducing Real-World Harness Bugs in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37864)
*Yiming Cheng,Alfin Wijaya Rahardja,Mengshi Zhang,Zihao Chen,Zhenpeng Chen,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentBug-Smith 自动复现框架与 Live-Harness-Bench 基准，用于持续发现、复现并评估智能体 harness 缺陷修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent harness 缺陷具有独特性，现有软件智能体难以修复；而现有基准仅含少量固定可执行 harness 缺陷，且构建需耗费数百人工小时，阻碍了该领域进展。

Method: 提出 AgentBug-Smith，一种自动化 harness 缺陷复现方法，能从开源智能体系统中持续发现并复现真实 harness 缺陷；并据此构建可动态扩展的 Live-Harness-Bench 基准。

Result: AgentBug-Smith 在不同骨干 LLM 上复现成功率比通用软件缺陷复现技术高 10.67%–27.56%；Live-Harness-Bench 当前包含 200 个可复现 harness 缺陷。评估显示当前软件智能体修复能力有限；从缺陷修复知识中蒸馏的可复用修复技能将修复率提升 6.32%。

Conclusion: AgentBug-Smith 与 Live-Harness-Bench 为持续评估和提升软件智能体的 harness 缺陷修复能力提供了可扩展基础，将真实失败转化为可执行评测实例与可复用知识，助力递归自我改进智能体的目标。

Abstract: Agent harness bugs exhibit unique characteristics and remain challenging for state-of-the-art software agents to repair. Progress in this area is further hindered by existing benchmarks, which contain only a small and fixed number of executable harness bugs while requiring hundreds of human hours to construct. This work presents AgentBug-Smith, an automated harness bug reproduction approach that continuously discovers and reproduces real-world harness bugs from open-source agentic systems. Across different backbone LLMs, AgentBug-Smith consistently outperforms existing bug reproduction techniques designed for general software, achieving 10.67% - 27.56% higher success rates of reproducing harness bugs. By applying AgentBug-Smith to open-source agentic systems in the wild, we construct Live-Harness-Bench, a live and extensible benchmark that currently contains 200 reproducible harness bugs. We further demonstrate the utility of Live-Harness-Bench through two downstream applications. First, we use Live-Harness-Bench as the evaluation benchmark to systematically evaluate state-of-the-art software agents, revealing their limited capabilities in repairing real-world harness bugs. Second, we use Live-Harness-Bench as a knowledge base of real-world harness bug fixes, from which reusable repair skills can be distilled to improve existing software agents, increasing their harness-bug repair rates by 6.32%. Together, AgentBug-Smith and Live-Harness-Bench establish a scalable foundation for continuously evaluating and improving software agents on harness bug repair, turning real-world agent failures into executable evaluation instances and reusable knowledge for harness improvement, thus contributing to the ultimate goal of recursively self-improving agents.

</details>


### [36] [Merged, Not Measured: An Empirical Study of Performance Issues Fixed by Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37985)
*Zhenyu Qi,Haotang Li,Jinfu Chen,Huashan Chen,Yutong Zhao,Derui Zhu,Tomas Cerny,Bo Liu,Sen He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对AIDev v4中6个编码代理提交的1,262个性能修复PR进行筛选与代码分析，并复现53个修复，发现已关闭修复中57%被合并，但复现的23个被拒修复仅6个成立；合并结论更多取决于代理在仓库中的历史记录，而非修复本身的内容，合并并不等于性能提升成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理提交了大量声称加速软件的PR，但现有关于人类性能修复的研究难以说明维护者如何回应此类代理修复、以及其性能声明是否可信，因此需要系统考察代理性能修复的接受情况与真实性。

Method: 从AIDev v4的71,677个代理PR出发，通过文本过滤与大模型及作者双重编码，筛选出6个代理在582个仓库中修复的1,262个性能问题；对每个问题及其测试进行编码，并重新执行23个被拒与30个被合并的修复，采用三次运行的小规模验证。

Result: 57%的已关闭修复被合并，61%的拒绝未给出理由；复现的23个被拒声明中仅6个成立。接受率随代理在该仓库的历史表现（31-37%升至70%）以及仓库对该代理其他PR的既往合并率（33%升至84%）而上升。合并修复删除的代码行占比更高（0.26对0.15），且在代理内和仓库内均成立，但在编码内容、描述、测试或测量上未见差异。44%的问题源于重复计算与冗余数据处理，46%的修复属于架构层面。37%的修复改动了测试，11%带有性能测试或基准；30个合并修复中18个达到交付标准，3个未达声明，9个无显著提升或出现退化，14个在未测试输入上改变了行为。

Conclusion: 修复结果更多取决于仓库与代理的历史交互，而非修复的编码内容；PR被合并并不能证明其真正实现了所声称的性能提升。

Abstract: Coding agents open pull requests (PRs) that claim to speed up software, but studies of human performance fixes say little about how maintainers respond to such a fix or whether its claim holds. From the 71,677 agent PRs of AIDev v4, a text filter and codebook coding by language models and by the authors select 1,262 performance issues fixed by six agents in 582 repositories. We code each issue and its tests and re-execute 23 rejected and 30 merged fixes. (1) 57% of closed fixes are merged, 61% of rejections give no stated reason, and only 6 of the 23 re-executed rejected claims held under our three-run pilot on mostly agent-built workloads. (2) Acceptance rises with the agent's track record in the repository (31-37% to 70%) and with the repository's pre-opening merge rate on its other agent PRs (33% to 84%). Merged fixes delete a larger share of the lines they change (0.26 versus 0.15), a difference that holds within agent and within repository, with no such difference detected in the coded content, description, tests or measurements. (3) Repeated computation and redundant data processing cause 44% of the issues, and 46% of fixes are architectural-level. (4) Agents change tests in 37% of fixes and 11% carry a performance test or benchmark; of the 30 merged fixes, 18 met our delivery criterion, 3 fell short of the claim, 9 showed no significant gain or regressed, and 14 change behavior on untested inputs. The outcome tracks the repository's history with the agent rather than the coded content of the fix, and a merge does not show that the fix delivers what it claims.

</details>

### [37] [Toward Generative Video Communication: A Dual-Stream Digital Transmission Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36565)
*Bingyan Xie,Longyu Zhou,Tianhao Liang,Yongpeng Wu,Zehui Xiong,Wenjun Zhang,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出双流数字生成视频通信（DGVC）框架，将传统数字链路与生成链路结合，以在无线带宽受限场景下实现个性化内容传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频通信在带宽受限的无线传输中具有潜力，并能支持个性化内容分发，但其可靠性、同步与协调等关键问题仍待解决。

Method: 提出DGVC框架：传统链路提供基于源的视觉参考，生成链路传输紧凑的语义与感知信息供接收端生成；并讨论三种带宽相关操作模式及双流协调、同步、可靠性与时延控制的关键技术。

Result: 通过一个实际案例研究，验证了DGVC在无线衰落信道下在感知质量与时间质量方面的优势。

Conclusion: DGVC能提升无线衰落信道下的感知与时间质量，文章最后讨论了生成式视频通信的开放挑战与未来研究方向。

Abstract: Generative video communication has shown promise for bandwidth-constrained wireless transmission and has the potential to support personalized content delivery. In this article, we propose a dual-stream digital generative video communication (DGVC) framework that integrates a traditional digital link with a generative link. The traditional link provides source-grounded visual references, while the generative link conveys compact semantic and perceptual information for receiver-side generation. We further discuss three bandwidth-dependent operating regimes and key technologies for dual-stream coordination, synchronization, reliability, and latency control. A practical case study demonstrates the perceptual and temporal-quality benefits of DGVC under wireless fading channels. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future research directions for generative video communication.

</details>

### [38] [Sage: Formalization with Semantic Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35790)
*Thomas Hirtz,Farzad Jafarrahmani,Abdelmouksit Sagueni,Xiang Zhou,Wengping Deng,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sage 是一个智能体框架，通过四阶段分解生成流程和双信号语义校正循环，将非形式化自然语言数学问题转化为忠实的 Lean 4 形式化陈述，显著降低答案泄漏并提升语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经定理证明器通常假设已有忠实的 Lean 4 形式化陈述，但自然语言到形式语言的翻译存在"严谨性幻觉"：类型检查器会接受那些虽能编译却丢失假设、引入空洞真理或微妙改变数学边界的陈述。这一数据瓶颈亟需解决。

Method: 提出 Sage（语义智能体引导的形式化引擎），用四阶段分解生成流程替代单体翻译，并配合双信号语义校正循环：将 Lean 4 编译器诊断与多维语义反馈配对，在保证语法有效性的同时强制数学保真度，并显式处理开放式查询与声明式形式目标之间的差距。

Result: 在 Omni-MATH（无证明）上，Sage 将答案泄漏率从 70.9% 抑制到 2.7%，pass@4 联合编译与语义保真度达 73.3%，而微调的 Goedel-Formalizer-V2 基线为 42.0%。在 IMO-Unformalized（175 道未形式化的 IMO 问题）上，Sage 实现有效零样本泛化，pass@4 验证保真度达 87.4%，基线仅 19.4%，并在盲测成对评估中赢得超过 79% 的对比。

Conclusion: Sage 通过分解生成与语义校正有效解决了自然语言形式化中的严谨性幻觉问题，大幅提升了形式化陈述的语义忠实度与泛化能力。

Abstract: While neural theorem provers have achieved impressive milestones in formal mathematics, they largely operate on the assumption that faithful Lean 4 formal statements are already provided. Translating informal natural language into a formal language is a critical data bottleneck plagued by an "illusion of rigor": standard type-checkers accept statements that compile but drop hypotheses, introduce vacuous truths, or subtly alter mathematical bounds. To resolve this, we introduce Sage (Semantic Agent-Guided Formalization Engine), an agentic framework that replaces monolithic translation with a four-stage decomposed generation pipeline coupled with a dual-signal semantic correction loop. By pairing Lean 4 compiler diagnostics with multi-dimensional semantic feedback, our correction loop enforces mathematical fidelity alongside syntactic validity. By explicitly accounting for the gap between open-ended queries and declarative formal targets, our pipeline prevents models from achieving high formalization rates by guessing unverified answers (exhibiting a 70.9% answer leakage rate). Consequently, Sage suppresses leakage to 2.7% while achieving 73.3% pass@4 joint compilation and semantic fidelity on the Omni-MATH without proofs (compared to 42.0% for a fine-tuned Goedel-Formalizer-V2 baseline). Finally, on IMO-Unformalized, a novel frontier of 175 unformalized International Mathematical Olympiad problems, Sage demonstrates effective zero-shot generalization with 87.4% pass@4 verified fidelity compared to just 19.4% for the baseline, winning over 79% of blind pairwise evaluations.

</details>


### [39] [Serverless gossip training of LSTM failure detectors: A matched-protocol comparison with federated, local and centralized learning on NASA C-MAPSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35792)
*Yusuf Öztürk,Enes Göktekin,Bengisu Atlı,Akın Öztürk,Zhixiang Wang,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在NASA C-MAPSS涡扇发动机基准上，对堆叠LSTM故障检测模型比较同步环形gossip与FedAvg等方法，发现gossip可达到接近FedAvg的性能且无需中心服务器，但异构性强时性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业预测性维护需要跨站点设备数据学习，但传感器数据难以集中汇聚。FedAvg依赖中心聚合服务器，gossip learning去中心化，但对循环故障检测模型在受控条件下的行为尚未被测量。

Method: 在NASA C-MAPSS涡扇发动机基准上，对检测临近故障的堆叠LSTM，比较同步环形gossip、FedAvg、孤立本地训练与集中式参照。所有方法共享同一开放实现、架构、初始化、优化器、数据划分与训练预算；主要评估指标每个测试发动机使用单个终端窗口，避免重叠窗口的统计依赖。

Result: FD001（5个种子）：gossip终端窗口F1为89.6±1.3%，FedAvg为89.9±1.1%，本地训练83.6±6.7%，集中式93.5±2.1%，gossip与FedAvg传输相同载荷且无协调者。节点模型接近但不完全一致（成对决策分歧1.8%，无通信时5.6%）。FD002-FD004上对等通信较本地训练提升13-28个百分点；gossip在FD003和FD004匹配FedAvg，但在多工况FD002上低4.3个百分点。模拟消息丢失、节点故障和服务器中断对两方法影响很小，而环越大gossip退化越快。

Conclusion: 当数据异构性适中时，环形gossip是实用的无服务器替代方案；随着异构性增长，更快速混合的拓扑变得重要。

Abstract: Industrial predictive maintenance increasingly depends on learning from equipment spread across sites whose sensor data cannot easily be pooled. Federated averaging (FedAvg) solves this with a central aggregation server; gossip learning removes the server, but its behaviour for recurrent failure-detection models has not been measured under controlled conditions. We compare synchronous ring gossip with FedAvg, isolated local training and a centralized reference for a stacked LSTM that detects imminent failure on the NASA C-MAPSS turbofan benchmark. All methods share one open implementation, architecture, initialization, optimizer, data split and training budget, and the primary endpoint uses one terminal window per test engine to avoid the statistical dependence of overlapping windows. On FD001 (five seeds), gossip reached a terminal-window F1 of 89.6 +/- 1.3%, compared with 89.9 +/- 1.1% for FedAvg, 83.6 +/- 6.7% for local training and 93.5 +/- 2.1% for centralized training, while transmitting the same payload as FedAvg without a coordinator. Node models agreed closely but not exactly (1.8% pairwise decision disagreement versus 5.6% without communication). Across FD002-FD004, peer communication improved terminal-window F1 over local training by 13-28 points; gossip matched FedAvg on FD003 and FD004 but was 4.3 points lower on the multi-condition FD002 subset. Simulated message loss, node failure and server outage changed neither method appreciably, whereas larger rings degraded gossip faster. Ring gossip is therefore a practical serverless alternative when data heterogeneity is moderate, and faster-mixing topologies become important as heterogeneity grows.

</details>


### [40] [Learning from the Gap Between Pass@K and Pass@1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35793)
*Xuan Liu,Jingbin Qian,Haosheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GapFT，通过针对 Pass@K-Pass@1 差距（单样本失败但多样本可解的问题）来挑选训练样本，在匹配预算下提升单样本解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 后训练方法未能区分「首次解码即解决」与「K 次采样内恢复」的问题，在固定预算下会浪费算力重复已具备的行为，因此需要更有效的样本选择策略。

Method: GapFT 依据源检查点单样本结果选择训练证据，仅在 Pass@K 与 Pass@1 的差距样本（单样本失败但 K 次内可解）上微调，保持目标函数不变，并在训练样本、处理 tokens 和优化步数上做匹配对比；采用精确分解区分恢复失败修正、遗漏失败修正与首次成功样本上的回退。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B 上，LogiQA 2.0 与 ReClor 的 Pass@1 分别提升 14.4 与 13.9 分，优于同预算均匀 verified RFT，且用三分之一数据即达到全量验证池微调水平；单次解码可达源模型验证器选择 Pass@4 的准确率；随机对照实验证实收益来源于覆盖不同的失败；Qwen2.5-7B 三随机种子复现也保持正向增益。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦单样本失败但可恢复的问题，GapFT 能以更少数据在匹配预算下显著提升单样本解码能力，并将搜索暴露行为高效内化到模型中。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR). An exact verifier can also support test-time scaling by selecting a passing response from multiple samples, while other deployments use beam search, adaptive sampling, or tools. We study single-sample decoding, where each query receives one response without search, to ask whether search-exposed behavior can be absorbed into the model. Existing verified-response post-training recipes do not generally distinguish problems already solved on the first decode from failures recovered within K samples. Under a fixed budget, this can spend examples repeating behavior the deployed policy already has. We introduce GapFT, which selects training evidence by the source checkpoint's single-sample outcome and fine-tunes on the Pass@K-Pass@1 gap: problems the policy fails on one sample but solves within K samples. We match training examples, processed tokens, and optimizer steps while keeping the objective unchanged. GapFT fills the matched budget with recovered failures and uses an exact decomposition to distinguish corrections of recovered and missed failures from regressions on first-decode successes. On LogiQA 2.0 and ReClor with Llama-3.1-8B, GapFT improves Pass@1 by 14.4 and 13.9 points over the source model, outperforms budget-matched uniform verified RFT at the same learning rate, and matches fine-tuning on the full verified pool using one third of the data. A single decode matches the source model's verifier-selected Pass@4 accuracy. A randomized control attributes gains to covering distinct failures, and our analysis relates available gains to transferable failure support. A three-seed Qwen2.5-7B replication retains positive gains over uniform RFT on both logic tasks.

</details>


### [41] [Calibration-First Cross-Cohort Multimodal Temporal Learning for Transferable Asthma-Risk Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35795)
*Taimoor Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CALIBRA，一个以校准为核心的多模态时序框架，用于跨队列、数据不完整条件下的短期哮喘恶化风险预测，并在三队列半合成基准上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型多在单队列内优化判别能力，当患者群体、传感器生态和模态变化时迁移后概率校准差。因此需要一种在校准优先、可迁移、可弃权的框架。

Method: 采用专用循环编码器处理环境、肺功能、症状、用药、可穿戴与情境等多模态流；可靠性条件门控抑制失效或缺失模态；梯度反转训练减少队列特征；基于收缩的分层逻辑层用患者不交叉的目标子集校准概率；分裂共形预测提供可弃权预测集。

Result: 在受控分布偏移、信息性缺失与目标患者封存的三队列半合成基准上，5个种子下平均目标测试AUPRC为0.224，强基线TemporalTransformer为0.240；平均AUROC为0.717，Brier为0.098。另评估了模态失效、校准、共形覆盖、决策曲线、亚组、消融、运行时间与参数量。

Conclusion: 结果在受控偏移下验证了方法与可复现流程，但未确立临床有效性，仍需在已协调的真实哮喘数据上进行外部验证，为真实队列验证提供依据。

Abstract: Asthma deterioration forecasting must remain reli- able when patient populations, sensor ecosystems, and available modalities change across cohorts. Existing models commonly optimize within-cohort discrimination and may produce poorly calibrated probabilities after transfer. We present CALIBRA, a calibration-first multimodal temporal framework for short- horizon risk prediction with incomplete data. Dedicated recurrent encoders process environmental, pulmonary, symptom, medication, wearable, and context streams; a reliability-conditioned gate suppresses stale or absent modalities, while gradient-reversal training discourages avoidable cohort signatures. A shrinkage- based hierarchical logistic layer calibrates probabilities using a patient-disjoint target subset, and split conformal prediction provides abstention-capable prediction sets. To avoid fabricating clinical evidence, we evaluate the complete implementation on a documented three-cohort semi-synthetic benchmark with controlled distribution shift, informative missingness, and sealed target patients. Across five configured seeds, CALIBRA achieved mean target-test AUPRC 0.224 versus 0.240 for the strongest non-ablation comparator, TemporalTransformer; mean AUROC was 0.717, and Brier score was 0.098. Experiments additionally assess complete-modality failures, calibration, conformal coverage, decision curves, subgroup behavior, ablations, runtime, and parameter count. The results verify the method and reproducible pipeline under controlled shift, but do not establish clinical effectiveness. External validation on harmonized real asthma. Overall this artifact provides evidence for carefully governed real-cohort validation.

</details>


### [42] [Binarization Flattens the Score Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35797)
*Jacob Cole*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM评判员奖励的二值化（通过/不通过）会掩盖评分的连续尺度信息，导致策略可进行不可检测的“拉伸”操纵；保留至少三档评分可消除该盲点，但仍有部分变化无法与真实改进区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判员常用作奖励以训练策略，但奖励常被压缩为通过/不通过的{0,1}，只报告结论而非满足标准的程度，这造成信息丢失并可能产生被策略利用的盲点。论文旨在分析二值化带来的可识别性缺陷及可行的补救方案。

Method: 将每个通过/不通过判定建模为未报告尺度上的分数与一个 cutoff 的比较：尺度上的拉伸使分数按比例趋近或远离 cutoff 但不跨越，因此判定不变。在联合高斯模型下，引入第三档评分（第二个阈值）可消除此类仿射拉伸歧义。用七准则评判员在 MATH 与 SciBench 输出上构造 14 个准则级拉伸进行检验，并在 n=1,024 下计算检验功效；同时分析残余不可区分变化的类型（任意档内变化，以及固定协方差、载荷对齐的均值漂移）。

Result: 14 个构造的准则级拉伸在二值化后全部不可见，但在三档评分下可见。n=1,024 时，同时给定两种总体分布的检验在 1.5 倍压力下功效至少 96.5%。共享因子参考近似在 MATH 与 SciBench 上拟合良好，但在 HealthBench 上不成立，界定了其经验适用范围。仍存在不可区分的变化，包括档内任意变化和固定协方差、载荷对齐的均值漂移——即被评判员读作能力提升的迎合式抬升特征。

Conclusion: 建议评判员至少保留三档评分（如判定每个准则为完全满足、部分满足或未满足，并奖励{0,0.5,1}），并对残余方向上的增益进行外部验证，因为该方向无法通过更细的评分尺度消除。保留评分档位只关闭了二值化造成的一个盲点，仅凭判定仍不足。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges are often used as rewards to train policies on objectives that deterministic verifiers cannot capture. However, these rewards are often collapsed to pass/fail ({0, 1}), which reports the verdict but not how well a response met each criterion. We model each pass/fail verdict as a score on an unreported scale, compared with one cutoff. A stretch of that scale moves every score proportionally toward or away from the cutoff, but never across it, so no verdict changes. A policy is therefore free to apply any stretch without changing anything the panel reports. Under a joint-Gaussian model, a third grade adds a second threshold and removes this affine stretch ambiguity. On MATH and SciBench outputs from one seven-criterion judge, all 14 constructed criterionwise stretches were invisible after binarization but visible with three grades. At $n=1{,}024$, a test given both population laws had at least 96.5% power at a $1.5\times$ stress. Retaining grades closes one blind spot created by binarization, but verdicts alone remain insufficient as some changes are still indistinguishable from genuine improvement. These include arbitrary within-grade changes and fixed-covariance, loading-aligned mean shifts -- the signature of a sycophancy-shaped lift the panel reads as competence. The shared-factor reference approximation fit MATH and SciBench but not HealthBench, delineating its empirical scope. We recommend keeping at least three grades (for example, asking the judge whether each criterion is fully, partially, or not met and rewarding {0, 0.5, 1}), and externally validating gains along the remaining direction, which no finer scale removes.

</details>


### [43] [HeadGuard: Selective Head Protection for Low-Bit VLM KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35800)
*Nenad Banfic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HeadGuard 是一种可组合的头部保护方法，通过在低比特 KV 缓存量化器上叠加固定的高精度掩码（保护约 1/8 的物理 KV 头为 BF16），在不替换底层量化器的前提下显著恢复 VLM 精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特 KV 缓存量化虽能节省存储，但会严重损害视觉语言模型（VLM）的准确率，因此需要一种既保留量化收益又能恢复精度的方法。

Method: 利用图像敏感性和输出敏感性分数离线选择物理 KV 头，约保护 1/8 的头，其图像键（可选值）保持 BF16，其余图像条目由基础量化器处理；方法与三种基础量化器兼容，并测试了 keys-only 保护及两种校准数据集。

Result: 在八个 VLM、三种基量化器和八个基准上评估：2 比特时，八模型六任务判别均值在最强基线上从 0.436 提升至 0.580；Qwen 与 InternVL 增益最大；还能提升生成答案与字幕保真度；精度增益在三种量化器和两种校准集上均持续存在。

Conclusion: HeadGuard 通过模拟量化验证，提供了一种可组合的方式提升低比特 VLM 精度，无需替换底层量化器。

Abstract: Low-bit key-value (KV) cache quantization saves storage but can sharply degrade vision-language model (VLM) accuracy. We introduce HeadGuard, a composable head-protection method that augments a base KV-cache quantizer with a fixed high-precision mask. Image-sensitivity and output-sensitivity scores select physical KV heads offline, with approximately 1/8 protected in the main experiments; their image keys and optionally values remain in bfloat16 (BF16), while the base quantizes unprotected image entries. Across eight VLMs, three base quantizers, and eight benchmarks (six discriminative and two generative), HeadGuard recovers a substantial fraction of lost accuracy on weaker quantizers, with the strongest gains for Qwen and InternVL. At 2 bits, the six-task discriminative mean over eight models rises from 0.436 to 0.580 on the weakest base; protection can also improve generated answers and caption fidelity to BF16 outputs. Mean accuracy gains persist across all three quantizers with both tested calibration datasets. Keys-only protection retains substantial recovery at lower modeled storage cost. Evaluated through simulated quantization, HeadGuard offers a composable way to improve low-bit VLM accuracy without replacing the underlying quantizer.

</details>


### [44] [Learned Compression of SAR Phase-History Data: A Rate-Honest Feasibility Study on GOTCHA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35848)
*Alizishaan Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试小型卷积自编码器（编码器置于传感器端）能否在SAR相位历史数据压缩上媲美BAQ。实验表明自编码器在所有码率下均失败，性能远逊于BAQ、KLT及自适应FFT阈值方法，问题出在模型、归一化与目标函数而非学习编码的本质极限；文章还揭示评估中的边界伪影与裁剪范围问题，并提出评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载SAR相位历史压缩对带宽至关重要，块自适应量化（BAQ）是现行运行标准。作者希望检验小型卷积自编码器（编码器部署于传感器端）能否在压缩性能上与该标准竞争，并探讨学习式编码在雷达压缩中的可行性。

Method: 使用AFRL GOTCHA数据集的复数相位历史块，所有方法统一计入全部传输比特，码率以bits per complex sample (b/cs)报告；检测性能用CA-CFAR检测的一对一匹配评分。对比方法包括：小型卷积自编码器（编码器28,656参数）、8-bit BAQ（±3σ裁剪与调优裁剪范围）、16×16块Karhunen-Loève变换（KLT），以及自适应FFT阈值化和在压扩傅里叶域中运行网络。还分析块内lag-1相干性和频谱集中度。

Result: 自编码器在所有码率下均失败：16 b/cs时NMSE为-2.87 dB，而8-bit BAQ（±3σ裁剪）为-35.5 dB、调优裁剪范围为-41.0 dB；也输给成本仅十分之一的16×16块KLT（-5.39 dB）。在压扩傅里叶域运行网络有帮助，但检测F1上限仅33%。数据每块lag-1相干性适中（|ρ|≈0.3），且具有块特定频谱集中性。评估发现：原始64×64块上97%的CFAR跨越是零填充检测器的边界伪影；在内部单元，BAQ的裁剪范围决定检测性能——8-bit BAQ在调优裁剪下保持69% F1，而±3σ时仅17%；在8 b/cs及以下，自适应FFT阈值化保留的检测多于BAQ。

Conclusion: 自编码器表现不佳的根源在于该模型、其归一化方式及目标函数，而非学习式编码的根本极限。研究还强调评估本身的两个问题（边界伪影与裁剪范围敏感）。作者据此提出一套面向学习式雷达压缩的评估协议。

Abstract: On-board compression of synthetic aperture radar (SAR) phase history is bandwidth-critical, and block-adaptive quantization (BAQ) remains the operational standard. We test whether a small convolutional autoencoder, with its encoder on the sensor, can compete with BAQ on complex phase-history patches from the AFRL GOTCHA collection. Every method is charged for all transmitted bits, rates are reported in bits per complex sample (b/cs), and detection is scored by one-to-one matching of CA-CFAR detections. The autoencoder (28,656 encoder parameters) loses at every rate. At 16 b/cs it reaches -2.87 dB NMSE, against -35.5 dB for 8-bit BAQ with $\pm 3σ$ clipping and -41.0 dB with a tuned clipping range. It also loses to a $16 \times 16$ block Karhunen-Loève transform (KLT), a local linear coder with a tenth of its encoder cost (-5.39 dB). Running the network in a companded Fourier domain helps, but its detection F1 remains bounded at 33%. The evidence points to this model, its normalization, and its objective, not to a fundamental limit of learned coding. Per patch, the data have modest lag-1 coherence ($|ρ| \approx 0.3$) and patch-specific spectral concentration. Two findings concern evaluation itself. First, 97% of CFAR crossings on raw $64 \times 64$ patches are border artifacts of the zero-padded detector. Second, on interior cells BAQ's clipping range decides detection: 8-bit BAQ keeps 69% F1 with tuned clipping but 17% at $\pm 3σ$, and at 8 b/cs or less adaptive FFT thresholding preserves more detections than BAQ. We close with an evaluation protocol for learned radar compression.

</details>


### [45] [A Mesoscopic View of Transformer Weights Through Row and Column Scale Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35852)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Transformer权重的“汇总统计”与“单个权重”之间提出介观层面的行列尺度场（row/column scale fields），即权重矩阵沿通道的中位数中心化log-RMS剖面，并配合全局尺度与平衡核，可精确表示矩阵。研究发现不同规模Pythia检查点及三类初始化家族的受控实验中，平衡后核心幅度剖面相似；用预先固定形式的混合桥模型可预测场宽导致的汇总形状偏离；索引场与共享功能通道的投影对齐，query/key剖面随RoPE频率重分配而非固定矩阵坐标；训练轨迹显示早期场形成后出现组件依赖的拓宽或收缩；AdamW二阶矩也存在类似功能组织；最后通过编辑冻结检查点分离了互易尺度平衡与相对通道增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究要么使用Transformer权重的汇总统计，掩盖了幅度在功能通道间的分布；要么直接比较单个权重，数量过多难以直接分析。因此需要研究介于两者之间的介观结构，以连接汇总幅度统计与通道组织，并为追踪和检验训练后的权重结构提供坐标。

Method: 提出行列尺度场：对权重矩阵沿通道计算中位数中心化的log-RMS剖面，并与全局尺度和完整平衡核一起精确表示矩阵。在四个规模的公开Pythia检查点及三类初始化家族的受控运行中进行分析；使用形式预先固定、系数拟合的混合桥模型预测汇总形状偏离；检查索引场对齐、query/key剖面与RoPE频率重分配关系、训练轨迹变化，并将通道分析扩展到AdamW二阶矩；最后通过编辑冻结检查点分离互易尺度平衡与相对通道增益。

Result: 平衡后测得的核心幅度剖面在不同规模和初始化家族中相似；混合桥模型能在受控网格的留出运行和数据臂上预测场宽导致的汇总形状偏离；索引场在共享功能通道的投影间对齐，query/key剖面跟随重分配的RoPE频率而非固定矩阵坐标；训练轨迹表现为早期场形成后组件依赖的拓宽或收缩；AdamW二阶矩在log空间行/列因子中显示相关功能组织；编辑冻结检查点时，展平相对通道增益会增加分布内损失，同时保持矩阵范数与平衡核不变。

Conclusion: 行列尺度场将汇总幅度统计与通道组织联系起来，并提供了用于追踪和测试训练权重结构的坐标系统。

Abstract: Pooled statistics of Transformer weights obscure how magnitude is distributed across functional channels, while individual weights are too numerous to compare directly. We study the mesoscopic level between them: row and column scale fields, the median-centred log-RMS profiles of a weight matrix over its channels, which together with a global scale and a full balanced core represent the matrix exactly. Across public Pythia checkpoints at four sizes and controlled runs from three initialization families, balancing reveals similar measured core magnitude profiles. A mixture bridge, with its form fixed before the analysis and its coefficients fitted, predicts the pooled-shape departure from field width on held-out runs and data arms of the controlled grid. The indexed fields retain further structure: they align across projections that share a functional channel, and query/key profiles follow reassigned RoPE frequencies rather than fixed matrix coordinates. Training trajectories show early field formation followed by component-dependent broadening or recession. Extending the channel-based analysis to AdamW's second moment reveals related functional organization in its log-space row and column factors. Finally, edits of a frozen checkpoint separate reciprocal scale balance, which preserves the forward computation, from relative channel gain: flattening the gain increases in-distribution loss while preserving matrix norms and the balanced core. Row and column scale fields thus connect pooled magnitude statistics to channel organization and provide coordinates for tracking and testing trained weight structure.

</details>


### [46] [Position: Let's Strengthen Verifiability If We Can't Enforce Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35854)
*Samet Hicsonmez,Nermin Samet,Renaud Marlet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出机器学习领域中结果难以复现、代码常不可得，并量化分析了这些问题，提出改进结果可核查性（结果可核查性）的具体建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习论文普遍包含支撑结论或展示方法性能的实证结果，但多数从业者清楚这些结果通常难以复现且情况日益恶化，代码往往不可获取，这阻碍了研究发展。作者因此希望分析并量化这些问题。

Method: 作为立场论文，作者对上述问题进行定性与定量的分析，并在分析基础上提出改进结果可核查性的具体建议；同时提供了代码与支撑材料（GitHub 仓库）。

Result: 论文分析并量化了结果难以复现、代码缺失及其对研究发展造成阻碍的问题，给出了相应数据支撑与观察结论。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁并提出了改善结果可核查性（即便无法完全实现可复现）的具体建议，并公开代码与材料以支持落实。

Abstract: In the field of Machine Learning, many papers contain empirical results supporting claimed statements or illustrating the performance of a proposed method. However, most practitioners know that (1) results are generally hard to reproduce, and increasingly so, (2) code is not often available to do so, and (3) it hinders the development of research. In this position paper, we analyze and quantify these issues, and make concrete proposals to improve result checkability, if not reproducibility. Code and supporting materials are available at https://github.com/giddyyupp/position-enforce-verifiability.

</details>


### [47] [Mara Chain: Rethinking Failure as a Stepping Stone for AI System Auto-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35855)
*Yubin Lyu,Fu Li,Jiawei Fei,Yang Zhao,Weixing Mei,Yinan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mara Chain 是一种将「被拒绝的候选配置」保留并迭代精炼的优化流程，通过固定深度精炼链与 Pareto 过滤 Top-N 选择约束候选池，在多个优化任务上以更少 rollout 取得更高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署期 AI 系统的优化越来越依赖编辑提示、技能、harness 和代码，而非模型权重。现有方法多采用「提议—评估—选择」流程，只保留满足接受准则的候选，丢弃其余候选。作者分析发现，被丢弃的候选往往含有对后续优化至关重要的信息，直接丢弃会使后续提议重复访问相同的失败模式。

Method: 提出 Mara Chain 精炼流程：不再丢弃被拒绝的候选，而是保留并利用此前各次尝试积累的证据对其进行迭代精炼，使被拒候选成为后续优化的垫脚石。该流程将每条精炼链限制在固定深度，并采用 Pareto 过滤的 Top-N 选择来约束候选池规模。

Result: 在 AppWorld 技能优化、TerminalBench 2.1 harness 优化和 MuSiQue 检索流水线优化上，Mara Chain 以更少 rollout 获得更大的任务性能提升。在 AppWorld 上相对性能比 GEPA、ACE 和 SkillOpt-Lite 最多高出 20.5%，达到目标分数所需 rollout 比 GEPA 少 65.5%；在 TerminalBench 2.1 上通过率分别比 AHE 和 Meta-Harness 提升 20.2 和 22.5 个百分点；在 MuSiQue 上相比手写检索流水线，测试集 nDCG@10 和 Recall@10 分别提升 0.104 和 0.131。

Conclusion: 保留并精炼被拒绝候选、配合固定深度与 Pareto 过滤 Top-N 选择，能有效利用失败候选中的信息，避免重复失败模式，从而在更少 rollout 预算下取得更优的优化效果，为部署期 AI 系统的提示/技能/harness/代码优化提供了一种更高效的方案。

Abstract: Optimizing deployed AI systems increasingly amounts to editing prompts, skills, harnesses, and code rather than model weights. Existing approaches commonly optimize these artifacts through propose-evaluate-select procedures, where candidate configurations are evaluated and only those meeting an acceptance criterion are selected. Yet our analysis shows that discarded candidates often contain information critical for subsequent optimization. Discarding them causes later proposals to revisit the same failure modes. We introduce Mara Chain, a refinement procedure that turns rejected candidates into stepping stones. Rather than discarding a rejected candidate, Mara Chain retains and iteratively refines it using evidence accumulated across preceding attempts. The procedure limits each refinement chain to a fixed depth and applies Pareto-filtered Top-N selection to bound the candidate pool. Across AppWorld skill optimization, TerminalBench 2.1 harness optimization, and MuSiQue retrieval-pipeline optimization, Mara Chain delivers greater task-performance gains with fewer rollouts. It outperforms GEPA, ACE, and SkillOpt-Lite by up to 20.5% in relative performance on AppWorld, reaching the target score with 65.5% fewer rollouts than GEPA. It improves the pass rate by 20.2 and 22.5 percentage points over AHE and Meta-Harness on TerminalBench 2.1, respectively, and improves MuSiQue test nDCG@10 and Recall@10 by 0.104 and 0.131 over a hand-written retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [48] [Beyond Keywords: Leveraging Generative LLMs and Label Aggregation to Classify Economic Policy Uncertainty in News Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35856)
*Paul Trust*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将大语言模型（LLMs）用于公共部门的经济监测，通过弱监督和生成式LLM提示生成合成标签，自动判断文章是否涉及经济政策不确定性（EPU）并识别其具体类型，兼顾成本与可扩展性，并支持多标签和层级分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究存在两类局限：基于关键词的方法误报率高；基于机器学习的方法需要大量高质量人工标注数据，成本高且耗时。因此需要一种既经济又可扩展的EPU监测方案。

Method: 提出基于弱监督技术的方法，利用生成式LLM通过提示（prompting）创建合成标签；同时提出面向EPU相关文章的多标签与层级分类方法。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验结果或定量指标，主要贡献在于方法的提出，即合成标签生成与多标签、层级分类方案。

Conclusion: 研究表明可利用生成式LLM的弱监督方式实现经济政策不确定性监测，具备成本效益和可扩展性，并支持更细粒度的多标签与层级分类需求。

Abstract: This research describes the adaptation of Large Language Models (LLMs) for economic monitoring in the public sector to automatically determine whether an article discusses Economic Policy Uncertanity (EPU) and to identify its specific type. Previous studies either rely on keywords, which often result in a high count of false positives, or use machine learning approaches that require a large number of quality human labeled data that is costly and time consuming to acquire. In this study, we propose approaches based on weak supervision techniques, using generative LLMs to create synthetic labels through prompting, making the approach both cost-effective and scalable. Additionally, we propose methods for for multi-label and hierarchical classification of articles related to EPU.

</details>


### [49] [ReLOBGen: Replayable Limit Order Book Message Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35867)
*Junoh Kang,Kiseop Lee,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ReLOBGen，一种可直接回放的限价订单簿（LOB）消息生成方法，通过生成过程中的引用订单选择与字段一致性约束，实现 100% 可回放性并提升市场真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环市场模拟要求 LOB 消息可回放，但现有生成器可能产生与当前市场状态不一致的不可回放消息，需依赖事后修正或拒绝重采样，这会改变回放消息分布或增加推理成本。

Method: ReLOBGen 在生成过程中保证可回放性：先从当前 LOB 的挂单中选取被引用订单，再生成与订单及市场状态一致的其余字段。引用选择通过从基于缓存订单表示和上下文查询高效计算的学习分布中采样；其余字段通过屏蔽无效 token 来强制一致性。

Result: 在 500 条消息的 rollout 中，ReLOBGen 实现 100% 可回放性，提升市场真实性（尤其是盘口顶部统计和 LOB 消息相对价格），相比 LOBS5 基线每条回放消息提速 2.7–3.6 倍。

Conclusion: ReLOBGen 无需事后修正或重采样即可高效生成真实且可回放的 LOB 消息，兼顾真实性与推理效率。

Abstract: We propose ReLOBGen, a method for generating limit order book (LOB) messages that are replayable by construction. Replayability is required for closed-loop market simulation, yet existing LOB message generators may produce non-replayable raw messages, i.e., messages inconsistent with the current market state. These generators therefore rely on post-hoc correction or rejection followed by resampling, which may alter the replayed message distribution or increase inference cost. ReLOBGen instead ensures replayability during generation: it selects the referenced order from the resting orders in the current LOB and then generates the remaining message fields to be consistent with that order and the market state. For realistic reference selection, ReLOBGen samples from a learned distribution over eligible resting orders, efficiently computed from cached order representations and a context-dependent query. It then enforces the consistency of the remaining fields by masking out invalid tokens. Together, these components enable efficient generation of realistic messages without post-hoc correction or resampling. In 500-message rollouts, ReLOBGen achieves 100% replayability, improves market realism, particularly for top-of-book statistics and the relative prices of LOB messages, and provides a $2.7\text{-}3.6\times$ speedup per replayed message over the LOBS5 baseline.

</details>

*Mason Dana,Khandaker Mamun Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于EEG癫痫发作预测的校准混合集成框架，将五种深度学习模型与三种经典机器学习模型通过逻辑回归堆叠元学习器融合，在CHB-MIT数据集上以严格LOPO交叉验证评估，达到74.2%发作级灵敏度与每小时1.24次误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作预测旨在发作前提供可操作的预警，但患者无关的泛化能力与误报控制仍是主要挑战，现有方法在这两方面难以兼顾。

Method: 提出校准的混合集成框架，融合五种深度学习模型与三种经典机器学习模型，采用逻辑回归堆叠元学习器；流程整合信号预处理、手工特征提取、类别不平衡处理、概率校准与基于临床动机的后处理；在CHB-MIT上使用严格留一患者交叉验证（LOPO），阈值与后处理参数仅在留出元数据上选取。

Result: 在过滤队列（排除发作前比率低于1%或高于15%的异常患者）上，模型实现74.2%发作级灵敏度，每小时1.24次误报，平均预警时间16.9分钟；受目标误报预算约束的测试调优oracle在每小时0.951次误报下达到60.9%灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该混合集成方法在患者无关设定下实现了较好的灵敏度与误报权衡，同时强调应结合实际误报率共同报告灵敏度指标，代码已开源。

Abstract: Epileptic seizure forecasting aims to provide actionable warnings before seizure onset, yet patient-independent generalization and false-alarm control remain major challenges. We propose a calibrated hybrid ensemble for EEG-based seizure forecasting that combines five deep learning models and three classical machine learning models through a logistic regression stacking meta-learner. The proposed pipeline integrates signal preprocessing, handcrafted feature extraction, class-imbalance handling, probability calibration, and clinically motivated post-processing. We evaluate the framework on CHB-MIT using strict Leave-One-Patient-Out (LOPO) cross-validation, with threshold and post-processing parameters selected only on held-out meta data. On the filtered cohort, excluding patients with anomalous preictal rates below 1\% or above 15\%, the model achieves 74.2\% seizure-level sensitivity at 1.24 false alarms per hour, with an average warning time of 16.9 minutes. A test-tuned oracle constrained to the target false-alarm budget achieves 60.9\% sensitivity at 0.951 false alarms per hour, highlighting the importance of reporting sensitivity together with realized false-alarm rates. Our code is available at: https://github.com/DanaMason/IEEE-CARS-Hybrid-Ensemble-Learning-for-EEG-Based-Epileptic-Seizure-Forecasting

</details>


### [51] [Making Cross-Continental Federated Learning Repeatable with FLIP: a Multi-Application Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36001)
*Rafael Garcia-Dias,Alexandre Triay Bagur,Chayanin Tangwiriyasakul,Virginia Fernandez,Parhom Esmaeili,Piyalitt Ittichaiwong,Yang Li,Lawrence Adams,Wason Buncharoen,Martin Chapman,Benjamaporn Chayanond,Sadthavud Chunrod,Tanawat Fongsri,Kass Gibson,Supat Plungprasertkul,Supawit Tangpanithandee,Kanyakorn Veerakanjana,Vicky Goh,Michela Antonelli,Joe Zhang,Kongkiat Kespechara,Sebastien Ourselin,M. Jorge Cardoso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开源多应用联邦学习平台FLIP，将常见联邦学习流程实现为可组合服务，并在英国与泰国的两个节点上通过合成胸部X光队列完成联邦微调与联邦评估两个用例，验证平台可支持国际联邦学习协作并提升可重复性、可审计性与站点治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域的联邦学习面临治理难题：每次协作都需要重建治理保障，导致多数项目停留在概念验证阶段。因此需要一个能提升可重复性、降低治理重建成本的平台方案。

Method: 提出开源的多应用平台FLIP，将常见联邦学习工作流实现为一组可组合服务，包括针对各站点结构化数据库的队列查询、按需从机构PACS获取DICOM数据、站点级项目审批以及可复用的联邦学习任务类型；并在英国和泰国两个客户端节点上，基于合成胸部X光队列运行联邦微调与联邦评估两个用例，每个机构独立审批参与并依据本地IT安全流程运行自身节点。

Result: 该平台成功支撑了跨越英国与泰国的国际联邦学习协作，两个用例均得以运行；平台提升了可重复性、可审计性和站点特定治理能力。研究还提供了对现有平台的全面比较，帮助研究者与运维者选择合适平台。作者强调这是操作性而非算法性主张，未比较联邦与集中式训练。

Conclusion: FLIP表明此类平台能够实现国际联邦学习协作，并改善可重复性、可审计性与站点级治理；研究定位为操作性贡献，而非算法性能比较。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) in healthcare remains challenging, as the overhead of rebuilding governance guarantees for every collaboration stops most projects at the proof-of-concept stage. Here we present FLIP (Federated Learning Interoperability Platform), an open-source, multi-application platform that makes FL training and evaluation repeatable. FLIP implements common FL workflows as a set of composable services: cohort queries against per-site structured databases, on-demand DICOM retrieval from institutional PACS, per-site project approval, and reusable FL job types. To demonstrate FLIP, we ran two distinct use cases, federated fine-tuning and federated evaluation, on synthetic chest X-ray cohorts across two client nodes based in the United Kingdom (UK) and Thailand. In FLIP, each institution independently approves its participation in each project and operates its own node under local IT security processes. This study makes an operational rather than an algorithmic claim. It does not compare federated with centralised training; for that question, we refer the reader to existing systematic reviews and meta-analyses. The central result is evidence that such platforms enable international FL collaboration and improve repeatability, auditability, and site-specific governance. We also present a comprehensive comparison of existing platforms to help researchers and operators choose the right platform for their use case.

</details>


### [52] [From Static Policies to Adaptive Priors in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35880)
*Tianwei Ni,Vineet Jain,Akash Karthikeyan,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"自适应离线强化学习(AORL)"这一新视角，主张离线RL的目标应从"直接部署"转向"学习自适应策略先验"，以支持后续在线交互中的持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线RL以保守目标学习可直接部署的策略，将数据分布外的不确定性悲观处理以保证稳健性。然而当离线训练的策略后续通过在线交互（如测试时适应、在线微调）更新时，这种"静态部署"的范式变得不完整，需要重新定义离线RL的目标。

Method: 本文为立场论文(position paper)。作者将新视角形式化为自适应离线强化学习(AORL)，并将其与offline-to-online RL加以区分；随后分析在分布偏移、数据集覆盖有限、测试时条件变化等情形下适应性的重要性，并探讨将贝叶斯离线RL作为构造自适应策略先验的一种原则性方向。

Result: 文章阐明了AORL与offline-to-online RL的区别，论证了适应性在分布偏移等条件下的必要性，并指出贝叶斯离线RL可通过保留对潜在环境的认知不确定性来构建自适应策略先验。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁将离线RL视为"为未来经验做准备"而非静态部署问题，并给出了相关联系、开放挑战与研究方向。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) has traditionally focused on learning policies for direct deployment under conservative objectives, where uncertainty outside the offline dataset is treated pessimistically to ensure robustness. We argue that this formulation becomes incomplete when an offline-trained policy is subsequently updated through online interaction, as increasingly occurs in modern intelligent systems through test-time adaptation and online fine-tuning. This position paper argues that, in such settings, the objective of offline RL should extend beyond immediate deployment and instead prioritize learning adaptive policy priors: policies that preserve the capacity to improve during subsequent interaction through memory, exploration, and self-correction. We formalize this perspective as adaptive offline reinforcement learning (AORL), distinguish it from offline-to-online RL, and explain why adaptability becomes important under distributional shift, limited dataset coverage, and changing test-time conditions. We further discuss Bayesian offline RL as one principled direction for constructing adaptive policy priors by preserving epistemic uncertainty over plausible environments. Finally, we outline connections, open challenges, and research directions for treating offline RL as preparation for future experience rather than as a static deployment problem.

</details>


### [53] [Encoder-Sharing Hierarchical Federated Multi-Task Learning for VANETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36157)
*M. Saeid HaghighiFard,Sinem Coleri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出编码器共享的层次化多任务联邦学习框架 EN-HMTFL，让执行不同感知任务的车辆共享编码器、保留本地解码器，在提升精度的同时减少通信轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车联网联邦学习框架普遍假设所有车辆协作训练同一任务的单一模型，这限制了其在真实车载环境中的适用性——实际中车辆可能执行输出空间不同的异构但相关的感知任务。

Method: EN-HMTFL 将基于聚类的层次化联邦学习与全局共享编码器和车辆本地解码器相结合。车辆执行不同任务时可协同学习可迁移的特征表示，同时任务特定模型保留在本地；仅编码器通过层次结构进行交换与聚合，原始数据和本地解码器参数始终留在车辆端。

Result: 在 MNIST 和 GTSRB 数据集的不同车载场景下评估，EN-HMTFL 相比对照的表示共享基准方法，精度最高提升 24.0%；在 EN-HMTFL 更早收敛的场景中，通信轮次最多减少 69 轮（28.8%）。

Conclusion: EN-HMTFL 通过编码器共享与层次化聚合，有效支持车联网中异构多任务协同学习，在保护数据隐私的同时兼顾精度提升与通信效率。

Abstract: Most federated learning frameworks for vehicular ad hoc networks assume that all vehicles collaboratively train a single model for a common task. This assumption limits their applicability to practical vehicular environments, where vehicles may perform heterogeneous but related perception tasks with different output spaces. This paper proposes encoder-sharing hierarchical multi-task federated learning (EN-HMTFL), which integrates cluster-based hierarchical federated learning with a globally shared encoder and vehicle-local decoders. EN-HMTFL enables vehicles performing different tasks to collaboratively learn a transferable feature representation while preserving their task-specific models locally. Only the encoder is exchanged and aggregated through the hierarchy, whereas raw data and local decoder parameters remain at the vehicles. The proposed framework is evaluated on the MNIST and GTSRB datasets in different vehicular scenarios. Across the evaluated scenarios, EN-HMTFL improves accuracy by up to 24.0% relative to the compared representation-sharing benchmark. In scenarios where EN-HMTFL converges earlier, the reduction reaches up to 69 communication rounds (28.8%).

</details>


### [54] [Learning in the Transverse Subspace: A Minimal Representation for Divergence-Free Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35884)
*Yifei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种最小表示，将 D 维无散向量场编码为同域的 (D−1) 维场，通过 Householder 正交变换构造可逆、保角的约化坐标，使神经算子直接在约化空间学习时间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无散向量场是流体与 PDE 的基本状态变量，但已有的冗余参数化（如 NCL 势）是多对一映射：虽然通过扩大等价解集降低了静态拟合误差，却无法为算子学习提供唯一状态。作者希望消除这种非唯一性。

Method: 利用傅里叶空间中的横截结构，用 Householder 正交变换直接构造 (D−1) 分量约化坐标。周期与闭合不可渗透场下变换可逆、等距、保角；开放非周期流用傅里叶延拓构造兼容周期场，并以最小能量准则选取唯一约化表示。神经算子仅在约化空间学时间演化，推理时直接解码为物理无散场，无需预测环境场或事后投影。

Result: 静态拟合与时间预测实验显示任务相关的权衡：冗余有利于静态优化，而唯一可逆坐标更适合时间动力学。移除学习状态空间中的无约束纵向/零方向后，预测误差更低、鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: 该约化表示在构造上保持无散性，同时为时间演化提供良定义的唯一状态，在预测精度与鲁棒性上优于冗余参数化。

Abstract: Divergence-free vector fields are fundamental state variables in incompressible flows and many PDE systems. Redundant parameterizations, including Neural Conservation Law (NCL) potentials, map multiple auxiliary representations to the same physical field. Our experiments show that this redundancy can reduce static representation-fitting error by enlarging the set of equivalent solutions, but the resulting many-to-one mapping does not provide a unique state for operator learning.
  We introduce a minimal representation that encodes a real \(D\)-component divergence-free vector field on a \(D\)-dimensional domain as a real \((D-1)\)-component field on the same domain. Exploiting the transverse structure imposed by incompressibility in Fourier space, we use a Householder orthogonal transformation to construct the reduced coordinates directly. For periodic and closed impermeable fields, the transform is invertible, isometric, and angle-preserving. For open nonperiodic flows, Fourier extension constructs a compatible periodic field, and a minimum-energy rule selects a unique reduced representation.
  Neural operators then learn temporal evolution entirely in this reduced space. At inference, the predicted \((D-1)\)-component field is decoded directly into a physical divergence-free \(D\)-component field, without predicting an ambient field or applying post-hoc projection.
  Experiments on static fitting and temporal prediction reveal a task-dependent trade-off: redundancy facilitates static optimization, whereas unique invertible coordinates provide a well-defined state for temporal dynamics. By removing unconstrained longitudinal or null directions from the learned state space, the proposed formulation achieves lower prediction error and greater robustness while preserving divergence freedom by construction.

</details>


### [55] [Replay the Curvature: Accurate and Scalable NVFP4 Quantization for Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36654)
*Ruiyi Ding,Jie Li,Kang He,Ziyan Liu,Chengru Song,Yuedong Xu,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Schur Replay 尺度选择算法与配套执行基础设施，用于低精度（NVFP4）大模型量化，在 Qwen3.5-397B-A17B 与 Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 上实现接近 BF16 的恢复率并显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的权重存储与显存流量是推理的主要开销，因此需要每权重仅用几比特的低精度格式。NVFP4 通过让每 16 个 E2M1 权重共享一个 E4M3 块尺度来提升局部范围利用率，但在 GPTQ 中选取该尺度很困难：量化一列会更新后续列，独立评估一个块会误估其最终重建误差。此外，大模型无法把全精度权重、校准激活和二阶状态同时放在单个加速器上，而按整层分配到设备又会让耗时的层求解串行化。

Method: 提出 Schur Replay 尺度选择算法，复现每个块尺度引起的 GPTQ 更新，并在考虑未量化列补偿后对块误差打分。配套执行基础设施只保留当前活跃层常驻，将激活在设备、主机和磁盘间分层存储，导出后即释放全精度层，并把相互独立的输出行分布到张量并行 rank 上。

Result: 在七个基准上，算法与基础设施结合在 Qwen3.5-397B-A17B 和 Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 上分别达到相对 BF16 的 99.35% 与 100.84% 问题加权恢复率。在 397B 模型上，基础设施将实测每层时间相对 ModelOpt 减少 15.17 倍、相对 LLM Compressor 减少 23.14 倍，且每 GPU 内存占用更低。

Conclusion: 通过尺度选择算法与执行基础设施的协同设计，可在极低位宽下保持接近全精度的质量，并大幅提升大模型量化效率、降低显存占用。

Abstract: Large language models make weight storage and memory traffic major inference costs, motivating low-precision formats that represent each weight with only a few bits. Such formats use a scale to map floating-point values into a small codebook; NVFP4 improves local range utilization by letting every 16 E2M1 weights share an E4M3 block scale. Choosing that scale is difficult in GPTQ because quantizing one column updates those that follow, so evaluating a block independently can misestimate its final reconstruction error. Large models pose a second challenge: full-precision weights, calibration activations, and second-order state cannot all remain on one accelerator, while assigning complete layers to devices leaves each time-consuming layer solve serial. We introduce \emph{Schur Replay}, a scale-selection algorithm that reproduces the GPTQ updates caused by each block scale and scores the resulting block error after accounting for compensation from unquantized columns. Separately, our execution infrastructure keeps only the active layer resident, tiers activations across device, host, and disk, retires full-precision layers after export, and distributes independent output rows across tensor-parallel ranks. Together, the algorithm and infrastructure attain $99.35\%$ and $100.84\%$ question-weighted recovery from BF16 across seven benchmarks on Qwen3.5-397B-A17B and Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct. On the 397B model, the infrastructure reduces measured per-layer time by $15.17\times$ over ModelOpt and $23.14\times$ over LLM Compressor, with lower memory used per GPU.

</details>


### [56] [The Decision Value of Perception Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35910)
*Hoang Pham Cong,Ho Viet Duc Luong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"感知计算的决策价值"概念，并构建DEEP基准，用预算受限的决策oracle评估级联感知的算力分配策略，发现大量升级反而损害下游决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应感知通常假设更精细的感知输出必然带来更好的决策，但感知服务于下游决策系统时，更好的感知结果未必改善决策。作者希望明确感知算力分配的评判标准，即应以下游损失变化（决策价值）而非感知指标本身来衡量。

Method: 定义感知计算的决策价值为将输入从廉价感知模式升级到昂贵模式所引起的下游损失变化；提出DEEP基准，在选择、延迟与能耗预算下，用预算受限的决策oracle对升级前分配器打分，并计入分配器自身计算开销；在KITTI与nuScenes上使用部署的单目几何模型，并在nuPlan上以真实检测器结果对PDM-Closed规划器做开环评估。

Result: 在KITTI与nuScenes上，34%–54%改变下游损失的升级反而使其变差；PDM-Closed规划器同样存在有害升级。nuScenes上感知增益与决策价值经常符号不一致。按缺失物体感知增益而非决策价值选择固定信号，会使实际测试决策增益平均损失全部廉价方案损失的7.4%。学习型分配器能找到有益升级但选出的有害升级接近随机水平；计入自身计算并在20%延迟预算下，仅轻量路由器（约全检测器一次前向的3.5%）仍优于随机。

Conclusion: 感知算力的分配应以决策价值而非感知增益来评判，均匀全保真推理只能作为基线而非上界；现有分配器在计入自身成本后大多难以稳定超越随机，亟需面向决策价值的高效路由方法。

Abstract: Adaptive perception spends extra computation on inputs where perception is expected to improve. When perception feeds a downstream decision system, a better perception output need not produce a better decision. We define the decision value of perception compute as the change in downstream loss from escalating an input from a cheap to an expensive perception mode. Because this value can be negative, the allocation of perception compute should be judged against a budget-constrained decision oracle, with uniform full-fidelity inference as a baseline rather than an upper bound. We introduce DEEP (Decision Evaluation for Escalated Perception), a benchmark that scores pre-escalation allocators against this oracle under selection, latency and energy budgets, charging each allocator for its own computation. With deployed monocular geometry on KITTI and nuScenes, we find that 34--54% of the escalations that change downstream loss make it worse; harmful escalations also occur for the published PDM-Closed planner, evaluated open-loop on nuPlan with real detector outcomes. On nuScenes, perception-level gain frequently disagrees in sign with decision value. This mismatch has practical consequences: choosing among fixed deployable signals by missed-object perception gain rather than by decision value reduces realized test decision gain by 7.4% of the all-cheap loss on average. Learned allocators recover part of the oracle's value by finding beneficial escalations but select nearly as much harm as random, and once their own computation is charged at a 20% latency budget, only the lightweight routers, at about 3.5\% of a full detector pass, still beat random.

</details>


### [57] [Federated Clustering with Unknown Local and Global Cluster Cardinalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36762)
*Mitushi Goyal,Tarun S.,Riddhanya Senapathi,Arun Raman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段联邦聚类框架，在全局与本地簇数K、K_g均未知时，用自适应分裂-合并(ASM)估计本地簇数，再驱动FedGEM等聚合器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免全局簇数K的联邦聚类方法仍假设每个客户端已知本地簇数K_g，这在客户端与服务器掌握信息同样有限（如独立运营工业站点的故障诊断）的场景下难以成立。

Method: 两阶段框架：客户端先用ASM从本地数据估计K_g。ASM通过BIC驱动的分裂生长球状高斯混合，再合并多余分量；无标签、仅在客户端留出数据上选超参数、不对簇的跨客户端共享方式作假设。推导了闭式分裂准则，其临界簇大小随各向异性增大而下降、随维度升高而上升。

Result: 在八个数据集上，ASM+FedGEM平均ARI为0.333，优于次优无标签估计器的0.256，接近提供真实本地簇数时的0.361；同时给出最可靠的全局K估计，并在客户端规模与本地基数解耦时保持稳健。经验上过度分裂随每簇点数增加而增长。

Conclusion: 在全局与本地簇数均未知的现实联邦场景下，先用ASM估计本地簇数可有效替代真实值，提升聚类精度与全局K估计可靠性。

Abstract: Federated clustering methods that do not require the global number of clusters $K$ still assume that each client knows its local number $K_g$. This assumption is hard to justify when clients know no more about their data than the server does, as in fault diagnosis across independently operated industrial sites. We propose a two-phase framework in which neither count is known: each client first estimates $K_g$ from its own data, and an aggregator that requires local counts, such as FedGEM, then uses these estimates in place of the true values. For the first phase we introduce Adaptive Split--Merge (ASM), which grows a spherical Gaussian mixture by BIC-driven splitting and then merges excess components. ASM uses no labels, selects its hyperparameters on held-out client data only, and makes no assumption about how clusters are shared across clients. We derive a closed-form split criterion whose critical cluster size falls with anisotropy and rises with dimension, and show empirically that over-fragmentation grows with the number of points per cluster, which federation divides among clients. Across eight datasets, ASM with FedGEM attains a mean ARI of 0.333, against 0.256 for the next best label-free estimator and 0.361 when the true local counts are supplied. It also gives the most reliable global estimates of $K$ and is robust when client size is decoupled from local cardinality.

</details>


### [58] [Learn Now, Use Next, Trust Later: Prequential Test-Time Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35911)
*Tong Zhao,Reed Li,Yuyang Hu,Yutao Zhu,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi,Yu Lu,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StepLearn：一种非参数框架，将"即时使用"与"持久信任"分离，在交互中把有信息量的转移转为假设，经跨回合前瞻验证后才用于持久指导，参数固定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署阶段的大模型智能体需要将新观察及时转化为指导。但许多测试时学习方法仅从已完成的回合中获取知识，反馈无法及时蒸馏以帮助下一步决策。若按单个转移粒度获取知识可减少延迟，却带来新问题：单个回合内有用的规则未必足够可靠以指导未来回合；等待验证会丧失即时收益，而无限制复用会传播偶然或错误归因的指导。

Method: 提出StepLearn非参数框架，分离即时使用与持久信任：将有信息量的转移转化为可指导下一步的假设；跨回合复用前需经前瞻验证，其预测效果在源回合之外由后续观察检验，只有得到充分支持的假设才能用于持久指导。整个过程更新外部知识而保持模型参数不变。

Result: 在WebArena-Lite与ALFWorld上进行五轮实验，GPT-5-mini平均成功率分别为59.9%与84.0%，Qwen3.5-35B-A3B分别为57.8%与88.1%，在四个设置中均超过最强基线EvoTest达2.2-12.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 学习动态表明，收益不限于最终重复轮次；多数设置下首次任务尝试即已出现优势，说明该框架能及时且可靠地利用交互反馈。

Abstract: Adapting large language model agents during deployment requires not only retaining past experience, but also turning new observations into timely guidance. Many test-time learning methods, however, acquire knowledge from completed episodes. Feedback from an ongoing interaction may therefore not be distilled into knowledge soon enough to help the next decision. Acquiring knowledge at the granularity of individual transitions could reduce this delay, but raises a separate challenge: a rule that is useful within one episode may not be reliable enough to guide future episodes. Waiting for validation can forfeit immediate benefits, whereas unrestricted reuse can propagate accidental or misattributed guidance. We introduce StepLearn, a nonparametric framework that separates immediate use from persistent trust. It turns informative transitions into hypotheses that can guide the next step, while requiring prospective validation before reuse across episodes. Their predicted effects are checked against subsequent observations outside the source episodes, and only sufficiently supported hypotheses become available for persistent guidance. This process updates external knowledge while keeping all model parameters fixed. Over five rounds on WebArena-Lite and ALFWorld, StepLearn achieves average success rates of 59.9% and 84.0% with GPT-5-mini, and 57.8% and 88.1% with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, respectively. It outperforms EvoTest, the strongest baseline, by 2.2-12.7 percentage points across the four settings. Learning dynamics further shows that these gains are not restricted to the final repetition, with advantages already present on first task attempts in most settings.

</details>


### [59] [Replication Failure and Trivial Baselines in Road-Level Crash Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35917)
*Maurya Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对近期"不确定性感知"路级事故预测模型进行独立复现，发现其多数设计增益无法跨区稳健复现，且图网络相对历史基线的优势主要源于基线回溯时窗设置过短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络被越来越多用于路级事故预测，但其报告增益的稳健性缺乏审查，尤其是跨区域泛化性、随机种子敏感性以及基线时窗是否公平匹配等问题未被检验。

Method: 独立重建近期不确定性感知模型的数据流水线，在伦敦三个自治市采用扩展窗口协议评估其11项设计决策；用多种子实验检验效应方向稳定性；并引入无参数基线（按历史累计事故数排序路段），在匹配历史深度与扫描不同回溯时窗（1–5年）下进行对比。

Result: 11项设计决策中仅4项在第二个自治市复现成功，7项失败，其中4项效应符号反转；单 borough 内不同随机种子可致效应变号，参考架构的每区种子波动高达35.7个点而本文模型仅4个点。在匹配历史深度下，本文模型与无参数基线无统计差异（-0.90点，p=0.61），而参考架构在18/18个留出窗口上均劣于基线（-17.37，p<10^-6）。基线准确率随回溯时窗在22.71%–83.94%之间大幅波动，该领域已发表的每个数值都可在1–5年时窗下被基线匹配。

Conclusion: 图网络在该任务上相对历史基线的表面优势很大程度上是基线时窗过短造成的假象；建议将时窗匹配基线与多种子报告作为最低实践标准。

Abstract: Graph neural networks are increasingly applied to road-level crash prediction, but the stability of their reported gains has received little scrutiny. We independently reconstruct the data pipeline of a recent uncertainty-aware model and evaluate eleven of its design decisions across three London boroughs under an expanding-window protocol. Four survive replication on a second borough; seven do not, and four of those reverse sign rather than attenuate. Multi-seed evaluation is decisive: one effect reverses sign between random seeds within a single borough, and the reference architecture exhibits per-borough seed spreads of up to 35.7 points against 4 points for ours. We further compare both networks against a parameter-free baseline that ranks segments by cumulative past crash count. At matched history depth our model is statistically indistinguishable from that baseline ($-0.90$ points, $p=0.61$), and the reference architecture loses to it on 18 of 18 held-out windows ($-17.37$, $p<10^{-6}$). Sweeping the baseline's lookback horizon shows it spans 22.71% to 83.94% accuracy on that variable alone, and that every published figure in this line of work is matched by the baseline at a horizon of one to five years. We argue that the apparent margin of graph networks over historical baselines in this task is substantially an artefact of the short horizons those baselines were computed over, and recommend horizon-matched baselines and multi-seed reporting as minimum practice.

</details>


### [60] [Normative Loss Landscape Navigation: A Trajectory-Based Approach to Mitigating Forgetting in Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35926)
*Isabelle Aguilar,Zayn Andre Zainal,Luis Fernando Herbozo Contreras,Zhaojing Huang,Omid Kavehei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TMLN（轨迹调节的损失景观导航），将持续学习建模为弯曲损失景观上的最优控制问题，用对角经验Fisher信息矩阵定义局部黎曼流形，并借助参数历史轨迹动态调节预条件器，从而在梯度更新中主动保护关键参数方向，减少连续任务间的损失壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习模型在非平稳数据分布上顺序训练时会出现灾难性遗忘。已有方法中，权重正则化虽常用，但只是施加刚性的标量欧氏惩罚，完全忽略了参数空间底层的黎曼几何结构；而当前基于梯度预条件的方法又过于短视。因此需要一个能同时兼顾几何结构与历史轨迹信息的统一框架来缓解遗忘。

Method: TMLN将持续学习形式化为弯曲损失景观上的最优控制问题，提出规范化的导航策略。具体地，它使用内存高效的对角经验Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）定义局部黎曼流形；为弥补对角近似的空间局限，TMLN利用网络参数值的归一化历史轨迹动态调节预条件器，并将这种基于轨迹的预条件直接整合进梯度更新中，从而无需依赖加性惩罚项即可主动屏蔽历史上关键的参数方向。

Result: 在类增量与域增量基准上的实证评估表明，该方法显著降低了相邻任务之间的损失壁垒（loss barrier）。

Conclusion: TMLN通过将黎曼几何、轨迹调节预条件与最优控制视角结合，提供了一种不依赖加性正则惩罚的持续学习导航策略，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，并在类/域增量任务上验证了其降低任务间损失壁垒的有效性。

Abstract: Continual learning models suffer from catastrophic forgetting when trained sequentially on non-stationary data distributions. Previously, this has been addressed through weight regularization. While preconditioning gradients offer a promising alternative to mitigate forgetting, current approaches are myopic. Conversely, standard regularization methods apply rigid, scalar Euclidean penalties that entirely ignore the underlying Riemannian geometry of the parameter space. To overcome this gap, we propose TMLN (Trajectory-Modulatory Landscape Navigation), a normative navigation policy that formalizes continual learning as an optimal control problem over a curved loss landscape. TMLN utilizes a memory-efficient diagonal empirical Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) to define a localized Riemannian manifold. To compensate for the spatial limitations of the diagonal approximation, TMLN dynamically modulates a preconditioner using the normalized historical trajectory of the network's parameter values. By integrating this trajectory-based preconditioning directly into the gradient update, we actively shield historically critical parameter directions without relying on additive penalties. Empirical evaluations on class- and domain-incremental benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly reduces the loss barrier between consecutive tasks.

</details>


### [61] [CADENCE: A Confidence-Adaptive Dual-Expert Network for Fast and Accurate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35929)
*Onisa Mpaunda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CADENCE，一种CPU原生的双专家时间序列分类架构，融合卷积线性专家与分布区间专家，并以置信度自适应元路由动态选择路由或软融合。在109个UCR数据集、30次重采样共3270次运行中，平均精度达0.8864，位列第二，仅落后HIVE-COTE 2.0（0.8895，差异不显著），单数据集平均耗时仅17.53秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类存在精度与计算可扩展性的尖锐权衡：元集成方法（如HIVE-COTE 2.0）精度最高但计算开销巨大，而超快随机卷积变换（如MiniRocket、Hydra）虽能在数秒内运行，却在相位无关分布、信号运动学建模及大量类别下的决策树碎片化问题上表现不佳。因此需要一种兼具高精度与高计算效率的方法。

Method: CADENCE将表示学习解耦为两条专用路径：一是卷积线性专家，将10,000个确定性膨胀特征与闭式L2正则化Woodbury岭分类配对；二是分布区间专家，将竞争性膨胀核（Hydra）与在信号运动学及FFT频谱带上的二进Cornish-Fisher矩近似配对，并用ExtraTrees集成拟合。此外，引入带稀有类保留的内部验证元路由，在纯专家路由与置信度加权软融合之间动态选择，最后在全部训练数据上重新拟合。

Result: 在全部109个等长UCR档案数据集上经30次重采样（共3,270次运行）评估，CADENCE取得总平均精度0.8864，在全档案中排名第2，仅次于HIVE-COTE 2.0（0.8895，pHolm=0.295，无统计学显著差异），并优于Hydra+MultiRocket（0.8818）、MultiRocket（0.8797）和HIVE-COTE 1.0（0.8786，pHolm=0.048）。CADENCE将差距缩小至0.31个百分点，同时每个数据集在双核CPU上平均仅需17.53秒。

Conclusion: CADENCE以CPU原生双专家架构在精度与效率之间取得良好平衡，精度接近HIVE-COTE 2.0且无显著差异，同时运行时间大幅缩短。源码与评估脚本已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Time series classification (TSC) exhibits a sharp trade-off between accuracy and computational scalability. Meta-ensembles like HIVE-COTE 2.0 reach state-of-the-art accuracy but require extensive compute, whereas ultra-fast random convolutional transforms (e.g., MiniRocket, Hydra) run in seconds but struggle with phase-independent distributions, signal kinematics, and decision tree fragmentation on large class counts.
  In this work, we present CADENCE (Confidence-Adaptive Dual-Expert Network for time series Classification Excellence), a unified, CPU-native dual-expert architecture. CADENCE decouples representation learning into two specialized pathways: (i) a Convolutional Linear Expert pairing 10,000 deterministic dilated features with closed-form L2-regularized Woodbury ridge classification, and (ii) a Distributional Interval Expert pairing competing dilated kernels (Hydra) with dyadic Cornish-Fisher moment approximations across signal kinematics and FFT spectral bands, fitted with an ExtraTrees ensemble. An internal validation meta-router with rare-class preservation dynamically selects between pure expert routing and confidence-weighted soft blending, followed by a full refit on 100% of training data.
  Evaluated across all 109 equal-length UCR Archive datasets over 30 resamples (3,270 total runs), CADENCE achieves a grand mean accuracy of 0.8864. This ranks #2 across the archive, surpassed only by HIVE-COTE 2.0 (0.8895, p_Holm = 0.295, no statistically significant difference), while outperforming Hydra+MultiRocket (0.8818), MultiRocket (0.8797), and HIVE-COTE 1.0 (0.8786, p_Holm = 0.048). CADENCE closes the gap to HIVE-COTE 2.0 to 0.31 percentage points while taking an average of only 17.53 seconds per dataset on a dual-core CPU. Source code and evaluation scripts: https://github.com/onisa-jr/CADENCE.git

</details>


### [62] [Graph neural networks for sampling-invariant embeddings of organized signal sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35934)
*Martin Bauw,Santiago Velasco-Forero,Jesus Angulo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"有组织信号集"的神经网络编码器，目标是将异构采样的信号集投影到固定维度的向量空间，从而消除采样差异，实现与拓扑无关的通用处理，并通过波形分离实验评估其判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器网络与雷达输出的是有组织的信号集合（如有序信号、网格内距离单元信号、图节点信号），且集合内各信号的采样参数可能各不相同。这种异构采样为后续统一处理带来困难，因此需要一种能将异构采样信号集映射到统一表示空间的方法。

Method: 研究面向有组织信号集的神经网络编码器。目的是把异构采样的信号集投影到任意固定大小的向量空间，使信号集可作为向量处理，摆脱采样差异限制，从而支持任意拓扑感知处理且不受信号处理约束。在能降低异构采样参数影响的表示空间中，通过信号集判别潜力（重点为波形分离）来评估表示的优劣。编码与嵌入判别实验仅使用合成复值射频信号。

Result: 论文通过编码和嵌入判别实验，在合成的复值射频信号上评估了信号集表示的判别潜力，重点关注波形分离效果。（摘要未给出具体数值结果。）

Conclusion: 所提出的编码器可将异构采样的有组织信号集映射为固定维度向量表示，降低采样参数差异的影响，并支持灵活的拓扑感知处理；其表示质量可通过波形分离等判别任务进行衡量。

Abstract: Sensor networks and radars can deliver signals as organized sets, e.g. ordered signals, signals describing range cells within a grid or signals perceived as graph nodes. Within such sets, individual signals may be characterized by distinct sampling parameters. This paper investigates organized signal sets neural network encoders. In the context of this work, the purpose of such encoders is to project heterogeneously sampled signal sets into an arbitrary fixed-size vectors space. This new representation space is designed so that signal sets can be processed as vectors rid of sampling differences to allow for arbitrary topology-aware processing with no signal processing constraints. Within this representation space designed to reduce the influence of heterogeneous sampling parameters, the relevance of signal sets representations is evaluated by considering signal sets discrimination potential with a focus on waveforms separation. The encoding and embeddings discrimination experiments conducted rely exclusively on synthetic complex-valued radiofrequency signals.

</details>


### [63] [KernelOnet: An Interpretable Neural Operator Based on Kernel Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35938)
*Yuan Guo,Hanshu Chen,Qiang Xi,Timon Rabczuk,Zhuojia Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可解释神经算子框架KernelOnet，将核函数显式引入神经算子结构，使其与边界型核展开方法的形式一致。该框架提供数据驱动可学习核、物理信息核与混合核三种互补核，在三个基准与一个浅水波导工程问题上取得高精度，且在可比情形下比DeepONet更准、参数更少，无监督配置无需内部解标签，推断成本远低于逐实例求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经算子（如DeepONet）通过深度网络隐式学习基函数，缺乏可解释性，且难以处理无界外部域中的声传播等问题；而边界型核展开方法具有明确的核结构优势。因此需要一种将核函数显式嵌入神经算子、兼顾物理先验与数据拟合的框架。

Method: 提出Kernel Operator Network（KernelOnet），用显式核替代DeepONet的trunk网络，并与核展开形式对齐。设计三种核：1）数据驱动可学习核，由神经网络参数化从数据学到的径向基函数，在常系数线性问题中可视为非奇异基本解；2）物理信息核，将解析基本解等物理信息嵌入网络结构，使展开自动满足控制方程，可仅凭边界条件无监督训练；3）混合核，按控制方程线性主部分将解拆分为由解析基本解张成的齐次部分与由低秩学习修正核承担的源项部分，在缺少解析基本解的非线性问题上平衡物理先验与数据拟合。

Result: 在三个基准和一个浅水波导工程问题上，KernelOnet均达到高精度；在可与DeepONet比较的场景中，它以更少可学习参数获得更高精度。其无监督配置无需内部解标签，单次查询推断成本远低于逐实例求解器，为通用神经算子难以处理的无界外部域声传播问题提供了有效途径。

Conclusion: KernelOnet通过显式核结构实现了可解释的神经算子，兼顾物理先验与数据拟合，在精度、参数效率、无监督能力和推断成本上具优势，是处理无界外部域声传播等问题的有效方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes an interpretable neural operator framework, the Kernel Operator Network (KernelOnet), which incorporates kernel functions explicitly into the neural operator architecture, so that the operator structure matches the kernel-expansion form used in boundary-type kernel-expansion methods. Unlike traditional neural operators such as DeepONet, which learn basis functions implicitly through deep networks, KernelOnet replaces the trunk network with explicit kernels and offers three complementary kernels: a data-driven learnable kernel, in which a neural network parameterizes a radial basis function learned from data, and which for constant-coefficient linear problems can be regarded as a non-singular fundamental solution; a physics-informed kernel, which embeds physical information such as analytic fundamental solutions into the network structure, so that the expansion satisfies the governing equation automatically and can be trained without supervision on boundary conditions alone, with no interior solution data; and a hybrid kernel, which splits the solution, according to the linear principal part of the governing equation, into a homogeneous part spanned by analytic fundamental solutions and a source part carried by low-rank learned correction kernels, thereby balancing physical priors against data fitting on nonlinear problems lacking an analytic fundamental solution. On three benchmarks and one engineering problem in a shallow-water waveguide, KernelOnet attains high accuracy; where comparable with DeepONet, it is more accurate with fewer learnable parameters. Its unsupervised configuration needs no interior solution labels, and its per-query inference cost is far below that of per-instance solvers, offering an effective route to acoustic propagation in unbounded exterior domains that general-purpose neural operators struggle to handle.

</details>


### [64] [EnJoi: Ensemble Joint Score Filter for Generative Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35944)
*Julien Moreau,Marc Lelarge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的基于扩散的数据同化算法，通过学习包含过去与未来状态的联合分布，动态平衡当前状态与新观测的置信度，从而改进稀疏、非均匀观测下的重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归扩散模型在数据同化中依赖前一步状态进行条件生成，但未考虑过去预测的不确定性，导致在观测稀疏或非均匀时重建效果不佳。

Method: 提出的扩散同化算法动态平衡当前状态与新观测的置信度，其关键设计是学习同时包含过去与未来状态的联合分布，并采用改进版的En4DVar算法，利用粒子集合的协方差进行求解。

Result: 在流体和交通流仿真实验中，该方法的 reconstruction 性能得到提升，尤其在观测稀疏且非均匀的情况下效果更明显。

Conclusion: 通过建模过去与未来状态的联合分布并动态权衡信度，所提方法有效克服了自回归扩散模型忽略历史预测不确定性的缺陷，提升了数据同化在困难观测条件下的表现。

Abstract: Data Assimilation (DA) aims to recover the full state of a dynamical system that is only partially observed. A solution is to use Score-based models to generate physically consistent trajectories that agree with the observations. These Autoregressive Diffusion models are trained by conditioning on the previous state; however, they do not take into account the uncertainty of their past predictions. We propose a new diffusion-based assimilation algorithm that dynamically balances the confidence in the current state and the new observations. Crucially, we choose to learn the distribution of the joint state containing both the past and future. This allows us to use a modified version of En4DVar, a classical DA algorithm that relies on the covariance of an ensemble of particles. Experiments on fluid and traffic flow simulations show improved reconstruction performance, especially in situations where observations are sparse and non-homogeneous.

</details>


### [65] [FluxLite: Inference-Time Proposal Control for Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35947)
*Yinuo Ren,Haoxuan Chen,Grant M. Rotskoff,Jiequn Han,Lexing Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FluxLite是一个无需训练的提议控制框架，通过在预训练反向速率的稀疏有向图上补偿稀疏跳变率扰动，降低Feynman-Kac SMC的权重退化，提升离散扩散模型的倾斜采样效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练离散扩散模型与扩散语言模型在许多推理任务中需要从预训练分布的倾斜分布采样。Feynman-Kac序贯蒙特卡洛原则上可精确校正，但其权重在提议动态与倾斜不匹配时易退化，增加粒子数收益有限。

Method: 在预训练反向速率的稀疏有向图上，任意稀疏跳变率扰动可通过Feynman-Kac势中的q_t加权图散度项精确补偿，从而保持目标路径并将残余重加权方差的控制转化为局部凸目标。实例化为两种采样器：单步局部重分配规则（HEU）与基于预训练速率基的小型非负二次规划（D-VCG）。并证明在标准score-entropy训练损失下的群体稳定性，识别出控制score误差鲁棒性的倾斜路径覆盖因子，以及固定受控Feynman-Kac递推的有限粒子收敛性。

Result: 在可解析有限状态CTMC基准上，终端KL较标准Feynman-Kac SMC基线最高提升两个数量级；在2D Ising采样上，行相关MSE几何平均降低5-7倍，峰值降低最高55倍。

Conclusion: FluxLite通过精确补偿与凸化方差控制，有效缓解Feynman-Kac SMC的权重退化，为离散扩散模型提供轻量、无需训练的高效倾斜采样方案。

Abstract: Many inference-time tasks for pretrained discrete diffusion models and diffusion language models reduce to drawing samples from a tilted version of the pretrained distribution. Feynman-Kac sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) makes this correction exact in principle, but its prescribed weights routinely degenerate when the proposal dynamics are misaligned with the tilt, capping the practical gains from additional particles. We introduce FluxLite, a lightweight, training-free proposal-control framework for discrete diffusion. On the sparse directed graph of pretrained reverse rates, any sparse jump-rate perturbation can be exactly compensated by a $q_t$-weighted graph-divergence term in the Feynman-Kac potential; the target path is therefore preserved while the residual reweighting variance becomes a local convex objective. We instantiate this principle as two practical samplers: a one-hop local reallocation rule (HEU) and a small nonnegative quadratic program over pretrained-rate bases (D-VCG). We further prove population stability under the standard score-entropy training loss, identifying a tilted-path coverage factor that governs robustness to score error, together with finite-particle convergence for a fixed controlled Feynman-Kac recursion. Empirically, FluxLite improves over standard Feynman-Kac SMC baselines by up to two orders of magnitude in terminal KL on an analytically tractable finite-state CTMC benchmark, and reduces row-correlation MSE on 2D Ising sampling by 5-7x in geometric mean and up to 55x at peak.

</details>


### [66] [Intrinsic Associative Memory on Riemannian Manifolds: Curvature, Capacity, and Emergent Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35948)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Zhaoyang Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在黎曼流形上构建内在稠密联想记忆，将记忆视为Epanechnikov核密度众数搜索，揭示曲率决定记忆内容与生成状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联想记忆的几何通常只作为约束条件，而曲率本身对记忆能力的作用未被系统研究。作者希望证明曲率不仅是数据属性，更是联想记忆的设计变量。

Method: 将记忆建模为黎曼流形上的Epanechnikov核密度众数搜索，比较测地线与体积校正两种能量；证明测地记忆总能保留孤立模式，而校正记忆服从Ricci曲率阈值；推导测地容量标度；分析重叠如何生成新记忆；并用黎曼均值漂移实现一步召回。

Result: 证明正曲率在高维可抹除记忆、负曲率强化记忆；得到保留全部模式容量标度q_β^{-1/2}、典型模式q_β^{-1}；设计配置可实现全部2^N-1子集模式，随机数据在存储阈值下仅产生泊松数量模式；仿真复现曲率转变与所有设计模式；在WordNet名词层级上体积校正改善低容量检索。

Conclusion: 曲率是联想记忆的设计变量而非仅数据属性，体积校正与测地能量在不同曲率下表现分离，为几何感知的记忆设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: Geometry does more than constrain an associative memory: curvature determines what it remembers and which states it creates. We develop intrinsic dense associative memories on Riemannian manifolds by casting memory as Epanechnikov kernel-density mode seeking. We compare geodesic and volume-corrected energies and show that curvature separates their behavior.
