We prove that geodesic memory always retains an isolated pattern, while corrected memory obeys a sharp Ricci-curvature threshold: positive curvature can erase memories in high dimensions, while negative curvature reinforces them. We derive geodesic capacity scalings of $q_β^{-1/2}$ for retaining every pattern and $q_β^{-1}$ for a typical one, where $q_β$ is the pairwise kernel-overlap probability. We show how overlap \emph{creates} novel memories: designed $N$-pattern configurations realize all $2^N-1$ subset modes, but random data at the storage threshold yield only a Poisson number. We establish exact one-step recall using Riemannian mean shift. In simulations, we recover the predicted curvature transition and every designed mode. On WordNet's full noun hierarchy, we demonstrate that volume correction improves low-capacity retrieval. Together, our work shows that curvature is a design variable for associative memory, not merely a property of the data.

</details>


### [67] [ROSS: Relearning from Self-Generated Rollouts through Selective Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35954)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Huayu Deng,Fei Zhao,Jiayan Fu,Bin Liang,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ROSS 方法通过选择性监督，将历史自生成 rollout 作为可复用经验进行离线 SFT，在数学、代码、指令遵循和软件工程任务上持续提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型后训练通过强化学习和在线蒸馏生成自 rollout，但策略更新后这些经验常被视为过时而被丢弃。历史 rollout 虽可能与新策略兼容并保留其不再稳定表达的行为，但也包含错误、废弃尝试和冗余动作，不宜全盘模仿，因此需要更细粒度的选择性监督。

Method: 提出 ROSS（通过选择性监督从自生成 rollout 中再学习）：保留完整历史轨迹作为上下文，仅对选定的模型生成续写部分施加损失，实现细粒度的选择性监督。

Result: 在领域强化学习、多教师在线蒸馏和智能体强化学习中，ROSS 持续改进上游 checkpoint 并在数学、代码生成、指令遵循、软件工程等任务上超越基线。在 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 上，六基准 MOPD 平均从 58.40% 提升至 62.20%，SWE-bench Verified 从 64.20% 提升至 68.40%。

Conclusion: 自 rollout 训练遗留了可复用的行为经验，可通过离线监督微调（SFT）进一步获益，且无需额外策略 rollout。

Abstract: Large language model post-training generates self-generated rollouts through reinforcement learning and on-policy distillation, yet this experience is often treated as stale once the policy advances. Historical rollouts can remain compatible with a later policy while preserving behaviors that the policy no longer expresses reliably. However, they may also contain mistakes, abandoned attempts, and redundant actions that should not be imitated, motivating finer-grained selective supervision. We introduce ROSS (Relearning from Self-Generated Rollouts through Selective Supervision), which preserves the full historical trajectory as context while applying loss only to selected model-generated continuations. Across domain-specific reinforcement learning, multi-teacher on-policy distillation, and agentic reinforcement learning, ROSS consistently improves upstream checkpoints and outperforms baselines across mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and software engineering. On Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, ROSS improves the six-benchmark MOPD average from 58.40% to 62.20% and SWE-bench Verified from 64.20% to 68.40%. These results show that self-rollout training leaves behind reusable behavioral experience that can yield further gains through offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT), without additional policy rollouts.

</details>


### [68] [Preferent Compression Bounds Are Tight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36030)
*Dario Paccagnan,Marius Tirlea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了偏好压缩（preferent compression）最新界是可证明紧的，并通过基于均匀分布与次序统计量的显式构造在极限下达到该界；同时给出更简短、仅依赖初等计数论证的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于学习的方法部署的关键瓶颈是缺乏严格的安全与性能证书。样本压缩为推导此类证书提供了有力工具，其中满足偏好性质（即学习理论中的稳定性）的算法可获得特别紧的界，并应用于场景方法、Pick-to-Learn、支持向量方法等。但这些界是否紧仍是未决问题。

Method: 通过基于均匀分布和次序统计量的显式构造，证明偏好压缩的最新界在极限下可达，从而证明其紧性；同时提供仅用初等计数论证、无需无限维对偶性的更简短证明。

Result: 证明了偏好压缩的最新界是可证明紧的，并给出达到该界的构造；此外给出了更短且更易理解的证明。

Conclusion: 偏好压缩的界是紧的，解决了此前的开放问题，并简化了该界的证明方法。

Abstract: The lack of rigorous safety and performance certificates remains a key bottleneck to the deployment of modern learning-based methods. Sample compression has recently emerged as a powerful tool for deriving such certificates, with particularly sharp bounds available for algorithms satisfying a so-called preference property -- also known as stability in the learning theory literature. These bounds find direct application across domains as different as the Scenario Approach, Pick-to-Learn, and Support Vector methods. However, whether they are tight has remained an open problem. In this paper we resolve this question affirmatively and show that the state-of-the-art bound for preferent compressions is provably tight. We establish this by exhibiting an explicit construction based on the uniform distribution and order statistics that attains the bound in the limit. Along the way, we also provide a considerably shorter and more accessible proof of this bound, requiring only elementary counting arguments and no infinite-dimensional duality.

</details>


### [69] [TORQUE: Optimizing What (not) to Quantize Before and After Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36032)
*Ran Ben Basat,Michael Mitzenmacher,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TORQUE：在均匀随机旋转量化中，联合优化旋转前后高精度保留的坐标数量与位置，在固定比特预算下提升重建精度与存储的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机旋转使归一化坐标分布近似高斯，从而可用离线优化的码本量化。但既有方法未考虑旋转前后大坐标被旋转扩散的问题，本文旨在通过选择性高精度保留大坐标降低量化误差。

Method: 提出TORQUE框架：在固定总期望比特预算下，联合优化旋转前与旋转后保留高精度的坐标数量与子集。推导量化误差上界，证明按top-k旋转前保留该上界最小，将子集搜索化简为对k的优化；配合离线码本与并行参数选择实现快速优化器。

Result: 在高斯模型下的数值评估，以及最近邻检索、KV-cache压缩和激活压缩实验中，展示出重建精度与存储开销之间更优的权衡。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化旋转前后高精度坐标的保留策略，TORQUE在固定比特预算下实现了优于既有随机旋转量化方法的重建精度与存储成本权衡。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations are an effective preprocessing step for quantization: they make normalized coordinate distributions approximately Gaussian, enabling the use of codebooks optimized offline. We introduce TORQUE, a framework that improves on previous quantization works that use random rotations by jointly optimizing how many and which coordinates to preserve at high precision both before and after rotation, under a fixed overall expected bit budget.
  Intuitively, before rotation, preserving large input coordinates at high precision can reduce overall error by preventing the rotation from spreading their values across many coordinates. Likewise, after rotation, preserving a small fraction of the largest-magnitude coordinates at high precision allows the remaining values to be quantized more accurately using codebooks optimized offline for the resulting truncated Gaussian distribution.
  We derive a quantization error upper bound and prove that top-$k$ pre-rotation retention minimizes it for each $k$. This reduces the search over coordinate subsets to an optimization over $k$, enabling a fast optimizer that uses offline codebooks and parallel parameter selection for practical implementation. We demonstrate an improved tradeoff between reconstruction accuracy and storage cost through numerical evaluation under the Gaussian model and experiments on nearest-neighbor retrieval, KV-cache compression, and activation compression.

</details>


### [70] [Neural networks for spectral optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36047)
*Alexis de Villeroché,Beniamin Bogosel,Stéphane Breuils,Dorin Bucur,Jacques-Olivier Lachaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种直接从区域几何预测微分算子谱的神经网络模型，用于谱泛函的形状优化，精度高且能恢复经典谱最优形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定依赖微分算子谱的泛函，需寻找最优化该泛函的区域。传统方法依赖PDE求解器进行优化，计算成本高昂，因此希望用神经网络直接从几何学习谱，以加速优化。

Method: 提出两种表示与模型：一是用傅里叶系数编码区域并结合轻量MLP，适用于星形几何；通过系数重缩放满足特征值缩放律，并对旋转与反射的输出取平均实现不变性。二是采用景观函数、指示函数及其梯度作为输入，经Gram-Schmidt过程输出正交特征函数及对应特征值。

Result: 傅里叶模型精度达0.2%；景观模型在前十个特征值上平均相对误差1%，优于FNO的4%；用SDF替换景观函数会同时恶化预测与优化误差；模型可从合成形状泛化到经典图像数据集。两种方法均能恢复经典谱最优形状，如第一特征值对应的圆盘及更高特征值的猜想极小值。

Conclusion: 模型可对特征值给出准确且可微的估计，适用于涉及谱量的形状优化问题。

Abstract: Given a functional dependent on the spectrum of a differential operator, we address the problem of finding a domain which optimizes this functional. PDE solvers might be used to tackle this optimization. It is however computationally expensive. We propose two neural network models which learn the spectrum directly from the geometry of the domain and can be used to optimize the domain from one or more eigenvalues. We investigate two representations. The first encodes the domain through Fourier coefficients and a light MLP, which is efficient on star-shaped geometries, achieving a precision of 0.2\%. Through a rescaling of the coefficients the designed models satisfy the scaling law of the eigenvalues. Additionally, averaging the outputs of the trained surrogates over rotations and reflections induces invariance for these transformations. The second is a model that takes the landscape function, the indicator function and the gradient of the landscape function. A Gram-Schmidt process produces orthogonal eigenfunctions as output of the model along with the associated eigenvalues. The landscape model reaches 1\% mean relative error on the first ten eigenvalues, compared with 4\% for an FNO model. Replacing the landscape by an SDF worsened both prediction and optimization errors. The trained model also generalizes from synthetic shapes to domains given as classical image dataset. The resulting surrogates of both approaches recover classical spectral optima such as the disk for the first eigenvalue or the conjectured minima of higher eigenvalues. This confirms that our models produce accurate differentiable estimates of eigenvalues, which can be used in shape optimization problems involving spectral quantities.

</details>


### [71] [Improving scalable oversight with co-trained monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36049)
*Joseph H. Rudoler,Kevin Tan,Benedict Tessler,Timothy Kong,Enric Boix Adserà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 worker-monitor 框架中对抗监控规避问题，提出通过 co-training 监控器与 worker 来避免该失效模式，并分别给出监督与自监督的理论刻画与算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对固定监控器训练 worker 会激励其对监控器进行规避这一问题，作者希望探究通过联合训练监控器（co-training）是否能够避免这种失效模式。

Method: 监督设定下证明监控可行（误差与查询率趋零）当且仅当可能监控函数类具有有限 Littlestone 维数；自监督设定下提出基于 test-time distillation 的 co-training 流程：监控器利用额外测试时算力生成训练标签，再在标准算力策略上训练这些标签，并针对多数投票标签在自适应 worker 分布与动作覆盖条件下给出有限样本锐化保证。

Result: 理论上给出了监控可行性等价于有限 Littlestone 维数的刻画，以及多数投票标签下监控器判决收敛到其初始众数判决的锐化保证；在代码安全场景中对监督方法进行了压力测试，worker 被对抗性训练以欺骗监控器。

Conclusion: 自适应监控器更能跟上不断演化的 worker 策略，而固定监控器更易被规避。

Abstract: Worker-monitor setups are a promising approach to AI oversight, but training workers against fixed monitors can incentivize monitor evasion. We study whether this failure mode can be avoided by co-training the monitor alongside the worker, and explore both supervised and self-supervised approaches. In the supervised setting, we prove a characterization: monitoring is possible with vanishing error and query rates exactly when the class of possible monitor functions has finite Littlestone dimension. This connects worker monitoring with an established literature on adversarial online learning. For self-supervision, we propose a co-training procedure based on test-time distillation: the monitor uses additional test-time compute to generate training labels, then trains its standard-compute policy on those labels. For majority-vote labels, we give a finite-sample sharpening guarantee under adaptive worker distributions with action coverage, that shows that the monitor's verdicts converge to its initial modal verdicts. We stress-test the former in code-security settings where the worker is trained adversarially to fool the monitor. Our results suggest that adaptive monitors are better at keeping pace with evolving worker strategies, while fixed monitors are more vulnerable to evasion.

</details>


### [72] [ABC: Advantage-Based Control Variates for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36058)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Florent Draye,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Advantage-Based Control Variates (ABC)，将轨迹级控制变量与 Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) 结合成单一 actor-critic 算法，用于 RLVR 场景，在数学推理任务上以明显更少的在线优化步数达到与 GRPO 相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 中无 critic 的 GRPO 等策略梯度方法表现优异，而 actor-critic 方法因价值函数近似误差通过时序差分等优势估计器引入偏差。作者希望重新审视价值函数与控制变量的作用，降低偏差同时保持高效。

Method: 通过 advantage-value 形式重新审视轨迹级控制变量，提出 ABC 公式；发现其协方差结构与 DAE 中的回报分解密切相关，进而将 ABC 与 DAE 结合为单一 actor-critic 算法，并在 offline-to-online RLVR 设置中评估：critic 先在已收集轨迹上训练，再在线学习过程中自适应调整。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，ABC 使用显著更少的在线优化步数即可达到与 GRPO 相当（competitive）的性能。

Conclusion: ABC 表明合理利用 advantage-value 形式的控制变量并结合 DAE，可在 RLVR 中提升 actor-critic 方法的样本效率，为 critic 辅助的策略优化提供有效方向。

Abstract: Recent progress in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has highlighted the effectiveness of simple critic-free policy-gradient methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). In contrast, actor-critic methods rely on learned value functions whose approximation error can introduce bias through commonly used advantage estimators such as temporal-difference error. Motivated by this observation, we revisit trajectory-level control variates through an advantage-value formulation, which we call Advantage-Based Control Variates (ABC). This formulation reveals that the covariance structure is closely related to the return decomposition used in Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE). Finally, we combine ABC with DAE into a single actor-critic algorithm and evaluate it in an offline-to-online RLVR setting, where the critic is first trained on previously collected trajectories and adapted during online learning. On mathematical reasoning tasks, ABC achieves performance competitive with GRPO using substantially fewer online optimization steps.

</details>


### [73] [Introducing the CZAR Loss: A Tailored Objective Function for Financial Log-Return Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36061)
*Joel Pfeffer,J. M. Diederik Kruijssen,Florian Stecker,Steven N. Longmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出金融收益率预测中对称损失函数（MSE、MAE）与经济学目标错位，因条件均值接近零而使零预测近最优。提出CZAR分段二次损失，满足凸性、方向不对称、近线性惩罚、大误差发散和自适应损失下限五项要求。理论上证明在均值对数损失下CZAR评估的方向准确率阈值接近50%，远优于对称损失。LightGBM实验显示CZAR降低零收益偏差并提升大波动收益的方向准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融对数收益率条件均值接近零，导致MSE、MAE等对称损失将常数零预测视为近最优解，训练中压缩预测值、评估中让平凡预测器占据排名优势，从而惩罚具有真实但噪声较大的方向性预测能力的模型。

Method: 在高斯线性预测模型下分析对称单调损失的普适盈亏平衡方向准确率；提出CZAR（Composite Zero-Agnostic Return）分段二次损失函数，基于五项设计要求构建：预测凸性、与真实收益方向相关且在零处消失的不对称性、欠估与错误方向预测的近线性惩罚、大误差发散、评估用自适应损失下限。该损失具有闭式梯度与Hessian，四个超参数通过相关默认值简化为单一选择。

Result: 理想化测试中，CZAR评估的预测器在均值对数损失下超越零预测器所需的最低方向准确率接近50%随机水平，而对称损失的相应阈值随预测噪声急剧上升；该优势在重尾收益分布下依然存在。LightGBM在日内BTC对数收益率实验中，CZAR训练的模型降低了"零收益偏差"并提升了大波动收益的方向准确率。

Conclusion: CZAR损失函数有效缓解了对称损失在金融收益预测中的错位问题，在理论和实证上均显示出对方向性预测能力的更好保留与评估，为量化金融中的收益预测提供了更契合经济学目标的损失设计。

Abstract: In quantitative finance, standard regression losses are misaligned with the economics of return prediction. As the conditional mean of financial log-returns is close to zero, symmetric losses such as the mean squared and mean absolute errors make the constant zero forecast a near-optimal solution, penalizing models with genuine but noisy directional skill. This applies both during training, where predictions shrink toward zero, and during evaluation, where trivial forecasters can lead loss-based rankings. Under a Gaussian linear prediction model, we show that all symmetric monotonic losses share a universal breakeven directional accuracy against the zero predictor, which rises sharply and becomes unobtainable as the prediction noise approaches the standard deviation of the returns. We introduce the CZAR (Composite Zero-Agnostic Return) loss function, a piecewise quadratic loss built around five key requirements: convexity in the prediction, asymmetry with true return direction that vanishes at zero, near-linear penalization of undershoots and wrong-direction predictions, divergence for large errors, and an adaptive loss floor for evaluation. CZAR is provably convex in the prediction at fixed true value, has closed-form gradient and Hessian suitable for custom objectives in gradient-boosted libraries, and its four hyperparameters reduce to a single choice through correlated defaults. In idealized tests, the minimum directional accuracy required for a CZAR-evaluated forecaster to outperform the zero predictor under mean log loss remains near the 50% chance level, whereas the corresponding threshold for symmetric losses rises sharply with prediction noise. This advantage persists under heavy-tailed return distributions. In a LightGBM experiment on intraday BTC log-returns, CZAR-trained models reduce the `zero-returns bias' and improve directional accuracy on large-magnitude returns.

</details>


### [74] [Understanding Decision-Making Mechanisms in Neural Routing Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36063)
*Fatemeh Askari,Mazdak Teymourian,Mohammad Izadi,Mahdieh Soleymani Baghshah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对三种自回归神经组合优化(NCO)模型(AM、POMO、LEHD)进行行为分析、表征探测与因果干预，揭示其决策机制差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCO虽在经验上表现优异，但其驱动决策的内部机制仍鲜有探索，缺乏可解释性分析。

Method: 选取两类编码器-解码器配置的代表模型：AM与POMO(重编码器-轻解码器)、LEHD(轻编码器-重解码器)，通过行为分析、表征探测和因果干预进行研究。

Result: AM与POMO全程遵循持续几何模式；LEHD则含可线性获取的多步未来动作信息。因果实验证实未来节点表征在LEHD决策中的作用；LEHD强依赖当前节点表征做即时局部决策，起始节点表征起更广泛的导航作用；跨实例对齐分析显示LEHD将当前节点表征映射到较共享的潜空间区域。

Conclusion: 在TSP与CVRP上，这些架构不同的求解器呈现不同决策模式，为NCO求解器的可解释分析奠定基础。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) has achieved strong empirical success, yet the internal mechanisms driving model decisions remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate three representative autoregressive NCO models spanning two encoder-decoder configurations: AM and POMO (heavy-encoder, light-decoder), and LEHD (light-encoder, heavy-decoder). Through behavioral analyses, representation probing, and causal interventions, we examine how these models construct solutions and use internal representations during decoding. Our results suggest that AM and POMO predominantly follow a persistent geometric pattern throughout solution construction, whereas LEHD contains linearly accessible information about multiple future actions. Causal experiments further provide evidence for the role of future-node representations in LEHD's decision-making. We also observe that LEHD relies strongly on the current-node representation for immediate local decisions, while the start-node representation plays a broader navigational role over the subsequent route. Cross-instance alignment analyses additionally indicate that LEHD maps current-node representations into a relatively shared latent region, which may provide a stable reference for evaluating subsequent decisions. Across the Traveling Salesman Problem and the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem, these results reveal distinct decision-making patterns across these architecturally distinct solvers and provide a foundation for more interpretable analyses of NCO solvers. Code and additional visualizations are provided in the https://github.com/NCO-Interpretability/NCO-Interpretability.

</details>


### [75] [FLOORA: A Human-Aligned Domain-Specific Language Model for Architectural Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36064)
*Sahand Rezaei-Shoshtari,Patryk Wozniczka,Shu Ishida,Gregg Streuber,Farnoosh Javadi,Jeffrey Landes,Angela Ju,Muhammad Azam,Bryan Lim,Johan Luttun,Indrajeet Haldar,Jonathan Shaw,Beatriz Guerra,Ivan Sosnovik,James Stoddart,Robert Giaquinto,Adam Gaier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLOORA，一套面向建筑平面布局生成的领域专用小模型（DSL模型），通过专用数据与对齐技术，0.6B模型超越大得多的前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用基础模型虽生成能力强，但在需要结构化表示的工程领域表现不佳，因此需要领域专用的小规模模型来生成结构化、可验证的输出。

Method: FLOORA结合了高效的DSL、自定义分词、领域专用预训练、监督微调（SFT）以及带有人类偏好学习奖励和可验证奖励的强化学习（RL）。

Result: 0.6B的FLOORA在VLM评判中于分布外真实建筑达到最高92.0%胜率，合成建筑达96.0%；人类评估中89.3%被评为最佳模型，并附有大量实验与消融研究验证其建筑与几何有效性。

Conclusion: 尽管聚焦建筑领域，该结果表明类似的领域专用方案可能对其他具有结构化、可验证输出的工程领域同样有效。数据集、模型与推理代码已开源。

Abstract: Foundation models are powerful generators, but many engineering domains require structured representations that general-purpose systems handle poorly. We introduce FLOORA (Floor Layout Optimization with RL Alignment), a family of small domain-specific language (DSL) models for architectural layout generation. With specialized data and alignment, our 0.6B model outperforms much larger frontier models, achieving VLM judge win rates up to 92.0% on out-of-distribution real-world buildings and 96.0% on synthetic buildings. Human evaluations further corroborate these results, with FLOORA selected as the best model in 89.3% of evaluations. FLOORA combines a token-efficient DSL, custom tokenization, domain-specific pretraining, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL) with learned human-preference and verifiable rewards. This pipeline improves architectural and geometric validity, supported by extensive empirical evaluation and ablation studies. Although focused on architecture, our results suggest that similar domain-specific recipes may be useful in other engineering domains with structured, verifiable outputs. Datasets, models, and inference code are available at https://github.com/AutodeskAILab/floora.

</details>


### [76] [GEM-KMeans: Memory-Efficient and Accurate Clustering on Massive Scale with GPU Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36074)
*Peng Xu,Nihar Koganti,Volodymyr Kindratenko,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GEM-KMeans，一种可光谱归一化且数学等价的NLR矩阵分解聚类公式，通过IO感知的GPU实现将内存占用降至单个因子，实现大规模高效精确聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数据聚类需要在保证统计精度的同时实现内存高效扩展。NLR矩阵分解用于K-means可连接半定松弛并获得最优平均情况精确恢复保证，但直接GPU实现需多个大因子缓冲区及大量数据移动，本质上是内存受限的。

Method: 提出GEM-KMeans，一种光谱归一化但数学等价的NLR公式，将梯度更新、非负投影及用于归一化与迭代移动的充分统计量融合进矩阵乘法epilogue。IO感知的GPU实现仅物化单个因子，外加小块归约数组存于HBM。推导了显式内存开销与光谱归一化平滑界。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集的大规模场景下实现了精确聚类。相比现有GPU加速的Lloyd算法，GEM-KMeans的性能提升涉及依赖数据的运行时权衡。

Conclusion: GEM-KMeans通过减少内存占用与数据移动，在不牺牲统计精度的情况下实现了大规模内存高效聚类，为大规模聚类提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Memory-efficient scaling on clustering problems without sacrificing statistical accuracy is of central interest for large-scale data analysis and machine learning problems. Nonnegative low-rank (NLR) matrix factorization for $K$-means is a scalable clustering method, which connects to semidefinite relaxations with optimal average-case exact recovery guarantees. However, a direct GPU implementation of NLR requires multiple large factor-sized buffers and substantial data movements that are essentially memory-bound. In this paper, we introduce GEM-KMeans, a spectrally normalized yet mathematically equivalent NLR formulation that fuses the gradient update, nonnegative projection, and sufficient statistics for normalization and iterate movement into a matrix-multiplication epilogue. Instead of retaining three massive factor-sized arrays, our IO-aware GPU implementation materializes only one single factor with small tile-reduction arrays as additional storage in the High Bandwidth Memory (HBM). We derive explicit memory costs and spectrally normalized smoothness bounds for optimizing the clustering objective function. Accurate clustering is demonstrated at massive scales on synthetic and real datasets, where performance gains of GEM-KMeans over existing GPU-accelerated Lloyd's algorithms involve data-dependent runtime tradeoffs.

</details>


### [77] [Early Learning Shapes Later Directions Of Representation Change In Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36081)
*Yuantao Deng,Jinnuo Liu,Kaizhen Tan,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 网络早期表征变化形成低维“脚手架”子空间，后续学习会持续复用该子空间，体现历史依赖的几何印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络在学习新任务时表征不断变化，早期表征变化是否自发形成一种几何结构，并持续影响后续学习。

Method: 在四个预训练视觉编码器和两个数据集上，识别早期表征漂移的低维子空间（scaffold），检验后续任务是否复用它；对比随机子空间；考察不同早期任务但相同后期输入的历史依赖性；分析单个优化器更新；并用约束实验限制运动方向。

Result: 后续表征变化一致偏好早期定义的子空间而非匹配的随机替代；复用具有历史依赖性，各网络偏好自身学习历史诱导的脚手架；该偏好也出现在单个优化器更新中，即使主导局部响应方向已偏离原脚手架；在脚手架内约束运动比随机约束更显著减缓新任务习得，而对旧任务保持的影响不一致。

Conclusion: 早期经验在神经网络如何适应未来任务上留下持久的几何印记。

Abstract: Representations continually change as a network learns new tasks. We ask whether early representational changes naturally form a geometric structure that continues to shape later learning. We identify a low-dimensional subspace of early representation drift, which we call a scaffold, and test whether it is reused across subsequent tasks. Across four pretrained visual encoders and two datasets, later representational changes consistently favor this early-defined subspace over matched random alternatives. This reuse is history-dependent: when networks experience different early tasks but identical later training inputs, each network preferentially reuses the scaffold induced by its own learning history. The same preference appears in individual optimizer updates, even though the network's dominant local response directions shift away from the original scaffold. Finally, constraining motion within the scaffold slows new-task acquisition more than matched random constraints, while effects on old-task retention are less consistent. In summary, these results suggest that early experience leaves a persistent geometric imprint on how neural networks adapt to future tasks.

</details>


### [78] [PHASE: A Physiology-Guided Hierarchical Foundation Model for Intracranial EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36087)
*Yipeng Zhang,Chenda Duan,Yuanyi Ding,Tianyi Wang,Atsuro Daida,Masaki Izumi,Yuta Tanoue,Naoto Kuroda,Shaun A. Hussain,Nishant Sinha,Eishi Asano. Hiroki Nariai,Vwani Roychowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出PHASE，一种生理学引导的iEEG基础模型，将临床可识别的生理特征作为显式学习目标，结合掩码潜空间预测构建时序（PHASE-T）与时空（PHASE-ST）两阶段预训练，在多个临床任务上刷新了SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有iEEG基础模型主要依靠重建或预测输入进行学习，导致关键生理特征仅被隐式保留；其评估也多局限于认知解码和狭窄的癫痫检测任务。在Omni-iEEG等广泛临床基准上，冻结使用的基础模型仍逊于任务专用模型，因此需要一种能让生理特征成为显式学习目标的基础模型。

Method: 提出PHASE，将生理特征与掩码潜空间预测配对，构建时序阶段PHASE-T（通道内）与时空阶段PHASE-ST（跨同步通道间）。模型在来自9个临床中心、222名参与者的异质iEEG记录上进行预训练。通过匹配消融实验，与仅用潜空间预测或辅助波形重建的变体进行对比。

Result: 在Omni-iEEG全部五项临床任务上，冻结的PHASE-T超越所有评估过的基础模型，最高提升31%；微调后的PHASE-T超过任务专用模型，达到新的SOTA。PHASE-T受益于生理监督，在每项任务的匹配消融中均优于仅潜空间预测或波形重建变体。它能泛化到未见机构，在少量或无本地标签时也优于对比模型。PHASE-ST在癫痫起始区识别上优于PHASE-T，冻结时在BrainTreebank上解码音量与音高的表现优于已发表模型。此外，PHASE能习得临床医生所识别的生理特征，如癫痫发作起始及传播、解剖区域身份，尽管其预训练中并不包含发作期记录或解剖标签。

Conclusion: PHASE通过显式生理监督与两阶段时序-时空预训练，在广泛临床基准上超越现有基础模型与任务专用模型，并展现出良好的跨机构泛化能力和可解释的生理特征表征学习能力。

Abstract: Clinicians and neuroscientists have long analyzed intracranial electroencephalography (iEEG) through directly measurable physiological characteristics, which carry much of the information that downstream tasks depend on. Recent iEEG foundation models learn by reconstructing or predicting their inputs, which leaves the retention of these characteristics implicit. They are also evaluated mainly on cognitive decoding and a narrow clinical task, i.e., seizure detection. On a broad, clinically relevant benchmark such as Omni-iEEG, they remain below task-specific models when used frozen. We introduce PHASE, a physiology-guided foundation model that makes these characteristics explicit learning targets, pairing them with masked latent prediction in a temporal stage (PHASE-T) within each channel and a spatiotemporal stage (PHASE-ST) across synchronized channels. PHASE is pretrained on heterogeneous recordings from 222 participants at nine clinical sites. On all five Omni-iEEG clinical tasks, frozen PHASE-T outperforms every evaluated foundation model by up to 31\%, and fine-tuned PHASE-T surpasses the task-specific models, setting a new state of the art. PHASE-T benefits from physiological supervision, outperforming variants trained with latent prediction alone or auxiliary waveform reconstruction on every task in matched ablations. PHASE-T generalizes to unseen institutions, outperforming the compared models with few or no local labels. PHASE-ST further improves seizure-onset-zone identification over PHASE-T and, when frozen, decodes sound volume and pitch on BrainTreebank better than published models. Beyond task performance, PHASE learns to encapsulate the physiological characteristics clinicians recognize, from seizure onset and its propagation to anatomical region identity, even though its pretraining contains no ictal recordings or anatomical labels.

</details>


### [79] [Data Unlearning via Inverse Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36099)
*Aleksei Leonov,Nikita Kornilov,Zhenhe Zhang,Evgeny Burnaev,Iaroslav Koshelev,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IDU统一框架，将多步匹配模型（流匹配、扩散）同时蒸馏为高效单步生成器并遗忘指定训练子集，无需保留数据或额外分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步匹配模型（流模型、扩散模型）生成质量高但推理成本高，且可能复现训练数据中不希望出现的成分，因此需要同时对模型进行蒸馏加速与遗忘（unlearning）处理。

Method: 将蒸馏建模为数据分布上的min-max目标，把该分布表示为遗忘集与生成分布的混合，并与教师训练分布比较，从而在最优点仅恢复保留数据；方法仅需预训练的全数据教师模型和遗忘集数据。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上，于流匹配与基于分数的扩散设定下，IDU显著降低遗忘类别的生成频率，同时保持保留类别的生成质量。

Conclusion: IDU是首个在无条件下流匹配与分数扩散模型中同时实现遗忘与蒸馏的统一框架。

Abstract: Multi-step matching models, including flow and diffusion models, produce high-quality outputs but incur substantial inference costs and may reproduce unwanted components of their training datasets. We introduce Inverse Distillation Unlearning (IDU), a unified framework that simultaneously distills a teacher multi-step matching model into an efficient one-step student generator and suppresses outputs corresponding to a designated training subset. We first formulate distillation as a min-max objective over a data distribution and then represent this distribution as a mixture of the forget-set and the generated distributions. This allows us to compare this mixture with the teacher's training distribution and recover only the retained data at the optimum. Our method requires only a pretrained full-data teacher and data from the forget set, without access to retained training examples, extra feature extractors or classifiers. Extensive experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 datasets under flow-matching and score-based diffusion settings demonstrate that IDU substantially reduces the generation frequency of forgotten classes while preserving generation quality on the retained classes. To the best of our knowledge, IDU is the first unified framework for simultaneous unlearning and distillation in unconditional flow-matching and score-based models.

</details>


### [80] [LoopICL: Looping a single transformer block to solve tabular tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36108)
*Amir Rezaei Balef,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopICL 是一种循环式 Transformer（looped transformer），通过单一模块反复迭代处理数据，将参数量与计算深度解耦。在相同 FLOPs 下，其性能与 TabICLv2 相当，但参数量减少近 90%，并支持推理成本与性能的灵活权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期机制性研究表明，表格基础模型（TFM）中大量参数是冗余的。借助上下文学习（in-context learning）的表格基础模型虽已在预测任务上超越梯度提升树，但参数冗余问题促使作者探索更高效的架构设计，即在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅减少参数。

Method: 提出 LoopICL，一种循环式 Transformer，核心设计将参数量与计算深度解耦。它仅由单个模块构成，通过两条耦合流处理数据：单元流（cell stream）捕获逐单元特征表示，行流（row stream）捕获上下文示例表示，二者通过列内注意力与跨列注意力联合精炼。预训练阶段变化循环次数，使模块在测试时可展开不同迭代次数，并用学习到的退出门（exit-gate）自动决定何时退出。

Result: 在标准设置下，LoopICL 在相同计算成本（FLOPs）下于 TabArena 和 TALENT 基准上的表现与 TabICLv2 相当，同时参数量减少近 90%。此外，其循环设计使用户能够权衡推理成本与性能。

Conclusion: LoopICL 证明了通过循环架构将参数量与计算深度解耦的可行性，在保持竞争力的同时极大降低参数规模，并提供资源感知的表格基础模型，支持推理成本与性能的动态权衡。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models using in-context learning have recently surpassed gradient-boosted trees on predictive tabular tasks. However, recent mechanistic insights suggest that parameters in these models are largely redundant. We introduce LoopICL, a looped transformer whose core design decouples parameter count from computational depth. LoopICL consists of a single block, processing data through two coupled streams: a cell stream capturing per-cell feature representations and a row stream capturing in-context example representations, jointly refined through within-column and cross-column attention. During pre-training, we vary loop counts, allowing the block to be unrolled for a varying number of iterations at test-time and use a learned exit-gate to automatically exit. In its standard setting, LoopICL performs competitively with TabICLv2 on TabArena and TALENT at the same computational cost (FLOPs), while using nearly 90% fewer parameters. Furthermore, its recurrent design enables users to also trade off inference cost and performance, providing a resource-aware TFM.

</details>


### [81] [Koa-action: Fast and Consistent Structured Decision Making with Generative LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36115)
*Shenghong Dai,Shiva Kumar Pentyala,Yingchi Liu,Shubham Mehrotra,Suman Banerjee,James Zhu,Bin Bi,Sitaram Asur,Phil Mui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Koa-action 通过单 token 输出与原子标签 token 的监督微调，把分类等原子动作转化为确定性一步解码，在保持竞争性精度的同时实现低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景需要低延迟分类，但现有 LLM 方法生成冗长多 token 输出、推理昂贵；编码器模型虽快却牺牲任务灵活性。因此需要兼顾速度与灵活性的方案。

Method: 提出 Koa-action 框架，面向低延迟原子动作（分类、语义端点检测、布尔校验、打分等），将其形式化为单 token 输出的约束生成。方法引入原子标签 token，并采用监督微调，把分类简化为确定性的一步解码问题。

Result: 在标准基准上取得有竞争力的精度且延迟低而稳定。生产级意图路由基准上达到 85.5% 准确率，与最强前沿模型相当并领先部分模型；约半秒响应，在相同服务条件下比各前沿模型快数倍（中位数最高约 7.5 倍）。相比专用单 token 系统 Jev/TypeSafe，精度相当、中位数更快，并支持多模态输入与多标签输出。

Conclusion: Koa-action 表明将原子动作重构为单 token 约束生成，可在低延迟下实现竞争性精度，并在速度、多模态与多标签能力上优于现有单 token 与通用 LLM 方案。

Abstract: Industry applications often demand low-latency classification, yet current large language model (LLM) approaches remain poorly suited for latency-critical applications. Existing prompting and constrained decoding produce verbose, multi-token outputs that require expensive token-by-token generation, while encoder-based models achieve faster inference but sacrifice task flexibility. We propose Koa-action, a framework for low-latency atomic actions -- fast, single-step decisions such as classification, semantic endpointing, Boolean checks, and scoring -- formulated as constrained generation with single-token outputs. By introducing atomic label tokens and applying supervised fine-tuning, our method reduces classification to a deterministic one-step decoding problem. Across standard benchmarks, Koa-action delivers competitive accuracy with consistently low and stable latency. On a production intent-routing benchmark, Koa-action reaches 85.5% accuracy -- competitive with the strongest frontier models (Claude-4.8-Opus, Gemini-Pro-3.1) and ahead of GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5-Pro -- while answering in about half a second, several-fold faster than every frontier model (up to ~7.5x at the median) under identical serving conditions. Against the dedicated single-token system Jev/TypeSafe, Koa-action is competitive on accuracy and faster at the median, while also handling multimodal inputs and multi-label outputs that single-label text systems do not.

</details>


### [82] [Dyad: Extending Large Language Models with Native Typed Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36116)
*Yundaichuan Zhan,Weishi Wang,Wenbiao Liu,Daniel Dahlmeier,Chengwei Qin,Juncheng Li,Fredrik D. Johansson,Zhongqi Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dyad，一种为预训练 LLM 增加原生类型化决策能力的通用智能体架构，通过环境条件动作编码器将候选动作描述并行嵌入并与 LLM 内部状态打分，实现可复用表征学习与高效动作评分，在多种交互任务上取得优于常规 RL 后训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用智能体多依赖 LLM 的文本生成能力进行决策，缺乏对"类型化动作"的原生支持，导致动作空间扩展时效率低、表征难以复用。作者希望在不破坏 LLM 通用能力的前提下，为其引入结构化的决策归纳偏置，从而构建更强大的通用智能体。

Method: Dyad 在预训练 LLM 上增加一个环境条件动作编码器：对每个候选动作描述进行并行嵌入，再与 LLM 的内部状态进行打分，得到类型化动作上的分布。该方法将决策分解为"演化交互状态表征"与"环境特定动作语义"两部分，从而引入可复用表征的归纳偏置，并保持动作空间增长时评分的高效性。作者研究了两种由环境交互驱动的强化学习设置：一是冻结 LLM、仅训练动作编码器；二是联合优化两者。

Result: 冻结 LLM 仅训练动作编码器时，在四个未见环境中均取得稳定增益，实现了不修改任何 LLM 参数的模块化适配。联合优化两个组件在多种交互任务与不同模型规模上均优于常规 RL 后训练，其中在 ALFWorld 上使用 9B 模型取得平均 3.80% 的绝对提升，同时改善了通用知识、推理与编程能力。

Conclusion: 通过将类型化决策原生嵌入 LLM，Dyad 证明了决策与语言能力可以相互促进：既能以模块化方式低成本适配新环境，也能在联合训练下超越传统 RL 后训练，并保持甚至提升模型的通用知识与推理能力。

Abstract: We study how to build more capable general-purpose agents by extending large language models (LLMs) with native typed decision-making. We introduce Dyad, an architecture that augments a pretrained LLM with an environment-conditioned action encoder that embeds each candidate action description in parallel, then scores these embeddings against the LLM's internal state to yield a distribution over typed actions. By factorizing decision-making into representations of the evolving interaction state and environment-specific action semantics, Dyad introduces an inductive bias for learning reusable representations while keeping action scoring efficient even as the action space grows. We investigate two complementary reinforcement learning settings driven by environment interaction. With the LLM frozen, training the action encoder alone achieves consistent gains across four unseen environments, enabling modular adaptation without modifying any LLM parameters. Jointly optimizing both components outperforms conventional RL post-training across diverse interactive tasks and model scales, including a 3.80% average absolute gain on ALFWorld with a 9B model, while improving general knowledge, reasoning, and coding.

</details>


### [83] [Why Backdooring Neural Networks is so Easy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36117)
*Issam Seddik,Mohamed El Amine Seddik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对二次神经元在高斯混合数据上的后门攻击进行闭式理论分析，揭示特征学习动态会加剧后门脆弱性：懒惰学习需要 $\\alpha\\propto\\pi^{-1/2}$，而特征学习将预算改善至 $\\alpha\\propto\\pi^{-1/4}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统难以防御后门攻击，需要从原理上估计攻击者所需的毒化比例 $\\pi$ 与触发器强度 $\\alpha$。已有实证表明，毒化大语言模型所需恶意样本数几乎不随干净数据集增长而增加，但缺乏理论解释。

Method: 作者对在高斯混合分布上、被毒化数据训练的二次神经元进行精确闭式分析，对比懒惰学习与特征学习两种机制下的攻击成功率条件，并在保持干净精度为一阶 $O(\\pi)$ 的前提下推导触发器强度与毒化比例的标度关系。

Result: 懒惰学习下成功攻击要求 $\\alpha\\propto\\pi^{-1/2}$；特征学习下检测器的损失裕度按 $O(\\alpha^4)$ 缩放，从而把攻击预算改善为 $\\alpha\\propto\\pi^{-1/4}$。即在毒化比例较小时，非线性特征学习显著降低所需触发器强度。

Conclusion: 使神经网络强大的特征学习动态同时也使其更易受后门攻击。该理论机制与大规模实证观察一致，并说明基于线性启发式的安全审计会系统性低估特征学习范式的后门脆弱性。

Abstract: Securing modern AI systems against backdoor attacks remains an open challenge and requires fundamentally principled estimates of the adversary's budget -- the poison fraction $π$ and trigger strength $α$ needed to construct successful yet stealthy attacks. Motivated by recent empirical evidence that poisoning large language models can require a nearly constant number of malicious samples even as clean datasets grow, we derive an exact closed-form analysis of a quadratic neuron trained on a poisoned Gaussian mixture. We show, perhaps counterintuitively, that the same feature-learning dynamics that make neural networks powerful can also make them more vulnerable to backdoors. Specifically, with clean accuracy preserved to first order, $O(π)$, we demonstrate that lazy learning imposes the inverse-square-root scaling $α\propto π^{-1/2}$ for a successful attack, while feature learning induces a quadratic detector whose loss margin scales as $O(α^4)$, improving the attack budget to $α\propto π^{-1/4}$. Consequently, nonlinear feature learning substantially reduces the trigger strength required at small poison fractions, thereby in a sense making feature learners more backdoor vulnerable. These results provide a theoretical mechanism consistent with large-scale empirical observations and demonstrate that security audits based on linear heuristics can systematically underestimate backdoor vulnerability in the widely adopted feature-learning regimes.

</details>


### [84] [ThinQuant: Scalable Rotation Learning for Weight and Activation Quantization of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36120)
*Mehdi Makni,Ryan Lucas,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ThinQuant提出数据选择与精确降维优化两个思路，大幅提升旋转学习效率，实现低比特量化下大规模语言模型的高效校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转学习方法（如SpinQuant、DartQuant）在最大规模架构上难以扩展，梯度自由旋转学习存在计算瓶颈。

Method: 利用激活凸包的几何结构进行数据选择，仅需极少校准数据即可匹配现有方法性能；在约简校准集上，激活张成r维子空间（r<d），将d×d旋转优化等价为Stiefel流形上的d×r矩阵优化，并用高效ADMM算法迭代求解薄矩阵更新。

Result: Llama-3-70B W4A4KV4量化下，ThinQuant校准不到12分钟，WikiText-2困惑度5.63，优于DartQuant的7.55（需111分钟）；在单张H200 GPU上扩展至Llama-3.1-405B，校准约2小时，W4A4困惑度2.97，优于GPTAQ+QuaRoT的3.48。

Conclusion: ThinQuant通过数据选择与降维优化显著提升旋转学习效率，可扩展至超大模型并保持优异量化性能。

Abstract: Learned rotations play an important role in enabling low-bit weight and activation quantization of large language models by smoothing outliers in the activation distribution. State-of-the-art approaches include gradient-based procedures such as SpinQuant and computationally friendlier gradient-free approaches such as DartQuant, but both remain hard to scale to the largest architectures. To address the computational bottlenecks in gradient-free rotation learning, we introduce two ideas for efficiency, (i) a data selection procedure which reduces the required number of calibration data points, and (ii) an exact reduction of the associated optimization on this reduced calibration set. Our data selection procedure exploits the geometric structure of the convex hull of the activations. Using this idea, we show that a carefully selected calibration set with several orders of magnitude fewer activations than state-of-the-art rotation-based methods can match their performance in low-bit quantization settings. Under this extreme data efficiency, the selected activations span an $r$-dimensional subspace with $r<d$, making optimization over a $d\times d$ rotation equivalent to optimizing a $d\times r$ matrix on the Stiefel manifold. We solve this reduced problem using an efficient ADMM algorithm that iteratively employs thin matrix updates at every step, hence the name ThinQuant. For Llama-3-70B with W4A4KV4 quantization, ThinQuant completes the entire rotation calibration in under 12 minutes and achieves a WikiText-2 perplexity of 5.63, compared with 7.55 for DartQuant, which requires 111 minutes. Unlike SpinQuant and DartQuant, ThinQuant also scales to Llama-3.1-405B on a single H200 GPU, completing rotation calibration in just over 2 hours and achieving WikiText-2 perplexity of 2.97 at W4A4, compared with 3.48 for GPTAQ+QuaRoT.

</details>


### [85] [Reasoning with Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36126)
*Mayalen Etcheverry,Pietro Miotti,Aidan Sirbu,Konstantin Schürholt,Mariia Drozdova,Arna Ghosh,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,James Manyika,Blake Richards,Eyvind Niklasson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经细胞自动机（NCA）——仅使用严格局部连接和异步更新的循环细胞网络——能否完成复杂多步视觉推理，并证明其能求解大型迷宫、数独和 ARC-AGI-1 等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 视觉推理架构普遍依赖全局连接与同步，而生物系统表明复杂计算可在更去中心化的方式下完成。NCA 虽在人工生命领域被广泛研究，但其能否执行复杂多步推理尚不清楚，因此作者希望检验去中心化、局部连接的 NCA 的推理能力。

Method: 采用神经细胞自动机（NCA），即具备严格局部连接和异步更新的循环细胞网络；通过训练使其产生时空动态，并引入样本回放（sample replay）和随机扰动进行训练，同时在测试时保留随机性；还使用冗余轨迹剪枝提升并行试验效率。

Result: NCA 能求解大型迷宫、数独和 ARC-AGI-1 等具有挑战性的视觉推理任务；在更大网格、更长 rollout 或并行试验下表现出分布外泛化能力，且并行试验可通过剪枝冗余轨迹提高效率；泛化能力依赖于样本回放与随机扰动训练，测试时随机性仍有益；NCA 还具备鲁棒性，可动态调节计算以从损伤中高效恢复，并能扩展到原始像素空间求解推理。

Conclusion: 去中心化、局部连接且异步更新的 NCA 具备强大的多步视觉推理能力，并能实现多种分布外泛化与鲁棒恢复，表明复杂推理不必依赖全局连接与同步。

Abstract: Modern AI architectures used to solve visual reasoning tasks typically rely heavily on global connectivity and synchronization. As biological systems demonstrate, though, sophisticated computation can be performed in a more decentralized fashion. In this work, we test the reasoning capabilities of Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs), networks of recurrent cells that use strictly local connectivity and asynchronous updates. NCAs have been extensively studied in artificial life experiments, but it is unclear whether they can perform complex multi-step reasoning. We show that NCAs produce spatio-temporal dynamics capable of solving challenging visual reasoning tasks, including large mazes, Sudoku, and ARC-AGI-1. Furthermore, we provide evidence that NCAs generalize out-of-distribution when running with larger grids, longer rollouts, or parallel trials; and that the latter can be made more efficient via pruning of redundant trajectories. We find that these generalization capabilities depend on training with sample replay and stochastic perturbations, and that stochasticity remains beneficial at test time. Finally, we show that NCAs are robust reasoners capable of dynamically modulating compute to recover efficiently from damage, and that they can scale to solve reasoning in raw pixel space.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Continuous Patient Trajectories from Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36144)
*Silas Ruhrberg Estévez,Kara Liu,Christopher Chiu,Benjamin Atta Owusu,Umesh Kadam,Russ B. Altman,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EHRFlow：一种以编码患者历史为条件、支持不规则时间观测与任意预测跨度的多边际流匹配框架，用于电子健康记录中的未来事件预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录对潜在患者状态的观测是不规则的，而状态在时间上连续演化。现有自回归模型虽能基于临床历史预测离散事件序列，但多边际流匹配虽具连续时间优势，却仅用多个观测监督训练路径，未让学习到的动力学获得既往病史信息，因此需要一种兼顾连续时间建模与病史条件的方法。

Method: 提出 EHRFlow 多边际流匹配框架，以编码后的患者历史作为条件，使未来动力学依赖患者先前的临床轨迹；框架可容纳不规则的观测时间，并支持任意时间跨度的预测。

Result: 在受控合成基准上，EHRFlow 提升了临床代码预测与潜在状态恢复表现；在包含逾百万患者、并含独立外部验证队列的真实临床数据集上，其跨跨度平均 top-5 临床代码准确率优于自回归与不依赖历史的流匹配基线；在受控反事实模拟中，条件引导近似再现了抗高血压干预的已知效应，且无需训练任务专属结局模型。

Conclusion: EHRFlow 通过将病史条件引入多边际流匹配，实现了对不规则、连续演化患者状态的有效建模与多跨度预测，并在真实数据与反事实场景中展现优势。

Abstract: Electronic health records provide irregular observations of latent patient states that evolve continuously over time. Recent autoregressive models condition on clinical histories to forecast future events as sequences of discrete observations. Conversely, multi-marginal flow matching provides a continuous-time formulation, but using multiple observations to supervise training paths does not itself give the learned dynamics access to preceding patient history. We introduce EHRFlow, a multi-marginal flow-matching framework that conditions on encoded patient history, thereby allowing future dynamics to depend on the patient's prior clinical trajectory. Our proposed framework accommodates irregular observation times and supports forecasting at arbitrary horizons. Across controlled synthetic benchmarks, EHRFlow improves clinical-code forecasting and latent-state recovery. On real-world clinical datasets comprising more than one million patients, including an independent external validation cohort, EHRFlow improves horizon-averaged top-5 clinical-code accuracy over autoregressive and history-independent flow-matching baselines. Finally, in a controlled counterfactual simulation, conditional guidance approximates the known effect of an antihypertensive intervention without training a task-specific outcome model.

</details>


### [87] [Draft in Parallel, Condition Through Depth: Adjacent Causal Injection for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36173)
*Haohui Zhang,Keyu Chen,Haocheng Sun,Weibo Gu,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DSpine 通过在骨干网络每一层进行门控相邻注入，让因果条件信息随深度传递，从而提升投机解码的接受长度与吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行投机草稿虽能一次生成多个候选，但独立 token 选择会导致续写不一致，缩短被接受的前缀。现有方法多把条件解码放在骨干网络之后的轻量模块中，限制了前驱信息向后继的流动。对 DFlash 的分析表明，浅层早期位置已形成可恢复的预测，且准确的相邻前驱能更早地帮助后继。

Method: 提出 DSpine，在骨干网络全程注入因果条件：每层通过门控相邻注入，把每个前驱的预测特征写入其后继，使因果条件链随网络深度展开，同时所有位置并行更新。基于目标模型输出嵌入构建统一迁移空间，融合逐层注入与前驱条件解码；逐层输出嵌入监督促进浅层预测特征形成。并以融合内核与转移缓存，在 SGLang 中高效并行执行。

Result: 在七个数学、代码与对话基准上，DSpine 在 Qwen3-4B 与 Qwen3-8B 的两种温度下均取得最长接受长度。Qwen3-8B 温度为零时，七基准均值从 DFlash 的 3.77 提升至 4.82（+27.8%）；SGLang 服务测试中平均吞吐量比 DFlash 高 23.3%。

Conclusion: 通过将因果条件注入贯穿骨干网络深度，DSpine 有效缓解了并行起草中的续写不一致问题，显著提升接受长度与推理吞吐，验证了浅层预测特征与早期前驱信息的价值。

Abstract: Parallel speculative drafting generates multiple candidates in one backbone pass, but independent token selection can produce inconsistent continuations that shorten the accepted prefix. Existing methods mostly leave conditional decoding to a lightweight module after the backbone, which limits the flow of predecessor information to successors. Our analysis of DFlash shows that early positions already form recoverable predictions in shallow layers, and that accurate adjacent predecessors help successors more when they enter earlier. We therefore propose DSpine, a drafter with causal conditioning injection throughout the backbone: at every layer, gated adjacent injection writes each predecessor's predicted feature into its successor, so the causal conditioning chain unfolds over network depth while all positions update in parallel. A unified transfer space built from the target model's output embeddings unifies layer-wise injection with predecessor-conditioned decoding, and layer-wise output-embedding supervision promotes the formation of predicted features in shallow layers. Fused kernels and a transition cache execute both efficiently in parallel within SGLang. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DSpine achieves the longest acceptance length at both temperatures on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B. At temperature zero on Qwen3-8B, it raises the seven-benchmark mean from DFlash's 3.77 to 4.82 (+27.8%); in SGLang serving tests, it delivers 23.3% higher throughput than DFlash on average.

</details>


### [88] [Fair Policy Optimization in Major-Minor Weakly Coupled Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36174)
*Xiaohui Tu,Yossiri Adulyasak,Erick Delage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究主-次弱耦合马尔可夫决策过程（M2WCMDP）中的公平资源分配问题，将传统功利目标替换为一般类公平函数，证明对称情形可转化为功利目标优化，并提出基于计数比例的深度强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体序列决策环境中，资源约束会耦合主智能体与大量次智能体的动作空间。传统方法通常采用总和的功利主义目标，忽视了资源分配的公平性，因此需要在保持可扩展性的同时实现公平感知的优化。

Method: 针对同质次子MDP，证明在对称性下公平问题可归约为在置换不变策略类上优化“平台加平均参与者”的功利目标，从而复用高效功利算法。对更一般设置，提出基于计数比例的深度强化学习方法，并设计基于优先级的采样器以生成可行的计数动作。

Result: 在机器更换问题以及基于纽约市校准数据的定价与出租车调度联合控制问题上进行了实验，验证了理论结论，表明所提方法在实现强公平感知性能的同时具备良好可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架具有通用性，其算法与理论保证可迁移到任何具有对称M2WCMDP结构的领域，为公平感知的序列资源分配提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider fair resource allocation in sequential decision-making environments modeled as major-minor weakly coupled Markov decision processes (M2WCMDP). In this framework, resource constraints couple the action spaces of a major sub-Markov decision process (sub-MDP) and a population of minor sub-MDPs that would otherwise operate independently. Instead of using the traditional utilitarian (total-sum) objective, we optimize a general class of monotone, concave, permutation-invariant, normalized fairness functions. With homogeneous minor sub-MDPs, we prove that the problem under symmetry reduces to optimizing the platform-plus-mean-participant utilitarian objective over the class of \textit{permutation-invariant} policies, which allows us to exploit efficient algorithms that optimize the utilitarian-based objective to solve this fairness-aware problem. For more general settings, we introduce a count-proportion-based deep reinforcement learning approach with a priority-based sampler that generates feasible count actions. The generality of our framework means that the proposed algorithms and theoretical guarantees transfer to any domain with a symmetric M2WCMDP structure. We consider two applications: the machine replacement problem and the joint control of pricing and taxi relocation problem on a New York City-calibrated dataset. We validate our theoretical findings with comprehensive experiments, confirming the effectiveness of our proposed method in achieving strong fairness-aware performance while remaining scalable.

</details>


### [89] [EvoMO-SR: Multiobjective LLM-based Evolution of Symbolic Expressions with substructure guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36187)
*Cristina Rossetti,Anna V. Kononova,Thomas Bäck,Fei Liu,Niki Van Stein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoMO-SR，一种由LLM驱动的符号回归框架。LLM负责生成方程骨架，系数由外部优化器单独拟合，并结合多目标生存选择与子结构引导机制，取得了优于传统方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归（SR）是一种数据驱动的科学发现方法，旨在寻找数据中可解释的解析关系；近期大语言模型（LLM）在科学发现自动化方面展现出巨大潜力，因此利用LLM嵌入的科学知识与编程能力来解决SR任务成为可能，且相较传统方法已有初步成效。本文即在此背景下提出新框架以进一步提升SR性能。

Method: 提出EvoMO-SR框架：由LLM生成方程骨架，其系数交由外部优化器单独拟合；框架包含多目标生存选择机制，通过平衡精度与复杂度来控制表达式膨胀（bloating），并引入子结构引导机制，利用候选可复用构建块对表达式进行变异。

Result: 在LSR-Synth数据集的八个域内与域外设置中，EvoMO-SR在七个设置上取得了最佳精度，且仅使用小型LLM模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）。此外，通过两种基于规范化子树重叠和项匹配的符号精度指标评估结构恢复能力，表明该方法更有可能恢复出高精度的符号结构。

Conclusion: EvoMO-SR证明了借助小型LLM即可在符号回归任务中实现领先的精度与结构恢复能力，多目标生存选择与子结构引导机制有效提升了框架性能，为LLM驱动的科学发现提供了新思路。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) is a data-driven method for scientific discovery which searches for interpretable analytical relationships within data. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have also had a significant impact on scientific discovery, enabling the automation of various stages of the process. For these reasons, the possibility of harnessing the embedded scientific knowledge and programming capabilities of LLMs to solve SR tasks has emerged, showing promising performance compared with traditional methods. We propose EvoMO-SR, a novel LLM-driven SR framework in which the LLM generates equation skeletons, with their coefficients fitted separately by an external optimizer. The framework includes a multi-objective survival selection which controls bloating by balancing accuracy and complexity, and a substructure guidance mechanism which mutates expressions with candidate reusable building blocks. EvoMO-SR achieves the best accuracy in seven of the eight in-domain and out-of-domain settings for LSR-Synth, using a small LLM model, i.e., Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. We also evaluated structural recovery through two symbolic accuracy metrics based on canonicalized subtree overlap and term matching, showing that our method has a greater probability of recovering highly accurate symbolic structures.

</details>


### [90] [Physical Cross-Modal Masked Autoencoding for Seismic-to-Well Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36193)
*Meher Gajula,Keyla Gonzalez,Ben Lasscock,Alejandro Valenciano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CM-MAE跨模态掩码自编码器，在地震数据与测井之间做表示学习，地震上下文对测井重建提升明显（9.73%），测井对地震仅0.65%，盐体识别AUROC达0.910。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学测量中不同模态常具有不同空间支撑与分辨率，地下表征是典型的稠密-稀疏问题：3D地震提供体积但间接的测量，测井仅在少量井位提供高分辨率1D信息。需要一种能跨模态对齐并联合学习的表示方法。

Method: 提出物理约束的跨模态掩码自编码器（CM-MAE）。联合对地震体与测井深度补丁进行tokenize，用四轴旋转位置编码将两模态嵌入连续物理坐标，并以跨模态解码器重建被掩码目标。采用稀疏MoE层提供模态专属容量，密集注意力促进模态间信息交换。预训练使用23个地震体（约17.8万平方公里，涵盖美国海上与陆上盆地）及约9.2万口井。

Result: 匹配掩码消融显示信息流强烈不对称：地震上下文使留出测井重建提升9.73%，测井上下文使地震重建提升0.65%。以独立解释员绘制的盐体掩码评估仅地震的伪测井预测，海上调查中由纵波慢度导出的盐分数AUROC达0.910；陆上盆地中预测纵波慢度保留地层尺度结构及部分相对组织性，但存在标定漂移。

Conclusion: CM-MAE在极端模态不对称下能学到有用的地震-测井表示，但伪测井的绝对标定仍依赖具体调查区域。

Abstract: Learning from scientific measurements often requires aligning modalities with different spatial support and resolution. Subsurface characterization is an extreme dense-sparse problem in which 3D seismic provides volumetric but indirect measurements and well logs provide high-resolution 1D measurements at sparse borehole locations. We introduce a physically grounded cross-modal masked autoencoder (CM-MAE) for seismic-to-well representation learning. The model jointly tokenizes seismic volumes and well-log depth patches, embeds both modalities in continuous physical coordinates using four-axis rotary position embeddings, and reconstructs masked targets with a cross-modal decoder. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts layers provide modality-specific capacity while dense attention allows information exchange between modalities. Pretraining uses 23 seismic volumes covering approximately 178,000 square km across U.S. offshore and onshore basins, together with approximately 92,000 wells. Matched-mask ablations show strongly asymmetric information flow. Seismic context improves held-out well-log reconstruction by 9.73%, while well-log context improves seismic reconstruction by 0.65%. We evaluate seismic-only pseudo-log predictions against independent interpreter-drawn salt-geobody masks to determine whether they contain geologic signal. Across offshore U.S. surveys, compressional-slowness-derived salt scores reach an AUROC of 0.910. In onshore basins, predicted compressional slowness preserves formation-scale structure and partial relative organization in an unseen survey despite calibration drift. CM-MAE learns useful seismic-to-well representations under extreme modality asymmetry, although absolute pseudo-log calibration remains survey-dependent.

</details>


### [91] [Representable but Unlearned: Encoding Rank and the Interaction-Prediction Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36208)
*Zahra Khodagholi,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需标签、损失或拟合模型的方法，从编码器的等价类和固定对比设计计算可达对比空间，为任意无限制解码器给出经验误差下限，用于区分"编码允许什么"与"模型实际学到什么"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入编码本身可能限制预测器能够联合复现的测量对比，即使没有任何单个对比被强制为零。作者希望将"编码所能表达的内容"与"拟合模型实际达到的性能"分离，从而量化编码带来的误差下限。

Method: 从编码器的等价类与固定对比设计出发计算可达对比空间，不使用标签、损失或拟合模型；将记录的对比投影到该空间得到经验误差下限。在140个矩形的siRNA相互作用面板上，用图神经网络的仅训练特征掩码分析端点状态合并与对比秩的下降，并进行恢复化学列、去掉掩码重拟合等对照实验；另在已发布的RNA剪接预测器上做同样的秩检验。

Result: siRNA面板中，掩码将165个端点状态合并为90类，并把140个相互作用对比的秩降到72，得到下限0.009980，占拟合模型相互作用平方误差的14.6%；拟合模型达到0.068335，略差于完全不预测相互作用的对照。恢复至少三列化学特征可恢复满秩。去掉掩码重拟合可消除下限，但相互作用MSE仅改善0.000017，且恢复的列在各训练输入中仍缺失。在编码为单射的RNA剪接预测器上，同一计算给出完整设计秩1,986和恰好为零的下限。

Conclusion: 这些结果区分了编码所允许的范围与拟合模型实际达到的效果，但并未识别限制剩余误差的因素。该秩检验无需拟合，即可界定在固定编码下任何训练量所能恢复的上限。项目仓库见 https://github.com/shadi97kh/REPRESENTABLE-BUT-UNLEARNED。

Abstract: Input encodings can restrict which measured contrasts a predictor can jointly reproduce, even when no single contrast is forced to vanish. We compute the attainable contrast space from an encoder's equivalence classes and a fixed contrast design, without labels, loss, or a fitted model; projecting the recorded contrasts onto that space gives an empirical error floor for any unrestricted decoder on those classes. On a 140-rectangle siRNA interaction panel, a graph neural network's training-only feature mask merges 165 endpoint states into 90 classes and cuts the rank of the 140 interaction contrasts to 72. The resulting floor is 0.009980, which is 14.6% of the fitted model's interaction squared error; the fitted model reaches 0.068335, slightly worse than a control predicting no interaction at all. A minimum of three restored chemistry columns recovers full rank. Refitting without the mask removes the floor entirely, yet interaction MSE improves by only 0.000017 under the reported protocol, and the restored columns remain absent from every training input. On a released RNA-splicing predictor, whose encoding is injective on the measured states, the same computation returns the full design rank of 1,986 and a floor of exactly zero. These results separate what an encoding permits from what a fitted model achieves; they do not identify what limits the remaining error. The rank check needs no fits and bounds what any amount of training under a fixed encoding can recover. The project repository is available at https://github.com/shadi97kh/REPRESENTABLE-BUT-UNLEARNED.

</details>


### [92] [On the spectral properties of generative denoiser Jacobians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36210)
*Alexandros Graikos,Nebojsa Jojic,Dimitris Samaras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以去噪器雅可比谱作为分析生成式去噪模型差异的工具，并基于此设计正则化方案，通过扰动输入调控雅可比谱，在ImageNet上验证可改进生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散与流匹配等生成式去噪模型多通过合成样本质量进行比较，这类指标难以揭示驱动生成过程的去噪器本身的差异，因此需要一种能刻画模型内在差异的分析工具。

Method: 提出分析去噪器雅可比矩阵的谱特征来刻画模型差异；受"更好生成性能伴随更大雅可比特征值"这一观察启发，引入一种正则化方案，通过在扰动输入上训练去噪器（扰动用于抑制或放大雅可比响应）来调控雅可比谱，并在ImageNet上检验直接修改雅可比谱性质能否改善生成。

Result: 在预训练去噪模型上观察到更好的生成性能与更大的雅可比特征值相关；在ImageNet实验中，去噪器同时受益于增强数据相关主特征方向的响应以及抑制噪声性、数据无关方向的响应。

Conclusion: 去噪器雅可比可作为识别生成式去噪模型差异的有效工具，直接调控其谱性质有助于改进生成。

Abstract: Generative denoising models, such as diffusion and flow-matching, learn to sample from complex distributions by training a deep neural network denoiser to recover clean data from noise-corrupted samples. While such models are typically compared on the quality of their synthesized samples, these metrics provide limited insight into how the underlying denoiser, which drives generation, differs. In this work, we propose to analyze the spectrum of the denoiser Jacobian as a tool to characterize these differences. Across pre-trained denoising models, we observe that better generative performance is associated with larger Jacobian eigenvalues. Motivated by this, we introduce a regularization scheme that controls the Jacobian spectrum by training the denoiser on perturbed inputs, with perturbations suppressing or amplifying Jacobian responses. On ImageNet, we test whether directly modifying the Jacobian spectral properties leads to improved generations. Our findings suggest that denoisers benefit from both strengthening responses along data-relevant principal eigen-directions and suppressing the noisy, data-irrelevant ones. This establishes the denoiser Jacobian as a useful tool for identifying differences between generative denoising models.

</details>


### [93] [EnergyEminence: Source-Aware Environmental Calibration and Evaluation in a Physics-Grounded Grid Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36215)
*Huy Trinh,Michael Mai,Yu Nong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出早期阶段的 EnergyEminence 测试床，耦合图-时序预测、非线性 AC 级联仿真与类操作员仪表盘的时间回放，并引入有界校准将野火检测置信度与空间范围转化为可比较证据；通过视频源不交叉的测试揭示环境捷径学习，强调环境告警与电气级联推断应分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网数字孪生需要同时具备数据驱动预测与物理意义的状态演化，并保留环境观测的来源可追溯性。现有评估常因场景级随机划分而掩盖环境捷径学习，使多模态电网韧性模型的真实泛化能力被高估。

Method: 构建 EnergyEminence 测试床：将 IEEE 118 节点式图-时序预测器、非线性 AC 级联仿真与类似操作员仪表盘的时间回放相耦合；引入共享的有界校准，把野火检测置信度与空间范围转换为来源可比较的、可解释的证据；用视觉多样化的火情视频与难负样本视频评估；整理 16 段合成环境视频生成 160 个带来源追踪的电网场景，并采用源视频不交叉的测试划分。

Result: 源视频不交叉测试得到 10 个真阳性、8 个假阳性、22 个真阴性和 0 个假阴性。错误集中出现在受压但未级联的场景中，且条件为未见过的增长型火源。诊断由此暴露出被场景级随机划分掩盖的环境捷径学习现象。

Conclusion: 论文贡献了一套以数据为中心、可检查的多模态电网韧性模型评估工作流，并提供支持将环境告警与电气级联推断相分离的证据。

Abstract: Power-grid digital twins must combine data-driven prediction with physically meaningful state evolution while preserving the provenance of environmental observations. This paper presents an early-stage EnergyEminence testbed that couples an IEEE 118-bus-style graph-temporal predictor, nonlinear AC cascade simulation, and operator-dashboard-like temporal replay. In addition, we introduce a shared bounded calibration that converts wildfire-detection confidence and spatial extent into source-comparable wildfire interpretable and explainable evidence. We then evaluate it with visually diverse fire and hard-negative videos. Sixteen synthetic environmental videos are curated to generate 160 source-tracked grid scenarios, and a source-video-disjoint test yields 10 true positives, 8 false positives, 22 true negatives, and no false negatives. The errors occur in stressed, non-cascading scenarios conditioned on an unseen growing-fire source. Our diagnostic then reveals environmental shortcut learning that is obscured by scenario-level random splitting. The paper therefore contributes a data-centric and inspectable evaluation workflow for multimodal grid-resilience models, together with evidence supporting separation of environmental alerting from electrical cascade inference

</details>


### [94] [Preconditioned Physics-Informed Neural Operator Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36216)
*Shizheng Wen,Siddhartha Mishra,Marius Zeinhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于预条件残差损失的物理信息神经算子训练方法，利用多重网格实现与网格无关的条件数，在多个方程上无需标签即可匹配监督训练，精度比已有物理信息算子学习方法高4–25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子通常依赖经典求解器生成数据进行监督训练，离线成本高；物理信息训练可去除该成本并在线采样，但受限于简化问题且精度不及监督训练，其核心障碍是微分算子导致的物理信息损失病态问题，且随离散加密而加剧。

Method: 提出预条件残差损失函数，通过几何与代数多重网格实现该构造；适用于线性与非线性、稳态与时间依赖、结构与非结构网格，且与神经算子架构无关，推理阶段无额外开销。

Result: 对椭圆问题实现网格无关的条件数，对鞍点问题的条件数显著改善；在Poisson、Allen-Cahn与定常Stokes方程上，无标签训练达到与监督训练相当的效果，并比先前物理信息算子学习方法精度高4至25倍。

Conclusion: 预条件残差损失有效缓解物理信息算子学习的病态问题，使无标签训练在精度上可与监督训练媲美，为物理信息神经算子提供了通用且高效的训练途径。

Abstract: Neural operators are typically trained in a supervised fashion, which requires a dataset to be generated with a classical solver. Training them physics-informed, i.e., purely from the governing equations, removes this large offline cost and allows fresh samples to be drawn at every optimization step, but has so far been limited to simplified problems and trails supervised training in accuracy. The obstacle is the ill-conditioning of physics-informed losses, which differential operators induce and which worsens as the discretization is refined. We therefore propose a preconditioned residual loss function and show mesh-independent conditioning for elliptic problems and greatly improved conditioning for saddle point problems. Realized through geometric and algebraic multigrid, the construction applies to linear and nonlinear equations, steady or time-dependent, on structured and unstructured meshes, is agnostic to the neural operator architecture, and adds no cost at inference. On the Poisson, Allen-Cahn and stationary Stokes equations, the resulting label-free training matches supervised training and is four to twenty-five times more accurate than previous physics-informed operator learning methods.

</details>


### [95] [How Language Models Differ in Redistributing Attention-Head Activity Under Serial Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36221)
*Johnny Jingze Li,Abdulla Kuleib,Kalyan Basu,Gabriel A. Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型在不同串行步骤需求任务下，各层注意力头活动是集中于少数头还是分散到多个头，发现不同模型存在可复现的层间路由模式差异，并提出以"集中/分散"作为比较模型的新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型如何随深度路由信息是机制性解释需要回答的问题，而任务需求变化时这一路由方式如何改变尚不清楚。作者希望找到一种粗略但有效的观察视角，即每层注意力头活动的分布（集中还是分散），并用它来刻画和比较不同模型的信息路由方式。

Method: 固定提示长度，仅改变任务的串行步骤需求；在17个开放权重模型的每一层中，测量随需求上升，注意力头活动是集中于少数头还是分散到多个头。并进行消融实验，检验活动的集中程度与模型对顶部注意力头依赖程度之间的关系，同时考察后训练模型是否保留基座模型的模式。

Result: 两种现象同时存在：多数模型中，接近中深度的前几层倾向于集中活动，而后面的层倾向于分散活动。不同模型在发生位置与强度上有差异。例如Qwen2.5基座模型（0.5B至7B）在每个任务中都比平均模型更分散，并在后半部分某些层集中活动，而Llama（1B至8B）和OLMo-2则在该处分散；这一对比在层数与头数相同的Llama-3.1-70B与Qwen2.5-72B之间也大体成立。差异可复现，后训练模型保留基座模型的大部分模式。消融表明，同一任务内活动更集中于顶部头的模型，也更依赖这些头来给出答案。

Conclusion: 跨层的集中与分散为比较模型提供了一种新方式，即通过模型如何沿深度路由信息来区分不同模型。代码已开源。

Abstract: The way a model distributes activity over each layer's attention heads offers a coarse view of how it routes information through depth; how this changes with the task is part of what a mechanistic account must explain. Holding prompt length fixed, we vary how many serial steps a task demands and measure, in every layer of 17 open-weight models, whether activity concentrates on a few heads or spreads across many as demand rises. Both occur: in most models, layers just before mid-depth concentrate activity and later layers spread it. Models differ in where and how strongly this happens. The Qwen2.5 base models from 0.5B to 7B, for example, spread less than the average model in every task and concentrate activity in parts of their second half, where Llama models from 1B to 8B and OLMo-2 spread; the contrast largely holds between Llama-3.1-70B and Qwen2.5-72B, which have the same number of layers and heads. These differences are reproducible, and post-trained models keep much of their base model's pattern. An ablation study suggests that, within a task, models whose activity is more concentrated on their top heads also depend more on those heads for the answer. Concentration and spreading across layers thus offer a new way to compare models, by how they route information through depth. Code is available at https://github.com/johnnyjli/serial-demand-heads.

</details>


### [96] [BASE: Batch-Aware Selection of Experts Using Predicted Removal Error for Efficient MoE Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36222)
*Ali Abbasi,Justin Shi,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出BASE，通过按“移除某专家对MoE层输出的影响”对专家排序来减少服务时的专家权重传输，在三个MoE架构上无需重训练即改善质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MoE服务中，自回归解码常受限于从HBM向片上SRAM传输专家权重；批量解码时不同请求激活不同专家，导致合起来的活跃专家集合远超稀疏假设，传输开销大。现有专家裁剪方法多为逐token独立决策，未解决批级专家集合膨胀；较新的批感知方法虽协调并发请求的专家使用，但选择标准基于路由器排名或校准统计，与“丢弃专家造成的输出误差”无直接关联，也无法反映推理时专家贡献随token变化。

Method: BASE以“移除某专家会导致MoE层输出变化多少”为准则对专家排序；为在服务中应用该准则，在校准阶段训练一个轻量线性预测器，用于估计每个到达token的专家移除代价；并为代价预测和专家选择开发了定制GPU kernel。

Result: 在三种MoE架构上，BASE无需重训练即可改善质量-效率权衡。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，当专家预算紧张且吞吐相近时，平均准确率比最强基线高29.5个百分点；在更宽松的专家预算下，比稠密推理快60%，准确率差距在0.4个百分点以内。

Conclusion: BASE通过将专家选择与输出误差直接挂钩，并借助轻量预测器与定制kernel实现服务时在线应用，能有效缓解MoE批量解码中的权重传输瓶颈，在无需重训练的情况下取得显著的质量-效率提升。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly expensive to serve. In large-scale serving systems, autoregressive decoding is often bottlenecked by transferring model weights from accelerator high-bandwidth memory into on-chip SRAM. Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models reduce computation by activating only a small subset of experts per token, but this sparsity does not translate directly to batched decoding. Different requests select different experts; therefore, the combined active set across many concurrent requests can span a substantial fraction of the expert pool and require significantly more expert weights to be transferred. Most expert-reduction techniques make retention decisions independently for each token and therefore do not address this batch-level expansion. More recently, batch-aware methods have attempted to coordinate expert use across concurrent requests and reuse experts already fetched for the batch. Yet their selection criteria are based primarily on router rankings or expert statistics collected during calibration. Consequently, these criteria are not directly tied to the output error caused by dropping an expert, nor do they capture how an expert's contribution changes across tokens at inference time. We instead rank experts according to how much their removal would change the MoE-layer output. To apply this criterion during serving, we train a lightweight linear predictor during calibration that estimates the expert removal cost for each incoming token, and develop custom GPU kernels for cost prediction and expert selection. Across three MoE architectures, BASE improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff without retraining. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, it improves average accuracy by 29.5 points over the strongest baseline at comparable throughput under a tight expert budget. At a higher expert budget, it is 60% faster than dense inference while remaining within 0.4 accuracy points.

</details>


### [97] [The Role of Feed-Forward Layers in Transformer Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36230)
*Thomas Jacob Maranzatto,Semih Akkoc,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从控制论视角研究Transformer中token的动力学行为，将自注意力机制视为交互粒子系统、前馈层视为独立控制，理论上证明前馈网络能将token任意逼近共识（consensus），并推广到多聚类与多头注意力，数值实验验证了理论并与真实LLM的阈值行为比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer内部token表示如何随层演化、趋于一致或分化，是解释大模型行为的重要问题。作者希望用控制论框架定量刻画自注意力与前馈层对token动力学的作用，从而给出可证明的收敛性结论并连接实际LLM现象。

Method: 建立控制论模型：将自注意力机制建模为交互粒子系统（interacting particle system），将自注意力之后的前馈层视为对每个token的独立控制输入。通过控制论分析推导前馈层对token轨迹的驱动作用，并将结果推广至多聚类收敛与多头注意力情形；同时进行数值实验，并与真实LLM中的阈值行为进行对比。

Result: 主要理论结果证明：无论key、query、value矩阵如何取值，前馈网络都能将token驱动到任意接近共识的状态。该结论可容易地扩展到多聚类收敛及多头注意力设置。数值实验验证了理论预测，且理论给出的阈值行为与真实LLM的观察相一致。

Conclusion: 前馈层在Transformer中扮演类似控制器的角色，足以克服自注意力带来的任意参数影响并实现token共识或聚类。该控制论视角为解释Transformer的表示收敛与分化提供了理论工具，并与实际LLM行为对应。

Abstract: We study the dynamical behavior of tokens in transformers from a control-theoretic perspective. Our model includes the feed-forward layer present after the self-attention mechanism, with the self-attention mechanism interpreted as an interacting particle system and the feed-forward layer as an independent control. Our main theoretical result establishes that the feed-forward network can steer the tokens arbitrarily close to consensus regardless of the key, query, and value matrices. Our result are easily extended to convergence to many clusters and to multi-head attention. We conduct numerical experiments to verify our results, and compare thresholding behavior from our theory to real-world LLMs.

</details>


### [98] [Transversal Pooling Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36237)
*Emily J. King,Dustin G. Mixon,Michael Perlmutter,Lander Ver Hoef*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出横向池化神经网络（transversal pooling neural networks），将空间最大池化推广到仿射群作用，建立对所选子群的等变性，并推导了在输入仿射扰动下各池化小波系数的显式稳定性界；实验表明该网络在低数据环境和热带气旋强度预测中具有实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学习任务需要模型对微小变换保持稳定，同时对较大变换保持敏感性。传统空间最大池化难以直接处理仿射群作用下的这种稳定性与敏感性的平衡问题。

Method: 提出横向池化神经网络，将空间最大池化推广到仿射群作用；建立网络对所选子群的等变性，并推导在输入发生仿射扰动时单个池化小波系数的显式稳定性界。

Result: 实验验证了该网络在低数据环境下的有效性，并在热带气旋强度增强预测任务中展示了其实用价值。

Conclusion: 横向池化神经网络为处理仿射变换下的稳定性与敏感性提供了一种理论上有保障、实验上有效的池化框架，适用于数据稀缺场景及特定科学预测任务。

Abstract: Many learning tasks require stability to small transformations while retaining sensitivity to larger ones. We introduce \emph{transversal pooling neural networks}, which generalize spatial max pooling to affine group actions. We establish equivariance to a chosen subgroup and derive explicit stability bounds for individual pooled wavelet coefficients under affine perturbations of the input. Experimentally, we demonstrate the utility of our networks in low-data environments and for predicting tropical cyclone intensification.

</details>


### [99] [Can Representation Learning Decouple from Loss Minimization? Polar Updates Have an Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36240)
*Akash Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究训练损失不再改善时表征学习是否停止，聚焦于Muon优化器。发现在损失平台期（period-2振荡）权重仍持续移动，学习到的特征继续与教师子空间对齐，AGOP可在损失下降前精确恢复教师子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往常用训练损失是否改善来判断表征学习是否停止，但Muon的极坐标归一化更新步长由梯度秩而非范数决定，其动力学可能使损失平台期仍存在持续的表征学习。本文旨在探究损失停滞是否等价于表征学习停滞。

Method: 针对矩阵Muon（polar-normalised updates），研究teacher-student问题在稳定边缘附近的full-batch动力学。对线性teacher-student玩具模型推导显式周期与对齐公式及条件性的平台与衰减界；对population mean-field ReLU模型证明在给定维度、初始化和small-head条件下，AGOP的主特征空间在损失平台期精确恢复教师子空间。实验覆盖33种ReLU、GELU、SiLU教师配置，并考察深度残差ReLU学生中冻结下游层的效果。

Result: Muon在稳定边缘附近进入约period-2的损失振荡并持续数千步，周期平均损失平坦或上升，但权重持续移动、特征持续与教师子空间对齐。所有33种配置中方向对齐指标显示AGOP对齐或仍在对齐教师子空间；head refitting表明学习到的方向对预测有用。深度残差学生中冻结下游层可重现几乎平坦的周期平均损失与改善的input-AGOP对齐，冻结/解冻在平台与损失下降间切换，且效果对动量和正交化器选择敏感。

Conclusion: 损失停滞并不等于表征学习停止：在Muon的period-2振荡平台期，权重仍在运动且特征继续向教师子空间对齐，AGOP可在损失真正下降前恢复教师子空间。这说明用损失曲线判断表征学习进程可能具有误导性，应结合方向性对齐指标等来评估。

Abstract: Does representation learning stop when the training loss stops improving? We study this question for matrix Muon, whose polar-normalised updates have a step length set by the gradient's rank rather than its norm. Near the edge of stability, full-batch Muon on teacher-student problems enters approximately period-2 loss oscillations that persist for thousands of steps: the cycle-mean loss stays flat or rises, yet the weights keep moving and the learned features continue to align with the teacher subspace. For linear teacher-student learning toys, we derive explicit cycle and alignment formulas and conditional plateau and decay bounds. For a population mean-field ReLU model, we prove that, under stated dimension, initialisation and small-head conditions, the leading eigenspace of the average gradient outer product (AGOP) recovers the teacher subspace exactly during a loss plateau, before the loss later drops. In all 33 ReLU, GELU and SiLU teacher configurations we study, direction-only alignment metrics show the student AGOP aligned with, or still aligning to, the teacher subspace during the period-2 oscillations; projected head refitting on selected configurations shows that the learned directions are useful for prediction, and further measurements distinguish AGOP alignment from weight-mass concentration. In deep residual ReLU students, freezing the downstream layers while the first layer trains with full-batch exact polar updates recreates a nearly flat cycle-mean loss with improving input-AGOP alignment; freezing and unfreezing switch between this plateau and loss decrease, and the effect is sensitive to momentum and to the choice of orthogonaliser.

</details>


### [100] [Action Chunking Proximal Policy Optimization with Feedback Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36250)
*Sanghyun Hahn,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ACPPO及ACPPO-Corr，用分块actor+标准状态价值critic避免chunk级Q函数，并加入逐步闭环校正，在25个仿真机器人任务上取得最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维机器人控制中，动作分块虽提供时间抽象，但现有方法存在两大局限：一是依赖动作块上的价值函数，随动作维度与块长增加难以学习；二是开环执行整块动作缺乏块内反馈，在接触密集任务中反应性不足。

Method: 提出ACPPO：PPO扩展，使用分块actor但保留标准状态价值critic，从而避免chunk级Q函数。进一步提出ACPPO-Corr：在块规划器上增加逐步反馈校正器，在每块内在线调整计划动作。在IsaacGym与Bi-DexHands的25个仿真机器人任务上评估，涵盖运动、机械臂操作与灵巧手物交互。

Result: ACPPO-Corr在评估方法中综合性能最强，并在决策频率敏感与决策频率中性两个任务子集上均表现最佳。消融实验表明中等块长效果最好，校正器正则在平衡块级规划与局部反馈中很重要。

Conclusion: 当块级规划与闭环校正结合时，动作分块在在线PPO中可有效应用。代码已开源。

Abstract: Action chunking provides temporal abstraction in reinforcement learning by selecting short action sequences instead of individual actions, but many existing approaches face two limitations in high-dimensional robotic control. First, many rely on value functions over action chunks, which can be difficult to learn as action dimensionality and chunk length grow. Second, executing chunks open-loop removes within-chunk feedback, limiting reactivity in contact-rich tasks. We present Action Chunking PPO (ACPPO), a PPO extension that uses a chunked actor while retaining a standard state-value critic, thereby avoiding chunked Q-functions. We further propose ACPPO-Corr, which augments the chunk planner with a stepwise feedback corrector that adjusts planned actions online within each chunk. Across 25 simulated robotics tasks from IsaacGym and Bi-DexHands, spanning locomotion, arm manipulation, and dexterous hand-object interaction, ACPPO-Corr achieves the strongest aggregate performance among evaluated methods and performs best on both decision-frequency-sensitive and decision-frequency-neutral task subsets. Ablations show that moderate chunk lengths work best and that corrector regularization is important for balancing chunk-level planning with local feedback. These results suggest that action chunking can be effective in online PPO when chunk-level planning is paired with closed-loop correction. The code is available at: https://github.com/hshhahn/ACPPO.

</details>


### [101] [The Signed Geometry of One-Shot Recourse: On-Path Validity and the Signed-Curvature Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36252)
*Hazar Yueksel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究闭合形式追索(closed-form recourse)的一步成败条件：当路径曲率κ非负时，一步按承诺距离d_p沿梯度方向移动大概率落在有利一侧；并提出基于单次查询f的极小极大最优规则及非对称曲率惩罚训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭合形式追索承诺将用户沿单位梯度移动距离d_p=|f(x)|/||∇f(x)||即达线性化零点，但该一步是否真正成功、失败条件及额外模型查询能否改善尚不清楚，作者希望给出理论刻画与实用规则。

Method: 在线性化+曲率展开下推导成功条件(κ≥0)；对80个浅层模型统计成功比例与κ≥0比例的相关性；证明仅用分数值与梯度的规则无法对所有曲率有界(K)函数有效(过冲量级Kd_p²/||∇f(x)||)；在曲率Lipschitz且步长较短时，构造单次查询规则并用split-conformal校准达到1-δ的首次跨越或弃权；用非对称曲率惩罚训练模型。

Result: 80个模型中成功比例与κ≥0比例相关系数r=0.985，但Fashion-MNIST上前者平均低8.2个百分点；非对称惩罚使99-100%路径在承诺步内跨越，代价是过冲约为对称惩罚的4-22倍(Fashion-MNIST、COMPAS)；匹配有效性下小规模审计未发现对调优膨胀法的统一优势。

Conclusion: 一步成功由路径曲率符号主导；单查询规则在相应假设下达到极小极大最优并可校准保证；但κ与d_p依赖分数尺度，部分收益可能仅来自更长的承诺步。当可承担逐用户射线搜索时，其精确到网格分辨率，优于闭合形式方案。

Abstract: Closed-form recourse moves a rejected user along the unit gradient $\hat g$ of the classifier score $f$ by the promised distance $d_p=|f(x)|/\|\nabla f(x)\|$, at which the linearized score reaches zero. We ask when this one-shot step succeeds and what additional model queries change. To leading order the step ends on the favorable side exactly when the path curvature $κ=\hat g^\top\nabla^2 f(x)\,\hat g$ is nonnegative. Across 80 shallow models, the fraction of rejected users whose step ends there and the fraction with $κ\ge0$ correlate at $r=0.985$, although on Fashion-MNIST the first falls below the second by 8.2 points on average. No rule that uses only the score value and gradient can be valid for every score with path curvature bounded by $K$ without overshooting some by order $Kd_p^2/\|\nabla f(x)\|$. When the curvature is also Lipschitz and the step is short, one evaluation of $f$ at the promised point attains the minimax rate among deterministic one-query rules that know the curvature bound and its Lipschitz constant, and split-conformal calibration makes such a rule reach the first crossing or abstain with probability at least $1-δ$. Training with an asymmetric curvature penalty lets 99-100% of paths cross within the promised step on undershoot-prone shallow data, at about 4-22 times the overshoot of symmetric penalties (Fashion-MNIST, COMPAS). Because $κ$ and $d_p$ depend on how the score is scaled, part of this gain can be a longer promised step, and at matched validity a smaller audit of briefly trained models finds no uniform advantage over tuned inflation. Where a per-user line search along the ray is affordable, it is exact to grid resolution and preferable.

</details>


### [102] [CyFA: Linear Sequence Modeling with Relative-Time-Partitioned Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36259)
*Yixiao Chen,Shuojin Yang,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.LG
