TL;DR: CyFA 是一种带相对时间分片记忆的线性 RNN，通过循环流动注意力改善长程回忆能力，并支持高效分块训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性 RNN 虽具线性时间处理和常数内存解码优势，但其固定大小的循环状态需容纳所有历史键值关联；现有遗忘机制和 Delta Rule 更新仅能部分缓解干扰，早期关联仍难检索。

Method: 提出 CyFA（Cyclic Flow Attention）：在每步中由可学习时钟控制键状态与值状态上的循环传输，使当前键值对进入“零龄槽”，并将历史关联组织到不同相对时间槽中；进一步推导出到绝对时钟坐标的精确变换，将 CyFA 表示为两个标量衰减的线性注意力递归，从而实现高效分块训练。

Result: 在 400M–1.4B 预训练实验中，在相同循环状态规模下，CyFA 提升了回忆密集型任务的性能，同时保持有竞争力的语言建模能力和高计算效率。400M 规模下，CyFA 在 FDA 上以 42.60 超过 KDA 的 26.07，且前向和反向核心算子执行时间分别仅为 KDA 的 46.7% 和 48.3%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: CyFA 通过相对时间分片记忆与循环流动机制，在不增加状态规模的情况下显著提升长程关联检索能力，并兼顾语言建模性能与计算效率，为线性 RNN 的记忆组织提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Linear RNNs offer linear-time sequence processing and constant-memory decoding, but their fixed-size recurrent states must accommodate all past key--value associations. Existing forgetting mechanisms and Delta Rule updates reduce interference by selectively clearing or correcting the state, yet earlier associations can still become difficult to retrieve. We introduce CyFA (Cyclic Flow Attention), a Linear RNN with relative-time-partitioned memory. At each step, a learned clock controls the cyclic transport applied jointly to the key and value states before the current key--value pair enters the age-zero slot, thereby organizing stored associations across relative-time slots. We further derive an exact change to absolute-clock coordinates that expresses CyFA as two scalar-decay linear attention recurrences and enables efficient chunk-wise training. Across 400M--1.4B pretraining experiments with matched recurrent-state sizes, CyFA improves recall-intensive performance while maintaining competitive language modeling and high computational efficiency. At 400M, CyFA outperforms KDA on FDA (42.60 vs. 26.07) while requiring only 46.7% and 48.3% of KDA's forward and backward core-operator execution times, respectively. Our code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/Chyxx/CyclicFlowAttention}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [103] [Understanding LLM Parameter Update Sparsity through the Lens of Fisher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36262)
*Yufan Zhang,Sagnik Mukherjee,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从对角Fisher信息角度统一解释了LLM后训练中参数更新集中于少数坐标的稀疏性现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究在强化学习、同策略蒸馏和监督微调中均观察到后训练参数变化集中在少数坐标，跨范式重复出现暗示训练动力学存在共享结构，但目前缺乏统一的解释。

Method: 使用与具体奖励或教师信号无关的对角模型Fisher衡量输出分布对参数的敏感度；理论上推导小Fisher导致小期望梯度；在RL与OPD中实证检验，并分析同策略训练中低Fisher的机制。

Result: Fisher能识别梯度集中的位置；从初始Fisher选出的固定稀疏掩码可保留全量训练的大部分改进；高概率的下一token往往具有相似的参数敏感度，导致Fisher偏低。

Conclusion: 对角模型Fisher可作为统一视角，将更新稀疏性与LLM后训练中的同策略训练动力学联系起来。

Abstract: Recent studies have observed that parameter changes during language-model post-training can be concentrated in a small subset of coordinates. This phenomenon has been reported in reinforcement learning, on-policy distillation, and supervised fine-tuning on near-policy data. Its recurrence across different post-training paradigms suggests shared structure in training dynamics. In this paper, we examine this pattern through the diagonal model Fisher, which measures the sensitivity of the model's output distribution to individual parameters and is independent of any particular reward or teacher signal. Theoretically, we show that small diagonal Fisher leads to small expected gradients across a range of training objectives, providing a common explanation for sparse gradient updates. Empirically, we test this connection in RL and OPD. We find that Fisher identifies where gradients are concentrated, and fixed sparse masks selected from the initial Fisher retain a large proportion of the improvement from full training. Finally, we investigate the mechanisms underlying low Fisher in on-policy training. Our results show that high-probability next tokens tend to have similar parameter sensitivities, contributing to low Fisher. Together, these results establish the diagonal model Fisher as a unifying perspective linking update sparsity to on-policy training dynamics in LLM post-training.

</details>


### [104] [Paired Multimodal Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36263)
*Marcus Ma,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究固定数据预算下配对数据比例对多模态分类损失曲线的影响，发现配对比例通过任务中的信息协同（synergy）显著影响损失，并提出由四类信息通道（冗余、单模态独有、协同）组成的多模态缩放定律新族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态缩放定律多依赖对多模态项的经验拟合，且在固定数据预算下从不改变配对数据量，无法刻画配对比例对损失的影响。

Method: 在相同单模态总数据量下，改变配对数据数量，于三种环境中训练模型，并针对数据规模与配对预算进行实验扫描，分析损失曲线与任务信息协同的关系。

Result: 配对比例对损失影响显著，且影响程度与任务所含信息协同直接相关；仅配对数据能降低协同损失，未配对数据只能把冗余或单模态信息降至单模态下限；协同获取存在临界阈值，配对数据须超过阈值协同损失才下降。拟合测试误差为3.2%，优于已发表定律最佳配对扩展的10.4%。

Conclusion: 提出新的多模态缩放定律族：总数据可归因损失为四个幂律之和，对应冗余、每种模态独有通道、协同共四类信息通道；该定律理论上更合理，实验上跨环境有效，且比现有定律预测更准确。

Abstract: Existing multimodal scaling laws fit multimodality terms empirically after testing and never vary how much data is multimodally paired at fixed data budgets. We investigate how, under the same total data per modality, changing the number of paired data affects loss curves in multimodal classification tasks. We train models in three different environments and run experiment sweeps varying data sizes and pairing budget. Pairing ratios have a dramatic impact on loss and this impact is directly tied to how much information synergy the task contains. Only paired data is able to reduce synergistic loss, while unpaired data can reduce redundant or unimodal information up until unimodal floors. Unlike traditional scaling laws where loss drops immediately in power law decay, synergy acquisition is gated, requiring a critical threshold of paired data before synergistic loss falls at all. We introduce a new family of multimodal scaling laws where total data-attributable loss is the sum of four individual power laws corresponding to the four different information channels of redundancy, a unique channel per modality, and synergy, and show how this law is both more theoretically sound and empirically valid across our experiments. This law predicts multimodal loss in our experiments more accurately than existing laws, with 3.2% error on fit tests versus 10.4% error for the best pairing extension of published laws.

</details>


### [105] [In-Context Learning Amplifies a Latent Symbolic Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36265)
*Melissa Wessel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大模型从少量上下文示例中学习抽象规则的内部机制被揭示为一个三阶段符号推理回路（抽象、归纳、检索），且该回路在模型达到高准确率之前就已被检测到并发挥作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能从少量上下文示例中学习抽象规则，但其内部机制如何随示例积累而激活尚不清楚。本文旨在追踪该推理回路随示例数量的变化并理解其功能。

Method: 追踪三个模型家族中随示例数（shot）变化的三阶段符号推理回路（抽象、归纳、检索）；分析每头因果贡献、跨示例激活修补（activation patching）、以及函数向量（function vectors）的缩放与注入干预。

Result: 回路在模型达到高准确率之前即可被检测到并具功能性。每头因果贡献从1-shot到10-shot增长达8倍；跨示例激活修补使0-shot准确率从1%升至56%，1-shot从17%升至88%；0-shot注入函数向量可将准确率提升至86%，基本替代归纳阶段，但关键依赖下游完整的检索阶段。

Conclusion: 抽象规则遵循的基础设施在任何示例存在之前就已存在于模型权重中；上下文示例、函数向量及相关干预似乎都为同一潜在回路提供输入。

Abstract: Large language models can learn abstract rules from just a few in-context examples, but how their internal mechanisms activate as examples accumulate is not well understood. We trace a three-stage symbolic reasoning circuit (abstraction, induction, retrieval) across shot counts in three model families and find it is detectable and functional well before the model achieves high accuracy. Per-head causal contribution grows up to 8x from 1- to 10-shot, and cross-shot activation patching raises accuracy from 1% to 56% at 0-shot and 17% to 88% at 1-shot. Function vectors scaled and injected at 0-shot rescue accuracy up to 86%, largely substituting for the induction stage but depending critically on an intact downstream retrieval stage. The infrastructure for abstract rule-following is present in the weights before any demonstrations; in-context examples, function vectors, and related interventions appear to supply input to the same latent circuit.

</details>


### [106] [Stochastic Optimization Under Power-Law Spectra: Tight Bounds and Shuffling Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36271)
*Thomas Dybdahl Ahle,Yaroslav Bulatov,Christopher De Sa,Christopher Ré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将幂律谱理论从确定性梯度下降推广到随机梯度下降（SGD），并在各向同性高斯数据下精确分析数据洗牌策略，证明单次洗牌严格优于往返洗牌和IID采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典指数收敛界与实际观测到的幂律学习曲线存在冲突；已有工作通过数据上的幂律谱条件在确定性梯度下降中解决了该冲突，但尚未扩展到高维机器学习的随机情形。论文旨在填补抽象谱理论与实际随机训练选择之间的空白。

Method: 作者将幂律谱理论推广至随机梯度下降，证明相同的谱指数主导随机动力学；针对各向同性高斯数据这一基本情形，对数据洗牌进行精确分析，推导出精确常数以比较不同采样策略。

Result: 理论上证明了幂律谱指数同样支配SGD的随机动力学；在各向同性高斯数据下推导出精确常数，证明单次洗牌（Single Shuffle）严格优于往返洗牌（Flip-Flop）和IID采样。

Conclusion: 研究统一了数据几何如何驱动优化速度的图景，连接了抽象谱理论与实际随机训练策略的选择。

Abstract: Recent work has established that power-law spectral conditions on data enable tight convergence bounds for deterministic gradient descent, resolving the conflict between classical exponential bounds and observed power-law learning curves. In this work, we extend this result to the stochastic regime of high-dimensional machine learning. We provide two main contributions: (1) We generalize the power-law spectral theory to Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD), showing that the same spectral exponents govern stochastic dynamics; (2) For the fundamental case of isotropic Gaussian data, we provide a precise analysis of data shuffling, deriving exact constants that prove Single Shuffle is strictly superior to Flip-Flop and IID sampling. Our results bridge the gap between abstract spectral theory and practical stochastic training choices, offering a unified picture of how data geometry drives optimization speed.

</details>


### [107] [GNA: Granular Neighbor Assembly for Retrieval-Augmented Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36281)
*Vincent Uhse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出检索增强预测层GNA，在多变量时间序列中同时进行整窗与逐变量邻居组装，用学习门控融合检索未来、持久预测与骨干预测，在多数基准上取得更低MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定长度回看窗口的预测存在收益递减与成本上升问题；检索增强虽能提供相似历史情境的后续走势，但整窗检索会让所有变量共享同一历史时刻的延续，忽略了多变量序列中各变量最佳匹配时刻不同的特性。

Method: GNA在两种粒度上组装邻居：整段历史窗口（保持变量间一致性）和逐变量邻居（每个变量取自身最佳匹配历史的未来）。使用学习门控，按预测步与变量决定对检索未来与持久预测的信任度，并与骨干模型自身预测并存。候选来自以预测窗口未来为目标的嵌入表示，且严格因果：历史窗口仅在其未来被观测后才可使用。

Result: 在相同回看长度与相同检索超参下，GNA在96个数据集-预测步设置中85个优于两个Transformer骨干；在12个标准基准中8个取得最低MSE，其中10个在每个随机种子上都优于骨干。两种粒度都必要，查询不匹配的邻居比不使用邻居更差。检索在回看信息最少处帮助最大，门控将信任转向更远期的检索未来。失败情形出现在长预测步的小时级非平稳序列，其损失与检索未来的水平漂移一致。

Conclusion: 多变量预测的检索增强需按变量粒度匹配历史并因果使用邻居；双粒度组装加门控可稳定提升Transformer骨干预测性能，但需注意长时间跨度下非平稳序列中检索未来的漂移风险。

Abstract: Deep forecasters predict from a fixed-length lookback window, and lengthening it gives diminishing returns at a growing cost. Retrieval augmentation instead shows the model how similar past situations continued. Retrieving a whole past window gives every variate the continuation of the same past moment. In multivariate series, however, the best past match differs from variate to variate. We present GNA (Granular Neighbor Assembly), a retrieval layer for forecasting backbones that assembles neighbors at two granularities: whole past windows, which keep the variates coherent, and per-variate neighbors, in which each variate takes its future from its own best-matching past. A learned gate decides, per forecast step and variate, how much to trust these futures against a persistence forecast, next to the backbone's own forecast. Candidates come from an embedding trained to predict each window's future, and retrieval is strictly causal: a past window is used only once its future has been observed. With the same lookback for every model and the same retrieval constants for all datasets, GNA improves two Transformer backbones in 85 of 96 dataset-horizon settings, gives the lowest MSE on 8 of 12 standard benchmarks and beats its backbone in every seed on 10 of them. Both granularities are needed, and neighbors of mismatched queries are worse than none. Retrieval helps most where the lookback says least: the gate shifts trust to retrieved futures further ahead. Where it fails, on hourly non-stationary series at long horizons, the loss is consistent with a drifting level of the retrieved futures.

</details>


### [108] [Representational and Functional Robustness to Electrode Montages in EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36288)
*Jakob Steglich,Justus Meyer zu Bexten,Shakiba Moradi,Laure Ciernik,Simon M. Hofmann,Mina Jamshidi Idaji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对四个采用不同电极蒙太奇处理设计的EEG基础模型进行功能与表征双轴分析，发现蒙太奇鲁棒性是编码器与聚合读出方式的联合属性，仅输入兼容并不能证明鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型旨在跨数据集泛化，理想情况下学到的表征应对电极蒙太奇等数据集特定配置保持不变。然而，能够接受不同蒙太奇输入的模型并不保证其表征和预测在不同电极配置下（尤其是训练设定之外）保持稳定，因此需系统考察蒙太奇鲁棒性及其评估方式。

Method: 选取四个代表不同蒙太奇处理设计的EEG基础模型，在空间信息指导下的通道削减条件下，评估跨被试静息态睁眼/闭眼与被试内运动想象分类。功能鲁棒性通过线性探针跨通道数的泛化性测试，表征鲁棒性通过被试内相似性与被试间几何结构的保持程度评估。此外，对同一编码器的两种读出方式（解剖对齐区域池化 vs. 学习式全局读出）进行比较。

Result: 四个模型呈现不同的鲁棒性轮廓，且功能轴与表征轴彼此解耦：嵌入相似度的大幅变化未必伴随探针性能的相应下降，而稳定的嵌入仍可能丢失下游性能。两种读出的对比表明，聚合方式而非编码器本身决定功能鲁棒性——解剖对齐区域池化的退化小于学习式全局读出。

Conclusion: 蒙太奇鲁棒性是编码器与其聚合方式的联合属性，刻画它需要同时考察表征与功能两个维度；仅有输入兼容不构成对功能或表征鲁棒性的证据。

Abstract: EEG foundation models (EEG-FMs) are intended to generalize across different datasets by learning representations that, ideally, are invariant to dataset-specific EEG configurations such as electrode montages. However, EEG-FMs that accept different montages as input do not guarantee that representations and predictions remain stable across different electrode configurations, especially outside the training setting. In this work, we investigate the effects of different electrode montages through a joint functional and representational analysis of four EEG foundation models selected to span distinct montage-handling designs. We evaluate embeddings on cross-subject resting-state eyes-open/closed and within-subject motor-imagery classification under spatially informed channel reduction. Functional robustness is tested through the generalizability of linear probes across channel counts, while representational robustness is assessed through within-subject similarity and preservation of between-subject geometry. The four models show distinct robustness profiles, and the two axes dissociate: large changes in embedding similarity need not come with comparable probe degradation, and stable embeddings can still lose downstream performance. Comparing two readouts of the same encoder further shows that aggregation, not the encoder alone, determines functional robustness: pooling into anatomically aligned regions degrades less than a learned global readout, despite being montage-invariant by construction. Montage robustness is therefore a joint property of the encoder and its aggregation, and characterizing it requires both a representational and a functional axis. Input compatibility alone is evidence for neither.

</details>


### [109] [When Trees Are Not Enough: Learning Mixed-Topology Feature Graphs with Adaptive Graph Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36294)
*Xiaozuo Shen,Yifei Cai,Tian Tan,Rui Ning,Chunsheng Xin,Hongyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应图稀疏自编码器（AG-SAE），将特征的完整父集合视为原子结构假设，通过竞争零/单/多父解释来诱导混合拓扑结构，并将该结构作为可微损失引导SAE训练，实现词典与图的自洽循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化SAE只能施加单父树或森林结构，拓扑表达能力受限；而事后构建的图虽允许多父，却不参与特征学习、也无法保证关系恢复可靠。因此需要一种既能表达多父混合拓扑、又能作为训练信号指导特征学习的方法。

Method: 将每个特征的完整父集合作为原子结构假设，与空集、子集及替代解释竞争，筛选出联合必要的多父关系并排除冗余或虚假关系，同时验证子特征相对父特征的额外贡献。基于诱导拓扑定义可微结构损失指导SAE训练；利用拓扑引导的细化缓解特征吸收，并依据结构暴露的持续重构误差初始化新特征；最后从修订后的词典重新诱导全图，形成词典-图自洽循环。

Result: 在受控玩具模型中实现精确的混合拓扑恢复；在真实LLM激活上，关系可靠性与语义有效性优于结构化及事后基线方法；特征级因果干预效果强于传统SAE特征。

Conclusion: AG-SAE把恢复出的混合拓扑特征结构转化为无监督训练信号，既改进了词典质量，又实现了超越树结构限制的可靠特征组织，并在重构之外展现出更强的因果控制能力。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) expose interpretable features in large language model activations, yet existing structured SAEs impose single-parent trees or forests, while post-hoc graphs permit multiple parents but neither guide feature learning nor ensure reliable relation recovery. We introduce the Adaptive Graph Sparse Autoencoder (AG-SAE), a structure-guided training paradigm that treats each feature's complete parent set as an atomic structural hypothesis and lets evidence select zero, one, or multiple parents. By competing complete parent sets against null, subset, and alternative explanations, AG-SAE identifies jointly necessary multi-parent relations while rejecting redundant or spurious alternatives and verifying that each child contributes beyond its parents. The induced topology over SAE features then defines a differentiable structural loss that guides SAE training, while topology-guided refinement mitigates feature absorption and uses persistent reconstruction gaps exposed by the learned structure to initialize new features. The entire graph is then induced again from the revised dictionary by reassessing every feature's complete parent set, closing the dictionary-graph self-consistency cycle. Experiments demonstrate exact mixed-topology recovery in a controlled toy model, greater relational reliability and semantic validity than structured and post-hoc baselines on real LLM activations, and stronger feature-level causal interventions than conventional SAE features. AG-SAE thereby turns recovered mixed-topology feature structure into an unsupervised training signal that improves the dictionary, enables reliable feature organization beyond the topological limitations of trees, and exhibits stronger causal control beyond reconstruction.

</details>


### [110] [MoRE: Scaling mixture of experts with hardware-aware low-rank routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36301)
*Honam Wong,Surbhi Goel,Enric Boix-Adserà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoRE通过将MoE路由器权重矩阵进行低秩分解，将路由开销从Θ(Mh)降至O((h+M)r)，在保持路由表达力和负载均衡的同时，使模型能以相同的激活FLOPs容纳更多专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿MoE架构趋向于使用更多、更小的专家。在此情境下，标准线性路由器随专家数M增长，其每token开销Θ(Mh)会成为瓶颈，限制了专家数量的扩展效率。

Method: MoRE（Mixture of Rank-reduced-routed Experts）将路由器权重矩阵做秩为r的分解，把路由开销降为O((h+M)r)。理论上证明了在激活专家数固定时，对数级秩足以保证路由表达力（且在精度因子内是必要的），并证明对数秩在高斯记忆模型中可保持负载均衡。推理时设计了融合的Triton kernel以避免HBM上的昂贵内存操作。

Result: 在手机簿合成任务上训练显示低秩不损害记忆能力；在匹配激活FLOPs下可容纳约Θ(h/r)倍的专家；实证中MoRE在手机簿记忆任务和知识密集型Q&A基准上均有提升，同时推理能力保持不变。

Conclusion: 低秩路由分解是一种有效方法，可在不牺牲表达力、负载均衡与推理能力的前提下突破MoE路由器瓶颈，支持更多专家的高效扩展，并具备实际墙钟时间收益。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers are central to frontier language models, and recent architectures push toward more and smaller experts. In this regime, the standard linear router becomes a bottleneck: with $M$ experts and hidden dimension $h$, its per-token cost $Θ(Mh)$ dominates the MoE layer once $M$ is large. We introduce MoRE (Mixture of Rank-reduced-routed Experts), which factorizes the router weight matrix at rank $r$ and reduces the routing cost to $O((h + M)r)$. We prove that rank logarithmic in $M$ suffices for routing expressivity when the number of active experts is fixed, and is necessary up to precision factors. We also prove that logarithmic rank preserves load balance in a Gaussian memorization model, and training on a synthetic phonebook task shows that low rank does not hurt memorization. At matched active FLOPs, the factorization allows a factor of $Θ(h/r)$ more experts. To realize this gain in wall-clock time, we design a fused Triton kernel at inference that avoids expensive memory operations on HBM. Empirically, MoRE improves memorization on the phonebook task and performance on knowledge-intensive Q\&A benchmarks after pretraining, while matching reasoning ability. Code available at https://github.com/Matheart/MoRE_code.

</details>


### [111] [The Universal Classifier for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36302)
*Ben Finkelshtein,André Linhares,Petar Veličković,Bryan Perozzi,Mikhail Galkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出通用分类器（UC），通过将节点、边、图层面的预测统一为相似度最大化目标，构建可跨域迁移的图基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图机器学习缺乏统一的特征与结构表征，所谓图基础模型多局限于节点级预测或固定特征维度，无法成为任务无关的通用骨干。

Method: UC将异构特征与标签提升为三维潜在张量，把节点、边、图级任务统一重构为潜在空间中最大化相似度的分类目标，从而学习与输入模式无关的可迁移特征。

Result: 单个预训练模型可泛化到特征语义各异、未见过的图上的节点分类、节点回归和链接预测任务；实验显示在节点、链接和图级任务上均有较强零样本迁移性能。

Conclusion: UC支持任意特征与类别基数，为图学习提供了任务无关的统一骨干，展示了图基础模型跨任务与跨图泛化的潜力。

Abstract: While foundation models have revolutionized natural language processing and computer vision by leveraging universal vocabularies, Graph Machine Learning (GML) remains fractured due to the absence of a unified feature and structural representation across diverse domains. Existing works claiming to be Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) are typically restricted to node-level predictions or require fixed feature dimensions, failing to provide a truly task-agnostic backbone for the full spectrum of graph learning applications. In this paper, we introduce the Universal Classifier (UC), which supports arbitrary feature and class cardinalities, unifying node-, edge-, and graph-level objectives under a single similarity-based classification objective. The UC reformulates all node-, edge-, and graph-level prediction tasks as maximizing similarity in the latent space: by lifting heterogeneous features and labels into 3D latent tensors, the model learns transferable features independent of specific input schemas. This architecture allows a single pre-trained model to generalize to node classification, node regression, and link prediction across unseen graphs with varying feature semantics. Experiments show strong zero-shot transfer performance across node-, link-, and graph-level tasks.

</details>


### [112] [Cheap and Powerful Tests for Supervised Subspaces: Per-Component Inference for PLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36307)
*Paweł Lenartowicz,Hubert Plisiecki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为PLS回归(及supervised PCA、线性探针)的拟合结果提供高效、有效的统计推断方法:通过held-out OLS重拟合监督子空间,提出Nadeau-Bengio校正渐近t检验与置换检验两种方法,兼具高功效与低计算成本,并发布跨语言Rust库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PLS回归在高维X中提取与结果对齐的方向,应用广泛,但其拟合结果的推断要么代价高昂、有偏而被不推荐使用,要么根本缺失。作者希望为PLS等监督子空间方法提供廉价且统计上有效的推断工具。

Method: 将推断归约为对监督子空间的held-out OLS重拟合(PLS、supervised PCA、线性探针共用的原语),并基于held-out相关性给出两种检验:1)Nadeau-Bengio校正的渐近t检验,作为快速近似;2)置换检验,功效相当,在结果-预测变量独立且行iid下有限样本有效。由于held-out预测在任何正交重基下不变,可解释基(如varimax)继承联合结论但不产生逐轴p值;逐成分结论由PLS提取顺序上的固定序列检验给出。

Result: 在合成几何、两个NIR化学计量数据集及跨语言词嵌入回归上验证;精确检验也可迁移到supervised PCA与ridge探针。所提检验功效高于CV-permutation-Q²,成本仅为其一小部分。并提出基于n与X谱的预运行检查以判断近似是否安全。

Conclusion: 论文为PLS类监督子空间拟合提供了快速、低成本的推断框架,发布了含Python、R、Julia绑定的Rust库及Python文本流水线。

Abstract: Partial Least Squares (PLS) regression extracts a few outcome-aligned directions in a high-dimensional X and is widely used across applied science, but inference on the resulting fit is either expensive, biased and discouraged, or absent. We reduce inference to held-out OLS refits of the supervised subspace, a primitive shared by PLS, supervised PCA, and linear probes, and supply two tests using held-out correlations: a Nadeau-Bengio corrected asymptotic t-test as a fast approximation, and a permutation test with comparable power, finite-sample valid under outcome-predictor independence and iid rows. Held-out predictions are unchanged under any orthogonal rebasing of the supervised span, so an interpretable basis such as varimax inherits the joint claim but not a per-axis p-value; per-component claims come from a fixed-sequence test on the PLS extraction order. We validate on synthetic geometries, two NIR chemometric datasets, and cross-lingual word-embedding regressions; the exact test also transfers to supervised PCA and a ridge probe. The proposed tests have more power than CV-permutation-Q^2, at a fraction of its cost. A pre-run check on n and the spectrum of X says when the approximation is safe. We release a Rust library with Python, R, and Julia bindings, plus a Python text pipeline.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Samples Importance: Parameterizing Dual Variables in Everywhere Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36310)
*Ignacio Boero,Jonathan Nixon,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出学习泛化性对偶变量（参数化函数）以解决everywhere learning中的约束问题，并给出理论误差保证与敏感性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: everywhere learning需在数据分布各处满足点式约束，其对偶形式中约束对应函数型对偶变量。标准经验对偶方法为每个训练样本分配独立乘子，无法推广到新样本，且忽略了乘子值所蕴含的对约束问题的有用信息。

Method: 将对偶变量表示为每个样本的参数化函数，从而可在未见样本上评估学到的乘子。理论刻画由于把对偶变量限制在参数函数类中而导致的原始解恢复误差，并分析其与函数类逼近最优统计乘子能力的联系；同时证明学到的参数化乘子保留了最优统计乘子的敏感性解释。

Result: 理论上，原始解的误差由参数函数类对最优统计乘子的逼近程度控制；所学乘子具备近似敏感性保证，且该保证可推广至训练样本之外。实验在多种everywhere learning任务上验证了约束问题可被高效求解，且学到的对偶变量能作为样本级敏感性的有意义的表示。

Conclusion: 通过学习参数化对偶变量，可将everywhere learning的约束对偶从逐样本独立乘子推广为可泛化的函数形式，兼具理论误差界与样本级敏感性解释，并在实验中验证了有效性与效率。

Abstract: Everywhere learning provides a principled framework for training AI models under constraints that must hold throughout the data distribution. In the dual domain, these pointwise constraints give rise to functional dual variables. In this work, we propose to learn these dual variables, motivated by the fact that their values encode useful information about the underlying constrained problem. By representing the dual variable as a parametric function of each sample, we enable the learned multiplier to be evaluated on new, unseen samples. This contrasts with standard empirical dual formulations, which assign an independent multiplier to each training sample. We characterize the error in the recovered primal solution induced by restricting the dual variable to a parametric function class and show that it is controlled by how well this class approximates the optimal statistical multiplier. Moreover, we show that the learned parametric multiplier retains the sensitivity interpretation of the optimal statistical multiplier, yielding approximate sensitivity guarantees that extend beyond the samples used for training. We empirically validate our theory across a variety of everywhere learning tasks, showing that the resulting constrained problems can be solved efficiently and that the learned dual variables provide meaningful representations of sample-level sensitivity.

</details>


### [114] [Fractional State Space Transition for Long Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36314)
*Ivan Kobyzev,Abbas Ghaddar,Ali Nasiri-Sarvi,Lifeng Shang,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FRAC，一种基于分数阶动力学的选择性SSM架构，用幂律长记忆替代指数遗忘，通过有限状态、对数间隔的指数模态求和近似重尾核，实现高效递归模块（可并行训练与预填充，保持有界状态自回归解码），在1.3B语言建模等实验中持续超越SOTA SSM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代SSM多依赖ODE动力学，导致指数遗忘，限制其在宽时间范围内保留信息的能力，亟需更优的长上下文记忆先验。

Method: FRAC由分数阶动力学推导，用幂律长记忆替代指数衰减；为实用化，将重尾目标核近似为有限状态、对数间隔的指数模态求和，从而把分数阶记忆转化为高效递归模块，支持并行训练与预填充，并保留有界状态自回归解码。

Result: 包括1.3B参数语言建模在内的大量实验中，FRAC在长上下文性能上持续优于SOTA SSM基线，同时在短上下文上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 分数阶动力学为长上下文SSM提供了一种实用且有效的先验。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) compress sequence history into a bounded recurrent state, making the resulting memory law a central architectural choice for long-context performance. Most modern SSMs rely on ODE-based dynamics that lead to exponential forgetting, limiting their ability to retain information over broad temporal ranges. We introduce FRAC, a selective SSM architecture derived from fractional dynamics that replaces this exponential decay with power-law long memory. To make fractional dynamics practical, FRAC approximates the heavy-tailed target kernel with a finite-state, log-spaced sum of exponential modes. This construction turns fractional memory into an efficient recurrent module with parallel training and prefill, while retaining bounded-state autoregressive decoding. Extensive experiments, including 1.3B-parameter language modeling, demonstrate that FRAC consistently improves long-context performance over state-of-the-art SSM baselines while staying competitive on short-context. These results show that fractional dynamics provide a practical and effective prior for long-context SSMs.

</details>


### [115] [PyroStack: A Multi-Band Spatio-Temporal Sub-Daily Dataset for Wildfires in the United States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36315)
*Arya Kondur,Giosue Migliorini,Cameron Schmitt,Francesco Immorlano,Tairan Wang,Rebecca C. Scholten,Efi Foufoula-Georgiou,Gary Johnson,Chris Lautenberger,Valentin Waeselynck,J. Shane Romsos,Kasra Shamsaei,Alejandro Tejedor,Tianjia Liu,Yang Chen,Padhraic Smyth,James T. Randerson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PyroStack是一个覆盖美国本土和阿拉斯加的、基于事件的野火与环境数据集，整合卫星火观测与气象、植被、燃料、地形数据，涵盖2012–2024年6994场野火，支持火蔓延模型的系统开发与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火对生态系统、空气质量和人类系统危害日益加剧，亟需能支持跨多样景观火蔓延预测模型开发与评估的数据集。有效预测需在适合物理模拟与数据驱动方法的时空分辨率上整合气象条件、燃料、植被和地形，但现有数据集往往缺乏捕捉这些相互作用控制因素所需的分辨率与覆盖范围。

Method: 构建PyroStack数据集：将卫星衍生的火观测数据与大气再分析资料、植被、燃料特征及地形信息整合到统一框架中，形成覆盖美国本土和阿拉斯加、基于事件的野火与环境数据集合。

Result: 数据集涵盖2012至2024年间发生的6994场野火，跨越多种生态系统与气候条件；提供30 m至9 km的空间分辨率和逐小时时间分辨率，以及12小时间隔的火蔓延进程数据，用于模型初始化与评估。

Conclusion: 通过结合广泛空间覆盖与精细时空细节，PyroStack支持野火动态的系统分析与物理及机器学习方法，为火蔓延模型的基准测试与改进提供基础，未来将扩展至更多区域并纳入灭火数据流。

Abstract: Wildfires are an increasing hazard to ecosystems, air quality, and human systems, creating a growing need for datasets that support systematic development and evaluation of models for predicting fire spread across diverse landscapes. Effective prediction requires integrating meteorological conditions, fuels, vegetation, and topography at spatial and temporal resolutions suitable for both physical simulation and data-driven approaches. However, existing datasets often lack the resolution and coverage needed to capture these interacting controls. The PyroStack dataset addresses this gap by providing a harmonized, event-based collection of wildfire and environmental data across the contiguous United States and Alaska. It integrates satellite-derived fire observations with atmospheric reanalysis, vegetation, fuel characteristics, and topographic information into a unified framework spanning 6994 wildfires that occurred between 2012 and 2024 across a wide range of ecosystems and climate conditions. PyroStack offers spatial resolutions ranging from 30 m to 9 km and hourly temporal resolution, along with fire progression data at 12-hour intervals to support model initialization and evaluation. By combining broad spatial coverage with fine spatial and temporal detail, the dataset enables systematic analysis of wildfire dynamics and supports both physics-based and machine learning approaches, providing a foundation for benchmarking and improving fire spread models, with future extensions aimed at incorporating additional regions and fire suppression data streams to further advance wildfire prediction.

</details>


### [116] [Periodic Weak Spots: Phase Sensitivity from Chunked KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36322)
*Xingyu Zhu,Pu,Yi,Ziheng Cheng,Ang Lv,Jing Liu,Lexing Ying,Yiyuan Ma,Xin Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分块 KV 缓存压缩会让模型的检索能力随"相位"（token 相对压缩窗口边界的位置）出现周期性波动，即"相位敏感性"，平均得分会掩盖这种系统性弱区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块 KV 缓存压缩在降低长上下文推理的内存与注意力开销时，引入了新的位置坐标——相位。作者发现同一信息在不同相位下检索难度差异显著，而现有评测只看平均分，会掩盖这种周期性的检索失败，因此有必要系统研究该现象。

Method: 1）在多个开源大模型上测量跨相位的长上下文检索准确率，量化相位敏感性；2）从头预训练一族 transformer，覆盖多种 KV 压缩设计，复现该现象；3）用因果干预做机制分析，考察不同注意力组件在各源相位上的贡献；4）用理想化检索模型分析梯度流动力学，解释相位专精的形成。

Result: 在采用分块压缩的大型开源模型中，检索准确率跨相位差异最高可达 40 个百分点，呈现周期性弱区；从头预训练的多种压缩变体均能复现相位敏感性；因果干预显示不同注意力组件对源相位的贡献不对称，存在"相位专精"；理想化模型表明梯度流动力学可能偏好锐利的相位专精。

Conclusion: 分块 KV 缓存压缩模型的高平均准确率可能与系统性的位置性失败并存，评测此类模型必须跨压缩相位进行度量，而不能仅依赖平均基准分数。

Abstract: Chunked KV-cache compression reduces the memory and attention costs of long-context inference by compressing windows of consecutive tokens into fewer cache entries at a fixed stride. Such compression also introduces a new positional coordinate: a token's phase, or its position relative to compression-window boundaries. We uncover a systematic asymmetry in models using such compression: the same information can be easy to retrieve at one phase and difficult at another. We call this periodic variation in retrieval performance phase sensitivity. In large open-weight models with such compression, long-context retrieval accuracy can differ by up to 40 percentage points across phases, revealing periodic weak spots that average benchmark scores can conceal.
  To investigate this behavior, we pretrain a family of transformers from scratch across multiple KV-compression designs, reproducing phase sensitivity across the variants. Mechanistic analysis using causal interventions in these models reveals phase specialization: different attention components contribute asymmetrically to retrieving information at different source phases. We further analyze idealized retrieval models, showing how gradient flow dynamics may favor sharp phase specialization. Evaluating models with chunked KV-cache compression thus requires measuring across compression phases: high average accuracy can coexist with systematic positional failures.

</details>


### [117] [Reducing the Adaptation Gap Through Reachable Fisher Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36329)
*Wasif Jalal,Sachin Deb,Asif Salekin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 PEFT 中 Fisher 迹的启示与损失，通过"可达 Fisher"揭示：迹可作可扩展诊断与训练启发式，但不能证明局部差距行为或矩阵对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似适配器因训练局部方向不同，可能对子群损失产生不同影响。由于适配器规模下无法直接构造曲率矩阵，实践常用 Fisher 迹等标量摘要，但其揭示与丢失的信息尚不明确，需加以理论刻画。

Method: 提出"可达 Fisher"：将各子群的全模型 Fisher 通过适配器 Jacobian 拉回，表示适配器可触及的 Gauss-Newton 曲率，其迹可由得分梯度范数直接计算。理论上分析其在匹配子群梯度下的正定差异、受限算子范数与无矩阵 Frobenius 差异；实证覆盖 306 个单种子模型、1,218 次评估与 30 组数据集-编码器-适配器组合。

Result: 在 75.7% 的合格评估中，较高迹伴随更大子群难度；迹匹配在全部 30 组组合中缩小最优-最差子群差距。但留出审计显示，算子差异仅在 30 组中的 23 组下降，无偏平方 Frobenius 统计量仅在 16 组下降。等迹排除正定差异，却仍可掩盖巨大算子差异。

Conclusion: Fisher 迹是有效的可扩展诊断工具与训练启发式，但不足以作为局部差距行为或矩阵对齐的证明。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) determines not only how many parameters are trained, but also which local directions a model can move in, so similar adapters can affect subgroup losses differently. Since curvature matrices are infeasible to form at adapter scale, scalar summaries such as the Fisher trace are often used instead. We study what the trace reveals and what it loses through the reachable Fisher: each subgroup's full-model Fisher pulled back through the adapter Jacobian. Under likelihood losses, it represents the Gauss-Newton curvature accessible to the adapter, and its trace can be computed from score-gradient norms without forming the full matrix. Under matched subgroup gradients, a positive-definite reachable-Fisher difference, with a margin exceeding the Hessian-Fisher defect, implies that every sufficiently small nonzero model-changing update increases the signed gap. In contrast, the restricted operator norm determines worst-case quadratic change, while a matrix-free Frobenius discrepancy bounds its reachable-Fisher component. Trace alone cannot certify definiteness or control matrix mismatch. Equal traces rule out a positive-definite difference but can still hide large operator discrepancies. Across 306 single-seed models, higher trace accompanies greater subgroup difficulty in 75.7 percent of 1,218 eligible evaluations, while trace matching reduces the best-worst subgroup gap in all 30 dataset-encoder-adapter combinations. However, held-out audits show that operator discrepancy decreases in 23 of 30 combinations, while the unbiased squared-Frobenius statistic decreases in only 16 of 30. Fisher trace is therefore a scalable diagnostic and training heuristic, but not a certificate of local gap behavior or matrix alignment.

</details>


### [118] [Adapting Linear-Time Architectures for Tabular In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36337)
*David Schnurr,Felix Sarnthein,Thomas Hofmann,Imanol Schlag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了面向表格上下文学习（ICL）的线性序列混合器，发现最佳训练方式是类下一词预测，DeltaNet 这类因果线性混合器表现最佳，但存在超出预训练上下文长度后性能退化的问题，并通过时间依赖衰减调度干预稳定了长度泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型依赖上下文中的标注样本进行条件建模，性能强但 softmax 注意力限制了其在大数据集上的应用。现有线性时间替代方案多为因果结构，其在表格 ICL 中的潜力尚未被充分探索，因此需要系统研究因果训练设置、线性序列混合器比较以及超出预训练上下文长度的 ICL 泛化能力。

Method: 论文分三步：(1) 重新审视因果训练设置；(2) 比较多种线性序列混合器；(3) 研究其超出预训练上下文长度的 ICL 泛化。分析上使用隐藏状态 oracle 定位问题根源，并通过时间依赖衰减调度干预来调节 DeltaNet 学习到的写入速率。

Result: 因果模型最佳训练设置类似下一词预测；最promising的线性混合器是因果的 DeltaNet，甚至优于非因果线性注意力；但性能在预训练上下文长度的 2–4 倍之后退化，双向性等缓解策略只能推迟问题。隐藏状态 oracle 表明这不是容量问题，而是因果模型深层循环状态不稳定/漂移所致。时间依赖衰减调度干预稳定了长度泛化；再从最终状态读出重新引入非因果性后，在 OpenML-CC18 与 TabArena 上接近受控 softmax 注意力基线。

Conclusion: DeltaNet 等因果线性混合器在表格 ICL 中具有潜力，但受循环状态不稳定性制约长度泛化；通过衰减调度干预可缓解该问题，结合最终状态读出可接近 softmax 注意力基线表现。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models achieve strong performance by conditioning on labelled examples in context, but softmax attention limits their use on large datasets. Existing linear-time alternatives, however, are mostly causal, and their potential for tabular in-context learning (ICL) remains underexplored. To address this, we (1) revisit causal training setups, (2) compare linear sequence mixers, and (3) investigate their ICL generalisation beyond the pretraining context length. First, we show that the best training setup for causal models resembles next-token prediction. Then, perhaps surprisingly, the most promising linear sequence mixer is causal: DeltaNet outperforms even non-causal linear attention. However, it degrades beyond $2$-$4\times$ the pretraining context length, and existing mitigation strategies such as bidirectionality defer the problem at best. A hidden-state oracle shows that this is not a capacity problem. Instead, our analysis points to an instability in the recurrent state, which drifts in deeper layers of causal models. Since DeltaNet's learned write rates overfit to the pretraining regime, we modulate them with a time-dependent decay schedule intervention to stabilise length generalisation. Finally, re-introducing non-causality by reading out from the final state allows us to closely match a controlled softmax attention baseline on OpenML-CC18 and TabArena.

</details>


### [119] [Explainability from Training with Applications to TCR-Epitope Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36354)
*Jiarui Li,Zixiang Yin,Samuel Landry,Zhengming Ding,Ramgopal Mettu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的可解释性范式EFT（explainability from training），通过追踪训练过程中模型的解释来理解模型为何依赖特定特征以及如何组织这些特征作为预测证据，并以TCR-表位预测为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在科学任务中表现优异，但其黑箱特性限制了对其学习过程的理解。现有可解释性方法难以揭示模型如何组织证据以及在学习过程中如何演化。

Method: 提出EFT，一种模型无关范式，在训练过程中追踪模型解释。将EFT应用于四种SOTA的TCR-表位预测模型（TCR-SRIM、TULIP、MixTCRpred、NetTCR-2.2），涵盖事后解释与设计即可解释方法以及transformer和CNN架构。同时引入TCR-XAI2基准，包含388个实验解析的TCR-表位结构，并辅以AlphaFold3、Boltz-2、TCRModel2、tFold-TCR、OpenFold3预测的结构。

Result: 研究发现：(1) CNN与transformer模型表现出不同的学习轨迹；(2) TCR α与β链证据在学习中可能冲突，限制了联合建模两条链的收益，而MHC信息可缓解这一问题；(3) 真实与预测结构数据在TCR和肽段特征偏好上存在差异，且模型确定性轨迹不同。

Conclusion: EFT能够揭示模型在训练中如何组织和演化预测证据，为科学任务的深度学习模型提供了深入的过程性可解释性，并揭示了TCR-表位预测中结构信息与模型学习行为的复杂关系。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved strong performance in artificial intelligence for science, yet their black-box nature limits our understanding of how they learn scientific tasks. Existing methods for interpretability provide limited insight into how models organize evidence and evolve during learning. We introduce explainability from training (EFT), a model-agnostic paradigm that traces model interpretation during training to explain why models rely on specific features and how they organize these features as predictive evidence. We apply EFT to four state-of-the-art T cell receptor (TCR)-epitope prediction models, TCR-SRIM, TULIP, MixTCRpred, and NetTCR-2.2, spanning post-hoc and interpret-by-design approaches as well as transformers and CNNs. To investigate how structural information affects model explanations, we introduce a benchmark, TCR-XAI2, containing 388 unique experimentally resolved TCR-epitope structures, complemented by structures predicted using AlphaFold3, Boltz-2, TCRModel2, tFold-TCR, and OpenFold3. Using EFT with TCR-XAI2, we demonstrate that (1) CNN and transformer models exhibit distinct learning trajectories; (2) TCR $α$ and $β$ evidence can conflict during learning, limiting the benefits of jointly modeling both chains, while MHC information mitigates this; and (3) real versus predicted structural data for TCR-epitope prediction exhibits distinct TCR and peptide feature preferences as well as differing trajectories of model certainty.

</details>


### [120] [AdaKerNet: Neural Kernel Decoding for Task-Adaptive Prediction with Multimodal Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36368)
*Konstantinos D. Polyzos,Eleni Oikonomou,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaKerNet是一种与MLLM参数无关的可学习任务自适应神经核解码器，仅利用冻结的多模态表示，在少标签场景下显著优于现有解码器，平均误差降低达41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型（MLLM）在有限监督下难以适配下游任务。现有方法依赖参数微调或训练神经解码器，前者在少监督下效果差且需访问模型参数（闭源模型常不可用），后者同样受限于监督不足。

Method: AdaKerNet完全与底层MLLM参数无关，仅作用于其冻结的丰富多模态表示。它包含三部分：(i)一组可学习、Lipschitz控制的多模态特征；(ii)提供软结构先验的参考核；(iii)轻量非线性神经预测器，自适应变形该结构。在统一优化框架中联合学习核表示与神经预测器。

Result: 在BLIP-2、LLaVA-1.5、Qwen2.5-VL和Gemini Embedding 2四个MLLM上，以及文本、音频、图像、表格等多模态输入上测试，AdaKerNet在多种稀缺标签预算下均优于MLP、注意力、自编码器和核基解码器，平均误差降低高达41%。结构消融显示各组件贡献互补。

Conclusion: AdaKerNet是冻结多模态表示在稀缺标签场景下进行预测的有效方法。

Abstract: Large foundation models have been introduced with the promise of efficient adaptation to downstream tasks. Yet, under limited supervision, MLLMs, an important class of large foundation models, remain challenging to adapt to various downstream tasks. Adaptation typically relies either on MLLM parameter fine-tuning or on training neural-based decoders. Both approaches struggle under limited supervision, while fine-tuning additionally requires access to model parameters, which is often unavailable for closed-source models. We introduce AdaKerNet, a novel learnable task-adaptive neural kernel decoder. AdaKerNet is fully agnostic to the parameters of the underlying MLLM and operates solely on its (frozen) rich representations obtained from the diverse available modalities. AdaKerNet relies on (i) a set of learnable, Lipschitz-controlled multimodal features derived from these MLLM representations; (ii) a reference kernel that provides a soft structural prior on those features; and (iii) a lightweight nonlinear neural predictor that adaptively deforms that structure. Learning the kernel representation and the neural predictor jointly within a unified optimization framework allows AdaKerNet to capture features and geometric relationships relevant to the downstream task. Numerical tests across four MLLMs: BLIP-2, LLaVA-1.5, Qwen2.5-VL, and Gemini Embedding 2, and multimodal inputs spanning text, audio, images, and tabular measurements demonstrate significant and consistent improvements over direct MLP, attention-, autoencoder- and kernel-based decoders, across a range of scarce-label budgets, with average error reduction of up to 41% across baselines. These results establish AdaKerNet as an effective approach for prediction from frozen multimodal representations in the scarce label regime. Additional structural ablations highlight the complementary contributions of AdaKerNet's components.

</details>


### [121] [Neural Succession: A Mesoscopic Theory of Invasion, Coexistence, and Stabilization in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36375)
*Shoaib Ahmed Dipu,Md Salman Shamil,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"演替学习理论"(SLT)，将持续学习中的表示视为"驻留群落"、任务为"入侵者"、遗忘为"驻留位移"、共存为"联合保持"，并以"定向入侵前兼容性"为经验坐标，在8个实验和84个转换中验证其能预测遗忘、区分共存与排斥，并给出多个理论界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习通常通过保护旧知识的机制来研究，缺乏一个能预测"何时会发生遗忘"的前瞻性诊断工具。本文希望建立一个中观层面的理论框架，把群落生态学的演替视角引入持续学习，从而在任务学习之前就能预测遗忘与共存。

Method: 提出SLT：当前表示为驻留群落，新任务为入侵者，遗忘为驻留位移，联合保持为共存，回放为驻留强化。核心经验坐标是"定向入侵前兼容性"，即在学习新任务前对驻留模型进行测量。在20个有向Split-CIFAR-10转换、MNIST排列、CIFAR-10旋转、84个转换套件、CIFAR-100分区及5种优化器设置上验证，并与激活、表示、雅可比、固定系数Lotka-Volterra等特化方法对比。

Result: 兼容性可排序后续遗忘（三次重复r=-0.789，每个重复r≤-0.67），预测留出遗忘误差比无信息基线低24%（r=-0.804、-0.718）。在84转换套件中，兼容性在每个保持阈值下都能区分共存与排斥（AUC 0.93-0.97）。回放用最多325个样本修复所有转换，且在位移最大处最有效。兼容性达到|r|=0.720，而其他特化方法未达到。可塑性从训练早期到晚期可靠下降（15/15和14/15）。

Conclusion: SLT是一种预适应诊断工具，可补充回放、正则化和投影方法。理论贡献包括最小栖息地修改界、位移下限、充分共存条件、可识别性定律及范围限制推论、演替稳定化和局部强化，并预测了坐标失效的位置，与CIFAR-100分区和优化器实验相符。

Abstract: Continual learning is usually studied through mechanisms that preserve old knowledge. We develop Successional Learning Theory (SLT), a mesoscopic account in which the current representation is a resident community, the incoming task is an invader, forgetting is resident displacement, joint retention is coexistence, replay is resident reinforcement, and training moves from establishment toward stabilization. Its empirical coordinate is directional pre-invasion compatibility, measured on the resident model before the incoming task is learned. Across eight experiments, compatibility orders later forgetting on the 20 directed Split-CIFAR-10 transitions (three-repeat r=-0.789, incoming-task cluster 95% CI [-0.90,-0.72], every repeat alone r<=-0.67), forecasts held-out forgetting with 24% lower error than a no-information baseline, and reproduces under controlled MNIST permutations and CIFAR-10 rotations (r=-0.804, -0.718). On an 84-transition suite, compatibility separates coexistence from exclusion at every retention threshold (AUC 0.93-0.97). Replay repairs every transition with at most 325 stored examples and is most efficient where displacement is largest. Compatibility reaches |r|=0.720, while activation, representation, Jacobian, and fixed-coefficient Lotka-Volterra specializations do not. Plasticity and feature turnover fall reliably from early to late training (15/15 and 14/15 runs). We formalize a minimum habitat-modification bound, a displacement floor, a sufficient coexistence condition, an identifiability law with a range-restriction corollary, successional stabilization, and local reinforcement. The identifiability law also predicts where the coordinate loses leverage, and the prediction matches three CIFAR-100 partitions and five optimizer regimes. SLT is a pre-adaptation diagnostic that complements replay, regularization, and projection methods.

</details>


### [122] [Reward-rate Policy Gradient for Efficient Machine Learning Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36393)
*Muhang Tian,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reward-rate Policy Gradient (RPG)，将连续时间强化学习与半马尔可夫决策过程引入智能体强化学习，优化"单位时间长期奖励"（奖励率），并在 bandit 理论与 MLE-Bench、NanoGPT 实验中验证其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习假设每个动作耗费固定单位时间，仅最大化期望累计奖励。但在 MLE 智能体等任务中，数据加载、特征工程、模型训练等动作耗时各不相同，时间成本不可忽略。因此需要在动作代价高昂的智能体 RL 中把效率纳入优化目标。

Method: 作者从连续时间 RL 与半马尔可夫决策过程 (SMDP) 出发，提出 Reward-rate Policy Gradient (RPG)，以奖励率（单位时间的长期奖励）为优化目标。RPG 利用 off-policy 样本估计奖励率，并按该费率为每个动作的时间消耗"收费"。作者先在 bandit 设定下做理论分析，再将 RPG 应用于小型语言模型 Qwen3.5-4B 的自改进循环。

Result: 理论分析表明 RPG 可逼近最优奖励率，且在 bandit 实验中优于基线并避免了现有方法需枚举策略空间的问题。在 MLE-Bench 与 NanoGPT 上，RPG 在固定时间预算内取得比普通 RL 更高的奖励，分别领先 19.2% 和 85.7%。

Conclusion: RPG 为现代智能体强化学习中动作与外部环境交互并消耗时间的场景，提供了一种在考虑等待时间前提下优化性能的实用方案。

Abstract: Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) techniques focus on maximizing expected cumulative reward, where each action assumes to take a constant unit of time. However, this assumption does not hold for agentic RL tasks such as machine learning engineering (MLE) agents, where actions involve data loading, feature engineering, and model training that take variable durations. Efficiency matters in modern agentic RL where actions are costly. To address this limitation, we adapt from continuous-time RL and Semi-Markov Decision Process (SMDP) formulation and propose Reward-rate Policy Gradient (RPG), where we focus on optimizing the reward rate -- the long-term reward per unit of time. RPG estimates the reward rate from off-policy samples, then charges each action for the time it consumes at that rate. We first conduct theoretical analysis in the bandit setting to establish that RPG approximates the optimal reward rate and empirically demonstrate it outperforms baselines while avoiding enumeration over the policy space, a known issue for an existing method. We then further apply RPG on a small language model (Qwen3.5-4B) with self-improvement loops and empirically show it obtains higher rewards within a fixed time budget than vanilla RL on MLE-Bench and NanoGPT, with a 19.2% and 85.7% margin, respectively. Our method provides a practical solution for optimizing performance under wait time considerations in modern agentic RL tasks, where actions interact with external environments and cost time.

</details>


### [123] [Longer Records, Broader Invariance: The Hidden Scaling Problem in Longitudinal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36409)
*Rameen Mahmood,Xuhai "Orson" Xu,Zachary Beattie,Jeffrey Kaye,Danny Yuxing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 纵向数据价值在于个体变化，但对比学习目标会无意中抹去变化。作者区分"记录跨度"与"监督跨度"，证明扩大监督跨度会系统性地抑制可恢复的变化状态信息，并提出解决路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向数据的核心价值在于捕捉人的变化，然而当前从这些数据中学习的目标（尤其是person-level对比学习）会将同一人的所有观测视为正样本对，从而在记录增长时把越来越远、越来越不同的行为状态强行拉近，导致更多历史不仅带来更多数据，还带来更强的"不变性"，反而抹掉了变化信息。作者希望厘清这一矛盾，并找到在记录增长时保留变化的方法。

Method: 作者将"记录跨度"（学习者可见的历史长度）与"监督跨度"（正样本对监督跨越历史的距离）分离研究。在长达2.7年的居家传感记录上，分别考察不同监督跨度对可恢复变化状态信息的影响；并在固定可用历史的前提下做对照。同时测试"保持正样本局部化"是否足够，以及显式对比同一人其他观测的效果。最后在199名GLOBEM参与者中进行前瞻性复现。

Result: 更宽的监督跨度会系统性地抑制可恢复的变化状态信息，即使可用历史固定也如此；在最宽跨度下，未训练编码器中可恢复的信息不足10%。仅保持正样本局部化并不足够：随记录增长，即便从未配对的远端状态也会越来越相似。显式对比同一人的其他观测可在不缩短记录的前提下逆转这一信息损失，揭示了纵向规模扩大不变性的第二条路径。监督跨度效应在199名GLOBEM参与者中得到前瞻性复现。

Conclusion: 纵向规模是一种选择而非必然：更多历史并不必然意味着更强不变性。通过控制记录增长时"被保持什么不变"，可以同时利用长历史的规模优势并保留使纵向数据有价值的个体变化信息。监督跨度是理解与设计纵向对比学习目标的关键维度。

Abstract: Longitudinal data are valuable because people change. Yet the objectives used to learn from these data can inadvertently erase that change. In person-level contrastive learning, observations from the same person are treated as positives; as records grow, those positives can span increasingly distant---and increasingly different---behavioral states. More history can therefore produce not only more data, but broader invariance. We show that this distinction is fundamental. We separate \emph{record span}, how much history the learner sees, from \emph{supervision span}, how far across that history positive-pair supervision reaches. Across in-home sensing records spanning up to 2.7 years, broader supervision systematically suppresses recoverable changing-state information, even when the available history is held fixed. At the broadest span, less than 10\% of the information recoverable from an untrained encoder remains. Yet keeping positives local is not sufficient: as records grow, even distant states that are never paired become increasingly similar. Explicitly contrasting other observations from the same person reverses this loss without shortening the record, revealing a second route by which longitudinal scale can broaden invariance. Finally, we prospectively reproduce the supervision-span effect in 199 GLOBEM participants. Longitudinal scale therefore presents a choice: more history need not mean more invariance. By controlling what is held invariant as records grow, we can preserve the change that made the longitudinal data valuable in the first place.

</details>


### [124] [Theory on Attention Dynamics for Out-of-Distribution In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36448)
*Junze Deng,Daouda Sow,Sen Lin,Yingbin Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上刻画了Transformer在分布偏移（OOD）下的上下文学习误差，揭示了其与α型和β型注意力权重动态的关系，并分析了OOD微调对源域遗忘的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽具备强大的上下文学习能力，但在遇到偏离训练分布的OOD输入时性能常显著下降，且缺乏理论解释。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过刻画α型（正确特征置信度）与β型（错误特征置信度）注意力权重动态之间的相互作用来分析输入分布偏移下的OOD误差；并进一步研究使用OOD数据微调时对源域性能（遗忘）的影响。

Result: OOD误差依赖于训练特征与OOD特征之间所有两两交互，某些情况下Transformer表现不优于随机猜测；微调对源域性能的影响取决于特征偏移的性质——微调会持续增强识别原始分布正确特征的置信度，而其他错误特征的干扰可能增强或减弱。

Conclusion: 研究在合成与真实数据上的大量实验验证了理论发现，为理解Transformer的OOD泛化与微调遗忘提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities, enabling them to perform new tasks without additional fine-tuning. However, their performance often deteriorates when encountering out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs that deviate from the training distribution, and the underlying theory remains poorly understood. To fill this gap, we characterize the OOD error under the input distribution shift through the interplay between the dynamics of the so-called $α$-type and $β$-type attention weights, which represent the transformer's confidence in identifying the correct and incorrect features, respectively. Our results indicate that the OOD error for each feature depends on all pairwise interactions between the training features and OOD features, and under certain cases the transformer performs no better than random guessing. To improve the OOD generalization performance, we next investigate the impact of model finetuning with the OOD data, and particularly, characterize the model forgetting performance on the source domain. Interestingly, the performance on the source domain may not always degrade after finetuning, which highly depends on the nature of the feature shift: finetuning on OOD domain keeps enhancing the confidence of identifying correct features from the original distribution, while the interference from other incorrect features may either increase or decrease. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real data are conducted to corroborate the theoretical insights.

</details>


### [125] [Invariant Atoms: Sparse Coordinates of Local Semantic Geometry in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36451)
*Muhammad Ahtesham,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"不变原子假说"，认为语言模型中的局部语义变化可沿稀疏、稳定的方向组织，并通过学习共享语义框架与稀疏坐标来验证该假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在措辞、风格和句法大幅变化时仍能保持语义，但细微的语义编辑却会系统性改变其隐藏表示。作者由此推测，语义变化可能沿可重复出现的局部方向组织，并希望据此建立局部语义几何的稀疏坐标系统。

Method: 提出"不变原子假说"：局部语义移动在保持语义的变换下沿着稳定的稀疏坐标方向进行。方法上学习一个共享语义框架与稀疏坐标，用于重建语义位移同时抑制无关（nuisance）变化，并采用依赖锚点的对角调制调整原子强度，而不引入样本特定的旋转。

Result: 实验显示原子具有较强的语义—无关变化分离、稀疏重建能力、可复现的方向性以及对模型预测的因果影响；所学几何可泛化到未见过的语义邻域和无关变化族；局部重加权提升语义选择性，并保持一致的全局—局部结构；原子签名在模型修改后仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持将可复用的不变方向作为语言模型局部语义几何的稀疏坐标系统。

Abstract: Large language models often preserve meaning despite substantial changes in wording, style, and syntax, while small semantic edits can systematically alter their hidden representations. This suggests that semantic variation may be organized along recurring local directions. We propose the Invariant Atom Hypothesis: local semantic motion admits preferred sparse coordinates along directions that remain stable under meaning-preserving transformations. We learn a shared semantic frame and sparse coordinates that reconstruct semantic displacements while suppressing nuisance variation, with anchor-dependent diagonal modulation adjusting atom strengths without sample-specific rotations. Empirically, the atoms exhibit strong semantic--nuisance separation, sparse reconstruction, reproducible directions, and causal effects on model predictions. The learned geometry generalizes to unseen semantic neighborhoods and nuisance families, while local reweighting improves semantic selectivity and preserves a consistent global-to-local structure. Atom signatures also remain stable under model modification. These findings support reusable invariant directions as a sparse coordinate system for local semantic geometry in language models.

</details>


### [126] [Channel-Dependent State Space Model for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36453)
*Yu-Cheng Wu,Fan-Keng Sun,Li-Chun Lu,Duane S. Boning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chameleon，一种专门的通道依赖（CD）状态空间模型，通过将选择性SSM与卡尔曼滤波关联，在保持线性扩展的同时实现数据依赖的细粒度变量间交互，在多个基准上取得最优MSE和MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测（MTSF）至关重要，但现有深度学习方法存在两种范式局限：通道独立（CI）方法无条件忽略跨变量依赖、仅建模时间动态；通道依赖（CD）方法虽同时考虑两者，却通常依赖架构妥协来缓解过拟合与计算开销。因此需要一种能兼顾细粒度跨变量交互与线性扩展的专门CD方法。

Method: 提出Chameleon，一种专门的CD状态空间模型：1）将选择性SSM与卡尔曼滤波关联，利用前者缺失的测量更新进行跨变量建模，同时保留SSM主干进行稳健的时间建模；2）识别GatedDeltaNet对时间序列的有利归纳偏置，将其适配为主干；3）通过额外技术提升泛化能力，包括对可逆实例归一化的一种此前未探索的随机扰动。

Result: 在强依赖的ODE和PEMS数据集上，Chameleon在所有设置下取得最佳MSE和MAE，而其CI消融与先前CD方法平均MSE高出61-178%。在28个标准基准设置中，Chameleon的MSE和MAE分别至少在27个和22个案例中优于每个基线。在Traffic和ETT上的训练时间和峰值内存分析表明其具有竞争力的效率和良好的内存扩展性。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过将选择性SSM与卡尔曼滤波结合并适配GatedDeltaNet主干，成功实现数据依赖的细粒度跨变量交互与线性扩展，在多个基准上验证了其在精度与效率上的优越性。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting (MTSF) is critical across many real-world domains. Existing deep learning approaches fall into two paradigms with distinct limitations: channel-independent (CI) methods unconditionally ignore cross-variable dependencies and model only temporal dynamics, while channel-dependent (CD) methods consider both but typically rely on architectural compromises to mitigate overfitting and computational overhead. We therefore propose Chameleon, a specialized CD state space model (SSM) that enables data-dependent, fine-grained interactions across variables while scaling linearly with their number. By connecting selective SSMs with the Kalman filter, we leverage the missing measurement update in the former for cross-variable modeling while preserving the SSM backbone for robust temporal modeling. We further identify favorable inductive biases of GatedDeltaNet for time series, adapt it as our backbone, and improve generalization through additional techniques, including a previously unexplored stochastic perturbation of reversible instance normalization. On strongly dependent ODE and PEMS datasets, Chameleon achieves the best MSE and MAE across all settings, while its CI ablation and prior CD methods incur 61-178% higher MSE on average. Across 28 standard benchmark settings, Chameleon also achieves better MSE and MAE than each baseline in at least 27 and 22 cases, respectively. Training-time and peak-memory analyses on Traffic and ETT further demonstrate competitive efficiency and favorable memory scalability across different variable counts.

</details>


### [127] [Emergent phases of superposition: from partial to full representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36455)
*Lihao Guo,Yizhou Liu,Jeff Gore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究Anthropic的叠加玩具模型中,模型宽度变化如何驱动表征向量配置发生连续相变,并揭示宽度与数据统计如何共同决定表征与损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被认为通过隐空间向量表征特征,而叠加现象(表征向量重叠使特征数超过宽度)是其组织方式的主要解释。但宽度与数据统计如何决定表征向量配置,以及在大特征数与大宽度下的损失规律,仍缺乏理解。

Method: 在Anthropic的叠加玩具模型中,采用部分随机投影近似建立理论,并结合实验验证。

Result: 增大宽度会驱动连续相变,从部分表征相(仅部分特征有显著表征向量,其余消失)转为全表征相(所有特征均被表征)。临界宽度随活跃特征数近似线性增长(至多相差对数因子)。损失标度在相变前后改变:低于临界宽度时损失随活跃特征数线性增长且弱依赖宽度;高于临界宽度时损失近似随活跃特征数二次增长并随宽度反比衰减。非均匀激活概率会延迟相变并降低损失,高频特征占据更多空间。

Conclusion: 研究给出了模型宽度与数据统计共同塑造表征与损失的机制,是迈向理解大模型表征标度规律的一步。

Abstract: Large language models are thought to represent features by vectors in a hidden space of dimension given by the model's width. Superposition, in which more features are represented than the width by letting representation vectors overlap, is a leading account of how representation vectors are organized. However, how model width and data statistics determine the configuration of representation vectors and the resulting loss when the number of features and the width are large remains less understood. Here we show, in Anthropic's toy model of superposition, that increasing the width drives a continuous phase transition from a partial-representation phase, where only a subset of features receives appreciable representation vectors while the rest vanish, to a full-representation phase, where every feature is represented. Our theory via a partial random projection approximation predicts, and experiments confirm, that the critical width grows linearly with the number of active features up to a logarithmic factor. The loss scaling changes across the transition: below the critical width, the loss grows linearly with the number of active features and depends weakly on the width in a form set by data statistics; above it, the loss grows approximately quadratically with the number of active features and decays inversely with the width. Non-uniform firing probabilities delay the transition and lower the loss, as more frequent features occupy more space. Our results provide an account of how model width and data statistics jointly shape representations and loss, a step toward understanding representation scaling in large models.

</details>


### [128] [Fisher-IRG: Fisher-Induced Local Invariant Representation Geometry across Language and Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36458)
*Abdullah All Tanvir,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fisher诱导不变表示几何（Fisher-IRG），通过预测敏感性度量表示的局部方向，利用对比广义特征值问题恢复语义不变方向，在语言与视觉模型中展现出优于协方差几何的语义选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义保持变换可能在表示中引发显著位移，而微小变化却可能强烈影响模型预测。这引出核心问题：何种局部度量最能刻画语义相关的变异，因此需要一种基于预测的几何度量。

Method: 构建每个表示周围的语义保持与语义改变邻域，聚合其局部Fisher信息，并通过对比广义特征值问题恢复不变方向，从而定义Fisher-IRG。

Result: 受控位移分析显示，相近的欧氏位移可能带来截然不同的预测后果；在语言与视觉模型中，Fisher-IRG比协方差几何具有更强的语义与冗余预测选择性及更可复现的子空间，并恢复出系统性不同的局部方向。

Conclusion: 表示干预将语义效应定位于Fisher导出的子空间，留出分离与检索表明恢复几何可泛化到发现邻域之外，支持Fisher-IRG作为刻画局部不变表示几何的原理性框架。

Abstract: Semantic-preserving transformations can induce substantial motion in learned representations, while small changes may strongly affect model predictions, raising a basic question: what local metric best captures semantically consequential variation? We propose Fisher-induced invariant representation geometry (Fisher-IRG), which measures local representation directions through their predictive sensitivity. Around each representation, we construct semantic-preserving and semantic-changing neighborhoods, aggregate their local Fisher information, and recover invariant directions through a contrastive generalized eigenvalue problem. Controlled displacement analyses first show that comparable Euclidean motion can have substantially different predictive consequences, supporting the need for a predictive geometry. Across language and vision models, Fisher-IRG yields stronger semantic-versus-nuisance predictive selectivity and generally more reproducible subspaces than covariance-based geometry, while recovering systematically distinct local directions. Representation interventions further localize semantic effects to the Fisher-derived subspace, and held-out separation and retrieval show that the recovered geometry generalizes beyond the discovery neighborhoods. These results support Fisher-IRG as a principled framework for characterizing local invariant representation geometry.

</details>


### [129] [DisCoMBO: Steering Expert-in-the-Loop Black Box Optimization via Distributional Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36472)
*Jonas Seng,Bennet Wittelsbach,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分布一致性分数DisCo及基于它的DisCoMBO框架，将概率电路生成代理与形式化不确定性结合，实现知识感知的零遗憾优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SMBO依赖贝叶斯或集成代理进行不确定性量化，虽逐渐融入外部领域知识以加速发现，但标准采集函数的重加权方式存在引导不透明、知识整合保真度低的问题。概率电路作为生成式代理虽能通过条件采样注入知识，却缺乏严格的探索-利用语义用于严谨优化。

Method: 提出Distributional Conformance Score (DisCo)——一个有界且[0,1]归一化的模型"惊讶度"度量，统一了概率电路的灵活高效与形式化不确定性框架，保持线性时间推断，并可评估外部知识相对模型证据的一致性。基于此构建DisCoMBO框架进行鲁棒的知识感知优化。

Result: DisCo能恢复与高斯过程等核方法不确定性相当的性质，同时保持线性时间推断，并准确评估外部知识一致性；DisCoMBO在AutoML、材料优化和风电场优化等多样基准上表现有效。

Conclusion: DisCoMBO被证明是零遗憾算法，为知识感知优化提供了兼顾灵活性与形式化不确定性的有效方案。

Abstract: Sequential Model-Based Optimization (SMBO) traditionally relies on Bayesian or ensembling surrogates for uncertainty quantification. While historically treated as fully data-driven, SMBO increasingly integrates external domain expertise to accelerate discovery. To overcome the opaque guidance and diminished integration fidelity of standard acquisition re-weighting, Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) have emerged as a generative surrogate alternative, enabling direct knowledge injection via conditional sampling. However, these generative routines lack the formal exploration-exploitation semantics required for rigorous optimization. We introduce the Distributional Conformance Score (DisCo), a novel metric that unifies the flexibility and efficiency of PCs with a formal uncertainty framework. DisCo provides a bounded, $[0, 1]$-normalized measure of model "surprise" that (1) recovers properties comparable to kernel-based uncertainty known from, e.g., Gaussian Processes, while maintaining linear-time inference, and (2) enables accurate assessment of conformance of external knowledge w.r.t. model evidence. We then present DisCoMBO, a framework leveraging these properties for robust, knowledge-aware optimization. We prove that DisCoMBO is a zero-regret algorithm and demonstrate its effectiveness across diverse benchmarks from AutoML, material optimization, and wind park optimization.

</details>


### [130] [Guard Models Are Overconfident Where Base Models Are Uncertain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36477)
*Jonghyun Hong,MinJae Jung,Minwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 评估五个guard模型发现：干净输入下接近校准，但对抗攻击使校准性恶化一个数量级，将假阴性变成高置信错误；对比基座模型发现不确定性信号仍存在，差异定位于后层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guard模型作为安全分类器，其置信度分数驱动下游内容审核决策，因此校准性至关重要。但对抗攻击对guard模型校准性的影响尚不清楚，需要评估并找出置信度失准的根源。

Method: 在提示分类任务上评估五个guard模型；对它们施加对抗攻击并测量校准性变化；将每个guard与其对应的基座语言模型对比不确定性信号；通过逐层分析定位guard与基座模型的分歧来源，比较表示秩以及干净安全/有害区域的分布。

Result: 多个guard模型在干净输入上近乎校准，但对抗攻击使其校准性下降约一个数量级，假阴性转变为与正确检测难以区分的高置信错误。基座模型通常在guard失败的同一些输入上仍表达不确定性。逐层分析显示分歧集中在后层，guard模型在此处表现出更锐利的安全/不安全分离和更低秩的表示，且对抗性有害输入更靠近干净安全区域。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了对抗攻击下guard模型置信度与基座模型不确定性之间的错配，说明guard模型的置信度不能可靠反映基座模型已有的不确定性信号，为改进guard模型的校准与鲁棒性提供了方向。

Abstract: Guard models are used as safety classifiers, with confidence scores driving downstream moderation decisions. We evaluate five guard models for prompt classification and find that although several are nearly calibrated on clean inputs, adversarial attacks degrade their calibration by an order of magnitude, turning false negatives into high-confidence errors indistinguishable from correct detections. Comparing each guard with its corresponding base LM, we find that uncertainty signals often remain available, with the base model typically expressing uncertainty on the same inputs where the guard fails. Layer-wise analyses localize this guard-base divergence to later layers, where guard models exhibit sharper safe/unsafe separation and lower-rank representations, while adversarial harmful inputs lie closer to the clean-safe region. These findings highlight a mismatch between guard confidence and base model uncertainty under attack.

</details>


### [131] [The Teacher Is a Direction, Not a Destination: Extrapolating RL-Induced Representation Residuals in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36484)
*Hao Li,MeiJia Chen,Weijie Ren,Donghan Li,Zijun Tian,Jingchun Huang,Naibo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RIDE 方法，通过在表示空间（隐状态）而非输出空间直接外推 RL 诱导的变化方向，使学生模型逼近甚至超越 RL 教师模型，并避免了输出空间外推的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy 蒸馏（OPD）中学生匹配教师的下一 token 分布，其泛化变体虽可让学生在输出空间外推隐式奖励从而超越教师，但存在两个问题：一是语言模型 head 会各向异性地衰减这种变化（教师隐状态中的变化只有很小比例到达 logits）；二是输出空间外推依赖采样 token 的 log-probability 比值，会引入被外推放大的噪声，导致训练不稳定。作者观察到 RL 会使模型内部表示相对其基础检查点发生偏移，且该偏移方向可在每一层测量。

Method: 提出 RIDE（RL-Induced Direction Extrapolation），在表示空间直接外推 RL 诱导的变化：在每一层和每个 token 位置，计算教师与其 RL 前检查点之间的残差，并将学生隐状态回归到沿该残差方向、位于教师之外的目标上。在给定采样轨迹的条件下，该回归等价于在教师处二次惩罚约束下，最大化由残差定义的线性方向奖励，从而明确地沿 RL 诱导方向移动学生，同时限制其偏离教师。

Result: 在四组不同的基础模型/RL 教师对（涵盖不同规模、架构和预训练来源）上，RIDE 在每一对上都接近或超过 RL 训练的教师，是唯一在平均意义上做到这一点的方法；并且它持续优于输出空间外推，后者在教师接近其基础模型时会使学生性能退化。

Conclusion: 在表示空间而非输出空间外推 RL 诱导的变化方向，能够有效规避语言模型 head 的各向异性衰减和输出空间外推的噪声放大问题，稳定地使学生达到或超越 RL 教师，具有良好的跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student to match the teacher's next-token distributions on the student's own trajectories and has yielded substantial empirical gains. Generalized variants allow the student to surpass the teacher by extrapolating an implicit reward in output space. The language-model head, however, attenuates this change anisotropically: much of the change encoded in the teacher's hidden states reaches the logits at a small fraction of its weight, and the sampled-token log-probability ratios on which output-space extrapolation relies inject noise that the extrapolation amplifies, making training unstable. We observe that reinforcement learning (RL) shifts a model's internal representations relative to its base checkpoint, and that the direction of this shift can be measured at every layer. Motivated by this observation, we propose RIDE (RL-Induced Direction Extrapolation), which extrapolates the RL-induced change directly in representation space: at every layer and token position, RIDE computes the residual between the teacher and its pre-RL checkpoint and regresses the student's hidden states toward targets displaced beyond the teacher along this residual. Conditioned on a sampled trajectory, this regression is equivalent to maximizing a linear directional reward defined by the residual under a quadratic penalty centered at the teacher, which makes explicit how the objective moves the student along the RL-induced direction while limiting its deviation from the teacher. Across four base/RL-teacher pairs spanning different scales, architectures, and pre-training lineages, RIDE approaches or exceeds the RL-trained teacher on every pair and is the only method whose mean does so, and it consistently outperforms output-space extrapolation, which degrades the student whenever the teacher is close to its base. Project page: https://github.com/xixixixixxxx/RIDE.

</details>


### [132] [Optimal Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36486)
*Zijun Chen,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究未知转移、有限时域MDP，在已知有限奖励函数集合下，仅用在线回合交互为每个奖励输出ε-最优策略，提出达到极小极大样本复杂度 O(SAH³/ε² · log M · polylog(...)) 的算法，无额外预热代价，近乎匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多奖励设置下，需要仅通过在线回合交互为集合中每个奖励函数同时学习到ε-最优策略，且希望达到信息论最优的样本效率，并避免额外的预热（burn-in）开销。

Method: 算法结合三个技术要素：(1) 将MVP适配到奖励切换学习中，构造乐观价值估计；(2) 使用新采样的重放样本对候选策略进行保守评估；(3) 基于间隙的乘性权重更新，利用估计差异调整奖励采样分布。

Result: 样本复杂度上界为 O(SAH³/ε² · log M · polylog(SAH log M/(min{ε,1}δ)))，与信息论下界仅相差 polylog 因子，且无额外预热代价。

Conclusion: 该方法在未知转移多奖励MDP中实现了近乎极小极大最优的样本复杂度，并可同时为所有奖励提供保证。

Abstract: We study an unknown-transition finite-horizon Markov decision process (MDP) with a finite collection of known reward functions $\{r^1, r^2, \ldots, r^M\}$. The goal is to output an $ε$-optimal policy for every reward using online episodic interaction only. Performance is measured by the policy error $V_{0}^{*, m} - V_{0}^{\widehatπ^{m}, m}$ where $m\in [M]$ represents the reward function and $V_{0}^{*, m}=\mathbb{E}_{s_1\sim μ}[V_{1}^{*, m}(s_1)]$. Under this setting, we design a provably efficient algorithm to establish a minimax sample complexity bound of $$ O\left(\frac{SAH^3}{ε^2}\log M \mathrm{polylog}\left(\frac{SAH\log M}{\min\left\{ε, 1\right\}δ}\right)\right)$$ episodes, with no additional burn-in cost. This matches the information-theoretic lower bound up to a factor of $ \mathrm{polylog}(SAH\log M/(\min\left\{ε, 1\right\}δ))$. Our method combines three technical ingredients. First, we adapt MVP to reward-switching learning to construct optimistic value estimates. Second, we use fresh replay samples to conservatively evaluate the candidate policies. Third, gap-based multiplicative weights updates adjust the reward-sampling distribution using the differences between these estimates, converting weighted learning progress into simultaneous guarantees for all rewards.

</details>


### [133] [AdaptArena: Evaluating Test-Time Personalization of Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36488)
*Dongchan Shin,Xing Han Lù,Jiaqi Deng,Jay Gala,Tomás Vergara Browne,Jaewon Moon,Fengyuan Liu,Alexandre Drouin,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Lacoste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdaptArena 基准与 AdaptiveAgent 框架，用于评估 web 智能体在推理时通过隐式偏好推断实现个性化，并揭示隐式偏好推断与动作落地是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 web 导航基准大多假设用户意图明确、偏好统一，但真实场景中用户意图往往不完整、偏好异构且很少提供显式画像，智能体需从隐式信号推断潜在偏好。该问题对实际部署至关重要，却在现有基准中被严重忽视。

Method: 提出 AdaptArena 基准，包含 480 个任务，涵盖单偏好和双偏好两种场景；每个任务需检索并利用最相关的历史用户轨迹（隐式编码目标偏好）来求解。同时提出 AdaptiveAgent，一个基于检索的框架，用于对隐式偏好推断进行标准化评估。

Result: 实验显示显著性能差距：拥有真实偏好的 oracle 智能体成功率达 82.92%，而使用该框架的 LLM 智能体最高仅 15.62%。此外发现正确推断用户偏好是任务成功的必要条件但非充分条件，即使智能体对齐目标偏好，下游 web 交互中的执行失败仍是重要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 隐式偏好推断与稳健的动作落地是部署可靠、面向用户的 web 智能体的关键挑战。作者开源了代码。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong performance on complex web navigation tasks, yet they remain brittle in real-world settings where user intentions are underspecified and preferences are heterogeneous. In practice, users rarely provide explicit profiles, requiring agents to infer latent preferences from implicit signals. Despite its importance for deployment, this problem setting is largely underexplored in existing benchmarks. To address this gap, we introduce AdaptArena, a benchmark for evaluating test-time personalization of web agents via implicit preference inference. AdaptArena consists of 480 tasks, featuring both single-preference and double-preference scenarios. Each evaluation task must be solved by retrieving and leveraging the most relevant historical user trajectory that implicitly encodes the target preference. In addition, we introduce AdaptiveAgent, a retrieval-based framework for standardized evaluation of implicit preference inference. Experiments reveal a substantial performance gap: while oracle agents with access to ground-truth preferences achieve an 82.92% success rate, the evaluated LLM agents using our framework reach at most 15.62%. Furthermore, we find that correctly inferring user preferences is necessary but not sufficient for task success, as execution failures in downstream web interactions remain a significant bottleneck even when agents align with the target preference. These findings highlight implicit preference inference and robust action grounding as key challenges for deploying reliable, user-facing web agents. We release our code: https://github.com/McGill-NLP/web-agents-test-time-adaptations

</details>


### [134] [LLMs Learn to Evade Latent Monitors from Prior Feedback Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36490)
*Hugo Lyons Keenan,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究潜伏空间监控的反馈信道漏洞：LLM智能体可通过监控器反馈推断决策规则，并选择性编辑自身激活以规避检测，而无需被告知监控目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜伏空间监控通过检查智能体内部激活而非输出来检测不良行为。但交互式监控形成反馈信道，每次裁决都会向模型泄漏其内部状态如何被评估的信息。因此需要探究：智能体能否从反馈中推断监控器的决策规则，并有选择地编辑激活以逃避检测。

Method: 提出一种反馈条件下的激活编辑规避方法。观察现成模型已能产生与监控方向对齐的激活编辑但幅度不足；将其放大8倍；再用rank-1 LoRA在前向传播中放大该行为。通过机制分析考察模型如何从上下文轮次计算激活编辑。

Result: 简单放大编辑8倍将监控器TPR从100%降至27%；rank-1 LoRA进一步在留出概念监控器上降至4%，其他概念保持正常检测率；标准基准能力保留，规避技能在监控器用新激活重训练后仍存活；随示例增多，编辑与监控方向对齐度提高。

Conclusion: 结果证明存在反馈条件下的激活控制，提示潜伏监控应被视为交互过程，智能体能够观察并回应监督措施。

Abstract: Latent space monitors aim to detect undesired behaviors in LLM agents by inspecting an agent's internal activations rather than its outputs. However, interactive monitoring creates a feedback channel where each verdict the monitor delivers leaks information to the model about how its internal states are being evaluated. We ask whether an agent can infer the monitor's decision rule from this feedback and then selectively edit its activations to evade detection. Unlike prior evasion attacks, the model is never explicitly told what the monitor detects. Surprisingly, off-the-shelf models already produce activation edits aligned with the monitored direction, but at insufficient magnitude for evasion. Simply scaling up these edits by a factor of 8 reduces the monitor's TPR from 100% to 27%. A rank-1 LoRA amplifies this behavior into effective evasion within the forward pass, reducing TPR further to 4% on held-out concept monitors while leaving other concepts at their normal detection rates. Capabilities on standard benchmarks are retained under this finetuning, and the evasion skill survives retraining the monitors on the new activations. Mechanistically, we find evidence that the model computes its activation edit from the prior in-context turns, and show that the edit becomes more aligned with the monitored direction as more examples are provided. These results demonstrate feedback-conditioned control over activations and suggest that latent monitoring should be treated as an interactive process in which agents can observe and respond to oversight measures.

</details>


### [135] [Efficient and Scalable Physics-Guided Fully Convolutional Spatiotemporal Learning for 3D Microstructure Evolution Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36504)
*Michael Trimboli,Wenxi Liu,Xianqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理引导的全卷积时空框架，可直接多帧预测完整三维微观组织演化序列，在保证高精度的同时实现超过30倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真相场模拟需在大体积域与长时间跨度上反复数值积分，三维微观组织演化预测计算成本高昂，亟需高效可扩展的替代方法。

Method: 模型结合共享三维空间编解码器与分解式隐空间转换器，融合时间、局部三维空间与通道交互；训练时引入离散Cahn–Hilliard残差作为正则项引导学习符合物理动力学，但不改变推理路径。

Result: 在高分辨率三维旋节分解轨迹上评估，完整时间上下文下平均三维结构相似度保持在0.97以上；时间信息减少时物理引导的收益增强；相比参考谱相场求解器获得30倍以上墙钟加速，且物理引导不增加推理成本。

Conclusion: 直接多帧、物理引导的全卷积学习可作为稠密三维相场动力学与重复微观组织预测的高通量代理策略。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of three-dimensional (3D) microstructure evolution remains computationally demanding because high-fidelity phase-field simulations require repeated numerical integration over large volumetric domains and long temporal horizons. This study develops an efficient and scalable physics-guided fully convolutional spatiotemporal framework for direct multi-frame prediction of complete 3D microstructure sequences. The model combines shared 3D spatial encoding and decoding with a factorized latent translator that integrates temporal, local 3D spatial, and channel interactions. A discrete Cahn--Hilliard (CH) residual is incorporated during training to regularize the learned evolution toward the governing dynamics without altering the inference pathway. The framework is evaluated on high-resolution 3D spinodal-decomposition trajectories under nominal, long-horizon, and reduced-temporal-context forecasting. Under full temporal context, the model accurately reproduces volumetric evolution, with average 3D structural similarity remaining above 0.97 over the nominal prediction horizon. Physics guidance becomes increasingly beneficial as temporal information is reduced, improving predictive robustness and preservation of interface-level morphology. The framework also achieves more than a 30-fold wall-clock speedup relative to the reference spectral phase-field solver, while physics guidance introduces no additional inference cost. These results establish direct multi-frame, physics-guided fully convolutional learning as a high-throughput surrogate strategy for dense 3D phase-field dynamics and repeated microstructure forecasting.

</details>


### [136] [PDE-OBS: Controlled Evaluation Across Observation Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36521)
*Ruichen Xu,Siyao Wang,Fang Wan,Jiacheng Qiu,Wenhan Gao,Jiaxing Zhang,Linsey Pang,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Yann LeCun,Yuefan Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出PDE-OBS基准平台，用于在变化的观测条件下系统评估物理场重建与预测方法，揭示跨观测模式误差显著高于匹配模式误差等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理场重建与预测的性能同时依赖测量密度和空间布局，但仅在单一观测模式下评估无法刻画观测模式变化时的表现。因此需要构建可在不同观测条件下系统评估方法的基准平台。

Method: PDE-OBS集成了数值数据生成、模型训练及不同观测条件下的推理与评估，包含七类偏微分方程家族的56万个场与轨迹，配以可配置观测算子和七种适配的基线方法，支持稳态重建与短时程预测。其将观测构造与物理记录分离，允许用户为训练和测试指定参数化模式与确定性混合，同时保持预测目标和数据划分不变。评估协议使用针对每个测试模式训练的参考模型，在相同测试观测与目标上比较模型，并设等样本组用于空间布局比较。

Result: 在14,000条记录子集上，对441个训练模型在9种测试模式下评估，共产生3,969次评估。全部49个PDE-方法对的跨模式平均误差均高于匹配模式平均误差，该结论在117个配置匹配模型子集中依然成立。更密集的测试观测并未一致降低固定模型的误差。五种完整配对的混合模式训练可降低大的单模式迁移误差，但目标模式训练的参考模型通常仍更准确。

Conclusion: 该基准及发现支持对观测模式敏感性进行系统评估，并为在变化测量条件下开发方法提供了可复用工作流。

Abstract: Physical-field reconstruction and forecasting depend on both measurement density and spatial layout, yet evaluation under a single observation pattern does not characterize performance when that pattern changes. We introduce PDE-OBS, an integrated benchmarking platform spanning numerical data generation, model training, and inference and evaluation under varying observation conditions. It combines 560,000 fields and trajectories from seven partial differential equation families with configurable observation operators and seven adapted baseline methods for stationary reconstruction and short-horizon forecasting. Separating observation construction from physical records allows users to specify parameterized patterns and deterministic mixtures for training and testing while preserving prediction targets and data splits.
