The evaluation protocol uses references trained for each test pattern to compare models on identical test observations and targets, alongside equal-count groups for spatial-layout comparisons. On a 14,000-record subset, we evaluate 441 trained models under nine test patterns, yielding 3,969 evaluations. Mean cross-pattern error exceeds mean matched-pattern error in all 49 PDE-method pairs, and this finding persists in a configuration-matched subset of 117 models. Denser test observations do not consistently reduce error for a fixed model. Mixed-pattern training on five completed pairs reduces large single-pattern transfer errors, although destination-trained references usually remain more accurate. Together, the benchmark and findings support systematic evaluation of observation-pattern sensitivity and provide a reusable workflow for developing methods under changing measurement conditions. Code: https://github.com/ru1ch3n/PDE-OBS.

</details>


### [137] [Adapting Context Compression for Long-Horizon Agents with Counterfactual Continuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36526)
*Guanghui Min,Liang Wu,Mingjia Shi,Yinhan He,Mayank Darbari,Liangjie Hong,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR方法，通过介入式rollout定位并修正导致执行退化的单次压缩事件，改进结构化压缩提示，从而提升长时程智能体的可靠性与可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体需借助上下文压缩应对不断增长的交互历史，而压缩质量最终由下游执行效果决定。现有提示自适应方法通过比较完整上下文与压缩轨迹来推断压缩误差，但无法隔离单次压缩事件，且受智能体随机性干扰。

Method: 作者首先发现压缩先损害可靠性再损害可解性；随后采用配对的“反事实延续”实验，在同一智能体状态下对比有/无压缩的执行结果，证明严重退化集中在孤立的压缩事件上。基于此提出PAIR：识别使后续执行退化的单次压缩，诊断其影响，并修订固定压缩模板中的相关部分。

Result: 在所有主要基准与范围组合中，PAIR在压缩类方法里取得最强的跨运行可靠性，并持续优于竞争的提示自适应基线；在不修改下游智能体的前提下，使压缩执行的性能接近甚至有时数值上超过无压缩基线。

Conclusion: 通过介入式rollout实现单次压缩事件的归因与提示修正，PAIR有效缓解压缩带来的执行退化，为压缩提示自适应提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents require context compression to manage growing interaction histories. Compression quality, however, is ultimately determined by downstream execution. Existing prompt-adaptation methods infer compression errors by comparing full-context and compressed trajectories. Such comparisons cannot isolate individual compressions and are confounded by agent stochasticity. We first find that compression degrades reliability before solvability. Using matched counterfactual continuations that compare execution from the same agent state with versus without compression, we further show that severe degradation concentrates at isolated compression events. Motivated by this finding, we propose PAIR (Prompt Adaptation using Interventional Rollouts) for adapting structured compression prompts. PAIR identifies individual compressions that degrade subsequent execution, diagnoses their effects, and revises the relevant sections of a fixed compression template. PAIR achieves the strongest cross-run reliability among compressed methods in every main benchmark-scope combination, consistently exceeding the competing prompt-adaptation baseline. Without modifying the downstream agent, PAIR brings compressed execution close to the no-compression baseline and sometimes numerically exceeds it.

</details>


### [138] [SCOPE: Observation-Conditioned Full-Target Prediction for Sparse PDE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36527)
*Ruichen Xu,Siyao Wang,Fang Wan,Jiacheng Qiu,Wenhan Gao,Jiaxing Zhang,Linsey Pang,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Prakhar Mehrotra,Yann LeCun,Yuefan Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SCOPE，通过耦合全场景潜变量预测与物理重构，实现稀疏观测下的 PDE 场恢复，性能优于掩码感知神经算子和扩散类求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏观测恢复完整物理场具有挑战性，因为观测可能无法唯一确定潜在状态。扩散类 PDE 求解器依赖迭代采样，神经算子虽可单次确定性预测但精度不足，需要在保持单次推理优势的同时提升恢复精度。

Method: SCOPE 将全场景潜变量预测与物理重构相耦合，采用共享解码器从预测视角和完整视角的表征中重构物理场，使表征学习同时受物理恢复与潜变量匹配引导。作者在固定教师-解码器对上推导了二次风险分解，并给出解码器改进从完整输入迁移到部分观测恢复的充分条件。

Result: 在五个 PDE 场景、共十项正向与逆向任务上，SCOPE 均优于掩码感知神经算子，误差低于 DiffusionPDE、FunDPS 等扩散类求解器；仅适配解码器即可在不重训主干的情况下进一步提升恢复精度，并保持确定性单次推理。

Conclusion: 耦合潜变量预测与物理重构、并配合可迁移的解码器适配，是提升稀疏观测 PDE 场恢复的有效途径，兼顾精度与推理效率。

Abstract: Recovering complete physical fields from sparse observations is challenging because the measurements may not uniquely determine the underlying state. Diffusion-based PDE solvers address this problem through iterative sampling whereas neural operators provide deterministic one-pass predictions. We propose SCOPE (Sparse-Context Observability-aware Predictive Embeddings) to recover complete PDE fields from sparse observations by coupling full-field latent prediction with physical reconstruction. A shared decoder reconstructs fields from both predicted and complete-view representations so that representation learning is guided by both physical recovery and latent matching. We derive a quadratic risk decomposition at fixed teacher-decoder pairs showing why optimal latent prediction need not yield optimal field reconstruction. We also establish sufficient conditions for decoder improvements on complete inputs to transfer to recovery from partial observations. Experiments across five PDE settings show that SCOPE outperforms mask-aware neural operators on all ten forward and inverse tasks and achieves lower errors than those reported for diffusion-based solvers including DiffusionPDE and FunDPS. Decoder-only adaptation further improves recovery without retraining the backbone while retaining deterministic single-pass inference.

</details>


### [139] [Triadic Linear Attention: Three-Dimensional Recurrent States for Long-Context Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36529)
*Oliver Sieberling,Bharat Runwal,David Jin,Ryan Chin,Rameswar Panda,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出三元线性注意力（triadic linear attention），通过将键、第二键和值的三元外积写入三阶张量状态，以仅增加两个投影的代价将状态规模扩大E倍，在长上下文语言建模和召回任务上显著优于其他扩大状态的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN将历史上下文压缩为固定大小的记忆状态，状态大小是性能的关键因素。线性注意力通过将普通RNN的向量隐状态扩展为矩阵隐状态而表现出色，且以参数高效的方式实现（用键与值的外积写入）。作者希望进一步推广这一构造，在保持参数效率的同时更有效地扩大状态规模以提升长上下文建模性能。

Method: 提出三元线性注意力：将键、第二键和值的三元外积写入三阶（3D）张量状态，读取时用两个查询分别与两个键轴做收缩。一个E维的第二键使状态规模扩大E倍，而只增加两个投影。该方法与数据依赖遗忘、delta规则以及分块并行训练相兼容。

Result: 将三元线性注意力应用于Gated DeltaNet和标量门控线性注意力，在长上下文语言建模和召回任务上大幅提升，优于其他扩大状态的替代方案。

Conclusion: 通过三阶张量状态与三元外积写入的推广式构造，可以在参数高效的前提下有效扩大记忆状态，从而显著提升长上下文语言建模与召回能力。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks (RNNs) compress the historical context into a memory state of fixed size, thus allowing for constant-time inference. The memory state size is a crucial factor in their performance, as exemplified by the strong performance and resurgence of linear attention, which extends the vector-valued hidden states of ordinary RNNs to matrix-valued hidden states. Crucially, linear attention does so in a parameter-efficient way, in particular by using an outer product of the key and value vectors to write to the matrix-valued hidden state. We generalize this construction and propose triadic linear attention, which writes the triadic outer product of a key, a second key, and a value, into a third-order (i.e., 3D) tensor state, and reads from it by contracting both key axes with two queries. An $E$-dimensional second key thus yields an $E$-fold increase in state size while adding only two projections. Triadic linear attention is compatible with data-dependent forgetting, the delta rule, and chunkwise-parallel training. Applied to Gated DeltaNet and scalar-gated linear attention, triadic linear attention substantially improves long-context language modeling and recall, outperforming alternatives that enlarge the state.

</details>


### [140] [Interactive-Policy Distillation with Bidirectional Propose-and-Verify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36546)
*Shutong Wu,Xiwen Chen,Brendan Rappazzo,Daiheng Zhang,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Interactive-Policy Distillation (IPD)，通过双向 propose-and-verify 状态机让师生交替担任提议者与验证者，生成混合来源轨迹并施加来源分离损失，缓解 on-policy distillation 的 teacher unanchoring 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation 在学生自生成轨迹上用教师密集 token 级反馈，但存在 teacher unanchoring 问题：学生推理轨迹漂移过远，教师被查询到其几乎不会访问的状态，从而给出不可靠监督。

Method: IPD 对学生 rollout 施加自适应教师干预；在双向 propose-and-verify 状态机下，学生与教师交替扮演提议者与验证者，协同生成混合来源轨迹；再按 token 来源施加不同监督（source-split loss）。同时设计融合推理引擎：在同一服务实例中共托管两个模型，使用独立 KV cache，并实例化状态机来分发、收集与处理请求，以提升交错双模型 rollout 效率。

Result: 在数学推理任务及多种师生模型组合上，IPD 训练的学生优于 OPD，且数据效率更高。将 Qwen3-30B-A3B 蒸馏到 Qwen3-1.7B-Base 时，IPD 相比 OPD 在基准平均准确率上提升 +3.28 mean@8 与 +3.28 best@8。IPD 仅用约 1/4 训练样本与步数即超过 OPD 全数据集训练一个 epoch 的效果。还考察了不同损失变体与 takeover/handback 配置的影响，并验证了 IPD 在不同训练数据上的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: IPD 不仅在性能上优于 OPD，还成为 on-policy 与 off-policy 范式之间的统一桥梁，并具备更高数据效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model on its self-generated trajectories with dense token-level teacher feedback. However, naive OPD may suffer from teacher unanchoring, where the student's reasoning trajectory drifts far from the teacher, causing the teacher to be queried on states it would hardly visit and thus provide unreliable supervision. We propose Interactive-Policy Distillation (IPD), which applies adaptive teacher intervention to the student rollout. Under a bidirectional propose-and-verify state machine, the student and teacher alternately exchange their roles as proposer and verifier, and collaboratively generate mixed-source trajectories. Then different supervisions are applied according to the source of each token. This bidirectional propose-and-verify mechanism and the source-split loss make IPD not only a more performant distillation method, but also a unified bridge between on-policy and off-policy paradigms. To make the interleaved dual-model rollouts more efficient, we also design a dedicated fused inference engine that co-hosts both models in one serving instance with separate KV caches and instantiates the state machine model to distribute, collect, and process requests. On math reasoning tasks and across multiple teacher-student model pairs, student models trained with IPD not only outperform those trained with OPD, but also demonstrate higher data efficiency. Specifically, when distilling Qwen3-30B-A3B into Qwen3-1.7B-Base, IPD brings a +3.28 mean@8 and a +3.28 best@8 benchmark-averaged accuracy improvement compared with OPD. Besides, IPD only consumes about 1/4 of the training examples and steps to outperform OPD trained on the whole training dataset for one epoch. We also investigate the impact of different loss variants and takeover / handback configurations, and demonstrate the robustness of IPD on different training data.

</details>


### [141] [SERA: Scale-Equalized Rollout Allocation for Maximum Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36552)
*Zihao Chen,Fanxiang Xiong,Hongran Ren,Xuefeng Bai,Zhongxiang Dai,Kehai Chen,Zhiguo Zhang,Zhiyong Wang,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对最大似然强化学习(MaxRL)在有限采样预算下梯度估计被成功概率相关因子衰减的问题，提出SERA方法，通过重分配采样预算来均衡缩放因子，提升与真实似然梯度的一致性和多解覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MaxRL在有限采样预算下，其估计器会以依赖每个prompt成功概率和采样次数的因子衰减该prompt的似然梯度。在均匀采样分配下，共同的采样次数无法补偿这种成功相关的衰减，导致低成功率prompt被更强衰减，扭曲了它们对期望聚合梯度的相对贡献。

Method: 作者提出SERA（Scale-Equalized Rollout Allocation），将固定采样预算重新分配，以近似均衡这些有限采样缩放因子。基于对有限采样如何扭曲prompt级似然梯度的理论分析，将分配问题formulate为固定预算的max-min问题，推导其连续松弛的水线（waterline）解，并引入多重性校正以消除因异质采样次数带来的额外prompt加权。

Result: 实验显示：在受控的ImageNet设定下，SERA与精确似然梯度有更强的一致性；在迷宫导航和数学推理任务上，在匹配训练采样预算下，SERA相比MaxRL具有更好的多样本解覆盖（multi-sample solution coverage）。

Conclusion: SERA通过均衡有限采样下的成功相关缩放因子，纠正了均匀分配对低成功率prompt的梯度扭曲，在理论上给出最优分配方案并在多个任务上验证了其优于MaxRL的效果。

Abstract: Maximum Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (MaxRL) targets prompt-wise log-success and has shown strong performance on reasoning tasks. Under finite rollout budgets, however, the estimator used by MaxRL attenuates each prompt's likelihood gradient by a factor that depends on its success probability and rollout count. Under uniform rollout allocation, the common rollout count fails to compensate for success-dependent attenuation, leaving low-success prompts more strongly attenuated and distorting their relative contributions to the expected aggregate gradient. We introduce SERA (Scale-Equalized Rollout Allocation), which redistributes a fixed rollout budget to approximately equalize these finite-rollout scaling factors. Building on our theoretical analysis of how finite rollouts distort prompt-wise likelihood gradients, we formulate the allocation as a fixed-budget max--min problem, derive a waterline solution to its continuous relaxation, and introduce a multiplicity correction to remove the additional prompt weighting induced by heterogeneous rollout counts. Experiments show stronger alignment with exact likelihood gradients in a controlled ImageNet setting and improved multi-sample solution coverage over MaxRL on maze navigation and mathematical reasoning under matched training rollout budgets.

</details>


### [142] [HiTS-CL: A Continual Learning Framework for Long-Horizon Temporal Knowledge Graph Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36559)
*Yansong Liu,Rui Liu,Yuan Zuo,Hongwei Zhao,Da Fu,Fuwei Zhang,Fuzhen Zhuang,Yong Chen,Zhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将外推式时序知识图谱推理重新定义为流式快照上的持续学习问题，提出与骨干无关的HiTS-CL框架，通过持续微调、多教师自适应蒸馏和选择性记忆，提升长时程外推精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多在时间线早期前缀上一次性训练，再用冻结模型预测未来所有时间戳。但目标数据流是非平稳的：新实体和事实不断出现，时间依赖会跨阶段漂移，历史信号需要在线刷新。固定前缀训练导致模型过时并在长时程上性能退化，与真正的外推需求不匹配。

Method: 将外推式TKGR表述为流式快照上的持续学习，并提出HiTS-CL框架，同时处理三方面需求：1）通过持续微调跟踪当前动态；2）通过多教师自适应蒸馏保留稳定知识；3）通过最近且频繁事实的选择性记忆保留周期性历史证据。框架与具体TKGR骨干解耦。

Result: 在五个代表性TKGR骨干上集成HiTS-CL，并在四个基准数据集上评估。实验显示HiTS-CL持续提升外推精度，缓解长时程性能退化，并优于强持续学习基线（包括近期提出的时序知识图谱方法）。

Conclusion: 固定前缀训练协议不适合非平稳的外推场景。将外推式TKGR视为持续学习，并联合处理当前动态、稳定知识和周期性历史证据，可有效提升长时程外推表现。HiTS-CL是一种通用的、可与多种骨干兼容的持续学习框架。

Abstract: Extrapolative temporal knowledge graph reasoning (TKGR) predicts future facts from historical snapshots. Most existing methods train once on an early prefix of the timeline and then use a frozen model for all future timestamps. We argue that this fixed-prefix protocol is misaligned with extrapolation. It learns from a static prefix, whereas the target stream is non-stationary: new entities and facts emerge, temporal dependencies shift across regimes, and recurring historical signals must be refreshed online. As a result, models trained only on early snapshots become outdated and degrade over long horizons. We address this mismatch by formulating extrapolative TKGR as continual learning over streaming snapshots. Under this view, effective extrapolation must jointly handle current dynamics, stable knowledge, and recurring historical evidence. Based on these requirements, we propose History-enhanced Two-Step Continual Learning (HiTS-CL), a backbone-agnostic continual learning framework for extrapolative TKGR.  HiTS-CL tracks current dynamics via continual fine-tuning, preserves stable knowledge via multi-teacher adaptive distillation, and retains recurring historical evidence via a selective memory of recent and frequent facts. We integrate HiTS-CL into five representative TKGR backbones and evaluate it on four benchmark datasets. HiTS-CL consistently improves extrapolation accuracy, reduces long-horizon degradation, and outperforms strong continual-learning baselines, including a recent method for temporal knowledge graphs. Source code and data are available at https://github.com/liuyansong98/HiTS-CL.

</details>


### [143] [Sharp Convergence and Sampling Trade-offs for Riemannian Diffusion under Nonnegative Ricci Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36568)
*Yuhao Liu,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将分数离散化与布朗运动模拟分离的通用框架，证明在非负Ricci曲率下，黎曼扩散模型仅需 $\tilde{O}(d/\epsilon^2)$ 次分数评估即可达到 $\epsilon^2$ 的KL散度，匹配欧氏情形收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黎曼扩散模型的收敛保证通常需要 $\tilde{O}(\mathrm{poly}(d,T)/\epsilon^2)$ 次分数评估，对维度的依赖可能不利，缺乏与欧氏扩散模型相当的复杂度刻画。

Method: 构建通用框架，将分数离散化与布朗运动模拟解耦，允许在每次分数评估之间执行多步测地随机游走；在非负Ricci曲率假设及精确布朗运动模拟预言机下分析收敛性。

Result: 证明达到 $\epsilon^2$ KL散度只需 $\tilde{O}(d/\epsilon^2)$ 次分数评估；用 $\tilde{O}(d^4T/\epsilon^2)$ 步测地随机游走可将漂移布朗运动近似到 $\epsilon$ 全变差误差；组合后得到 $\tilde{O}(d/\epsilon^2)$ 次分数评估与 $\tilde{O}(d^4T/\epsilon^2)$ 步随机游走的采样方案。

Conclusion: 结果为黎曼扩散模型的收敛性与采样复杂度提供了更精细的刻画，并支持在连续分数评估之间执行多步随机游走的策略。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative models, with recent extensions from Euclidean spaces to Riemannian manifolds. However, existing convergence guarantees for Riemannian diffusion models typically require $\tilde{O}(\mathrm{poly}(d,T)/ε^2)$ score evaluations, with potentially unfavorable dependence on the dimension. In this work, we develop a general framework that separates score discretization from Brownian-motion simulation and allows multiple geodesic random-walk steps per score evaluation. Under nonnegative Ricci curvature assumption and an exact Brownian-motion simulation oracle, we show that $\tilde{O}(d/ε^2)$ score evaluations suffice to achieve an $ε^2$ KL divergence from the target distribution, matching the existing convergence rate of Euclidean diffusion models. We further show that $\tilde{O}(d^4T/ε^2)$ geodesic random-walk steps suffice to approximate the required drifted Brownian motion to $ε$ total variation error. Combining these results yields a sampling scheme with $\tilde{O}(d/ε^2)$ score evaluations and $\tilde{O}(d^4T/ε^2)$ geodesic random-walk steps, motivating multiple random-walk steps between consecutive score evaluations. Our results provide a sharper characterization of the convergence and sampling complexity of Riemannian diffusion models.

</details>


### [144] [From Checkpoint Variation to Selection Gains in Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36569)
*Yupeng Chang,Wenxuan Zhang,Yuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将检查点选择视为有限信息决策问题，通过固定训练轨迹并变化验证预算，分离并测量三个不同的经验命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调中训练产生多个检查点但只保留一个，然而固定预算的比较无法区分三个问题：更多验证数据是否改善选择、选择规则是否优于验证损失选择、是否优于直接保留最终检查点。

Method: 固定已完成的训练轨迹、候选检查点和独立测试项，变化验证预算，分别测量额外验证数据的改善、相对NLL选择的增益、相对最终检查点的增益。基于60条数学SFT轨迹和19种配置，以及12条常识轨迹的跨域复现。

Result: 验证预算从32增至305-313例时，生成准确率选择和检查点一致性选择的独立测试准确率分别提升0.32pp和0.29pp（95% CI分别为[0.10,0.56]和[0.11,0.50]）。全预算下两种生成式规则分别比NLL选择高0.71和0.85pp，但相对最终检查点的增益未确定。常识域复现显示类似分离。

Conclusion: 从更多验证数据获益、优于NLL选择、优于最终检查点是三个不同的经验命题，需要分别提供证据。

Abstract: Checkpoint selection is a routine decision in supervised fine-tuning (SFT): training produces multiple checkpoints, but only one is retained. Yet fixed-budget comparisons do not by themselves distinguish three empirical claims: whether more validation data improve checkpoint selection, whether a selection rule outperforms validation-loss selection, and whether it improves over simply retaining the final checkpoint. We therefore treat checkpoint selection as a finite-information decision problem. Holding completed training trajectories, candidate checkpoints, and independent test items fixed, we vary the validation budget and separately measure improvement from additional validation data, gain over negative log-likelihood (NLL) selection, and gain over the final checkpoint. Across 60 mathematical SFT trajectories and 19 configurations, increasing the validation budget from 32 to 305-313 examples raises independent-test accuracy by 0.32 percentage points (pp) for generated-accuracy selection and 0.29 pp for checkpoint agreement, with 95% configuration-bootstrap CIs of [0.10, 0.56] and [0.11, 0.50], respectively. At the full validation budget, the two generation-based rules outperform matched NLL selection by 0.71 and 0.85 pp, respectively, while their gains over the final checkpoint remain unresolved. A cross-domain replication on 12 newly trained Commonsense trajectories shows the same qualitative separation: increasing the validation budget from 32 to 1,024 questions improves generated-accuracy and checkpoint-agreement selection by 0.87 and 0.27 pp, while gains over the final checkpoint again remain unresolved. Together, these results show that benefiting from more validation data, outperforming NLL selection, and outperforming the final checkpoint are distinct empirical claims that require separate evidence.

</details>


### [145] [Factorized Scheduling Principle: Learning Interpretable and Transferable Policies via Structured Additive Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36578)
*Hong Je-Gal,Hyun-Suk Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子化调度原则(FSP)框架，通过将系统状态表示为条件分布并把全局调度原则分解为可识别的加性一元与成对组件，学习可解释且可迁移的调度规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调度问题通常归结为基于候选状态分配优先级分数并选择最高排名项。作者希望学习既具可解释性又具可迁移性的调度规则，以克服传统方法在可解释性和跨规模泛化上的不足。

Method: FSP框架将系统状态表示为条件分布，并将全局调度原则分解为具有可识别性约束的加性一元和成对组件；该原则通过基于策略的目标函数结合定义在条件分布上的时序差分信号进行学习，部署时保持简单的基于优先级的结构。

Result: 在合成和真实调度任务上的实验表明，FSP框架在性能、可解释性以及跨不同系统规模的零样本泛化方面均表现强劲。

Conclusion: FSP框架能够学习可解释且可迁移的调度规则，验证了因子化加性分解结合策略与时序差分学习在调度问题上的有效性，并具备良好的零样本跨规模泛化能力。

Abstract: Scheduling problems arise from repeatedly selecting one item from a set of candidates based on their states. These problems often reduce to assigning priority scores and choosing the highest-ranked item. In this work, we propose a factorized scheduling principle (FSP) framework to learn interpretable and transferable scheduling rules. The FSP framework represents system states as condition distributions and decomposes a global scheduling principle into additive univariate and pairwise components with identifiability constraints. The scheduling principle enables the framework to maintain a simple priority-based structure during deployment. This principle is learned by using a policy-based objective combined with a temporal-difference signal defined on the condition distribution. Experiments on synthetic and realistic scheduling tasks demonstrate the FSP framework's strong performance, interpretability, and zero-shot generalization across different system scales.

</details>


### [146] [Making Analog Training Scale: Co-Designing Mapping, Optimizer, and Converters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36584)
*Zhaoxian Wu,Tayfun Gokmen,Omobayode Fagbohungbe,T. Patrick Xiao,Tianyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合精度训练框架与系统-算法协同设计，使模拟内存计算可扩展训练至1.23亿参数Transformer，验证损失缩放接近数字训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟内存计算（AIMC）可直接在权重存储处执行矩阵运算，是模型训练的替代方案。但硬件非理想性（有限动态范围与写入粒度、有限转换器分辨率、噪声且不对称的权重更新）使其难以扩展到现代深度模型训练。

Method: 基于"梯度累积对精度与舍入误差敏感"的洞察，采用混合精度：前向与反向矩阵乘法在模拟域执行，权重梯度在数字域计算。系统-算法协同设计包括：协同优化权重映射以保证物理与逻辑权重分布良态；将预处理优化器与阈值触发的开环脉冲耦合以稳定训练轨迹；对齐转换器动态范围以抑制量化误差。通过基于电化学RAM测量校准的硬件仿真评估。

Result: 框架将Transformer训练扩展至1.23亿参数，验证损失随参数量N的缩放为L∝N^-0.231，与数字训练的L∝N^-0.238相当。

Conclusion: 混合精度配合系统-算法协同设计可有效缓解AIMC硬件非理想性，使其训练缩放行为接近数字训练，支持中等规模现代深度模型训练。

Abstract: Analog in-memory computing (AIMC) offers an alternative for model training by executing matrix operations directly where weights are stored. However, scaling AIMC to train modern deep models remains an open challenge due to severe hardware non-idealities, including physical weights with finite dynamic range and write granularity, analog-digital converters with finite resolution, and noisy and asymmetric updates. Guided by the insight that gradient accumulation is sensitive to precision and rounding errors, we adopt a mixed-precision training paradigm: executing forward and backward matrix multiplications in the analog domain while computing weight gradients in the digital domain. To enable scalable training, we present a holistic system-algorithm co-design that co-optimizes weight mapping to ensure well-conditioned physical and logical weight profiles, couples a preconditioned optimizer with threshold-triggered open-loop pulsing to stabilize training trajectories, and aligns converter dynamic ranges to suppress quantization errors. Evaluated via hardware-calibrated architectural simulations calibrated with electrochemical RAM measurements, our framework scales Transformer training up to $123\text{M}$ parameters with validation loss scaling as $L\propto N^{-0.231}$, where $N$ is the parameter count, comparable to $L\propto N^{-0.238}$ for digital training.

</details>


### [147] [Learned Reporting Preferences in RLVR Can Conflict with the Current Request](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36587)
*Yupeng Chang,Wenxuan Zhang,Yuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出，仅用与训练约定一致的评测无法揭示RLVR带来的报告偏好偏移，并主张同时评估"当前请求遵从度"与"实际作答正确性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR评测多采用与训练约定匹配的方式，只能反映约定下的任务准确率，无法判断强化某一报告约定是否会损害初始策略本已具备的、对另一种请求的遵从能力，因此需要更细致的评估设计来分离训练后的不同行为后果。

Method: 作者采用交叉设计：在两种报告约定下训练匹配策略，并用同一初始策略作为共享参照，在两种"当前请求"下评测各策略；辅以受控干预（仅改变监督目标中的最终答案标记）和独立人工校准，在GSM8K等设置上考察Qwen2.5-7B等多个训练种子与多种改写请求。

Result: 在GSM8K上，boxed格式RLVR使Qwen2.5-7B回答中包含所请求hash格式内容的比例，相对95.45%的初始基线在五个种子中的四个下降35.33–74.37个百分点，第五个反而提升2.50点；四个劣化种子中，几乎所有遗漏所请求内容的回答都保留boxed约定，并在两种固定改写下同样劣化。仅改监督目标的最终答案标记，即可在三个种子上反转模型偏好的报告约定。在三个人工校准设置中，约定敏感评分器下的增益高于实际作答正确性的增益。

Conclusion: 研究区分出三种不同的后训练结果：习得的报告偏好、当前请求遵从度、实际作答正确性；约定匹配准确率不足以完整刻画后训练行为，应把当前请求遵从度与约定匹配任务准确率并列评估。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a prominent approach for improving language-model performance on reasoning tasks using automatically checked answers. Yet convention-matched evaluation cannot reveal whether reinforcing one reporting convention reduces adherence to a different request that the initial policy already follows. To test this, we train matched policies under two reporting conventions and evaluate each policy under both current requests, using the same initial policy as a shared reference. We complement this crossed design with controlled interventions and independent human calibration. On GSM8K, boxed-format RLVR reduces the fraction of Qwen2.5-7B responses containing the requested hash-format payload by 35.33--74.37 percentage points relative to a 95.45% initial baseline in four of five training seeds; the fifth improves by 2.50 points. In the four deteriorating runs, almost every response that omits the requested payload instead retains the trained boxed convention, and the same four seeds deteriorate under two fixed paraphrases. Changing only the final-answer marker in supervised targets reverses which reporting convention the model prefers across three seeds, providing controlled evidence that this preference is learnable. Across three settings with independent human calibration, gains under a convention-sensitive scorer exceed the corresponding gains in committed-answer correctness, i.e., the correctness of the answer the model actually commits to. Together, these results separate three distinct post-training outcomes: learned reporting preference, current-request adherence, and committed-answer correctness. They show that convention-matched accuracy alone does not fully characterize post-training behavior and motivate evaluating current-request adherence alongside convention-matched task accuracy.

</details>


### [148] [Act First, Reason Later: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents via Reference-Conditioned Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36608)
*Zubin Zheng,Jiahao Wu,Shaofeng Zhang,Zhirui Zhang,Yew-Soon Ong,Shengcai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ActFirst-OPD，一种"先行动、后推理"的在线策略蒸馏框架，将环境交互与完整响应生成解耦，在保持或提升成功率的同时显著加速多轮智能体训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 think-then-act 的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在每次短动作前都需要冗长推理，拖慢环境切换与经验收集；若直接生成动作虽可减少延迟，却会降低 rollout 质量。

Method: 学生模型利用当前交互上下文与参考下一观测，通过参考条件逆动力学推断并执行动作；当实际转移偏离参考轨迹时，切换为自主的下一动作预测。随后从收集到的交互上下文中异步生成完整的 think-then-act 响应，用于 token 级教师监督。

Result: 在 0.6B、1.7B、4B 参数规模的 Qwen3 学生上：相对 Vanilla OPD，ALFWorld 平均墙钟训练加速 2.3×、WebShop 1.8×、ScienceWorld 4.9×；在九个基准-模型设置中有八个的平均任务成功率持平或超过所有对比 OPD 基线。

Conclusion: 推理无需阻塞行动，ActFirst-OPD 证明多轮智能体蒸馏中可以在不牺牲（甚至提升）任务表现的前提下大幅提升训练效率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains multi-turn language agents with dense teacher supervision on student-generated responses. However, standard think-then-act rollouts require lengthy reasoning before each short action, delaying environment transitions and experience collection. Generating actions directly reduces this delay but can degrade rollout quality. To address this, we propose ActFirst-OPD, an act-first, reason-later training framework that decouples environment interaction from full-response generation. The student infers and executes actions through reference-conditioned inverse dynamics using its current interaction context and a reference next observation, and switches to autonomous next-action prediction when the resulting transition deviates from the reference trajectory. From the collected interaction contexts, the student asynchronously generates full think-then-act responses for token-level teacher supervision. Experiments across 0.6B-, 1.7B-, and 4B-parameter Qwen3 students show that ActFirst-OPD achieves average wall-clock training speedups of $2.3\times$ on ALFWorld, $1.8\times$ on WebShop, and $4.9\times$ on ScienceWorld over Vanilla OPD. It matches or exceeds all compared OPD baselines in mean task success rate across eight of nine benchmark-model settings. These results demonstrate that reasoning need not block acting during multi-turn agent distillation.

</details>


### [149] [Communication-Efficient Agnostic Federated Learning via Faster Convergence and Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36610)
*Haomin Bai,Junyan Sun,Sifan Yang,Bo Xue,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对无关联联邦学习（AFL）的通信瓶颈，提出通过更快收敛减少同步轮数、通过压缩减少每轮通信开销的方法，包括 AFL-BR 与 AFL-Com 两种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFL 需要在 m 个异构 worker 上可靠运行，但通信是瓶颈；希望同时降低同步轮数和每轮通信成本。

Method: 提出 AFL-BR，用带 KL 散度和分块重启的在线镜像上升更新 worker 对偶权重；在其上提出 AFL-Com，对双加权梯度做双向压缩与误差反馈（EF），以在时变权重下直接控制聚合压缩误差。

Result: AFL-BR 达到 O((log m)^{1/4}T^{-1/8}) 的稳定性速率，将收敛所需同步轮数对 m 的依赖从多项式降到对数；AFL-Com 在一般 δ-近似压缩器下达到 O((δ^{-1}+(log m)^{1/4})T^{-1/8})，并在共享随机性（SR）下的加性幂等压缩器上将 δ 依赖从 δ^{-1} 改进到 δ^{-1/2}；总通信复杂度在 Top-k 下降低 (log m)^{1/4} 倍，在 Rand-k 与 SR 下降低 (log m)^{1/2} 倍。

Conclusion: AFL-Com 在合适压缩水平下保持与 AFL-BR 相同收敛速率，同时降低每轮通信成本，实验验证了同步与通信效率的提升。

Abstract: Agnostic federated learning (AFL) seeks a model that performs reliably across $m$ heterogeneous workers, but communication remains a bottleneck. We improve communication efficiency by reducing the number of synchronization rounds via faster convergence and the communication cost per round via compression. We first propose AFL-BR, which updates the dual weights over workers using online mirror ascent with KL divergence and blockwise restarts. It achieves an $O((\log m)^{1/4}T^{-1/8})$ stationarity rate after $T$ update rounds, reducing the $m$-dependence of the synchronization rounds required for convergence from polynomial to logarithmic order. Building on AFL-BR, we develop AFL-Com by applying bidirectional compression with error feedback (EF). Instead of compressing local gradients, workers apply EF to their dual-weighted gradients, enabling direct control of the aggregated compression error under time-varying weights. We then establish an $O((δ^{-1}+(\log m)^{1/4})T^{-1/8})$ stationarity rate for AFL-Com under general $δ$-approximate compressors and improve the $δ$-dependence from $δ^{-1}$ to $δ^{-1/2}$ for additive-and-idempotent compressors with shared randomness (SR). With suitable compression levels, AFL-Com retains the same convergence rate as AFL-BR at a lower per-round communication cost, yielding reductions in total communication complexity by factors of $(\log m)^{1/4}$ with Top-$k$ and $(\log m)^{1/2}$ with Rand-$k$ and SR. Experiments validate the improved synchronization and communication efficiency of our methods.

</details>


### [150] [Do LLMs Really Forget? Hidden-State Leakage in Model Unlearning and How to Fix it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36612)
*Hadi Reisizadeh,Jiajun Ruan,Sijia Liu,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示现有LLM遗忘评估存在"遗忘幻觉"：即使输出层面无泄漏，敏感信息仍编码在隐藏表示中；提出PARS方法在表示层面进行对抗性抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘研究主要在输出层面评估，模型看似抑制了敏感内容。但作者认为这种评估可能造成"遗忘幻觉"：输出级泄漏消除并不代表信息真正被抹除，敏感信息可能仍留存于模型的隐藏表示中，这会带来潜在的安全风险。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明输出抑制与表示擦除存在根本性分离——可让解码器对敏感方向任意不敏感，使输出泄漏趋于零，而隐藏表示仍保留信息。2) 实证验证：在各transformer层的隐藏状态上训练生成式探针解码器，逐层测量信息泄漏。3) 提出Probe-Adversarial Representation Suppression (PARS)，一种对抗性地最小化隐藏表示中可提取信息的遗忘目标。

Result: 在TOFU、MUSE、WMDP三个基准及多个最先进遗忘方法上，即使标准输出级指标显示遗忘成功，隐藏表示中仍可大量恢复敏感信息。PARS在对抗性探测与重新学习攻击下提供显著更强的擦除保证，优于所有评估的基线方法。

Conclusion: 现有遗忘范式存在根本缺陷；真正的LLM遗忘不仅需控制模型输出，还需控制隐藏表示中编码的信息。

Abstract: Unlearning in large language models (LLMs) is typically evaluated at the output level, where a model appears to suppress sensitive or undesirable content. In this work, we show that such evaluations can create an illusion of forgetting: even when output-level leakage is eliminated, sensitive information can remain encoded in the model's hidden representations. We first provide a theoretical analysis establishing a fundamental separation between output suppression and representational erasure. Specifically, we show that the decoder can be made arbitrarily insensitive to sensitive directions, driving output-level leakage to zero, while the hidden representations retain the underlying information. To empirically validate this phenomenon, we train generative probe decoders on hidden states across transformer layers, enabling layer-wise measurement of information leakage. Across three widely used benchmarks, TOFU, MUSE, and WMDP, and state-of-the-art unlearning methods, we find that substantial sensitive information remains recoverable from hidden representations, even when standard output-level metrics indicate successful unlearning. To address this gap, we propose Probe-Adversarial Representation Suppression (PARS), an unlearning objective that adversarially minimizes the extractable information from hidden representations. PARS directly targets representational leakage and provides significantly stronger guarantees of erasure under adversarial probing and relearning attacks, outperforming all evaluated baselines. Our results highlight a fundamental limitation of existing unlearning paradigms and suggest that true forgetting in LLMs requires controlling not only model outputs, but also the information encoded in hidden representations. Codes are available at https://github.com/OptimAI-Lab/HiddenStateUnlearning.

</details>


### [151] [Selective Elicitation as a Commercial Influence Channel: A Reproducible Synthetic Shopping-Agent Stress Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36614)
*Jiapeng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 商业激励未必需要通过改变推荐排序算法来影响推荐结果，它可以通过影响助手提出哪个偏好问题来起作用。作者在小型合成实验框架中验证了这一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注广告激励如何直接影响推荐排序或最终推荐结果，但忽视了激励可能作用于"提问环节"——即助手向用户询问哪个偏好问题。作者希望在受控的合成环境中使这一区别可被实验观测。

Method: 构建一个刻意的、小规模合成任务：每个任务含两个产品、三个可验证数值属性、一个价格上限和一个私有固定偏好向量。模拟诚实用户回答一个成对问题；另一个推荐器接收产品和该答案但不知赞助分配。对比三种提问方式：中立问题、软性商业指令、明确对抗性指令（询问赞助方优势而省略对手优势）。共40个留出的赞助分配案例（对应20个不同目录-偏好情境）。

Result: 软性指令不改变任何选择。对抗性指令相对中立提问使赞助产品选择率提升0.30，平均合成效用下降0.0547（95%情境自助区间[-0.0828, -0.0291]），对某语言模型推荐器成立；固定贝叶斯推荐器表现类似；第二个模型在全部120个冻结问答输入上选择一致。终答一致性评审认为20个抽样对抗答案全部"一致"，尽管其中5个合成遗憾超过0.05；单独的问题覆盖维度揭示了其一侧化提问。稳健部分偏好证书在合成效用下仍有效，但仅认证40个对抗案例中的16个，并不优于直接问中立问题。

Conclusion: 该结果既不能说明广告激励下的典型行为，也不能说明对真实消费者的影响，仅为受控合成环境下的机制验证。

Abstract: A commercial incentive need not enter the final ranking algorithm to affect a shopping assistant's recommendation: it may instead influence which preference question the assistant asks. We make this distinction experimentally observable in a deliberately small, synthetic setting. Each task has two products, three verified numerical attributes, a price limit, and a private fixed preference vector. An honest simulated user answers one pairwise question. A separate recommender receives the products and this answer but not the sponsorship assignment. We contrast a neutral question, a soft commercial instruction, and an explicitly adversarial instruction to ask about the sponsor's advantage while omitting the rival's advantage. Across 40 held-out sponsorship-assignment cases (20 distinct catalog-preference contexts), the soft instruction changes no selections. The targeted instruction raises sponsored selection by 0.30 and reduces mean synthetic utility by 0.0547 relative to neutral questioning (95% context-bootstrap interval [-0.0828, -0.0291]) for one language-model recommender. A fixed Bayesian recommender shows a similar effect; a second model makes the same choices on all 120 frozen question-answer inputs. A terminal-answer consistency judge rates all 20 sampled targeted answers consistent, although five have synthetic regret above 0.05; a separate question-coverage dimension flags their one-sided elicitation. A robust partial-preference certificate remains valid under the stipulated synthetic utility but certifies only 16 of 40 targeted cases and is not better than asking a neutral question directly. These results establish neither typical behavior under advertising incentives nor effects on actual consumers.

</details>


### [152] [SemPSG: A Semantic Channel-Aware Foundation Model for Polysomnography Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36619)
*Junyu Chen,Chenxi Liu,Shiqin Tang,Hao Miao,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Hongbin Liu,Gaofeng Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SemPSG，一种语义通道感知的基础模型，通过显式建模通道身份的生理语义，实现异构PSG数据的可迁移表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSG整合多种生理信号，但不同中心的通道配置异构，给可迁移表示学习带来挑战。现有基础模型主要关注生理建模或时序学习，而把通道身份当作固定结构索引，忽略了信号模态与参考配置所编码的生理语义。

Method: SemPSG显式表示通道身份的生理语义，并将其融入信号表示学习与通道聚合。模型包含两部分：语义条件时间序列编码器，用于捕获信号特有的时序动态与跨信号交互；多视图图像编码器，从同一生理记录中提取互补的时频与形态模式。

Result: 在睡眠与健康相关任务上评估，包括睡眠分期、睡眠呼吸障碍分析、疾病预测、认知与情绪识别及人口学估计，一致优于通用时序基础模型和PSG专用基础模型，并在不同通道配置的异构数据集上展现泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模通道语义，SemPSG实现了对异构PSG配置的灵活建模，提升了多任务性能与跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Polysomnography (PSG) integrates multiple physiological signals to provide a comprehensive characterization of human sleep, yet its heterogeneous channel configurations across centers pose substantial challenges for transferable representation learning. Existing foundation models mainly focus on physiological modeling or temporal learning, while channel identity is often treated as a fixed structural index, overlooking the physiological semantics encoded by signal modality and reference configuration. To this end, we propose SemPSG, a Semantic channel-aware foundation model for heterogeneous PSG analysis. SemPSG explicitly represents the physiological semantics of channel identity and incorporates them into both signal representation learning and channel aggregation, enabling flexible modeling across diverse data configurations. Specifically, a semantic-conditioned time-series encoder captures signal-specific temporal dynamics and cross-signal interactions, while a multi-view image encoder extracts complementary time-frequency and morphological patterns from the same physiological recordings. We evaluate SemPSG on sleep and health-related tasks, including sleep staging, sleep-disorder breathing analysis, disease prediction, cognition and emotion recognition, and demographic estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over both general-purpose time series foundation models and PSG-specific foundation models, together with generalization across heterogeneous datasets across diverse channel configurations.

</details>


### [153] [Physics-Aware Machine Unlearning for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36633)
*Mohammad Zakaria Haider,Muhammad Nadeem,Mohammad Ashiqur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理引导的梯度上升式机器遗忘方法，将遗忘信号与信息物理系统的物理残差耦合，使遗忘过程中的权重更新被引导至物理可行区域，从而在去除毒化的同时恢复物理合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的信息物理系统中，机器遗忘不能只追求"忘掉"恶意/被投毒的行为，还必须保证更新后的模型满足物理约束。传统梯度上升可能将模型推向一个虽遗忘却仍违反域约束的任意解，因此需要一种能够同时兼顾遗忘与物理合规的方法。

Method: 将遗忘信号与目标信息物理系统的物理残差耦合，构成物理引导的梯度上升机器遗忘方法。物理残差充当梯度上升过程中的"安全围栏"，使模型在远离被投毒行为盆地的同时被导向符合物理约束的权重区域，而非随机替代解。

Result: 在IEEE 34节点配电系统上，以两个基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的分布式能源控制器为对象，通过高保真OpenDSS潮流协同仿真进行验证，并与四种基线（朴素梯度上升、精确遗忘、SISA、完全重训练）对比。结果表明，所提方法能同时去除毒化并恢复物理合规性。

Conclusion: 物理引导的机器遗忘能够兼顾毒化去除与物理合规，这对安全关键信息物理系统的安全部署至关重要。

Abstract: This paper proposes a physics-guided gradient-ascent-based machine unlearning method that couples the forgetting signal with the physical residual of the target cyber-physical systems, ensuring that weight updates during unlearning are steered toward physically feasible regions of the weight space. The physics residual acts as a safety fence during gradient ascent: the model is steered away from the poisoned behavioral basin and simultaneously toward physics-compliant territory, rather than toward an arbitrary alternative that may still violate domain constraints. We evaluate the proposed method against four baselines: naive gradient ascent, exact unlearning, SISA, and full retraining on an IEEE 34-bus distribution system, driven by two physics-informed neural network-based distribution energy resource controllers and validated through high-fidelity OpenDSS power-flow co-simulation. From the evaluation, we found that our proposed physics-guided model simultaneously removes poison and restores physical compliance, which are essential for the safe deployment of safety-critical cyber-physical systems

</details>


### [154] [What Makes Recurrence Effective in Looped Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36636)
*Xinlin Zhuang,Siyuan Wang,Imran Razzak,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究循环语言模型(LoopLM)中循环计算何时有益、应施加于何处以及初始状态注入方式的影响，并提出历史状态注入等改进设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoopLM通过参数共享增加计算深度，可在不增加参数的情况下扩展推理计算，但额外循环何时有益、架构选择如何影响效果尚不明确。

Method: 在训练视界之内、之下与之上设置不同推理预算，覆盖知识与推理任务，进行受控实验：(1)考察循环何时有益；(2)考察循环施加位置（层与迭代分配、输入输出层放置）；(3)考察条件注入方式，并提出历史状态注入(逐通道历史状态注入结合时间步条件)。

Result: 循环可提升超出训练视界的推理表现，但会损害知识表现，且更难推理样本未必获益更多；性能依赖层与迭代的分配，有效深度不足以预测行为；非循环输出层提升欠展开鲁棒性，输入输出层优选位置随推理预算变化；常规初始状态注入对循环深度变化鲁棒性有限。

Conclusion: 逐通道历史状态注入结合时间步条件是一种低成本且更有效的设计，能在扩展展开时更好保留知识并提升跨推理预算的鲁棒性，为变推理预算下的LoopLM设计提供实用指南。

Abstract: Looped language models (LoopLMs) increase computational depth through parameter sharing, offering a path to scale inference computation without adding parameters. However, it remains unclear when additional recurrence is beneficial and how architectural choices affect its effectiveness. Through controlled experiments, we systematically examine (1) when recurrence helps, (2) where it should be applied, and (3) how its conditioning affects performance. Our evaluation covers inference budgets below, within, and beyond the training horizon under knowledge and reasoning tasks. (1) We find that recurrence can improve reasoning beyond the training horizon while degrading knowledge performance, but harder reasoning instances do not consistently benefit more. (2) Performance also depends on how distinct layers and recurrent iterations are allocated, showing that effective depth alone is insufficient to predict behavior. Non-recurrent output layers improve robustness to under-unrolling, while the preferred placement of input and output layers varies with inference budget. (3) Finally, we find that conventional initial-state injection offers limited robustness to varying recurrence depth. We therefore propose history-state injection as an alternative, and show that channel-wise history-state injection combined with timestep conditioning offers a low-cost and more effective design, better preserving knowledge under extended unrolling while improving robustness across inference budgets. Overall, our results clarify when recurrent computation helps, where it fails, and offer practical guidelines for designing LoopLMs across variable inference budgets.

</details>


### [155] [PE-OPSD: Internalizing Prompt Enhancement into Flow-matching Models via On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36638)
*Mingfeng Lin,Chengfei Cai,Lin Xu,Chengqian Ma,Yuxiang Wei,Liang Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PE-OPSD 方法，将增强提示的作用内化到文本到图像模型中，训练后无需提示增强器即可从原始提示直接生成，兼顾保真度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户输入往往简短、欠具体，而生成模型需要详细文本条件才能可靠遵循指令。现有做法是用提示增强器在推理时改写原始提示，但这会带来额外延迟，且提示细化过程仍处于生成器之外，未被模型本身吸收。

Method: 将增强提示视为特权训练信息，提出 Prompt-Enhanced On-Policy Self-Distillation（PE-OPSD）。训练时，原始提示学生模型沿自身生成轨迹采样，增强提示教师模型在学生访问到的状态上提供向量场目标，进行同策略监督蒸馏；该过程无需额外的文本-图像配对数据。推理时移除提示增强器与教师模型，学生直接从原始提示生成。

Result: 在多种模型家族、提示增强器与基准上，PE-OPSD 在评估基线中取得最强的综合提示保真度，综合视觉吸引力也有正向提升，并保留基座模型的推理效率。

Conclusion: 增强提示的收益可以被内化到生成器本身，从而在推理阶段免除提示增强器，实现保真度提升与效率保持的统一。

Abstract: Text-to-image users often provide concise and underspecified prompts, whereas generative models benefit from detailed textual conditions for reliable instruction following. Existing systems bridge this gap with Prompt Enhancers (PEs) that rewrite raw prompts at inference time, introducing additional latency and leaving prompt elaboration external to the generator. We instead view enhanced prompts as privileged training information and ask whether their benefits can be internalized. We propose Prompt-Enhanced On-Policy Self-Distillation (PE-OPSD) for text-to-image flow-matching models. During training, a raw-prompt student follows its own generation trajectory, while an enhanced-prompt teacher provides vector-field targets at the states visited by the student. This on-policy supervision distills the behavior induced by enhanced prompts into the raw-prompt student without requiring additional text--image pairs. At inference, both the PE and teacher are removed, and the student generates directly from raw prompts. Across multiple model families, PEs, and benchmarks, PE-OPSD achieves the strongest aggregate prompt fidelity among the evaluated baselines, yields positive aggregate visual appeal gains, and retains the base-model inference efficiency.

</details>


### [156] [A Digital Simulation Toolkit for Physics-Based Generation of Realistic Experimental Scanning Tunneling Microscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36639)
*Huanhuan Zhao,Laxmi Bhurtel,Connor Vernachio,Fahmy Paiziah,Wonhee Ko,Arpan Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一种低成本、物理驱动的数字工具包，用于快速生成大量逼真的STM图像，从而为监督式去噪模型提供AI-ready训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STM具有极高空间分辨率但对噪声极为敏感。现有无监督去噪方法训练成本低，但主要关注去噪而难以恢复关键物理信息；监督方法性能更优，但瓶颈在于难以获取大量配对的干净-含噪实验图像。

Method: 构建物理驱动数字工具包：首先从选定材料体系模拟干净图像；再依据STM实验中伪影与噪声的物理特征，构造高斯电子噪声、1/f闪烁噪声、扫描线噪声、背景倾斜和机械漂移等伪影-噪声函数；最后将这些物理噪声分量叠加到模拟干净图像上，生成逼真STM图像。

Result: 该工具包可为铜(111)和铅(111)表面图像去噪生成AI-ready数据，并保留原子、缺陷与电子波。基于Cu(111)图像验证了量子干涉诱导电子波模式的下游学习分析质量。结果表明，在数字生成数据上训练的监督模型在Cu(111)图像去噪与电子波模式学习上优于基准无监督方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的数字工具包能够有效生成AI-ready数据，帮助监督模型更有效地去噪并学习电子波模式，从而促进科学发现。

Abstract: Scanning Tunneling Microscopy (STM) is a widely used tool for characterizing surfaces of materials at the atomic scale, playing a crucial role in discoveries across condensed matter physics and materials science. Despite its extreme spatial resolution, STM is one of the most sensitive microscopy techniques and is highly prone to noise. While existing unsupervised denoising methods are very cheap to train, these are primarily focused on removing the noise with minimal recovery of key physical information. While supervised methods can offer superior performance, the major bottleneck is that a large amount of paired clean-noisy experimental images is required which are impractical to obtain. Thus, we developed a low-cost physics-driven digital toolkit to rapidly generate large volume of realistic STM images. Firstly, we simulate clean images from a chosen material system. Then, with prior knowledge of the physical characteristics of the artifacts and noise present in STM experiments, we formulate several artifact-noise functions such as Gaussian electronic noise, 1/f flicker noise, scan-line noise, background tilt and mechanical drift. These physically informed noise components are then added to the simulated clean images to generate realistic STM images. We demonstrated the capability of the proposed digital toolkit to generate AI-ready data for denoising images of the (111) surfaces of copper and lead, while preserving atoms, defects, and electron waves. We also validated the quality of the downstream image analysis of learning electron wave patterns induced by quantum interference from Cu(111) images. Results show that the supervised models trained on digitally generated AI-ready data can more effectively denoise and learn electron wave patterns on Cu(111) images than benchmarked unsupervised approaches, indicating that the proposed toolkit facilitate scientific discovery.

</details>


### [157] [Inducing Process Supervision from Outcome-Only Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36641)
*Shengda Fan,Xin Cong,Zhong Zhang,Haotian Chen,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIPS：一种仅需结果监督的强化学习框架，通过生成式思维链让模型自主学习步骤级验证，低成本训练强过程奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练强PRM成本高昂：人工步骤标注难以规模化，蒙特卡洛估计计算昂贵且可能偏离步骤的内在正确性。目标是低成本获得有效PRM。

Method: 提出TIPS（Thinking-Induced Process Supervision），一种仅结果监督的强化学习框架，用于训练生成式PRM。模型生成CoT，随后输出步骤级标签与结果标签；奖励仅取决于预测结果是否匹配真实答案，用组相对优势优化整个生成回复。核心直觉：当检查中间步骤有助于判定结果时，更准确的检查会带来更准的结果判断和更高奖励，从而无需显式过程监督即可强化步骤级验证。

Result: 在数学和智能体基准、四个主干模型家族上验证有效。TIPS-Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507在ProcessBench上达到85.2 F1，仅用3.2K条结果标注轨迹，超越所有评估过的已训练PRM及GPT-5.4-Instruct、Claude-4.7-Opus等强提示式评判器，但仍落后于o1-mini。

Conclusion: 仅凭结果监督的强化学习即可高效训练出强过程奖励模型，无需人工步骤标注或昂贵的蒙特卡洛估计，为低成本构建PRM提供了新范式。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) have become a key component for LLMs, as their step-level feedback supports both post-training and test-time reasoning. However, training strong PRMs remains costly: human step annotation is difficult to scale, while Monte Carlo estimation is computationally expensive and can drift from the intrinsic correctness of steps. To get effective PRMs at low cost, we introduce TIPS (Thinking-Induced Process Supervision), an outcome-only reinforcement learning (RL) framework for training generative PRMs. In TIPS, the model generates a chain-of-thought (CoT) followed by step-level labels and an outcome label. The reward depends solely on whether the predicted outcome matches the ground truth, and the resulting group-relative advantage is used to optimize the entire generated response. Intuitively, when checking intermediate steps helps determine the outcome, more accurate checks can lead to better outcome judgments and higher rewards. Outcome-only RL can therefore reinforce step-level verification without explicit process supervision. We validate the effectiveness of TIPS across math and agent benchmarks and four backbone families. Notably, TIPS-Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 reaches 85.2 F1 on ProcessBench with only 3.2K outcome-labeled trajectories, surpassing all evaluated trained PRMs and strong prompt-only judges such as GPT-5.4-Instruct and Claude-4.7-Opus, while still trailing o1-mini. Code and data are available at https://github.com/RUCBM/TIPS.

</details>


### [158] [PR-OPD: Privileged Representation On-policy Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36642)
*Muyang Li,Jie Yang,Zhengyu Fang,Junchao Zhu,Zhengkun Xiao,Ruining Deng,Zhe Jiang,Shigang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现奖励蒸馏中"技能"对概率影响微弱但深层隐状态变化显著，提出PR-OPD方法，通过对齐特权隐状态实现自蒸馏，在ALFWorld和WebShop上取得最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体训练依赖每回合单一奖励，特权自蒸馏虽用同一策略在给定技能下指导无技能自我，但研究发现两个问题：Invisible Advantage（技能显著提升成功率却只改变不到四分之一采样token的概率）和Much to Align（技能改变超80%响应token的隐状态且可被线性探针追溯）。这说明token概率并非有效蒸馏通道，需转向隐状态对齐。

Method: 提出PR-OPD：先GRPO热启动，策略为每条轨迹写出事后技能，以该技能重读自身响应作为stop-gradient教师，在每一层将其投影隐状态与教师对齐，同时结合奖励目标，无需外部技能库、独立教师或推理开销。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，使用两个骨干网络，PR-OPD在所有设置下均取得最佳总体结果；相较GRPO，ALFWorld成功率最高提升4.7点，WebShop准确率最高提升14.0点。

Conclusion: 通过对齐特权表示而非token概率，PR-OPD有效利用技能信息，无额外推理开销，在多个环境和骨干上一致优于GRPO，验证了隐状态对齐作为自蒸馏通道的有效性。

Abstract: Language-model agents are usually trained by reinforcement learning from one reward per episode, and privileged self-distillation enriches it by letting the same policy, given a skill, teach its skill-free self through token probabilities. However, we identify two phenomena that question this channel. Invisible Advantage: a skill in context lifts WebShop success from 42.2% to 56.2%, yet changes the probabilities of fewer than a quarter of the sampled tokens. Much to Align: a skill changes the hidden states of over 80% of response tokens, in a way that linear probes can trace back to the specific skill. To exploit this, we propose Privileged Representation On-policy Self-Distillation (PR-OPD). After a GRPO warm start, the policy writes a hindsight skill for each trajectory, re-reads its own responses with that skill as a stop-gradient teacher, and aligns its projected hidden states to the teacher's at every layer alongside the reward objective, with no external skill library, separate teacher, or inference overhead. On ALFWorld and WebShop with two backbones, PR-OPD achieves the best overall results in every setting, improving over GRPO by up to 4.7 points in ALFWorld success and 14.0 points in WebShop accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/balibata/PR-OPD.

</details>


### [159] [Scheduling Recursive Reasoning in Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36653)
*Boyuan Wang,Chengyao Yu,Jiaxi Ren,Hongxin Wei,Bingyi Jing,Yuxin Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹自适应进度-波动调度器（TAPS），通过在线调整循环推理中的更新步长来提升终端准确率并减少循环次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环推理模型通常以固定单位尺度应用每次学习到的更新，当更新持续取得进展时过于保守、当更新波动时又过于激进，限制了增加循环带来的收益，因此需要研究更新尺度应如何沿轨迹变化。

Method: 首先分析终端损失对循环更新尺度的敏感性，证明其时间平均可精确分解为持续进展项与中心化波动项；据此提出TAPS，在循环更新中追踪二者的平衡并在线自适应调整步长；进一步将进度-波动原则引入训练。

Result: 理论证明TAPS在充分条件下可降低期望终端损失并在更少循环内达到目标质量；实验中在结构化推理任务上无需重训即提升终端准确率，结合训练后可获得额外准确率增益，并在匹配基线准确率时实现最高1.56倍墙钟加速；在多种循环架构与推理策略上均有效。

Conclusion: 更新尺度可作为循环推理中与架构、深度并列的互补控制轴。

Abstract: Recurrent reasoning models have attracted growing attention for scaling test-time computation, typically by iteratively refining latent states with shared parameters. However, these models apply each learned update with a fixed unit scale, which can be conservative when updates make persistent progress and overly aggressive when they fluctuate, limiting the benefit of additional loops. To understand how the scale should vary along the trajectory, we first analyze the sensitivity of terminal loss to recurrent update scale. We show that its temporal average admits an exact decomposition into persistent-progress and centered-fluctuation contributions. Based on this, we introduce the Trajectory Adaptive Progress-Fluctuation Scheduler (TAPS), which tracks their balance across recurrent updates and adapts the step size online. Theoretically, we establish sufficient conditions under which TAPS reduces expected terminal loss and reaches a target quality in fewer recurrent loops. Empirically, we show that TAPS improves terminal accuracy across structured reasoning tasks without retraining. By further incorporating the progress-fluctuation principle into training, TAPS yields additional accuracy gains with up to 1.56 times wall-clock speedup at matched baseline accuracy. The broad applicability of TAPS is supported by its effectiveness across diverse recurrent architectures and inference strategies. Together, these results establish update scale as complementary control axis of recurrent inference alongside architecture and depth.

</details>


### [160] [Constitutional adapters: Inference-time interventions for misalignment and misuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36657)
*Adam S. Lowet,Mark Kurzeja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从合成语料中蒸馏"宪法一致性"到轻量对象（低秩适配器与引导向量），得到"宪法适配器"(CA)：从未见过有害请求，却能提升越狱防御与对齐，尤其在长上下文与多轮攻击下表现更佳，且可跨检查点迁移、按需调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按明确定义的"宪法"原则训练模型是AI对齐中稳健透明的机制，但其通用性与灵活性尚不清楚。作者希望验证：这种宪法一致性是否能被压缩进轻量、可移植的对象中，并在未见攻击样本的情况下仍具备防御能力。

Method: 从合成语料中蒸馏宪法一致行为，注入低秩适配器（LoRA）与引导向量（steering vectors）。训练中不使用任何有害请求或越狱样本。进一步用"宪法训练对象"减去"对照训练对象"以强化效果，得到"宪法适配器"(CAs)。CA可在基座模型上训练，零样本迁移至后训练检查点，并在推理时调节强度。

Result: CA显著提高越狱防御成功率与对齐指标，尤其在长上下文长度和多轮攻击下，优于提示式与引导式基线。相减操作进一步放大效果。CA支持零样本跨检查点迁移，并可通过推理时缩放按预期在"防御"与"良性合规"之间权衡。

Conclusion: CA是一种轻量、可移植、可调的对齐工具，可用于缓解API部署中的错位与滥用问题。

Abstract: Training models to act in accordance with an explicitly defined set of principles, or "constitution," has shown promise as a robust and transparent mechanism for AI alignment. However, the generality and flexibility of such methods remain unclear. Here, we show that constitution-consistent behavior can be distilled from synthetic corpora into lightweight objects (low-rank adapters and steering vectors). Despite never seeing a harmful request or jailbreak during training, such objects increase jailbreak defense success and measured alignment -- particularly at long context lengths and against multi-turn attacks, where they outperform both prompted and steered baselines. Subtracting control-trained from constitution-trained objects further accentuates these effects, yielding defenses we call "constitutional adapters" (CAs). CAs can be trained on a base model, transferred zero-shot to its post-trained checkpoint, and scaled at inference time to predictably trade off defense for benign compliance. Taken together, these results recommend CAs as a lightweight, portable, and tunable lever for mitigating misalignment and misuse in API deployments.

</details>


### [161] [On-Policy Parameter Update Direction Underlies Generalization in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36659)
*Shufan Shen,Zhongni Hou,Junshu Sun,Yufei Zhang,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Qingming Huang,Shuhui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现on-policy范式在训练中持续调整参数更新方向，而SFT更新方向一致；据此提出OPSFT，将SFT更新约束到on-policy范式所识别的累积更新方向上，从而把on-policy的强泛化优势迁移给SFT，兼顾高效训练与高质量轨迹的利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于on-policy后训练参数更新行为的研究仅把这些行为视为训练副产物，未将其作为可提升其他范式（如SFT）泛化能力的优化原则。作者希望探究是否存在某种on-policy更新行为可迁移以改善泛化。

Method: 分析显示SFT沿一致方向更新参数，而on-policy范式会不断调整更新方向。基于此差异，作者关注每个参数的累积更新方向，提出On-Policy方向约束的监督微调（OPSFT）：用少量on-policy训练步骤识别出支持强泛化的更新方向，再将SFT更新约束到该方向。

Result: OPSFT表现出强有力的性能，表明on-policy范式的泛化优势可通过参数更新方向迁移到SFT。即使SFT，只要更新被约束在该方向上也能获得良好泛化。

Conclusion: 结合on-policy的强泛化与SFT的优势带来两项实际收益：一是仅需少量on-policy步骤确定方向即可高效训练；二是OPSFT可利用高质量轨迹沿该方向继续改进已后训练模型，而不破坏on-policy习得的能力。

Abstract: The strong generalization performance of on-policy post-training paradigms has motivated studies of their parameter update behaviors. However, these studies treat the observed behaviors only as byproducts in on-policy training, overlooking their potential to serve as optimization principles for improving the generalization of other paradigms such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT). To address this limitation, we investigate whether there exists a specific on-policy update behavior that can achieve such improvements. First, our analyses reveal that SFT updates parameters along consistent directions, while the on-policy paradigm continuously adjusts the direction during training. This difference inspires us to focus on the cumulative update direction of each parameter as a promising behavior. Then, we evaluate its effectiveness for improving generalization by proposing On-Policy direction-constrained Supervised Fine-Tuning (OPSFT), which constrains SFT updates to the direction identified by on-policy paradigms. The strong performance of OPSFT indicates that the generalization advantage of on-policy paradigms can be transferred to SFT through the parameter update direction. Once such a direction is identified, even SFT can generalize with its updates constrained to this direction. This finding offers two practical benefits by combining the strong generalization of on-policy paradigms with the advantages of SFT, including the high training efficiency and ability to leverage high-quality trajectories. For efficiency, we identify update directions that support strong generalization using a few on-policy training steps, and subsequently apply OPSFT to achieve high training efficiency. For leveraging high-quality trajectories, OPSFT can utilize these trajectories to continue improving a post-trained model along its update direction without disrupting the ability learned from on-policy training.

</details>


### [162] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36660)
*Leonardo F. Toso,James Anderson,Rafael Pinot,Nirupam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究含恶意客户端的联邦学习，通过表示学习（个性化线性头+拜占庭鲁棒聚合共享非线性表示）来消除模型异质性偏差，并给出非渐近参数恢复误差界，在多个数据集上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质性（heterogeneity）下，单一共享模型参数在统计上不合适：无法捕捉各客户端不同的数据生成过程，导致不可约的模型异质性偏差，并严重削弱对恶意（拜占庭）客户端的鲁棒性。目标是在不知道哪些客户端是诚实的情况下，最小化诚实客户端的平均损失。

Method: 采用表示学习框架：每个客户端学习个性化的线性头（linear head），同时通过拜占庭鲁棒聚合协同估计一个共享的非线性表示。对诚实表示梯度的异质性进行分析，并推导非渐近参数恢复误差界，再将该回归分析扩展到多分类问题。

Result: 参数恢复误差界包含三项：(i) 依赖初始化的误差，随迭代次数 T 消失；(ii) 有限样本噪声项，随每客户端样本数 τ 和诚实客户端数量减少；(iii) 随机梯度方差项，也随 T 减小。关键的是，界中不含不可约的模型异质性偏差。在 CIFAR-10、FEMNIST 和 School Exam Score 数据集上实证验证。

Conclusion: 通过表示学习可在异质且含恶意客户端的环境中实现拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习，理论界表明不存在不可约的模型异质性偏差，且误差随样本量、客户端数和迭代次数下降，实验证实了理论。

Abstract: We study federated learning (FL) with adversarial clients, where the goal is to minimize the average loss of the honest (non-adversarial) clients without knowing their identity. Under heterogeneity, a single shared model parameter is statistically inappropriate: it cannot capture the distinct data-generating processes across clients, incurring an irreducible model-heterogeneity bias and severely limiting robustness to adversarial clients (a.k.a. Byzantine-robustness). We address this problem through representation learning, where each client learns a personalized linear head, while collaboratively estimating a shared nonlinear representation through Byzantine-robust aggregation. We demonstrate that the heterogeneity among honest representation gradients is controlled by the representation error and statistical errors that decay either with the number of data samples per client ($τ$) or the number of iterations ($T$). In particular, our non-asymptotic parameter recovery error bound reveals three terms: (i) an initialization-dependent error that goes away with $T$, (ii) finite-sample noise terms that decreases with $τ$ and the number of honest clients, and (iii) a stochastic gradient variance term that also reduces with $T$. Importantly, with no irreducible model-heterogeneity bias in our bounds. We extend the regression analysis to multiclass classification, and empirically validate it on CIFAR-10, FEMNIST, and School Exam Score datasets.

</details>


### [163] [AutoLoCo: Communication Efficient Distributed LLM Training via Adaptive Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36662)
*Pengyu He,Yan Zhang,Ruien Li,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoLoCo 是一个自适应训练框架，通过根据训练状态动态调整本地更新间隔并校正外层优化器，在保持性能的同时将 LLM 训练的通信频率降低 27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 预训练日益跨多个数据中心进行，随着加速器规模扩大，计算时间占比下降而通信时间占比上升，频繁同步成为越来越大的瓶颈。本地更新方法通过在同步之间执行多步优化来降低通信成本，但多数方法在训练前设定固定的本地优化步数间隔并全程保持不变。然而最优间隔会在整个训练过程中变化，若能根据当前训练状态自适应调整间隔与优化器，就能在维持训练性能的同时降低通信频率。

Method: 提出 AutoLoCo 自适应训练框架以减少 LLM 训练中的通信。它利用标量训练统计量自适应调整本地间隔，并对每次外层更新进行校正。方法基于两点观察：1）合适的本地间隔随训练阶段而变化；2）改变每个间隔内的内层步数会与保持不变的外层优化器产生不匹配，需要对外层更新进行校正。作者利用累积的内层学习率，针对动量和学习率对外层优化器进行校正以缓解这种不匹配。

Result: 在通信受限的实验条件下，AutoLoCo 相对于 DiLoCo 将通信频率降低了 27%，同时保持了训练性能。

Conclusion: 通过自适应调整本地更新间隔并校正外层优化器，AutoLoCo 能在通信受限环境中显著减少 LLM 训练的通信开销而不损失性能，为大规模跨数据中心训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The pre-training of Large Language Models (LLMs) is increasingly conducted across multiple data centers. As training scales to a larger number of accelerators, the fraction of time spent on computation decreases, while the fraction spent on communication increases. Therefore, frequent synchronization becomes a growing bottleneck. Local update methods reduce this cost by allowing workers to perform several optimizer steps between synchronizations. Most local update methods set the number of local optimizer steps between synchronizations before training and keep this interval fixed throughout the run. However, the best interval can change during the entire train process. If the interval and optimizer are adapted to the current training state, the communication frequency is reduced while maintaining the training performance. In this work, we introduce AutoLoCo, an adaptive training framework to reduce communication in LLM training. It adapts the local interval using scalar training statistics and corrects each outer update. Our method is motivated by two observations: 1) the appropriate local interval varies across training stages, and 2) changing the number of inner steps per interval creates a mismatch with an unchanged outer optimizer, requiring a correction to the outer update. We optimize this mismatch by correction of the outer optimizer for the momentum and the learning rate using the accumulated inner learning rate. Our experiments under communication constraints demonstrate that AutoLoCo reduces communication frequency by 27% relative to DiLoCo while maintaining training performance.

</details>


### [164] [GenLimitLib: A Formal Library for Language Generation in the Limit and AI-Assisted Mathematical Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36663)
*Shuangping Li,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GenLimitLib，一个面向“极限语言生成”领域的源对齐 Lean 4 形式化库，整合 30 篇论文的形式化成果，并展示其在人类数学研究与 AI 辅助研究中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “极限语言生成”由 Kleinberg 和 Mullainathan 在 NeurIPS 2024 提出，是受 LLM 启发的理论问题：如何从观测样例中生成有效的新字符串。该领域年轻且快速发展，需要系统化、结构化的形式化基础设施来整合分散的成果，因此成为研究大规模形式化的理想试验台。

Method: 构建 Lean 4 库 GenLimitLib，对 30 篇论文进行形式化开发：提取共享定义与可复用证明组件，同时保留各论文特有的假设与命题，并记录论文之间的相互关系，从而给出该文献领域的结构化视图。

Result: GenLimitLib 成功整合了 30 篇论文的形式化内容，提供源对齐的共享定义与可复用证明组件，并记录跨论文关系；通过数学案例研究和 LLM 实验验证了其可用性。

Conclusion: 该库能够同时支持人类数学研究和 AI 辅助研究，为“极限语言生成”领域提供了具体且结构化的文献视图，并为大规模形式化提供了可行范式。

Abstract: We present GenLimitLib, a source-aligned Lean 4 library for language generation in the limit. Introduced by Kleinberg and Mullainathan at NeurIPS 2024, language generation in the limit studies a theoretical question motivated by LLMs: how to generate valid new strings from observed examples. This young and rapidly evolving field offers a natural testbed for studying large-scale formalization. GenLimitLib contains formal developments for 30 papers. It extracts shared definitions and reusable proof components while preserving paper-specific assumptions and statements, and records relationships across papers. In this way, GenLimitLib provides a concrete and structured view of the literature. We show through mathematical case studies and LLM experiments how our library can support both human mathematical research and AI-assisted research. Our Library: https://github.com/pengzhang91/generation-in-the-limit-lib.

</details>


### [165] [Stochastic Heavy Ball with Polyak Step Size and Armijo Line Search: A General Convergence Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36668)
*Jiawei Zhang,Qitan Shi,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为配备 Polyak 步长（PS）和 Armijo 线搜索（ALS）的随机重球法（SHB）建立了统一的收敛性分析，并在插值或强增长条件下进一步给出几乎必然收敛速率与最后迭代收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PS 与 ALS 在随机优化中受到越来越多关注，具有良好的经验表现与理论保证，但它们针对随机重球法（SHB）的收敛理论仍然有限，缺乏统一且不依赖强假设的分析框架。

Method: 提出与 Polyak 步长密切对应的修正 Armijo 规则，并设计解耦分析以分离动量带来的历史依赖性；在此基础上对标准 PS 与 ALS 下的 SHB 进行统一收敛性分析。

Result: 对强凸、凸与非凸目标建立了期望收敛，无需插值或对动量参数的额外限制；在插值或强增长条件下强化为几乎必然速率与最后迭代收敛；在插值之外的一般情形下，利用 PS 与 ALS 的递减变体证明了几乎必然收敛到精确最优解或稳定点。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机重球法中的 Polyak 步长与 Armijo 线搜索提供了更全面的理论视角，扩展了其收敛性保证的适用范围。

Abstract: Polyak step size (PS) and Armijo line search (ALS) have received increasing attention in stochastic optimization, with encouraging empirical performance and theoretical guarantees. However, their convergence theory for stochastic heavy ball (SHB) methods remains limited. In this work, we develop a unified convergence analysis for SHB equipped with PS and ALS. To this end, we introduce a modified Armijo rule that closely parallels the Polyak step size, together with a decoupling analysis that isolates the historical dependence induced by momentum. For SHB with standard PS and ALS, we establish expected convergence for strongly convex, convex, and non-convex objectives without interpolation or restrictive conditions on the momentum parameter. Under interpolation or strong growth, we further strengthen the results to almost sure rates and last-iterate convergence. Moreover, for general settings beyond interpolation, we prove almost sure convergence to the exact optimum or to stationarity for SHB with diminishing variants of PS and ALS. These results provide a more comprehensive theoretical view of Polyak step size and Armijo line search for stochastic heavy ball methods.

</details>


### [166] [Human-inspired, Task-Dimension-Guided Exploration for Efficient Learning in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36672)
*Fanyu Zhu,Jiahui An,Ni Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出受人类决策启发的TDGE算法，通过自动构建"任务-维度-特征-物品"层次结构实现高维决策空间的高效探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维决策空间中的高效探索是决策系统的核心难题，而人类却能高效应对。近期行为研究表明，人类通过探测候选特征维度、识别与奖励相关的维度，并限制有效决策空间来降低维度。本文受此机制启发，旨在将这种降维探索策略引入算法。

Method: 提出TDGE（Task-Dimension-Guided Exploration），一种类人、模型无关的算法，自动构建"任务-维度-特征-物品"层次结构。采用自顶向下探索策略：先选择任务相关的特征维度，再在所选维度内识别有信息量的特征，最后基于选定特征推荐具体物品。

Result: 在MovieLens-20M、Last.fm和Amazon推荐数据集上，TDGE相比基线算法显著提升了探索效率和冷启动适应能力。与其他结构化算法的对比及消融实验将这些增益归因于TDGE的层次结构与语义特征空间探索，且结果在不同聚类方法和层次深度下保持稳健。推荐轨迹可视化显示出与人类维度引导行为相似的探索模式。

Conclusion: TDGE通过模拟人类维度引导的降维探索机制，有效提升了高维决策空间的探索效率与冷启动适应能力，验证了类人层次化探索策略在决策系统中的价值。

Abstract: Efficient exploration in high-dimensional decision spaces remains a central challenge for decision-making systems. Humans, in contrast, can navigate large decision spaces with remarkable efficiency. Recent behavioral studies suggest that humans reduce dimensionality in large decision spaces by probing candidate feature dimensions, identifying reward-relevant ones, and restricting the effective decision space. Inspired by this mechanism, we propose TDGE (Task-Dimension-Guided Exploration), a human-inspired, model-agnostic algorithm with an automatically constructed task-dimension--feature--item hierarchy. TDGE follows a top-down exploration strategy: it first selects task-relevant feature dimensions, then identifies informative features within those dimensions, and finally recommends concrete items based on the selected features. Experiments on MovieLens-20M, Last.fm, and Amazon recommendation datasets show that TDGE substantially improves exploration efficiency and cold-start adaptation over baseline algorithms. Comparisons with other structured algorithms and ablation studies attribute these gains to TDGE's hierarchical structure and semantic feature-space exploration, with robust results across clustering methods and hierarchy depths. Recommendation-trajectory visualizations also show exploration patterns similar to human dimension-guided behavior.

</details>


### [167] [Understanding Private Evolution as Learning-Augmented Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36678)
*Audra McMillan,Kunal Talwar,Felix Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从生成模型增强的Wasserstein学习视角重新分析Private Evolution（PE），在理论上给出了更好的性能界，并针对标准PE在良簇实例上的收敛失败问题提出了几何感知的新版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PE是一种差分隐私合成数据生成算法，可视为Wasserstein学习算法，但其实际表现远好于最坏情况Wasserstein分析所预测的结果，这一理论与实践之间的差距需要更合理的理论解释。

Method: 将PE重新表述为生成模型增强的Wasserstein学习，在理论分析中纳入生成模型对真实分布信息的捕捉能力；同时分析标准PE变体的收敛性，并提出一种几何感知（geometry-aware）的PE新版本。

Result: 理论表明，若生成器在与分布相同的低维空间中采样，样本复杂度取决于内在维度而非环境维度；标准PE变体在简单良簇实例上可能不收敛；实验显示新算法与标准基线具有竞争力，并能提升召回率。

Conclusion: 引入生成模型信息可显著改善PE的性能理论界，且几何感知的PE在良簇实例上具有可证明的收敛性，实验上兼具竞争性与更好的召回表现。

Abstract: Private Evolution (PE) is a differentially private algorithm for synthetic data generation. While it can be viewed as a Wasserstein learning algorithm, it performs much better in practice than worst-case Wasserstein analyses would predict. We recast PE as generative model-augmented Wasserstein learning. We show theoretically that when we take into account the use of a generative model that is able to capture something about the true distribution, then we can obtain much better performance bounds. For example, if the generator gives samples in the same low-dimensional space as the distribution, then sample complexity depends on intrinsic, not ambient, dimension. We also show that standard variants of PE can fail to converge on simple well-clustered instances, and propose a new geometry-aware version of PE with provable convergence on such instances. Experimentally, we show that our new algorithm is competitive with standard baselines and can improve recall.

</details>


### [168] [MARCO: Multi-Round Agentic Reinforcement for Conditional Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36683)
*Shicheng Fang,Yuxin Wang,Zhuo Yang,Xiaohu Xu,Jiahao Lu,Chuanyuan Tan,Tong Zhu,Yining Zheng,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARCO 是一个以评估器为反馈的强化学习框架，用有限的"提议—反馈—修订"轨迹训练分子编辑模型，在 MuMOInstruct 多目标基准上以属性成功率与相似度的乘积取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化本身是迭代过程：提出候选、依据多个目标评估、在保持与源分子关系的前提下修订。但多数指令跟随模型只输出一次编辑结果，把有效性、属性提升与相似度控制压缩到单个回答中，缺乏迭代修正能力。

Method: MARCO 将分子编辑器训练于有界的"提议—反馈—修订"轨迹上，把每轮塑形奖励聚合成无折扣的轨迹回报，用于组相对策略优化（group-relative policy optimization）。实验设计两种评估设定：Same-1 在仅一次回答预算下测试策略，Same-5 在最多五次回答且可借助验证器反馈的条件下测试同一策略。

Result: 在三目标 MuMOInstruct 基准、三种 Qwen 主干模型以及已见/未见指令划分上，经 SFT 初始化的 MARCO 在所有主要报告设定中均取得属性成功率与相似度乘积的最高值。在测试预算下，Same-5 进一步提升了观测得分；四目标实验与公开检查点实验则检验了跨约束集合与初始化方式的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 将分子编辑建模为受评估器反馈引导的迭代轨迹，并通过强化学习优化轨迹回报，能有效缓解单次输出模型的局限，在属性优化与结构相似度之间取得更优平衡，并具备跨约束与跨初始化的迁移潜力。

Abstract: Molecular optimization is inherently iterative: a candidate is proposed, evaluated against several objectives, and revised while preserving a relationship to the source molecule. Most instruction-following models instead emit one edited molecule, forcing validity, property improvement, and similarity control into a single response. We introduce MARCO, an evaluator-grounded reinforcement-learning framework that trains molecular editors on bounded proposal--feedback--revision trajectories. MARCO aggregates shaped turn rewards into an undiscounted trajectory return for group-relative policy optimization. We evaluate two consequences of this training: Same-1 tests the trained policy under a one-response budget, while Same-5 tests whether the same policy can use verifier feedback when up to five responses are available. Across the three-objective MuMOInstruct benchmark, three Qwen backbones, and seen/unseen instruction splits, SFT-initialized MARCO obtains the highest product of property success rate and similarity in every reported primary setting. Same-5 further improves the observed score under the tested budget, while four-objective and public-checkpoint experiments test transfer across constraint sets and initialization regimes.

</details>


### [169] [Where Root Cause Analysis Fails: A Retrieval-Reranking Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36686)
*Hada Melino Muhammad,Luan Pham,Laure Barrière,Sachin Shetty,Leonardo Pulga,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出 top@k 准确率掩盖了两种失败模式，提出检索-重排分解方法，并构建两阶段流水线显著提升根因分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析（RCA）方法普遍使用 top@k 准确率评估，但该指标存在根本盲点：它混淆了"检索失败"（真因从未被纳入候选）与"重排失败"（真因被纳入但排名过低）两种不同的失败模式，导致无法准确定位方法瓶颈。

Method: 作者提出检索-重排分解框架，并据此审计四个知名基准，分别考察检索阶段与重排阶段的独立表现。基于该分解的洞察，构建了两阶段流水线：多信号检索器 + LLM 重排器，无需因果图或标注数据。

Result: 在复杂故障基准上，统计基线误排真因的比例达 79-100%，图方法即使在有保证包含真因的候选池上也未明显优于最佳统计基线；而简单基准上检索几乎已解决（98-100%）。两阶段流水线以单一固定配置在所有六个基准套件上达到或超过最佳基线的 top@1（最高 +12 点）；加入简短系统描述文档后，重排器在所有基准上领先最佳基线 +7 至 +18 点。

Conclusion: top@k 指标掩盖了 RCA 的真实瓶颈，检索-重排分解能更细致地诊断方法缺陷；基于该分解的 LLM 重排流水线无需因果图与标注数据即可超越现有方法，表明重排阶段（尤其引入系统描述信息）是提升 RCA 性能的关键方向。

Abstract: Identifying the root cause of an anomaly among hundreds of sensors is critical for preventing safety incidents and costly downtime in complex monitored systems. Existing studies evaluate root cause analysis (RCA) methods using top@k accuracy. We show that this metric has a fundamental blind spot: it conflates two failure modes, retrieval failure, where the true cause is never considered, and reranking failure, where it is considered but ranked too low. In this work, we introduce a retrieval-reranking decomposition and audit four well-known benchmarks to expose this blind spot. Our experiments show that, on benchmarks with complex faults, statistical baselines mis-rank the true cause 79-100% of the time, and graph-based methods never clearly beat the best statistical baseline, whether their causal graphs are learned on short fault windows, on retrieved candidate pools guaranteed to contain the cause, or on multi-day normal-operation data. Meanwhile, on simple benchmarks where faults manifest significantly at their origin, retrieval is nearly solved (98-100%). Guided by the decomposition, we build a two-stage pipeline combining a multi-signal retriever with an LLM reranker that, as one fixed configuration, matches or exceeds the best baseline's top@1 accuracy on all six benchmark suites (by up to +12 points), with no causal graph or labeled data required. When all methods rank the same retrieved candidates with the true cause guaranteed present, adding a short system-description document lets the reranker lead the best baseline by +7 to +18 points on every benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/DecompRCA.

</details>


### [170] [CHAIN: Calibrated LLM Forecasting via Causal-Temporal Hypergraph Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36689)
*Wenjin Liu,Chenxi Wang,Yue Lu,Zhe Cui,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出大语言模型在事件预测中的概率输出存在系统性校准偏差，且该偏差在不同领域和问题类型上异质。作者将概率预测分解为证据加权、证据聚合、来源融合三阶段，提出CHAIN，通过三个阶段各自的机制在预测过程中直接缓解偏差，而非预测后校正。跨域预测基准实验显示，CHAIN在期望校准误差、Brier分数和准确率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在事件预测中虽然取得进展，但其概率输出存在系统性校准偏差，且在不同领域与问题类型上偏差程度异质，影响不确定性决策的可信度。现有校准方法多为预测完成后的后处理校正，未建模预测过程内部的结构性偏差来源。

Method: 将因果-时间超图上的概率预测分解为三个可解释阶段：证据加权、证据聚合与来源融合，并针对每一阶段设计专门机制：(i) 用因果拓扑距离调节时间衰减函数；(ii) 在方向感知去重后，通过Noisy-OR聚合近似独立的因果链；(iii) 由因果覆盖度与方向平衡驱动自适应融合。

Result: 在跨域预测基准测试中，CHAIN在期望校准误差（ECE）、Brier分数和准确率三个指标上均优于现有方法。项目代码已开源于 https://github.com/QwenQKing/Chain。

Conclusion: 通过在预测过程内部按阶段建模并缓解偏差来源，可以在不依赖事后校正的情况下改善概率预测的校准性与准确性，为不确定性决策提供更可信的概率输出。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved significant progress in event forecasting, yet their probability outputs exhibit systematic calibration bias that varies heterogeneously across different domains and question types, undermining the trustworthiness of probabilistic outputs for decision-making under uncertainty. However, existing calibration methods typically correct probability outputs after prediction is complete, without modeling the structural sources of bias within the prediction process itself. To address this challenge, we decompose probabilistic prediction over causal-temporal hypergraphs into three stages, evidence weighting, evidence aggregation, and source fusion, and propose CHAIN, which designs stage-specific mechanisms to mitigate bias at each stage: (i) modulating the temporal decay function by causal topological distance, (ii) aggregating approximately independent causal chains via Noisy-OR after direction-aware deduplication, and (iii) driving adaptive fusion by causal coverage and directional balance. Experimental results on cross-domain forecasting benchmarks show CHAIN outperforms existing methods in expected calibration error, Brier score, and accuracy. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/Chain.

</details>


### [171] [Normalize-Then-Precondition: A Hierarchical Approach to Marginal Scale and Interaction Geometry for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36692)
*Zixuan Gong,Zeyu Gan,Jiaye Teng,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Normalize-Then-Precondition 矩阵优化框架，并基于此设计 NormPre-G 与 NormPre-L 两种优化器，在大规模预训练中优于 AdamW、Muon 和 MANO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵优化器是当前有前景的方向，Muon 是其中的代表性设计。作者从 Muon 的全 Gram 表示出发，发现它同时处理边际尺度信息与交互信息，这为按层次组织几何信息提供了新思路，从而提出 Normalize-Then-Precondition 框架。

Method: 该框架先用对角 Gram 信息构造边际归一化更新，再对方向性交互几何施加谱预条件。据此提出 NormPre，包含 NormPre-G 与 NormPre-L 两个变体，分别采用全局与局部谱预条件，理论基础为谱范数最速下降与正则化公式加主模态选择。为支持大规模训练，NormPre-G 使用 Newton-Schulz 迭代，NormPre-L 使用随机投影近似主导交互特征空间。

Result: 理论上，作者为 NormPre 的简化版本建立了 O(T^{-1/2}) 的收敛保证。在 GPT-2 Small、LLaMA 和 Qwen3 的大量预训练实验中，两种变体在相同训练预算下均持续优于 AdamW、Muon 和 MANO。进一步的效率与谱分析揭示了两种变体的互补优势，并刻画了其性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: NormPre 通过层次化组织几何信息，在矩阵优化中实现了更优的性能，其两个变体在效率与效果上各有侧重，代码已在 GitHub 开源。
