Abstract: Matrix optimizers have emerged as a promising direction, with Muon standing out as a prominent design. Revisiting Muon through its full-Gram representation, we observe that it jointly processes marginal-scale and interaction information. This opens an alternative way to organize geometric information hierarchically, motivating the Normalize-Then-Precondition framework. Specifically, it first uses diagonal-Gram information to construct a marginally normalized update, then applies spectral preconditioning to its directional interaction geometry. Building on this framework, we develop NormPre with NormPre-G and NormPre-L adopting global and localized spectral preconditioning, grounded in spectral-norm steepest descent and a regularized formulation followed by leading mode selection, respectively. To enable large-scale training, NormPre-G uses Newton-Schulz iterations and NormPre-L employs randomized sketching to approximate the leading interaction eigenspace. Theoretically, we establish $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ convergence guarantees for simplified versions of NormPre. Across extensive pretraining experiments on GPT-2 Small, LLaMA and Qwen3, both variants consistently outperform AdamW, Muon and MANO under matched training budgets. Further efficiency and spectral analyses reveal the complementary strengths of two variants and characterize their performance-efficiency trade-off. We open-source our code through a GitHub repository at https://github.com/zx-gong/NormPre.

</details>


### [172] [Know Thyself, Teach Thyself: Internal Information Flow for Selective Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36695)
*Rui Wang,Ruijie Wang,Bo Chen,Jiangxuan Long,Yingyu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 InFlow，一个检索引导的在线策略自蒸馏框架，将自蒸馏建模为“潜在信息到已实现信息”的流动过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏形成闭环学习，有望实现递归自我提升，但缺乏外部教师时，模型需自行判断哪些信息能改进监督、哪些变化应被学习；现有方法多孤立地改进教师数据或选择训练样本，未度量两阶段间传递的信息。

Method: InFlow 先用经确定性校准的隐状态轨迹检索潜在有信息量的来源，再通过教师初始答案信念与检索条件答案信念之间的 Jensen-Shannon 散度度量其已实现效果，选取信念偏移较大的样本进行在线策略蒸馏；并形式化分析了检索与选择所优化的信息，将答案层面偏移与教师-学生蒸馏差距关联起来。

Result: 在四个开放权重语言模型和三个知识领域上，InFlow 在对比的选择方法中取得最强的跨模型平均表现，消融实验支持框架的两个阶段。

Conclusion: 信息流建模对自蒸馏的有效信息选择具有价值，InFlow 通过联合建模检索与选择的潜在-已实现信息流提升了自蒸馏效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: Self-distillation turns knowledge distillation into a closed learning loop and offers a path toward recursive self-improvement. Without an external teacher, however, the model must determine both what information can improve its supervision and which induced changes should be learned. Existing methods typically improve teacher-generated data or select training examples in isolation, leaving the information transferred between these stages unmeasured. We introduce InFlow, a retrieval-guided on-policy self-distillation framework that models this process as potential-to-realized information flow. InFlow first retrieves potentially informative sources using certainty-calibrated hidden-state trajectories, then measures their realized effect through the Jensen--Shannon divergence between the teacher's initial and retrieval-conditioned answer beliefs. Examples with larger belief shifts are selected for on-policy distillation. Our analysis formalizes the information optimized by retrieval and selection and relates the answer-level shift to the teacher--student distillation gap. Across four open-weight language models and three knowledge domains, InFlow achieves the strongest cross-model average among the compared selection methods, with ablations supporting both stages of the framework. Our code is available at https://github.com/1240148048/INFLOW.

</details>


### [173] [Learned Queries and Keys Are All You Need: Replacing the Value Projection with Structured Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36698)
*Ene Meco,Emadeldeen Hamdan,A. Enis Cetin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"双头Transformer"替代传统三头结构，以减少参数与缓存需求，并结合多种正交变换构造双头，在ImageNet上取得优于三头Transformer的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer参数量大、缓存内存需求高，作者希望在不损失性能的前提下降低资源消耗，因此尝试将原有的三头结构缩减为双头结构。

Method: 用双头Transformer替换三头结构。研究了Walsh-Hadamard变换(WHT)、离散余弦变换(DCT)、离散傅里叶变换、基于滤波器组的Shearlet变换以及避免乘法(MA)算子来构造双头。将空间patch与其正交变换（或Shearlet、MA算子）以类似attention block的结构组合。

Result: 在ImageNet上，双头Transformer取得优于三头Transformer的结果；文中给出了大量仿真示例。

Conclusion: 双头Transformer可在减少参数与缓存的同时提升ImageNet性能，多种正交变换与MA算子均可用于构造双头，为Transformer轻量化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: To reduce the number of parameters and cache memory requirements of transformers we introduce dual-headed transformers instead of three heads. We studied Walsh-Hadamard Transform (WHT), Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT), Discrete Fourier Transform, filterbank based Shearlet Transform, and Multiplication-Avoiding (MA) operators to construct dual heads. We combine spatial patches and their orthogonal transforms (or Shearlet and MA operators) in a structure similar to the attention block. We obtained better results than triple headed transformers in ImageNet. Extensive simulation examples are presented.

</details>


### [174] [When Is Coarse Supervision Worth It? Cost-Aware Learning under Unknown Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36704)
*Jianyu Xu,Smriti Jha,Aarti Singh,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究成本感知的双分辨率学习，其中昂贵细标签给出向量响应，廉价粗标签给出未知权重的标量聚合，目标仍是完整响应；作者刻画其信息几何并提出估计-跟踪策略以学习聚合规则并跟踪最优分辨率混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习系统常以多分辨率获取监督，在标注成本与信息量之间权衡。难点在于未知聚合会改变粗数据可识别的方向，使粗监督的价值同时取决于成本、噪声与可识别性，因此需要一套能刻画并优化这种权衡的方法。

Method: 作者刻画双分辨率学习的信息几何，提出估计-跟踪（estimate-and-track）策略，在在线学习中同时学习未知聚合规则并跟踪最优的分辨率混合比例；并给出粗监督的闭式盈亏平衡条件。

Result: 理论证明在线策略达到最优的主导累积风险系数，并给出匹配的局部渐近极小极大下界。合成实验支持预测的全细标签/混合标签相变，显示在线学习器接近Oracle份额基准，并在粗监督足够有利时相对全细标签采集取得有限预算增益。

Conclusion: 结果为在不同监督分辨率之间平衡信息量与标注成本提供了有原则的方法，并给出何时值得引入粗监督的闭式判据。

Abstract: Modern learning systems often acquire supervision at multiple resolutions, trading annotation cost against information content. We study cost-aware two-resolution learning, where expensive fine labels reveal a vector response and cheaper coarse labels reveal a scalar aggregate formed with unknown weights, while the target remains the full response. The challenge is that unknown aggregation changes which directions coarse data can identify, so the value of coarse supervision depends jointly on cost, noise, and identification. We characterize this information geometry and develop an estimate-and-track policy that learns the aggregation rule and tracks the optimal resolution mix. We derive a closed-form break-even condition for coarse supervision and prove that the online policy attains the optimal leading cumulative-risk coefficient, with a matching local asymptotic minimax lower bound. Synthetic experiments support the predicted all-fine/mixed transition, show the online learner approaching the oracle-share benchmark, and demonstrate a finite-budget gain over all-fine acquisition when coarse supervision is sufficiently favorable. Our results provide a principled way to balance information and annotation cost across supervision resolutions.

</details>


### [175] [CALIBUDGET: Calibration-Guided Source Allocation for Fixed-Budget Mixed-Reasoning Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36721)
*Yupeng Chang,Yuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALIBUDGET 是一种"下限保护、可靠性感知"的整数配额分配器，把数据源曝光量当作显式自适应变量，在不改模型、目标和总预算的前提下生成精确、容量受限的各源配额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构数据源的固定预算适配中，不仅要决定用多少数据，还要决定每个源获得多少曝光。按规模比例分配会挤占小源，而仅按难度分配会追逐噪声估计、或把剩余预算给已近饱和的数据池。

Method: 从训练集内部的小规模校准切分出发，CALIBUDGET 综合"模型需求"、"扣下限后的可用量"和"bootstrap 稳定性"三项信号，输出满足容量约束的整数配额；仅调整源级分配，不改动模型、优化目标或总预算。

Result: 在数学与常识数据组合的受控设定下，于三次配对的 LLaMA-2-7B LoRA+ 运行中，CALIBUDGET 相对最强对比方法（带下限的验证误差分配）全面更优：CommonAvg、FragileAvg、MacroAvg 平均提升 0.56、0.46、0.41 个百分点；Overall 提升 0.18 pp，但 MathAvg 下降 0.20 pp。它仅改变 1.14%–1.42% 的源预算，却在常识任务与随机种子的 24 次比较中 15 次取得提升。

Conclusion: 源配额的微小调整即可带来性能变化，但收益并非均匀分布，存在"覆盖保留"边界；在配额确定后，可再由样本级选择决定用哪些样本填满各配额。

Abstract: Fixed-budget adaptation from heterogeneous data sources requires deciding not only how much data to use, but how much exposure each source receives. Size-proportional rules can crowd out small sources, whereas difficulty-only rules can chase noisy estimates or allocate residual budget to nearly saturated pools. We introduce CALIBUDGET, a floor-protected, reliability-aware integer allocator that treats source exposure as an explicit adaptation variable. From small train-internal calibration splits, it combines model need, post-floor availability, and bootstrap stability, then produces exact capacity-respecting quotas without changing the model, objective, or total budget. In a controlled setting combining mathematical and commonsense data, CALIBUDGET improves CommonAvg, FragileAvg, and MacroAvg over validation-error-with-floor, the strongest matched comparator, in all three paired LLaMA-2-7B LoRA+ runs. The respective mean gains are 0.56, 0.46, and 0.41 percentage points (pp). Overall increases by 0.18 pp, whereas MathAvg decreases by 0.20 pp, exposing a coverage-retention boundary rather than a uniform gain. CALIBUDGET changes only 1.14-1.42% of the source budget but improves performance in 15 of 24 comparisons across commonsense tasks and seeds. These results suggest that small changes in source quotas can matter; example-level selection can then determine which examples fill each quota.

</details>


### [176] [Routing in Gradient Space: Balanced Usage Is Not Expert Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36724)
*Yuchen Li,Mingyu Du,Zongqi Fan,Nguyen H. Tran,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将稀疏专家模型的路由问题建模为梯度划分问题，提出梯度对齐路由（GAR），通过负载归一化的路由器目标奖励梯度对齐的观测分组，在多任务文本分类上取得优于多种基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏专家模型虽能均匀分配流量，却仍可能把不兼容的训练信号分到同一专家中。作者希望从梯度划分角度研究路由，使同一专家聚合梯度方向一致的样本，从而提升多任务学习效果。

Method: 提出梯度对齐路由（GAR），其负载归一化的路由器目标奖励梯度对齐的观测被分到同一专家。在五个多任务文本分类混合数据集上，与仅任务损失路由、梯度组合与梯度冲突方法以及负载均衡损失进行对比；设置包括可训练RoBERTa骨干加分类头专家、冻结DeBERTa与Qwen3-1.7B骨干加低秩适配器专家，并考察Top-1路由、可训练全参数FFN专家和更大骨干。

Result: GAR取得最高聚合验证准确率：RoBERTa设置下比仅任务损失路由高1.07个百分点；冻结DeBERTa与Qwen3-1.7B加低秩适配器设置下再次排名第一，高1.10个百分点，同时专家负载更均衡、梯度质量纯度更高。负载均衡损失能进一步压平负载，但纯度仍接近仅任务损失水平。Top-1路由、可训练全参数FFN专家和更大骨干也显示正向聚合收益。

Conclusion: 结果区分了专家负载均衡与基于梯度的路由组织，表明梯度信息指导的路由在多任务文本分类中具有预测价值。

Abstract: Sparse expert models can distribute traffic evenly while still grouping incompatible training signals within the same experts. We study routing as a gradient-partitioning problem and introduce gradient-aligned routing (GAR), whose load-normalized router objective rewards grouping observations with aligned gradients. On five multi-task text-classification mixtures, we compare GAR with task-loss-only routing, gradient-combination and gradient-conflict methods, and load-balancing losses. With a fully trainable RoBERTa backbone and classification-head experts, GAR has the highest aggregate validation accuracy, 1.07 percentage points above task-loss-only routing. With frozen DeBERTa and Qwen3-1.7B backbones and low-rank adapter experts, it again ranks first, 1.10 points above task-loss-only routing, with better-balanced expert load and higher gradient-mass purity, the share of each expert's gradient-norm mass from its dominant task; the load-balancing losses flatten load further but leave this purity near its task-loss-only level. Top-1 routing, trainable full-parameter feed-forward network (FFN) experts, and a larger backbone also show positive aggregate gains. The results distinguish expert-load balance from gradient-based routing organization and indicate the predictive value of gradient-informed routing in multi-task text classification.

</details>


### [177] [Backpropagated Output Momentum: Relocating Optimizer History from Parameters to Task Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36738)
*Yuchen Li,Zongqi Fan,Nguyen H. Tran,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Backpropagated Output Momentum (BOM)，将动量存储从参数级梯度滑动平均改为输出端预测误差的紧凑滑动平均，并在每步通过当前网络重投影，作为插件减少优化器状态并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化器动量以参数规模的滑动平均存储历史梯度，成本高，且将历史信号固定在计算时的坐标中，无法随网络变化而更新。

Method: BOM 在模型输出端存储预测误差的紧凑滑动平均，每步通过当前网络重投影该历史；保留当前监督梯度，可替代多种自适应优化器的第一动量分量，兼容已有状态压缩优化器。

Result: 参数规模优化器状态减少 49.7-99.8%（三种组合），三个语言骨干上平均配对步时间降低 4.0%；语言与视觉微调平均验证性能提升，在主要五任务比较中提升 1.42 分；语言与视觉预训练及机制对照实验进一步验证。

Conclusion: 将动量重定位到输出空间并经当前网络重投影，可在大幅压缩优化器状态的同时保持或提升性能，具有跨输出空间与模型规模的适用性。

Abstract: Optimizer momentum is usually stored as a parameter-sized moving average of past gradients, which makes history costly and fixes each past signal in the coordinates in which it was computed. We introduce Backpropagated Output Momentum (BOM), which instead stores a compact moving average of prediction errors at the model output and reprojects that history through the current network at every step. A batch-level analysis characterizes the information retained and omitted by this relocation, while the implementation preserves the current supervised gradient and can replace the first-moment component of several adaptive optimizers. As a plug-in for momentum-based optimizers, including ones that already compress their state, BOM reduces parameter-shaped optimizer state by 49.7-99.8% in three compositions and, averaged over three language backbones, paired step time by 4.0%. It also improves mean validation performance across language and vision fine-tuning, by 1.42 points in the primary five-task comparison. Language and vision pretraining studies, together with matched mechanism controls, further test the construction across output spaces and model scales.

</details>


### [178] [Efficient Offline Learning of Ranking Policies via Top-$k$ Policy Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36740)
*Ren Kishimoto,Koichi Tanaka,Haruka Kiyohara,Yusuke Narita,Yasuo Yamamoto,Nobuyuki Shimizu,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 R-POD，一种将排序策略分解为 top-k 选择与底部排序两阶段、结合策略梯度与回归的离线排序策略学习方法，旨在降低方差并控制偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商、新闻等平台的排序推荐需要在仅有历史日志数据的条件下学习新的排序策略（离线策略学习，OPL）。但排序场景的动作空间是唯一物品的排列组合，规模极其庞大，使 OPL 异常困难。现有策略梯度方法因重要性加权面临高方差，回归方法虽规避方差却可能产生严重偏差。

Method: R-POD 将排序策略分解为两阶段：第一阶段策略负责选取 top-k 动作，第二阶段策略在给定 top-k 的前提下选择底部动作。第一阶段用新的策略梯度估计器学习，第二阶段用回归方法学习。由于重要性加权只施加于 top-k 动作，可显著降低方差。

Result: 该方法能实质性降低方差；作者进一步证明，在一种“条件成对正确性”条件下，第一阶段的策略梯度估计器是无偏的——该条件仅要求共享相同 top-k 动作的成对排序之间，其期望奖励差可被正确估计。

Conclusion: R-POD 有效融合策略梯度与回归两类方法，在抑制方差的同时避免严重偏差，为大规模排序动作空间下的离线策略学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Many recommender systems such as for e-commerce and news platforms aim to provide users with rankings they are likely to interact with. Off-Policy Learning (OPL) of ranking policies enables us to learn new ranking policies using only historical logged data. However, ranking settings make OPL remarkably challenging because their action spaces consist of permutations of unique items, being extremely large. Existing methods primarily use either policy- or regression-based approaches. The policy-based approach, which typically uses importance-weighted policy gradients, can suffer from high variance due to large action spaces. The regression-based approach, on the other hand, estimates the expected reward using conventional machine learning methods, avoiding variance issues but potentially suffering from severe bias. To circumvent these issues of existing methods, we propose a new OPL method for ranking, named Ranking Policy Optimization via Top-$k$ Policy Decomposition (R-POD), which combines the policy- and regression-based approaches in an effective fashion. Specifically, R-POD decomposes a ranking policy into a first-stage policy for selecting top-$k$ actions and a second-stage policy for choosing the bottom actions given the top-$k$ actions. It learns the first-stage policy using a new policy gradient estimator and the second-stage policy via the regression-based approach. This method can substantially reduce variance, since it applies importance weighting only to the top-$k$ actions. We also demonstrate that our policy-gradient estimator for the first-stage policy is unbiased under a conditional pairwise correctness condition, which only requires that the expected reward differences of pairs of rankings sharing the same top-$k$ actions can be estimated correctly.

</details>


### [179] [Group-Marginalized Self-Rewarding RL Drives Zero-Label Self-Evolving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36750)
*Yiming Wang,Yikang Liu,Qingyuan Tian,Xingyu Chen,Zhuosheng Zhang,Zhaopeng Tu,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Group-Marginalized Advantage Estimation (GMAE)，通过将奖励实现跨上下文聚合为响应级分布并估计期望优势，解决自奖励 RL 中单一分组上下文导致奖励信号不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于集成（ensemble）的自奖励 RL 方法从 rollout 分组中构建奖励参照并分配奖励。但一个响应的奖励表示还依赖于其随机采样到的分组上下文（即同组其他响应）。仅使用单一分组上下文实现可能遗漏期望的奖励信号，为策略优化提供不可靠的指导。

Method: 提出 Group-Marginalized Advantage Estimation (GMAE)，将奖励实现跨可能上下文聚合成响应级分布，并基于该分布估计期望优势。

Result: 在八个基准和四个基础模型上的实验表明性能强劲，且具有跨域泛化能力；GMAE 还表现出学习稳定、额外开销低，并且在训练数据集与 RL 骨干上具有良好适用性。

Conclusion: 通过边缘化分组上下文来估计期望优势，可获得更可靠的奖励信号，从而实现稳定、低成本且通用的自奖励强化学习。

Abstract: Self-rewarding reinforcement learning (RL) enables large language models (LLMs) to self-evolve without human labels. Existing ensemble-based methods construct reward references from rollout groups and assign rewards accordingly. However, a response's reward representation also depends on its randomly sampled group context, i.e., the other responses in its group. Using only one group-context realization may miss desired reward signals and provide unreliable guidance for policy optimization. To address this issue, we propose Group-Marginalized Advantage Estimation (GMAE), which aggregates reward realizations across possible contexts into a response-level distribution and estimates expected advantages. Experiments across eight benchmarks and four base models demonstrate strong performance and cross-domain generalization. GMAE also exhibits stable learning, low extra cost, and good applicability across training datasets and RL backbones.

</details>


### [180] [cktFormer: Transformer-Based Approach for Automated Analog Circuit Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36752)
*Pasindu Dodampegama,Praveen Wijesinghe,Naveen Basnayake,Keshawa Jayasundara,Tharindu Bandaragoda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双Transformer架构，通过节点预测与边预测两个互联模型从图表示中学习电路拓扑，生成比AnalogGenie和cktGNN更有效的电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路设计复杂且迭代性强，需兼顾功耗、成本和信号完整性等约束，但人工设计耗时且易出错；尽管制造流程其他环节已受益于AI优化，电路设计仍是瓶颈，因此需要利用生成式AI与机器学习来自动化并提升该环节的效率与准确性。

Method: 提出双Transformer架构，利用注意力机制建模电路的非序列化复杂关系；将网表数据组织为图结构表示，以学习电路拓扑与元件交互。系统由两个相互关联的模型组成：提议元件的节点预测模型和推断有效连接的边预测模型，二者协作且解耦，兼顾元件级语义与整体结构一致性。

Result: 在实验中，该架构在生成电路的有效性方面优于AnalogGenie和cktGNN等近期模型。

Conclusion: 该工作针对现有方法的关键局限，推进了电子工程自动化，并为AI驱动的电路综合提供了基准。

Abstract: Circuit design is a complex and iterative process that requires expertise in electronic engineering. It involves selecting components while meeting performance constraints, such as power efficiency, cost-effectiveness, and signal integrity. However, manual design is time-consuming and prone to errors. Although other stages of the manufacturing pipeline have benefited from AI-driven optimizations, circuit design remains a bottleneck, limiting overall productivity. Generative AI and machine learning offer the potential to automate and improve this stage, boosting efficiency and accuracy. To address this, we introduce a dual transformer architecture that bridges the gap between AI and circuit design by leveraging attention mechanisms to model complex, non-sequential circuit relationships. Our approach structures netlist data into graph-based representations, enabling effective learning of circuit topology and component interactions. The system consists of two interlinked models: a node prediction model that proposes components and an edge prediction model that infers valid connections. This collaborative and decoupled design captures both component-level semantics and global structural coherence. In our experiments, this architecture outperforms recent models such as AnalogGenie and cktGNN in the validity of generated circuits. By addressing key limitations in existing methods, our work advances automation in electronics engineering and contributes a benchmark for AI-driven circuit synthesis.

</details>


### [181] [QuantMLA: Function-Aligned Dual-Path Quantization for Low-Bit MLA KV Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36760)
*Zunhai Su,Yuxuan Sun,Jianchao Tan,Tao Zhang,Ruihan Hu,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 QuantMLA，一个针对 MLA 双路径（content 与 RoPE）低比特量化的函数对齐框架，实现首次内容与 RoPE 缓存的联合 INT4 缓存，并可进一步将内容缓存压至 INT2 而保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA 虽能实现紧凑缓存，但缓存内存仍随上下文长度和批量大小线性增长。现有量化方法缺乏对 MLA 双路径量化误差的系统建模，尤其是 RoPE 路径误差的显著放大问题未被充分理解，导致难以在低比特下保持精度。

Method: 首先建立 MLA 双路径量化误差的系统模型，刻画其对注意力输出失真的不同影响并解释 RoPE 路径误差的放大机制。在此基础上提出 QuantMLA：推导路径特定的变换空间，在全精度计算保真且可离线融合进模型参数的前提下消除在线变换开销；在该空间内以函数对齐目标学习路径特定变换——注意力输出重建捕获内容路径的耦合匹配与聚合误差，位置 QK 重建保留注意力 logits 中 RoPE 分量并给出输出失真的理论界。此外开发原生低比特 MLA 注意力内核，将解包与反量化直接融入注意力计算。

Result: 在四个 MLA 模型家族上，QuantMLA 实现了首次内容缓存与 RoPE 缓存的联合 INT4 缓存且精度损失极小；将内容缓存进一步压至 INT2、RoPE key 缓存保持 INT4，在挑战性推理与代码基准上仍保持竞争力。物理缓存布局在 128K 上下文下实现 3.59 倍压缩；在缓存压力服务负载下，整任务输出吞吐比 BF16 高 5.168 倍。

Conclusion: 通过双路径量化误差建模与函数对齐的路径特定变换，QuantMLA 在极低比特下实现 MLA 紧凑缓存的高效压缩与吞吐提升，代码将在接收后发布。

Abstract: Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA) enables expressive multi-head attention with compact caches for its content and decoupled RoPE paths, yet cache memory still scales linearly with context length and batch size. In this work, we establish a systematic model of MLA's dual-path quantization errors, characterizing their distinct effects on attention-output distortion and explaining the pronounced amplification of RoPE-path errors. Guided by this analysis, we introduce QuantMLA, a function-aligned framework for low-bit dual-path quantization. We derive path-specific transformation spaces that preserve full-precision computation while remaining fully fusible into model parameters offline, eliminating online transformation overhead. Within these spaces, QuantMLA learns path-specific transformations with function-aligned objectives: attention-output reconstruction captures the content path's coupled matching and aggregation errors, while positional QK reconstruction preserves the RoPE-induced component of the attention logits and admits a theoretical bound on output distortion. Across four MLA model families, QuantMLA enables, to our knowledge, the first reported joint INT4 caching of the content and RoPE caches with minimal accuracy degradation. Further compressing the content cache to INT2 while retaining the RoPE key cache at INT4 maintains competitive performance on challenging reasoning and code benchmarks. We develop a native low-bit MLA attention kernel that integrates unpacking and dequantization directly into attention computation. The physical cache layout provides 3.59x compression at 128K context, while a cache-pressure serving workload achieves 5.168x higher whole-job output throughput than BF16. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [182] [Graph-Spectral Flow Matching for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36765)
*Zepeng Zhang,Jhony H. Giraldo,Wenbin Wang,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRASP框架，将图结构引入flow matching的概率路径以实现多元时间序列异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准flow matching采用线性概率路径，忽略变量间依赖关系，导致与结构化数据分布不匹配。

Method: 通过最小化结合动能与图Dirichlet能量的固定端点作用量，将图结构融入概率路径，得到基于图频率依赖双曲插值的闭式路径；用正常数据训练速度预测器，通过加权速度差异进行异常检测。

Result: 理论上证明GRASP对拉普拉斯特征基选择不变，并将期望oracle异常分数分解为有界端点不确定性与图频率加权Fisher差异；在四个基准上取得优异性能。

Conclusion: GRASP通过图谱路径有效提升多元时间序列异常检测性能，验证了其路径与加权机制的有效性。

Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection typically relies on evaluating discrepancies between observations and outputs produced by models trained on normal data. An alternative perspective is to characterize the distribution of normal data through the generative dynamics, i.e., the velocity field, of flow matching models. However, standard flow matching typically adopts linear probability paths that overlook dependencies among variables, leading to a misalignment with the structured data distribution. To address this issue, we propose GRASP, a flow matching framework with a graph-spectral path for multivariate time series anomaly detection. GRASP incorporates graph structure into the probability path by minimizing a fixed-endpoint action that combines kinetic energy with graph Dirichlet energy. This formulation yields a closed-form path based on graph-frequency-dependent hyperbolic interpolation. A velocity predictor trained on normal data then detects anomalies using weighted velocity discrepancies aggregated across source samples, flow times, and graph frequencies. Theoretically, we establish that GRASP is invariant to the choice of Laplacian eigenbasis and decompose its expected oracle anomaly score into bounded endpoint uncertainty and graph-frequency-weighted Fisher discrepancy. Experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate the superior anomaly detection performance of GRASP and validate the effectiveness of its graph-spectral path and weighting mechanism.

</details>


### [183] [When Can Prefixes Compile LoRA? Exact Resource-Capped Tests for Frozen Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36766)
*Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"固定连续前缀能否替代给定低秩适配器（注意力头冻结）"，给出可观测性、可实现性、实现性三类条件及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在注意力头冻结情况下，用固定连续前缀替代低秩适配器的可行性，明确替代成立的条件与误差来源。

Method: 通过因果读出下的可观测性分析、共同查询下前缀约化为两个聚合变量并构建带范数上限的二阶锥规划，以及仿射查询暴露下2r个带符号槽位近似秩-r值更新的构造进行理论推演，并在GPT-2首层读出与float64、bfloat16下做数值验证。

Result: 可观测性上，目标若在相同摘要的两个输入上不同，则任意前缀长度都存在误差下界，范数上限可将其扩展到近乎相等的摘要；实现性上，带范数上限的最优解为可达的二阶锥程序，两个等范数秩一值更新分居可编译性两侧；实现性上，2r个带符号槽位可近似秩-r值更新但取值以O(ε^{-3/2})增长，400项容差检查在float64通过而在bfloat16仅通过38项；三个GPT-2首层读出中，约18.4%至74.2%的投影适配器效应未被编译，且值-查询顺序依赖具体头。

Conclusion: 固定连续前缀能否替代低秩适配器取决于适配器目标，受可观测性、可实现性与实现性三条件约束；所有结论仅针对单头局部近似，而非整网等价。

Abstract: Can a fixed continuous prefix replace a given low-rank adapter while the attention head stays frozen? In this research, we show that the answer depends on the adapter's target through three conditions. First, observability: at one causal readout, every independent key--value prefix sees the content only through the query, attention partition, and value numerator, so a target that differs on two inputs with equal summaries incurs an error floor at every prefix length; norm caps extend this floor to nearly equal summaries. Second, realizability: at a common query, any prefix reduces exactly to two aggregate variables, and the norm-capped optimum is an attained second-order-cone program, also after a fixed output projection; it places two equal-norm rank-one value updates on opposite sides of compilability. Third, implementation: under affine query exposure, $2r$ signed slots approximate a rank-$r$ value update, but their values grow as $O(ε^{-3/2})$, and the construction passes all 400 tolerance checks in float64 yet only 38 in bfloat16. A first-layer GPT-2 readout with fixed token and position meets the common-query condition without clamping activations; at three such heads, the capped optimum leaves 18.4\% to 74.2\% of the projected adapter effect uncompiled, with a head-dependent value--query ordering. All claims concern local approximation at one head, not whole-network equivalence.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Conditional Independence: Root Cause Analysis with Deep Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36771)
*Md Musfiqur Rahman,Kenneth Lee,Ziwei Jiang,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Ruocheng Guo,Murat Kocaoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RCA-DCM，将根因分析建模为因果模型结构函数的变化，通过分布约束检验处理隐变量，并在多个数据集上超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析方法依赖启发式方法或带强无混杂假设的条件独立性检验，无法利用存在隐变量时的复杂分布约束，因此作者希望放宽这些假设。

Method: 将底层系统建模为因果模型，将异常系统视为同一因果模型结构函数的变化；建立分布约束检验与根因分析之间的隐式联系以处理未观测混杂；采用深度因果模型（DCM）框架，用神经网络设计因果模型以适配任意因果模型生成的数据；并利用不同层次的局部图知识执行 RCA。

Result: 在模拟数据集、基于物理的因果腔和两个微服务应用上与先进基线比较。RCA-DCM 在 Sock Shop 上 top-1 准确率为 0.880（对比 0.752），在 Online Boutique 上为 0.776（对比 0.712）；当真实根因未被观测并作为隐混杂因子时，完美恢复率为 0.846（对比 0.731）。

Conclusion: RCA-DCM 能有效放宽现有方法假设，在隐混杂和部分图知识场景下优于所有竞争方法，证明其根因分析的有效性。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical problem in many real-world scenarios. RCA enables the identification of faulty or failing mechanisms in a system by comparing anomalous observations with corresponding reference (i.e., regular) observations. However, existing approaches rely either on heuristic methods or on conditional independence tests with a strong unconfoundedness assumption, and thus fail to exploit other complicated distributional constraints in the presence of latent variables. To relax these assumptions, we model the underlying system as a causal model and the anomalous system as a change in the structural functions of the same causal model. Specifically, to handle unobserved confounders, we establish an implicit connection between distributional constraint testing and root cause analysis. To adapt our approach to data generated from arbitrary causal models, we employ the deep causal model (DCM) framework, in which we design the causal model using neural networks. Finally, we illustrate how our method, RCA-DCM, can utilize different levels of partial graphical knowledge to perform RCA. We evaluate RCA-DCM against state-of-the-art baselines on simulated datasets, a physics-based causal chamber and two micro-service applications. RCA-DCM improves top-1 accuracy over the strongest baseline on both Sock Shop (0.880 vs. 0.752) and Online Boutique (0.776 vs. 0.712), and when the true root cause in the causal chamber is unobserved and acts as a latent confounder, it recovers the exact root-cause set more often than any competing method (perfect recovery rate (PRR) 0.846 vs. 0.731).

</details>


### [185] [Harnessing Large Language Models to Compile Task-Relevant Context into Bayesian Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36788)
*Zhongwei Yu,Sourabh Roy,Bin Cao,Xue Yan,Anjie Liu,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出HarBO框架，将LLM编译的广义上下文整合进标准贝叶斯优化（BO），并给出不完美编译下的遗憾分析，实验表明其可与专用LLM方法竞争，但在陌生领域可靠性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富任务上下文（如领域知识、外部观测）对贝叶斯优化至关重要，但将其有效融入BO仍具挑战。近期实践者用LLM通过编码框架生成并执行BO程序，使得后验信念由贝叶斯推断和LLM生成的模型与数据制品共同塑造，为任务上下文以可执行代码形式进入BO提供了灵活途径。论文旨在研究LLM能否以及如何被用于为BO编译多样化的上下文信号。

Method: 作者将LLM编译的BO形式化为广义上下文决策问题，提出HarBO——一个BO专用harness，通过经验证的多阶段工作流将广义上下文编译为标准BO的核心制品。理论上分析了不完美编译下的遗憾以及新增上下文的影响。在合成函数和真实世界基准上进行实验，与基于LLM嵌入的专用BO方法和LLM直接参与循环的BO方法比较。

Result: LLM harness能有效将上下文编译进标准BO，取得与专用LLM嵌入方法和直接LLM-in-the-loop方法相当的性能。通用编码harness在超参数优化等熟悉领域表现良好，但在陌生、上下文丰富的领域表现不佳。

Conclusion: LLM harness是让丰富任务上下文在BO中可用的一条有前景的路径，但并非自动可靠，其有效性依赖领域熟悉度。

Abstract: Incorporating rich task-relevant context, such as domain knowledge and external observations, is a key capability yet remains challenging for Bayesian optimisation (BO). Recently, practitioners have started to use large language models (LLMs) to generate and execute BO programs through coding harnesses. In such emerging practices, the posterior belief is shaped not only by Bayesian inference but also by LLM-generated model and data artefacts, offering a flexible route for task context to enter BO as executable code. To study whether and how LLMs can be harnessed to compile diverse contextual signals for BO, we formulate LLM-compiled BO as generalised-context decision making. We propose HarBO, a BO-specialised harness that compiles generalised context into the core artefacts of standard BO through a validated multi-stage workflow. Our theory analyses the regret under imperfect compilation and the effect of adding new context. Across synthetic functions and real-world benchmarks, we find that LLM harnesses can effectively compile context into standard BO, achieving competitive performance with specialised LLM-embedding-based and direct LLM-in-the-loop BO methods. General coding harnesses can be effective in familiar domains such as hyperparameter optimisation, but fall short in unfamiliar, context-rich domains. Together, these results establish LLM harnesses as a promising, but not automatically reliable, route for making rich task context usable in BO.

</details>


### [186] [RAE-PPG: Duration-Grounded Retain-and-Extend Pretraining for PPG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36794)
*Suyeong Lee,Hochang Lee,Seokyong Sheem,Daekyum Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAE-PPG（Retain-and-Extend PPG），一种按信号时长递进组织自监督的 PPG 基础模型：单个 Transformer 编码器依次在 10 s、30 s、240 s 输入上训练，通过时长专属参数组与目标复用，实现特征保留与扩展。最终冻结编码器在 8 个数据集 18 项任务中于 12 项取得最优成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPG 的不同信号特征需要不同的信号时长才能被刻画。现有 PPG 基础模型仅把时长当作预训练或评估条件，未利用"不同特征需要不同时长"这一特性来组织自监督学习。作者假设自监督应随信号时长扩展，使单一编码器逐步习得新特征，同时保留并复用已有学习成果。

Method: 提出 RAE-PPG：单一 Transformer 编码器依次在 10 s、30 s、240 s 输入上训练，每个更长时长阶段为对应时长可支持的特征新增监督信号。编码器划分为时长专属参数组，后续阶段可复用早期参数组，仅更新当前阶段所属组。同时复用部分早期目标来监督后续阶段，促使相应特征在长输入表示中保持可访问。

Result: 从最终编码器直接解码表明：早期特征在长输入表示中仍可恢复，而后期阶段特征在其引入时长下具有更高的平均解码性能。对照实验显示，在两个阶段转换中，前一阶段的学习都为新引入特征的学习提供了更好的基础。在 8 个数据集的 18 项任务上，最终冻结编码器在其中 12 项取得对比五种现有 PPG 基础模型的最优成绩。

Conclusion: 按信号时长递进、保留并扩展自监督的方式可有效训练单一 PPG 编码器，兼顾早期特征的可恢复性与新特征的学习效果，并在多任务上优于现有 PPG 基础模型。

Abstract: Signal features derived from photoplethysmography (PPG) require different signal durations to characterize. Existing PPG foundation models treat duration as a pretraining or evaluation condition rather than using the different durations required by PPG features to organize self-supervision. We hypothesize that self-supervision should expand with signal duration, allowing a single encoder to progressively acquire additional features while preserving and reusing earlier learning. We introduce Retain-and-Extend PPG (RAE-PPG), which trains a single Transformer encoder successively on 10 s, 30 s, and 240 s inputs, adding supervision for signal features supported by each longer observation. The encoder is partitioned into duration-specific parameter groups, allowing later stages to reuse earlier groups while updating only the group assigned to the current stage. Selected earlier targets are reused to supervise later stages, encouraging the corresponding features to remain accessible in longer-input representations. Direct decoding from the final encoder shows that earlier features remain recoverable from longer-input representations, while later-stage features show higher mean decoding performance at their introduction durations. Controlled comparisons further show that prior-stage learning provides a better basis for learning newly introduced features at both transitions. Across 18 tasks from eight datasets, the final frozen encoder achieves the best observed score on 12 tasks compared with five existing PPG foundation models.

</details>


### [187] [Where Does Randomness Matter in Neural Cellular Automata?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36797)
*Fei Zuo,Jiaqi Shi,Yujing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过分离神经细胞自动机（NCA）的训练与评估更新模式，探究随机更新是否有助学习及是否须在执行时保留，并揭示优化可靠性、执行模式与任务特定行为应被区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCA整个生命周期（含BPTT与最终rollout）普遍使用随机细胞更新，导致两个问题纠缠不清：更新随机性是否有助于学得有用规则，以及该随机性是否必须保留到执行阶段。论文旨在解耦这两点。

Method: 在受控的Growing NCA实验中分离训练与评估更新模式，并改变训练时展示的状态；对平移不变的标量格点推导精确的均方判据，分析随机掩码对均值模态与方差的影响；对比grow、persist、regenerate等训练配方在4096步与损伤恢复下的表现。

Result: 标准constant-rate persist配方下，异步训练10/10通过短时质量测试，同步仅3/10；十个异步模型在确定性评估下均保持目标4096步。均方判据显示随机掩码可阻尼均值模态但注入方差，仅用均值检验会误判四个非边缘设置。30个通过同一重建测试的模型中，十个grow-trained里八个在4096步偏离目标，而persist与regenerate均保持目标；损伤恢复可区分persist与regenerate。

Conclusion: 研究结果应区分优化可靠性、执行模式与任务特定行为，而非将它们视为单一稳定性属性；执行阶段无需保留训练随机性，且训练时展示状态影响任务行为。

Abstract: Stochastic cell updates are often used throughout the life of a neural cellular automaton (NCA), from backpropagation through time to final rollout. This leaves two questions entangled: does update randomness help learn a useful rule, and must that randomness remain at execution? We separate training and evaluation update modes in controlled Growing NCA experiments, then vary the states shown during training. Under the standard constant-rate persist recipe, asynchronous training passes the short-horizon quality test in 10/10 runs, compared with 3/10 synchronous runs. All ten asynchronous models also retain the target for 4,096 steps under deterministic evaluation. For a scalar translation-invariant lattice, we derive an exact mean-square criterion: random masking can damp mean modes, but it also injects variance, and a mean-only test misclassifies four non-marginal settings. Finally, among 30 models that all pass the same reconstruction test, eight of ten grow-trained models become off-target at 4,096 steps, while all persist and regenerate models retain the target; damage recovery separates persist from regenerate. The results distinguish optimization reliability, execution mode, and task-specific behavior instead of treating them as one stability property.

</details>


### [188] [EasyPPO: Stabilizing the Critic Is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36802)
*Xuanyi Zhou,Qiuyang Mang,Huanzhi Mao,Dacheng Li,Wenhao Chai,Mayank Mishra,Yichuan Wang,Karthik Narasimhan,Alvin Cheung,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现PPO中critic是LLM强化学习不稳定的主要来源，识别出两种critic失效模式，并提出EasyPPO方法加以解决，在多个任务上稳定且超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO的关键优势在于其学习到的critic能利用历史轨迹估计期望回报并降低策略梯度方差。然而作者发现critic也是大语言模型强化学习中不稳定的主要来源，具体表现为两种失效模式导致训练不稳定。

Method: 识别两种critic失效模式：1）从actor和critic中过滤截断的rollout会将策略目标转变为以完成为条件的奖励，导致截断增加而条件奖励改善；2）异质性回报噪声会使高方差prompt在有限批次中主导critic更新。提出EasyPPO方法：采用仅对actor进行过长过滤的机制，让critic在完整和截断rollout的回报上训练；使用噪声归一化critic回归，按每个prompt采样回报标准差的倒数对其critic损失加权；并使用适度更小的critic小批次以限制梯度裁剪时的离群影响。

Result: 在FrontierCS连续奖励编码、AIME24二元奖励数学推理和Search-R1多轮搜索任务上，EasyPPO在整个训练周期内保持稳定，并持续优于vanilla PPO、VAPO和HL-Gauss PPO。其最佳验证分数相对PPO分别提升14.89%、2.28%和9.47%。

Conclusion: EasyPPO通过针对性的critic修正策略有效解决了PPO在LLM强化学习中的不稳定问题，并在多样化任务上验证了其稳定性和性能优势，表明关注critic失效模式对提升大模型强化学习稳定性具有重要意义。

Abstract: A key strength of Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) is its learned critic, which uses historical trajectories collected during reinforcement learning to estimate expected returns and reduce policy-gradient variance. However, we find that the critic is also a major source of instability in reinforcement learning for large language models (LLMs). We identify two critic failure modes that destabilize PPO. First, filtering truncated rollouts from both actor and critic shifts the policy objective to reward conditioned on completion, allowing truncation to increase even as conditional reward improves. Second, heterogeneous return noise can cause high-variance prompts to dominate critic updates in finite batches. We introduce EasyPPO to address these failures. Actor-only overlong filtering trains the critic on returns from both completed and truncated rollouts. Noise-normalized critic regression weights each prompt's critic loss by the inverse standard deviation of its sampled returns, balancing noise contributions across prompts. Moderately smaller critic mini-batches confine outlier influence to fewer rollouts during gradient clipping. Across continuous-reward coding on FrontierCS, binary-reward mathematical reasoning on AIME24, and multi-turn search on Search-R1, EasyPPO remains stable throughout the full training horizon and consistently outperforms vanilla PPO, VAPO, and HL-Gauss PPO. Its best validation scores show relative gains of 14.89%, 2.28%, and 9.47% over PPO, respectively.

</details>


### [189] [Diffusion Policy Improvement with Proposal-Conditioned Refinement Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36812)
*Junhyun Ha,Juho Lee,Byungwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PReFlow，一种结合critic提案选择与条件精化流的策略提取方法，在50个OGBench任务上取得竞争性离线性能及在线微调后最高总分（500K步达91%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流策略能建模离线RL中的复杂行为，但对其与行为策略KL散度的惩罚会抑制那些行为密度低却critic值高的动作。直接精化行为提案是一种替代方案，然而高斯或确定性编辑器表达能力有限，无法表示同一提案下多个分离的模式。

Method: PReFlow将基于critic的提案选择与条件精化流结合。为联合优化提案选择与精化，提出KL正则化目标，其最优解在高斯平滑行为先验下诱导出关于最终动作的Gibbs策略。精化流可表示多个高价值模式，而以提案为中心的高斯参考调节大幅动作变化。该高斯参考使方法能利用无需仿真、闭式的伴随匹配目标，从采样端点与critic梯度出发，得到单一的速度回归损失，无需反向伴随求解。

Result: 在50个OGBench任务上，PReFlow取得竞争性的离线性能，并在在线微调后于所比较方法中获得最高总分，500K环境步后达到91%。

Conclusion: PReFlow通过提案选择与条件精化流的联合优化，克服了KL惩罚抑制高价值动作及编辑器表达力受限的问题，实现高效且表达力强的策略提取，并在离线和在线微调场景中表现优异。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow policies can model complex behaviors in offline reinforcement learning (RL). However, penalizing their KL divergence from the behavior policy can discourage actions having high critic values with low behavior density. Directly refining behavior proposals may be an alternative, yet Gaussian or deterministic editors limit expressiveness to represent multiple separated modes for the same proposal. In this work, we introduce Proposal-Conditioned Refinement Flows (PReFlow), a policy extraction method combining critic-based proposal selection with a conditional refinement flow. To optimize proposal selection and refinement together, we formulate a KL-regularized objective whose optimum induces a Gibbs policy over final actions under a Gaussian-smoothed behavior prior. The refinement flow can represent multiple high value modes, while a proposal-centered Gaussian reference regulates large action changes. This Gaussian reference further enables us to make use of simulation-free, closed form adjoint matching targets from sampled endpoints and critic gradients, yielding a single velocity regression loss without a backward adjoint solve. On 50 OGBench tasks, PReFlow achieves competitive offline performance and the highest aggregate score among the compared methods after online fine-tuning, reaching 91\% after 500K environment steps.

</details>


### [190] [RESCUE: Repairing Language Model Errors to Sparse Circuits via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36813)
*Chuanpu Liu,Miao Yu,Yikai Cai,Yuanhe Zhang,Zhenhong Zhou,Li Sun,Zuming Jiang,Yufei Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出RESCUE框架，通过定位并编辑与推理错误相关的稀疏电路来修复LLM在多种任务上的失败，在数学推理和医疗问答上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路研究多聚焦于保留功能或解释安全性，忽略了LLM在更广泛任务中失败背后的机制，本文尝试从"能力"扩展到"错误"，探索失败是否同样源于内部的错误计算，并可通过针对性调参加以纠正。

Method: 提出RESCUE（Reasoning-Error Sparse-Circuit Uncovering and Editing）框架：先定位错误相关电路，再用强化学习配合多次掩码模型rollout优化掩码以解决长生成中前缀漂移导致的SFT掩码优化遗漏问题，最后通过剪枝技术精确微调错误电路，实现稀疏且有针对性的模型更新。

Result: 在数学推理任务上识别出密度1.40%的数学错误电路，修复后准确率从6.0%提升至75.5%；在医疗问答任务上识别出密度1.44%的紧凑电路，将修复集准确率从0%提升至81%。

Conclusion: 失败可被解释为内部错误计算，通过定位并编辑错误相关稀疏电路，可在保持其他能力的同时有效修复任务失败，验证了从能力分析扩展到错误分析的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong general capabilities that mechanistic interpretability has attributed to sparse computational circuits. However, existing circuit studies emphasize preserving functionality or explaining safety, leaving the mechanisms underlying failures across a broader range of tasks largely unexplored. Extending circuit analysis from abilities to errors, we explore the perspective that such failures may likewise arise from erroneous internal computations and that targeted tuning of the corresponding parameters can correct such errors while largely preserving other capabilities. Motivated by this insight, we introduce RESCUE (Reasoning-Error Sparse-Circuit Uncovering and Editing), a framework that localizes error-associated circuits and surgically repairs them for performance enhancement. General tasks typically involve multi-step reasoning and long-form generation, where early deviations can cause prefixes to drift from supervised references, leading SFT-based mask optimization to overlook circuits involved in generation-time errors. RESCUE therefore refines these masks through reinforcement learning with multiple masked-model rollouts, improving their relevance to observed task failures. Finally, RESCUE introduces a pruning technique and precisely fine-tunes error circuits to correct task failures, thereby translating error localization into a sparse and targeted model update. We validate RESCUE on heterogeneous repair sets across two domains: (1) mathematical reasoning, identifying a math error circuit of 1.40% density whose repair raises accuracy from 6.0% to 75.5%; and (2) medical QA, where a similarly compact 1.44% circuit improves repair-set accuracy from 0% to 81%. Our code is available at: https://github.com/chuanpupig/RESCUE.

</details>


### [191] [Towards Better Training Signal: Advantage Clipped Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36816)
*Ruichuan Huang,Jinghan Liu,Congliang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACPO算法，通过裁剪重要性采样比率与优势函数的乘积来稳定梯度估计，在数学推理基准上优于PPO和GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习提升LLM推理能力时，on-policy数据需求限制了训练效率；重用off-policy数据虽能提升效率但会引入不稳定性。现有PPO、GRPO采用IS比率裁剪来稳定训练，但训练稳定性和梯度估计主要由IS比率与优势的乘积决定。

Method: 提出ACPO算法，直接裁剪IS比率与优势函数的乘积，从而获得更稳定的梯度估计。同时建立ACPO与策略镜像下降(PMD)中梯度裁剪的联系，并证明标准RL设定下clipped-PMD的收敛性。

Result: 在常用数学推理基准上，ACPO在准确率和训练效率上持续优于PPO和GRPO，在标准数学基准上取得4-6个百分点的提升（Qwen3-8B+PPO）。

Conclusion: ACPO是LLM强化学习后训练中传统IS比率裁剪的实用且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a cornerstone for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), but the need for on-policy data substantially limits training efficiency. Reusing off-policy data through importance sampling (IS) can improve efficiency but introduce considerable instability. Hence, algorithms such as PPO and GRPO widely adopt IS-ratio clipping to stabilize training. However, training stability and gradient estimate are mainly determined by the product of IS ratio and advantage. To further stabilize training, we propose ACPO, which clips the product of the IS ratio and the advantage, leading to more stable gradient estimates. We also establish a connection between ACPO and gradient clipping in policy mirror descent (PMD), which is a standard technique to stabilize optimization process, and prove the convergence of clipped-PMD under the standard RL setting. Experiments on widely used mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that ACPO consistently outperforms PPO and GRPO in both accuracy and training efficiency, delivering 4-6 percentage points gains on standard math benchmarks, with Qwen3-8B+PPO. Hence, ACPO is a practical and effective alternative to conventional IS-ratio clipping for RL post-training of LLMs.

</details>


### [192] [CorrGRPO: Correlation-Normalized GRPO for Multi-Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36820)
*Wenbin Hu,Huihao Jing,Haochen Shi,Yuxuan Liu,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CorrGRPO，将GRPO中多奖励的成对协方差归一化为Pearson相关系数，在保持中心化总奖励不变的同时，避免大尺度相关奖励主导归一化，从而在代码生成、工具调用与智能体安全任务上优于GRPO及多种变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO通过同组rollout奖励的中心化与标准化计算优势，多奖励时用组内标准差归一化总奖励，其方差等于所有成对奖励协方差之和。这样虽能让更新幅度随奖励依赖性自适应，但大尺度相关奖励会主导归一化，压制小尺度奖励信号。

Method: 提出CorrGRPO（Correlation-Normalized GRPO）：将成对协方差归一化为Pearson相关系数。该方法保持中心化总奖励不变，同时平衡不同尺度奖励对基于相关性的归一化的影响，使优势幅度能随奖励相关性自适应，而不被大尺度奖励成分主导。

Result: 在代码生成、工具调用和智能体安全三类任务上，使用0.5B至8B参数规模的模型，将CorrGRPO与GRPO及其他变体进行比较。这些任务均涉及可协同提升或存在权衡的多奖励。结果显示在三个领域均有提升。代码已开源。

Conclusion: CorrGRPO通过相关性归一化有效缓解多奖励尺度不平衡导致的信号压制问题，在多领域多模型规模下取得一致改进，为多奖励推理语言模型训练提供了更稳健的优势估计方式。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely used to train reasoning language models, where it computes advantages by centering and normalizing rewards across rollouts of the same prompt. For multiple rewards, GRPO sums the reward components and normalizes the total reward by its within-group standard deviation. The corresponding variance equals the sum of all pairwise reward covariances. For a fixed centered reward, larger aggregate covariance produces smaller advantages, and vice versa, allowing update magnitudes to adapt to reward dependence. However, correlated rewards with large scales can dominate this normalization and suppress signals from smaller-scale rewards. We propose Correlation-Normalized GRPO (CorrGRPO), which normalizes pairwise covariances into Pearson correlation coefficients. CorrGRPO keeps the centered total reward unchanged while balancing the influence of differently scaled rewards on the correlation-based normalization. This allows advantage magnitudes to adapt to reward correlations without the normalization being dominated by large-scale reward components. We compare CorrGRPO with GRPO and other variants on code generation, tool calling, and agent security, using models ranging from 0.5B to 8B parameters. These tasks all involve multiple rewards that can improve together or present tradeoffs. Results show improvements across three domains, including code generation, tool calling, and agent security. Our code is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/CorrGRPO.

</details>


### [193] [RolloutFaith: Auditing Persistent Internal Interventions in Visual World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36843)
*Junchi Yao,Ziyi Wang,Youling Huang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出RolloutFaith框架，衡量世界模型内部干预的"持续语义改善"（干预时及后续自主预测），并发现参考激活补丁效果最佳但随预测步数衰减，进而提出Delayed LoReFT以奖励未来后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的预测会成为后续预测的输入，因此有效的内部修正必须在干预停止后仍能持续。现有可解释性方法（探针、激活补丁、学习编辑器）多评估干预当下的效果，难以满足这一更强要求。

Method: 提出RolloutFaith，在固定事件、动作、噪声与信息预算下，同时度量干预时刻预测的语义改善与后续自主预测的表现；在Crafter、Cartpole、CoinRun三个任务、三个世界模型上评估十种拟合编辑器，并用Reference Activation Patching作为参考干预；通过将单个状态分量还原到未干预值定位持续效应路径；提出Delayed LoReFT，在四个冻结的未来转移上优化同一低秩干预。

Result: 参考干预在全部九种模型-任务组合中均改善后续预测，在八种中优于最佳拟合编辑器，但其持续增益在九种组合中的五种随预测步数衰减；现有拟合编辑器仅恢复有限且不一致的长期效果。持续效应路径因模型而异：DIAMOND经由最新生成帧，DreamerV3经由循环记忆，STORM两者兼有。

Conclusion: 结果表明训练应奖励未来后果。作为验证，Delayed LoReFT在四个冻结未来转移上优化同一低秩干预，可在一定程度上提升持续干预效果。

Abstract: Interpretability methods such as probes, activation patches and learned editors are designed to reveal or modify a model's current computation. World models pose a harder requirement: because their predictions become inputs to later predictions, a useful internal correction must survive after editing stops. We therefore propose RolloutFaith, a framework that measures semantic improvement both in the prediction produced at intervention time and over later autonomous predictions under fixed events, actions, noise, and information budgets. We evaluate ten fitted editors on three world models across Crafter, Cartpole, and CoinRun. We also use Reference Activation Patching, which replaces a model activation with the paired activation computed from the real observation, to measure the correction available at the chosen interface. This reference intervention improves later predictions in all nine model and task combinations and outperforms the best fitted editor in eight, yet its sustained gain decreases with horizon in five of nine combinations. Current fitted editors recover only limited and inconsistent long term effects. By restoring individual state components to their untouched values, we find that persistent effects travel through the newest generated frame in DIAMOND, recurrent memory in DreamerV3, and both in STORM. These findings suggest that training should reward future consequences. To test this hypothesis, we propose Delayed LoReFT, which optimizes the same low rank intervention through four frozen future transitions and improves sustained intervention effects to some extent.

</details>


### [194] [An Effective, Reliable, and Robust Framework for Human Activity Recognition Using Wearable Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36848)
*Nafees Ahmad,Ho-fung Leung,Muhammad Adil Abid,Sadia Shakil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对穿戴式传感器人体活动识别（HAR）中多通道判别信息难获取、数据集小导致过拟合的问题，提出 ALAE-TAE-CutMix+ 框架及其扩展版 ALAE-CIE-TAE-CutMix+，通过增强通道潜在信息、建模多潜在特征与活动关系并引入新的数据增强策略，在四个不同领域的 HAR 数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR 中多传感器通道信息对性能至关重要，注意力机制可优先选择判别性通道。但在老年住院患者等场景下，从多通道获取判别信息并非易事，尤其对性质相近的活动难以分类。同时，HAR 模型因数据集小而易过拟合、性能不佳，现有数据增强方法又存在依赖领域、导致测试序列模型失真等缺陷。

Method: 提出 ALAE-TAE-CutMix+ 框架，聚焦两方面：一是增强每个传感器通道的潜在信息，学习多潜在特征与当前活动之间的关系，从而丰富各活动的判别性特征表示；二是引入新的增强策略以克服现有多传感器通道数据增强的不足。并进一步扩展为 ALAE-CIE-TAE-CutMix+，学习捕捉每对传感器通道特征之间的交互。

Result: 第一个框架性能略优于第二个，但第二个框架更可靠、更稳健。两个框架在来自不同领域的四个 HAR 数据集上均显著优于 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: 通过增强通道潜在信息、建模多通道潜在特征关系并引入针对性的数据增强策略，可有效缓解 HAR 中小样本过拟合与相近活动难分类的问题；扩展版本的通道交互建模虽精度略低，但鲁棒性和可靠性更强，整体方案在多领域数据集上表现优越。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) through wearable sensors greatly improves the quality of human life through its multiple applications. For HAR, multi-sensor channel information is vital for optimal performance. Current work states that applying an attention neural network to prioritize discriminatory sensor channels helps the model classify activity more precisely. However, obtaining discriminatory information from multisensory channels is not always trivial, such as when collecting data from older hospitalized patients. In this context, existing HAR methods struggle to classify activities, particularly activities with similar natures. Moreover, HAR models predominantly suffer from overfitting due to the small size of available datasets, which leads to poor performance. Data augmentation (DA) is a viable solution to this problem. However, available DA methods have various drawbacks, including the possibility of being domain-dependent, resulting in distorted models for test sequences. To address these HAR problems, we propose a novel framework, ALAE-TAE-CutMix+, which focuses on two aspects. First, it enhances the latent information across each sensor channel and learns to exploit the relation among multiple latent features and the ongoing activity. Consequently, the discriminatory feature representations of each activity is enriched. Second, a new augmentation strategy is introduced to address the shortcomings of existing multi-sensor channel data augmentation. We then extend the framework to create a further enhanced version, namely ALAE-CIE-TAE-CutMix+, which learns to capture the interactions between the features of each pair of sensor channels. We find that although the first framework performs slightly better than the latter, the latter is nonetheless more reliable and robust. Both frameworks significantly outperform SOTA approaches on the four HAR datasets from diverse domains.

</details>


### [195] [Markovian Nonconvex ADMM for Reinforcement Learning: Bellman-Resolvent Stability Beyond Smooth Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36859)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别并提出一种用于强化学习中 Markovian 非凸 ADMM 的结构性机制：折扣 Bellman 预解算子 $(I-\gamma P_\pi)^{-1}$ 可提供通常由光滑块提供的乘子稳定性。基于此机制，在受控 Markov 采样与随机观测下建立了几乎必然收敛与有限样本复杂度，并进一步讨论了全局最优性条件及多种策略表述下的可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典非凸 ADMM 分析通常依赖某个指定光滑块来获得乘子稳定性，而强化学习问题（折扣 MDP）具有特殊结构但缺乏针对性的理论刻画。作者希望利用折扣 MDP 自身算子结构来解释并保证 Markovian 非凸 ADMM 的收敛性，同时避免无偏乘积与双重采样等较强假设。

Method: 以有限折扣 MDP 为分析基准，利用折扣 Bellman 预解算子 $(I-\gamma P_\pi)^{-1}$ 构造乘子稳定性；在受控 Markov 采样下分析收敛，进而用经验 Bellman 代理（同时表示随机残差及其 Jacobian）处理随机观测。将 Markov 混合、初始化漂移、观测噪声与衰减偏差合并为单一算子扰动，从而规避无偏乘积与双重采样要求。

Result: 当扰动平方可和时，真实 KKT 残差几乎必然收敛到零。在有限条件四阶矩条件下，伴随迭代满足期望上界 $\mathbb{E}[\widetilde G_{K+1}] \le A/T+(B/T)\sum_{k<T}m_k^{-1}$，在总 Markov 样本预算 $N$ 下化为 $O(T^{-1}+T/N)$，对应平方 KKT 精度 $\varepsilon$ 的 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ 迭代复杂度与 $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 折扣占用覆盖给出 $J^\star-J(\pi)=O(\sqrt G)$，说明被覆盖的精确 KKT 点是全局最优的，而状态级二次 Bellman 改进条件将关系加强到 $O(G)$。非线性策略、投影 Bellman 与显式占用等表述均展现相同的算子可逆性—对偶表示—乘子稳定性链条，表明折扣算子可逆性可作为原始-对偶强化学习的一个可复用结构性原理。

Abstract: We identify and study a structural mechanism for Markovian nonconvex ADMM in reinforcement learning. Using finite discounted MDPs as a canonical proving ground, we show that the discounted Bellman resolvent $(I-γP_π)^{-1}$ can provide the multiplier stability that classical nonconvex ADMM analyses often obtain from a designated smooth block. Starting from this mechanism, we establish convergence under controlled Markov sampling and then under stochastic observations using an empirical Bellman surrogate that jointly represents the random residual and its Jacobian. Markov mixing, initialization drift, observation noise, and decaying bias enter as one operator perturbation, avoiding unbiased product and double sampling requirements. When the perturbations are square summable, the true KKT residual converges almost surely to zero. Under a finite conditional fourth moment condition, a companion iterate satisfies $ \mathbb{E}[\widetilde G_{K+1}] \le A/T+(B/T)\sum_{k<T}m_k^{-1}, $ which becomes $O(T^{-1}+T/N)$ for total Markov sample budget $N$, giving $O(ε^{-1})$ iteration complexity and $O(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity for squared KKT accuracy $ε$. Beyond stationarity, discounted occupancy coverage yields $J^\star-J(π)=O(\sqrt G)$ for direct tabular policies, so covered exact KKT points are globally optimal, while a statewise quadratic Bellman-improvement condition sharpens the relation to $O(G)$. Finally, nonlinear policy, projected Bellman, and explicit occupancy formulations exhibit the same chain of operator invertibility, dual representation, and multiplier stability. This supports discounted operator invertibility as a reusable structural principle for primal-dual reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [196] [Where the Model Changes Its Mind: Hindsight-Divergence Localization for Efficient Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36864)
*Fanchao Chen,Hengyu Fu,Shivaram Venkataraman,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Hindsight-Divergence Localization (HDL)，用后见之明引起的 token 对数似然变化选择分支点，以少量完整根轨迹加分支续写构建训练组，在降低生成成本的同时提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对方法（如 GRPO）依赖 rollout 结果的差异学习，但独立采样完整轨迹成本高，且未在关键决策位置显式探索决策空间。完整轨迹的反馈能揭示策略会重新考虑哪些早期选择，从而指示可采样替代续写的位置。

Method: HDL 利用后见之明引起的 token 对数似然变化来选取分支点。先采样少量完整根轨迹，在每个训练组中填充来自所选位置、处于原任务上下文下的续写。每条续写复用根前缀，仅通过新生成的 suffix 贡献策略更新。

Result: 在三个模型、数学/代码/智能体三类任务上的实验表明：与相同组规模与训练步数的 GRPO 相比，HDL 最高减少 2.5 倍生成 token、rollout 墙钟时间加速 1.8 倍；在更少生成预算下性能全面提升，智能体任务最高提升 12.5 分。

Conclusion: HDL 通过定位关键分支位置并在其后续写，降低了生成成本并将额外探索与学习集中于分支后的决策，实现了 rollout 效率与任务性能的双重提升。

Abstract: Group-relative methods for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) learn from differences in rollout outcomes. Independently sampling complete trajectories is costly and does not explicitly explore the decision space at critical positions. Feedback on a completed trajectory can reveal which earlier choices the policy reconsiders, suggesting where to sample alternative continuations. We introduce Hindsight-Divergence Localization (HDL), which uses hindsight-induced changes in token log-likelihoods to select branch points. HDL generates a small number of complete root trajectories and fills each training group with continuations from the selected positions under the original task context. Each continuation reuses its root prefix and contributes policy updates only through its newly generated suffix, reducing generation cost while focusing additional exploration and learning on decisions after branching. Experiments with three models across math, code, and agent tasks show gains in both rollout efficiency and task performance. Compared with GRPO at matched group sizes and training steps, HDL yields up to a 2.5$\times$ reduction in generated tokens and a 1.8$\times$ speedup in rollout wall-clock time. Despite this reduced generation budget, HDL improves performance across all three domains, with gains of up to 12.5 points on agent tasks.

</details>


### [197] [Seeing Time: Visual-Temporal Representation Learning for Interpretable Time Series Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36873)
*Zheng Zhu,Zexi Tan,Yuming Deng,Yiqun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出WAVE方法，将多变量时间序列及其渲染波形图视为同一观测的互补视图，通过对齐细粒度时序变化与整体视觉模式进行深度聚类，并将每个聚类关联到离质心最近的真实样本，实现波形级别的可追溯性。在10个真实数据集上取得最高宏平均聚类性能与最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多变量时间序列深度聚类方法虽能学习有判别力的时序表征，但其潜在聚类难以回溯到从业者可直接检查和比较的波形特征，从而限制了对所发现模式是否反映有意义时间行为的评估能力。

Method: 提出WAVE（Waveform Aligned Visual-temporal Embedding）：把时间序列与其确定性渲染的波形图作为同一观测的互补视图；对齐并融合细粒度时序变化与整体视觉模式，以产生聚类结构可追溯到可观测波形特征的判别性表征；同时将每个发现的聚类关联到其质心最近的真实样本。此处的可解释性特指波形级可追溯性，而非对模型决策的一般性解释。

Result: 在10个真实世界公开数据集上的广泛评估表明，WAVE在对比方法中取得最高的宏平均聚类性能和最佳平均排名；定性案例研究展示了如何通过真实波形记录检视所发现的聚类。源代码已公开。

Conclusion: WAVE通过时序-视觉双视图对齐与真实样本关联，使多变量时间序列聚类结果具备波形级可追溯性，在聚类性能与可解释性上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series (MTS) clustering is an important tool in temporal data mining, aiming to discover latent group structures from complex observations without supervision. Although existing deep clustering methods can learn discriminative temporal representations, the resulting latent clusters are often difficult to relate back to waveform characteristics that practitioners can directly inspect and compare, limiting their ability to assess whether the discovered patterns reflect meaningful temporal behaviors. This paper, therefore, proposes WAVE (Waveform Aligned Visual-temporal Embedding), which treats time series and their deterministically rendered waveform plots as complementary views of the same observations. To produce discriminative representations whose cluster structures can be traced to observable waveform characteristics, WAVE aligns and integrates fine-grained temporal variations with holistic visual patterns, while associating each discovered cluster with its centroid-nearest authentic sample. Accordingly, interpretability in this work specifically refers to waveform-level traceability rather than a general explanation of model decisions. Extensive evaluations across 10 real-world public datasets show that WAVE achieves the highest macro-averaged clustering performance and the best average rank among the compared methods, while qualitative case studies illustrate how the discovered clusters can be inspected through authentic waveform records. The source code is available at https://github.com/Zheng-Zhu1/WAVE.

</details>


### [198] [What You Observe Determines How You Identify Causal Effects: Evaluating Causal Models across Observational Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36881)
*Heejin Jung,Gyeongdeok Seo,Hoyoon Byun,Joseph Lee,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CausalIDView 多视图基准，在固定 SCM 实现与目标估计量的前提下仅改变可观测视图，从而受控比较因果基础模型（CFM）在不同识别机制下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFM 虽被用于从观测数据估计因果效应，但预训练环境与评估协议的差异使得难以判断其性能如何依赖于可用于因果识别的信息，缺乏受控比较手段。

Method: 构建多视图基准 CausalIDView：固定 SCM 实现与目标估计量，仅变化估计器可用的观测视图，每个视图对应在维持假设下的不同识别机制；并考察显式识别与强预测估计相结合的模块化方法。

Result: 在匹配视图下，没有 CFM 持续最优，模型排名差异显著；在受控结构变化下，CFM 表现出模型特有的失效——真效应不变时估计不稳定，以及无法追踪真实效应变化。将预测型表格基础模型与特定机制识别程序配对的模块化方法，与 CFM 具有竞争力并优于其中若干。

Conclusion: 应通过跨识别机制的对比来评估 CFM 的实证价值，而非仅依赖单一环境或协议。

Abstract: Causal foundation models (CFMs) pre-trained on data generated from various structural causal models (SCMs) have been proposed for estimating causal effects from observational data. However, differences in pre-training environments and evaluation protocols make it difficult to assess how their performance depends on the information available for causal identification. To enable controlled comparisons, we introduce CausalIDView, a multi-view benchmark that holds fixed SCM realization and target estimand while varying only the observational view available to the estimator. Each observational view corresponds to a distinct identification regime under the benchmark's maintained causal assumptions. Across these matched views, no CFM consistently performs best and model rankings vary substantially. Under controlled structural changes, CFMs exhibit model-specific failures to maintain stable estimates when true effects are unchanged and to track genuine effect changes. We also examine whether combining explicit identification with strong predictive estimation is effective. A modular approach that pairs a predictive tabular foundation model with regime-specific identification procedures is competitive with CFMs and outperforms several of them. These findings motivate cross-regime comparisons to assess the empirical value of CFMs.

</details>


### [199] [Architecture Alignment With Sparse Priors in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36883)
*Tianqi Zhao,Tianyi Zhuang,Shuo Duan,Guanyang Wang,Yan Shuo Tan,Qiong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究表格基础模型(TFM)的无关特征抑制能力，发现行令牌模型TabDPT在加入空特征时性能退化显著大于单元格令牌交替轴模型TabPFN v2，并通过受控实验证明架构本身带来稀疏任务下的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发布的TFM在预训练先验、架构和目标上各不相同，导致其归纳偏置相互混淆，难以判断哪种设计真正有助于任务自适应的特征相关性推断。作者因此聚焦"无关特征抑制"这一具体能力来解耦架构的作用。

Method: 在合成任务和真实数据集上对比TabDPT与TabPFN v2等TFM的空特征鲁棒性；在相同的稀疏到稠密线性先验下训练简化的行令牌与交替轴Transformer，并用精确贝叶斯分析刻画稀疏预测（需上下文相关的特征门控）与稠密端点（仅需均匀特征加权）的差异；进一步对受控模型和冻结的TabPFN v2进行特征注意力输出干预实验。

Result: 加入空特征后TabDPT预测退化明显大于TabPFN v2及其他TFM；受控实验中交替轴模型在稀疏任务上更接近贝叶斯最优预测器，稠密任务上架构差距可忽略，且稀疏差距几乎全部来自线性系数估计误差；干预实验显示存在沿特征索引通路的选择性计算路由证据。

Conclusion: 结果支持"架构—先验对齐"：保留可寻址的特征轴为任务自适应的相关性推断提供了归纳偏置，架构设计本身是TFM鲁棒性的重要来源。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are increasingly popular because they deliver strong predictions on new datasets through in-context learning, without task-specific training or extensive tuning. Yet released TFMs differ simultaneously in their pretraining priors, architectures, and objectives, obscuring their respective inductive biases. We therefore examine one concrete capability: irrelevant-feature suppression. Across synthetic tasks and real-world datasets, adding null features causes substantially greater predictive degradation in the row-token model TabDPT, whereas the cell-token alternating-axis model TabPFN v2 and other TFMs remain comparatively stable. This gap motivates us to ask whether architecture contributes to irrelevant-feature suppression. Because released TFMs remain confounded by other design choices, we train streamlined row-token and alternating-axis transformers under identical sparse-to-dense linear priors. Exact Bayes analysis shows that sparse prediction requires context-dependent feature gating, whereas the dense endpoint requires only uniform feature weighting. Consistent with this distinction, the alternating-axis model is substantially closer to the Bayesian optimal predictor on sparse tasks, while the architecture gap becomes negligible on dense tasks; almost all of the sparse gap arises from linear coefficient-estimation error. Finally, in both the controlled model and frozen TabPFN v2, we examine the effect of interventions on the feature-attention outputs on the linear coefficients, finding evidence of task-dependent selective routing of computation through feature-indexed pathways. Together, these results support architecture-prior alignment: preserving an addressable feature axis provides an inductive bias for task-adaptive relevance inference. Code is available at https://github.com/Tianqi-Zhao/ArchitecturePriorTFMs.

</details>


### [200] [SINO: Scale-Invariant Neural Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36890)
*Kaichen Ouyang,Chenglei Yu,Chuanrui Wang,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出尺度不变神经算子SINO，通过谱域与空域双分支架构在归一化物理尺度上学习，以瓶颈MLP生成连续卷积核并嵌入显式低秩归纳偏置，在多类湍流闭合问题上实现显著误差降低与参数效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习中，PDE物理场具有低秩结构与尺度不变性。在粗网格求解时存在闭合问题，需建模未解析物理以恢复丢失动力学。闭合项虽依赖网格分辨率，却表征尺度不变的物理规律。真正学习物理的模型应以低秩参数化捕捉这些机制，而非记忆网格特定模式。

Method: 提出尺度不变神经算子SINO，在归一化物理尺度上学习，采用谱域与空域双分支架构，使用瓶颈MLP生成连续卷积核，嵌入显式低秩归纳偏置（PCA验证2-3个模态集中超95%方差），同时大幅减少参数量。

Result: 在外部强迫Burgers湍流、衰减Burgers湍流、KS湍流、Kolmogorov强迫NS湍流和衰减NS湍流等闭合问题上，与U-Net、DeepONet、Transolver、Oformer、GK-Transformer、FNO、AMFNO、UFNO对比，SINO实现1.5-38倍误差降低、2-23倍参数效率提升，缩放律指数比FNO陡峭38倍。

Conclusion: SINO通过原则性低秩设计实现优越的数据效率与缩放律，验证了尺度不变低秩参数化在闭合问题上的有效性。

Abstract: In scientific machine learning, physical fields governed by partial differential equations exhibit low-rank structure and scale invariance. When solving equations on coarse grids, missing information leads to the closure problem: modeling unresolved physics to recover lost dynamics. Although closure terms depend on grid resolution, they represent scale-invariant physical laws. A model truly learning physics should capture these mechanisms with low-rank parameterization rather than memorizing grid-specific patterns. Inspired by this, we propose the Scale-Invariant Neural Operator (SINO), which learns on normalized physical scales via a dual-branch architecture operating in spectral and spatial domains. SINO uses bottleneck MLPs to generate continuous convolution kernels, embedding an explicit low-rank inductive bias that concentrates more than 95 percent of variance in 2-3 modes, as validated by PCA across benchmarks, while drastically reducing parameters. This principled design yields 38 times steeper scaling law exponents than FNO, demonstrating superior parameter efficiency. We compare SINO with traditional models (U-Net, DeepONet), Transformer models (Transolver, Oformer, GK-Transformer), and frequency-domain models (FNO, AMFNO, UFNO) on closure problems spanning externally forced Burgers turbulence, decaying Burgers turbulence, KS turbulence, Kolmogorov-forced NS turbulence, and decaying NS turbulence. Experiments show SINO achieves 1.5-38 times error reduction and 2-23 times parameter efficiency over baselines, with superior scaling laws reflecting exceptional data efficiency from principled low-rank design. Code is available at https://github.com/AI4Science-WestlakeU/SINO.

</details>


### [201] [Variational Mixtures and Multi-Marginal Flow Matching: Advancing Statistical Inference with Biological Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36911)
*Oskar Kviman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对复杂生物系统产生的多模态、几何结构化且有时仅定义至归一化常数的分布，发展了从变分推断到流匹配的统计推断方法，并将成果应用于三维空间转录组学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂生物系统产生的分布往往具有多模态、几何结构化特征，有时仅定义到归一化常数，这给统计推断带来挑战。作者希望从变分推断出发，逐步构建更具表达力的近似方法，并最终扩展到流匹配，以应对这些推断难题，同时服务于三维空间转录组学等生物应用。

Method: 论文从变分推断入手，在无法获得解析更新方程时转向黑盒变分推断；引入新的未归一化目标密度（CoLN分布）作为受控测试案例。随后沿表达力递增的轨迹推进：用多重重要性采样ELBO评估的集成（Paper A）、自动实现组分协作与探索的变分混合（Paper B）、以蒙特卡洛目标估计器提升混合学习效率（Paper C）。进而转向流匹配，处理多边际情形下插值学习（Paper D），并在5.5节结合A-D提出多边际流匹配与变分插值混合的新方法。

Result: 作为新的理论结果，论文推翻了关于在变分推断中使用混合模型可带来潜在性能优势这一长达三十年的误解；提出了更高效的混合学习目标估计方法；并给出多边际流匹配结合变分插值混合的新方法。

Conclusion: 论文构建了从黑盒变分推断到流匹配的连贯方法体系，强调表达力提升与计算成本之间的权衡，并将这些方法发展与生物应用相连接，尤其聚焦于堆叠组织切片在空间上引发多模态动态的三维空间转录组学。

Abstract: In this thesis I develop methods for statistical inference when the distributions arising from complex biological systems are multi-modal, geometrically structured, and sometimes only defined up to a normalizing constant. I start from variational inference and, when analytic update equations are unavailable, move to black-box variational inference. To build intuition regarding inference challenges and the proposed methodologies, I introduce a novel unnormalized target density (the CoLN distribution) and reuse it as a controlled test case in the kappa. I then trace a trajectory of increasingly expressive approximations: ensembles evaluated with the multiple importance sampling ELBO (Paper A) and variational mixtures that automate component cooperation and exploration (Paper B). Because expressivity comes at a cost, I develop efficient mixture learning ideas, including Monte Carlo objective estimators to scale mixture learning more efficiently (Paper C). As a new result in the kappa, I overturn a three decades long misconception regarding the potential performance benefits of using mixtures in variational inference. Finally, I move from variational inference to flow matching, where I address the need for specialized treatment of interpolant learning in multi-marginal settings (Paper D). By combining insights from Papers A-D, I derive in Section 5.5 a new method: multi-marginal flow matching with mixtures of variational interpolants. I connect these methodological developments to biological applications, with special emphasis on three-dimensional spatial transcriptomics, where stacked tissue slices induce multi-modal dynamics across space.

</details>


### [202] [State Transport Routing for Short-horizon Adaptation in Multi-horizon Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36926)
*Xu Yuqing,Zhou Liguo,Sun Ze,Yu Lei,Jiang Mingming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出轻量级适配器 STR，仅微调短期预测而不改变长期预测，在四个光伏数据集上优于残差适配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最新功率测量对光伏功率预测有重要价值，但直接外推短期趋势在更长预测期会带来显著误差，需要一种既能利用近期信息又不破坏长时段预测的方法。

Method: STR 结合原始预测与两条互补轨迹（来自最新实测功率水平及其近期趋势），并使用时域条件路由（horizon-conditioned router）在前 120 分钟内动态调整二者权重，其余时段保持原预测不变；作为冻结预测模型上的轻量适配器，参数规模与残差适配器匹配以作对照。

Result: 在四个公开光伏数据集上，STR 持续优于参数匹配的残差适配器；在 PVDAQ 上，该方法对五种神经预测骨干均有提升，全预测期归一化平均绝对误差降低 0.0201–0.2364 个百分点，成对 95% 置信区间不含零；LightGBM 上未观察到可靠提升。

Conclusion: 结构化状态适配可在无需重训练底层模型、不改变长时段预测的前提下，跨多种神经架构改进短期光伏预测。

Abstract: Recent power measurements provide valuable information for photovoltaic(PV) power forecasting, but directly extrapolating short-term trends can introduce substantial errors over longer forecast horizons. To address this challenge, we propose state transport routing (STR), a lightweight adapter that refines the predictions of a frozen forecasting model. STR combines the original forecast with two complementary trajectories derived from the latest measured power level and its recent trend. A horizon-conditioned router adjusts their contributions over the first 120 min, while leaving subsequent predictions unchanged. Experiments on four public PV datasets show that STR consistently outperforms a parameter-matched residual adapter. On PVDAQ, the same approach improves five neural forecasting backbones, reducing all-horizon normalized mean absolute error by 0.0201-0.2364 percentage points, with paired 95% confidence intervals excluding zero. No reliable improvement is observed for LightGBM. These findings demonstrate the potential of structured state adaptation to improve short-term forecasting across different neural architectures without retraining the underlying models or altering their longer-horizon predictions.

</details>


### [203] [Safe-by-Design Learning via Energy-based Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36942)
*Simone Betteti,Morteza Lahijanian,Luca Laurenti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于能量型现代Hopfield网络与端口哈密顿神经ODE融合的神经架构，通过设计即保证安全性，能构造障碍函数并给出显式可容许输入集与鲁棒半径，在多个基准（含12维纳米无人机）上达到SOTA且不变集更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键场景要求动力系统神经网络模型具备安全保证，通常需证明状态空间中期望子集的不变性。但现有方法要么依赖计算昂贵的后验验证，要么采用强制安全机制却缺乏形式化正确性保证，难以兼顾效率与可证性。

Method: 将现代Hopfield网络与端口哈密顿神经ODE相结合，构建一种新型神经架构，实现"设计即安全"；该结构可自然构造障碍函数，从而导出显式的可容许输入集合以及定量的鲁棒半径，同时保持建模复杂非线性动力学的表达能力。

Result: 在多个基准任务（包括12维纳米无人机模型）上取得最先进性能，并产生经认证的不变集，其对外部扰动的鲁棒性优于同类现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，借助能量型现代Hopfield网络与端口哈密顿结构的结合，可在保持高表达能力的同时实现安全性的设计内保证，为安全关键系统中动力学建模提供了兼具可证性与鲁棒性的新范式。

Abstract: Learning neural-network models of dynamical systems with safety guarantees is a fundamental requirement for their deployment in safety-critical settings. Safety is commonly established by proving the invariance of a desired subset in state-space, ensuring that every trajectory initialized in this subset remains confined to it for all time under admissible inputs. Existing frameworks, however, either rely on computationally expensive post-hoc verification or employ safety-enforcing mechanisms without formal correctness guarantees. In this paper, we introduce a novel neural architecture grounded in energy-based modern Hopfield networks to guarantee safety-by-design while retaining sufficient expressiveness to model complex nonlinear dynamics. Specifically, we integrate modern Hopfield networks with a port-Hamiltonian neural ODE, enabling by design the construction of barrier functions yielding explicit admissible-input sets and quantitative robustness radii. Across several benchmarks, including an 12-dimensional nanodrone model, our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance while producing certified invariant sets that are more robust to external solicitations than comparable existing approaches.

</details>


### [204] [Fine-Tuning on Self-Generated and Reward-Weighted Data: Learning Dynamics, Convergence Rates, and Benefits of Off-Policyness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36945)
*Zhiwei Wang,Yanxi Chen,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为奖励加权自训练中的广义REINFORCE算法RE(S)建立了覆盖任意S≥1的统一收敛理论，揭示S只影响预热期长度而不改变Θ(1/T)的渐近收敛率，并说明适度离策略性反而能避免弱初始化下的次优陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型后训练等场景中，奖励引导的自训练即使不频繁更新rollout分布（离策略）也有效，但已有策略梯度理论多强调在策略采样（S小、理想为1）的必要性，缺乏对这类离策略方法收敛性质的理论理解，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出RE(S)的统一理论：每S步梯度更新一次rollout分布，可解释为分阶段优化过程，每阶段用S步梯度最小化与固定奖励加权rollout分布的KL距离。针对softmax策略的多臂老虎机，进行深入分析与数值实验，推导单侧与双侧紧界。

Result: (1) 对任意固定S，当rollout更新次数B=⌊T/S⌋→∞时RE(S)全局收敛到最优策略；(2) 证明紧的双侧界，次优性间隙达到渐近Θ(1/T)速率，S只影响预热期长度；(3) 弱策略初始化（最优动作概率很小）时，RE(1)会长时间困在次优策略附近，而合适S的RE(S)可避开绕路、显著更快收敛到全局最优。

Conclusion: 离策略性并非总是劣势：S只改变收敛的预热长度而不影响渐近速率，且在弱初始化下适度增大S反而加速收敛。该理论统一刻画了从在策略到离策略的全谱系行为，为奖励加权自训练提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study the learning dynamics of fine-tuning a policy model on self-generated and reward-weighted data, with particular focus on a generalized version of REINFORCE -- referred to as RE(S) -- that updates the rollout distribution once every $S \ge 1$ gradient steps. Prior work in bandits and reinforcement learning has developed rich theory for policy gradient methods, and on-policy sampling (i.e., a small $S$, ideally $1$) is often viewed as crucial to their success; yet in prominent application like post-training large language models, reward-guided self-training has proved to be effective even when the rollout distribution is updated infrequently, but theoretical understanding remains limited for the convergence properties of these off-policy methods. To bridge these gaps, we develop a unified theory for RE(S) that covers the full spectrum of $S \ge 1$: it can be interpreted as a stage-wise optimization process, where each stage takes $S$ gradient steps for minimizing the Kullback-Leibler distance to a fixed reward-weighted rollout distribution. For multi-arm bandits with softmax policies, our in-depth analysis and numerical experiments reveal three key findings: (1) for any fixed $S$, RE(S) enjoys global convergence to the optimal policy as the number of rollout distribution updates $B = \lfloor T / S \rfloor \rightarrow \infty$, where $T$ denotes the number of gradient steps; (2) we prove tight two-sided bounds showing that the suboptimality gap of RE(S) achieves an asymptotic $Θ(1 / T)$ convergence rate, while $S$ only affects the length of a burn-in phase; (3) when initialized at a weak policy with a small optimal-action probability, RE(1) gets trapped around suboptimal policies for a long period, whereas RE(S) with a suitable $S$ avoids the detour and achieves significantly faster convergence to the global optimum, highlighting the benefits of off-policyness in this case.

</details>


### [205] [Scalable Diffusion SBI for Compositional Inference under Simulator Misspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36950)
*Vincent D. Zaballa,Elliot E. Hui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了面向设计条件模拟的扩散推断方法：连续时间扩散系数扩展组合式评分推断，HBDS实现单模型分层采样，路径正则化微调缓解模型误指定，并在多个基准和BMP信号模型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模拟的推断面临三大挑战：需要在不同实验条件下组合大量异构观测、需保留分层潜变量结构、模拟器相对于真实观测存在误指定。现有方法通常需要针对固定观测集和结构重训练，且缺乏对误指定校正的有效手段与判别依据。

Method: 1) 扩展组合式评分推断，引入依赖观测数量的连续时间扩散系数，避免Jacobian与辅助协方差校正；2) 提出分层块式扩散采样（HBDS），用单个预训练模型推断共享参数与组特异潜状态，分层结构仅在采样时指定；3) 提出路径正则化微调，使学习到的似然适配观测并把校正迁移到后验推断；4) 用Girsanov定理量化预训练与微调模型在不同实验设计下的路径散度，并结合预测误差判别应采用的误指定校正。

Result: 在精确评分高斯、Simple Likelihood、Complex Posterior基准上评估组合采样；在受控分层模型上用解析与学习评分评估HBDS；在具已知设计依赖差异的解析模型上评估微调与定位；最后应用于机制性BMP信号模型中四条细胞系的940个测量，微调提升了后验预测精度，并使后验边缘向最小二乘参考移动，同时保留扩散度。

Conclusion: 所提方法支持可变观测集与分组而无需重训练，能够有效处理分层结构与模拟器误指定，并通过路径散度分析为误指定校正提供可解释的诊断依据，在真实生物信号数据上表现出更好的后验预测与校准。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference is challenging when many heterogeneous observations must be composed, hierarchical latent structure must be preserved, and the simulator is misspecified relative to observed data. We develop sampling and fine-tuning methods for diffusion-based inference in design-conditional settings, where the same simulator is queried across different experimental conditions $ξ$. We extend compositional score-based inference with a continuous-time diffusion coefficient that accounts for the number of observations, avoiding Jacobian and auxiliary-covariance corrections.
