We introduce Hierarchical Blockwise Diffusion Sampling (HBDS), which infers shared parameters and group-specific latent states using a single pretrained model, with the hierarchy specified only at sampling time. Together, these methods support variable observation sets and groupings without retraining. To address misspecification, we introduce path-regularized fine-tuning that adapts the learned likelihood to observations and transfers corrections to posterior inference. Using Girsanov's theorem, we quantify path divergence between pretrained and fine-tuned models across experimental designs and interpret it alongside predictive errors to distinguish candidate misspecification correction from unnecessary adaptation. We evaluate compositional sampling on exact-score Gaussian and Simple Likelihood, Complex Posterior benchmarks, HBDS with analytic and learned scores on a controlled hierarchical model, and fine-tuning and localization on a separate analytic model with known design-dependent discrepancy. Finally, we apply the framework to 940 measurements across four cell lines in a mechanistic Bone Morphogenetic Protein signaling model, where fine-tuning improves posterior-predictive accuracy relative to the pretrained model and shifts posterior marginals toward the least-squares reference while retaining spread.

</details>


### [206] [Cool the Sampler, Not the Learner: Sampling Temperature Moves the Staleness Cliff of Importance-Corrected GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36953)
*Taiheng Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在重要性修正的 GRPO 中，采样器滞后会导致性能骤降；作者提出"解耦冷却"（采样温度 0.8，学习器/参考模型/重要性权重保持温度 1），在不改变学习目标的前提下显著提升采样器的陈旧容忍度，使刷新间隔延长一倍仍不损失性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境 RL 训练语言模型时，采样器（生成器）往往落后于学习器，产生分布不匹配，通常用截断重要性权重来修补。作者关心：在这种修正下，采样器能"撑"多久不刷新？并发现在 Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B 和 GSM8K 上存在"悬崖"现象——每 192 次更新刷新一次的重要性修正 GRPO，在 180 步内学习良好，随后在三个数据种子上严重退化。现有对陈旧性（staleness）的补救都作用于"更新"，作者转而作用于"采样器"。

Method: 提出"解耦冷却"（Decoupled Cooling）：让采样器以温度 0.8 采样，而学习器、参考模型和重要性权重仍使用温度 1，行为概率从加温分布中记录，因此学习目标保持不变（只在采样端改温度，不改变优化目标）。

Result: 所有冷却运行都稳定。相同更新预算下，每 192 步刷新的冷却采样器与每 96 步刷新的未冷却采样器最终性能相当（均为 0.857），平均性能也相近（0.79）；而仅降低学习率到安全值的做法最终低 3-7 个点。在 Qwen2.5-Math-7B 上，192 间隔的退化点预测 144 间隔不冷却即致命、冷却则存活；两个数据种子上未冷却运行在首次刷新前就退化，冷却运行则通过并以 92-93% 对比 68-81% 收尾（一个冷却运行在第二周期后期短暂退化）。该收益存在窗口：在安全间隔的三倍处以及高不匹配的 MATH 设定下，冷却只是延迟而非阻止退化；更强的冷却并不更好；去掉修正的冷却会崩溃。

Conclusion: 采样温度是控制陈旧容忍度的一个旋钮，温度与刷新间隔应联合选择。冷却只能延长容忍窗口而非无限防止退化，存在适用边界。

Abstract: Production RL for language models lets the sampler fall behind the learner and repairs the resulting mismatch with a truncated importance weight. We ask how long the sampler can go without a refresh under that correction, and find a cliff: on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and GSM8K, importance-corrected GRPO refreshed every 192 updates learns well for 180 steps and then degrades severely in all three data seeds before the refresh arrives. Published remedies for staleness act on the update; we act on the sampler instead. Decoupled cooling draws samples at temperature 0.8 while the learner, the reference model and the importance weights stay at temperature 1, with the behaviour probability recorded from the tempered distribution, so the learner's objective is unchanged. All corresponding cooled runs are stable, and the longer interval keeps what the short one delivered: at the same update budget, a cooled sampler refreshed every 192 steps matches an uncooled sampler refreshed every 96 at the end of training (0.857 for both) and averaged over it (0.79), whereas lowering the learning rate to a safe value ends 3-7 points lower. On Qwen2.5-Math-7B the degradation points at interval 192 predict that an interval of 144 is fatal without cooling and survivable with it; on two data seeds the uncooled runs degrade before their first refresh and the cooled runs pass it and end at 92-93% against 68-81%, with one cooled run degrading transiently late in the second cycle. The benefit has a window: at three times the safe interval and in a high-mismatch MATH setting cooling delays degradation without preventing it, stronger cooling is not better, and cooling without the correction collapses. Sampling temperature is a control on staleness tolerance, and temperature and refresh interval should be chosen together.

</details>


### [207] [VStress: Correlation-Aware Auditing and Adaptive Budget Allocation for Repeated Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36958)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Peng Zhang,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 VStress（可审计重放契约）与 VStress-CA（相关性感知分配策略），通过估计未查询验证器的条件边际信息、折扣不确定性、按调用成本归一化，并在信息不足时停止或弃权，把"相关性"从后验警告转化为可审计的分配决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复调用验证器只有在提供条件信息时才有价值，但现有做法常忽视验证器之间的相关性，导致冗余调用与预算浪费，且缺乏可审计的决策依据。

Method: 构建 VStress 可审计重放契约与 VStress-CA 相关性感知分配策略：在封存校准集上估计未查询验证器的条件边际信息，对不确定性进行折扣，按调用成本归一化，并当下一步调用无信息时停止或弃权；控制器在接入干净 oracle 前冻结决策与成本账本，并设置依赖漂移告警以禁用通道偏好、回退到精确停止。

Result: 受控审计给出机制边界：35% 对称损坏下 majority-5 将平衡准确率从 0.6578 提升至 0.7739，而 65% 时反而损失 0.1226。在匹配固定预算比较中，广度、冗余与自适应分配分别取得 0.6048、0.6375、0.6538 的平衡准确率，每项 3.4216 次调用，VStress-CA 的 RLVR 得分为 0.6417；依赖诊断从同模型重复到跨家族通道递增，条件边际增益分别为 0.0126、0.0462、0.0913。

Conclusion: 这些测量使相关性由事后警告转变为可审计的分配决策，为验证器调用提供可复现、可审计的边界与判据。

Abstract: Repeated verifier calls are useful only when they contribute conditional information. We introduce VStress, an auditable replay contract, and VStress-CA, a correlation-aware allocation policy that estimates the conditional marginal information of an unqueried verifier on a sealed calibration split, discounts uncertainty, normalizes by call cost, and stops or abstains when the next call is not informative. The controller freezes its decision and cost ledger before joining the clean oracle; a dependence-shift alarm disables channel preference and falls back to exact-stop. The controlled audit gives the mechanism boundary: at 35% symmetric corruption, majority-5 improves balanced accuracy from 0.6578 to 0.7739, whereas at 65% it loses 0.1226 points. In the matched fixed-budget comparison, breadth, redundancy, and adaptive allocation obtain balanced accuracies 0.6048, 0.6375, and 0.6538, with 3.4216 calls per item and an RLVR score of 0.6417 for VStress-CA. Dependence diagnostics also increase from same-model repeats to cross-family channels, with conditional marginal gains of 0.0126, 0.0462, and 0.0913. These measurements turn correlation from a post-hoc warning into an auditable allocation decision.

</details>


### [208] [JudgeCast: Time Series Forecasting with Experience-Informed Covariate Judgements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36966)
*Donguk Kwon,Wooseok Jeong,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JudgeCast 是一个基于经验的协变量时间序列预测框架，用冻结的 TSFM 生成基础预测、冻结的 LLM 做判断性调整，并通过观测残差重构并验证经验，在多个真实数据集上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量效应随情境和时间变化，预测者需针对每个预测情境判断如何使用协变量。随着预测推进，早期预测的观测可提供反馈，但当多个协变量共同作用时，预测误差只揭示数值偏差，无法说明协变量应如何被使用，因此难以形成可复用的预测经验。

Method: 借鉴判断性调整实践：冻结的 TSFM 给出基础预测，冻结的 LLM 结合当前情境与相关经验进行调整。由于"评估协变量效应"与"确定数值调整"角色不同，JudgeCast 先生成显式的逐协变量判断，再确定调整量。观测后，利用基础预测的观测残差重构替代判断，并通过其产生的调整评估原始与替代方案，选出表现最佳者作为验证过的经验保留，供后续预测使用。

Result: 在多样化的真实世界数据集上，JudgeCast 优于强基线。消融实验表明：显式的逐协变量判断能改善预测时的调整；基于残差构造经验比直接保留原始决策作为经验，带来更可靠的预测增益。

Conclusion: 将协变量效应评估与数值调整分离为两步判断，并借助观测残差重构与验证经验，能有效提升带协变量时间序列预测的准确性与可靠性。

Abstract: Covariate effects vary across contexts and shift over time, requiring forecasters to assess how to use them for each forecasting context. As forecasting proceeds, observations for earlier forecasts become available, providing feedback on past covariate use for subsequent forecasts. However, when multiple covariates act together, the forecast error reveals the numerical discrepancy from the observation but not how the covariates should have been used. We introduce JudgeCast, an experience-based framework for time series forecasting with covariates. Following the judgmental adjustment practice, a frozen TSFM provides the base forecast, while a frozen LLM uses the current context and relevant experience to adjust it. Within the adjustment, assessing covariate effects and determining the numerical adjustment serve distinct roles, so JudgeCast first forms explicit covariate-wise judgments and then determines the adjustment. After observation, JudgeCast uses the observed residual of the base forecast to reconstruct alternative judgments and evaluates the original and alternatives through their resulting adjustments. The best-performing decision is selected and retained as validated experience for subsequent forecasts. Across diverse real-world datasets, JudgeCast outperforms strong baselines. Ablations show that explicit covariate-wise judgment can improve forecast-time adjustment, while residual-guided experience construction yields more reliable forecasting gains than retaining raw decisions as experience.

</details>


### [209] [TaskBridge: Bridging Unsupervised Tabular Anomaly Detection and In-Context Learning via Virtual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36968)
*Doyun Choi,Dooho Lee,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TaskBridge框架，通过构建虚拟监督任务，将无监督表格异常检测转化为通用表格基础模型（TFM）的上下文监督推理，无需异常专用预训练或数据集特定优化，在790个数据集上超越30个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督表格异常检测（TAD）传统方法依赖数据集特定的训练与配置搜索。近期基于TFM的方法虽能通过上下文学习实现零样本检测，但多数需从头进行异常专用预训练，导致检测依赖合成先验且更新成本高；另一些复用通用TFM的方法则计算开销大且带有受限的异常归纳偏置。

Method: TaskBridge通过构造虚拟监督任务，将异常检测直接重塑为TFM的监督式上下文推理。这些虚拟任务诱导出预测结构：正常查询及其目标对获得高支持度，而异常样本往往违反该结构因而获得低支持度，从而提供直接的异常证据。该方法无需异常专用TFM预训练，也不需要数据集特定的模型优化。

Result: 在790个真实世界数据集上，TaskBridge持续优于30个基线方法，包括当前最先进的基于TFM的方法。

Conclusion: TaskBridge为无监督表格异常检测提供了一种高效复用预训练通用TFM的新范式，避免了异常专用预训练与昂贵的配置搜索，展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Unsupervised tabular anomaly detection (TAD) aims to identify anomalous rows in tabular data using normal training samples. While conventional methods rely on dataset-specific training and configuration search, recent tabular foundation models (TFMs) enable zero-shot anomaly detection on unseen datasets via in-context learning. Most TFM-based approaches, however, require anomaly-specific pretraining from scratch, making detection inherently dependent on synthetic TAD-specific priors and costly to update. Some approaches instead repurpose pretrained general-purpose TFMs for TAD to avoid this burden, but rely on computationally expensive formulations with restrictive anomaly inductive biases. In this work, we introduce TaskBridge, a new framework that efficiently repurposes pretrained general-purpose TFMs for unsupervised TAD by constructing virtual supervised tasks that directly recast anomaly detection as supervised in-context inference of TFMs. The resulting virtual tasks induce predictive structures under which normal queries and their target pairs receive high support, whereas anomalies tend to violate the induced structures and receive lower support, providing direct anomaly evidence. Across 790 real-world datasets, TaskBridge consistently outperforms 30 baselines, including state-of-the-art TFM-based approaches, without anomaly-specific TFM pretraining or dataset-specific model optimization.

</details>


### [210] [Equally Good, Yet Different: Benchmarking Rashomon sets in AutoML packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36970)
*Katarzyna Woźnica,Katarzyna Rogalska,Zuzanna Sieńko,Mustafa Cavus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ARSA ML开源框架以量化AutoML中的Rashomon集结构与预测多重性，并揭示x-hacking风险；对AutoGluon和H2O在28个二分类数据集上的基准测试显示，H2O因解释不稳定而更易受x-hacking影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rashomon效应表明存在多个性能相近但解释迥异的近优模型，这使AutoML面临x-hacking风险，即用户基于解释而非预测性能事后选择性挑选模型。然而现有AutoML框架均未暴露或量化这一风险。

Method: 提出开源Python框架ARSA ML，用于在AutoML流水线中量化Rashomon集结构和预测多重性；利用该框架在28个二分类数据集上对AutoGluon和H2O进行基准测试，并开展事后x-hacking分析。

Result: 发现一致的结构性不对称：AutoGluon产生的Rashomon集更大、更多样且解释稳定；H2O生成的集合更紧凑，但预测分歧与解释不稳定性显著更高，使H2O用户更易暴露于x-hacking。该差距在所有评估指标和epsilon阈值下均持续存在。

Conclusion: 该差距反映了两种框架在模型构建策略上的根本差异。ARSA ML可有效量化AutoML中的Rashomon集结构与x-hacking风险，已在PyPI开源发布。

Abstract: The Rashomon effect describes the existence of multiple near-optimal models that achieve comparable performance while offering fundamentally different explanations. This creates a critical vulnerability in AutoML: x-hacking, the selective post-hoc choice of a model based on its explanation rather than predictive merit. No existing AutoML framework exposes this risk. We introduce ARSA ML, an open-source Python framework that quantifies Rashomon set structure and predictive multiplicity within AutoML pipelines. Using ARSA ML, we benchmark AutoGluon and H2O across 28 binary classification datasets, and conduct a post-hoc x-hacking analysis revealing a consistent structural asymmetry: AutoGluon produces larger, diverse sets with stable explanations, while H2O generates compact sets with markedly higher prediction divergence and explanation instability -- making H2O users considerably more exposed to x-hacking. This gap persists across all evaluated metrics and epsilon thresholds, pointing to a fundamental difference in each framework's model-building strategy. ARSA ML is available at https://pypi.org/project/arsa-ml/ .

</details>


### [211] [Abductive World Modeling via Causal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36985)
*Ziqi Liu,Songhan Yang,Linfan Zhou,Jiatong Liu,Lijun Peng,Long Wan,Yinqi Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper proposes Abductive World Modeling (AWM), a framework that abductively infers latent causes from predicted futures to learn structured causal representations, realized via the Hierarchical Abductive State Pyramid (HASP).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing world models predominantly represent future states without explicitly capturing the latent causes underlying their evolution, which limits their ability to reason about why and how the world changes.

Method: AWM learns structured causal representations by abductively inferring latent causes from predicted futures. It is realized through the Hierarchical Abductive State Pyramid (HASP), which organizes the inferred world state into three components: Entity (what exists), Dynamic (how it changes), and Relation (how entities interact). HASP jointly reasons over the current observation and its predicted future to infer latent factors and integrate them into a structured state representation for downstream reasoning.

Result: Experiments on physical prediction, causal reasoning, and action understanding show effectiveness. Compared with V-JEPA (a state-of-the-art latent-space world model), AWM improves physical prediction AUROC by 10.7%, causal reasoning accuracy by 16.8%, and action Top-1 accuracy by 68.0%.

Conclusion: AWM is the first framework to introduce abductive state inference into latent-space world modeling for learning structured representations of world dynamics.

Abstract: The central challenge of world modeling is to learn representations that capture how the world evolves. However, existing world models predominantly represent future states without explicitly capturing the latent causes underlying their evolution, limiting their ability to reason about why and how the world changes. To address this limitation, we propose Abductive World Modeling (AWM), a framework that learns structured causal representations by abductively inferring latent causes from predicted futures. Specifically, we realize AWM through the Hierarchical Abductive State Pyramid (HASP), which organizes the inferred world state into three complementary components - Entity, Dynamic, and Relation - capturing what exists, how it changes, and how entities interact, respectively. By jointly reasoning over the current observation and its predicted future, HASP abductively infers these latent factors and integrates them into a structured state representation for downstream reasoning. To the best of our knowledge, AWM is the first framework to introduce abductive state inference into latent-space world modeling for learning structured representations of world dynamics. Experiments across physical prediction, causal reasoning, and action understanding demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Compared with V-JEPA, a state-of-the-art latent-space world model, AWM improves physical prediction AUROC by 10.7%, causal reasoning accuracy by 16.8%, and action Top-1 accuracy by 68.0%.

</details>


### [212] [LongSpark: Efficient speculative decoding with a fixed-cost parallel drafter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37029)
*Hao-Yuan He,Peng-Fei Liu,Si Shen,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongSpark 是一种块扩散（block-diffusion）草稿模型，通过从目标模型的验证前向过程中提取固定尺寸的多尺度视图，使草稿模型的解码成本与上下文长度无关，从而在长上下文场景下实现最优的端到端效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码通过一次目标前向验证多个草稿 token 来加速自回归推理，但现有最优草稿模型随上下文增长而成本上升，侵蚀了效率优势。作者认为这种随前缀增长的扩展是不必要的：独立语言模型必须随前缀增长因为它独自负责每个 token，而草稿模型只提出候选，错误由目标模型纠正，因此其解码成本可以完全独立于前缀长度。

Method: 提出 LongSpark，一种块扩散草稿模型。它从目标模型的验证前向过程中提取固定尺寸、多尺度的视图，从而消除对不断增长的持久状态的需求，使草稿解码成本与前缀长度解耦。

Result: 在多种模型规模和真实服务条件下，LongSpark 实现了最优的端到端效率；在长上下文任务上取得最低的每输出 token 时间（time-per-output-token），同时将草稿模型的上下文状态减少数个数量级。

Conclusion: 草稿模型无需随前缀增长，通过固定尺寸多尺度视图的块扩散设计可在长上下文下保持高效，LongSpark 验证了这一思路并显著降低了草稿模型的状态开销。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive inference by verifying multiple draft tokens in a single target forward pass. However, as the context grows, existing state-of-the-art drafters become increasingly expensive, eroding the very efficiency advantage they are designed to provide. We argue that this scaling is unnecessary. A standalone language model must grow with its prefix because it is solely responsible for every token it produces. A drafter, by contrast, only proposes candidates; the target catches and corrects every error before any token is committed. The drafter's decoding cost can therefore be made entirely independent of the prefix length. We introduce LongSpark, a block-diffusion drafter that achieves this by extracting fixed-size, multiscale views from the target's verification pass, thereby eliminating the need for a growing persistent state. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that LongSpark achieves state-of-the-art end-to-end efficiency across multiple model scales and realistic serving conditions. Notably, it delivers the lowest time-per-output-token on long-context tasks while reducing the drafter's context state by several orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [213] [High-Resolution Dynamic Functional Connectivity Generation with Graph-Variate Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37037)
*Om Roy,Yashar Moshfeghi,Keith Malcolm Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GVD-CFM，一种用于高分辨率动态脑网络连接的条件生成模型。它将图变量动态连接表示为SPD流形上的矩阵，结合log-Euclidean映射与可逆DCT基，用Transformer条件流模型非自回归生成完整轨迹，在多个EEG运动想象数据集上于分布保真度、时间动态保持和合成到真实分类方面取得最佳综合表现，且计算高效、支持更密集时间网格解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率动态功能连接（DFC）能揭示快速演化的脑网络交互，但短时间窗导致协方差估计噪声大且常为低秩。图变量动态（GVD）连接通过稳定试次级支撑调制快速瞬时交互来抑制虚假波动，但现有方法在SPD流形上生成高分辨率动态连接时仍面临需岭正则化或事后投影等问题，且缺乏对完整轨迹的高效、保真生成能力。

Method: 提出GVD-CFM：先将每个试次表示为乘积黎曼流形上的SPD GVD矩阵，通过全局log-Euclidean微分同胚和可逆时间DCT基进行映射；再用基于Transformer的条件流模型在欧氏坐标中联合建模所有谱模态，非自回归地生成完整轨迹，同时保持与有效SPD序列的精确对应。利用Hadamard构造将低秩瞬时连接从半正定锥提升到正定锥，无需岭正则化或事后投影。保留完整DCT基还支持在更密时间网格上解码而无需重新训练。

Result: 在多个EEG运动想象数据集上，GVD-CFM在留出数据的分布保真度、时间动态保持和合成到真实分类三方面均取得最强综合结果，且相对强原始信号基线和直接GVD空间生成基线保持计算高效。

Conclusion: GVD-CFM为真实、时间连贯、高分辨率脑网络生成提供了实用框架，能保持流形结构，并支持单一训练模型的灵活分辨率解码。

Abstract: High-resolution dynamic functional connectivity (DFC) can reveal rapidly evolving brain-network interactions, but short temporal windows yield noisy, often low-rank covariance estimates. Graph-Variate Dynamic (GVD) connectivity addresses this by modulating fast instantaneous interactions with stable trial-level support. This suppresses spurious fluctuations and emphasizes persistent, informative connections. We show that the Hadamard construction lifts low-rank instantaneous connectivity from the positive-semidefinite to the positive-definite cone, keeping high-resolution trajectories on the SPD manifold without ridge regularisation or post-hoc projection.
  We introduce GVD-CFM, a class-conditional generative model for high-resolution dynamic connectivity. Each trial is represented as SPD GVD matrices on a product Riemannian manifold, then mapped through a global log-Euclidean diffeomorphism and an invertible temporal DCT basis. A Transformer-based conditional flow models all spectral modes jointly and generates the full trajectory non-autoregressively in Euclidean coordinates while preserving exact correspondence with valid SPD sequences. Retaining the full DCT basis also enables decoding on denser temporal grids without retraining.
  Across multiple EEG motor-imagery datasets, GVD-CFM delivers the strongest overall results for held-out distributional fidelity, temporal-dynamics preservation, and synthetic-to-real classification. It also remains computationally efficient relative to strong raw-signal and direct GVD-space generative baselines. GVD-CFM therefore provides a practical framework for realistic, temporally coherent, high-resolution brain-network generation with preserved manifold structure and resolution-flexible decoding from a single trained model.

</details>


### [214] [NowcastDiT: Diffusion Transformers are Effective Precipitation Nowcasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37038)
*Haoran Xu,Xingzhuo Guo,Yuchen Zhang,Jincheng Zhong,Jianmin Wang,Mingsheng Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NowcastDiT，证明标准Diffusion Transformer即可作为降水临近预报的简单可扩展基础，通过动力学感知噪声先验和端到端强化学习两项适配，在SEVIR与MRMS基准上取得感知质量与气象技能的双重最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降水临近预报在强时空变异下需要准确的短期预测。扩散模型适合建模复杂降水分布，但现有方法倾向于引入日益专门化的设计，反而使标准扩散架构的潜力未被充分探索。

Method: 以标准Diffusion Transformer为基础框架，将领域需求自然纳入其设计空间，并提出两项互补适配：(1) 动力学感知噪声先验，用于产生时间连贯的预报；(2) 端到端强化学习，配合时间步感知奖励以提升气象技能。

Result: 在SEVIR与MRMS基准上，NowcastDiT在感知质量和气象技能两方面均达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 标准DiT可作为降水临近预报的有效基础，无需过度专门化设计即可兼顾感知质量与气象技能。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting demands accurate short-term forecasts under strong spatiotemporal variability. Diffusion models are well suited to modeling complex precipitation distributions, yet existing approaches often introduce increasingly specialized designs, leaving the capability of a standard diffusion architecture underexplored. We show that a standard Diffusion Transformer already provides a simple and scalable foundation for precipitation nowcasting, with domain-specific requirements accommodated naturally within its design space. Based on this principle, we develop NowcastDiT and instantiate this flexibility through two complementary adaptations: a dynamics-aware noise prior for temporally coherent forecasts, and end-to-end reinforcement learning with timestep-aware rewards for meteorological skill. Experiments on SEVIR and MRMS benchmarks show that NowcastDiT achieves state-of-the-art performance in both perceptual quality and meteorological skill. These results suggest that standard DiT can serve as an effective foundation for precipitation nowcasting.

</details>


### [215] [Beam Search as Test-Time Self-Distillation via Counterfactual Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37041)
*Su Ee Tan,Xiaotong Ji,Rasul Tutunov,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Matthieu Zimmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"测试时自蒸馏"解码方法，用反事实模板替代演示，通过log-odds比构造奖励信号，以Gibbs重加权目标并结合beam search近似，在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA上优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原SDFT方法只在训练时可用，依赖梯度更新与专家演示，无法用于推理。作者希望在推理阶段实现自蒸馏原理，无需参数更新、奖励模型或训练数据。

Method: 用固定文本模板构造反事实上下文（分别"诱导"优秀与糟糕推理）替代演示；在两种反事实条件下的log-odds比定义新奖励信号；推导KL正则下的最优策略，得到对基分布的Gibbs重加权；因该重加权是全局的、不能逐token分解，故用beam search近似目标分布。

Result: 在数学推理MATH500、代码生成HumanEval、研究生级科学问答GPQA上，跨多个模型规模，测试时自蒸馏平均优于标准采样、低温采样、beam search和power sampling基线。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏原理可成功在推理时实现，无需训练即可通过反事实上下文与全局重加权解码提升生成质量。

Abstract: Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning (SDFT) enables a language model to act as its own teacher: by conditioning on a demonstration, the model produces an implicit reward via pointwise mutual information, which guides on-policy learning without external supervision. However, SDFT operates at training time: it requires gradient updates and access to expert demonstrations, making it inapplicable at inference. We propose test-time self-distillation, a decoding-time method that extracts a steering signal from the self-distillation framework without any parameter updates, reward models, or training data. Our key insight is that counterfactual contexts, i.e. fixed textual templates that hypothetically prime the model for excellent versus poor reasoning, can substitute for the demonstration. The log-odds ratio of a candidate answer under these two counterfactual conditions defines a new reward signal. We derive the optimal KL-regularized policy under this reward, which takes the form of a Gibbs reweighting of the base distribution. Crucially, this reweighting is global: it cannot be decomposed into independent per-token operations without ignoring future trajectory quality. We therefore approximate the target distribution via beam search. Experiments on mathematical reasoning (MATH500), code generation (HumanEval), and graduate-level science QA (GPQA) across multiple model scales show that test-time self-distillation improves over standard sampling, low temperature, beam search and power sampling baselines on average, demonstrating that the self-distillation principle can be operationalized at inference time.

</details>


### [216] [Iterative Exact Discrete Guidance for Energy-Based Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37043)
*Yuwen Qian,Yidong Ouyang,Zhengyan Wan,Hongyuan Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代精确离散引导（IEDG），一种面向未归一化离散目标的种群精确、轨迹级引导框架，通过退火轨迹上的全局玻尔兹曼倾斜分阶段学习局部后验修正，实现精确反向动力学并复现目标分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多模态目标分布远离可处理的参考分布时，从大规模离散状态空间上的未归一化分布中采样十分困难。现有方法通常试图一步学习从参考到目标的完整修正，难以应对重叠不足带来的拟合误差放大问题。

Method: IEDG不一步学习完整修正，而是沿退火轨迹引入全局玻尔兹曼倾斜。每个阶段针对当前源的增量玻尔兹曼倾斜学习阶段局部后验修正，并将修正相对于固定的解析后验进行累积。阶段增量由相对有效样本量（rESS）选择，以控制Rényi-2位移并局部自适应步长。理论上给出阶段式全变差分析。

Result: 在可精确枚举的Ising 4×4上，IEDG在全部分布级误差上优于神经基线；在16×16 Ising/Potts上于各热力学区间显著降低one-shot误差，并在多项局部统计与相覆盖指标上取得最佳神经采样器结果；在Max-Cut上，best-of-512与平均样本比率超过所有基线。

Conclusion: IEDG通过在退火路径上分阶段学习局部修正并利用rESS自适应步长，为未归一化离散目标提供了种群精确的采样框架，理论与实验均表明其在多模态离散采样任务上优于现有神经基线。代码已开源。

Abstract: Sampling from unnormalized distributions over large discrete state spaces becomes difficult when a multimodal target is far from a tractable reference. We introduce Iterative Exact Discrete Guidance (IEDG), a population-exact, trajectory-wise guidance framework for unnormalized discrete targets. Rather than learn the full reference-to-target correction in one step, IEDG introduces a global Boltzmann tilt along an annealing trajectory. Each stage learns a stage-local posterior correction for an incremental Boltzmann tilt of the current source, while the resulting corrections are accumulated relative to a fixed analytic posterior. At the population optimum, exact stage posteriors recover the correct reverse dynamics, whose exact simulation reproduces the target distribution. IEDG chooses stage increments by relative effective sample size (rESS), which controls Rényi-2 displacement and locally adapts the step size to the thermodynamic geometry of the annealing path. Our stagewise total-variation analysis shows that limited overlap amplifies Bregman fitting error by $1/\sqrt{\mathrm{rESS}}$, while posterior, simulation, and truncation errors enter additively. IEDG improves all distribution-level errors over the neural baselines on ordered, exactly enumerated Ising $4\times4$, while substantially reducing one-shot errors on Ising/Potts $16\times16$ across thermodynamic regimes and attaining the best neural-sampler result on several reported local-statistic and phase-coverage metrics. On Max-Cut, its best-of-512 and average-sample ratios exceed all the baselines. Code and artifacts are available at https://github.com/StillFantasy123/iterative-exact-discrete-guidance.

</details>


### [217] [Message Passing Does More with Less for In-Context Learning on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37057)
*Dooho Lee,Jinmo Lee,Minho Jeong,Kijung Shin,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ephris是一个基于稀疏消息传递的图上下文学习器，避免了稠密注意力带来的高昂推理成本，在51个节点分类数据集上取得最优综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN通常需要对每个数据集进行训练和超参数调优，代价重复且高昂。图上下文学习（ICL）用单个预训练模型直接从有标注的上下文节点预测未知节点标签，从而避免上述成本。但现有图ICL方法依赖节点间的稠密注意力，随着图规模增长推理成本急剧上升。

Method: Ephris建立在稀疏消息传递之上，计算量随节点特征条目数和图边数线性扩展。模型完全在由结构因果模型生成的合成图上预训练，这些图具有多样的图结构和关系动态，使模型接触到拓扑、特征与标签之间的多种依赖关系。

Result: 在51个节点分类数据集上，与15个经过充分调优的GNN及现有图ICL方法对比，在高标签与低标签两种train/val/test划分下，Ephris在Elo、可提升性（improvability）、平均排名和准确率四项综合指标上均排名第一。其推理成本相当于训练单个GNN一次的代价，且比以往的图ICL模型快10倍以上。

Conclusion: 结果表明强性能的图ICL并不需要稠密注意力，Ephris推进了性能-运行时间的帕累托前沿。代码与模型权重已开源于 https://github.com/nums-ai/ephris。

Abstract: Achieving strong performance with graph neural networks (GNNs) typically requires training and hyperparameter tuning for each dataset, incurring repeated costs and effort. Graph in-context learning (ICL) avoids this by using a single pretrained model to predict unknown node labels directly from labeled context nodes. Existing approaches, however, rely on dense attention across nodes, making inference increasingly expensive as graphs grow. In this work, we present Ephris, a new graph in-context learner built on sparse message passing, scaling linearly with the number of node-feature entries and graph edges. Ephris is pretrained entirely on synthetic graphs generated from structural causal models with diverse graph structures and relational dynamics, exposing the model to varied dependencies among topology, features, and labels. We evaluate Ephris on 51 node-classification datasets against 15 extensively tuned GNNs and existing graph ICL methods under both high- and low-label train/validation/test splits. Across both settings, Ephris ranks first on all four aggregate measures: Elo, improvability, average rank, and accuracy. Its inference cost remains comparable to training a single GNN once, while being over 10 times faster than previous graph ICL models. Together, these results advance the performance-runtime Pareto frontier, demonstrating that strong graph ICL does not require dense attention. Code and model weights are available at https://github.com/nums-ai/ephris.

</details>


### [218] [A Comprehensive View of Fairness through Distributional Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37061)
*Gayane Taturyan,Charlotte Laclau,Stephan Clémencon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将公平性视为分布稳定性：若预测器在改变受保护群体组成的扰动下保持稳定，则视为公平，并据此提出学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平性评估通常基于固定数据分布，无法保证在测试时人口组成变化下仍然公平，因此需要一种不依赖部署分布知识的公平性视角。

Method: 将公平性定义为对特定扰动的稳定性，用预测率泛函的Lipschitz常数刻画不公平差距；提出基于重加权预测器凸组合的学习方法，形式化为二阶锥规划，并建立泛化界。

Result: 该表述可在不预先知道部署分布的情况下，提供对测试时一系列人口组成均成立的公平性保证；在标准基准实验上验证了方法效果。

Conclusion: 公平性可统一理解为分布扰动下的稳定性，经典公平概念可作为特例出现；所提凸优化学习过程具备泛化保证，并通过基准实验得到说明。

Abstract: We view fairness as a property of distributional stability. Rather than assessing a predictor under a fixed data distribution, we study how its predictions change under perturbations that modify the composition of protected groups. A predictor is fair if it remains stable under such shifts. Under this perspective, several classical notions of fairness arise as stability with respect to specific perturbations, with the associated unfairness gap given by a Lipschitz constant of a prediction-rate functional. This formulation also yields guarantees that hold uniformly over a range of demographic compositions at test time, without requiring knowledge of the deployment distribution. It leads to a learning procedure based on convex combinations of reweighted predictors, formulated as a second-order cone program, for which we establish generalization bounds. Experiments on standard benchmarks illustrate the approach.

</details>


### [219] [RL-PaO: Prediction as Action in Decision Making under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37065)
*Jiahui Feng,Dafang Zhao,Zheng Chen,Zhengmao Li,Lingwei Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RL-PaO强化学习框架，将"预测即动作"，从决策后果中学习，使预测与下游优化对齐；在日前能源调度任务上取得非神谕基线中最低年成本，平均降本约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定环境下的决策依赖预测参数，但预测准确并不必然带来好的运营决策；需要让预测从其所诱导决策的后果中学习，以实现预测与下游优化的对齐。

Method: 将系统建模、优化求解与决策执行整合为单一环境，构建马尔可夫决策过程，把预测视为动作：动作推动环境产生后续上下文与奖励，使预测误差与真实成本显式对齐；学习最优策略无需对黑盒求解器求导。在基于真实历史数据的日前能源调度上评估。

Result: 在测试年份，RL-PaO在非神谕基线中取得最低年成本，平均成本降低约10%；并可从策略演化与成本-精度权衡两方面进行深入分析，具备较强可解释性。

Conclusion: RL-PaO表明，以强化学习直接优化决策后果、而非追求预测精度，能更有效地对齐预测与优化，并在实际能源调度中获得显著成本收益与可解释性。

Abstract: Decision-making under uncertainty often relies on predicted parameters, yet accurate prediction does not necessarily lead to good operational decisions. Aligning prediction with downstream optimization requires learning from the consequences of the decisions those predictions induce. We introduce RL-PaO, a reinforcement learning framework that integrates system formulation, optimization, and decision execution into a single environment. This yields a Markov decision process in which prediction is regarded as action: it shifts the environment to produce subsequent context and reward that explicitly aligns prediction error with realized cost, and learning the optimal policy does not require differentiating through the black-box solver. We evaluate RL-PaO on day-ahead energy scheduling using real historical data. On the test year, RL-PaO achieves the lowest annual cost among the non-oracle baselines, achieving on average $10\%$ cost reduction. Moreover, RL-PaO is capable of further analyses to provide strong interpretability both from the policy evolution perspective and the cost-accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [220] [Beyond Compression: Diagnosing How Post-Training Changes Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37066)
*Hongyang Li,Yiming Zhu,Xiao Li,Caesar Wu,Said Mammar,Pascal Bouvry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过跨表层pass@K、一致性、分布形态与SFT成员资格等诊断方法，比较了三种后训练路径（自研充分训练的离策略蒸馏、Qwen3发布的离策略+在策略蒸馏端点、DeepSeek-Math的GRPO端点），发现后训练存在两种机制：在简单题上主要压缩采样成本，在难题上则扩张可达上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM的数学推理高度依赖后训练，但仅用端点的pass@1无法识别能力变化的本质。收益可能来自新可解问题、潜在解采样更廉价、表层稳健性或记忆。因此需要一个共同诊断读数来区分这些来源，明确后训练究竟改变了什么。

Method: 在统一诊断读数下比较三条后训练路径：充分训练的离策略蒸馏轨迹、Qwen3发布的离策略+在策略蒸馏端点、DeepSeek-Math的GRPO端点。探针包括对逐字提示、复述、数值同构与翻译的跨表层pass@K，以及一致性、分布形态和经核验的SFT成员资格分析。另进行受控过拟合审计以检验SFT成员资格探针的敏感性。

Result: 发现两种机制。简单AMC题上大K上限接近饱和，后训练主要压缩采样成本；困难AIME题上后训练扩张大K上限：充分离策略蒸馏已能抬高上限，Qwen3发布端点进一步抬高，而DeepSeek-MathGRPO在大K下并不优于充分离策略蒸馏。英语主导的蒸馏改善非英语推理，但仍保留语言层级差距。受控过拟合审计发现当前SFT成员资格探针敏感度有限。

Conclusion: 压缩只是后训练的一种机制，并非普遍解释。端点pass@1会掩盖大K上限变化、语言层级差距与记忆敏感度等差异，采用跨表层pass@K与多重诊断读数才能更准确刻画后训练带来的能力变化。

Abstract: Post-training is central to mathematical reasoning in modern large language models (LLMs), but endpoint pass@1 alone underidentifies what has changed. Gains may reflect newly reachable solutions, cheaper sampling of latent solutions, surface robustness, or memorisation. We compare three post-training paths under a common diagnostic readout: our sufficiently trained off-policy distillation trajectories, released Qwen3 off-policy-plus-on-policy distillation endpoints, and a released DeepSeek-Math endpoint trained with Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO). Our probe uses cross-surface pass@K over verbatim prompts, paraphrases, numerical isomorphisms, and translations, plus consistency, distribution-shape, and verified supervised-fine-tuning (SFT) membership analyses. We find two regimes. On easier AMC problems, large-K ceilings are near saturation, so post-training mainly compresses sample cost. On harder AIME problems, post-training expands the large-K ceiling over the base model: sufficient off-policy distillation already raises this ceiling, Qwen3 released endpoints raise it further, and DeepSeek-Math GRPO does not dominate sufficient off-policy distillation at large K. English-dominant distillation improves non-English reasoning but preserves language-tier gaps. A controlled-overfit audit finds limited sensitivity in current SFT-membership probes. Compression is one regime of post-training, not a universal explanation.

</details>


### [221] [UnlearningSoup: Is Repeated Tuning Necessary for Large Language Model Unlearning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37076)
*Puning Yang,Qizhou Wang,Junchi Yu,Bo Han,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 UnlearningSoup 框架，利用权重空间中的"共享性能盆地"结构，通过插值与重加权"汤"策略替代反复调参，实现更高效的机器遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于训练的遗忘方法（如梯度上升）需在遗忘与保留之间权衡，超参数选择困难，需反复调参，且跨模型、跨数据集迁移性差。

Method: 发现不同遗忘运行得到的模型仍处于共享的评估性能盆地，据此提出统一框架 UnlearningSoup，包含两种策略：EfficientSoup 用二分搜索插值在早期快速找到表现良好的模型；PerformanceSoup 用重加权"汤"（模型融合）在后期高效挖掘剩余性能潜力。

Result: 在多种数据集和模型上的实验表明，UnlearningSoup 在超参数选择上带来 2.4 倍至 3.3 倍的效率提升，并在各设置下持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 利用遗忘运行在权重空间中的可挖掘结构，可通过"汤"策略替代反复调参，兼顾效率与性能，并具有跨设置的良好适应性。

Abstract: Large language models trained on vast corpora inherently risk memorizing harmful content that may later re-emerge in their outputs. To mitigate this issue, existing unlearning methods typically rely on training-based parameter updates, such as gradient ascent and its variants, to delete targeted content while preserving other knowledge. However, balancing the competing goals of forgetting and retention makes hyperparameter choices for these methods particularly difficult, often requiring repeated tuning to obtain a strong model that still leaves substantial room for improvement and transfers poorly across models and datasets. To address this challenge, we investigate whether unlearning runs exhibit exploitable structure in weight space, and observe that models from different runs still lie in a shared evaluation-performance basin. This suggests that stronger models may be recovered through an unlearning-tailored soup strategy, reducing the need for repeated tuning for further improvement or new settings. Motivated by this, we propose UnlearningSoup, a unified framework that provides two strategies: EfficientSoup uses binary-search-based interpolation to quickly discover a well-performing model in the early stage, where repeated tuning would otherwise make strong model selection costly. PerformanceSoup uses reweighted souping to efficiently unlock the remaining performance potential in the later stage, where repeated tuning becomes increasingly inefficient. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and models show that UnlearningSoup delivers 2.4x to 3.3x efficiency gains in hyperparameter selection, while consistently improving performance across settings.

</details>


### [222] [ZeroDiff: Zero-Shot Time Series Reconstruction via Informed-Prior Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37078)
*Yingda Fan,Dan Lu,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出零样本时间序列重建任务，用扩散模型从仅外生变量构建先验并校准重建误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列建模需要高质量监督，但目标观测常常缺失：外生输入易获得，而目标测量因成本、基础设施或可及性限制而难以采集，因此希望模型能在从未采集过目标数据的观测位置重建目标序列。

Method: 指出直接将外生输入映射到目标会产生过于平滑、低估极值的输出，无法捕捉目标变量内在动态，存在系统性误差。为此提出 ZeroDiff：仅利用外生变量构建有信息量的先验，再通过扩散模型学习校准重建误差，在已观测位置训练并泛化到未观测位置。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，ZeroDiff 相比现有方法有显著提升。

Conclusion: 零样本时间序列重建是一个有价值且具挑战性的问题，显式建模并校准重建误差的扩散框架能有效提升未观测位置的重建质量。

Abstract: Time series modeling increasingly demands high-quality supervision, yet target observations remain scarce - exogenous inputs are broadly available, but target measurements are often unavailable due to cost, infrastructure, or accessibility constraints. Can models trained on observed locations reconstruct target time series where measurements have never been collected? We term this zero-shot time series reconstruction. A naive approach - directly mapping exogenous inputs to targets - can yield predictions at unobserved locations, but without target signals, such models fail to capture the intrinsic dynamics of the target variable, producing overly smooth outputs that underestimate extremes. This reveals systematic errors that call for explicit modeling and calibration. We propose ZeroDiff, which constructs an informed prior from exogenous variables alone, then learns to calibrate reconstruction errors through diffusion - training on observed locations and generalizing to unobserved ones. Experiments across diverse real-world datasets demonstrate significant improvements over existing approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/YingdaFan/ZeroDiff-ICML2026.

</details>


### [223] [Identifying ODEs from Unstructured Data with Causal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37083)
*Alessandro Trenta,Riccardo Massidda,Davide Bacciu,Sara Magliacane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPEED-AE框架结合预训练因果表示学习与逐分量自编码器，从高维观测（如图像）中恢复稀疏ODE，并将变量可识别性从多项式收缩到单项式微分同胚，在多个系统上实现最优拟合与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从图像等非结构化高维观测中发现动力系统的ODE是重要问题，但现有ODE发现方法通常假设可直接测量变量，或缺乏对所学变量与方程的理论保证。CRL方法虽能在分量级微分同胚意义下识别变量，但这些变量一般无法直接用于方程发现（后者假设变量能导出稀疏方程）。

Method: 提出SPEED-AE：将预训练CRL方法与逐分量自编码器结合，学习有利于稀疏ODE发现的变量变换。对多项式ODE，该额外步骤将每个变量的可识别性从多项式微分同胚收紧为单项式微分同胚。

Result: 在Lotka-Volterra、Lorenz和双摆系统上的实验表明，SPEED-AE改善了CRL方法的解耦效果，恢复的ODE最接近真实方程，并达到最优预测性能。

Conclusion: SPEED-AE通过显式学习利于稀疏发现的变量变换，桥接了CRL与方程发现，为从高维观测中可识别地恢复ODE提供了可行且有效的方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering the governing ODE of a dynamical system from unstructured, high-dimensional observations such as images. Existing methods for ODE discovery typically assume direct measurements of the variables, or do not provide theoretical guarantees on the learned variables and equations. While Causal Representation Learning (CRL) methods provide guarantees on identifying variables from high-dimensional observations up to component-wise diffeomorphisms, we show that in general these variables cannot be used directly as input to equation discovery methods, which typically assume that the variables will lead to sparse equations. So we introduce SParse Equivalent Equation Discovery AutoEncoder (SPEED-AE), a framework that combines a pretrained CRL method with a component-wise autoencoder that learns transformations of variables that are amenable to sparse ODE discovery. We show that for polynomial ODEs, this additional step allows us to restrict the identifiability of each variable from polynomial to monomial diffeomorphisms. Experiments on Lotka-Volterra, Lorenz, and a two-pendulum system show that SPEED-AE improves on the disentanglement of the CRL methods and that it recovers ODEs that are closest to the ground truth, while achieving state-of-the-art forecasting performance.

</details>


### [224] [VACE: Validation-Gated Alternating Co-Evolution of Agent Models and Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37105)
*Jiexing Qi,Yu He,Jun Liu,Qichen Huang,Shaohua Hu,Zhan Dang,Guohua Chen,Rui Yang,Wen Jiang,Yang Liu,Tao Lyu,Fangming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VACE方法，交替进行智能体强化学习与轨迹驱动的harness精炼，并通过验证门控决定是否采纳harness修改，在OfficeQA和AutomationBench上显著超越仅更新权重RL与无门控交替方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体的提升可来自模型权重更新或执行框架（harness）改进，但二者相互耦合：权重更新改变模型对harness的使用方式，harness更新又改变训练轨迹。现有方法未充分处理这种耦合关系，因此需要一种能协同演化两者并避免有害更新的机制。

Method: VACE（Validation-Gated Alternating CoEvolution）交替执行智能体强化学习与轨迹驱动的harness精炼。每轮RL阶段后，复用已采集轨迹提出harness修订方案，在固定更新后模型的条件下评估现有与候选harness；仅当候选方案提升验证性能时，才用于后续训练。

Result: 使用Qwen3.5-9B，VACE在OfficeQA上达到45.26%测试准确率，在AutomationBench上平均部分得分75.19%，分别超过仅权重RL 6.43和9.09个百分点，超过无门控交替4.59和6.95个百分点。44个harness提案中有17个在更新检查点下降低验证性能并被拒绝。

Conclusion: 验证门控是VACE有效性的关键，可防止有害的harness修改进入后续RL训练。权重与harness的协同演化优于单一组件优化，为智能体持续改进提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Language model agents can be improved by updating their model weights or refining the harness that guides task execution. These components are coupled: weight updates change how the model uses the harness, while harness updates change the trajectories used for training. We propose VACE, Validation-Gated Alternating CoEvolution, which alternates agentic reinforcement learning with trajectory-driven harness refinement. After each RL stage, VACE reuses the collected trajectories to propose a harness revision and evaluates the incumbent and candidate with the updated model held fixed. The candidate guides subsequent training only if it improves validation performance. With Qwen3.5-9B, VACE achieves 45.26% test accuracy on OfficeQA and a mean partial-credit score of 75.19% on AutomationBench, exceeding weight-only RL by 6.43 and 9.09 percentage points and ungated alternation by 4.59 and 6.95 points, respectively. Across 44 harness proposals, 17 reduce validation performance at the updated checkpoint and are rejected before subsequent RL training, highlighting the importance of validation gating.

</details>


### [225] [Neural Constitutive Learning for Generalized Reaction-Diffusion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37113)
*Shang-Ke Chen,Yu-Peng Wang,Shih-Hsuan Hung,Wei-Fang Sun,Chao-Shun Zhan,Simon See,Min-Jhe Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NCL-MCT求解器：在共享MCT积分器中学习PDE本构响应，跨七类系统实现10^-4到10^-2的rollout误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义反应-扩散系统包含多样传输机制与耦合反应动力学。对神经PDE求解器的核心问题是：应学习什么内容，才能用统一接口兼容相场与退化传输、局部反应以及多物种耦合。

Method: 提出神经本构定律—质量压缩传输（NCL-MCT）求解器：学习PDE专属的本构响应，而将时间演化保留在共享的MCT积分器中。传输通过迁移率与热力学驱动力表示，反应通过相对反应速率表示。本构响应仅依赖当前密度，而显式依赖初始条件或已流逝时间，因此可跨初始条件与时间跨度复用。同一接口支持速度数据监督与已知定律监督，二者训练时均无需时间积分；当本构定律已知时，可在独立采样的密度场上评估监督，实现无轨迹的本构学习。

Result: 在七个系统上，独立训练的本构模块共享同一接口与MCT积分器，相对rollout L2误差为10^-4至10^-2。未见初始条件族与延长时间跨度的测试评估了训练条件之外的复用性，另有实验验证了无轨迹本构学习。

Conclusion: 本构响应可作为共享神经PDE框架的有效学习目标。

Abstract: Generalized reaction-diffusion systems encompass diverse transport mechanisms and coupled reaction kinetics. A central question for neural PDE solvers is what should be learned so that a common interface can accommodate phase-field and degenerate transport, local reactions, and multispecies coupling. We propose the Neural Constitutive Laws--Mass-Compression-Transport (NCL-MCT) Solver, which learns PDE-specific constitutive responses while retaining temporal evolution in a shared MCT integrator. Transport is represented through mobility and thermodynamic driving force, and reaction through relative reaction rates. These constitutive responses depend on the current density rather than explicitly on the initial condition or elapsed time, motivating their reuse across different initial conditions and time horizons. The same interface supports velocity-data supervision and known-law supervision, neither of which requires time integration during training. When constitutive laws are known, supervision can be evaluated on independently sampled density fields, enabling trajectory-free constitutive learning without generating solution trajectories. Across seven systems, separately trained constitutive modules share the same interface and MCT integrator and achieve relative rollout $L^2$ errors of $10^{-4}$ to $10^{-2}$. Tests with unseen initial-condition families and an extended time horizon assess reuse beyond training conditions, while separate experiments demonstrate trajectory-free constitutive learning. These results support constitutive responses as an effective learning target for a shared neural PDE framework.

</details>


### [226] [Interpretable intrinsic dimension estimation through componentwise calibration of distance and angle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37114)
*Chih-Hsuan Huang,Chih-Wei Chen,Szu-Chi Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将DANCo按距离与角度分量分解重构，分别用闭式KL散度改进距离估计（Gride）与均值方向对齐（Profiled）改进角度估计，从而在噪声与幅度异质数据上诊断并提升内在维度估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始DANCo联合校准最近邻距离与角度统计，在干净基准上精度领先，但真实数据中的邻域相对噪声与样本振幅异质性会扭曲这些几何信号，且联合形式难以判断偏差来源，因此需要分量化的重构以便识别与解释估计来源。

Method: 将DANCo按分量重写，保留独立的距离与角度差异曲线；对距离分量推导广义比值ID估计器（Gride）通用阶数比值的闭式KL散度，并在角度参数均匹配（Full）时使用；对角度分量针对两种采样情形，提出在对齐均值方向的同时保留集中度匹配（Profiled）。

Result: 在24个流形上、噪声为典型邻居间距40%时，Full下Gride将平均百分比误差从27.7%降至17.6%；在生成维度70嵌入100维的高斯尺度混合上，Profiled将MiND从22.8提升到66.7，去除已知振幅后MiND-Full恢复至71.9，从而把Full的不足归因于振幅异质性；在CIFAR-10与ImageNet上，降幅归一化使角度位置趋近参考并缩小Full-Profiled差距；在四个预训练CNN上，Gride-Profiled、TWO-NN与MLE呈相似升降轮廓，而Full-Profiled差异可识别对角度校准最敏感的层。

Conclusion: 分量化的DANCo框架既能提供可解释的估计来源归因，又能通过距离侧闭式KL改进与角度侧Profiled对齐，在噪声和振幅异质性条件下稳健提升内在维度估计，并为深度网络层敏感性的观测性诊断提供工具。

Abstract: DANCo (Dimensionality from Angle and Norm Concentration) jointly calibrates nearest-neighbor distance and angular statistics and consistently reaches state-of-the-art accuracy on clean intrinsic-dimension (ID) benchmarks. Practical data, however, introduce neighborhood-relative noise and sample-amplitude heterogeneity that can distort these geometric signals. We reformulate DANCo componentwise, retaining separate distance and angular discrepancy curves so that the source of an estimate can be identified and interpreted. For the distance component, we derive a closed-form Kullback-Leibler divergence for the generic-order ratios of the generalized ratios ID estimator (Gride); when both angular parameters are matched (Full), Gride reduces mean percentage error from $27.7\%$ to $17.6\%$ at noise equal to $40\%$ of typical neighbor spacing on 24 manifolds. For the angular component, two sampling regimes motivate aligning mean direction while retaining concentration matching (Profiled). On a Gaussian scale mixture with generating dimension 70 embedded in 100 dimensions, profiling raises the Minimum Neighbor Distance (MiND) estimate from $22.8$ to $66.7$, while removing the known amplitudes restores MiND-Full to $71.9$; the control thus attributes the Full shortfall to amplitude heterogeneity. On CIFAR-10 and ImageNet, amplitude-reducing normalizations move angular location toward the references and narrow the Full-Profiled gap, an observational counterpart to the controlled mixture. Across four pretrained convolutional neural networks, Gride-Profiled, the two-nearest-neighbor estimator (TWO-NN), and the maximum-likelihood estimator (MLE) exhibit similar rise-and-fall profiles, while Full-Profiled differences identify the layers most sensitive to angular calibration.

</details>


### [227] [Unlocking the Critic: Reward-Free Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37119)
*Hongyang Li,Xiao Li,Caesar Wu,Said Mammar,Grégoire Danoy,Pascal Bouvry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了无评论家（critic-free）范式，提出利用预训练评论家作为奖励与基线来提升长时程推理的强化学习训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期大语言模型强化学习后训练倾向于去除评论家以减少训练不稳定性和内存开销，但评论家学到预测未来结果的能力被浪费。作者希望重新利用这一能力服务于高效的长时程推理。

Method: 首先指出评论家式RL的不稳定主要是优化伪影，通过小步、低方差策略更新可恢复稳定收敛。然后将一个已校准、冻结的评论家复用为rollout级奖励、用于GAE的值基线以及未完成前缀的成功预测器，并提出免奖励策略优化（RFPO）；进一步对去偏分数进行二值化以抑制长度偏差。

Result: 二值化后的RFPO在训练循环中无需任何标签即可匹配有监督PPO，同时降低计算与内存开销；由于rollout可在完成前被奖励，训练无需等待所有轨迹完成。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了主流的无评论家范式，确立了基于评论家的、免奖励优化作为LLM后训练的可扩展且计算高效的路径。

Abstract: Recent approaches to reinforcement learning (RL) post-training for large language models increasingly remove the critic to reduce training instability and memory overhead. Even where a critic is trained, it is discarded once training ends, although it has learned to predict outcomes. We revisit this trend and show that a pretrained critic's ability to predict future outcomes can make it a valuable asset for efficient long-horizon reasoning. First, we find that instability in critic-based RL for long chain-of-thought reasoning is largely an optimization artifact: keeping policy updates small and low in variance restores stable convergence. Second, a well-pretrained critic estimates the posterior probability of eventual success from later trajectory states and unfinished prefixes. Its predictions provide outcome-derived, dense, per-prefix learning signals that, during policy optimization, require neither completed rollouts, step-level annotations, nor external reward labels. Building on this insight, we introduce Reward-Free Policy Optimization (RFPO), which repurposes a single calibrated, frozen critic as a rollout-level reward, a value baseline for generalized advantage estimation, and a success forecaster for unfinished prefixes. We further show that binarizing the debiased score stops the policy from exploiting the critic's length bias. Binarized, RFPO matches supervised PPO without a single label in the training loop, while cutting compute and memory overhead. This makes RFPO well suited to long-horizon reasoning tasks, where outcomes arrive late and generation dominates cost: because rollouts can be rewarded before they finish, training no longer has to pay for waiting on every trajectory to complete. Our findings challenge the prevailing critic-free paradigm and establish critic-based, reward-free optimization as a scalable and computationally efficient path for LLM post-training.

</details>


### [228] [Learning the Structure of Triangular Transport Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37122)
*Morten Blørstad,Pekka Parviainen,Berent Ånund Strømnes Lunde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SSTM 联合学习三角传输映射的映射、变量排序与稀疏结构，用 SoftSort 学排序、L0 门学稀疏，并用 BatchEnsemble 共享权重实现可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角传输映射的质量强烈依赖其结构（变量排序与稀疏模式，即 DAG），但寻找好结构计算昂贵，因为每个候选都需重新拟合映射。核心挑战是在维度增长时联合学习密度与结构并保持可计算性。

Method: 提出 Self-Structuring Transport Maps (SSTM)：用 SoftSort 学习变量排序，用 L0 gates 学习稀疏模式，同时保持三角结构；为可扩展性使用单调 BatchEnsemble，通过秩一适配器在所有映射组件间共享一个权重矩阵。

Result: 在合成和真实数据上，联合学习结构与映射比先估结构再拟合映射获得更好的密度估计；当结构可从密度中辨识时，SSTM 匹配使用真实结构拟合的映射的密度性能，并优于自回归流；在大数据集上与自回归流相当。

Conclusion: 联合学习映射与结构可提升采样式概率建模的密度估计效果与可扩展性，是应对高维结构学习挑战的有效方案。

Abstract: Triangular transport maps provide a flexible approach to sampling-based probabilistic modeling, including density estimation, generative modeling, and Bayesian inference. They transform an unknown target distribution into a simpler reference through a monotone triangular map. The map structure is defined by a variable ordering and sparsity pattern, which together encode a directed acyclic graph. Map quality can depend strongly on this structure, yet finding a good structure is computationally expensive because each candidate generally requires fitting a different map. A central challenge is therefore to learn density and structure jointly, while keeping computation manageable as dimension grows. We introduce Self-Structuring Transport Maps (SSTM), which learn the map, ordering, and sparsity jointly. We use SoftSort to learn the variable ordering and $L_0$ gates to learn the sparsity, while preserving a triangular structure. To keep the map scalable, we use a monotone BatchEnsemble that shares one weight matrix across all map components through rank-one adapters. Across synthetic and real data, jointly learning the structure and map gives better density estimates than estimating the structure first. When the structure is identifiable from the density, SSTM matches the density performance of a map fitted with the true structure and outperforms autoregressive flows. On large datasets, SSTM is competitive with autoregressive flows.

</details>


### [229] [Multimodal Detection of Higher-Order Behavioral Constructs: Self-Compassion in Structured Reflective Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37148)
*Siddhant Jain,Dimitra Tsovaltzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文以自我慈悲（self-compassion）为案例，研究如何在结构化的反思性访谈中，从视频、音频、文本多模态信号推断难以直接观测的心理建构，并构建了首个相关标注数据集与可复现的多模态建模流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多影响学习与成长的心理特质（如情绪调节、挫折反思、对话中的共情觉察）无法直接观测，只能通过言语、动作和声音随时间推断，且难以套用主流机器学习的清晰标注范式。自我慈悲有坚实的心理学理论支撑，却极少被计算建模。现有数据集也未捕捉此类建构在技术中介训练场景中的表达，因此需要专门收集与标注数据并探索其可计算性。

Method: 收集并标注了51段反思性对话会话，采用基于既有理论、独立且时间上重叠的标注方案；将原有六成分心理学模型整合为三类监督空间（自我友善与正念 对 自我批判或不堪重负状态）；构建可复现的基于窗口的流程，在统一时间轴上对齐视频、音频与文本；分别训练各模态的单模态模型，并与简单的概率级融合策略进行比较。

Result: 单模态模型与概率级融合策略相比，融合相对最佳单模态仅带来适度但一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了各模态在建模该建构时的优势与局限，揭示了此类心理特质在反思性言语中的实际表达方式，并指出若要更有效地建模自我慈悲及类似建构，还需在数据、标注与融合方法上做进一步工作。

Abstract: Many of the qualities that matter most in how people learn and grow, how someone regulates their emotions, reflects on a setback, or stays aware of others during a difficult conversation, are not directly observable. They have to be inferred from how someone speaks, moves, and sounds over time, and they resist the kind of clean labeling that most machine learning pipelines are built around. We study this challenge through a case that is well grounded in psychological theory but rarely modeled computationally: self-compassion, the tendency to respond to one's own setbacks with patience rather than harsh self-criticism. We examine how it appears during structured reflective interviews in a technology-mediated training setting, where people naturally talk through socio-emotionally demanding situations. Since no existing dataset captures this kind of construct in this kind of setting, we collected and annotated 51 reflective dialog sessions using an independent, temporally overlapping annotation scheme grounded in established theory. We consolidate the underlying six-component psychological model into a three-class supervision space, balancing self-kindness and mindfulness against self-critical or overwhelmed states, and build a reproducible window-based pipeline that aligns video, audio, and text on a shared timeline. Unimodal models trained on each modality separately are compared against a simple probability-level fusion strategy, which yields modest but consistent gains over the best single modality. We close by discussing where each modality succeeds or struggles, what this suggests about how this kind of construct is actually expressed in reflective speech, and what would be needed to model it, and constructs like it, more effectively.

</details>


### [230] [Lucid Dreaming for World Models: Learning to Doubt Imagination and Decide by Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37156)
*Ziqi Wen,Ting Xu,Lianyu Wang,Xian Lin,Yanda Meng,Huazhu Fu,Meng Wang,Ching-Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LucidWM，将主观逻辑引入类别潜在转移，从经验中学习“怀疑度”并通过想象传播信任，用于世界模型的不确定性估计与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型能让智能体在想象中学习与规划，但其超出经验的预测可能不可靠并误导决策。现有基于预测的不确定性估计在面对陌生状态-动作对时往往过度自信，亟需能区分预测结果与证据支持程度的不确定性度量。

Method: LucidWM将主观逻辑（Subjective Logic）集成到类别潜在转移中，区分预测结果与其证据支持，为每个转移赋予怀疑度；怀疑度的补集定义为转移级信任，沿想象轨迹以乘法方式累积，用于重新加权回报以指导策略学习和动作选择。该方法无需额外参数或前向传播即可估计不确定性。

Result: 在四种基础世界模型、十七种不确定性读取方式上评估，LucidWM能检测环境变化并在动作受损的rollout中发出不确定性信号。在受控导航案例中，基于信任行动将到达目标所需步数从362降至190。十五段演示视频展示了LucidWM如何怀疑其“梦境”并据此行动。

Conclusion: LucidWM通过从经验中学习怀疑并沿想象传播信任，提供了一种无需额外开销的不确定性估计与决策机制，能有效提升世界模型在陌生情境下的可靠性。

Abstract: World models enable agents to learn and plan in imagination, but predictions beyond their experience can become unreliable and mislead decisions. Existing uncertainty estimates derived from predictions can remain overconfident on unfamiliar state-action pairs. We propose the Lucid World Model (LucidWM), which learns doubt from experience and propagates trust through imagination. By integrating Subjective Logic into categorical latent transitions, LucidWM distinguishes predicted outcomes from their evidential support and assigns each transition a degree of doubt. The complement of this doubt defines transition-level trust, which accumulates multiplicatively along imagined trajectories to reweight returns for policy learning and guide action selection. Uncertainty estimation requires no additional parameters or forward passes. Evaluated on four base world models against seventeen uncertainty readouts, LucidWM detects environmental changes and signals uncertainty during action-corrupted rollouts. In a controlled navigation case study, acting on trust reduces the number of steps required to reach the goal from 362 to 190. Fifteen demonstration videos show how LucidWM doubts its dreams and acts on that doubt. Videos are available at https://lucidwm.github.io.

</details>


### [231] [GLASS: Global Latent Aggregation with Slot-based Set Decoding for Scalable All-Atom Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37158)
*Hendrik Kraß,Seyed Mohamad Moosavi,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GLASS方法，将原子对应关系从生成传输中分离，实现大规模晶体结构高有效性生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全原子晶体生成随体系规模（如金属有机框架MOF）增大而变难，作者将其归因于粒子空间生成中的"对应问题"：即使在固定目标集上，无索引的置换等变粒子流在集合规模和密度增加时需要更多训练才能可靠生成。

Method: GLASS（Global Latent Aggregation with Slot-based Set Decoding）：在置换不变的全局面隐空间中编码结构，用流匹配学习其分布；通过学习的槽解码器（slot decoder）并行构建所有原子，从而将原子级对应关系从生成传输中移除。

Result: 在MP20上，GLASS与粒子空间模型相当，且流训练在各结构尺寸下均能达到训练数据的有效性；在QMOF子集上，无需以构建块、拓扑或组成为条件即可生成每晶胞最多150个原子的MOF，结构有效性接近训练数据。两个数据集上均出现有效性—新颖性权衡，MOF新颖性受限于自编码器在可用数据上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将对应关系分配与生成传输分离，为更大原子体系的高有效性生成提供了简单途径。

Abstract: Generative models for crystals enable the discovery of novel structures, but scaling all-atom generation to larger systems such as metal--organic frameworks remains challenging. We connect this difficulty to the correspondence problem of particle-space generation. Even on a single fixed target set, index-free permutation-equivariant particle flows require substantially more training for reliable generation as set size and density increase, under both independent and optimal-transport couplings. To resolve this challenge, we introduce GLASS---Global Latent Aggregation with Slot-based Set Decoding, which encodes structures in a permutation-invariant global latent space and learns their distribution via flow matching. A learned-slot decoder constructs all atoms in parallel, removing atom-wise correspondence from generative transport. On MP20, GLASS is competitive with particle-space models, and flow training can reach the validity of the training data at every structure size. On a QMOF subset, GLASS generates MOFs with up to 150 atoms per unit cell without conditioning on building blocks, topology, or composition, and approaches the structural validity of the training data. On both datasets, flow training exposes a validity--novelty tradeoff, and MOF novelty remains limited by autoencoder generalization on the available data. These results show that separating correspondence assignment from generative transport provides a simple route toward high-validity generation of larger atomistic systems.

</details>


### [232] [The Vote Hides the Failure: Aggregation Choice and Noise Robustness in Heart Murmur Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37161)
*Nicholaus Dismas Ladislaus,Olatunji Damilare Emmanuel,Samuel Chol Buol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了两种心音（PCG）杂音检测流程（HMS-Net与BiLSTM）在多种噪声强度下的鲁棒性，发现噪声鲁棒性结论不仅取决于模型本身，也高度依赖评估方式（如聚合规则、准确率指标的选择）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管低资源筛查对心音杂音自动检测的需求日益增长，但该领域的噪声鲁棒性及其度量方法仍缺乏充分研究。

Method: 对两种独立重新实现的流程（HMS-Net——CNN，BiLSTM——LSTM）进行对比评估，采用可控的多强度噪声、噪声增强微调，并进行留出泛化测试；比较不同聚合方式（原生聚合 vs 多数投票）等多种评估设置。

Result: 在匹配聚合下，完整BiLSTM流程在所有条件下准确率与加权准确率均优于完整HMS-Net流程；HMS-Net原生聚合在椒盐噪声下退化远小于同强度下的多数投票聚合，反映其原生规则吸收了窗口级分歧；BiLSTM的多数投票准确率在噪声增强训练后上升，但其个体预测并未改善；HMS-Net的训练效应在一种准确率指标下显著、在另一种下不显著。

Conclusion: 噪声鲁棒性的结论可能取决于评估选择（聚合规则与指标）而非模型本身，因此评估方法需被明确并标准化。

Abstract: Noise robustness in automated phonocardiogram (PCG) murmur detection, and how it is measured, remains underexamined despite growing interest in low-resource screening. We evaluate two independently reimplemented pipelines, Hierarchical Multi-Scale Convolutional Network (HMS-Net)--CNN, and Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM)--LSTM, under controlled, multi-severity noise with noise-augmented fine-tuning and held-out generalization testing. Under matched aggregation, the complete BiLSTM pipeline outperforms the complete HMS-Net pipeline across all conditions in accuracy and Weighted Accuracy. A stable aggregate accuracy score can misrepresent what individual predictions show: HMS-Net's native aggregation degrades under salt-and-pepper noise far less than majority-vote (MV) aggregation at the same severity, a gap reflecting window-level disagreement its native rule absorbs, while BiLSTM's MV accuracy rises after noise-augmented training even though its individual predictions do not improve. HMS-Net's training effect is significant under one accuracy metric but not another. Noise-robustness conclusions can depend as much on evaluation choices as on the models themselves.

</details>


### [233] [Trajectory Soup: Pushing the Compute-Scaling Frontier of LLM Mid-training via Diverse Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37169)
*Zhehao Huang,Changxin Tian,Qingyuan Yang,Kunlong Chen,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Xiaolin Huang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Trajectory Soup，将中期训练预算分配到多个独立分支，通过轨迹内与轨迹间平均合并最强检查点，突破串行算力的饱和瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练大模型的中期训练虽赋予专门与推理能力，但额外串行算力带来回报有限，甚至导致部分能力退化，形成算力吸收上限。

Method: 将中期训练预算分配到多个独立分支，各分支从共享检查点出发；基于验证集选择最强检查点，通过轨迹内与轨迹间平均合并为单一模型；并用局部偏差-方差分析区分两级平均。

Result: 在不同模型规模、学习率调度、token 预算与轨迹数下，Trajectory Soup 在同等预算下优于最强单轨迹平均，且随预算扩大持续提升，经相同后训练流程后优势仍保持。

Conclusion: 轨迹分配与合并是突破中期训练串行饱和、扩展算力扩展前沿的实用方法。

Abstract: Mid-training equips pretrained large language models with specialized and reasoning capabilities, but the returns of this stage are bounded since additional serial compute yields little further downstream improvement and can even degrade some capabilities, which places a practical ceiling on how much compute mid-training absorbs. We revisit how this compute should be allocated to a single run or multiple similar optimizations. We find that branches forked from a shared checkpoint under various controlled recipe reaches measurably different regions of parameter space, and establish a form of compatible diversity that extending one run cannot supply. Therefore, we introduce Trajectory Soup, which distributes a mid-training budget over several independent branches, and consolidates strongest checkpoints selected on validation through intra- and inter-trajectory averaging into a single model. A local bias and variance analysis separates the two averaging levels, showing that inter-trajectory averaging removes residual error beyond the reach of averaging within a trajectory, while checkpoint selection carries a bias that bounds how many checkpoints are worth merging. Across model scales, learning-rate schedules, token budgets, and trajectory counts, Trajectory Soup improves aggregate downstream performance over the strongest single-trajectory average under matched budgets and keeps improving as budgets expand, with the advantage preserved after an identical post-training pipeline. These results position trajectory allocation and merging as a practical way to extend the compute-scaling frontier of mid-training beyond serial saturation.

</details>


### [234] [Interpolated Policy Distillation: A Controllable Continuum Between Off-Policy and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37170)
*Youxu Shi,Yifan Sun,Dacheng Yin,Haomiao Tang,Guangting Wang,Fengyun Rao,Jing Lyu,Dong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出插值策略蒸馏（IPD），将离策略与在策略蒸馏视为策略连续谱的两端，通过学生与教师分布的逐token线性插值构造中间策略，并用新的推测解码规则加速，在文本与多模态推理基准上稳定优于两端策略及其组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略蒸馏的教师轨迹质量高但偏离学生分布，在策略蒸馏的学生轨迹更易学但常含错误推理；两者各有优劣，作者认为更有效的rollout策略应位于两者之间。

Method: IPD在每个解码步将下一token分布定义为学生分布与教师分布的显式线性插值，插值系数直接控制轨迹质量与学生可学性的平衡；为降低逐token查询教师的开销，提出新的推测解码规则，在精确保持插值分布的前提下加速采样。

Result: 在纯文本与多模态推理基准上，IPD一致优于两端策略（SFT与OPD）、其常规两阶段组合（SFT-then-OPD）以及近期启发式分段交错方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹层面的学生/教师片段交错由精确实现的token级插值策略自然产生，而非手工切换规则，说明token级策略插值能更好地平衡轨迹质量与学生可学性。

Abstract: Off-policy and on-policy distillation have traditionally been formulated as separate paradigms, each favoring a different property of distillation trajectories. Teacher-generated (off-policy) traces are typically high-quality but lie far from the student's distribution, whereas student-generated (on-policy) rollouts are more learnable but often contain erroneous reasoning. We view these paradigms as the endpoints of a policy continuum and posit that a more effective rollout policy may lie in between. We introduce \textbf{Interpolated Policy Distillation (IPD)}, which defines the next-token distribution at every decoding step as an explicit linear interpolation between the student and teacher distributions. The interpolation operates at the distribution level, token by token, and its coefficient provides direct control over the balance between trajectory quality and student learnability. Naively sampling from this policy would require sequentially querying the teacher at every token and is thus expensive. To make IPD practical, we accelerate it with a new speculative-decoding rule while exactly preserving the interpolated next-token distribution.At the trajectory level, the resulting rollouts naturally interleave student- and teacher-generated segments. Unlike recent heuristic segment-interleaving methods, however, this interleaving is induced by an exactly realized token-level interpolated policy rather than by hand-designed switching rules. Across text-only and multimodal reasoning benchmarks, IPD consistently outperforms both endpoint policies (SFT and OPD), their conventional two-stage combination (SFT-then-OPD), and recent heuristic segment-interleaving methods, demonstrating that token-level policy interpolation better balances trajectory quality and student learnability.

</details>


### [235] [Corruption-Robust Sparse Linear Contextual Bandits with Knapsack Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37189)
*Yige Wang,Hanyang Li,Yiming Zong,Wanteng Ma,Jiashuo Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在奖励与消耗同时被污染情形下的带背包约束稀疏线性上下文老虎机问题，提出ROPD框架与Shared-Grid自适应方法，给出遗憾界并保证预算安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消耗污染比奖励污染更复杂：它不仅影响统计估计，还会影响记录预算、资源价格和停止决策，进而左右未来的资源分配。现有方法难以应对这种双重污染下的高维在线分配问题。

Method: 提出Robust Optimistic Primal–Dual (ROPD) 估计器模块化框架，将污染感知置信宽度与在线资源价格、预算安全规则结合，并给出具体稀疏实现。针对污染水平未知情形，提出Shared-Grid，通过围绕共享点估计自适应调整置信半径。

Result: ROPD在强制探索与总体设计覆盖下达到对干净总体LP基准的遗憾界 Õ(T^{2/3}+ΓT^{1/3})，在on-policy实现设计覆盖下达到 Õ(√T+Γ)。Shared-Grid在污染未知时引入显式初始化与主比较代价，其更优的on-policy保证还需推荐覆盖。两种方法在每次实现中保持观测预算，并将干净资源违反量界定为累积消耗污染。

Conclusion: 该工作将污染鲁棒稀疏估计与资源核算、定价和停止决策联系起来，为高维在线分配中的双重污染问题提供了具有遗憾保证和预算安全性的解决方案。

Abstract: We study sparse linear contextual bandits with knapsack constraints under joint reward and consumption corruption. Consumption corruption creates a challenge beyond corrupted rewards: it affects not only statistical estimates, but also the recorded budget, resource prices, and stopping decisions that govern future allocation. We develop Robust Optimistic Primal--Dual (ROPD), an estimator-modular framework that combines corruption-aware confidence widths with online resource prices and a budget-safety rule. With concrete sparse implementation, ROPD achieves regret against a clean population-LP benchmark of $\widetilde O(T^{2/3}+ΓT^{1/3})$ under forced exploration and population-design coverage, and $\widetilde O(\sqrt T+Γ)$ under on-policy realized-design coverage, for a supplied valid corruption bound $Γ$ under the stated proportional-budget scaling and fixed model/design parameters. When the corruption level is unknown, Shared-Grid adapts confidence radii around common point estimates fitted to a single realized history, incurring explicit initialization and master-comparison costs; its sharper on-policy guarantee additionally requires recommendation coverage. Both methods preserve observed budgets on every realization and bound clean resource violation by cumulative consumption corruption. These results connect corruption-robust sparse estimation with resource accounting, pricing, and stopping in high-dimensional online allocation.

</details>


### [236] [Pointwise or Pairwise: When Do Pairwise Losses Help Reward Learning, Provably?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37209)
*Junghyun Lee,Minsoo Ha,Sanghwa Kim,Yeongjong Kim,Eunjee Lee,Seiyun Shin,Kwang-Sung Jun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分组离线上下文赌博机中成对损失何时优于逐点损失，比较值回归(VR)与值差分回归(VDR)，并在半参数模型下给出有限样本回归与离线遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对损失在奖励学习中日益流行，即使能观测到逐点奖励，但其经验效果不一，缺乏对"何时、为何更优"的理论解释。

Method: 在允许多动作的分组离线上下文赌博机设定下，采用半参数模型（平均奖励=可学习的动作相关成分+任意上下文相关但与动作无关的干扰项）。通过统一的局部化分析，比较VR（逐点回归观测奖励）与VDR（回归同一上下文下动作对的奖励差分），针对有限函数类与线性函数类推导有限样本回归保证并转化为离线遗憾界。

Result: 对有限类，VDR消除了VR界中的误设项，并通过对每个上下文内动作平均改善了依赖奖励尺度的误差项，而VR无此优势。对线性类，两方法无一致占优：上下文内差分消除干扰引起的偏差，但当误设足够低时可能增大估计方差，形成依赖特征几何的偏差-方差权衡，数值实验予以验证。

Conclusion: 成对损失(VDR)的优劣取决于函数类与特征几何：有限类下可严格优于VR，线性类下仅存在偏差-方差权衡而无普遍优势，为选择逐点还是成对损失提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Pairwise losses are increasingly used for reward learning even when pointwise rewards are observed, with mixed empirical results. When and why do pairwise losses outperform pointwise losses? We study this question in a grouped offline contextual-bandit setting allowing multiple actions per context, capturing many reward learning scenarios. We compare Value Regression (VR), which regresses observed rewards pointwise, with Value Difference Regression (VDR), which regresses reward differences between a pair of actions sampled under the same context. We consider a semiparametric model where the mean reward is the sum of a learnable action-dependent component and an arbitrary context-dependent yet action-independent nuisance, capturing context-specific disturbances. Using a unified localized analysis, we prove finite-sample regression guarantees for finite and linear function classes and translate them into offline-regret bounds. For finite classes, VDR eliminates the misspecification term in the VR bound and improves a reward-scale-dependent error term by averaging over actions within each context, a benefit absent from the corresponding VR term. For linear classes, neither method uniformly dominates: within-context differencing removes nuisance-induced bias but may increase estimation variance relative to using absolute rewards when the misspecification is sufficiently low. This yields a feature geometry-dependent bias-variance tradeoff, which we corroborate with numerical experiments.

</details>


### [237] [Explainable Machine Learning for Multilayer Planar Winding Inductance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37211)
*Spyros Rigas,Theofilos Papadopoulos,Georgios Alexandridis,Antonios Antonopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可解释机器学习框架，结合SHAP/置换重要性与SR-KAN符号回归，自动发现多层矩形平面绕组自感系数的闭式解析公式，OOD相对误差8.22%，55个PCB样机实测平均绝对相对误差6.26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高频功率变换器需要快速准确估计多层矩形平面绕组的自感系数，但传统方法依赖复杂数学方程、刚性单项式公式或不可解释的黑盒ML模型，这些模型在训练域外性能严重下降。

Method: 提出可解释ML框架：融合事后特征归因（SHAP与置换重要性）与Kolmogorov-Arnold网络引导的符号回归（SR-KAN），在无先验结构假设下发现闭式解析方程；构建超10,000次有限元分析（FEA）仿真数据集，覆盖7类分布外（OOD）场景。

Result: 标准树集成模型外推误差超36%，而无约束SR-KAN表达式OOD相对误差仅8.22%；55个PCB样机（最多8层，电感4.11–559.27 μH）实验验证平均绝对相对误差6.26%。

Conclusion: KAN发现的解析表达式可有效迁移至真实硬件，兼具准确性与可解释性；FEA数据集与样机测量数据已开源以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Rapid and accurate self-inductance estimation for multilayer rectangle-shaped planar windings is essential for modern high-frequency power converters, yet traditional workflows rely on complex mathematical equations, rigid monomial formulas or unexplainable black-box machine learning (ML) models that degrade severely outside their training domain. This paper introduces an explainable ML framework unifying post-hoc feature attribution (SHAP and permutation importance) with Kolmogorov-Arnold Network-guided symbolic regression via the SR-KAN framework to discover closed-form analytical equations without prior structural assumptions. Evaluated on a new open-source dataset of over 10,000 Finite Element Analysis (FEA) simulations across seven out-of-distribution (OOD) classes, standard tree-based ensembles exhibit severe extrapolation errors (> 36%), whereas the unconstrained SR-KAN expression achieves a robust OOD relative error of 8.22%. Experimental verification across 55 physical printed circuit board prototypes (up to 8 layers, with inductances from 4.11 μH to 559.27 μH) confirms that the KAN-discovered expression translates effectively to real-world hardware, predicting inductance with a mean absolute relative error of 6.26%. To support reproducible research, the complete FEA simulation dataset and prototype measurements are released open-source.

</details>


### [238] [Interacting particle guidance for sampling reward-tilted generative priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37227)
*Adhithyan Kalaivanan,Zheng Zhao,Jens Sjölund,Fredrik Lindsten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出交互式粒子引导（IPG），用粒子间传输替代重要性重加权，避免权重退化与粒子坍缩，实现无偏的推理时引导采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时引导可将预训练扩散/流模型适配到新任务（条件生成或满足特定属性），其本质是从奖励倾斜的生成先验中采样。该分布精确采样不可解：引导类方法使用近似导致有偏样本；SMC 方法虽通过重要性权重纠偏，在大粒子极限下精确，但实际中存在权重退化与粒子坍缩问题。

Method: 提出交互式粒子引导（IPG）：用传输替代重加权，粒子通过额外漂移项相互作用，该漂移由 Feynman–Kac PDE 推导以抵消重加权项，使粒子保持无权重状态。在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中选择漂移，得到闭式解，计算开销小，与 SMC 相比开销可忽略。

Result: 在具有已知后验的高斯混合模型上验证，并在高维图像修复（inpainting）与蛋白质结构推断任务上展示效果。

Conclusion: IPG 通过粒子交互传输替代重加权，克服了 SMC 的权重退化与粒子坍缩，同时保持低成本计算，是一种有效且实用的推理时引导采样方法。

Abstract: Inference-time steering adapts pretrained diffusion and flow-based models to new tasks, e.g., to generate samples from a conditional distribution or samples with desired properties, without retraining. This can be formalized as sampling from a reward-tilted generative prior. As exact sampling from this distribution is intractable, guidance-based methods rely on approximations producing biased samples, and sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) methods correct for this bias using importance weights. However, while exact in the large particle limit, SMC suffers from weight degeneracy and particle collapse in practice. We propose interacting particle guidance (IPG), which replaces reweighting with transport. The particles interact through an additional drift, derived from the Feynman--Kac PDE to cancel the reweighting term, and remain unweighted. Choosing the drift in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space yields a closed-form solution that is cheap to compute, with negligible overhead compared to SMC. We demonstrate the method on Gaussian mixtures with known posteriors, and on high-dimensional image inpainting and protein structure inference tasks.

</details>


### [239] [Differentiating Bisimulation Metrics: A Framework for Parametric Markov Chain Fitting via Bicausal Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37239)
*Sergio Calo,Amy Zhang,Javier Segovia-Aguas,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将双因果最优传输（BOT）与双模拟度量统一为目标距离，并将其表述为线性规划，从而对模型动力学可微。作者通过包络定理在LP鞍点处推导闭式梯度，提出D-BOT算法，以距离计算与梯度步交替的方式学习模型，并在状态空间压缩、参数化模型学习和从观测模仿学习三个场景验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序决策中的多类问题（从观测模仿学习、状态空间压缩、世界模型学习、仿真到现实迁移）都可归结为学习一个使目标过程距离最小化的模型。作者希望找到一个通用且可微的距离度量框架，用统一的算法解决这些不同问题，而BOT/双模拟度量是一个合适候选，但其可微性与梯度的精确推导尚不充分。

Method: 作者将BOT（等价于双模拟度量）表述为线性规划，利用LP结构说明其相对模型动力学可微；随后对LP鞍点应用包络定理，推导出精确的闭式梯度。基于此提出通用算法D-BOT，通过交替执行距离计算与梯度更新来学习最优模型，并将其实例化到状态空间压缩、参数化模型学习和从观测模仿学习三个设定。

Result: 在状态空间压缩、参数化模型学习以及从观测模仿学习（ILfO）三个不同设置中应用D-BOT，实证结果确认了三种实例化均具有可行性。

Conclusion: D-BOT提供了一个统一、可微且带闭式梯度的框架，将BOT/双模拟度量用于顺序决策中的多种模型学习问题，实验验证了其通用性与可行性。

Abstract: Many problems in sequential decision-making, such as imitation learning from observations, state-space compression, world-model learning, and sim-to-real transfer, can be reduced to learning a model such that a notion of distance with respect to the target process is minimized. We consider this general framework and consider the bisimulation metric, equivalently Bicausal Optimal Transport (BOT), as the notion of distance to minimize. We show that BOT, since it can be formulated as a linear program (LP), is differentiable with respect to the model dynamics. We then derive an exact closed-form gradient via the envelope theorem applied to the LP saddle point. The result is a general algorithm, Differentiable Bicausal Optimal Transport (D-BOT), that can be applied to each of the problems above. The proposed algorithm learns the best model by alternating between distance computation and gradient steps. We apply D-BOT for three different settings: state-space compression, parametric model learning, and imitation learning from observations (ILfO). We show empirical results that confirm the viability of all three instantiations.

</details>


### [240] [Trident: Unifying Guarded Dispatch and Host Execution for PyTorch Triton Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37241)
*Jinjie Liu,Xiaoyan Liu,Shuhan Zhang,Wenjia Sun,Ruilin Yang,Chunlei Men,Yonghua Lin,Shaohua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Trident是一个编译器后端，通过将特化选择、参数准备和宿主执行编译为单一模块，消除特化缓存命中路径上的重复开销，实现最高1.68倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户编写的Triton内核在PyTorch中能实现高性能GPU计算，但当设备执行时间较短时，端到端延迟往往由宿主侧编排主导。torch.compile虽能为捕获图生成原生宿主包装器，但每次调用仍需经过运行时管理的特化查找、守卫求值和准备工作，带来重复开销。
