Method: 提出Trident编译器后端，引入特化缓存模块（SCM），将受守卫的特化选择、参数与执行环境准备以及多个特化的宿主执行编译为单一可执行模块。调用只需进入SCM一次，特化匹配时保持在编译代码中，仅当需要编译新特化时才返回Python。基于Torch-MLIR实现，将守卫和宿主侧编排下沉为原生代码，同时保留对受支持ATen算子优化运行时实现的调用。

Result: 在两个LLM上的评估表明，Trident在模型级端到端延迟上相比eager执行最高取得1.47倍加速，相比torch.compile最高取得1.68倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过消除特化缓存命中路径上的重复开销，Trident有效降低了Triton内核在PyTorch中的端到端延迟，在LLM场景下显著优于eager执行和torch.compile。

Abstract: User-written Triton kernels enable high-performance GPU computation within PyTorch, but their end-to-end latency can remain dominated by host-side orchestration, especially when device execution is short. Although torch.compile can generate native host wrappers for captured graphs, each invocation still passes through runtime-managed specialization lookup, guard evaluation, and preparation before reaching the wrapper. We present Trident, a compiler backend that removes this recurring overhead from the specialization cache-hit path. Trident introduces the Specialization Cache Module (SCM), which compiles guarded specialization selection, argument and execution-environment preparation, and host execution for multiple specializations into a single executable module. An invocation enters the SCM once, remains in compiled code when a specialization matches, and returns to Python only when a new specialization must be compiled. Built on Torch-MLIR, Trident lowers guards and host-side orchestration to native code while retaining calls to optimized runtime implementations of supported ATen operators. Our evalu- ation on two LLMs shows that Trident achieves up to a 1.47x speedup in model-level end-to-end latency over eager execution and up to 1.68x over torch.compile.

</details>


### [241] [Loss-Guided Pretraining Data Selection for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37255)
*Yike Li,Shaoxu Song,Jianmin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种静态数据选择框架：用参考预测器为每个时间窗口打分，保留每个源数据集中的中间区间窗口，以更少数据超越随机选择和全量预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在数十亿观测的异构数据上预训练，但训练窗口通常随机采样，未评估其是否提供有用学习信号，存在数据低效问题。

Method: 将预测损失与优化难度关联，证明在局部雅可比条件下归一化平方损失控制逐样本梯度范数；据此定义参考损失分数，并采用按数据集分层选择以保持样本多样性，筛选中间区间窗口。

Result: 在多种TSFM架构上，该方法绝对幅度上优于随机选择；仅用更少的候选预训练窗口，相对MASE与CRPS甚至优于全量数据预训练；分数具有强跨尺度与跨架构相关性。

Conclusion: 小的参考模型常能为更大目标模型选数据，前提是参考与目标的难度排序兼容；数据选择可提升TSFM预训练效率与效果。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are pretrained on heterogeneous collections containing billions of observations, yet their training windows are typically sampled without estimating whether they provide useful learning signal. We introduce a static data-selection framework that scores each window with a reference forecaster and retains an intermediate interval within every source dataset. Specifically, we connect forecasting loss to optimization difficulty by showing that normalized squared loss controls the per-sample gradient norm under a local Jacobian condition. We then define a reference loss score and apply dataset-stratified selection to preserve the diversity of samples. Across various TSFM architectures, our method outperforms random selection by an absolute margin and even improves both relative MASE and CRPS over full-data pretraining by retaining fewer candidate pretraining windows. Further analyses show strong cross-scale and cross-architecture score correlations, indicating that a small reference model can often select data for larger targets, provided that the reference and target share compatible difficulty orderings.

</details>


### [242] [Efficiently Approximating Attention Is Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37261)
*Lukas Haverbeck,Carmen Amo Alonso,Andres Felipe Posada-Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了softmax注意力在任何非平凡的统一近似保证下都无法用真正次二次时间算法实现，即使允许KV缓存预处理也不例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力的二次复杂度使其代价高昂，常用快速近似算法来降低开销，但其误差依赖于输入结构。作者希望寻找不依赖特定输入结构的统一近似保证，以应对注意力应用日益多样化的需求。

Method: 基于标准复杂性理论假设，通过归约论证分析统一近似注意力在次二次时间下的不可能性，并考察了KV缓存多项式预处理及稀疏情形下找出重要键等松弛。

Result: 证明不存在真正次二次算法能对所有输入统一提供任何非平凡加性或相对近似保证；即便对KV缓存做多项式预处理，也无法获得非平凡统一近似，或识别出受显著关注的小键集合。

Conclusion: 该结果界定了统一注意力近似的计算极限，从理论上否定了快速统一近似注意力的可能性。

Abstract: Softmax attention is ubiquitous in modern machine learning, but its quadratic scaling with sequence length makes it costly. To reduce this cost, attention is often approximated with fast algorithms, which incur error but can still perform well in practice and on some inputs. At the same time, the growing diversity of attention applications makes approximation guarantees that do not depend on particular input structure a compelling target. For such uniform guarantees over all inputs, known runtime lower bounds rule out fast algorithms for near-exact attention, but leave open the practically important regime: is there an efficient algorithm with even a modest uniform approximation guarantee? We answer this question negatively. Under standard complexity-theoretic assumptions, no truly subquadratic algorithm can approximate attention with any nontrivial additive or relative guarantee uniformly over all inputs. This impossibility holds in the mildest parameter regime for which known algorithms do not already achieve strong approximation guarantees in near-linear time, and extends to practically relevant relaxations: even after polynomial preprocessing of the KV cache, no efficient algorithm can obtain a nontrivial uniform approximation guarantee, or identify a small set of keys receiving substantial attention under sparsity. Overall, our results settle the computational limits of uniform attention approximation.

</details>


### [243] [Scaling Full Conformal Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37298)
*Julio Silva-Rodríguez,Ender Konukoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T-FCP利用零样本视觉语言模型引导可扩展的全共形预测，通过剪枝候选标签并配合高效求解器，实现高效图像分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测提供带分布无关覆盖保证的集合预测，适用于高风险图像分类。但split conformal数据效率低，而full conformal虽有更强统计效率却因需在测试时对每个候选标签重新拟合模型而计算不可行，尤其在大标签空间下。作者希望解决FCP的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出Targeted Full Conformal Prediction (T-FCP)：用轻量级归纳共形预测器剪除不太可能的标签，仅对剩余候选应用FCP，在降低计算量的同时保留组合共形过程的形式化保证。同时提出Stabilized Online LDA (SO-LDA)，一种基于秩一逆协方差更新的高效VLM适配求解器。

Result: 在包括ImageNet的多个基准上，T-FCP以适度的测试时开销实现了实用的全共形图像分类，产生高效的预测集，并比split conformal替代方案具有更稳定的经验覆盖。

Conclusion: T-FCP结合VLM引导的候选剪枝与高效适配求解器，使全共形预测在大标签空间图像分类中变得可行且高效，兼顾统计效率与计算可扩展性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides set-valued predictions with distribution-free coverage guarantees, making it attractive for high-stakes image classification. However, split conformal prediction is data-inefficient, while full conformal prediction (FCP), despite its stronger statistical efficiency, is computationally prohibitive at scale because it requires candidate-specific model refits at test time. We address this limitation by leveraging zero-shot vision-language models (VLMs) to guide scalable FCP in large label spaces. We introduce Targeted Full Conformal Prediction (T-FCP), which uses a lightweight inductive conformal predictor to prune unlikely labels and applies FCP only to the remaining candidates, reducing computation while retaining the formal guarantee of the combined conformal procedure. We further propose Stabilized Online LDA (SO-LDA), an efficient VLM adaptation solver based on rank-one inverse-covariance updates. Across multiple benchmarks, including ImageNet, T-FCP enables practical full-conformal image classification with modest test-time overhead, yielding efficient prediction sets and more stable empirical coverage than split conformal alternatives.

</details>


### [244] [Hidden Reasoning Must Leak, but Need Not Be Readable: Fundamental Opportunities and Limits for Chain-of-Thought Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37312)
*Mohammadali Mohammadkhani,Madhava Krishna,Yash Sarrof,Michael Hahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究推理模型能否在不暴露思维链的情况下进行隐藏计算，发现这取决于任务难度与模型规模：简单计算可以隐藏，但复杂任务的信息泄漏存在理论下界；且泄漏信息可被加密使多项式时间监控者无法提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对CoT监控能否有效检测模型隐藏推理这一问题，需要从理论与实证上厘清隐藏计算的可行边界以及对监控的挑战。

Method: 结合信息论分析与实证实验，刻画隐藏计算与思维链信息泄漏之间的关系；并基于密码学假设构造单层Transformer在线加密其推理过程的方案。

Result: 简单计算可被隐蔽完成；超过依赖模型规模的难度阈值后，成功求解任务会向CoT泄漏近似线性于隐藏输入的信息量，因此足够复杂的隐藏计算必留信息论痕迹；但在合理密码学假设下，该泄漏可不可读，即使单层Transformer也能加密推理使多项式时间监控者无法提取信息。

Conclusion: 结果为CoT监控的机会与局限提供了整体性视角：监控在复杂隐藏计算上存在信息论约束，但实际可读性又受密码学手段制约。

Abstract: Can reasoning models trick chain of thought (CoT) monitors and perform hidden computation without revealing it in their thinking traces? We show that the answer depends on the underlying task difficulty and the model size. Simple computations can be performed covertly; however, beyond a threshold depending on model size, successfully solving the task necessarily leaks a near-linear amount of information about the covert task input into the CoT. Therefore, sufficiently complex hidden computation always leaves an information-theoretic footprint. However, concerningly, this leakage need not be readable: Under plausible cryptographic assumptions, even a one-layer Transformer can encrypt its reasoning online so that no polynomial-time monitor can extract information about the hidden computation. Overall, our theoretical and empirical results provide a holistic view of both the opportunities and the limitations of CoT monitoring.

</details>


### [245] [Parallel Tempering for Diffusion-Based Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37323)
*Arman Mielke,Uwe Bauknecht,Thilo Strauss,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PT-Denoise 通过在推理阶段让并行的离散扩散去噪轨迹进行平行回火交换温度，从而在不重训练的情况下提升组合优化解的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型已成为求解图组合优化问题的强大范式。常见的推理做法是独立生成多个候选解并取最优，但这会带来明显的计算开销且各轨迹之间无信息交互。作者希望在不增加过多成本的前提下，让并行候选轨迹互相协作以进一步提升解质量。

Method: 提出 PT-Denoise 推理时过程：为每个扩散过程分配一个温度，并基于各过程相对表现允许它们交换温度，实现平行回火式的交互。低能量（有前景）的轨迹被动态重新分配到更冷、采样更集中的模式，而高能量状态则通过随机化探索逃离局部极小。整个过程无需对底层去噪器进行重训练或微调。

Result: 在经典图结构组合优化问题上的实验表明，该方法能持续提升所找到的最优解质量，且仅带来极小的额外计算开销。

Conclusion: PT-Denoise 是一种即插即用的推理时增强手段，通过平行回火让并行去噪轨迹协同，在不重训练、低开销的条件下改善离散扩散模型求解组合优化的表现。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving combinatorial optimization (CO) problems on graphs by learning to sample high-quality solutions. A common inference-time approach is to generate multiple candidate solutions independently and return the best-performing sample, improving solution quality at the expense of an increase in computational cost. In this work, we introduce PT-Denoise, an inference-time procedure that allows these concurrent denoising trajectories to interact through parallel tempering, without requiring retraining or fine-tuning of the underlying denoiser. Our method assigns a temperature to each diffusion process and allows processes to swap temperatures based on their relative performance. This dynamically reallocates promising, low-energy trajectories to colder, more concentrated sampling regimes while allowing higher-energy states to escape local minima through randomized exploration. Experiments on canonical graph-structured CO problems show that our approach consistently improves the quality of the best solution found, while only adding minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [246] [A Sharp Transition in Data Reconstruction under Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37344)
*Max Cairney-Leeming,Simone Bombari,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从 ρ-零集中差分隐私模型中重构单个 d 维训练样本的可重构性，证明了在 ρ≍d 处存在数据重构的尖锐相变，并发现该相变在数据位于 s 维子空间时移至 ρ≍s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据重构攻击已能从学习模型中恢复训练样本，引发隐私担忧。差分隐私虽提供形式化保护，但隐私预算的选择仍是难题：预算过小严重损害效用，而过大的预算又难以量化其是否会允许精确重构。因此需要明确在何种隐私预算下重构在信息论上不可能、在何阈值以上又实际可行。

Method: 作者考虑知情攻击者：在已知其他所有训练数据的前提下，从 ρ-零集中差分隐私模型中重构单个 d 维训练样本。一方面，对任意隐私机制和任意攻击推导基于熵的下界，刻画了一类目标先验，使得当 ρ≪d 时重构在信息论上不可能；另一方面，分析针对输出扰动式私有线性回归的一种简单攻击，表明当 ρ≫d 时重构实际可行。并将分析推广到数据位于 s 维子空间的情形。

Result: 研究确立了在 ρ≍d 处的数据重构尖锐相变：ρ≪d 时信息论上不可重构，ρ≫d 时实际可重构。对于位于 s 维子空间的数据，该相变移至 ρ≍s。实验在合成数据以及自然图像（CIFAR-10、ImageNet）上验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 保证充分保护的隐私预算必须依据数据的有效维度来评估，而非仅按环境维度 d 衡量；这意味着隐私预算的选择可以与数据的低维结构相联系，从而在保证抗重构的同时改善效用。

Abstract: Data reconstruction attacks have empirically been successful in recovering training samples from learned models, raising privacy concerns and motivating defenses with guarantees that remain valid against future threats. While differential privacy (DP) provides formal protection, choosing the privacy budget remains a challenge: small budgets severely reduce utility, but it is hard to quantify how large the budget can be without allowing accurate reconstruction. In this work, we study informed attackers who aim to reconstruct a single $d$-dimensional training sample from a $ρ$-zero-concentrated DP model, knowing all other training data. Our main contribution is to establish a sharp transition at $ρ\asymp d$ for data reconstruction: on the one hand, we derive entropy-based lower bounds for any private mechanism and any attack, characterizing a set of target priors for which reconstruction is information-theoretically impossible for $ρ\ll d$; on the other hand, we analyze a simple attack on private linear regression with output perturbation, showing that reconstruction is practically feasible for $ρ\gg d$. Remarkably, the transition moves to $ρ\asymp s$ for data lying in an $s$-dimensional subspace, demonstrating that the privacy budget guaranteeing adequate protection must be assessed in terms of the effective dimension of the data. We validate our findings via experiments on synthetic data and natural images (CIFAR-10, ImageNet).

</details>


### [247] [Port-Hamiltonian Latent Deliberation: Mitigating the Deliberation Drift Cliff in Test-Time Compute Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37351)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现连续隐空间推理存在"Deliberation Drift Cliff"现象，并提出Port-Hamiltonian Latent Deliberation (PH-LD)框架与DG-HHD方法，在保持高表达力的同时实现长程稳定推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时计算扩展是机器推理的关键，但在连续隐空间中进行迭代推理时会出现"Deliberation Drift Cliff"：无约束循环隐模型在短视界(K<=4)有收益，但深思考步(K>=16)推理崩溃，在逻辑基准上下降22%至62%。作者希望解决表达力、Lyapunov稳定性与计算效率之间的三难困境。

Method: 通过22轮实证与理论研究，建立Port-Hamiltonian Latent Deliberation (PH-LD)框架，并提出Direct-Gradient Pure-Tensor Helmholtz-Hodge Decomposition (DG-HHD)。DG-HHD将吸引流参数化为切空间投影张量网络，同时正交解耦非零环流，消除运行时autograd依赖。

Result: 保守标量势梯度流抑制长程漂移(cliff 3.40%)但峰值精度受限(32.73%)；无约束旋转流表达力高(82.33%)但漂移严重(36.87%)。DG-HHD在15臂Pareto基准中达58.67%峰值精度(+25.94%绝对增益)，K=32时保持35.27%；向量场加速1.84x，RK45 rollout加速2.09x；Hodge机器误差1.65e-17，收缩误差5.55e-17。迁移到SLM多跳因果推理实现单调计算扩展(49.33%到51.56%)，抑制OOD漂移(cliff -0.66%)，30项Level 0确定性不变量全部通过认证。

Conclusion: DG-HHD通过几何分解解决了表达力与稳定性的双重困境，在大幅提升长程推理稳定性与精度的同时显著降低计算开销，并可迁移至语言模型的多跳因果推理任务。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling has emerged as a cornerstone of advanced machine reasoning, yet performing iterative deliberation directly within continuous latent representation spaces reveals a catastrophic pathology: the Deliberation Drift Cliff. While unconstrained recurrent latent models achieve initial reasoning gains at short horizons (K <= 4), their reasoning collapses when extrapolated to deeper thinking steps (K >= 16), dropping by 22% to 62% across standard logical benchmarks. We resolve the trilemma among expressivity, Lyapunov stability, and computational efficiency in test-time latent reasoning through a 22-round empirical and theoretical investigation. We demonstrate that strictly conservative scalar potential gradient flows suppress long-range drift (cliff 3.40%) but bottleneck peak reasoning accuracy at 32.73%, whereas unconstrained rotational flows achieve high symbolic expressivity (82.33%) but suffer a severe 36.87% drift cliff. To resolve this geometric duality, we establish Port-Hamiltonian Latent Deliberation (PH-LD) and propose the Direct-Gradient Pure-Tensor Helmholtz-Hodge Decomposition (DG-HHD). DG-HHD parameterizes the attracting flow as a tangent projection tensor network while orthogonally decoupling non-zero circulation (Hodge machine error 1.65e-17, contraction error 5.55e-17), eliminating runtime autograd dependencies to achieve 1.84x vector field and 2.09x RK45 rollout speedups. In a 15-arm symmetrical Pareto benchmark, DG-HHD achieves 58.67% peak accuracy (+25.94% absolute gain over conservative HHD) and retains 35.27% at K=32. Transferred to small language model (SLM) multi-hop causal reasoning, DG-HHD delivers monotonic compute scaling (49.33% to 51.56%) and suppresses out-of-distribution drift (cliff -0.66%). All 30 Level 0 deterministic invariants are certified.

</details>


### [248] [Looped Transformers as Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37379)
*Yulong Huang,Chen Jiang,Zhanpeng Zhou,Hongtao Zhang,Tianyu Li,Tianyu He,Xiangyu Zhang,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将循环Transformer的循环隐藏状态视为随深度更新的"快权重"，把循环转移公式化为基于局部梯度的更新，并提出OperLoop改进循环设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过重复应用共享块实现参数高效的深度扩展，近期推理模型也强调测试时计算扩展的价值。但如何设计有效的循环转移仍缺乏理论理解。

Method: 将循环隐藏状态视为快权重，把循环转移表达为局部梯度更新，循环块在每层预测隐式目标；从投影、局部目标与优化器更新规则推导出闭式循环转移。据此分析现有代表性循环转移，指出其转移与投影的不匹配；先对齐现有转移的输入映射，再推导出OperLoop，融合显式权重衰减、自适应步长与delta目标。

Result: 对齐后的变体降低了训练损失并提升平均常识准确率；在训练FLOPs匹配条件下，OperLoop相比所对比的循环与非循环基线提升了平均生成性能。

Conclusion: 结果支持该框架对循环设计的有效性；作者将分析扩展至更多循环模型，并给出未来循环转移设计的研究路线图。

Abstract: Looped Transformers provide a parameter-efficient approach to depth scaling by repeatedly applying shared Transformer blocks. Recent reasoning models have likewise highlighted the value of scaling test-time computation through longer computation trajectories. However, the principles for designing effective loop transitions remain poorly understood. We view the looped hidden state as a fast weight that is updated throughout the depth. We formulate loop transitions as local gradient-based updates, with recurrent blocks predicting implicit targets at each depth. Our framework derives loop transitions in closed form from a projection, a local objective and an optimizer update rule. Mapping representative loop transitions into this framework reveals mismatches between their transitions and projections. We first align the input maps of existing transitions. We then derive OperLoop, which combines explicit weight decay, adaptive step size and a delta objective. The aligned variants reduce training loss and improve average commonsense accuracy. OperLoop improves average generative performance over the compared looped and non-looped baselines under matched training FLOPs. These results support the framework's usefulness for loop design. We extend the analysis to additional loop models and outline a roadmap for future loop transition design.

</details>


### [249] [High-Dimensional Simulation-Based Inference in Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37381)
*Lars Kühmichel,Stefan T. Radev,Bhanu Prasanna Koppolu,Masoumeh Davoudi,Jerry M. Huang,Paul-Christian Bürkner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将神经模拟推断（SBI）与潜变量生成建模相结合，通过学习模拟器参数的低维表示，直接在潜空间中进行后验推断，再映射回原始参数空间，从而在保证推断精度的同时大幅提升采样速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SBI的表示学习几乎只关注压缩用于条件化后验的观测数据，但当推断目标本身变得越来越高维时，是否也应压缩推断目标成为一个新问题。

Method: 将SBI与潜变量生成建模结合：学习模拟器参数的低维表示，在该潜空间中直接进行后验推断，并将后验样本映射回原始参数空间；同时刻画潜空间推断能恢复目标后验的条件，并系统研究其经验性权衡。

Result: 通过四个案例研究和三类生成模型族，在控制网络容量、正则化、优化和训练计算量的条件下比较潜空间与标准估计器。在匹配训练计算量时，潜空间推断达到与直接目标空间推断相当的精度和边际校准，采样速度提升最高超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: 潜空间推断是SBI与潜变量生成建模的实用融合，能在不牺牲精度和校准性的前提下显著加快采样，为高维参数空间推断提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Neural simulation-based inference (SBI) has been widely successful in inferring a relatively small number of interpretable parameters from potentially high-dimensional observations, such as images or time series. Accordingly, representation learning in SBI has focused almost exclusively on compressing the observations used to condition the posterior. More recently, however, SBI has begun to target increasingly high-dimensional parameter spaces, raising the complementary question of whether the inference target itself should be compressed. Our answer is a practical merger of SBI and latent generative modeling, which learns a low-dimensional representation of the simulator parameters, performs posterior inference directly in this latent space, and maps posterior samples back to the original parameter space. We characterize the conditions under which latent-space inference recovers the desired target posterior and systematically study its empirical trade-offs. Across four case studies and three generative families, we compare latent and standard estimators while controlling for network capacity, regularization, optimization, and training compute. At matched training compute, latent-space inference achieves accuracy and marginal calibration comparable to direct target-space inference while sampling up to more than an order of magnitude faster.

</details>


### [250] [MoTIF-X: A Multimodal Tokenized Framework for Interpretable and Extensible Molecular Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37384)
*Linqing Mo,Jiayu Zhou,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoTIF-X是一个以化学基序（motif）为中心的多模态分子表示学习框架，通过两阶段预训练实现分子性质预测、药物-靶点相互作用预测及亚结构级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子图、SMILES和3D构象多模态方法通常独立编码各视图、仅在后期对齐，限制了细粒度跨模态交互与亚结构级可解释性。

Method: 以图接地的化学基序作为共享锚点进行多模态整合与解释。第一阶段通过原子、基序、分子三层级对比学习获得基序表示；第二阶段用SMILES和扭转角token结合多模态掩码token建模进行上下文建模。在含多构象的类药分子上预训练。

Result: 在全部九个OpenADMET ExpansionRx终点上取得最低MAE和最佳整体性能，显著性分析支持多数比较中的优势；消融验证了基序token上下文建模、多模态整合与两阶段预训练的正向贡献；在药物-靶点相互作用预测中取得最佳平均分类性能，并可无额外微调泛化到外部药物冷启动数据集；基序归因分数越高对应实验活性变化越大。

Conclusion: MoTIF-X是一个可迁移且可解释的分子建模框架。

Abstract: Molecular representation learning is central to computer-aided drug discovery. Molecular graphs, SMILES strings, and 3D conformations provide complementary structural information, yet many multimodal approaches encode these views independently and align them only at a later stage, limiting fine-grained cross-modal interaction and substructure-level interpretability. To address these limitations, we introduce MoTIF-X, a motif-centered framework that uses graph-grounded chemical motifs as shared anchors for multimodal integration and interpretation. Its first pretraining stage learns motif representations through hierarchical contrastive learning across atomic, motif, and molecular scales. The second stage contextualizes these representations with SMILES and torsion-angle tokens through multimodal masked token modeling.
  After pretraining on drug-like molecules with multiple conformers, MoTIF-X achieved the lowest mean absolute error on all nine OpenADMET ExpansionRx endpoints and the best overall performance among the evaluated methods. Significance analyses supported its advantage in the vast majority of endpoint-baseline comparisons after multiple-testing correction. Ablation studies supported the complementary contributions of motif-token contextualization, multimodal integration, and two-stage pretraining. Beyond molecular properties, the framework extended to drug-target interaction prediction, achieving the best average classification performance across the evaluated benchmarks and generalizing to an external drug-cold-start dataset without additional fine-tuning. Its motif-centered design also enabled substructure-level interpretation: higher motif attribution scores were associated with larger experimentally measured activity shifts. Together, these findings support MoTIF-X as a transferable and interpretable framework for molecular modeling.

</details>


### [251] [Rethinking Soft Tokens for Parallel Decoding in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37391)
*Kodai Kawamura,Kenji Kawaguchi,Anji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）中软词元（soft token）反馈如何改善并行解码，发现在冻结预训练模型下常规软词元构造与预训练嵌入空间存在几何不匹配，并提出免训练、几何感知的软词元构造方法，在四个模型与四个基准上优于标准并行解码和欧氏软词元基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型并行生成多个词元时可能产生各自看似合理但彼此不一致的词元。已有工作表明软词元可缓解该问题，但软词元反馈究竟如何改善并行解码尚缺乏系统研究，且其作用是否仅在于保留预测不确定性仍不明确。

Method: 在冻结的预训练 DLM 上研究软词元反馈，以排除额外训练的影响。发现常规软词元构造与预训练嵌入空间之间存在几何不匹配，据此提出一种免训练、几何感知的软词元构造方法，用于在无训练条件下构建软词元输入。

Result: 分析表明，仅用不确定性保留无法充分解释软词元反馈如何重塑后续预测；实证证据显示软词元反馈倾向于促进连贯的词元序列。在四个预训练 DLM 与四个数学和代码基准上，该方法优于标准并行解码和免训练的欧氏软词元基线。

Conclusion: 软词元反馈改善并行解码的关键不仅在于保留不确定性，更在于其引导模型生成连贯的词元序列；所提出的几何感知、免训练软词元构造方法在多个模型和基准上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) enable parallel generation by predicting and committing multiple tokens at each denoising step, yet they can generate individually plausible but mutually inconsistent tokens. Recent work shows that \emph{soft tokens} can mitigate this issue by representing uncertain positions with continuous embeddings built from the model's predictive distribution at the previous decoding step. However, although soft tokens are commonly understood as preserving predictive uncertainty, how soft-token feedback improves parallel decoding has not been systematically examined. In this paper, we investigate this question in frozen pretrained DLMs to examine soft-token feedback without the effects of additional training. To construct soft-token inputs in a training-free setting, we identify a geometric mismatch between conventional soft-token construction and the pretrained embedding space. Based on this observation, we propose a training-free, geometry-aware construction of soft tokens. Our analysis of soft-token feedback suggests that uncertainty preservation alone does not fully explain how it reshapes subsequent predictions. To better explain how soft-token feedback improves parallel decoding, we provide empirical evidence that it favors coherent token sequences. Across four pretrained DLMs and four math and code benchmarks, our method outperforms standard parallel decoding and a training-free Euclidean soft-token baseline. Code: https://github.com/kodaikawamura/rethinking-soft-tokens

</details>


### [252] [Learning Macroscopic Dynamics without Reconstructing Microscopic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37392)
*Zhichao Han,Yue Zhao,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重构的框架，联合学习潜状态及其动力学以用于宏观性质预测，并通过交替训练避免了潜尺度崩溃问题，在多个复杂系统任务上取得更优的宏观预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统宏观性质的时间演化预测通常依赖将微观状态编码为紧凑潜状态并学习其演化。但基于微观状态重构学习潜状态时，在潜容量有限的情况下，重构会偏向高方差的微观细节，而忽略宏观预测所需的信息；而完全不做重构的联合学习又往往无法得到支持准确宏观预测的潜动力学。作者指出这一失败源于"潜尺度崩溃"：缩小潜状态尺度会降低训练损失，但宏观演化误差仍然很大。

Method: 提出一种无需重构的框架，直接为指定的宏观预测任务学习潜状态及其动力学。训练采用交替方式：固定转移模型和下一时刻潜目标，更新潜表示；再固定潜表示，更新转移模型。推理时，训练好的模型从初始微观状态出发递推地预测宏观状态。理论上分析了联合训练下的重构失配与尺度崩溃，并给出了该方法局部收敛到正确潜动力学的充分条件。

Result: 在三个任务上——晶格上的流行病传播、两种粒子物种的混合以及聚合物拉伸——实验表明所提方法在宏观预测上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了重构训练与联合训练各自的缺陷（重构失配与潜尺度崩溃），并提出交替优化的无重构框架，从理论和实验两方面验证了其能学到支持准确宏观预测的潜动力学。

Abstract: Modeling the temporal evolution of macroscopic properties of complex systems is an important scientific task. To predict this evolution without full microscopic simulation, a common approach encodes microstates into compact latent states, learns their evolution, and reads out macroscopic predictions from the latent trajectory. These latent states are often learned through microstate reconstruction. However, with limited latent capacity, reconstruction can favor high-variance microscopic details over information needed for macroscopic prediction. Yet jointly learning latent states and their transition without reconstruction often fails to obtain latent dynamics that support accurate macroscopic prediction. We show that this failure can arise from latent scale collapse: shrinking the latent state scale reduces training loss while macroscopic evolution error remains large. Here, we propose a reconstruction-free framework to learn latent states with their dynamics for prescribed macroscopic prediction. Training alternates between updating the latent representation with the transition and next-state latent targets fixed, and updating the transition with the latent representation fixed. At inference, the trained model predicts macroscopic states recursively from an initial microstate. Our theoretical analysis characterizes reconstruction misalignment and scale collapse under joint training, and gives a sufficient condition for local convergence to correct latent dynamics for our method. Experiments on epidemic spreading on a lattice, mixing of two particle species, and polymer stretching demonstrate that the proposed method achieves substantially better macroscopic prediction over baselines.

</details>


### [253] [Scale Sensitivity in Low-Bit Post-Training Quantization: Curvature of the Quantization Error Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37416)
*Jonas von Berg,Massimiliano Datres,Carlo Kneißl,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论到实验研究GPTQ类后训练量化(PTQ)中量化尺度(scale)对逐层重建损失的敏感性，证明在权重i.i.d.高斯、校准激活有效秩充分大的条件下，归一化round-to-nearest损失随宽度增大依概率收敛到标准高斯均匀量化器的MSE，并用五个LLM实验验证尺度敏感性随比特宽度增加而衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ类方法依赖逐层重建误差，需要在均匀网格上选择scale，而常用的max-based选择在低比特下性能急剧下降。作者希望理解该目标对scale的敏感性，从而解释为何尺度规则在低比特下至关重要、在高比特下几乎无关。

Method: 理论：对i.i.d.高斯权重和有效秩足够大的校准激活，证明随宽度增长，归一化round-to-nearest损失在所有尺度上一致地依高概率收敛到标准高斯均匀量化MSE；并对宽随机初始化MLP（奇Lipschitz激活、各向同性高斯校准数据）验证有效秩条件。实验：在五个LLM上做GPTQ实验，比较不同尺度规则对困惑度的影响，并使用局部尺度敏感性度量与高斯曲率对比；同时测试Hadamard非相干处理后的表现。

Result: 极限目标具有唯一非退化极小值；其最优尺度随量化级数严格单调下降，且相对尺度误差的曲率随比特宽度近似指数衰减。实验显示尺度规则在2–3比特显著改变困惑度，6比特起影响可忽略；GPTQ尺度敏感性的局部度量随比特宽度下降，与高斯曲率一致。高斯最优尺度在原始权重上失效，经Hadamard非相干处理后，在3比特及以上无需搜索即可匹配最优搜索规则，但2比特仍明显更差。

Conclusion: GPTQ类PTQ的尺度敏感性本质上由比特宽度主导：低比特下尺度选择关键，高比特下几乎无影响。理论给出的高斯最优尺度结合Hadamard非相干处理可免搜索地在中高比特达到最优性能，但2比特仍需进一步处理。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) methods in the GPTQ family minimize a layer-wise reconstruction error on a uniform grid whose scale must be chosen; the common max-based choice degrades sharply at low bit-widths. We study how sensitive this objective is to the scale. For a layer with i.i.d. Gaussian weights and calibration activations of sufficiently large effective rank, we prove that, as the width grows, the normalized round-to-nearest loss converges with high probability, uniformly over all scales, to the mean-squared error of a uniform quantizer applied to a standard Gaussian; we verify the effective-rank condition for wide, randomly initialized MLPs with odd Lipschitz activations and isotropic Gaussian calibration data. The limiting objective has a unique nondegenerate minimizer, whose scale decreases strictly with the number of levels and whose curvature with respect to relative scale errors decays approximately exponentially with the bit-width. GPTQ experiments on five LLMs show the same trend: the scale rule changes perplexity substantially at 2--3 bits and negligibly from 6 bits on, and a local measure of GPTQ scale sensitivity decreases with bit-width in line with the Gaussian curvature. The Gaussian-optimal scale fails on raw weights; after Hadamard incoherence processing it matches the best searched rule at 3 bits and above without any search, but remains clearly worse at 2 bits.

</details>


### [254] [Simultaneous Neural Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37424)
*Milena Gazdieva,Kirill Sokolov,Jiawei Chen,Evgeny Burnaev,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"同时最优传输"问题：用单一共享传输映射将多个源分布对齐到同一目标分布，并提出基于神经网络的max-min求解方法，用于图像恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输通常处理两个分布间的变换，但许多实际场景（如图像恢复中不同退化类型）需要单一映射将多个源分布都映射到同一目标分布。简单地将源分布合并只能保证整体对齐，可能导致单个源分布未对齐。

Method: 将任务形式化为"同时最优传输"问题：学习一个共享传输映射，在最小化平均传输成本的同时使每个源分布与指定目标分布对齐。提出神经方法求解该问题，并推导了学习该映射的max-min（最大最小）公式。

Result: 推导出学习共享传输映射的max-min形式，并将方法应用于图像恢复任务，用单一模型处理多种退化类型。

Conclusion: 所提神经方法能够求解同时最优传输问题，实现用单一共享模型对多源分布进行对齐，在图像恢复中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Optimal Transport (OT) provides a principled framework for learning transformations between probability distributions from unpaired samples. In many applications, however, a single transformation must map several source distributions to a common target distribution. For example, image restoration might require handling different types of degradation without knowing the degradation of each input at inference time. Simple approaches of pooling the source distributions only encourage alignment with the target at the aggregate level and may leave individual sources misaligned. In our paper, we consider the simultaneous OT problem which formalizes the task of learning a shared transport map that minimizes the average transport cost while aligning each source distribution with a prescribed target. We propose a neural method for solving the simultaneous OT problem by learning a shared transport map that minimizes the average transport cost while aligning each source distribution with a prescribed target. We derive a max-min formulation for learning this map. We illustrate its application to image restoration, where a single model handles multiple degradation types using a common collection of clean target images.

</details>


### [255] [Looped Actor: Depth-Recurrent Reasoning Models for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37432)
*T. Konstantin Rusch,Tim Seyde,Jared Boyer,Zach J. Patterson,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 循环推理模型可复用共享参数并在不增加模型规模的情况下增加计算量，还支持依输入动态决定循环次数。受循环 Transformer 在语言与推理任务中的成功启发，本文探究动态循环能否同样提升序列决策。作者先给出复杂性理论动机：存在某些 MDP，状态自适应策略能以渐近更少的期望计算达到最优回报。随后提出 Looped Actor——一种基于 Transformer 的策略，用共享计算块反复精炼隐表示至不动点，从而按状态动态分配计算。在 6 个环境、22 个任务上的实验表明其性能可匹配或超越参数量多 16 倍的基线，且在需要多步规划的任务上增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环推理模型在语言建模与推理中取得成功，其可在不增大参数规模的前提下增加计算，并能依输入动态决定循环次数。作者希望验证这种“动态循环/自适应计算”是否能同样用于序列决策（强化学习），以更高效地提升策略的规划能力。

Method: 1) 理论层面：构造性地证明存在某些马尔可夫决策过程，其中状态自适应策略能达到最优回报，且其期望计算量渐近小于任何最优固定运行时策略，从而为动态循环提供复杂性理论依据。2) 方法层面：提出 Looped Actor，一种基于 Transformer 的策略网络，通过一个共享计算块反复迭代更新隐表示，使其收敛到不动点；循环次数依当前状态变化，实现自适应计算分配，并可在在线/离线 RL、离散/连续动作场景中使用。

Result: 在 6 个环境、22 个任务（涵盖组合谜题到机器人操作、在线与离线 RL、离散与连续动作）上评估：Looped Actor 的性能匹配或超过参数量多 16 倍的“非绑定”（untied）基线，在动作选择需要大量多步规划的环境中增益最大。在 Boxoban 环境中，计算分配呈现结构性规律：循环次数随剩余推动步数增加而增加，且随着循环进行，未来最优推动动作从隐状态中变得越来越可预测。

Conclusion: Actor 循环是一种简单而高效的方式，可为 RL 智能体引入自适应计算并提升其规划能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Looped reasoning models repeatedly apply a shared set of parameters, enabling more computation without increasing the model size. These models also support input-dependent computation by dynamically deciding when to stop looping. Motivated by the recent success of looped transformers in language modeling and reasoning, we investigate whether dynamic looping can similarly benefit sequential decision-making. We provide a complexity-theoretic motivation for this approach by showing that there exist Markov decision processes in which a state-adaptive policy achieves the optimal return with asymptotically less expected computation than any optimal fixed-runtime policy. To learn compute-adaptive policies in practice, we introduce Looped Actor, a transformer-based policy that repeatedly refines a latent representation toward a fixed point using a shared computational block. This allows the model to allocate computation adaptively by varying the number of loops based on the current state. We evaluate Looped Actor on 22 tasks across six environments, ranging from combinatorial puzzles to robotic manipulation and spanning online and offline reinforcement learning (RL) with discrete and continuous actions. Looped Actor matches or exceeds the performance of an untied baseline with 16$\times$ more parameters, with the largest gains in environments where action selection requires substantial multistep planning. For the Boxoban environment, we find that the computation allocation is structured: the number of loops increases with the number of remaining pushes and future optimal pushes become increasingly predictable from the latent state over successive loops. Together, these results highlight actor looping as a simple and efficient way to equip RL agents with adaptive computation and improve their planning capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/camail-official/LoopedActor

</details>


### [256] [Variational Augmented Invertible Koopman Autoencoder for probabilistic time series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37435)
*Anthony Frion,Lucas Drumetz,Guillaume Tochon,Mauro Dalla Mura,Ali Can Bekar,Abdeldjalil Aïssa El Bey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出变分增广可逆Koopman自编码器（VAIKAE），将潜在嵌入建模为高斯分布以量化预测不确定性，并借助归一化流实现状态空间似然训练与不确定性感知的潜在数据同化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经Koopman自编码器虽能在潜在空间构建线性动力学并实现长期时间序列预测，但通常为确定性模型，无法量化预测不确定性。

Method: VAIKAE让潜在嵌入服从高斯分布而非确定性表示；利用归一化流模型，使训练可在动力系统状态空间中使用似然计算；并提出基于已训练VAIKAE的不确定性感知潜在数据同化策略。

Result: 在一系列长期时间序列预测基准实验中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: VAIKAE在保持Koopman线性动力学建模优势的同时实现了不确定性量化，并通过潜在数据同化提升了实用性。

Abstract: Neural Koopman autoencoder models have been shown to successfully build a latent embedding with linear dynamics for arbitrary dynamical systems, enabling strong performance in long-term time series forecasting. However, these models usually work in a deterministic setting, which does not allow the quantification of the uncertainty of their predictions. Thus, we propose the new Variational Augmented Invertible Koopman AutoEncoder (VAIKAE), in which the latent embedding follows a Gaussian distribution instead of being deterministic. A key property of the VAIKAE architecture is that it leverages normalizing flow models, enabling the use of likelihood computations in the state space of dynamical systems for training a model. We further propose new strategies for uncertainty-aware latent data assimilation with a trained VAIKAE model. The effectiveness of our methods is demonstrated in a series of experiments on long-term time series forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [257] [Engineering Efficient Self-Play Chess: Search, Replay, and Throughput Under Limited Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37447)
*Bertil Braun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在单台八卡GPU节点上训练2.5天，6.32M参数的AlphaZero风格国际象棋系统达到3251 Elo（10万次搜索/步，思考时间约5秒以内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在有限训练算力下，当整个学习闭环都针对效率进行工程设计时，AlphaZero风格国际象棋系统能达到的强度上限。

Method: 从随机初始化开始，通过搜索驱动的自我对弈训练；在单台八卡GPU节点训练2.5天；整体优化搜索分配、回放与重启状态选择、策略表示、渐进式模型规模、量化推理及吞吐工程等环节。

Result: 得到6.32M参数模型，在每步10万次搜索（估计思考时间不足5秒）下，对固定节点数Stockfish 13天梯达到3251基准Elo [3206, 3297]；训练消耗325万局完整对局、约1000亿次搜索模拟、8.366亿次训练呈现。

Conclusion: 该强度来自整体集成系统，而非任何单一设计选择带来的孤立Elo提升；论文同时记录了未能改善整体闭环或成本不合理的备选方案。

Abstract: How strong can an AlphaZero-style chess system become under limited training compute when its entire learning loop is engineered for efficiency? We train from random initialization through searched self-play on a single eight-GPU node for 2.5 days. The resulting 6.32-million-parameter model reaches 3,251 benchmark Elo [3,206, 3,297] at 100,000 searches per move (estimated at under five seconds of thinking time) against a fixed-node Stockfish 13 ladder. The run ingests 3.25 million completed games, involves an estimated 100 billion search simulations, and makes 836.6 million training presentations. We investigate search allocation, replay and restart-state selection, policy representation, progressive model sizing, quantized inference, and throughput engineering. Alongside the retained design, we document plausible alternatives that failed to improve the complete learning loop or did not justify their cost. The reported strength is a result of the integrated system, not an isolated Elo gain attributable to any single choice.

</details>


### [258] [Probability Contracts: Accuracy, Coherence, and Decisions Across LLM Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37470)
*Han Chen,Yingrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“概率契约”基准，将精确有限世界后验、经验证的事件变换与失效感知决策评估连接起来，在1000个世界上评估四种模型-接口配置，揭示准确率、连贯性与决策损失之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策所用的概率应在等价请求中指向同一事件。现有评估常将一致性等同于准确率，或将评分提升视为决策保证，缺乏统一基准来区分这些维度。

Method: 引入概率契约基准，连接精确有限世界后验、经验证的事件变换与失效感知决策评估；在1000个世界上评估四种模型-接口配置；进行后验分析与基础性的动作区域刻画，解释平均化何时改变决策损失。

Result: Kev的聚合规范后验误差低于Jev，但补集与粗化残差更大，且准确率排序因分层而异；在延迟成本0.10下，Jev的事件与选择接口在32.8%的有效对上产生不同二元动作；事后分析发现分歧仅能证明各配置平均二元对误差的11%–52%；平均化不会恶化该基线的期望Brier分数，但其决策效应取决于成本与交叉边界，观察到的同基线惩罚出现在已劣于始终延迟的配置中。

Conclusion: 该基准使这些区分可测量，同时不将一致性当作准确率，也不将评分提升当作决策保证。

Abstract: A probability used for a decision should refer to the same event across equivalent requests. We introduce probability contracts, a benchmark connecting exact finite-world posteriors, validated event transformations, and failure-aware decision evaluation. Four model-interface configurations are evaluated on 1,000 worlds. Their assessments differ across accuracy, coherence, and decision loss: Kev has lower aggregate canonical posterior error than Jev, but larger complement and coarsening residuals, with accuracy ordering varying by stratum. Jev's Event and Choice interfaces induce different binary actions on 32.8% of valid pairs at defer cost 0.10. A post-hoc analysis finds that disagreement certifies only 11-52% of mean binary pair error across configurations. An elementary action-region characterization explains when averaging changes decision loss relative to randomly selecting one interface. Although averaging cannot worsen that baseline's expected Brier score, its decision effect depends on the cost and crossed boundaries; the observed same-baseline penalties occur in configurations already worse than always deferring. The benchmark makes these distinctions measurable without treating consistency as accuracy or a score improvement as a decision guarantee.

</details>


### [259] [Risk-Controlled Selective LLM Answering by Pricing Label-Free Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37493)
*Dongyub Jude Lee,Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Hyeonseok Moon,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PriceCheck 提出一种基于"无标签检查"（如重新解题）的决策规则框架，用价格（在正确/错误答案上的一致率及单次成本）来预测调度方案的覆盖率与成本，并通过校准测试在指定选择性风险目标下选择调度，从而决定何时作答、何时弃答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型作答时需要决定何时弃答，但仅有验证器的排序准确率并不能决定已作答部分的错误率，因此需要一种能直接控制"服务答案错误率"的决策机制。

Method: 构建紧凑的决策规则族，每条规则基于无标签检查；为每个检查估计"价格"（正确/错误答案上的一致率与每次运行成本）；在少量类别增强的带标签集合上拟合价格，用于预测调度的覆盖率和成本，指导选择运行哪些检查及何时停止；再用校准测试在给定选择性风险目标下选择调度。

Result: 数学任务中，所选调度平均服务 76.1% 的答案，并在全部 15 个划分上把留出选择性风险控制在 1.5% 以下；在同一测试协议下，该目标下服务的答案数多于奖励模型、提示式评判器、生成器置信度和训练的正确答案分类器；在相同覆盖率下其错误答案最少；118 个诊断调度中，基于价格的覆盖率预测与实测覆盖率的秩相关为 0.97。

Conclusion: 如何组合与停止检查，与验证器排序答案的能力同等重要。

Abstract: Serving an answer from a large language model requires deciding when to abstain, yet a verifier's ranking accuracy alone does not determine the error rate among served answers. We introduce PriceCheck, which builds a compact family of decision rules from label-free checks such as re-solving a problem. Each check has a price: its agreement rates on correct and incorrect answers and its cost per run. Prices fitted on a small, class-enriched labelled set compose into predictions of a schedule's coverage and cost, guiding which checks to run and when to stop. A calibration test then selects a schedule at a stated selective-risk target. In mathematics, the selected schedules serve 76.1% of answers on average and keep held-out selective risk below 1.5% on all 15 splits. Under the shared testing protocol, PriceCheck serves more answers at that target than reward models, a prompted judge, the generator's confidence and a trained correctness classifier. At matched coverage, it keeps the fewest wrong answers among these scorers. Across 118 diagnostic schedules, price-based coverage predictions have a rank correlation of 0.97 with observed coverage. These results show that choosing how checks are combined and stopped matters alongside how well a verifier ranks answers. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/PriceCheck.

</details>


### [260] [REVO: Rollout-Efficient Off-Policy Distillation via Variance-Guided Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37500)
*Yuxiao Yang,Shangzhe Li,Tianrun Yu,Kaixiang Zhao,Taylor W. Killian,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REVO是一种离策略蒸馏框架，通过重复利用学生模型生成的轨迹进行多步更新，在显著降低生成成本的同时匹配或超越在策略蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略蒸馏依赖频繁刷新的学生模型rollout，导致生成成本高昂。作者希望提升rollout的利用效率，减少重新生成完整轨迹的开销。

Method: REVO在离策略设定下，将每条学生rollout复用于多步学习器更新。它通过稳定化的前缀加权和从当前学生的一步重采样，解决前缀级与当前token的策略不匹配问题；并利用学生-教师对数概率比值的方差来量化剩余token级学习信号，优先优化信息量高的token位置。

Result: 在多种学生-教师规模下，REVO仅用50次rollout迭代，就在域内与跨域推理基准上匹配或超过了训练200次迭代的在策略蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 重复利用学生rollout进行多步更新可有效提升蒸馏效率，REVO以更少生成成本取得了与充足训练的在策略蒸馏相当或更优的性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains language models using dense token-level teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, its reliance on frequently refreshed student rollouts often incurs substantial generation cost. We introduce REVO, an off-policy distillation framework that improves rollout efficiency by reusing each student rollout for multi-step learner updates. REVO addresses prefix-level and current-token policy mismatch through stabilized prefix weighting and one-step resampling from the current student, which enables repeated updates without regenerating full trajectories. To prioritize informative token positions within reused rollouts, REVO uses the variance of the student-teacher log-probability ratio to quantify the remaining token-level learning signal and guide repeated optimization. Across multiple student-teacher scales, REVO with only 50 rollout iterations matches or exceeds OPD baselines trained for 200 iterations on both in-domain and cross-domain reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [261] [ScaGNN: a Graph Neural Network for Multiple Scattering Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37509)
*Rémi Marsal,Stéphanie Chaillat,Alexandre Chapoutot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ScaGNN，一种基于图神经网络的方法，用于近似边界元法（BEM）中边界解迹的求解，通过动态自适应边采样实现线性复杂度，并超越现有学习类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界元法（BEM）虽能高效求解无界均匀域中的多重散射问题，但其第一步——求解边界积分方程获得边界解迹——是主要计算瓶颈。因此需要一种学习方法来加速该步骤。

Method: ScaGNN采用图神经网络架构，融合动态自适应边采样机制，依据对期望误差和边长的中间预测，在正向传播的不同阶段选择最相关的远距离交互进行建模，从而使复杂度随输入图节点数线性增长。同时构建了包含多种多重散射问题的基准数据集用于训练与评估。

Result: 在多个基准任务上，该方法超越了现有最先进的学习类方法，并进一步考察了在障碍物数量增加及分布外障碍物形状情形下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ScaGNN为BEM中边界解迹的高效近似提供了可行方案，实现线性复杂度并具备良好泛化性，代码已开源于github.com/LARIAD/ScaGNN。

Abstract: The boundary element method (BEM) provides an efficient numerical framework for solving multiple scattering problems in unbounded homogeneous domains. By restricting the discretization to the domain boundaries, it substantially reduces computational complexity. The procedure first consists in determining the solution trace on the boundaries of the domain by solving a boundary integral equation. Then, the volumetric solution can be recovered at low computational cost using a boundary integral representation. As the first step of the BEM represents the main computational bottleneck, we present ScaGNN, a learning-based approach designed to approximate the solution trace. It relies on a graph neural network architecture that incorporates a dynamic adaptive edge sampling mechanism for selecting the most relevant interactions to model. Guided by intermediate predictions of expected error and edge length, this mechanism selects, at various stages of the forward pass, the most relevant distant interactions to model. The proposed method is tailored to achieve linear complexity with the number of nodes in the input graph. To train and evaluate our network, we present a benchmark consisting of several datasets with different types of multiple scattering problems. Our experiments show that our approach surpasses existing state-of-the-art learning-based methods on the considered tasks and investigate the generalization capabilities to settings with an increased number of obstacles and out-of-distribution obstacle shapes. github.com/LARIAD/ScaGNN

</details>


### [262] [Hierarchical Compression of Vision-Language Model Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37515)
*Hyunjong Ok,Seunggu Kang,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PRIMEBench，一种视觉感知的分层基准压缩框架，可将VLM评测成本降低超97%同时保持模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM评测成本随能力范围扩大和新模型快速涌现而变得过高，而基准压缩方法在语言模型中已成熟，但在VLM领域尚缺乏探索。

Method: PRIMEBench为四阶段分层框架：数据清洗（去除无需图像即可作答及全对的题目）、类别代表选择（每类能力选一个基准）、基于视觉感知方差（VAW）的题目剪枝、类别计数剪枝。VAW结合模型间方差与仅由多模态嵌入计算的视觉依赖分数，并鼓励题目多样性覆盖。

Result: 在留出模型上，PRIMEBench在发布的5%保留率下取得最高平均保真度；发布套件在保持模型排名的同时剔除超过97%的题目。

Conclusion: 分层设计允许使用者按算力预算在任一阶段停止；分析还揭示了随模型群扩大与更迭时VLM评测的行为，为设计更高效、抗模型更迭、并明确评测侧剪枝局限的未来基准提供指导。

Abstract: Thorough evaluation of vision-language models (VLMs) has become prohibitively expensive, as benchmarks span an ever-broader spectrum of capabilities and new models arrive at a relentless pace. Benchmark compression methods that preserve model rankings at a fraction of the cost are well studied for language models, but for VLMs the question remains under-explored. We present PRIMEBench (Pruning Redundant Items for Multimodal Evaluation), a vision-aware hierarchical benchmark compression framework that substantially reduces evaluation cost while preserving model rankings. This hierarchical framework operates in four stages: data cleaning to remove items answerable without the image and all-correct items, category representative selection to pick one benchmark per capability category, item pruning with Vision-Aware Variance (VAW), and category-count pruning. VAW combines inter-model variance with a vision-dependence score computed from multimodal embeddings alone, while encouraging coverage of diverse items within each benchmark. On models held out from item selection, it has the highest mean fidelity at the released 5% retention. The hierarchical design lets practitioners stop at any stage to match their compute budget; the released suite removes over 97% of items while preserving model rankings. Beyond compression, our analyses show how VLM evaluation behaves as model panels grow and evolve, providing guidance for designing future benchmarks that are more efficient, robust to model turnover, and explicit about the limits of evaluation-side pruning.

</details>


### [263] [Graph-Conditioned On-Policy Agent Distillation from Off-the-Shelf Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37522)
*Xiaohan Yi,Wen Luo,Yani Huang,Junfeng Zhan,Asher Qin,Peilin Zhao,Xi Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GC-OPD通过图结构检索教师执行证据增强评分上下文，显著提升多轮智能体蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy蒸馏在多轮任务中因误差累积导致学生偏离教师有效监督范围，需要提供更丰富的监督信号。

Method: 构建图索引重复教师执行，按共享状态组织并保留完整成功/失败历史；每轮学生episode后检索当前状态参考或历史替代方案，结合学生后见之明对原始thought-action token打分。

Result: ScienceWorld 4B从24.70%提升至48.78%，ALFWorld Unseen从53.36%提升至85.26%，WebShop从29.10%提升至37.65%；优于所有基于GRPO教师的OPD基线（最强ScienceWorld 4B为46.66%）。

Conclusion: GC-OPD无需任务特定的教师优化，即可在多轮智能体蒸馏中显著提高成功率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains compact language agents with teacher feedback on student-generated trajectories. In multi-turn tasks, compounding errors can move students beyond the teacher's effective supervision. We introduce Graph-Conditioned On-Policy Agent Distillation (GC-OPD), which enriches an off-the-shelf teacher's scoring context with execution evidence. A graph indexes repeated teacher executions by shared states while preserving complete successful and failed histories. After each student episode, GC-OPD retrieves current-state references or historical alternatives and combines them with student hindsight to score the original thought-action tokens. Using the same original teachers, GC-OPD improves mean success over vanilla OPD from 24.70% to 48.78% on ScienceWorld (4B student), from 53.36% to 85.26% on ALFWorld Unseen, and from 29.10% to 37.65% on WebShop. At matched student sizes, it also achieves higher mean success than every evaluated OPD baseline using GRPO-trained teachers on ScienceWorld and ALFWorld; the strongest such ScienceWorld 4B baseline reaches 46.66%. GC-OPD requires no task-specific teacher optimization.

</details>


### [264] [Physical Muon: Orthogonalization as an Equilibrium Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37525)
*Yuren Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Physical Muon优化器，将Muon的正交化过程重新表述为连续时间流的平衡态，用随机探针借助矩阵-向量乘积和局部rank-1写入来近似，从而适配物理神经网络与模拟内存计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理神经网络和模拟内存计算有望降低神经网络训练能耗，但需要兼顾有效学习与物理可实现性的优化器。SGD适合局部模拟更新但在Transformer上表现不佳，Adam族对模拟偏置不稳定，Muon性能强但其Newton-Schulz正交化依赖稠密矩阵-矩阵乘积，难以物理实现。

Method: 提出Physical Muon，把正交化计算为连续时间流的平衡态；用随机探针通过矩阵-向量乘积、互易读取和局部rank-1写入来近似该流。并在10.95M参数Transformer上进行评估及电路仿真。

Result: 在10.95M参数Transformer上，稠密流相对Newton-Schulz的平均验证交叉熵高0.0085（每方法九个随机种子）；探针实现相对对照高0.0188（两个种子）。电路仿真复现了流的动力学并给出可比的训练行为。

Conclusion: Physical Muon通过连续时间流与随机探针近似，在保持与Newton-Schulz相近训练性能的同时，实现了对物理硬件的友好性，为物理神经网络训练提供了可行的优化器方案。

Abstract: Physical neural networks and analog in-memory computing could reduce the energy cost of neural network training. Realizing this potential, however, requires optimizers that combine effective learning with physical implementability. SGD fits local analog updates but struggles on transformers, while Adam family is unstable against analog bias. Muon offers strong training performance, but its Newton--Schulz orthogonalization relies on dense matrix-matrix products. To address this obstacle, we introduce Physical Muon, which computes the orthogonalization as the equilibrium of a continuous-time flow. Random probes approximate the flow using matrix-vector products, reciprocal reads, and local rank-1 writes. To test whether this replacement preserves training performance, we evaluate it on a 10.95M-parameter transformer. The dense flow's mean validation cross-entropy is 0.0085 above Newton--Schulz across nine seeds per method; the probe implementation is 0.0188 above the control across two seeds. Circuit simulations further reproduce the flow dynamics and yield comparable training behavior.

</details>


### [265] [Why Adaptive Optimizers Underestimate Rare Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37535)
*Sangsidhya Kar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应优化器（Adam、RMSProp、sign descent等）对稀有token的梯度归一化效应，刻画了哪些优化器保持输出层均值嵌入与无偏固定点，并推导出RMSProp在周期到达下的闭式偏差公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有token在多数步骤只收到小正梯度、在其作为目标时收到大负梯度。坐标级自适应方法会用梯度幅度的运行估计来缩放更新，而该估计在token刚出现后最大，从而产生偏差。作者希望厘清这一机制对输出层整体与单个稀有token训练固定点的影响。

Method: 从理论上分析优化器更新对输出层均值嵌入的影响，区分线性于历史梯度的方法（含Shampoo、Muon）与Adam、Adafactor、Lion、sign descent等非线性归一化方法；对后者给出逐步骤变化表达式。在unigram模型中分析sign descent的固定点偏移，对RMSProp在周期到达假设下求解闭式固定点，并对比SGD与AMSGrad。最后在unigram模型和已知生成分布的小型语言模型上做实验验证。

Result: 线性于历史梯度的更新方法、Kronecker分解与正交化方法（Shampoo、Muon）保持均值输出嵌入，而Adam、Adafactor、Lion、sign descent不保持。sign descent以恒定期望速率降低出现频率低于一半minibatch的token的logit。RMSProp在周期到达下，若token连续至少两个minibatch缺席，其均衡概率严格低于数据频率，比值趋于κ/(2(e^{κ/2}-1))，其中κ为相邻出现间隔步数均值与二阶矩时间常数1/(1-β₂)之比；SGD与AMSGrad保持无偏固定点。实验中随机到达时偏差大于周期公式预测，语言模型中有偏固定点的优化器对生成分布的拟合也更差。

Conclusion: 自适应归一化优化器会对稀有token引入系统性训练偏差，压低其输出概率并损害分布拟合；该偏差可由到达间隔与二阶矩时间常数的比值刻画，SGDM类保持无偏更新则可避免该问题。

Abstract: In the softmax output layer, a rare token receives a small positive logit gradient on most steps and a much larger negative gradient on the few steps when it is the target. SGD simply adds these contributions. Coordinate-wise adaptive methods such as Adam, RMSProp, and sign descent instead divide each update by a running estimate of its magnitude, and that estimate is largest immediately after the token appears. This imbalance has two effects. At the level of the whole output layer, we characterize which optimizers preserve the mean output embedding: every method whose update is linear in past gradients does, as do Kronecker-factored and orthogonalized methods such as Shampoo and Muon. Adam, Adafactor, Lion, and sign descent do not, and for these methods we obtain an exact step-by-step expression for the change. At the level of an individual rare token, the same normalization shifts the training fixed point. In the unigram model, sign descent lowers the logit of every token that occurs in fewer than half of the minibatches at a constant expected rate. For RMSProp with periodic arrivals, we can solve the fixed point in closed form: if a token is absent for at least two consecutive minibatches, its equilibrium probability is strictly below its data frequency for every learning rate, and the ratio tends to $κ/(2(e^{κ/2}-1))$. Here $κ$ is the mean number of steps between occurrences divided by the second-moment time constant $1/(1-β_2)$. In the same model, SGD and AMSGrad retain the unbiased fixed point. We test these predictions both in a unigram model and in a small language model trained from a known generating distribution. With random arrivals, the bias is larger than the periodic formula predicts; in the language model, the optimizers with the biased fixed point also fit the generating distribution less well.

</details>


### [266] [Benchmarking graph-based models for in-silico toxicity prediction in drug discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37555)
*Noel Suarez-Barro,Manuel Lama,Juan C. Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的图深度学习（GDL）毒性预测基准框架BenchTox，在一致实验条件下系统评测20余种代表性方法，结合文献分析揭示领域现状，并开源代码以促进可复现比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现成本高、风险大，毒性相关失败是临床前和临床阶段化合物淘汰的主要原因，因此早期准确预测化学毒性对降低成本、优化化合物筛选至关重要。图深度学习因能直接从分子图结构学习、表达能力强而成为毒性预测的重要范式，但现有文献比较因数据集、预处理流程和评测协议不一致而难以解读。

Method: 构建统一、标准化的GDL毒性预测基准框架，在一致的实验条件、多个数据集和多种数据划分策略下，系统评测20余种代表性方法；同时辅以结构化文献分析，梳理现有方法学趋势与性能主张，并将框架开源（https://gitlab.citius.gal/noel.suarez/benchtox）。

Result: 在一致条件下获得对当前领域更清晰、更可靠的性能评估，揭示了现有基于图的方法的优势与局限。

Conclusion: 该工作为GDL毒性预测提供了公平、可复现的比较基准，有助于社区在统一条件下评测模型，推动领域透明化与可复现研究。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a costly and high-risk process, where toxicity-related failures remain a major cause of attrition in both preclinical and clinical stages. As a result, accurate early prediction of chemical toxicity is essential to reduce downstream costs and improve compound prioritization. In this context, graph deep learning (GDL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for toxicity prediction, leveraging molecular graph representations to learn directly from chemical structure with improved expressivity over traditional approaches.
  Despite the growing number of proposed models, current literature-based comparisons are often difficult to interpret due to inconsistencies in datasets, preprocessing pipelines, and evaluation protocols. To address this limitation, we introduce a unified and standardized benchmarking framework for GDL-based toxicity prediction. We systematically evaluate more than 20 representative approaches under consistent experimental conditions and across multiple datasets and partitioning strategies, enabling a fair and reproducible comparison of model performance. In addition, we complement this empirical study with a structured literature analysis to contextualize existing methodological trends and performance claims. Our results provide a clearer and more reliable assessment of the current state of the field, highlighting both the strengths and limitations of existing graph-based approaches. To support transparency and reproducibility, we release our benchmarking framework as open-source software https://gitlab.citius.gal/noel.suarez/benchtox, allowing the community to evaluate and compare models under consistent conditions.

</details>


### [267] [A Model-Agnostic Physics-Guided Adapter for Few-Shot Transfer of Coastal Flood Prediction Models to Unseen Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37565)
*Bilal Hassan,Areg Karapetyan,Samer Madanat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Physics Adapter (PA)，一种轻量、架构无关的适配接口，通过可微分干湿响应与学习门控融合物理结构预测和数据驱动分支，实现跨沿海区域的少样本洪水预测迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习代理模型虽比物理水动力模拟器快数个数量级，但迁移到新沿海区域成本高，因为微调所需的目标区域数据依赖大量耗时模拟。需要解决跨区域数据稀缺的迁移瓶颈。

Method: PA通过可微分干湿响应（比较地形高程与学习的水位）预测峰值水位，并用学习门控将其与数据驱动分支融合。不同于物理信息方法，PA不施加PDE残差或守恒损失，而是利用高程作为架构归纳偏置，仅增加极少可训练参数。将PA集成到12个异构模型，在两个几何、地形和岸线防护差异显著的沿海区域评估，对比无物理基线、全微调和标准PEFT方法，覆盖区域内泛化与区域间迁移。

Result: 在低样本（K=3）下，对所有骨干和迁移设置平均，仅适配输出头时RMSE降低11.5%；与PEFT结合时降低15.4%；全微调下降低22.9%，均与不含PA的匹配配置对比。

Conclusion: PA为将基于深度学习的沿海洪水预测器扩展到新的数据稀缺区域提供了具体可行的方案。

Abstract: Deep learning surrogates can produce high-resolution coastal flood maps orders of magnitude faster than physics-based hydrodynamic simulators, yet transferring them to new coastal regions remains costly, since generating target-region data for fine-tuning typically requires numerous time-consuming simulations. To tackle this bottleneck, we introduce the Physics Adapter (PA), a compact, architecture-agnostic adaptation interface that enables efficient few-shot transfer of flood prediction models across diverse coastal regions. PA predicts peak water level through a differentiable wet/dry response that compares terrain elevation against a learned water level, and blends this physics-structured prediction with a data-driven branch through a learned gate. Unlike physics-informed formulations, PA imposes no PDE-residual or conservation losses and instead exploits elevation as an architectural inductive bias, adding a negligible number of trainable parameters. We integrate PA into 12 heterogeneous models, and evaluate them on two coastal regions with markedly distinct geometries, topographies, and shoreline protection configurations. The performance of PA is benchmarked against a no-physics baseline, full fine-tuning, and standard parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, considering both within-region generalization to unseen sea level rise values and between-region transfer. In low-shot regime (K=3), and averaged over all backbones and transfer settings, adding PA reduces root mean square error by 11.5% when only the output head is adapted on a frozen backbone, by 15.4% when combined with PEFT methods, and by 22.9% under full fine-tuning, compared to matched configurations without PA. Taken together, the findings of this work offer practitioners a concrete recipe for extending DL-based coastal flood predictors to new, data-scarce regions.

</details>


### [268] [ReLMem: Learning Recurrent Memory for Longitudinal EHR Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37587)
*Zijie Meng,Xiwei Dai,Yingying Zhang,Jian Wu,Xian Wu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ReLMem 框架，用轻量压缩适配器让冻结的大语言模型以固定容量记忆循环更新患者历史，在用药预测任务上接近全历史基线 F1，同时减少 97.1% 存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向电子健康记录（EHR）建模需不断把新就诊与日益扩大的病史整合，但完整病史的累积会给大语言模型带来急剧上升的计算与内存开销。可行替代方案是按次就诊的循环压缩，将每次新就诊并入紧凑且持续更新的患者记忆；难点在于固定记忆预算下，连续更新既要吸收新信息，又不能逐步丢失后续任务所需的关键历史证据。

Method: ReLMem 为冻结的大语言模型配备轻量压缩适配器，基于上一记忆状态与每次新到就诊循环更新固定容量患者记忆，无需重读早期记录。并设计多粒度优化策略：中间监督在相同查询下对齐压缩记忆与完整历史的注意力输出，预测监督则以最终记忆为条件、用交叉熵最小化与真实答案的差异，从而在循环更新中保留任务相关信息并支持下游预测。

Result: 在基于 EHR 的用药预测任务上，ReLMem 的 F1 接近全历史基线，同时将平均保留的历史存储降低 97.1%；在相同记忆预算下，其宏 F1 与微 F1 分别比最强压缩记忆基线提升 4.66 和 4.75 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习循环式患者记忆对高效的纵向 EHR 建模具有重要价值，可在显著降低存储成本的同时保持接近完整病史的预测性能。

Abstract: Longitudinal electronic health record (EHR) modeling requires integrating new visits with an expanding patient history. Yet the continual accumulation of clinical information imposes increasing computational and memory costs on large language models (LLMs) when they process and retain complete patient histories. A practical alternative is visit-wise recurrent compression, which incorporates each incoming visit into a compact, continually updated patient memory. However, under a fixed memory budget, successive updates must integrate new information without progressively losing critical historical evidence needed to subsequent tasks. To address this challenge, we introduce Recurrent Longitudinal Memory (ReLMem), a framework that learns to maintain fixed-capacity patient memory for efficient downstream prediction with a frozen LLM. ReLMem equips this LLM with lightweight compression adapters to recurrently update the memory from its previous state and each incoming visit, without rereading earlier records. Specifically, we develop a multi-granularity optimization strategy to preserve task-relevant information throughout recurrent updates and support downstream prediction from the final memory. The intermediate supervision aligns attention outputs from compressed memory and the full history under identical queries, while prediction supervision minimizes cross-entropy with ground truth answers conditioned on the final memory. On EHR-based medication prediction, ReLMem approaches the F1 scores of full-history baseline while reducing average retained historical storage by 97.1%. Under the same memory budget, it improves macro- and micro-F1 over the strongest compressed-memory baseline by 4.66 and 4.75 percentage points, respectively. These results highlight the value of learning recurrent patient memory for efficient longitudinal EHR modeling.

</details>


### [269] [GraphVQ: Structure-Aware Autoregressive Decoding over Context-Quantized Graph Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37604)
*Yuxiang Yao,Zijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphVQ 通过将节点上下文量化为共享码本 token，并引入结构感知的成对条件解码器，解决了图自回归生成中边缘跨窗口与全局条件无法区分候选边的两大障碍，实现了分布更忠实的图生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型需要离散 token 表示，但将图序列化为可生成的 token 序列存在结构障碍：跨越序列化窗口的边无法一次生成，导致单次自回归生成器系统性欠产生环；同时单一全局条件无法区分候选边。论文旨在消除这两个障碍。

Method: 1) 节点上下文（特征 + 多阶广度优先序列化下的局部边掩码）经 VQ-VAE 量化为共享码本，并采用 BCE 校准的伯努利边解码；2) 第二阶段结构感知解码器以 token 导出的成对特征为条件生成全局邻接矩阵；3) 通过有范围的不可行性结果形式化说明成对条件优于任意全局摘要条件的必要性。

Result: Tokenizer 重建节点特征准确率 0.86–0.99，局部边解码 AUROC ≥ 0.89（ECE ≤ 0.007）。同一划分协议下，成对条件将 orbit MMD 在 PROTEINS 上从 0.248 降至 0.174，在环形压力测试上提升 3.4 倍，而在随机标签对照上消失。GraphVQ 在 PROTEINS 上居学习型生成器首位，在 SYN-COMM 上并列第一，在三个数据集上较单阶段生成提升 orbit MMD 2.7–17 倍，bootstrap 区间确认排名非种子噪声；在 MUTAG 上无权重边目标欠生成并如实报告。

Conclusion: 自回归图生成的结构控制关键在于条件粒度：成对级 token 上下文将量化词汇表转化为可用的容量轴，支持分布忠实的图生成与未来 token 级预训练。

Abstract: Graph foundation models need a discrete token representation, but casting a graph as a generatable token sequence faces a structural obstacle: edges spanning beyond the serialization window cannot be emitted in one pass--so one-pass autoregressive generators systematically under-produce cycles--and a single global condition cannot tell candidate edges apart. GraphVQ removes both obstacles: node contexts--features plus a local edge mask under multi-order breadth-first serialization--are quantized into a shared codebook by a VQ-VAE with BCE-calibrated Bernoulli edge decoding, and a second-stage structure-aware decoder emits the global adjacency conditioned on token-derived pair features, whose necessity over any global-summary condition is formalized in a scoped impossibility result. The tokenizer reconstructs node features at 0.86--0.99 accuracy and decodes local edges at AUROC >= 0.89 (ECE <= 0.007). Under one same-split protocol on four datasets, pair conditioning improves orbit MMD 0.248 -> 0.174 on PROTEINS and 3.4x on a ring stress test, and vanishes on a random-label control--the signature of attribute--topology coupling--so the gain is claimed exactly where attributes carry edge-relevant signal. GraphVQ ranks first among learned generators on PROTEINS, ties for first on SYN-COMM, and improves orbit MMD 2.7--17x over one-stage generation on three datasets, with seed-level bootstrap intervals confirming the rankings are not seed noise; on MUTAG the unweighted edge target under-generates and is reported as such. These results locate the structural control of autoregressive graph generation in the granularity of the condition: pair-level token context turns a quantized vocabulary into a usable capacity axis for distribution-faithful graph generation and future token-level pretraining.

</details>


### [270] [PHASE: Multi-Regime Modeling of Incompressible Magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37609)
*Radhika Achikanath Chirakkara,Rajdeep Haldar,Zezheng Song,Jiequn Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PHASE物理自适应神经算子，单模型跨参数建模不可压缩MHD，误差降低超一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MHD在多个领域核心，但多尺度求解计算昂贵；现有机器学习代理需针对每个物理区域单独训练，泛化性差。

Method: PHASE结合迁移学习、区域感知自适应、以物理为中心的学习与残差精化，作为物理自适应可扩展算子。

Result: 二维MHD湍流上物理场相对L2误差比现有神经算子基线降低超一个数量级；无需重训练即可泛化到未见参数，并在物理量、谱与分布统计上验证；成功模拟开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性。

Conclusion: 证明单一算子模型可实现跨区域MHD建模，具备物理保真度与鲁棒泛化能力。

Abstract: Magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) is central to plasma modeling in astrophysics, space science, fusion, and engineering, but resolving multiscale MHD dynamics is computationally expensive. Machine-learning surrogates enable fast inference by learning reusable solution operators, yet existing models require separate training for each physical regime, limiting generalization across varying parameter settings. We introduce PHASE, a PHysics-Adaptive Scalable operator with residual Error correction, designed to model incompressible MHD across varying physical parameters with a single model. PHASE combines transfer learning, regime-aware adaptation, physics-centered learning, and residual refinement to improve both physical fidelity and generalization across MHD regimes. Together, these improvements achieve state-of-the-art prediction accuracy on two-dimensional MHD turbulence by reducing relative $L_2$ errors on physical fields by more than an order of magnitude compared to prior MHD neural-operator baselines. Moreover, PHASE generalizes successfully to unseen parameter values without retraining, demonstrating the cross-regime adaptability expected from operator learning. We evaluate PHASE beyond point-wise prediction errors using derived physical fields, spectral analysis, and distribution statistics, consistently observing improved physical fidelity. We further show that our framework can accurately simulate MHD instabilities by testing it on the Kelvin--Helmholtz instability, demonstrating the robustness of our method.

</details>


### [271] [Authority Bias in Language Models: Source Deference and User Agreement Are Not Interchangeable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37616)
*Abhinav Rajeev Kumar,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型对"用户断言"的谄媚已被后训练抑制，但对"被认证来源背书的错误答案"却高度顺从。论文在8个模型（5个开源系列+3个闭源API）中测量这一差距，并用因果干预证明：来源顺从与用户顺从在模型内部是两种可分离的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练主要针对"用户谄媚"（sycophancy）进行修正，让模型依据论据本身评估主张、而非迁就用户。但检索结果、工具输出、带grounding的搜索内容常以"已验证来源"的形式呈现信息，模型对这类权威来源的错误答案可能同样甚至更加顺从。若把来源顺从与用户顺承当作同一种行为，评估与干预就会遗漏这一风险，因此需要单独刻画二者。

Method: 在五个开源权重模型系列与三个闭源API上测量"来源顺从"与"用户顺从"的差距：加入一条背书错误答案的"已验证来源"注释，观察原本正确回答被翻转的比例，并变化注释的权威性措辞。随后用因果干预（激活方向拟合/消融）检验两种顺从是否可分离：分别拟合来源方向、用户方向、助手方向，并考察消融各自方向对两类顺从的选择性影响。另拟合一个基于"来源vs用户"线索激活的干预方向，在不改动词元文本的前提下双向调控顺从；并检验在trivia上拟合的权威方向能否迁移到PIQA与多轮SYCON对话。

Result: 一条背书错误答案的"已验证来源"注释，在八个模型中的七个上翻转了45-88%的原本正确回答；注释越具权威性，顺从率越高。行为上二者不可互换：在错误答案相同的匹配样本上，因果干预可选择性抑制其一而基本不影响另一。在三个开源系列中，消融拟合出的来源方向使来源顺从下降65-80个百分点，而消融用户或助手方向影响小得多；消融用户方向则表现出相反的偏好。基于"来源vs用户"线索激活拟合的干预可双向移动顺从且不改变提示文本。在trivia上拟合的权威方向无需重新拟合即可迁移到PIQA和多轮SYCON对话，消融它在五分之四的系列中使错误来源顺从下降数十个百分点，而在当前评估规模下MMLU-Pro与GSM8K准确率未见明显变化。

Conclusion: 来源顺从（source deference）与用户顺从（user agreement）是模型内部可分离的行为，需要分别评估与干预。仅针对用户谄媚做后训练，无法覆盖"被认证来源背书的错误信息"这一独立风险；权威方向具有跨任务可迁移性，且对其干预在测试规模下未损害通用能力，提示存在可行的定向缓解路径。

Abstract: Language models tend to agree with whatever a user asserts, and post-training increasingly targets this sycophancy so that models evaluate claims on their merits rather than deferring to the user. Yet the same models are far more compliant when a wrong answer is attributed to a verified source, which is how retrieval results, tool outputs, and grounded-search content often present information. We measure this gap across five open-weight families and three closed APIs. A single verified-source note endorsing a wrong answer flips 45-88% of baseline-correct responses in seven of eight models, and compliance rises with how authoritative the note sounds. Source deference and user agreement are not behaviorally interchangeable inside the model: on matched items with the same wrong answer, causal interventions can selectively suppress one without equally affecting the other. In three open-weight families, removing a fitted source direction lowers source compliance by 65-80 percentage points while removing a user or assistant direction has far smaller effects, and removing the user direction shows the reverse preference. A separately fitted intervention derived from source-versus-user cue activations moves compliance in both directions while leaving the prompt text unchanged. An authority direction fitted on trivia also transfers to PIQA and multi-turn SYCON dialogues without refitting, and removing it lowers wrong-source compliance by tens of percentage points in four of five families with no detected change in MMLU-Pro or GSM8K accuracy at our evaluation sizes. Source deference and user agreement therefore need separate evaluation.

</details>


### [272] [Procedural Core: A Compact Recurrent Initialization for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37631)
*Zachary Shinnick,Christian Internò,Hemanth Saratchandran,Anton van den Hengel,Damien Teney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Procedural Core 初始化策略，将过程生成数据训练出的紧凑权重扩展到任意宽度和深度的 Transformer，无需重复预训练即可获得通用结构先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Transformer 从随机初始化开始训练，需要大规模优化才能获得全部能力；已有工作虽用少量过程生成数据获取通用归纳结构，但每个目标模型都要重复预训练阶段，成本高。

Method: 先用过程数据训练一个最小循环（recurrent）Transformer，再将其权重扩展（expand）以初始化任意宽度与深度的 Transformer，得到一个可跨模型复用的紧凑权重初始化。

Result: 在图像分类、DINO 自监督视觉学习、自然语言（FineWeb-Edu）与代码（CodeParrot）建模上均有提升；图像分类中把 1M 参数核心扩展到 85M 的 ViT-Base，ImageNet top-1 提升 2.2 个百分点。ViT 中收益主要来自抑制高范数 token，使零样本分割（ImageNet-S mAP 32.3→42.9）、目标定位（VOC07 CorLoc 9.9→18.4）和深度估计（NYUv2 RMSE 1.104→0.998）显著改善。分析表明循环结构对学习可迁移的紧凑权重至关重要。

Conclusion: Transformer 不必从空白开始，可用低成本的通用初始化（无需领域或任务特定数据）赋予其通用能力。

Abstract: Transformers are typically trained from random initialization, requiring all their capabilities to emerge from large-scale optimization. Recent work showed that a small amount of abstract procedurally generated data can help acquire generic inductive structure at low cost. However, this adds a pretraining stage that must be repeated for every target model. We propose Procedural Core, an initialization strategy that captures this generic structure into a compact set of weights that can be reused across models. We train a minimal recurrent transformer on procedural data, then expand its weights to initialize transformers of arbitrary width and depth. The resulting initialization improves performance on image classification, self-supervised visual learning (DINO), and modeling natural language (FineWeb-Edu) and code (CodeParrot). For image classification, expanding a 1M-parameter core to initialize an 85M-parameter ViT-Base improves ImageNet top-1 accuracy by 2.2 pp over standard random initialization. Our analysis identifies recurrence as essential for learning compact weights that transfer across models. In ViTs, we localize a key benefit in the suppression of high-norm tokens that produces substantial improvements in zero-shot segmentation (ImageNet-S mAP 32.3 to 42.9), object localization (VOC07 CorLoc 9.9 to 18.4), and depth estimation (NYUv2 RMSE 1.104 to 0.998). This demonstrates that transformers need not start from a blank slate, and can be initialized with generic capabilities at low cost with no domain- or task-specific data.

</details>


### [273] [ProCTI: Prototype-Refined Global Conditioning for Diffusion-Based Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37632)
*Fariza Rashid,Duc Van Le,Rahat Masood,Gustavo Batista,Aruna Seneviratne,Suranga Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ProCTI，一种通过原型检索全局数据集级先验来增强局部条件的时间序列扩散插补框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的时间序列插补方法通常仅依赖当前或邻接窗口的局部上下文条件，而全局数据集级结构往往隐含未被利用，当局部观测稀疏、含噪或缺乏代表性时性能受限。

Method: ProCTI利用学习到的原型检索全局数据集级先验，并通过混合条件机制在反向扩散过程中将全局上下文与局部信号相融合。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，ProCTI在随机缺失场景下整体优于强基线，在属性级缺失场景下也保持竞争力；同时借助潜在状态数据模型刻画了原型全局条件可证明改善插补的条件，并给出局部-全局条件的一般理论分析。

Conclusion: 全局与局部相结合的条件机制能有效提升扩散式时间序列插补性能，并为相关理论提供了支持。

Abstract: Time series imputation has progressed from statistical and deep learning approaches to diffusion-based models, which have shown strong recent performance. Existing diffusion-based methods typically condition the reverse process using local contextual information from the current or neighbouring windows. Meanwhile, global dataset-level structure often remains implicit, limiting performance when local observations are sparse, noisy, or unrepresentative. To address this issue, we propose ProCTI, a diffusion-imputation framework that augments local conditioning with retrieved global dataset-level priors through learned prototypes. A hybrid conditioning mechanism integrates this global context with local signals during reverse diffusion, enabling more accurate reconstruction under varying missingness scenarios. Experiments across multiple benchmark datasets show that ProCTI outperforms strong baselines overall under random missingness, while remaining competitive under attribute-wise missingness. Furthermore, we use a latent-regime data model to characterise the precise conditions under which prototype-derived global conditioning provably improves imputation. We support this with a general theoretical analysis of local-global conditioning.

</details>


### [274] [RLTL;DR: Self-improvement by Internalizing Self-generated Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37633)
*Michael Kirchhof,Eleonora Gualdoni,Andrew Szot,Khashayar Gatmiry,Aryo Lotfi,Abbas Kazerouni,Omar Attia,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Alexander Toshev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RLTL;DR，让策略在失败后自行撰写 TL;DR 式洞见并基于洞见再试，同时回传洞见以内化"任务→洞见"映射，从而在极难任务上突破 RLVR 的学习瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 的标准做法是多次尝试并优化成功轨迹，但在自我改进场景中任务过难、成功率为零，且没有教师模型或范例可蒸馏，导致训练停滞。

Method: 每次失败后向策略展示验证器输出，让它写出一条 TL;DR 洞见；后续 rollout 以历史洞见为条件，顺序采样直至找到解；并对上下文中的洞见进行反向传播，以将"任务→洞见"直接映射内化。作者进一步简化为 SFTL;DR，仅在 (任务, 洞见) 元组上做监督训练，不展示也不回传 rollout。

Result: 在 Pass@128=0 的工具调用与编程数据集上，Qwen 3.5 9B Thinking 的 GRPO 训练 Pass@1 停滞在 0%–1%；RLTL;DR 训练时带洞见达 14–31%，评估时无洞见仍达 12–13%。仅用 4k 条 (任务, 洞见) 元组训练 SFTL;DR，即可恢复 RLTL;DR 与经典 rollout SFT 的几乎全部性能。

Conclusion: 关键在于"任务→洞见"的内化，而非洞见本身；由此提出一种紧凑训练范式——"遇到这类任务，记住这类要点"，为后续研究提供方向。

Abstract: The common paradigm of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is to let agents make multiple attempts at a task, and optimize towards the successful ones. This becomes problematic in the realms of self-improvement, where tasks are so difficult that the agent has a low or even no chance of success, and where there are no teacher models or example solutions to distill from. In this paper, we introduce RLTL;DR. After each failed attempt, we show the policy the verifier outputs and let it write its own feedback, in the form of a single TL;DR insight. The next rollout is conditioned on all previous insights, and we sequentially sample rollouts until a solution is found. Moreover, we enable backpropagation on the in-context insights to internalize a direct task to insight mapping. On challenging tool-calling and coding datasets (filtered to Pass@128=0), standard GRPO training of a Qwen 3.5 9B Thinking policy stays flat at a Pass@1 of 0% to 1%. RLTL;DR breaks through this learning barrier, achieving a Pass@1 of 14-31% with insights in context during training and, crucially, 12-13% when no insight is in context at eval time. We identify that the key is the task to insight internalization. To study this further, we reduce our approach to SFTL;DR, training only on (task, insight) tuples, without showing or backpropagating on any rollouts. Training on only 4k of these tuples recovers almost the full performance of RLTL;DR and classical SFT on full rollouts. This demonstrates a promising compacted training paradigm of the form "on this sort of task, keep this sort of thing in mind", which we hope to inspire future research on.

</details>


### [275] [RACE: Relation-Level Counterfactual Explanations for Heterogeneous Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37650)
*Yuxiang Yao,Zijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RACE，一种针对异质图 GNN 的关系感知反事实解释方法，通过穷举关系子集给出精确、确定的最小关系删除集，并细化为类型化边集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异质图反事实解释方法将图坍缩为无类型边，无法回答领域专家真正关心的问题：哪个关系类型驱动了该预测。同时软掩码基线结果不稳定。

Method: RACE 对每个被解释实例在关系子集上穷举搜索，返回经过认证的最小关系删除集（或明确报告不存在此类删除）；随后将关系级答案在带属性的关系内细化为类型化边集，并通过单边恢复在离散模型上验证。关系级答案在冻结骨干下精确且确定。

Result: 在 ACM、Cora 衍生图和 ogbn-mag 上，RACE 相比最强基线反事实成功率最高提升 +2.7pp，同时删除更少边，并在每个数据集上取得最高成功率；优势在 ogbn-arXiv 与 DBLP 的四种骨干上可复现，跨种子关系集一致性达 0.89。软掩码基线仅因随机顺序不同，成功率波动 6-8pp。合成实验验证能恢复模型实际依赖的关系，无依赖时报告不可行而非捏造归因。

Conclusion: RACE 提供精确、确定、可信的异质图关系级反事实解释，在多个数据集和骨干上优于基线，并在解释最重要的场景保持可信度。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations of graph neural networks identify edge deletions that flip a prediction.
