On heterogeneous graphs, however, existing methods first collapse the graph into untyped edges, so they cannot answer the question a domain expert actually asks: which relation type drives this prediction? We present RACE (Relation-Aware Counterfactual Explanations), which gives this question an exact, per-instance answer. For every explained instance, an exhaustive search over relation subsets returns the certified minimum relation-deletion set that flips the prediction -- or an explicit report that no such deletion exists; each relation-level answer is then refined into a typed edge set within the attributed relations, verified on the discrete model by single-edge restoration. The relation-level answer is exact and deterministic given the frozen backbone, whereas soft-mask baselines vary by 6-8 pp in success rate across runs differing only in random ordering. On ACM, a Cora-derived graph, and ogbn-mag, RACE improves counterfactual success rate over the strongest baseline by up to +2.7 pp while deleting fewer edges, and attains the highest success rate among all same-task baselines on every dataset; the advantage reproduces across four backbones on ogbn-arXiv and on DBLP, with cross-seed relation-set agreement up to 0.89. A synthetic study with known generating mechanisms confirms that the search recovers the relation the trained model actually relies on -- and reports infeasibility rather than fabricating an attribution when the model has learned none -- so the explanations stay trustworthy exactly where explanations matter.

</details>


### [276] [Nonpreemptive Scheduling While Learning Context-Dependent Service Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37660)
*Wansoo Choi,Seoungbin Bae,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究非抢占式情境队列赌博机，提出LCP算法并证明其队列长度遗憾最优（T≥d²时），并针对未知时域提出估计SEPT算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单服务器系统中，服务结果可学习，但即使已知模型，最优策略也非简单短视规则；最优动作会随剩余时域在同一队列状态下变化，因此需要时域依赖的决策方法。

Method: 提出Learn–Clear–Plan（LCP）：估计系统参数与上下文分布，利用有限时域Bellman递推进行时域依赖决策；针对未知时域，以SEPT（服务离去概率最高的等待任务）为固定参考，提出不需要模型知识的估计SEPT算法。

Result: LCP达到Õ(√(d/T))队列长度遗憾；下界显示任意学习策略在某实例上至少Ω(min{1/√d,√(d/T)})，故T≥d²时最优至多差polylog因子。未知时域下不存在时域无关策略能对有限时域最优取得消失遗憾；估计SEPT的跟踪误差为Õ(√(d/t))。

Conclusion: 非抢占式情境队列赌博机中，时域已知时LCP可达到近似最优队列长度遗憾；时域未知时，对有限时域最优无法实现消失遗憾，只能以SEPT为参考进行跟踪，并可由估计SEPT实现低跟踪误差。

Abstract: We study nonpreemptive contextual queueing bandits in a single-server system. Each job is represented by a $d$-dimensional context vector; in each round, a job may arrive with its context drawn from an unknown distribution $\mathcal{D}$, and its departure probability is determined by a logistic model of that context vector with an unknown parameter $θ^*$. The server learns from service outcomes while deciding which waiting job to serve and whether to idle, aiming to minimize queue-length regret, the gap between its expected terminal queue length and the minimum achievable by an admissible policy. Once selected, a job must be served until completion, and we refer to this as the nonpreemptive setting. A central challenge is that, even with full model knowledge, the optimal policy cannot in general be characterized by a simple myopic rule, since the optimal action can change with the remaining horizon at the same queue state. Nevertheless, when the model and horizon are known, the optimal action can be obtained through a finite-horizon Bellman recursion. Motivated by this, we propose Learn--Clear--Plan (LCP), which estimates the system and uses the resulting Bellman recursion to make horizon-dependent decisions. LCP achieves $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{d/T})$ queue-length regret, while a lower-bound construction gives $Ω(\min\{1/\sqrt{d},\sqrt{d/T}\})$ regret for every learning policy on some instance, establishing optimality up to polylogarithmic factors when $T\ge d^2$. When the horizon is unknown, no horizon-independent policy achieves vanishing regret against the finite-horizon optimum. We therefore use SEPT, the policy that serves a waiting job with the highest probability of departure, as a fixed reference, and suggest an estimated-SEPT algorithm that achieves a tracking error of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{d/t})$ without knowing the model.

</details>


### [277] [Learning Causal Normalizing Flows from Incomplete Data via Observed-Data Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37664)
*Trung-Dung Hoang,Alceu Bissoto,Tim Flühmann,David Herzig,Christos Nakas,Lia Bally,Lisa M. Koch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MissCNF 直接在含缺失数据上训练因果归一化流，通过最大化部分观测样本的边缘似然，并利用因果结构仅对观测集祖先闭包中的缺失变量积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果归一化流假设训练数据完全观测，实际数据常存在缺失，而直接删除行或先填补再拟合的流程会损失信息或引入偏差。

Method: 基于 CNF 自回归分解中的因果结构，只对被观测集祖先闭包内的缺失变量积分消去，其余直接丢弃以减少计算；同时给出恢复真实联合分布的条件，并提出因果族正性，使得在数据集中从未有完整记录时也能进行识别。

Result: 在八个合成因果基准、三种缺失机制、缺失率最高 90% 下，MissCNF 在 24 个非线性 MCAR/MAR 设置中的 23 个取得最低 KL 散度，在所有非线性 MNAR 设置中最优；反事实误差在 20/24 设置中最低。线性 SCM 上，在线性填补表现最好时，MissCNF 仍在 22/24 设置中排名前二。

Conclusion: MissCNF 证明可在不完整数据上直接训练因果归一化流，避免删行或填补流程，并在多种缺失机制和较高缺失率下取得优于常见策略的分布与因果推断表现。

Abstract: Causal Normalizing Flows (CNFs) enable causal inference from observational data given the causal structure, but they assume fully observed training data. We introduce MissCNF, which trains CNFs directly on incomplete data by maximizing the marginal likelihood of each partially observed sample, without discarding rows or constructing a completed dataset. Thanks to the causal structure encoded in the autoregressive factorization of CNFs, only missing variables in the ancestral closure of the observed set are integrated out, while the others are dropped without computation. We further establish the conditions under which MissCNF recovers the true joint distribution, and introduce \emph{causal-family positivity}, where identification is possible even when no record in the dataset is ever complete. We compare MissCNF with two common strategies for handling missing data: listwise deletion and impute-then-fit pipelines. Across eight synthetic causal benchmarks, three missingness mechanisms, and missing rates up to $90\%$, MissCNF achieves the lowest KL divergence in 23 of 24 nonlinear MCAR and MAR settings and in all nonlinear MNAR settings, as well as the lowest counterfactual error in 20 of 24 settings. On linear SCMs, where linear imputation performs best, MissCNF ranks in the top two in 22 of 24 settings.

</details>


### [278] [Where Privacy Belongs: Placement Diagnosis and Certified Selection for Private Counterfactual Explanations on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37667)
*Yuxiang Yao,Zijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PrivCFS，通过"构造式认证"在固定公开候选空间上选择反事实解释，实现图神经网络的纯ε-差分隐私反事实解释，避免读取敏感图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN反事实解释需读取敏感图结构，发布解释会泄露结构。既有两种方案均失败：先对图做隐私化会破坏恰好需要救济的边界节点预测，产生伪翻转；在干净图上解释再扰动发布解释则无法认证，标准启发式的隐含全发布预算高达573–753（Cora）和256（CiteSeer），远超其宣称预算，最坏单条目泄露AUC达1.0。

Method: PrivCFS用"构造式认证"替代"优化式认证"：在固定、数据无关的候选空间上做反事实选择——边干预取自公开先验图，特征干预取自公开模式；其no-op语义使相邻图获得相同输出支持。采用有效性门控、裁剪的效用函数，全局敏感度Δu≤1，经指数机制发布，为完整发布对象提供纯ε-DP，并可在查询间组合，是首个在图上的此类保证。

Result: 隐私噪声是最廉价环节：ε=8时，发布在救济人群上保留94–97%的支持受限非隐私最优，一般人群83–95%；最优边推断审计平均AUC 0.50、最坏对0.59，而启发式最坏条目为1.0；可迁移到15K节点图，有效率0.96。主要成本是公开披露中可测量、单调的价格，可在花费预算前从一张表读出。

Conclusion: 将解释隐私从"记账风险"转变为"可购买的决策"，首次为图上的GNN反事实解释提供纯ε-DP保证，且隐私噪声代价低、可审计性强。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations for graph neural networks (GNNs) find the minimal intervention that flips a node's prediction--but computing one requires reading sensitive graph structure, and releasing it discloses that structure. Both existing placements fail. Privatizing the graph before explaining corrupts the target on exactly the borderline nodes needing recourse, manufacturing spurious flips that flip the privatized graph but not the true one. Explaining on the clean graph and perturbing the released explanation resists certification: re-auditing the standard heuristic shows an implied full-release budget of 573--753 on Cora and 256 on CiteSeer--orders of magnitude beyond its advertised budget--with worst-case single-entry leakage at AUC 1.0. We propose PrivCFS, which replaces certification-by-optimization with certification-by-construction: counterfactual selection over a fixed, data-independent candidate universe--edge interventions from a public prior graph, feature interventions from a public schema--whose no-op semantics give neighboring graphs the same output support. A validity-gated, clipped utility of global sensitivity $Δu \le 1$ released through the exponential mechanism gives pure $\varepsilon$-DP for the complete released object, composable over queries--to our knowledge the first such guarantee on graphs. Privacy noise is the cheapest stage: at $\varepsilon$=8 the release retains 94--97% of its support-restricted non-private optimum on the recourse population and 83--95% on the general one; the optimal edge-inference audit attains AUC 0.50 on average and 0.59 worst-pair, versus the heuristic's worst entry 1.0; and transfers to a 15K-node graph at 0.96 valid rate. The dominant cost is a measurable, monotone price in public disclosure, readable off one table before any budget is spent--turning explanation privacy from an accounting risk into a purchasable decision.

</details>


### [279] [LEMON-ZEST: Evolution-Informed Tokenization for Efficient Protein Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37675)
*Biswajit Banerjee,Claudia Alvarez Carreno,Anton S. Petrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出进化信息驱动的分词方法ZEST及紧凑模型LEMON，以200M参数超越600M-3B模型，证明分词策略可替代大规模参数缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白语言模型遵循NLP的缩放规律，但分词策略的潜力未被充分挖掘。不同于自然语言，蛋白质在序列大幅变化下仍保持结构，而标准分词方法无法捕捉这一特性。

Method: ZEST（Zoned Encoding of Sequence Traits）是一种从多序列比对保守区衍生的进化信息词表，在分词阶段直接嵌入域级生物学先验，平均token长度压缩至4个残基，使1024 token窗口可处理4000残基。基于此构建LEMON（Layered Extraction of Molecular Ordering from Nature），一个200M参数的序列模型，用于远源同源检测，单张H100训练一周。

Result: LEMON以200M参数规模超越600M至3B参数的现有最优模型。

Conclusion: 进化信息驱动的分词可替代大规模参数缩放，为高效、生物学接地的蛋白表示学习开辟新方向。代码、权重与结果均以MIT许可公开。

Abstract: Protein Language Models (PLMs) have made remarkable progress following scaling laws established in natural language processing across sequence- and structure-based tasks, yet the potential of tokenization remains underexploited. Unlike human language, proteins preserve structure despite extensive sequence variation a property standard tokenization strategies fundamentally fail to capture. We introduce ZEST (Zoned Encoding of Sequence Traits), an evolution-informed vocabulary derived from conserved regions of multiple sequence alignments. ZEST allows embedding domain-level biological priors directly at the tokenization stage rather than learning them implicitly through scale. ZEST natively compresses sequences to an average token length of 4 residues, enabling our model to process 4,000 residues within a standard 1024-token context window. Building on this, we present LEMON (Layered Extraction of Molecular Ordering from Nature), a compact 200M-parameter sequence-based model for detection of remote homology between protein sequences trained on a single H100 GPU for one week. Despite its modest size, LEMON outperforms state-of-the-art models ranging from 600M to 3B parameters. Our results demonstrate that evolution-informed tokenization can substitute for massive parameter scaling, opening a new direction for efficient, biologically-grounded protein representation learning. All code, model weights, and results are publicly available under the MIT license.

</details>


### [280] [When Models Don't Manipulate Manifolds: The Geometry of a Comparison Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37680)
*Sai Sumedh R. Hindupur,Hadas Orgad,Thomas Fel,Demba Ba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct在数字比较任务中的计算几何，发现尽管数字表示存在曲线结构，模型主要依赖线性表征，通过注意力与残差流将两个数字相加到共享空间，再由MLP神经元在局部区间上进行比较以输出最大数的位置；该机制在三个数字比较时依然成立，表明流形假设与线性表征可以共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性研究的一个前提是：神经网络表征的几何结构能揭示模型如何计算以及如何干预。已有文献观察到数字编码在螺旋、星期编码在圆等低维流形上，但模型是否真的依赖这些结构进行计算、以及如何操作它们仍不清楚。本文希望以数字比较任务作为决策比较的抽象，明确回答模型在计算中到底如何使用几何结构。

Method: 作者研究Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct在数字比较任务中的"因果几何"。通过因果干预/分析手段，追踪模型在比较两个数字时的信息流动：每个数字先被编码为向量，经注意力机制与残差连接相加，进入残差流的共享空间；随后MLP神经元在该共享空间的局部区域（对应较小数字区间）上进行比较，并组合结果来确定最大值的位置。作者还将分析扩展到三个数字的比较任务。

Result: 模型在很大程度上使用数字的线性表征，尽管表示中存在曲线几何。比较过程可分解为：编码—相加—局部比较—组合定位最大值。共享空间中的MLP神经元按局部区域划分输入数字区间，实现成对比较。该依赖线性表征的机制在比较三个数字时仍然持续存在。

Conclusion: 流形假设与线性表征可以共存：有序概念在表征中可能呈现流形结构，但在某些具体计算中模型会利用该概念底层的线性结构。这一发现为理解模型如何利用几何进行计算、以及如何进行有效干预提供了精确的案例。

Abstract: One of the current premises of mechanistic interpretability research is that detailed accounts of the geometry of neural network representations can tell us how models perform computations, and how to effectively intervene on them. While low dimensional manifolds have been observed for multiple concepts in the literature (e.g. numbers encoded on helices, days of the week on a circle, ...), with structure believed to reflect properties of data and tasks, the extent to which models rely on them for computation, and how they manipulate them, remains unclear. We characterize precisely the geometry of computation in a number-comparison task, as an abstraction of comparison for decision making, and how models utilize geometry in an elegant fashion to implement it. Specifically, we study the causal geometry of number comparison in Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, a capable and widely studied open-weight model, and find Qwen largely uses linear representations of numbers despite the presence of curved geometry. To compare two numbers, the model first encodes each number along a vector and adds the two representations using attention and the residual connection, bringing them into a shared space in the residual stream. Then, the model uses MLP neurons to compare the pair of numbers on local regions in this shared space, which correspond to smaller intervals of input numbers, and combines these to obtain the position of the maximum. In fact, this reliance on linear representations for comparison also persists when the model compares three numbers. Our findings demonstrate that the manifold hypothesis can co-exist with linear representations: while concepts that are ordered may have manifold structure in representations, the model may use an underlying linear structure of the concept in certain computations.

</details>


### [281] [GARDiff: Graph-Aligned Residual Diffusion for Probabilistic Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37694)
*Rui Han,Min Yang,Xu Zhang,Xinghao Yang,Wei Liu,Yongshun Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GARDiff框架，通过不确定性感知结构细化与时步感知边稀疏化，动态调整确定性导出的依赖图以适配残差扩散，提升概率多元时间序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解耦扩散框架通常将确定性预测得到的依赖图直接作为残差生成的固定条件，但作者发现这种直接结构迁移不可靠：确定性导出的图虽包含有用的全局依赖先验，却与残差依赖结构存在明显的边级错位，从而引入不准确或冗余的条件。这揭示了被忽视的"确定性到残差结构对齐"问题。

Method: 提出GARDiff（Graph-Aligned Residual Diffusion）框架。不再把确定性导出的图当作固定扩散条件，而是让其逐步适配残差生成：估计残差不确定性以区分高/低不确定性区域，实现不确定性感知的结构细化；并在反向扩散过程中执行时步感知的边稀疏化，使图条件从广泛的依赖聚合逐步演化为局部化的残差细化。

Result: 在六个真实世界基准数据集上的大量实验表明，GARDiff在概率预测性能和不确定性校准方面持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 确定性到残差的结构对齐是解耦扩散预测中的关键问题，通过让依赖图随残差扩散过程动态演进（不确定性感知细化+时步感知稀疏化），可有效提升概率多元时间序列预测的准确性与校准性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently shown strong potential for probabilistic multivariate time-series forecasting by modeling complex conditional distributions. Recent decoupled diffusion frameworks further separate forecasting into deterministic prediction and stochastic residual generation, making it natural to derive dependency graphs from deterministic representations and use them to guide residual diffusion. However, we show that this direct structural transfer is unreliable. Although deterministic-derived graphs encode useful global dependency priors, they exhibit substantial edge-level misalignment with residual dependency structures, introducing inaccurate or redundant conditions during residual generation. This reveals a previously overlooked deterministic-to-residual structural alignment problem in decoupled diffusion forecasting. To address this problem, we propose GARDiff, a Graph-Aligned Residual Diffusion framework for probabilistic multivariate time-series forecasting. Instead of treating deterministic-derived graphs as fixed diffusion conditions, GARDiff progressively adapts them to residual generation. Specifically, GARDiff estimates residual uncertainty to distinguish high- and low-uncertainty regions, enabling uncertainty-aware structural refinement, and further performs timestep-aware edge sparsification during reverse diffusion to evolve graph conditions from broad dependency aggregation to localized residual refinement. Extensive experiments on six real-world benchmarks demonstrate that GARDiff consistently improves probabilistic forecasting performance and uncertainty calibration over strong baselines.

</details>


### [282] [Width Expansion as a Method for Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37702)
*A. L. S. Conde,Y. Elkhatib,C. M. Ranieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需任务标识的动态宽度扩展方法：当网络根据归一化损失准则判断表示能力不足时，在现有层内增加神经元，并结合带持久键值记忆的注意力机制，在类增量学习（Class-IL）中缓解灾难性遗忘，实验证明其优于固定容量架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类增量学习要求模型在不访问旧数据、也不获知任务身份的条件下持续学习新类并保留旧知识，这加剧了稳定性-可塑性困境，导致灾难性遗忘。已有方法（正则化、知识蒸馏、回放、结构扩展）中，许多扩展方法依赖显式任务标识或预设增长策略，在推理时无任务边界信息的情况下适用性受限。因此，本文希望设计一种不依赖任务特定信息的动态扩展机制。

Method: 提出按归一化损失准则在现有层内动态增加神经元数量的宽度扩展方法，无需任务特定信息；并引入带持久键值记忆的注意力机制，以稳定特征表示、减少旧类与新类之间的干扰。在标准 Class-IL 协议下于 Split MNIST 与 Split CIFAR-100 上进行评测，将固定容量与动态扩展架构（各含有无注意力两种情形）与 EWC、LwF、A-GEM 等持续学习方法结合对比。

Result: 渐进式宽度扩展相比固定架构持续提升性能，尤其在与功能性方法和 A-GEM 结合时更为明显；宽度扩展与注意力的组合带来的增益最为稳定一致。

Conclusion: 基于表示需求的动态宽度扩展是类增量学习中一种有效且灵活的策略，但不受控制的增长可能加剧过拟合并增加计算开销。

Abstract: Class Incremental Learning (Class-IL) requires models to learn new classes over time while preserving previously acquired knowledge without access to past data or task identity. This setting intensifies the stability-plasticity dilemma and makes catastrophic forgetting a central challenge. Existing approaches include regularization, knowledge distillation, replay, and architectural expansion. However, many expansion methods rely on explicit task identifiers or predefined growth strategies, limiting their applicability when task boundaries are unavailable at inference time. This work proposes a dynamic width expansion method that increases the number of neurons within existing layers according to a normalized loss criterion, without requiring task-specific information. An attention mechanism with persistent key-value memory is also incorporated to stabilize feature representations and reduce interference between previously learned and newly introduced classes. The approach is evaluated on Split MNIST and Split CIFAR-100 under the standard Class-IL protocol. Experiments compare fixed-capacity and dynamically expanding architectures, both with and without attention, combined with established continual learning methods including EWC, LwF, and A-GEM. Results show that progressive width expansion consistently improves performance over fixed architectures, particularly when combined with functional methods and A-GEM. The combination of width expansion and attention provides the most consistent gains. Overall, dynamic width expansion based on representational demand provides an effective and flexible strategy for Class-IL, although uncontrolled growth may increase overfitting and computational cost.

</details>


### [283] [Volatility-Clustering Adaptation for Financial Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37715)
*Manh Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Van Dai Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出时序基础模型"目标数据越多预测越好"的假设在金融预测中会失效，并提出波动聚集适应（VCA）方法，在下一词元交叉熵之外加入对平方收益自相关的可微惩罚，从而在金融领域自适应中取得更优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型普遍通过在目标数据上微调来适应新领域，隐含假设是目标数据越多、预测越好；但在金融预测中，单个价格变化难以预测，而大幅波动往往成簇出现，形成平静期与动荡期交替。这种波动聚集特性使得单纯依赖下一词元预测的训练信号不足以支撑有效的金融领域自适应。

Method: 作者使用基于开盘、最高、最低、收盘价与成交量（OHLCV）价格柱训练的金融基础模型，提出波动聚集适应（VCA）。该方法在下一词元交叉熵损失之外，增加一个对平方收益自相关的可微惩罚项，用以刻画波动聚集这一标准统计特征。该附加目标通过将自回归多步展开的依赖结构匹配到真实未来数据的依赖结构，提供了一种多步训练信号。

Result: 在三个资产集和两种评估设定下，VCA相较于预训练模型均改善了自适应效果，其中在主要评估设定（fore）下提升最为显著，且增益主要来源于方差误差的降低。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，有效的金融领域自适应需要能够捕捉领域特定时间结构的目标函数，而不能仅停留在词元级别的预测上。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models are increasingly adapted to new domains through fine-tuning on target data, under the implicit assumption that more target data yields better forecasts. We show that this assumption can fail in financial forecasting, where individual price changes are difficult to predict, but large moves tend to cluster, creating alternating calm and turbulent periods. Using financial foundation models trained on price bars of open, high, low, close, and volume, we argue that adapting to financial domains requires training signals beyond next-token prediction. We introduce Volatility-Clustering Adaptation (VCA), which augments next-token cross-entropy with a differentiable penalty on the autocorrelation of squared returns, the standard statistical signature of volatility clustering. This additional objective provides a multi-step training signal by matching the resulting dependence structure of autoregressive rollouts to those of the realized future. Across three asset sets and two evaluation conventions, VCA improves adaptation over the pre-trained model, with the strongest gains under the primary evaluation (\textsc{fore}), driven primarily by reduced variance error. Overall, our results suggest that effective financial adaptation requires objectives that capture domain-specific temporal structure beyond token-level prediction.

</details>


### [284] [Predictive Geometry of Hidden Trajectories in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37717)
*Timur Mudarisov,Mikhail Burtsev,Tatiana Petrova,Radu State*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究仅用终端下一词预测损失训练的decoder-only transformer中，中间隐状态的局部二阶几何由pullback Fisher算子主导，可识别输出敏感方向与近零预测方向，并据此构造token级曲率分数，用于扰动敏感性预测、token剪枝与低秩蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: decoder-only transformer仅通过终端next-token prediction loss训练，但该损失通过固定的下游计算约束了每个中间隐状态，这种约束的作用机制尚缺乏形式化理解。作者希望从层级loss-to-go函数的二阶几何出发，揭示隐状态空间中哪些方向对输出真正敏感，从而为压缩和蒸馏提供可测量、可利用的结构化信号。

Method: 定义layerwise loss-to-go函数：将候选隐状态经剩余transformer块继续前传所得的终端损失。在成功validation轨迹附近证明其局部二阶几何在低损失残差项意义上由隐状态空间上的pullback Fisher算子支配。其谱可区分output-sensitive方向与近似prediction-null方向，形成残差流的局部可观测子空间。对causal transformer，该几何诱导tokenwise curvature score，即目标logits对每个token隐状态扰动的Fisher加权敏感度，该分数在目标因果祖先集之外消失，并受下游Jacobian耦合控制。使用matrix-free JVP与VJP估计这些量，并在WikiText、OpenWebText、FineWeb上跨decoder-only语言模型评估。

Result: 该几何能预测扰动敏感性，支持非均匀层级秩分配，产生有竞争力的结构化token剪枝信号，并在reverse KL、skew KL等较强自回归蒸馏目标上附加后提升低秩student恢复效果。

Conclusion: 结果支持transformer计算的预测-几何视角：在成功轨迹附近，终端损失诱导出一组稀薄、各向异性的输出相关隐状态方向，这些方向可被测量并用于压缩与蒸馏。

Abstract: Decoder-only transformers are trained only through a terminal next-token prediction loss, yet this loss constrains every intermediate hidden state through the fixed downstream computation. We formalize this constraint by studying layerwise loss-to-go functions: the terminal loss obtained by continuing a candidate hidden state through the remaining transformer blocks. Around successful validation trajectories, we show that the local second-order geometry of these functions is governed, up to low-loss residual terms, by a pullback Fisher operator on hidden-state space. Its spectrum identifies output-sensitive directions and approximately prediction-null directions, yielding a local observable subspace of the residual stream. For causal transformers, the same geometry induces a tokenwise curvature score: a Fisher-weighted sensitivity of the target logits to perturbations of each token's hidden state. This score vanishes outside the causal ancestor set of the target and is controlled by downstream Jacobian couplings, making it a loss-aware alternative to attention magnitude. We estimate these quantities using matrix-free Jacobian-vector and vector-Jacobian products and evaluate them across decoder-only language models on WikiText, OpenWebText, and FineWeb. Empirically, the induced geometry predicts perturbation sensitivity, supports nonuniform layerwise rank allocation, yields competitive structured token-pruning signals, and improves low-rank student recovery when added to stronger autoregressive distillation objectives such as reverse KL and skew KL. These results support a predictive-geometric view of transformer computation: near successful trajectories, the terminal loss induces a thin, anisotropic set of output-relevant hidden-state directions that can be measured and exploited for compression and distillation.

</details>


### [285] [Weights Read and Write Features: Scalable Parameter Decomposition Grounded in Activation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37731)
*Tue M. Cao,Lisiane Pruinelli,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Activation-Supported Parameter Decomposition (ASPD)，联合分解激活空间与参数空间，将权重组件锚定到其读写的激活特征，实现可扩展、可解释、可因果编辑的参数分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法大多将激活空间与参数空间分开研究，导致"被表示的信息"与"参数层面的计算"之间的联系未被充分探索。激活表示信息，权重负责读取、变换和写入信息，二者需要联合建模。

Method: ASPD 同时分解激活空间和参数空间，并将每个学到的权重组件锚定到它所读取或写入的激活特征。该锚定利用模型内部激活约束原本非唯一的参数分解；同时通过一个内部重建目标，在分析的权重矩阵处提供局部学习信号。

Result: 在预训练大语言模型 Qwen-3-8B 上验证，实现了可扩展、可解释且可因果编辑的参数分解；学到的读写组件可组合为参数级机制回路。利用 ASPD 成功恢复了经典 IOI 回路背后的机制，并追踪了通过模型权重的语义变换。

Conclusion: ASPD 架起了激活空间与参数空间之间的桥梁，能够对预训练大模型进行参数级机制分析与因果编辑，为机制可解释性提供了统一且可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Activation space and parameter space provide complementary views of model computation. Activations represent information, while weights read, transform, and write that information. Yet existing interpretability methods largely study the two spaces separately, leaving the connection between represented information and parameter-level computation underexplored. We introduce Activation-Supported Parameter Decomposition (ASPD), which jointly decomposes activation and parameter spaces and grounds each learned weight component in the activation features it reads or writes. This grounding constrains otherwise non-unique parameter decompositions using the model's internal activations, while an internal reconstruction objective provides a local learning signal at the weight matrix being analyzed. Together, these properties enable scalable, interpretable, and causally editable parameter decomposition in pretrained large language models, demonstrated on Qwen-3-8B. The learned read--write components can also be composed into parameter-level mechanism circuits. We use ASPD to recover mechanisms underlying the classic IOI circuit and trace semantic transformations through model weights.

</details>


### [286] [HyDI: A hybrid Deep Learning-Inductive Logic Programming ensemble for multi-label classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37740)
*Simon Flügel,Till Mossakowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyDI是一种结合深度学习与归纳逻辑编程的混合集成架构，用于层次多标签分类，可提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型虽在诸多应用中表现优异，但难以解释。该工作旨在提升层次多标签分类的可解释性。

Method: HyDI将深度学习模型与由归纳逻辑编程（ILP）生成的基于规则的分类器相结合。对于标签层次中的叶子类别，用基于规则的分类器替代深度学习模型。将HyDI应用于ChEBI本体，为314个类别生成ILP规则。

Result: HyDI在ChEBI本体上为314个类别生成了ILP规则，并能针对这些类别生成全局解释以及结合视觉与文本描述的局部解释。

Conclusion: HyDI通过混合集成架构在保持分类能力的同时提升了透明度和可解释性。

Abstract: While attaining remarkable results for many applications, Deep Learning models are notoriously difficult to explain. This work introduces HyDI, a hybrid ensemble architecture for hierarchical multi-label classification. It combines a Deep Learning (DL) model with rule-based classifiers generated by Inductive Logic Programming (ILP). For leaf classes of the label hierarchy, the rule-based classifiers replace the DL model, leading to more transparent classification results. HyDI is applied to the Chemical Entities of Biological Interest (ChEBI) ontology, providing ILP-generated rules for 314 classes. For these classes, HyDI can generate global explanations as well as local explanations that combine visual and text-based descriptions.

</details>


### [287] [Optimizer-dependent training dynamics converge to the same one-third optimal data scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37745)
*Hyunseok Lee,Mihir Basil,Yizhou Liu,Jeff Gore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文区分了动态指数（单次训练中损失随步数下降的速度，依赖优化器）与最优数据指数（最优损失随数据集规模D下降的速度，跨优化器收敛到1/3）；通过分解损失并给出约束关系2α_r+α_t=1，解释了为何调优后各优化器均满足D^{-1/3}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律（损失随训练按幂律下降）对大语言模型至关重要，近期有研究提出1/3指数来自学习尖峰分布，但该解释仅针对SGD，而实践中模型多用自适应优化器训练，因此需要厘清优化器对缩放指数的影响。

Method: 在在线教师-学生模型中，将损失分解为范数增长（径向）和向教师方向对齐（切向）两部分，分别以动态指数α_r和α_t描述其幂律衰减；对比SGD与Adam，并进行随机动力学分析，推导跨优化器的动态指数关系。

Result: SGD下α_r与α_t均接近1/3，故数据指数为1/3（与学习率无关）；Adam下两者分离，α_r≈0.48、α_t≈0.08，导致最优学习率依赖优化器（SGD下与D无关，Adam下随D下降）。但调至最优后两者损失均回到D^{-1/3}。动力学分析给出统一关系2α_r+α_t=1，将最优数据指数固定为1/3；在包括Muon在内的七种优化器上测量结果与该关系及D^{-1/3}包络一致。

Conclusion: 优化器决定模型每步学习的速度，但经最优调优后，只改变缩放的前置系数，不改变损失随样本数下降的速率（均为1/3）。

Abstract: Neural scaling, in which loss falls as a power law with training, is central to large language models, and one recent proposal is that a $1/3$ exponent emerges from learning peaked distributions. That account describes SGD, but models in practice are trained with adaptive optimizers. Here we separate two exponents the $1/3$ account does not distinguish: how fast the loss falls with training steps along a single run, and how fast the optimally tuned loss falls with dataset size $D$. We show that the first, a dynamic exponent, is optimizer-specific while the second, an optimal data exponent, converges to $1/3$ across optimizers. In an online teacher-student model we decompose the loss into norm growth (radial) and alignment toward the teacher direction (tangential), each decaying as a power law with dynamic exponents $α_{r}$ and $α_{t}$. Under SGD, both are close to $1/3$, so the data exponent is also $1/3$ across different learning rates. Under Adam the two separate: $α_{r} \simeq 0.48$ but $α_{t} \simeq 0.08$. Since the total loss is minimized when these two parts are balanced, the optimal learning rate is optimizer-dependent: $D$-independent for SGD but falls with $D$ for Adam. Yet tuned to that optimum, the loss returns to $D^{-1/3}$ for both. A stochastic-dynamics analysis explains why: the optimizers can trade decay speed between the two channels, but they all fall on a single dynamic exponent relation, $2α_{r}+ α_{t} = 1$, which fixes the optimal data exponent at $1/3$. Across seven optimizers, including Muon, the measured exponents are consistent with this relation, and the optimal-loss envelopes agree with $D^{-1/3}$ across them. The optimizer sets how fast a model learns per step; tuned optimally, it changes the prefactor but not the rate at which loss falls per sample.

</details>


### [288] [Predictive Self-Supervised Learning Provably Identifies Stochastic Signals under Nuisance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37789)
*Fabian A. Mikulasch,Friedemann Zenke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上证明常见自监督学习方法能区分"预测相关的随机潜在信号"与"无关噪声"，并揭示其依赖的两大原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习在潜空间预测中能学到抽象有用的表示，通常被归因于其丢弃无关信息的能力。但存在一个困惑：观测中的随机变化既可能来自预测相关的潜在随机信号，也可能来自真正的噪声，两者都使观测部分不可预测，按理难以区分。论文旨在解释自监督方法究竟如何在两者之间做出区分。

Method: 作者通过理论证明展开分析，表明常见的自监督学习方法隐式地实例化了一个带有随机动态与观测私有噪声的潜变量模型，并据此推导其可识别性。具体归纳出两条互补原理：预测互信息最大化保证表示保留预测所需信息；潜在分布匹配约束信息的编码方式，从而使保留的信号可被识别。

Result: 理论证明自监督方法确实能够区分预测相关的随机信号与真实噪声。在高斯预测器的模拟实验中，即便在动态且充满噪声的环境下，方法也能恢复到真实信号（仅相差一个仿射变换），验证了可识别性结论。

Conclusion: 自监督学习的成功不仅在于丢弃无关信息，更在于通过预测互信息最大化与潜在分布匹配的互补作用，实现对预测相关随机信号的可识别恢复，为理解其表示学习机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) by predicting in latent space, without generating the input data itself, learns highly abstract, useful representations. Intuitively, this success is often attributed to its ability to discard nuisance information that is irrelevant to prediction. However, this poses a conundrum: both stochastic variation in a prediction-relevant latent signal and true nuisance make observations partly unpredictable; how could they be distinguished? Surprisingly, we prove that common SSL methods can achieve exactly this, by implicitly instantiating a latent-variable model with stochastic dynamics and observation-private nuisance. We trace their ability to recover the stochastic signal to two complementary principles: Predictive mutual information maximization ensures that representations retain the information needed for prediction, while latent distribution matching constrains how this information is encoded, thereby making the retained signal identifiable. We confirm this identifiability result in simulations for Gaussian predictors, which recover the true signal up to an affine transformation even in dynamic, nuisance-laden environments.

</details>


### [289] [Challenges and Solutions for Bandits in the Wild: Warm-Started Mixture Bandits for Cross-Cohort Slate Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37800)
*Serafima Lebedeva,Sumantrak Mukherjee,Ali Arshad Sadal,Ilias Ekşi,Rahul Sharma,Julia Mueller,Theresa Dombrowski,Jakob Karolus,Viktor Bengs,Eyke Hüllermeier,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CohortMix-TS，一种基于跨批次迁移的混合老虎机方法，用元数据构建新用户的组先验，并结合多样性与会话库存控制，缓解重复冷启动场景下的推荐难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐服务反复面临冷启动用户群：新用户交互历史极少，需快速学习偏好；同时每个用户可用物品有限，推荐易重复或枯竭。既有方法难以同时解决快速个性化与库存耗竭问题。

Method: CohortMix-TS为热启动混合老虎机：从早期批次学习潜在用户组，利用元数据为新用户构造组信息先验；以固定先验为起点，随用户反馈逐步独立个性化。会话列表（slate）将Thompson采样与多样性及库存耗竭控制相结合。通过模拟、半合成实验和25天随机实地部署（Campus Games问答应用，713名注册参与者）进行评估。

Result: 评估显示跨批次迁移提升了早期推荐质量并降低用户级regret；库存感知的列表构建有助于防止偏好物品过早耗尽。实地部署中，处理组用户正确率的早晚期变化大于随机推荐组。

Conclusion: 结果表明，热启动迁移与库存感知推荐能有效支持短生命周期、反复冷启动用户群的个性化服务。

Abstract: Many recommender services repeatedly encounter cold-start cohorts, where new users arrive with little or no interaction history. This creates two challenges: learning user preferences quickly from limited feedback and sustaining useful recommendations when each user has a finite catalog that can become repetitive or depleted over time. We propose CohortMix-TS, a warm-started mixture bandit that learns latent user groups from earlier cohorts and uses available metadata to construct group-informed priors for new users. Starting from these fixed priors, the model personalizes independently as feedback from each user becomes available. Session slates combine Thompson sampling with diversity and inventory-depletion controls. We evaluate CohortMix-TS through simulation, semi-synthetic experiments, and a 25-day randomized in-the-wild deployment with 713 registered participants in a Campus Games quiz application. Our evaluations show that cross-cohort transfer improves early recommendation quality and user-level regret, while inventory-aware slate construction helps prevent premature exhaustion of preferred items. In the field deployment, treatment users also showed a larger early-to-late change in correctness than users receiving random recommendations. Together, these results show how warm-start transfer and inventory-aware recommendations can support personalization for short-lived, repeatedly cold-starting cohorts.

</details>


### [290] [Feedback-Calibrated Protein Optimization with Batch-Aligned Tail Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37808)
*Zefeng Lin,Xianyong Fang,Tianfan Fu,Xiaohua Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BATA 方法，通过实验反馈自适应地融合先验信息与任务特定排序，并将校准聚焦于与批次对齐的高适应度区域，在 GB1、PABP、TrpB 数据集上取得最佳平均任务排名（1.67）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质优化方法依赖任务特定预测器、生物学先验或排序感知目标来选择下一轮实验变体，但无法适应随测量积累而变化的预测证据可靠性，也难以保证关键高适应度候选的正确排序。

Method: 提出 Batch-Aligned Tail Arbitration（BATA），利用实验反馈自适应地结合先验驱动与任务特定排序，用于下一批次选择，并将校准集中在与批次对齐的高适应度区域。

Result: 在已测量的 GB1、PABP 和 TrpB 适应度图谱上，BATA 在 480 次测量后的最终最佳适应度上取得最佳平均任务排名（1.67）；对照实验进一步显示高适应度校准和批次对齐带来任务依赖性的增益。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了反馈校准的预测器仲裁机制，即由实验反馈动态决定预测证据如何指导下一批次选择，为蛋白质优化开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Protein optimization aims to discover high-fitness sequences under a limited experimental budget. Existing machine-learning methods use task-specific predictors, biological priors, or ranking-aware objectives to guide which variants are tested in the next experimental round. However, these methods cannot adapt to shifts in the reliability of predictive evidence as measurements accumulate and ensure the correct ranking of key high-fitness candidates. To address these challenges, we propose Batch-Aligned Tail Arbitration (BATA), which uses experimental feedback to adaptively combine prior-informed and task-specific rankings for next-batch selection, with calibration focused on the batch-aligned high-fitness region. Across measured GB1, PABP, and TrpB landscapes, BATA achieves the best mean task rank (1.67) in final best fitness after 480 measurements. Controlled comparisons further show task-dependent gains from high-fitness calibration and batch alignment. Our work introduces feedback-calibrated predictor arbitration, where experimental feedback dynamically determines how predictive evidence guides next-batch selection, opening a new direction for protein optimization.

</details>


### [291] [Privy to the Foil: Recasting Value Estimation with a Self-Privileged Critic for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37825)
*Kun Liang,Chenming Tang,Clive Bai,Weijie Liu,Zeyuan Liu,Qingyang Zhang,Saiyong Yang,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 πPPO，一种自特权 actor-critic 框架，通过复用同提示的已验证 rollout 作为对比证据来改进 LLM 多步推理中的价值估计与信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏终端奖励下训练 LLM 多步推理时，信用分配是核心难题。PPO 等 actor-critic 方法依赖价值函数构造 token 级优势，但其效果取决于可靠的价值估计，而 critic 需同时评估推理进展并预测策略的未来行为，任一出错都会破坏信用分配并使在线训练不稳定。

Method: 论文重新审视仅基于状态的价值估计标准范式，提出 πPPO：一种自特权 actor-critic 框架。它复用已验证的同提示 rollout 作为对比证据，帮助 critic 将中间推理步骤与成功和失败尝试进行对照评估，同时保持标准策略优化流程与部署接口不变。

Result: 实验显示 πPPO 显著提升价值估计质量，在具有挑战性的数学推理基准上优于代表性的 actor-critic 与无 critic 的 RLVR 基线，并且在搭配显著更小的非对称 critic 时依然有效。

Conclusion: 通过引入同提示成功/失败 rollout 作为对比证据的自特权价值估计，πPPO 缓解了稀疏奖励下的信用分配与训练不稳定问题，在保持部署兼容性的同时提升了推理性能，并支持更小 critic 的高效配置。

Abstract: Assigning credit to intermediate steps remains a central challenge in training Large Language Models (LLMs) on multi-step reasoning tasks with sparse terminal rewards, and actor-critic methods such as PPO address this by learning value functions to construct token-level advantages. Their effectiveness, however, hinges on reliable value estimation, a difficult task requiring the critic to both assess progress toward a correct solution and anticipate an evolving policy's future behavior; errors in either can compromise credit assignment and destabilize online training. In this paper, we revisit the standard state-only formulation of value estimation and propose $π$PPO, a self-privileged actor-critic framework. By reusing verified same-prompt rollouts as contrastive evidence, $π$PPO helps the critic assess intermediate reasoning against successful and failed attempts, while preserving standard policy optimization and the deployment interface. Experiments show that $π$PPO consistently improves value-estimation quality by a substantial margin and outperforms representative actor-critic and critic-free RLVR baselines on challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, while remaining effective even when paired with substantially smaller asymmetric critics.

</details>


### [292] [Behavioral Convergence Without Representational Convergence: Persistent Training-History Dependence in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37836)
*Ertuğrul Mutlu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 配对卷积网络实验表明，即使行为表现趋同，训练历史仍会在内部表征中留下可测量的痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络在达到相似预测性能时，早期训练历史是否仍持续影响内部表征，即行为收敛是否意味着表征收敛。

Method: 采用受控顺序训练实验：配对卷积网络从相同权重出发，经历相反任务顺序，再进行相同的确定性共同松弛（common relaxation）；通过表征历史评分、预测分歧、线性探针、旋转MNIST对照及ReLU-LeakyReLU对照进行测量。

Result: 20对MNIST中16对满足行为匹配，但表征历史评分均值0.139（CI 0.127-0.153），约3.1%预测分歧。延长至50000次更新后，历史评分仍为0.190，而精度差距仅0.18个百分点。线性探针显示类别信息实际等价；旋转MNIST对照复现效应（评分0.162）；匹配学习率的激活对照使表征残留平均减少0.040。

Conclusion: 行为收敛并不必然意味着表征收敛，优化历史在长期共同训练后仍可留下可测量的内部痕迹，且激活介导的可塑性可能参与该效应的持续。

Abstract: Neural networks trained toward the same final objective can reach similar predictive performance while retaining internal representations shaped by earlier training history. We study this effect using controlled sequential-training experiments in which paired convolutional networks start from identical weights, experience reversed task orders, and then receive the same deterministic common-relaxation distribution. Across 20 paired MNIST runs, 16 satisfy a predeclared behavioral-matching criterion, yet their matched representations retain a mean history score of 0.139 (95% bootstrap CI: 0.127-0.153) and approximately 3.1% prediction disagreement. Extending common relaxation to 50,000 optimizer updates does not erase the measured difference: across five paired seeds, the representation-history score remains 0.190 (95% bootstrap CI: 0.161-0.219) at the end of the measured horizon while the mean accuracy gap is only 0.18 percentage points. Fresh linear probes show that, with sufficient labeled data, the two histories retain practically equivalent linearly accessible class information. A same-label rotated-MNIST control reproduces the effect: all five paired seeds reach behavioral matching while retaining a mean representation-history score of 0.162. Finally, a matched-learning-rate ReLU-LeakyReLU control reduces the 50,000-update representation residue by 0.040 on average in all five paired seeds, providing directional evidence that activation-mediated plasticity contributes to the persistence of training-history effects. These results provide protocol-scoped evidence that behavioral convergence need not imply representational convergence and that optimization history can leave measurable internal traces after prolonged common training.

</details>


### [293] [Counterfactual Probing for Parallel Unmasking with Hidden Forest Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37841)
*Ryotaro Kawata,Satoshi Hayakawa,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究掩码生成模型在隐藏森林结构离散分布下的并行采样问题，提出一种总评估量与序列长度N呈亚线性关系的采样器，并证明同类不可逆提交采样器的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行词元预测需要处理词元间的依赖关系，但依赖未知时寻找安全批次本身也消耗模型评估。作者希望探究包含依赖发现在内的总评估量能否达到亚线性，从而降低串行深度。

Method: 在隐藏森林结构、通过固定近似条件预言机访问的离散分布设定下，在显式正则性条件与均匀Hellinger误差界下设计采样器；通过跨依赖检验共享"假设性揭示"的评估，识别安全并行批次，无需完整恢复隐藏森林；并用可调参数在探测成本与不可逆提交轮数间权衡。

Result: 对任意固定目标精度ε∈(0,1/8]及足够大的N，采样器实现种子平均总变差误差至多ε，掩码状态提交数与串行深度均为O(N^C ε^a)，其中0<C<1、a>0；同时证明同类任何可容许不可逆乘积提交采样器在最坏情况下需Ω(N^c ε^b)次反事实提交或提交轮数。

Conclusion: 在隐藏森林依赖结构下，包含依赖发现的总评估量可实现相对序列长度的亚线性，串行深度亦随之亚线性；且该上界在同类不可逆提交采样器中具有匹配的下界支撑，表明该成本权衡本质存在。

Abstract: Masked generative models offer parallel token prediction, but accurate parallel sampling must account for dependencies among tokens. When dependencies are unknown, finding safe batches also costs model evaluations. We study whether total evaluations, including discovery, can be sublinear in sequence length $N$; sublinear sequential depth then follows. We consider discrete distributions with hidden forest structure, accessed through a fixed approximate conditional oracle. Under explicit regularity conditions and uniform Hellinger error bounds, for any fixed target accuracy $\varepsilon\in (0,1/8]$ and sufficiently large $N$, our sampler achieves seed-averaged total-variation error at most $\varepsilon$, with total masked-state submissions and sequential depth both bounded by $O(N^C \varepsilon^a)$ for constants $0 <C <1$ and $a > 0$. These guarantees use polynomial vocabulary size and an edge-response lower bound set by $N$ and $\varepsilon$. The sampler shares evaluations of hypothetical reveals across dependence tests to identify safe parallel batches without requiring full recovery of the hidden forest. A tunable parameter trades probing cost against irreversible commit rounds. In the same class, any admissible irreversible product-commit sampler attaining the same seed-averaged accuracy requires $Ω(N^c \varepsilon^b)$ counterfactual submissions or commit rounds in the worst case, for constants $c,b>0$.

</details>


### [294] [Scaling Influence Functions in LLMs through Eigenbasis-Corrected One-Bit Gradient Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37842)
*Jaeseung Heo,J Rosser,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何压缩训练梯度以降低影响力函数（influence functions）的重复计算成本，提出 EOGP 方法，在保持未来未知查询的影响力估计精度下大幅减少存储需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析训练数据对 LLM 不同行为的影响需要重复计算影响力，复用存储的梯度可降低成本，但在 LLM 规模下存储完整梯度代价过高，因此需要压缩梯度同时保留对未来未知查询的影响力估计能力。

Method: 通过最坏情况分析刻画最优固定维线性表示，并提出 eigenbasis-corrected one-bit gradient projection (EOGP)。该方法先用 EK-FAC 降低梯度维度，再在保留子空间内用 PCA 从训练梯度中学习压缩方向，最后对坐标做一比特量化，从而在固定存储预算下保留更多坐标。

Result: 在 GPT-2 上，EOGP 预测重训练结果的准确度优于所评估的压缩基线，且每样本存储仅为基线的十六分之一；在 1B 到 32B 参数的 OLMo 2 SFT 模型上，EOGP 与使用超过 100 倍每样本存储的基线相比仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: EOGP 能在显著降低存储开销的同时保持对未来查询的影响力估计精度，是一种适用于 LLM 规模的梯度压缩方案。

Abstract: Influence functions estimate how individual training examples affect the behavior of large language models (LLMs). Analyzing how training data influence different behaviors of an LLM involves repeated influence computation. Reusing stored training gradients reduces the computational cost, but storing full gradients is prohibitively expensive at LLM scale. We study how to compress these gradients while preserving influence estimates for future queries that are unknown at storage time. Through a worst-case analysis, we characterize the optimal fixed-dimensional linear representation and propose eigenbasis-corrected one-bit gradient projection (EOGP) to approximate it at scale. Specifically, EOGP uses EK-FAC to reduce gradient dimensionality, then applies PCA within the retained subspace to learn compression directions from the training gradients. We then apply one-bit quantization to the resulting coordinates, allowing more coordinates to be retained within a fixed storage budget. On GPT-2, EOGP predicts retraining outcomes more accurately than the evaluated compression baselines while using one-sixteenth of their per-example storage. On OLMo 2 SFT models from 1B to 32B parameters, EOGP remains competitive with the baselines allocated over 100 times as much storage per example.

</details>


### [295] [Delta-Matching: Closing the Final Gap of Native 8-bit Training for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37852)
*Haozhan Tang,Hao Kang,Han Cai,Song Han,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FP8注意力训练存在前后向不一致导致的"陈旧delta"问题，大模型上会累积优化误差；提出Delta-Matching恢复softmax梯度零行和不变性，实现无架构改动的原生FP8注意力训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的原生8位FP8注意力训练仍是实现全原生8比特大语言模型训练的障碍。作者发现前向-反向的不一致会产生"陈旧delta"（stale delta），并需要实证其对训练动力学的扭曲。

Method: 推导前向-反向不一致如何产生陈旧delta；通过stale-delta混合实验在569M、1.67B、5.29B规模上验证退化现象，并测试QK归一化、NoPE、低学习率上下文扩展等缓解手段。提出Delta-Matching方法，证明其在给定数值假设下可恢复softmax梯度的零行和不变性，从而在注意力核心矩阵乘的前向和反向中均采用原生块缩放FP8，且无需架构改动、更小的全局批大小或辅助前向输出。

Result: 陈旧delta在569M时损失差距较小，但在1.67B和5.29B时损失显著上升并导致下游性能退化；QK归一化、NoPE、低学习率上下文扩展可缓解或推迟退化但无法消除，表明存在被小模型和短训练掩盖的累积优化误差。Delta-Matching在多种架构、规模和训练阶段下匹配BF16/FP32混合精度训练的损失和整体下游性能。

Conclusion: Delta-Matching使得在不做架构改动的前提下实现全原生FP8注意力训练成为可能，且性能与BF16/FP32混合精度相当；作者将开源实现、训练模型和数据配方。

Abstract: Reliable FP8 attention remains a barrier to fully native 8-bit large language model training. We derive how forward-backward inconsistencies produce stale delta and empirically show how it distorts training dynamics. Our stale-delta hybrid runs show a modest loss gap at 569M parameters but substantial loss increases and downstream degradation at 1.67B and 5.29B. QK normalization, NoPE (no positional encoding), and lower-learning-rate context extension mitigate or delay degradation without eliminating it. This pattern suggests accumulated optimization error that smaller models and short runs can conceal. We propose Delta-Matching, proving that it restores the softmax gradient's zero-row-sum invariant under the stated numerical assumptions. It enables native block-scaled FP8 in every forward and backward attention-core matmul without architectural changes, smaller global batches, or auxiliary forward outputs. Across tested architectures, scales, and training stages, Delta-Matching matches BF16/FP32 mixed-precision training loss and overall downstream performance. We will release our implementation, trained models, and data recipes.

</details>


### [296] [Storage Is Not Strategy: State-Conditioned Support Control for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37858)
*Tianhao Qian,Ziming Hong,Chongyang Gao,Kezhen Chen,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出干预分数（Intervention Score）与选择性动态干预重排（DIR-R），将定位、初始干预选择与依赖检查点的支持集修正分离，在自然-TOFU、LACUNA及GradDiff上普遍优于静态干预价值基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部化大模型遗忘方法依据定位信号选定一小部分参数后固定优化。但与该目标最相关的参数未必是最适合更新的参数，且候选干预的价值会随优化推进而变化。一个受控实验显示：存储定位分数AUROC达0.981，但其存储身份仅在17/36个目标上与更优干预一致，而LoRA在35/36个目标上胜出，说明定位与可编辑性并不等价。

Method: 提出Intervention Score，按真实遗忘更新带来的预测效应并计入附带损害对可编辑组排序，据此形成静态干预价值基线Static-IV；进一步提出选择性动态干预重排DIR-R，仅当校准探针支持比较时才重新审视该子集。

Result: 在Natural-TOFU上，方法与目标之间的20组比较中有19组描述性优势为正（部分接近零）。在LACUNA定位精度基准上，六个NPO与SimNPO比较中平均终端效用均更高：NPO优势为+0.431至+0.848，SimNPO为+0.503至+0.571。GradDiff目标表现出明显领域依赖：相比Static-IV，主四领域评估为六胜六平无负，配对增益均值+0.165、中位数+0.0025。

Conclusion: 证据支持将定位、初始干预选择与依赖检查点的支持修订三者分离，动态重排可带来稳定但幅度不一的收益。

Abstract: Many localized large language model (LLM) unlearning methods select a small parameter subset from a localization signal and keep it fixed during optimization. The parameters most associated with a target, however, need not be the best ones to update, and candidate interventions can change value as optimization proceeds. In a controlled experiment, a storage-localization score reaches an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.981, yet storage identity agrees with the better intervention on only 17/36 targets, while low-rank adaptation (LoRA) wins 35/36. We introduce Intervention Score, which ranks editable groups by the predicted effect of the actual unlearning update while accounting for collateral damage, and use it to form the static intervention-value baseline (Static-IV). We then introduce selective dynamic intervention re-ranking (DIR-R), which revisits that subset only when a calibrated probe justifies the comparison. On the Natural-TOFU dataset, our method has positive descriptive margins in 19/20 comparisons between methods and objectives, although several are near zero. On the LACUNA localization-precision benchmark, our mean terminal utility is higher in all six negative preference optimization (NPO) and SimNPO comparisons: NPO margins range from +0.431 to +0.848, and SimNPO margins range from +0.503 to +0.571. The gradient-difference (GradDiff) objective reveals substantial field dependence. Relative to Static-IV, the primary four-field GradDiff evaluation has six wins, six ties, and no losses, with mean and median paired gains of +0.165 and +0.0025. The evidence supports separating localization, initial intervention selection, and checkpoint-dependent support revision.

</details>


### [297] [Strict-Saddle Landscapes and Multi-Rank Geometry in Low-Tubal-Rank Tensor Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37865)
*Eugene Agyei-Kodie,Longxiu Huang,Shuang Li,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究低管秩张量感知在平衡因子分解下的优化景观，证明在管状受限等距条件下不存在虚假局部极小，并揭示局部几何由Fourier切片秩而非管秩决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低管秩张量感知通常依赖非凸矩阵分解求解，其优化景观是否布满虚假局部极小、局部收敛几何由什么决定尚不清晰。

Method: 采用平衡因子分解建模低管秩张量感知问题，在管状受限等距条件（tubal restricted isometry condition）下分析优化景观，并结合Fourier多秩结构刻画鞍点与局部极小。

Result: 建立了量化的严格鞍点景观，证明在任意Fourier多秩分布下无虚假局部极小；发现局部几何取决于Fourier切片秩而非仅管秩：均匀秩在解轨道横向上呈二次增长，非均匀秩通过隐藏的逐频率过参数化产生四次平坦方向，即便因子宽度等于精确管秩时亦如此。

Conclusion: 优化景观的严格鞍点性质可保证全局优化行为，而局部收敛速度受Fourier切片秩结构控制，非均匀秩会导致更平坦的方向；数值实验验证了全局优化行为与不同局部几何的对比。

Abstract: We study the optimization landscape of low-tubal-rank tensor sensing through a balanced factorization. Under a tubal restricted isometry condition, we establish a quantitative strict-saddle landscape with no spurious local minima for arbitrary Fourier multi-rank profiles. We further show that the local geometry depends on the Fourier-slice ranks rather than the tubal rank alone. Uniform ranks yield quadratic growth transverse to the solution orbit, whereas nonuniform ranks produce quartically flat directions through hidden frequency-wise overparameterization, even when the factor width equals the exact tubal rank. Numerical experiments illustrate the global optimization behavior and the contrasting local geometries.

</details>


### [298] [Learning Beyond What You Sample: Off-Policy-Aware Cross-Model Trajectory Exchange for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37868)
*Doohyuk Jang,Yoonsik Park,Gyouk Chu,Sihwan Park,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRAFT框架，利用异构模型互补性，用同伴轨迹替换全失败组以改善RLVR训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR方法如GRPO依赖自我生成的成功轨迹，但有限的rollout预算会导致全失败组，无法产生基于奖励的策略梯度信号。虽然增加rollout可提高成功率但成本更高，而一个模型rollout中缺失的成功轨迹可能已被另一个模型发现，异构模型在互补的提示上各有成功，存在无需指定更强教师的互相学习机会。

Method: GRAFT（Gated Replacement of Answer-Failed groups with peer Trajectories）是一个off-policy感知的框架，用信息量丰富的同伴组替换全失败组。它迁移成功与失败的同伴响应及同伴计算的advantage，同时通过序列级兼容性加权和token级重要性比率裁剪来控制跨模型不匹配。

Result: 在三对异构模型和五个数学推理基准上，GRAFT在相同单模型rollout预算下持续优于GRPO，模型级平均性能平均提升2.1分，最高提升4.5分。存储的同伴轨迹保留了大部分收益，在无同步协同训练情况下平均较GRPO提升1.8分。

Conclusion: 利用异构模型间的互补成功轨迹进行同伴学习，可在不增加rollout预算的情况下有效提升RLVR训练效果，且存储轨迹能保留大部分增益。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) methods such as GRPO rely on successful self-generated trajectories, but finite rollout budgets can produce all-fail groups with no reward-based policy-gradient signal. While additional rollouts improve the chance of success at higher cost, successful trajectories missing from one model's rollouts may already have been discovered by another. Indeed, we observe that heterogeneous models often succeed on complementary prompts, creating opportunities for mutual learning without a designated stronger teacher. To exploit this complementarity, we propose GRAFT (Gated Replacement of Answer-Failed groups with peer Trajectories), an off-policy-aware framework that replaces all-fail groups with informative peer groups. GRAFT transfers both successful and unsuccessful peer responses with peer-computed advantages, while controlling cross-model mismatch through sequence-level compatibility weighting and token-level importance ratio clipping. Across three heterogeneous model pairs and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, GRAFT consistently improves both models over GRPO with the same per-model rollout budget, gaining 2.1 points on average and up to 4.5 points in model-level average performance. Stored peer trajectories preserve most of the gains, improving over GRPO by 1.8 points on average without simultaneous co-training.

</details>


### [299] [TopoEmbedX: A General Framework for Representation Learning on Topological Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37884)
*Florian Frantzen,Ibrahem AlJabea,Ines Henriques-Cadby,Theodore Papamarkou,Mustafa Hajij,Michael T. Schaub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TopoEmbedX，一个将拓扑结构（单纯复形、超图、胞腔复形等）嵌入欧氏空间的统一框架，整合了五种已有算法并新增五种算法，实验表明其嵌入可支持多维度数据的分类与回归任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑结构如单纯复形、超图和胞腔复形能建模高阶关系，广泛出现在现代数据集中，但需要专门的方法来生成有意义的嵌入。现有方法分散，缺乏统一、一致且易用的拓扑表示学习框架。

Method: TopoEmbedX 构建统一框架，整合五种已有拓扑嵌入算法（DeepCell、Cell2Vec、CellDiff2Vec、HOLE、HOGLEE），并新增五种算法（ComplexNetMF、ComplexRep、ComplexRandNE、ComplexWalklets、ComplexHeat）。新算法借助拓扑域的增广 Hasse 图，将经典图嵌入技术扩展到高阶设置。

Result: 实验表明，TopoEmbedX 生成的嵌入能够支持跨多维数据的分类和回归任务。

Conclusion: TopoEmbedX 为拓扑表示学习提供了清晰、一致且易用的框架，统一了多种拓扑嵌入算法，并将经典图嵌入技术推广到高阶拓扑域。

Abstract: Topological structures such as simplicial complexes, hypergraphs, and cell complexes extend standard graph models by modeling higher-order relationships. These structures appear in many modern datasets and require specialized methods for generating meaningful embeddings. In this paper, we introduce TopoEmbedX, a unified framework for embedding a wide range of topological domains into Euclidean spaces. The package brings together several existing topological embedding algorithms---DeepCell, Cell2Vec, CellDiff2Vec, HOLE, and HOGLEE---and introduces five new algorithms: ComplexNetMF, ComplexRep, ComplexRandNE, ComplexWalklets, and ComplexHeat. These algorithms extend well-known graph embedding techniques to higher-order settings using the augmented Hasse graph of a topological domain. TopoEmbedX provides a clear, consistent, and easy-to-use framework for topological representation learning. Experiments show that the embeddings generated by TopoEmbedX support tasks such as classification and regression across multidimensional data.

</details>


### [300] [Behavioral Capacity Certificates for Quantized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37887)
*Arian Eamaz,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出行为容量证书（BCC），按"完整实现"的先验总质量来计量模型复杂度，将权重、缩放、激活与缓存规则统一计入，并给出验证节省量的盈亏平衡律与三步部署流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接的权重编码界把行为不同的部署视为同等复杂度，又把行为相同的权重编码分别计费，因而无法反映量化后模型实际"计算什么"的变化。需要一种按行为而非按存储权重计价的复杂度度量。

Method: BCC以诱导相同有界损失的完整实现（权重、缩放、激活与缓存规则）的聚合先验质量作为行为代价：量化合并实现时共享质量降低复杂度惩罚，并由盈亏平衡律判定节省能否覆盖验证成本。配套三步工作流：前向筛选层比特宽度；边际认证单元识别可剪枝或翻转符号而不改变行为的权重；用BCC约束部署模型的总体损失。

Result: 前向筛选在更低预处理成本下取得与Hessian引导选择相当的质量；边际认证单元在OLMoE-1B-7B和SmolLM2-1.7B上所有允许组合均保持全部声明预测，独立探针给出新文本上预测改变概率的上界；BCC对完整解码器给出非空总体损失界且比压缩编码路径更紧。相同缓存内存下，键精度高于值时GPT-2、Qwen2.5、SmolLM2的NLL更低、预测一致率更高，GPT-2审计中复杂度界也更紧。

Conclusion: 按行为而非权重编码计量复杂度可更准确地刻画量化部署，BCC在筛选、认证与损失界定三方面均获实验支持，并支持"键比值更高精度"的缓存分配策略。

Abstract: Activation and key-value cache precision change what a quantized language model computes without altering its stored weights. Direct weight-code bounds, however, assign identical complexity to deployments that behave differently and charge separately for weight codes that behave identically. Behavioral Capacity Certificates (BCC) charge for behavior using the aggregate prior mass of complete implementations---weights, scales, activation and cache rules---that induce the same bounded loss. When quantization merges implementations, this shared mass lowers the complexity penalty, and a break-even law determines when the saving survives the cost of validating it. BCC supports a three-step deployment workflow, and our experiments verify each step. First, a forward-only screen shortlists per-layer bit-widths by how often candidate perturbations preserve the reference predictions, with quality comparable to Hessian-guided selection at lower preprocessing cost. Second, margin-certified cells identify weights that can be pruned or sign-flipped without changing the deployed behavior: every permitted combination preserves all declared predictions, and on OLMoE-1B-7B and SmolLM2-1.7B, independent probes bound the probability that any permitted combination changes a prediction on new text. Third, BCC bounds the population loss of the deployed model, nonvacuously for complete decoders and more tightly than the compressed-code route. At equal cache memory, giving keys higher precision than values yields lower NLL and higher prediction agreement on GPT-2, Qwen2.5, and SmolLM2, together with a tighter complexity bound in the GPT-2 audit.

</details>


### [301] [Scaling Zero-Order Pretraining through Model Sharding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37899)
*Francois Chaubard,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Chris Ré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOMA 是一种零阶优化方法，将 LSTM 专家独立训练在 N 个数据簇上，通过可分离损失消除跨专家扰动噪声，在降低梯度方差的同时提升训练计算效率，并在推理端通过大规模集成获得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化无需反向传播，适用于仅前向硬件和不可微损失，但其梯度方差随扰动维度增长，阻碍大模型训练。本文旨在解决该可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出 SOMA：用 SPSA 在 N 个数据簇上独立训练 LSTM 专家，不交换梯度、激活或优化器状态；采用可分离损失，消除跨专家扰动噪声，代价是失去跨域联合表征；并使用约 80,000 估算 RTX 5090 GPU 小时进行实验。

Result: 8.44M 参数、150 聚合 GPU 小时下，SOMA N=2、64 扰动达 1.76 test nats/byte，优于单块 SPSA 的 2.00–2.11 与 EGGROLL 的 2.21；WikiText-103 上分别为 2.07、2.25–2.36 和 2.49。理论上独立损失将相对梯度方差降至共享损失估计器的约 1/N。固定条件下，独立损失使 SOMA N=4 测试损失降低 0.035 nats/byte。推理端 SOMA N=256 配合 top-k 路由达 2.36M tokens/s，较 N=8 的 257k 快 9.19 倍，测试损失更低（1.68 vs 1.71），但聚合训练计算多 59.9 倍。

Conclusion: 适度分片可提升零阶训练的计算效率，可分离损失降低梯度方差，大规模专家集成还能带来显著推理加速；所有训练评估代码与检查点已开源。

Abstract: Zero-order optimization (ZO) trains without backpropagation, making it relevant to forward-only hardware and non-differentiable loss, but its gradient variance grows with perturbed dimension, inhibiting large-model training. Sharded Optimization Mixture of Assemblies (SOMA) trains LSTM experts independently on $N$ data clusters using simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation (SPSA), without exchanging gradients, activations or optimizer state. Its separable loss removes cross-expert perturbation noise at the cost of jointly learned representations across domains. Using 80,000 estimated RTX 5090 GPU-hours, we show modest sharding improves training compute efficiency over all tested monolithic ZO controls. At 8.44M parameters and 150 aggregate GPU-hours, SOMA $N=2$ with 64 perturbations reaches 1.76 test nats/byte, versus 2.00--2.11 for monolithic SPSA at 64, 256 or 1,024 perturbations and 2.21 for EGGROLL. On WikiText-103, these frozen checkpoints reach 2.07, 2.25--2.36 and 2.49, respectively. On a fixed separable objective with equal-size blocks, we prove independent losses reduce relative gradient variance to approximately $1/N$ of a shared-loss estimator's. Holding starting weights, data, perturbations and compute fixed, independent rather than summed losses lower SOMA $N=4$ test loss by 0.035 nats/byte after 1,000 updates across three seeds. Larger ensembles offer a separate inference benefit: at similar model size with top-$k$ routing ($k=4$), SOMA $N=256$ achieves 2.36M tokens/s versus 257k for SOMA $N=8$ ($9.19\times$, including routing), at lower test loss (1.68 versus 1.71), albeit using $59.9\times$ as much aggregate training compute. We release all training and evaluation code and checkpoints.

</details>


### [302] [Beyond Interaction Capacity: Estimator Scaling with Recursive Models for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37905)
*Shivang Chopra,Fotis Iliopoulos,Zsolt Kira,Gaurav Menghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"估计器扩展"(estimator scaling)这一新方向，指出现有显式交叉网络通过增大容量提升CTR预测性能存在快速递减回报，转而利用多个相关估计器带来的非共享预测变异，并提出参数高效的递归平均预测器RECAP，在多个基准上取得SOTA并位于性能-参数帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTR预测依赖稀疏类别特征交互建模，近年进展主要靠加深交叉网络和增强交叉算子来提升单一预测器的交互容量。但作者重新审视发现，持续增大交互容量的收益快速递减，因此需要寻找互补的扩展方向。

Method: 提出估计器扩展方向：用额外资源融合多个相关估计器而非仅扩大单一预测器。理论分析表明其收益取决于估计器间的非共享预测变异，但朴素实现（独立训练模型）部署成本随规模增长。为此提出RECAP，在三个层面实现：独立模型间的蒸馏、训练轨迹上的指数移动平均、以及权重共享递归主干内的推理路径聚合。

Result: 在多个基准实验中，RECAP取得新的SOTA预测性能，并在性能-参数帕累托前沿上处于有利位置。

Conclusion: 持续增加单预测器的交互容量并非提升CTR性能的最有效方式；估计器扩展是互补且有效的方向，RECAP以参数高效的方式实现了该扩展并取得优越性能。

Abstract: Click-Through Rate prediction, a core task in recommendation and advertising systems, relies on modeling interactions among sparse categorical features. Explicit cross networks are a central paradigm for CTR prediction, and recent progress has largely come from increasing the interaction capacity of a single predictor through deeper cross networks and more expressive cross operators. We revisit whether continually increasing interaction capacity remains the most effective way to improve predictive performance, and find that its benefits quickly exhibit diminishing returns even as capacity continues to grow. This motivates a complementary scaling direction that we call estimator scaling, where additional resources are used to incorporate multiple related estimators rather than only enlarging a single predictor. Through theoretical analysis, we show that the gains from estimator scaling are governed by the amount of non-shared predictive variation available across estimators. However, exploiting this variation naively can be expensive: independently trained models provide substantial estimator diversity but require deployment cost to grow with ensemble size. This motivates a parameter-efficient realization of estimator scaling that can incorporate diversity from multiple estimator sources without maintaining multiple full models. Building on this view, we introduce RECursive Averaged Predictor (RECAP), a parameter-efficient recursive CTR model that operationalizes estimator scaling at three levels: distillation across independently trained models, exponential moving averaging over training trajectories, and aggregation over inference-time routes within a weight-shared recursive backbone. Experiments across multiple benchmarks establish new state-of-the-art predictive performance on standard benchmarks, while placing the RECAP on a favorable performance-parameter Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [303] [Overcoming Scaling Limits in On-Policy Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37915)
*Md. Ismail Hossain,Humaira Kousar,Isidora Chara Tourni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文对on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）进行因子分析，发现"脚手架是否正确"比"教师上下文是否正确"更关键；据此提出OASIS，仅用最终答案标签、在已验证的on-policy轨迹上监督，并改用模型自生成的非验证尝试作为教师上下文，在多个模型与数学基准上取得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPSD在未验证的学生rollout上施加监督，同时让教师以特权上下文（通常为参考解答）为条件，存在角色混淆问题。教师可利用学生无法获得的信息，造成"模仿差距"，且该差距随规模缩小，但OPSD仍主要监督未验证轨迹，导致规模增大时收益衰减（3.05→0.14）。作者希望厘清"脚手架正确性"与"上下文正确性"各自的作用，并设计一种在模型扩展时仍有效的自蒸馏方法。

Method: 通过因子分析分离脚手架与教师上下文的角色，比较已验证/未验证脚手架及不同教师条件的影响。基于"已验证脚手架即使在教师仅以学生自身失败rollout为条件时仍有效"的发现，提出OASIS：保留OPSD目标，但主要监督按标签验证的on-policy轨迹，并以模型生成的非验证尝试替代书面参考解答作为教师上下文，因此只需最终答案标签。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B、4B、8B上，于AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025评测：OASIS相对基模型平均提升3.2–3.8分；而OPSD的增益从1.7B的3.05分降至8B的0.14分。在8B上，OASIS比OPSD高3.05分。

Conclusion: 脚手架是否正确比教师上下文是否正确对下游准确率影响更大；已验证的on-policy脚手架能在模型规模扩大时保持自蒸馏的有效性，OASIS仅依赖最终答案标签即可实现稳健提升。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student to match a privileged teacher distribution along its own sampled trajectory. Standard OPSD applies this supervision to unverified student rollouts while conditioning the teacher on privileged context, typically a reference solution. We separate these roles in a factorial analysis and find that scaffold correctness has a stronger effect on downstream accuracy than context correctness. Unverified scaffolds create an imitation gap because the teacher can use information unavailable to the student. This gap shrinks with model scale, yet OPSD continues to supervise mostly unverified trajectories. In contrast, verified scaffolds remain effective even when the teacher is conditioned on the student's own unsuccessful rollout. Based on this finding, we introduce OASIS, which retains the OPSD objective but supervises mostly verified by label on-policy trajectories and replaces written solutions with unverified model-generated attempts as the teacher context. OASIS therefore requires only final-answer labels. Across Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025, OASIS improves over the base model by 3.2--3.8 points on average, while OPSD's gain falls from 3.05 points at 1.7B to 0.14 at 8B. At 8B, OASIS improves over OPSD by 3.05 points, showing that verified on-policy scaffolds preserve the effectiveness of self-distillation as models scale.

</details>


### [304] [Search Dimension in Unlabeled Projection Pursuit: A Scaling Law for Subspace Restriction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37917)
*Rares Dimitrie Grozavescu,Mark Girolami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 投影寻踪在观测空间含大高斯补空间时，可能被无信号方向以低经验峰度"骗过"，样本划分无法修复；限制搜索于已知算子的列空间可精确消除负峰度分支的失败，数据估计主子空间是替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影寻踪旨在寻找数据最非高斯的方向，但当观测空间含大高斯补空间时，经验目标可被一个不携带信号、经验峰度与真值同样低的方向最小化，导致搜索失效；作者试图揭示这一失败机制并评估样本划分、坐标增补等处理是否有效。

Method: 分析经验目标与贝叶斯可恢复性的关系；考察样本划分与追加独立坐标的影响；在负峰度分支上将搜索限制于已知前向算子的列空间；并以数据估计主子空间作为替代；在受控双分量模型中比较协方差尖峰估计与四阶矩搜索所对应的充分缩放（$ς^{-4}$ 与 $ς^{-8}$）。

Result: 追加与潜在机制无关的坐标会降低搜索性能但不改变贝叶斯可恢复性；限制于已知算子列空间可精确消除失败。固定搜索维度时，实测阈值比塌缩到 $n/p^2$，指数 $0.156$，接近预测的 $1/8$；改变搜索维度时实测指数为 $0.325$，明显大于 $1/8$，且测试范围未能确定其函数形式；交点位置还依赖标定与模型配置；在下游超额误差准则下，该缩放基本消失。

Conclusion: 该缩放结论是基于充分性上界、经验支持的标度关系，而非已证明的渐近紧致律；其适用性受搜索维度、标定、模型配置及评价准则影响。

Abstract: Projection pursuit searches for a direction along which the data look least Gaussian. When the observation space contains a large Gaussian complement, the empirical objective can be minimized by a direction that carries no signal, with empirical kurtosis as low as at the truth. Sample splitting exposes rather than repairs this failure. Appending coordinates independent of the latent regime degrades the search while leaving Bayes recoverability unchanged. Restricting the search to the column space of a known forward operator removes the failure exactly on the negative-kurtosis branch. Estimating a principal subspace from the data is the alternative. In a controlled two-component model, the leading sufficient scalings differ in the gain with which the operator transmits the discriminant: $ς^{-4}$ for covariance-spike estimation and $ς^{-8}$ for fourth-moment search. At fixed search dimension, the measured threshold ratio collapses onto $n/p^2$ with exponent $0.156$, close to the predicted $1/8$. This is an empirically supported scaling motivated by sufficient bounds, not a proved asymptotically tight law. When the search dimension is varied, the measured exponent is $0.325$, substantially larger than $1/8$, and the tested range does not identify its functional form. The crossing location also depends on calibration and model configuration. Under a downstream excess-error criterion, the scaling largely disappears.

</details>


### [305] [Pattern Formation in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37921)
*Erkan Turan,Gaspard Abel,Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用斑图形成理论分析完整Transformer的前向传播，揭示其通过对位置编码、多头注意力和输出-值几何的选择性放大，形成包括行波、旋转波在内的多种涌现模式，从而构成一种可控的动力学归纳先验，可提升学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论要么只关注Transformer是否逃逸秩塌缩，要么基于简化架构假设指出自注意力使token趋向聚类模式，但这些视角无法解释实践中观察到的丰富结构。因此核心开放问题是：当完整Transformer逃逸秩塌缩后，它如何组织token表示？

Method: 采用斑图形成理论（pattern-formation theory），将Transformer的动力学视角扩展到包含位置编码、多头注意力和输出-值几何的完整架构，分析各架构组件对模式放大、稳定、竞争与共存的控制作用。

Result: 完整Transformer架构通过选择性放大一组此前未被报告过的丰富模式（如行波、旋转波等）来施加归纳先验，并明确了各架构组件在控制哪种模式被放大、稳定、竞争或共存中的角色。这些结构可作为可控动力学先验促进学习。

Conclusion: 通过选择与任务对齐的位置编码和权重初始化，在受控序列任务及CIFAR-10上的ConViT中实现了更高的数据效率和更快的优化速度，表明该动力学先验具有实用价值。

Abstract: What are the inductive biases of a Transformer architecture? Existing theory on how the forward pass shapes representations either considers whether Transformers escape from rank collapse or demonstrates that self-attention drives tokens toward cluster patterns. The latter view arises from an elegant dynamical systems perspective, but relies on simplified architectural assumptions, and does not explain the rich structures observed in practice. This leaves a major open question: when a full Transformer escapes rank collapse, how does it structure token representations? Using pattern-formation theory, we show that the dynamical view of Transformers can account for Positional Encoding, Multi-Head Attention, and Output-Value geometry. We demonstrate that a full Transformer architecture imposes an inductive prior by selectively amplifying a rich set of previously unreported patterns, including traveling or rotating waves among others. We characterize the role of each architectural component in controlling which pattern is amplified, which ones stabilize, compete, or coexist. Finally, we show that these structures can act as a controllable dynamical prior that facilitates learning. By choosing both task-aligned positional encoding and weight initialization, we demonstrate improved data efficiency and accelerated optimization on controlled sequence tasks and with ConViT on CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [306] [Time-Anchored Diffusion Language Models: Latent-Space Caching for Fast Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37924)
*Joel Anto Paul,Litu Rout,Aditya Akella,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"基于时间的(自监督)锚定"方法，通过缓存并复用潜在锚点表示来加速扩散语言模型的去噪过程，无需监督目标即可提升吞吐量并降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有锚定扩散语言模型依赖监督式的关键token目标来塑造中间潜在空间，需要额外标注目标。作者观察到锚点编码了干净序列的持久属性(如语义意图、全局结构或中间计划)，尽管随着token画布演化其隐藏表示会过时，但语义内容在相近扩散时间步仍有效，因此希望无需目标即可学习和复用锚点。

Method: 采用两阶段架构：一个相对昂贵的锚网络生成潜在缓存状态，一个轻量去噪网络在每个反向步骤通过融合模块将缓存潜在状态与当前状态智能结合。该方法赋予锚定"潜在空间缓存"的解释——锚网络周期性地被评估，其缓存表示在多个反向步骤中复用。实例化为TADM:Post-train(对预训练DLM进行时间锚定)和TADM:Pretraining(在预训练中学习时间锚定)。

Result: 在DiffusionGemma-26B上，TADM:Post-train在多个数学、代码和STEM基准(GSM8K、AIME26、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench-v6、HumanEval、MMLU-Pro)上提升吞吐量约49%至79%。TADM:Pretraining相对标准单阶段DLM减少Transformer层计算量最多38%，相比ADLM实现最多73%的实测吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 基于时间的自监督锚定无需监督目标即可学习并复用潜在锚点，通过潜在空间缓存的解释有效加速扩散语言模型，在多种基准上显著提升吞吐量并降低计算开销。

Abstract: Recent work on anchored diffusion language models improves denoising by shaping an intermediate latent space with supervised important-token targets. In this work, we introduce time-based (self-supervised) anchoring, which learns and reuses latent anchors without requiring such targets. Our key observation is that anchors encode persistent properties of the clean sequence, such as its semantic intent, global structure, or intermediate plan. Although their hidden representations become stale as the token canvas evolves, their semantic content remains useful across nearby diffusion times. This is implemented through a two-stage architecture consisting of a relatively expensive anchor network that generates the latent cache state and a lightweight denoising network that intelligently combines the cached latent state with the current state at each reverse step using a fusion module. This gives anchoring a latent-space caching interpretation: the anchor network is evaluated periodically, while its cached representation is reused across multiple reverse steps. We instantiate this framework as TADM:Post-train, which time-anchorizes pretrained DLMs, and TADM:Pretraining, which learns time-based anchors during pretraining. Applied to DiffusionGemma-26B, TADM:Post-train improves throughput by approximately 49% to 79% on several math, code, and STEM benchmarks (GSM8K, AIME26, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench-v6, HumanEval, MMLU-Pro). TADM:Pretraining reduces Transformer-layer computation by up to 38% relative to a standard single-stage DLM, achieves up to 73% higher measured throughput than ADLM.

</details>


### [307] [Learning When to Update: A Near-Optimal Timing Bandit Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37932)
*Qiulin Lin,Junyan Su,Liyuan Wang,Minghua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将动态环境中未知退化模式下的更新时机问题形式化为"timing bandit"问题，提出BCAE及OE-BCAE算法，达到 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾并匹配理论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行于动态环境的系统（如机器学习模型、数字孪生）需及时更新以维持性能。更新过频浪费资源，过疏导致性能退化。当系统退化模式事先未知（如新运行环境）时，如何把握更新时机尤为困难，作者将此挑战形式化为新的bandit问题。

Method: 提出timing bandit模型：每个arm代表一个候选更新间隔，含固定更新成本与未知随机退化成本。该设定有三个结构特性区别于标准MAB：选择间隔会承诺多个时间槽；arm成本由逐步退化成本与固定更新成本组成；选择较长间隔会自然揭示每个中间步的退化，提供与较短间隔相关的连续反馈。基于此提出Balanced Consecutive Arm Elimination (BCAE)，并进一步提出融合下置信界原则的Optimism-Enhanced变体OE-BCAE。

Result: BCAE达到 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾，优于标准bandit算法在此设定下的 $\tilde{\Omega}(K\sqrt{T})$（$K$ 为候选更新间隔数）；OE-BCAE在保持相同遗憾阶的同时提升经验自适应性。两算法的遗憾界在对数因子上匹配理论下界。仿真显示算法遗憾低，且在arm数与更新成本变化时保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态环境更新时机决策提供了新的bandit形式化框架与高效算法，理论上达到近最优遗憾，实验上表现出低遗憾与良好稳定性，可应用于机器学习模型、数字孪生等资源密集型系统的更新调度。

Abstract: Systems operating in dynamic environments require timely updates to sustain performance. For resource-intensive systems such as machine learning models and digital twins, strategically timing updates is essential. Updating too frequently wastes resources, while updating too infrequently leads to costly performance degradation. The problem is particularly challenging when the system's degradation pattern is unknown a priori, as is common in new operating environments. We formalize this challenge as a novel \emph{timing bandit} problem, where each arm represents a candidate update interval with a fixed update cost and an unknown, stochastic degradation cost. Three structural properties distinguish this setting from standard multi-armed bandits: selecting an interval commits the learner to multiple time slots before the next update; arm costs are composed of per-step degradation costs and a fixed update cost; and selecting a longer interval naturally reveals degradation at every intermediate step, providing consecutive feedback relevant to shorter intervals. By exploiting these structures, we develop Balanced Consecutive Arm Elimination (BCAE). BCAE achieves $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret, improving upon the $\tildeΩ(K\sqrt{T})$ regret of standard bandit algorithms in this setting, where $K$ is the number of candidate update intervals. We further propose an Optimism-Enhanced variant (OE-BCAE) that integrates lower-confidence-bound principles to improve empirical adaptivity while preserving the same regret order. Moreover, the regret bound achieved by our algorithms matches the theoretical lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Simulation results demonstrate that our algorithms achieve low regret and remain stable as both the number of arms and the update cost vary.

</details>


### [308] [An Efficient Machine Learning Approach for Degradation Forecasting in AEM Water Electrolysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37941)
*Marco Veneriano,Ani Gjergji,Sebastiano Bellani,Andrea Riva,Vito Paolo Pastore,Matteo Santacesaria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于新建的单电池阴离子交换膜水电解（AEMWE）数据集，在多种异构实验条件下恒流运行，采用从线性基线到LSTM、CNN等不同复杂度的机器学习模型，对电池电压退化曲线进行中期预测，并在一套针对异构工业数据设计的严格训练与评估框架下进行评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AEMWE是新兴的制氢技术，其耐久性与电压退化行为对实际应用至关重要；而现有数据往往来自不同实验条件、彼此异构，缺乏规范的建模与评估方法。因此需要针对异构工业数据开展数据驱动的退化预测研究。

Method: 构建新的单电池AEMWE数据集（恒流负载、多组异构实验）；训练并比较多种复杂度的机器学习模型，包括线性基线模型、LSTM和CNN；设计并采用专门面向异构工业数据的严格训练与评估框架，用于中期电压退化曲线预测。

Result: 论文摘要未给出具体量化结果，主要贡献在于提出数据集、建立针对异构工业数据的评估框架，并对不同复杂度模型的中期退化预测性能进行了系统对比。

Conclusion: 该研究为AEMWE电压退化预测提供了数据驱动的建模方案与可靠的评估范式，说明在异构工业数据条件下开展机器学习预测是可行且值得深入探索的方向。

Abstract: This study provides a data-driven analysis of a novel dataset of single-cell Anion Exchange Membrane water electrolyzers (AEMWE), operated under constant current load across multiple heterogeneous experimental campaigns. We train and evaluate a range of machine learning models with different complexity, including linear baselines, LSTMs and CNNs, to perform medium-term forecasting of the cell voltage degradation curve. The models are assessed within a rigorous training and evaluation framework specifically designed for heterogeneous industrial data.

</details>


### [309] [Scene-Consistent Illumination Transfer for Inserted Advertising Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37951)
*Rameshwar Mishra,Bishshoy Das,A. V. Subramanyam,Guan-Ming Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Ad-Relight，一种仅需推理的光照迁移流程，将场景光照转移到给定广告图形上，无需针对横幅的训练集；在 560 个生成放置上优于几何合成与直接重光照基线，尤其在地面非均匀光照下优势最明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广播画面中替换可见广告，几何上容易实现但光度上很敏感：贴入的图形即使透视正确，若亮度、阴影或明暗与承载表面不符，也会显得"浮在画面上"。论文旨在解决这种光照不一致问题，且不依赖针对特定横幅的训练数据。

Method: Ad-Relight 为仅推理流程：先把缓慢变化的明暗与图形结构分离；再用两张几乎相同的背景去探测预训练扩散重光照模型，以分离目标区域的贡献（残差）；最后将残差与平滑亮度场及软衰减掩码结合完成融合。

Result: 在 560 个生成放置上，方法在结构相似性、感知距离和光照一致性上均优于几何合成与直接重光照基线。人工评判与自动化偏好研究显示，在地面安装、非均匀光照的图形上提升最明显。

Conclusion: 该流程可在无需专门训练集的情况下实现较自然的光照迁移；当前工作基于静态图像，时间维度上的稳定性仍是待扩展方向。

Abstract: Replacing a visible advertisement in a broadcast frame is geometrically straightforward but photometrically delicate. A pasted graphic can have the correct perspective and still appear detached when its brightness, shading, or shadow disagrees with the surface beneath it. This paper presents Ad-Relight, an inference-only procedure for transferring scene illumination to a supplied advertising graphic without collecting a banner-specific training set. The procedure first separates slowly varying shade from graphic structure, then probes a pretrained diffusion relighter with two nearly identical backgrounds to isolate the contribution of the target region. A final pass combines this residual with a smoothed luminance field and a soft attenuation mask. Across 560 generated placements, the approach improves structural similarity, perceptual distance, and illumination agreement over geometric compositing and direct relighting baselines. Human judgments and an automated preference study show the clearest gains on floor-mounted graphics with nonuniform lighting. The current study is image based; temporal stabilization remains an open extension.

</details>
