### [310] [Kolmogorov-Arnold Classifier Systems as Universal Approximators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37958)
*Hiroki Shiraishi,Hisao Ishibuchi,Masaya Nakata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KACS，将规则按维度重组，规则数从O(m^n)降至O(mn^2)，参数大幅减少，并首次证明LCS是通用逼近器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的机器学习（如LCS）直接划分n维输入空间，随维度n增长，规则数和参数数呈指数爆炸，存在根本性可扩展性瓶颈。为突破该瓶颈，需要改变规则组织方式。

Method: 受Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理启发，将目标函数分解为若干一维子问题，为每个子问题分配专属规则集。用一维模型替代n维局部模型，每条规则仅需两个与n无关的参数。并给出KACS为紧致域上连续函数通用逼近器的首个构造性证明。

Result: 最坏情况规则数从O(m^n)降至O(mn^2)。在与直接n维划分方法同等条件下对比，KACS在多数设置中达到有竞争力的精度，同时仅使用2%~40%的参数。

Conclusion: KACS通过维度式规则重组有效缓解了LCS的维度灾难，显著降低参数与规则规模，并首次从理论上确立LCS的通用逼近能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: As the input dimension $n$ grows, rule-based machine learning, such as Learning Classifier Systems (LCSs), faces a fundamental scalability bottleneck for function approximation: both rule count and parameter count grow exponentially with $n$. Traditional LCSs partition the $n$-dimensional input space directly, requiring $\mathcal{O}(m^n)$ rules for adequate coverage, where $m$ is the per-variable resolution. This article breaks from this paradigm by reorganizing rules dimension-wise, guided by the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem: any continuous $n$-dimensional function can be expressed as a finite superposition of one-dimensional functions. The proposed Kolmogorov-Arnold Classifier System (KACS) decomposes the target function into one-dimensional subproblems and assigns a dedicated ruleset to each, reducing the worst-case rule count from $\mathcal{O}(m^n)$ to $\mathcal{O}(mn^2)$ and replacing $n$-dimensional local models with one-dimensional models requiring only two parameters per rule, independent of $n$. We also provide the first constructive proof that an LCS, namely KACS, is a universal approximator for continuous functions on compact domains. Evaluated against a direct $n$-dimensional input space partitioning approach under otherwise identical conditions, KACS achieves competitive accuracy in many settings while using only 2\% to 40\% of the parameters. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/YNU-NakataLab/KACS.

</details>


### [311] [TabFM: A Zero-Shot Foundation Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37959)
*Weihao Kong,Erez Louidor Ilan,Shuxin Nie,Taman Narayan,Rajat Sen,Yichen Zhou,Deqing Fu,Samet Oymak,Abhimanyu Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TabFM——一个 400M 参数的表格基础模型，将监督式表格预测转化为上下文学习（in-context learning），无需任务特定调参即可在单次前向传播中给出校准的零样本预测。模型完全基于结构因果模型生成的合成表格训练。在 TabArena 全部 51 个基准数据集上，零样本 TabFM 在默认表格基础模型中排名第一，并超过调优后的 AutoML 流程。基于同一冻结权重的两个扩展（TabFM+ 与 TabFM-Auto）进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格机器学习依赖逐个数据集的工作流：要么拟合树集成模型，要么为每个任务从头运行 AutoML 搜索。这种"每数据集一次"的范式缺乏跨任务迁移能力，计算与工程成本高。作者希望验证：表格数据领域能否像自然语言处理那样，通过一个通用基础模型实现零样本、免调参的预测。

Method: 1) 构建 400M 参数的表格基础模型 TabFM，将监督式表格预测建模为上下文学习问题，通过单次前向传播完成预测；2) 训练数据完全使用结构因果模型（Structural Causal Models）生成的合成表格，以学习可迁移的通用表格表示；3) 提出两个共享同一冻结权重的扩展：TabFM+（多视图特征扩展 + 集成 + 事后校准）和 TabFM-Auto（由 LLM 引导、针对具体数据集的数据处理与特征工程）。

Result: 在 TabArena 全部 51 个基准数据集（38 个分类、13 个回归）上：零样本 TabFM 在默认表格基础模型中排名第一，并优于经过调优的 AutoML 流程；TabFM+ 与 TabFM-Auto 在此基础上进一步在分类和回归两个方向上提升性能。

Conclusion: 表格预测可以有效转化为上下文学习任务，仅用合成数据训练的基础模型即可零样本迁移到真实表格任务，并在广泛基准上超越传统调优 AutoML，表明表格领域存在通用、可迁移的基础模型范式。

Abstract: Tabular machine learning typically relies on per-dataset workflows, fitting tree ensembles or running AutoML searches from scratch for every task. We present TabFM, a 400M-parameter tabular foundation model that formulates supervised tabular prediction as in-context learning. TabFM produces calibrated zero-shot predictions in a single forward pass without task-specific tuning. Trained entirely on synthetic tables generated from structural causal models, TabFM learns general tabular representations that transfer zero-shot to real-world tasks. Across all 51 benchmark datasets in TabArena (38 classification and 13 regression), zero-shot TabFM ranks first among default tabular foundation models and outperforms tuned AutoML pipelines. Two extensions over the same frozen weights improve performance further on both tracks: multi-view feature expansion with ensembling and post-hoc calibration (TabFM+), and LLM-guided, dataset-specific data processing and feature engineering (TabFM-Auto).

</details>


### [312] [On Trajectory-Aware Training for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37974)
*Manuel Madeira,Amitis Shidani,Alice Bizeul,Victor Turrisi,Louis Béthune,Bhavika Devnani,Dan Busbridge,Pierre Ablin,João Monteiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PUMBA是一个轨迹感知训练统一框架，通过让去噪器在策略诱导轨迹的连续步骤上训练、步骤间传递连续信息并进行时间反向传播联合优化，解决了掩码扩散模型训练与推理条件不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型（MDM）在训练与推理时条件不一致：训练基于随机掩码序列，而推理轨迹由模型自身预测塑造，且每步无法访问上一步的计算结果。现有方法从不同角度分别缓解这些局限，但它们之间的相互作用尚不清楚。

Method: 提出PUMBA统一框架，在策略诱导轨迹的连续步骤上训练去噪器，步骤间传递信息，并通过时间反向传播（BPTT）联合优化。对该设计空间进行受控研究，分析训练-推理对齐、信息传递方式以及BPTT步数的影响。

Result: 受控研究得出三点：i) 精确训练-推理对齐因局部过拟合而失败，较松的对齐反而使训练掩码更接近推理时掩码；ii) 传递连续信息在各步承诺上优于离散梯度估计器；iii) 性能随BPTT跨度增加而提升，并给出理论支持。组合后性能可比肩同规模自回归模型的最佳检查点。扩展到LLaDA-8B的有监督微调后，在全画布和块扩散生成中均改善性能与NFE的权衡：性能相同时全画布生成比标准微调（预算翻倍）少用最多22% NFE，块扩散中相同步数下少用最多26%。

Conclusion: PUMBA通过统一的轨迹感知训练有效缩小了掩码扩散模型训练与推理之间的差距，并在大规模监督微调中显著提升了性能与计算效率的权衡。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate text by unmasking several tokens per step, but they are trained and sampled under different conditions. The model is trained on randomly masked sequences, whereas inference follows a trajectory shaped by the model's own predictions. Additionally, each step has no access to what the previous one computed. Recent methods narrow these limitations from separate angles, leaving open how these choices interact. We introduce PUMBA, a unified framework for trajectory-aware training that trains the denoiser on consecutive steps of policy-induced trajectories, passes information between steps, and optimizes them jointly by backpropagation through time. A controlled study of this design space shows that i) exact train--inference alignment fails due to local overfitting, whereas a looser alignment still brings training masks closer to those seen at inference; ii) passing continuous information outperforms discrete gradient estimators through the commitment at each step; and iii) performance improves as backpropagation through time spans more steps, which we support theoretically. Combined, these components match the best checkpoint of a same-size autoregressive model. Building on these findings, we scale PUMBA to supervised fine-tuning of LLaDA-8B, where it improves the trade-off between performance and number of function evaluations (NFEs) in both full-canvas and block diffusion generation. At matched performance, it needs up to 22% fewer NFEs than standard fine-tuning with twice the budget in full-canvas generation, and up to 26% fewer than standard fine-tuning for the same number of steps in block diffusion.

</details>


### [313] [$S^3$: Spectral Null-Space Swap Makes Reasoning Models Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37976)
*Hongbo Ma,Sansheng Cao,Jiajun Fan,Bangji Yang,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现推理能力的核心在于 Thinking 模型权重中位于 Non-thinking 模型主导奇异方向投影的零空间分量，并提出免训练的 Spectral Null-Space Swap ($S^3$)，在不损失准确率的前提下提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 使用思维链推理虽强，但 token 开销过大。现有工作多在主导子空间内操作，未揭示零空间的关键作用，作者希望利用零空间实现高效模型优化。

Method: 提出 $S^3$，一种免训练的组合方法：保留 Non-thinking 模型在其自身主导子空间内的分量，同时从 Thinking 检查点中取位于该子空间之外（零空间）的分量进行组合。

Result: 在 2B-30B 的稠密与 MoE 架构、28 个评测环境（数学、多模态、音频推理）上验证。相比完整 Thinking 模型，平均减少 27.4% 推理 token 开销，同时整体准确率提升 1.0 个百分点（如 HMMT25 上准确率 +8.3%，token 提速 33.0%），建立新的 Pareto 前沿。

Conclusion: $S^3$ 揭示了零空间在推理中的关键作用并有效提升效率；通过注意力熵解释，保留的分量产生更集中的注意力，并借助简化优化模型说明零空间能降低注意力熵从而提升推理效率。

Abstract: LLMs trained with Chain-of-thought excel in reasoning capability, but often come with excessive token cost. We find that the core of reasoning capacity lies in the Thinking model's weight component within the null space of a projection defined by the corresponding Non-thinking model's dominant singular directions, and removing the subspace component can largely improve reasoning efficiency without hurting the accuracy gained during thinking-mode post-training. Unlike existing efforts that mostly operate within the dominant subspace, we are the first to unveil the critical role of the null space and harness it for model optimization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Spectral Null-Space Swap ($S^3$), a training-free composition of paired Non-thinking and Thinking checkpoints. Our method keeps the Non-thinking model inside its own dominant subspace and takes the Thinking checkpoint outside it, improving reasoning efficiency while maintaining accuracy. We extensively evaluate $S^3$ on 2B-30B dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures spanning 28 evaluation environments across mathematical, multimodal, and audio reasoning domains. $S^3$ establishes new empirical Pareto Frontiers among training-free model composition strategies: across all settings, it reduces inference token overhead by an average of 27.4% compared to full Thinking models while simultaneously improving overall task accuracy by 1.0 percentage point (e.g., yielding +8.3% accuracy on HMMT25 alongside a 33.0% token speedup). We further use attention entropy for explanation and find that the retained component produces more concentrated attention, and we use a simplified analytical model about optimization to demonstrate why null-space can effectively reduce attention entropy, thereby improving the efficiency of reasoning.

</details>


### [314] [TabFM-Auto: Self-Evolving Pipelines for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37989)
*Deqing Fu,Huangyuan Su,Rajat Sen,Taman Narayan,Sujay Sanghavi,Abhimanyu Das,Weihao Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabFM-Auto将表格基础模型TabFM与语言模型智能体结合，通过进化数据管线降低TabFM误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型忽略列名、任务描述和辅助文件等语义信息，而自进化MLE智能体逐数据集从零训练，联合搜索特征、架构和超参数噪声大且易过拟合。

Method: 用数据集元数据和验证反馈引导语言模型智能体，迭代优化数据清洗、特征工程、上下文选择和后处理，以降低TabFM的误差。

Result: 在TabArena的51个数据集上，五种配置包揽前五名，最佳将TabFM从1785提升到2013 Elo；发现的管线零搜索迁移到其他冻结表格基础模型（+69至+143 Elo）；在MLE-Bench的8个表格竞赛中总体排名第一。

Conclusion: 围绕冻结表格基础模型进化数据管线，可有效利用数据集语义并超越现有MLE智能体。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models achieve strong zero-shot accuracy on structured data by pretraining on synthetic tables, but they ignore the column names, task descriptions, and auxiliary files that carry dataset semantics. Meanwhile, self-evolving machine learning engineering (MLE) agents train models from scratch on each dataset, yet jointly searching over features, architectures, and hyperparameters is noisy and prone to overfitting. We introduce TabFM-Auto, which pairs a tabular foundation model, TabFM, with a language model agent that evolves the data pipeline around it. Guided by dataset metadata and validation feedback, TabFM-Auto iteratively refines data cleaning, feature engineering, context selection, and post-processing to reduce TabFM's error. Across all 51 datasets of the TabArena benchmark, five TabFM-Auto configurations with different agents and language models take the top five overall positions, and the best raises TabFM from 1785 to 2013 Elo. The discovered pipelines also transfer to other frozen tabular foundation models (+69 to +143 Elo) with no further search. On the 8 tabular competitions of MLE-Bench, TabFM-Auto ranks first overall among MLE agents.

</details>


### [315] [No Scale Left Behind: Multi-Scale Autoencoder with Bi-directional Attention for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38004)
*Jiaheng Guo,Haochen Zhang,Yu-Chao Huang,Jinhao Duan,Nicholas Konz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MSCAD，一种基于并行自编码器分支与跨尺度注意力的半监督多尺度时序异常检测框架，在TSB-AD基准上大幅超越50个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序异常跨越从亚秒级点尖峰到多小时漂移模式的巨大时间尺度差异，现有TSAD方法多局限于单一时间粒度，多尺度设计要么孤立分析各尺度，要么受限于预定义的粗到细层次结构，均无法充分捕捉多尺度交互。

Method: MSCAD采用对应不同patch size的并行自编码器分支，并堆叠对称双向跨尺度注意力块，使任意一对尺度在重建前交换信息，且不让任何单一尺度获得特权。

Result: 在综合的TSB-AD基准（40个数据集、530条序列）上，MSCAD在多指标下相较50个基线取得大幅性能提升，单变量划分VUS-PR达0.57（+9.6%），多变量划分0.47（+9.3%），均优于当前最优方法。

Conclusion: 对称双向跨尺度注意力机制可有效建模多尺度交互，MSCAD作为一个简单而强大的半监督框架，显著提升了多尺度时序异常检测性能。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) plays a crucial role in healthcare, finance, industrial monitoring, and other sectors. Within and between these settings, anomalies span vastly different temporal scales, from sub-second point spikes to multi-hour drift patterns. However, most existing TSAD methods commit to a single temporal granularity, and multi-scale designs either analyze different scales in isolation or are constrained to a predefined coarse-to-fine hierarchy, both failing to sufficiently capture multi-scale interactions. To resolve this limitation, we propose Multi-Scale Autoencoder with Cross-Scale Attention for TSAD (MSCAD), a simple yet powerful semi-supervised TSAD framework founded on parallel autoencoder branches corresponding to different patch sizes. A stack of symmetric bidirectional cross-scale attention blocks enables every pair of scales to exchange information before reconstruction without allowing any single scale to be privileged. On the comprehensive TSB-AD benchmark (40 datasets, 530 series), MSCAD achieves large performance gains against 50 baselines across multiple metrics, with VUS-PR of 0.57(+9.6%) on the univariate split and 0.47(+9.3%) on the multivariate split compared to the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [316] [When do data mixtures improve scaling laws? Insights from high-dimensional regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38011)
*Diyuan Wu,Lehan Chen,Theodor Misiakiewicz,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过高维混合数据回归模型，理论刻画了辅助数据在何种条件下能真正改善缩放律，并给出组合数据优于单域训练的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据混合与重加权研究多偏经验，尚不清楚辅助数据何时能真正改善缩放律，而非仅增加样本量。

Method: 研究共享回归函数、异质协方差与噪声、样本量可不同速率增长的高维混合数据回归模型；在椭球参数约束下建立一般协方差结构的极小极大风险，推导可交换协方差下岭回归测试误差的确定性等价，并针对对齐幂律协方差谱的目标域与辅助域给出显式缩放律。

Result: 理论给出以谱衰减、目标正则性和两数据集相对增长表达的显式缩放律，识别出混合数据可证明比单域训练获得更快缩放速率的区域；语言模型实验显示相同定性现象。

Conclusion: 改善缩放律需要谱结构与两域相对样本量之间的特定相互作用；合适的数据混合能比单域训练更快降低目标域测试损失。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems are trained on mixtures of data from different domains, and choosing the right mixture can substantially improve downstream performance. Despite an extensive literature on data mixing and reweighting, existing work is largely empirical and it remains unclear when auxiliary data genuinely improves scaling laws rather than merely providing more samples. To gain insight into this question, we study a high-dimensional mixed-data regression model with a shared regression function, heterogeneous covariances and noise levels, and dataset sizes that may grow at different rates. We establish the minimax risk under an ellipsoidal parameter constraint for the general covariance structure and derive deterministic equivalents for the test error of ridge regression under commutative covariances. We then specialize to a target domain and an auxiliary domain with aligned power-law covariance spectra, where the theory yields explicit scaling laws in terms of spectral decay, target regularity, and the relative growth of the two datasets. These laws identify regimes in which combining data mixtures provably yields a faster scaling rate than using either dataset alone. In particular, improving the scaling law requires a specific interplay between spectra and relative sample sizes of the domains. Our numerical experiments on language models exhibit the same qualitative phenomenon: appropriate data mixtures yield a faster decrease in target-domain test loss than training on either domain alone.

</details>


### [317] [Prompts Live on an Arc: Gaussian Curricula in Fisher--Rao Coordinates for Rollout-Efficient GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38018)
*Mei Okonkwo,Pixel Nomand,Julian Berg,Elena Voss,Lena Park,Marcus Hale,Adrian Cho,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在Bernoulli Fisher-Rao流形的弧长坐标ψ=arcsin√p下，GRPO的期望更新近似均匀、零方差组概率有界、pass-rate证据噪声恒定，并据此设计ARCUS采样器，通过Kalman滤波与目标匹配的高斯核实现课程式提示词选择，显著提升GRPO准确率并减少rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO仅从采样响应存在分歧的提示词中学习：全对或全错的组奖励方差为零，贡献不了梯度却仍消耗rollouts。现有提示词选择方法虽能将采样引导至中间通过率，但其目标、宽度与不确定性模型均在原始通过率或logit坐标下启发式选取，缺乏自然统一的坐标系。

Method: 作者证明弧长ψ=arcsin√p是GRPO通过率的自然坐标：在弧长下期望GRPO更新近乎均匀（仅两端有斜坡），零方差组的概率由宽度为1/√(2G)的两个高斯边界层界定，通过率证据噪声恒定，且pass@k与pass^k目标的梯度为高斯分布。基于此提出ARCUS：用Kalman滤波在弧长上追踪每个提示词，以目标匹配的高斯核乘以预测的informative组概率进行打分，仅保留informative组用于不变的GRPO更新，并将目标节奏推进至预测产出仍接近最优的最难目标。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准和三种backbone上，ARCUS将GRPO的平均准确率提升2.8-2.9个百分点，将dynamic sampling提升1.1-1.2个百分点，同时相比dynamic sampling减少48-57%的rollouts。

Conclusion: 通过在Bernoulli Fisher-Rao流形的弧长坐标下重新表述GRPO的通过率动态，可将提示词课程统一为弧长上的高斯分布，其中心选择等价于目标选择。ARCUS作为即插即用采样器，实现了准确率提升与采样开销降低的双重收益。

Abstract: Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns only from prompts whose sampled responses disagree: a group that is entirely correct or entirely incorrect has zero reward variance, contributes no gradient, and still consumes its rollouts. Prompt-selection methods reduce this waste by steering sampling toward intermediate pass rates, but they choose the target, its width, and the uncertainty model heuristically, in raw pass-rate or logit coordinates. We show that GRPO comes with a natural coordinate for pass rates: the arc length $ψ=\arcsin\sqrt{p}$ on the Bernoulli Fisher--Rao manifold. In arc length, the expected GRPO update is uniform up to two boundary ramps; the probability of a zero-variance group is bounded by two Gaussian boundary layers of width $1/\sqrt{2G}$; pass-rate evidence has constant noise; and the gradients of the pass@$k$ and pass$^k$ objectives are Gaussians whose center and width follow from $k$ in closed form. A prompt curriculum for GRPO is therefore a Gaussian in arc length, and choosing its center amounts to choosing the objective. We turn this observation into ARCUS, a drop-in sampler that tracks every prompt with a Kalman filter in arc length, scores prompts by an objective-matched Gaussian kernel times the predicted probability of an informative group, keeps only informative groups for the unchanged GRPO update, and paces the target toward the hardest objective whose predicted yield stays within a small slack of the best. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three backbones, ARCUS improves the average accuracy of GRPO by 2.8--2.9 points and that of dynamic sampling by 1.1--1.2 points, while generating 48--57\% fewer rollouts than dynamic sampling.

</details>


### [318] [Dr. OPD: Learning What to Follow for Optimal On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38025)
*Zhenyu Wang,Tianze Wang,Linjun Zhang,Yifan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dr. OPD，将 on-policy 蒸馏建模为双层优化，为每个 token 的教师监督学习最优权重，从而最大化学生期望奖励，在数学和代码任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 on-policy 蒸馏（OPD）平等对待所有 token 的教师信号，但不同 token 的教师监督对学生最终表现影响差异巨大：有些能纠正关键推理错误，有些几乎不影响最终答案。因此需要对 token 级监督进行加权。

Method: 将最优加权 OPD（Dr. OPD）形式化为双层优化问题：内层学生基于加权教师监督学习，外层选择权重以最大化学生的期望奖励。提出高效迭代求解器，交替更新 token 权重与学生策略：每轮以闭式解更新权重，然后在加权 OPD 目标上走一步梯度。在正则条件下证明该加权更新可获得比 vanilla OPD 更高的期望奖励。

Result: 在数学和代码任务上、跨强到弱与同规模蒸馏设置中，Dr. OPD 一致优于所有评估基线。尤其在强到弱蒸馏中，平均数学性能比 vanilla OPD 提升 9.7 分，并使较小的学生模型超越其更大的教师模型。

Conclusion: Dr. OPD 通过对 token 级教师监督进行可学习加权，把 OPD 重构为奖励驱动的双层优化，理论与实验均表明其优于均匀加权的 vanilla OPD。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own generated responses using dense, token-level supervision from a stronger teacher. Vanilla OPD treats all teacher signals equally, assuming that the teacher's supervision is equally important for every token. However, teacher signals at different tokens may have very different effects on the student's performance: some correct important reasoning errors, while others have little effect on the final answer. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Dr. OPD (OPD Done Right), which defines the optimal weighted OPD to maximize the student's performance. We formulate Dr. OPD as a bilevel optimization problem in which the student learns from weighted teacher supervision, while the weights are selected to maximize the expected reward of the resulting student. To solve Dr. OPD, we develop an efficient iterative solver that updates the token weights and student policy alternatively. At each round, it updates weights in closed form and then takes one gradient step on the resulting weighted OPD objective. Under regularity conditions, we show that this weighted update achieves a higher expected reward than a vanilla OPD update. Empirically, across strong-to-weak and same-size distillation on math and code, Dr. OPD consistently outperforms all evaluated baselines. In particular, in the strong-to-weak distillation setting, Dr. OPD improves average math performance by $9.7$ points over vanilla OPD, and enables the smaller student to surpass its larger teacher.

</details>


### [319] [Improving Function Space Flow Matching with Kernel Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38049)
*Fred Xu,Thomas Markovich,Barbora Barancikova,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出核函数流匹配（kFFM），用核诱导代价下的熵最优传输替代FFM的独立端点配对，理论上有界且离散不变，实验上在时间序列与PDE基准上改善分布匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数值数据（时间序列、PDE解）的生成模型需在无穷维空间学习分布。现有Functional Flow Matching（FFM）沿用标准流匹配的独立端点配对：批次内先验与数据样本随意匹配，导致条件桥同时承担数据集全局结构与实例残差。在函数空间中修正该问题更难，因为函数空间上的最优传输（OT）难以形式化，欧氏替代会忽略函数值数据的几何结构。

Method: 提出kernel Functional Flow Matching（kFFM）：将独立配对替换为核诱导代价下的熵最优传输，即采用Hilbert Sinkhorn Divergence（HSD）背后的耦合，保持FFM的神经算子架构不变。理论上证明核代价与HSD目标在Banach环境空间上一致有界且适定；给出与紧致度量空间上二次代价OT的误差分解，分离出不可约的核代价失配项；并证明离散化不变性界，其速率由Sobolev正则性决定。

Result: 在时间序列与PDE基准上，kFFM相较FFM、扩散、对抗及有限维OT基线改善分布匹配；相对FFM有显著的配对种子增益，且在非核与物理诊断下增益持续，包括湍流Navier-Stokes基准。有界核代价已优于原始L2 Sinkhorn，函数空间感知核（signature、Sobolev RBF）在粗糙或路径值数据上进一步提升。

Conclusion: 核诱导代价的熵OT配对是提升函数空间生成建模的有效途径，兼具理论适定性、离散不变性与实证优势。

Abstract: Generative models for function-valued data, such as time series and solutions of partial differential equations, must learn distributions over infinite-dimensional spaces. Functional Flow Matching (FFM) extends Flow Matching to this setting, learning a velocity field whose flow transports a Gaussian prior to the data distribution, but it inherits the independent endpoint pairing of standard Flow Matching: in each batch, prior and data samples are matched arbitrarily, so the conditional bridge must traverse both the shared global structure of the dataset and instance-specific residuals. In function space this is harder to fix than in finite dimensions, since optimal transport (OT) on function spaces is delicate to formulate and a flat Euclidean surrogate ignores the geometry that distinguishes function-valued data. We propose kernel Functional Flow Matching (kFFM), which replaces the independent pairing by entropic OT under a kernel-induced cost, the coupling underlying the Hilbert Sinkhorn Divergence (HSD), leaving the FFM neural-operator architecture unchanged. We prove that the kernel cost and the HSD objective are uniformly bounded and well-posed on Banach ambient spaces, derive an error decomposition against quadratic-cost OT on compact metric spaces that isolates an irreducible kernel-cost mismatch term, and prove a discretization-invariance bound whose rate is governed by Sobolev regularity. Empirically, kFFM improves distributional matching over FFM, diffusion, adversarial, and finite-dimensional OT baselines on time-series and PDE benchmarks, with significant paired-seed gains over FFM and improvements that persist under non-kernel and physics-based diagnostics, including a turbulent Navier-Stokes benchmark. Bounded kernel costs already outperform raw $L^2$ Sinkhorn, and function-space-aware kernels (signature, Sobolev RBF) give further gains on rough or path-valued data.

</details>


### [320] [Jaxolotl: A Unified High-Performance Benchmark Suite for LTL-Based Multi-Task RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38065)
*Mathias Jackermeier,Jacques Cloete,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Jaxolotl：一个面向多任务 LTL-RL 的统一高性能基准套件，包含 6 种代表性算法、4 个环境、新任务套件与标准化评估协议，通过将符号任务表示预编译为静态数组实现全 JIT 训练与评估，速度提升最高 220 倍，并据此系统评估现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练智能体遵循任意指令是多任务强化学习的重要目标，线性时序逻辑（LTL）为指令提供了精确、结构化的形式化描述，已被成功用于训练通用多任务策略。然而，现有方法在实现、任务分布和评估协议上存在差异，难以公平比较；同时高计算成本限制了实验规模与统计可靠性。

Method: Jaxolotl 提供模块化、端到端的 JAX 实现，涵盖 6 种代表性算法与 4 个环境，并配套新整理的任务套件和标准化的、具有统计稳健性的评估协议。其核心是将符号任务表示预编译为静态数组，从而实现完全 JIT 编译的训练与评估。

Result: 该框架实现端到端最高 220 倍加速，支持在显著更大实验规模下进行受控比较。系统评估揭示了现有方法的互补优势与局限：具备非短视推理能力的通用方法在命题数量增加时表现变差；而扩展性更强的方法依赖环境特定假设并存在短视问题。

Conclusion: Jaxolotl 通过统一实现、任务套件与评估协议，为多任务 LTL-RL 提供了高效、可复现且统计可靠的基准平台，并揭示了现有方法在可扩展性与非短视推理之间的权衡，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: Training agents to follow arbitrary instructions is an important goal of multi-task reinforcement learning (RL). Linear temporal logic (LTL) provides a precise and structured formalism for specifying instructions to agents, and has been successfully adopted for training generalist multi-task policies. However, differences in implementations, task distributions, and evaluation protocols make existing methods difficult to compare, while high computational costs limit the scale and statistical reliability of experiments. We introduce Jaxolotl, a unified high-performance benchmark suite for multi-task LTL-RL to address these concerns. Jaxolotl provides a modular, end-to-end JAX implementation of six representative algorithms and four environments, together with newly curated task suites and a standardised, statistically robust evaluation protocol. By precompiling symbolic task representations into static arrays, Jaxolotl enables fully JIT-compiled training and evaluation, achieving end-to-end speedups of up to $220\times$ and supporting controlled comparisons at substantially greater experimental scale. We use this framework to systematically evaluate existing approaches, revealing complementary strengths and limitations: general methods capable of non-myopic reasoning struggle as the number of propositions grows, while methods with stronger scaling rely on environment-specific assumptions and suffer from myopia.

</details>


### [321] [Alpha Diffusion Language Models: Factorization Alone Is Not the Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38066)
*Nikita Gushchin,Dmitry Baranchuk,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AlphaDLM，用序列级alpha损失训练离散扩散语言模型，缓解并行去噪的预测不一致问题，以极少模型评估次数提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型能并行生成多个token，但减少去噪步数会导致预测不一致。标准交叉熵训练只拟合条件token边缘分布，而并行生成需要一致的联合预测。

Method: 提出Alpha扩散语言模型（AlphaDLM），采用序列级alpha损失进行训练：该损失在alpha趋向0时退化为交叉熵，在alpha=1时具有联合模式最优。通过分析刻画目标函数与分解方式如何共同决定拟合分布，并给出在中间alpha下保留多个有效补全、同时排除无效token组合的条件。在TinyGSM上训练，并扩展到SDAR-1.7B。

Result: 在TinyGSM上训练后，仅用四次模型评估即在GSM8K上达到34.6%准确率。进一步扩展至SDAR-1.7B，并在代码与数学基准上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 改变训练目标可以改善分解式扩散语言模型的准确率-计算量权衡。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models can generate multiple tokens in parallel, but reducing the number of denoising steps can lead to inconsistent predictions. Standard cross-entropy training fits conditional token marginals, whereas parallel generation requires consistent joint predictions. We introduce Alpha Diffusion Language Models (AlphaDLM), trained with a sequence-level alpha loss that recovers cross-entropy in the limit of vanishing alpha and has a joint-mode optimum at alpha one. Our analysis characterizes how the objective and factorization jointly determine the fitted distribution. We identify conditions under which intermediate alpha preserves multiple valid completions while excluding invalid token combinations. Trained on TinyGSM, our method achieves 34.6% accuracy on GSM8K with only four model evaluations. We further scale the method to SDAR-1.7B and evaluate it on code and mathematics benchmarks. These results show that changing the training objective can improve the accuracy-computation trade-off of factorized diffusion language models.

</details>


### [322] [A foundation model for energy and radiation systems built on heterogeneous scientific interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38067)
*Samrendra Roy,Tapas Tripura,Yoon Pyo Lee,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEODE框架，将任务特定的科学接口与共享的路由小波算子库耦合，评估科学基础模型在异构物理接口下的多任务覆盖、保留性与预训练复用这三个独立属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学基础模型通常在异构物理问题已被转化为兼容的网格化、标记化或符号表示后才进行评估，这使得科学接口被排除在预训练模型和复用审计之外。作者希望研究保留原生推理类别的互补设定，探讨多任务覆盖、参数保留与预训练复用是否可被独立检验。

Method: GEODE将任务特定的科学接口耦合到共享的路由小波算子库。用单个联合预训练模型表示空腔流动、辐射剂量和弹塑性应力，再通过训练仅含2.1%参数的私有接口获得换热器和反应堆子通道任务。对比参数隔离与无限制微调，并采用范数匹配的随机库对照实验，分析预训练计算的贡献是否依赖任务和数据机制。另外分解全相对L2误差与空间变化误差的关系。

Result: 参数隔离使早期预测保持不变，而无限制微调使其退化14-29倍。范数匹配的随机库对照表明，预训练计算的贡献取决于任务和数据机制，仅有保留并不能确立复用。误差分解显示，当场水平主导范数时，全相对L2误差可能大幅低估相对于空间变化的误差。任务特定的算子在五个问题中的三个上仍更准确。

Conclusion: 多任务覆盖、保留性与预训练复用是三个独立属性，当科学基础模型跨异构接口时，必须分别对它们进行检验。

Abstract: Scientific foundation models are commonly evaluated after heterogeneous physical problems have already been translated into a compatible gridded, tokenized or symbolic representation. This leaves the scientific interface outside both the pretrained model and the audit of what is actually reused. We study the complementary setting in which boundary histories, sparse monitor records and loading histories retain their native inference classes and their outputs remain on Cartesian, latitude-longitude and unstructured domains. GEODE couples task-specific scientific interfaces to a shared routed library of wavelet operators. A single jointly pretrained model represents cavity flow, radiation dose and elastoplastic stress, then acquires a heat exchanger and a reactor subchannel by training a private interface containing 2.1% of its parameters. Earlier predictions remain unchanged by parameter isolation, whereas unrestricted fine-tuning degrades them by factors of 14-29. Crucially, preservation alone does not establish reuse: norm-matched randomized-library controls show that the contribution of pretrained computation is conditional on the task and data regime. A separate decomposition shows that full-field relative L2 error can substantially understate error relative to spatial variation when field level dominates the norm. Task-specific operators remain more accurate on three of the five problems. These results distinguish multi-task coverage, preservation and pretrained reuse as separate properties that must be tested independently when scientific foundation models span heterogeneous interfaces.

</details>


### [323] [Traversing the solution space of neural networks with Hessian Null Space Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38081)
*Ann Huang,Mitchell Ostrow,Zhouyang Lu,William T. Redman,Leo Kozachkov,Kanaka Rajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Hessian Null Space Continuation (HNC) 方法，首次证明在权空间中局部模态连通区域内存在多种不同的内部机制。HNC 利用局部曲率在不改变网络功能的前提下遍历权空间，可定向寻找具有特定属性的解，并在 RNN、ImageNet 训练的 ViT 及强化学习智能体中展示了大量未被标准梯度优化发现的表征多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在同一任务上可学到多种解，且许多解位于低损失的模态连通区域中，但它们在该区域内的内部计算如何变化尚不清楚。与此同时，表征退化研究发现了内部结构不同却损失相近的多种网络，但这些解在权空间中的关系未明。该论文旨在统一这两条研究线索，探究同一模态连通区域内是否存在不同的内部机制，并量化这种表征多样性对模型理解、合并、编辑与微调的潜在影响。

Method: 提出 Hessian Null Space Continuation (HNC)：一种可扩展方法，利用局部曲率在保持网络功能不变的前提下遍历权空间，并可被引导朝向具有指定属性的解。将该方法应用于 RNN 记忆任务、ImageNet 训练的 Vision Transformer 和强化学习智能体，并对解集的局部几何进行测量。

Result: 在 RNN 记忆任务中，HNC 在保持行为的同时达到显著不同的表征与动态；在 ImageNet 训练的 ViT 中，HNC 找到的表征与原始网络的差异超过任何采用不同架构或目标的独立训练模型；在强化学习智能体中，HNC 在相近回报下发现了一种不同的导航策略，并在 AI Safety Gridworld 中暴露了奖励黑客行为。此外，HNC 测量了解集的局部几何，显示模型规模和任务复杂度如何影响其维度与功能敏感性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在单个已训练解附近存在大量标准梯度优化未见过的表征多样性。HNC 能够识别并量化这种多样性，为解空间的机制性理解以及模型合并、编辑和微调开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: On a single task, deep networks can learn many solutions, depending on their optimizer, training data, architecture, and hyperparameters. Many of these solutions are mode-connected: rather than isolated points in weight space, they are connected by low-loss regions. Yet how their internal computation varies within these regions is unknown. A parallel line of work has identified the degeneracy of neural representations: many networks reach similar training loss with distinct internal structures. However, it is unclear how these solutions are related in weight space. We unify these subfields and show for the first time that many different internal mechanisms exist within a local mode-connected region in weight space. To do so, we introduce Hessian Null Space Continuation (HNC), a scalable method that uses local curvature to traverse regions of weight space that preserve network function, and can be steered toward solutions with specified properties. In RNNs trained on a memory task, HNC reaches drastically different representations and dynamics with maintained behavior. In ImageNet-trained Vision Transformers, HNC finds representations that differ more from the original network than any independently trained model with a different architecture or objective. In reinforcement-learning agents, HNC uncovers a distinct navigation strategy at comparable return and exposes reward hacking in an AI Safety Gridworld. Finally, HNC measures the local geometry of the solution set, showing how model size and task complexity shape its dimension and functional sensitivity. Our results show that a surprisingly large amount of representational diversity exists near a single trained solution, unseen by standard gradient-based optimization. HNC identifies and quantifies this diversity, opening new possibilities for mechanistic understanding of solution spaces and for model merging, editing, and fine-tuning.

</details>


### [324] [Mira: Memory-Efficient MoE Inference Using Adaptive Caching and Predictive Expert Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38090)
*Sanjali Yadav,Bahar Asgari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mira 通过算法-系统协同设计，在单 GPU 上实现高容量 MoE 推理，核心是用预测式专家管理与定制量化格式将原有的反应式卸载/缓存转变为主动预取，显著降低专家导致的停顿并大幅提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型适合在资源受限的单 GPU 上扩展容量，但专家参数占用大量显存，且 token 级路由动态、不可预测且分布偏斜。现有卸载与缓存方案本质上是反应式的，必须等路由器输出后才移动专家，导致缓存利用率低，且在显存预算紧张时无法将传输与计算重叠。

Method: Mira 采用算法-系统协同设计：一是引入轻量级逐层预测器，提前两层预判专家使用情况，实现主动预取；二是构建 HOT+STAGE 两级 GPU 缓存，利用 token 级路由遥测保留高频专家、暂存预测专家；三是设计面向专家参数的自定义压缩格式，减少元数据并提升打包效率，同时尽量不损失精度。整体实现为集成运行时，协调预测器、缓存策略与量化传输以最大化通信与计算重叠。

Result: 实验显示 Mira 减少了专家引发的停顿。在显存受限 GPU 上，相比最先进基线，平均吞吐提升 5.71 倍；首 token 时间加速 11.71 倍；beam search 推理平均加速 3.84 倍，证明其在不同推理场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: Mira 表明，将反应式专家管理转变为基于预测的主动预取，并结合定制量化传输与缓存策略，可有效支撑单 GPU 上的高容量 MoE 推理，并在吞吐、首 token 延迟与 beam search 场景中取得显著加速。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are a compelling architecture for scaling model capacity, making them especially attractive for deployment on resource-constrained, single-GPU systems. However, this benefit is difficult to realize because expert parameters dominate memory, and token-level routing is dynamic, unpredictable, and skewed. Prior work using offloading and caching remains fundamentally reactive, as systems wait for router outputs before moving experts, leading to inefficient cache utilization and an inability to overlap transfers with compute under tight VRAM budgets.
  To address these challenges, we propose Mira, an algorithm-system co-design that enables high-capacity MoE inference on a single GPU. Mira shifts from a reactive to a proactive stance by coupling predictive expert management with a tailored quantization format. It introduces lightweight per-layer predictors that anticipate expert usage two layers ahead, enabling proactive prefetching. These predictions feed a two-tier HOT+STAGE GPU cache managed by token-level routing telemetry to retain frequently used experts while staging predicted ones. To minimize transfer overhead, Mira implements a custom compression for expert parameters, which reduces metadata and improves packing efficiency, while minimally degrading accuracy.
  Mira is implemented as a fully integrated runtime that coordinates predictors, caching policies, and quantized transfers to maximize overlap between communication and compute. Our experiments show that Mira reduces expert-induced stalls. Compared against state-of-the-art baselines, Mira achieves a 5.71x speedup in average throughput on a memory-constrained GPU. It accelerates Time-to-First-Token by 11.71x and achieves a 3.84$x average speedup in beam search inference, demonstrating its effectiveness across diverse inference scenarios.

</details>


### [325] [Dimensionally consistent surrogate modelling through dimensional analysis and harmonic expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38094)
*Ernest Tarrus,Hector Gisbert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动方法，在假设类层面构建满足量纲齐次性约束的代理模型，通过Buckingham Π群、量纲前因子与截断调和展开实现，并在多个物理问题上验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量纲齐次性是物理模型的基本约束，即模型需在单位变换下保持不变。作者希望在数据驱动建模中从假设类层面直接嵌入这一约束，从而提升模型的条件性、抗噪性与样本效率。

Method: 从测量变量的量纲矩阵出发推导Buckingham Π群，构造可接受的量纲前因子，并在归一化不变域上用截断调和展开逼近剩余的无量纲依赖；前因子与字典固定后，通过正则化线性回归求取系数。

Result: 在单摆、普朗克黑体定律、双摆Lyapunov场以及COBE/FIRAS实验黑体光谱数据集上测试，结果显示量纲约束相比无约束基线改善了条件性、抗噪性和样本效率；在非周期或多不变量情形下，字典选择变得重要。

Conclusion: 学习到的表达式显式且计算成本低，适合作为结构化物理问题的代理模型；量纲约束在假设类层面嵌入是可行且有益的做法。

Abstract: Dimensional homogeneity is a fundamental constraint on physically meaningful models, requiring invariance under changes of units. We present a data-driven method for constructing surrogate models that satisfy this constraint at the level of the hypothesis class. Starting from a dimension matrix of measured variables, the method derives Buckingham $Π$-groups, constructs admissible dimensional prefactors, and approximates the remaining dimensionless dependence using truncated harmonic expansions on normalized invariant domains. Once the prefactor and dictionary are fixed, the coefficients are obtained from a regularized linear regression problem. We test the approach on the simple pendulum, Planck's black-body law, the double-pendulum Lyapunov field, and an experimental COBE/FIRAS black-body spectrum dataset. The results show that dimensional constraints improve conditioning, robustness to noise, and sample efficiency relative to unconstrained baselines, while the choice of dictionary becomes important in non-periodic or multi-invariant settings. The learned expressions are explicit and inexpensive to evaluate, which makes them useful as surrogate models for structured physical problems.

</details>


### [326] [Probe-Space Preconditioning for Fast and Stable Zero-Order Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38095)
*Francois Chaubard,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Chris Ré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出1.5-SPSA方法，通过重新分配训练计算预算并引入廉价对角预条件器，显著提升了零阶优化（ZOO）的收敛速度，在多个数据集上超越现有ZOO求解器甚至反向传播（BP），同时实现了在普通GPU上高效训练大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播（BP）虽主导深度学习，但内存开销巨大（如训练OPT-30B配Adam需约600GB GPU内存）。零阶优化（ZOO）仅需推理模式内存（约60GB），但不存储激活、梯度和优化器状态。然而，ZOO的收敛速度一直落后于BP，本文旨在缩小这一差距。

Method: 首先，将训练计算预算从大量步骤重新分配到大批量规模（更多扰动/探针但更少步骤），使1SPSA优于MeZO等零阶方法。其次，提出1.5-SPSA，在1SPSA每步基础上增加一次"干净"前向传播，在探针空间计算廉价的对角预条件器，通过降低高曲率方向的权重来提升收敛速率和收敛效果。最后，结合8位打包随机生成器、triton融合解包/应用内核及分布式并行，实现在普通GPU（如A100）上快速稳定地训练大规模模型。

Result: 在Qwen3和OPT模型家族的6个后训练数据集上，1.5-SPSA以更少的优化步骤达到超越先前ZOO求解器的最先进结果。例如，训练OPT-13B时，1.5-SPSA在仅70步内于SST-2上比MeZO和BP均提升+3.1%准确率，而MeZO需100,000步。同时实现高达OPT-30B规模模型在普通GPU上的快速稳定训练。

Conclusion: 1.5-SPSA通过重新分配计算预算和引入对角预条件器，大幅提升了零阶优化的收敛效率，在多个模型和数据集上超越现有ZOO方法乃至BP，并借助高效工程实现使大规模模型训练在普通GPU上成为可能，为内存受限场景下的深度学习训练提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) dominates deep learning but imposes a massive memory tax. For example, training OPT-30B with Adam requires $\approx$ 600GB of GPU memory (assuming batch size 8 and sequence length 2048). Alternatively, zero-order optimization (ZOO) trains in inference-mode (requiring only $\approx$ 60GB for the same model): no stored activations, no gradients, and no optimizer states. However, ZOO convergence has lagged behind BP. In this work, we evaluate two methods to close this gap. First, we show that reallocating training compute budget from many steps to large effective batch sizes with many perturbations (or probes) but fewer steps, allows 1SPSA (Spall, 1992) to outperform zero order methods like MeZO (Malladi et al., 2023) with less training compute. Next, we introduce 1.5-SPSA, adding a single "clean" forward-pass per step to 1SPSA to calculate a cheap diagonal preconditioner in probe-space, which improves convergence rate and convergence by down-weighting high curvature directions. Benchmarking on 6 post-training datasets on both Qwen3 and OPT model families, we show that 1.5-SPSA achieves State-of-the-Art results over previous ZOO solvers with much less optimization steps. For example, we train OPT-13B (for direct comparison to MeZO) and find 1.5-SPSA achieves +3.1% accuracy on SST-2 over both MeZO and BP in only 70 steps vs. MeZO's 100,000 steps. Finally, we combine an 8-bit-packing random generator, triton fused unpack/apply kernels, and distributed parallelism to achieve fast and stable training of models as large as OPT-30B in-place on commodity GPUs (e.g. A100).

</details>


### [327] [Tail-Influence Sampling for CVaR Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38096)
*Pauline Bourigault,Xiaotong Ji,Matthieu Zimmer,Rasul Tutunov,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在固定评估预算下，如何为随机工作流中可分别查询的条件组件分配查询预算，以最准确地估计固定策略的下尾CVaR。作者提出尾部影响（tail influence）概念及其方差导出的最优Neyman分配，并设计了TIS算法及其锚定变体，理论上达到oracle渐近方差，实验中显著降低MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值收益相近的策略在罕见失败上可能差异巨大，因此需要准确估计下尾CVaR。但准确估计下尾CVaR通常需要大量昂贵的rollout。作者希望利用随机工作流中不同条件组件可被单独查询的特性，在固定评估预算下最优地分配查询，以最准确地估计CVaR。

Method: 1) 为每个可查询的条件分布推导"尾部影响"（tail influence），聚合其不确定性在每个Bellman复用环节对CVaR的影响；2) 由该影响的方差得到固定设计效率下界与oracle Neyman分配；3) 提出Tail-Influence Sampling (TIS)，先从试点模型估计影响尺度，再将新查询重新分配给对尾部最重要的核；4) 提出visitation-anchored变体以防止试点分配不足。

Result: 在固定维度和正分位数边际条件下，TIS达到oracle渐近方差和含试点成本的一阶MSE，锚定变体在oracle的两倍以内；刻画了尾部最优与均值最优分配重合的精确网格区域。CliffWalking上，相同计费转移预算下TIS相比学习占用降低41% MSE，相比完整rollout降低76%。在冻结语言模型评审工作流中，锚定TIS在24个MMLU-Pro设置中的23个优于等正则化的均值影响混合方法，并在六次调用FinQA评审上达到比rollout低2.4–3.4倍的MSE。

Conclusion: 通过引入尾部影响并据此分配查询预算，TIS及其锚定变体能在固定预算下显著提升下尾CVaR估计效率，理论上达到oracle水平，并在强化学习与语言模型工作流任务上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Policies with similar mean returns can differ sharply in rare failures, yet estimating lower-tail conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) accurately can require many costly rollouts. When different conditional components of a stochastic workflow can be queried separately, we ask how to allocate a fixed evaluation budget to estimate a fixed policy's CVaR most accurately. We derive a tail influence for each queryable conditional law that aggregates how its uncertainty affects CVaR across every Bellman reuse. Its variance yields the fixed-design efficiency bound and the oracle Neyman allocation. Tail-Influence Sampling (TIS) estimates these influence scales from a pilot model and reallocates fresh queries toward kernels that matter most for the tail; a visitation-anchored variant protects against pilot underallocation. Under fixed dimension and a positive quantile margin, TIS attains oracle asymptotic variance and first-order MSE including pilot cost, while the anchored variant is within a factor two of the oracle. We also characterize an exact-grid regime in which tail- and mean-optimal allocations coincide. On CliffWalking, TIS reduces MSE by 41% versus learned occupancy and 76% versus complete rollouts at the same charged transition budget. In frozen language-model review workflows, anchored TIS beats an equally regularized mean-influence blend in 23 of 24 MMLU-Pro settings and reaches 2.4-3.4$\times$ lower MSE than rollouts on six-call FinQA reviews.

</details>


### [328] [Explore Broadly, Reason Sharply: Push Small Models toward the Frontier via Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38104)
*Panagiotis Theodoropoulos,Nan Jiang,Xintong Duan,Ali Hasan,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Evangelos A. Theodorou,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出并行功率调温（PPT），用并行回火方法实现功率锐化采样的推理时方案，以解决锐化强度带来的探索-利用权衡问题，并显著提升LLM推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功率锐化采样是RL后训练的一种推理时替代方案，能在不更新参数、不使用外部奖励的情况下放大基座模型中的高概率序列，避免RL昂贵的优化和泛化不稳定问题。但该方法面临探索-利用权衡：强锐化会限制探索，使采样陷入看似合理但错误的推理轨迹；弱锐化则导致答案分布过于分散。

Method: 作者提出并行功率调温（PPT），通过并行回火实现功率锐化的LLM采样。方法并行运行多个不同锐化水平且相互交互的副本：低功率副本负责探索多样化的推理轨迹，高功率链则进一步利用锐化目标偏好的高似然响应。针对推理时采样，作者缓解了先前功率采样器中存在的截断偏差，并在有限内存和计算预算下研究了有效的交换策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，PPT显著优于单链功率锐化采样，并且胜过RL后训练模型，能产生更高质量的推理轨迹，甚至达到与前沿模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: PPT通过并行回火有效解决了功率锐化采样的探索-利用权衡，为LLM推理增强提供了一个无需参数更新即可超越RL后训练的有效推理时方案。

Abstract: Power-sharpened sampling is an inference-time alternative to reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training for enhancing reasoning in large language models (LLMs). High-probability sequences are amplified under the base model without parameter updates or external rewards, avoiding the costly optimization and jagged generalization of RL. However, this approach faces a fundamental exploration--exploitation trade-off, as % strong sharpening restricts exploration, trapping samplers in plausible but incorrect reasoning trajectories, whereas weak sharpening leaves the answer distribution diffuse. To resolve this trade-off, we introduce \textbf{Parallel Power Tempering (PPT)}, instantiating power-sharpened LLM sampling via parallel tempering. Running multiple \emph{interacting} replicas in parallel at different sharpening levels allows lower-power replicas to explore diverse reasoning trajectories and higher-power chains to further exploit higher-likelihood responses favored by the sharpened target. Specifically, we tailor \method{} to inference-time sampling by mitigating a truncation bias, identified in prior power samplers, and investigate effective swap strategies under finite memory and compute budgets. Extensive experimentation shows that \method{} substantially improves single-chain power-sharpened sampling and outperforms RL-post-trained models, producing higher-quality reasoning traces and even achieving performance comparable to frontier models.

</details>


### [329] [WUSH-KV: KV Cache Quantization with Data-Adaptive Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38121)
*Jiale Chen,Vage Egiazarian,Eldar Kurtić,Torsten Hoefler,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WUSH-KV 是一种低比特 KV-cache 量化方法，通过数据感知变换降低量化误差，在 2-bit 下性能可比或优于 OSCAR 变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV cache 的内存占用和带宽开销随上下文长度和批大小增长，制约了高效的长上下文推理，因此需要低比特 KV-cache 量化来解决该瓶颈。

Method: WUSH-KV 基于 WUSH 方法，利用矩阵乘积中两个因子的二阶统计量构建数据感知变换以减小量化误差。它用校准数据分别构造 key 和 value 变换：value 变换折叠进模型权重，key 变换在 RoPE 之后应用，两者可与裁剪量化器配合。针对 QuEST INT 量化器，论文在温和假设下证明 WUSH 变换接近最优；端到端评估中将其集成进 SGLang，采用 OSCAR 风格的分位数裁剪仿射量化。

Result: 使用 QuEST INT 时，WUSH-KV 降低了逐层重构误差，并在所测试的变换中取得最低的端到端困惑度。在 2-bit 下，WUSH-KV 在所有评估模型和下游任务上性能与 OSCAR 变换相当或更优。

Conclusion: WUSH-KV 是一种有效且理论上有支撑的低比特 KV-cache 量化方法，能够在 2-bit 下实现与 OSCAR 相当或更优的性能。

Abstract: KV cache memory and bandwidth costs grow with context length and batch size, which limits efficient long-context inference. To address this bottleneck, we introduce WUSH-KV for low-bit KV-cache quantization. It adapts WUSH, which constructs a data-aware transform from the second-order statistics of both factors in a matrix product to reduce quantization error. WUSH-KV uses calibration data to construct separate key and value transforms, with the value transform folded into the model weights and the key transform applied after RoPE. The transforms can be paired with clipped quantizers. For one such quantizer, QuEST INT, we show that, under mild assumptions, the WUSH transform is near-optimal. With this quantizer, WUSH-KV reduces layerwise reconstruction error and achieves the lowest end-to-end perplexity among other tested transforms. For end-to-end evaluation, we integrate WUSH-KV into SGLang using OSCAR-style percentile-clipped affine quantization. At 2-bit, WUSH-KV performs comparably to or outperforms the OSCAR transform across all evaluated models and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [330] [Achieving an $O(1/N)$ Optimality Gap in Average-Reward Weakly-Coupled MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38132)
*Yige Hong,Xiangcheng Zhang,Qiaomin Xie,Yudong Chen,Weina Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究平均回报弱耦合马尔可夫决策过程（WCMDP），识别了可超越 $1/\sqrt{N}$ 最优性差距的条件，并设计出达到 $O(1/N)$ 最优性差距的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WCMDP 由 N 个共享多种每步预算约束的小型 MDP（称为臂）组成。对于其特例 restless bandits（RB），已有工作能在一般条件下达到 $O(1/\sqrt{N})$ 最优性差距，并确定了可优于 $1/\sqrt{N}$ 的条件；但对一般 WCMDP，此前尚无结果能超越 $1/\sqrt{N}$ 的最优性差距。

Method: 针对臂具有相同模型参数、多动作以及状态和动作相关成本的设定，识别与 RB 类似的可实现优于 $1/\sqrt{N}$ 最优性差距的条件，并设计与以往基于推广优先序的方法不同的、非优先序策略，其目标是诱导局部线性平均场动力学。

Result: 所设计策略在相应条件下达到 $O(1/N)$ 的最优性差距，优于此前一般 WCMDP 中 $1/\sqrt{N}$ 的水平。

Conclusion: 本文为一般 WCMDP 建立了超越 $1/\sqrt{N}$ 最优性差距的条件，并提出一种非优先序、基于诱导局部线性平均场动力学的策略，实现 $O(1/N)$ 最优性差距。

Abstract: We study average-reward weakly-coupled Markov decision processes (WCMDPs), where a WCMDP consists of $N$ smaller MDPs, called arms, that share multiple per-step budget constraints. We consider the setting where the arms have identical model parameters, multiple actions, and state- and action-dependent costs. For restless bandits (RBs), a well-studied special case of WCMDPs, prior work has developed policies that achieve an $O(1/\sqrt{N})$ optimality gap under general conditions, and has further identified conditions under which policies can achieve a better-than-$1/\sqrt{N}$ optimality gap. However, for general WCMDPs, no prior result achieves an optimality gap better than $1/\sqrt{N}$. In this paper, we identify conditions analogous to those for RBs under which a better-than-$1/\sqrt{N}$ optimality gap is achievable, and design a policy that attains an $O(1/N)$ optimality gap. Notably, unlike prior approaches based on generalizing priority orderings, our policy is not priority-based but rather is designed to induce locally linear mean-field dynamics.

</details>


### [331] [Multi-Agent Flow Matching with Decoupled Generative Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38133)
*Ruoyu Lin,Magnus Egerstedt,Fabio Pasqualetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeGG-Flow，一个用于多智能体流匹配与解耦生成引导的通用框架。通过将生成过程建模为控制仿射动力系统，针对共享需求与私有需求两类耦合约束建立可行性条件与有限时域收敛保证，并推导了刻画引导导致分布偏差的 Wasserstein 界。实验在多机器人协作跨越空间间隙与多物体场景可承担性生成两个任务上验证，能够直接生成满足全部硬约束的对象，且在训练未见过的团队规模下依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽能从复杂多模态分布中生成多样对象，但通常缺乏形式化保证来确保生成结果满足硬约束。在多智能体生成场景中问题更为突出：一个硬需求可能依赖多个智能体，而每个智能体需要在无法获知其他智能体同时计算出的引导输入的情况下确定自身引导。现有方法难以同时处理这种解耦性与约束满足的可靠性。

Method: 提出 DeGG-Flow 框架，实现多智能体流匹配中的解耦生成引导。将生成过程表示为控制仿射动力系统，据此为两类耦合需求推导引导条件：依赖多智能体共同的共享需求，以及与各智能体及其邻居相关的私有需求。针对这两类需求分别建立可行性条件与有限时域收敛保证，并进一步推导 Wasserstein 界以刻画引导引起的分布偏差。

Result: 在两类应用中验证了 DeGG-Flow：通过重构环境实现多机器人协作跨越空间间隙，以及带可承担性需求的多物体场景生成。在两个应用中，DeGG-Flow 均能直接生成满足所有相应硬需求的对象，并且在训练时未见过的团队规模下仍然有效。

Conclusion: DeGG-Flow 为多智能体生成提供了具有理论保证的硬约束满足能力，通过解耦引导机制解决了智能体引导输入相互依赖的难题，兼具可行性、收敛性与分布偏差刻画等理论支撑，并在多机器人协作与场景生成任务中展现出良好泛化性。

Abstract: Generative modeling is widely used for producing diverse objects from complex, multimodal distributions. However, its expressivity does not, in general, come with formal guarantees that the generated objects satisfy hard constraints or requirements. In multi-agent generation, this problem becomes more challenging because a hard requirement can depend on multiple agents, while each agent may need to determine its own guidance input without relying on the simultaneously computed guidance inputs of other agents. To this end, we introduce DeGG-Flow, a general framework for multi-agent flow matching with decoupled generative guidance. By representing the generative process as a control-affine dynamical system, we develop guidance conditions for two classes of coupled requirements: shared requirements whose satisfaction depends on multiple agents together, and private requirements associated with each individual agent dependent on its neighbors. For both classes, we establish feasibility conditions and finite-horizon convergence guarantees. We further derive a Wasserstein bound that characterizes the distributional deviation induced by the guidance. We demonstrate DeGG-Flow on multi-robot collaboration for crossing a spatial gap by reconfiguring the environment, and on multi-object scene generation with affordance requirements. Across both applications, DeGG-Flow directly generates objects that satisfy all corresponding hard requirements, including at team sizes unseen during training.

</details>


### [332] [A Spectral Theory of Distortion in LLM Graph Reconstruction: Sharp Bounds and Empirical Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38161)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了拉普拉斯谱Wasserstein距离可被两个边数统计量夹逼，并以此为诊断工具揭示语言模型图重建的编辑策略差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图重建评估仅报告原始图与重建图之间的单一聚合距离，掩盖了编辑行为的具体性质（新增、删除还是混合编辑），无法区分不同模型的编辑策略。

Method: 从理论上证明拉普拉斯谱Wasserstein距离被下界（边数净变化）与上界（边集对称差）各乘以2/n所夹逼；分析该夹逼的紧致条件；利用距离与下界之差的残差作为"混合编辑证书"；在45个合成图、3个开放权重模型产生的135个重建结果上刻画这些区间。

Result: 夹逼是紧的：当重建仅增边或仅删边时两端重合，此时距离退化为缩放后的边数；残差为正当且仅当同时发明和丢失边（混合编辑）。135个重建中77个为单侧编辑，29个混合输出满足残差X>0，含边数恰好不变但19条边同时发明与丢失的情形。三个模型编辑策略从复制输入到试图补全但伴随大量幻觉不等。

Conclusion: 仅凭聚合失真无法揭示的模型编辑策略差异，可由所提出的边数夹逼与残差证书从报告摘要中直接识别，为图重建评估提供了更细粒度的诊断工具。

Abstract: Evaluations of graph reconstruction by language models typically report a single aggregate distance between the original and the reconstructed graph. We prove that for the Wasserstein distance between Laplacian spectra such a summary is bracketed by two edge counts, the net change in edge number from below and the symmetric difference from above, each scaled by $2/n$ where $n$ is the number of vertices. The bracket is sharp: its two ends coincide exactly when the reconstruction only adds edges or only deletes them, and on that class the distance is a rescaled edge count that says nothing about which edges changed. When the ends differ, the residual between the distance and the lower end is positive only if the reconstruction both invented and lost edges, which turns it into a certificate of mixed editing computable from the reported summaries alone. We characterize these regimes in 135 reconstructions produced by three open-weight models over 45 synthetic graphs. Seventy-seven outputs are one-sided and 29 mixed outputs have $X > 0$, including cases where edge count is exactly preserved while nineteen edges were simultaneously invented and lost. The three models differ in editing policy, ranging from copying the input to attempting completion at the cost of large hallucination volume, a distinction that aggregate distortion does not reveal.

</details>


### [333] [LeapQuant: Efficient Linear Attention with Accurate Recurrent State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38166)
*Yi Pan,Haocheng Xi,Kan Zhu,Xingyang Li,Yibo Wu,Mayank Mishra,Hongtao Zhang,William X. Zheng,Baris Kasikci,Song Han,Kurt Keutzer,Rishabh Iyer,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeapQuant 是一种免训练方法，通过逐窗口量化与高精度补偿令牌，实现 8-bit 循环状态量化下近乎无损的性能，并显著降低混合线性注意力 LLM 的推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合线性注意力 LLM（如 GDN、KDA）将上下文压缩为固定大小的循环状态，虽降低长上下文处理成本，但反复读写该状态仍是推理瓶颈。量化可降低成本，却因舍入误差累积及状态中异常值行列的存在而严重损害模型质量。

Method: 提出 LeapQuant，免训练。其一为逐窗口量化：跨越一个 token 窗口，仅在窗口末端量化一次状态，窗口内输出由固定低位状态加高精度缓冲更新计算。其二保留状态最大异常值为少量高精度 Compensator Tokens，共享真实 token 的更新路径，并在量化前对残差平滑以进一步降误差。

Result: 在 Qwen、Kimi、GLM 系列上实验，精度与 FP32 基线相当，显著降低内存与计算成本。NVIDIA B200、RTX PRO 6000、RTX 5090 上内核级平均加速 2.05–3.70×，端到端推理加速 1.47×。

Conclusion: LeapQuant 通过逐窗口量化与补偿令牌机制，在免训练条件下实现近乎无损的 8-bit 循环状态量化，有效缓解误差累积与异常值问题，为混合线性注意力 LLM 提供高效推理方案。

Abstract: Recent LLMs increasingly adopt hybrid designs that replace standard attention with linear attention, such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN) and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA). Although they compress the context into a fixed-size recurrent state and substantially reduce the cost of long-context processing, repeatedly reading and updating that state remains a major inference bottleneck. Quantization offers a natural way to reduce this cost, but can significantly degrade model quality, due to the accumulation of rounding errors and the presence of outlier rows and columns in the state. To address these challenges, we propose LeapQuant, a training-free method that achieves near-lossless performance under 8-bit recurrent-state quantization. First, to mitigate error accumulation, we propose per-window quantization, which leaps over a window of tokens and quantizes the state only once at its end. Within a window, outputs are computed from the fixed low-bit state together with high-precision buffered updates. Second, to reduce the error introduced by each quantization, LeapQuant retains the state's largest outliers as a few high-precision Compensator Tokens, which share the update path of real tokens. We then smooth the remaining residual before quantization to further reduce the error. Comprehensive experiments across the Qwen, Kimi, and GLM model families show that LeapQuant substantially reduces memory and compute costs during inference. With accuracy comparable to the FP32 baseline, it achieves average speedups of 2.05--3.70$\times$ at the kernel level and 1.47$\times$ for end-to-end inference on NVIDIA B200, RTX PRO 6000, and RTX 5090 GPUs.

</details>


### [334] [Breakdown of Local Denoising as Semantic Speciation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38176)
*Guangkuo Liu,Mert Okyay,Yifan F. Zhang,Fangjun Hu,Rahul Nandkishore,Xun Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生成模型中"物种形成窗口"（样本确定语义类别的时段）与"非局域性窗口"（局部上下文不足以生成内容的时段）之间的关系，通过语义信息的空间分布证明在"共同原因"假设下非局域性窗口必包含于物种形成窗口内，并给出两窗口收缩为单一极限时间（相变）的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型动力学中存在两个看似独立的时间窗口：物种形成窗口与非局域性窗口，且多种前沿模型中二者出现时间几乎重合。作者希望探究这一近乎同时发生的现象背后的机制，并连接关于生成建模中语义结构涌现的两种互补视角。

Method: 从语义信息的空间分布出发，提出"共同原因"假设，即语义标签解释了远距离token之间的一部分相关性（对许多真实数据集而言是自然的条件）。在此假设下进行理论证明，并推导出两个窗口随系统规模增长而收缩为单一极限时间的条件，最后在高斯混合模型中进行解析验证。

Result: 在"共同原因"假设下证明了非局域性窗口必然位于物种形成窗口之内；给出了两窗口随系统规模增大收敛为单一极限时间的条件，从而定义了"相变"，并在高斯混合模型中解析验证了该相变行为。

Conclusion: 研究识别出了语义信息能够解释物种形成与非局域性同时发生（相变）的条件，从而将生成建模中语义结构涌现的两种互补视角联系起来。

Abstract: The dynamics of generative models exhibit two apparently distinct temporal windows: a speciation window, in which a sample commits to a semantic class, and a nonlocality window, in which local context windows become insufficient for generation. Motivated by evidence of their near-concurrence in a variety of frontier models, we investigate their relationship through the spatial distribution of semantic information. Under a "common cause" hypothesis, we prove that the nonlocality window must lie in the speciation window. This hypothesis postulates that semantic labels explain a fraction of the correlations between distant tokens, a condition that is natural for many real datasets. We further give conditions under which both windows shrink to a single limiting time as system size grows, defining a "phase transition", and verify this behavior analytically in Gaussian mixtures. Together, these results identify conditions under which semantic information explains the concurrence of speciation and nonlocality, connecting two complementary perspectives on the emergence of semantic structure in generative modeling.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [335] [Socrates-RAG: Premise-Directed Inquiry against Coordinated Evidence Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35773)
*Renyu Zhao,Xinyuan Zou,Lanbin Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Socrates-RAG：一种前提导向的主动检索策略，将下一轮查询纳入可靠性问题，通过表示竞争答案、选择能区分它们的关键前提来精炼后续查询，在有限预算下提升开放语料问答的安全准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG防御通常在固定的检索集合上决定如何过滤或聚合，但在开放语料问答中，决定性证据可能不在初始上下文中却可被检索到，因此下一轮查询本身也是可靠性问题的一部分。

Method: 提出Socrates-RAG，一种前提导向的主动检索策略：表示竞争答案，选择一个未解决且能区分这些答案的前提，并在回答或弃答前用新获得的证据精炼后续查询。作者形式化了有限预算下的证据状态，并给出相对重复查询或主题查询策略的条件性"挽救"保证。实验采用匹配对照，两策略共享初始证据、确定性检索器、两查询/前三条预算、回答提示及无标签证据链释放规则。

Result: 在一个不相交的48世界反事实评估中，前提导向的探究将安全准确率从79.2%提升到93.8%，战绩为8胜1负39平（双侧精确检验p=.0391）。决定性证据召回率提升幅度相同，不安全答案从一个降为零。两策略均解决全部24个单跳案例；增益集中在双跳案例，Socrates-RAG通过将新解决的前提代入第二次查询实现改进。

Conclusion: 该对照研究隔离出前提导向证据获取的具体收益，但作者未声称在开放Web上具有普遍鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) defenses typically decide how to filter or aggregate a fixed retrieved set. In open-corpus question answering, however, decisive evidence may be absent from the initial context but retrievable, making the next query part of the reliability problem. We introduce Socrates-RAG, a premise-directed active retrieval policy that represents competing answers, selects an unresolved premise whose resolution would discriminate them, and uses newly acquired evidence to refine a subsequent query before answering or abstaining. We formalize the resulting finite-budget evidence state and give a conditional rescue guarantee relative to repeated or topical-query policies.
  We evaluate Socrates-RAG against a matched control in which the same backbone generates ordinary relevance-oriented search queries; both policies share the initial evidence, deterministic retriever, two-query/top-three budget, answer prompt, and label-free evidence-chain release rule. On a disjoint 48-world counterfactual evaluation, premise-directed inquiry raises safe accuracy from 79.2% to 93.8%, with 8 wins, 1 loss, and 39 ties (two-sided exact p=.0391). Decisive-evidence recall improves by the same margin, while unsafe answers fall from one to zero. Both policies solve all 24 one-hop cases; the gain is concentrated in two-hop cases, where Socrates-RAG substitutes a newly resolved premise into its second query. This controlled study isolates a specific benefit of premise-directed acquisition without claiming general robustness on the open Web.

</details>


### [336] [Post-Generation Verification Dominates Retrieval Optimization: A 2^4 Factorial Ablation of RAG Pipeline Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35774)
*Ng S. T. Chong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过2^4全因子消融实验（768个条件）系统评估RAG管线中四个增强特征及其交互，发现生成后验证（CC）是主导性最强特征，其单独使用即优于任何不含它的组合，并提出质量-延迟帕累托前沿与查询类型依赖性的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RAG管线堆叠大量增强特征，但这些特征通常被孤立验证，它们之间的交互作用未被测量，导致对特征实际贡献的评估可能存在偏差。

Method: 对四个管线特征——章节扩展（SE）、智能体检索（AS）、完整性检查（CC）、目录引导检索（ToC）——进行2^4全因子消融，覆盖16种配置、24个查询（涵盖八种交互类型）、两种云级模型（共768个条件），在五份公开文档（78–492页）上运行，并将每个答案与已验证的参考答案打分对比。

Result: 生成后验证占主导：CC是最强特征（d=+0.48，p<0.001），同时提升准确性、完整性和有用性；CC单独（4.31/5）优于所有不含它的配置，包括三特征SE+AS+ToC（4.11）。ToC以零LLM成本带来显著增益（d=+0.22）；AS效果小且不稳定；SE中立。最高质量配置约使基线延迟翻倍，形成六配置的帕累托前沿。特征效用强烈依赖查询类型——CC在完整性要求高的查询上达d=+0.83，因此单查询类型评估会系统性错排特征。

Conclusion: 验证答案比优化检索更重要；评估RAG特征必须采用包含多样查询类型的因子设计，否则会系统性误判特征价值。

Abstract: Modern RAG pipelines stack many enhancement features, but these features are typically validated in isolation, leaving their interactions unmeasured. We run a 2^4 full factorial ablation of four pipeline features -- section expansion (SE), agentic search (AS), completeness check (CC), and table-of-contents-guided retrieval (ToC) -- across 16 configurations, 24 queries spanning eight interaction types, and two cloud-class models (768 conditions) on five public documents (78-492 pages), scoring every answer against a verified reference. Post-generation verification dominates: CC is the strongest feature (d=+0.48, p<0.001), improving accuracy, completeness, and usefulness simultaneously, and CC alone (4.31/5) outperforms every configuration without it, including the three-feature SE+AS+ToC (4.11). ToC yields a significant gain at zero LLM cost (d=+0.22); AS is small and unstable, helping some queries and harming others; SE is neutral. The highest-quality configuration roughly doubles baseline latency, producing a genuine quality-latency Pareto frontier of six configurations. Feature utility is strongly query-type dependent -- CC reaches d=+0.83 on completeness-demanding queries -- so single-query-type evaluations systematically mis-rank features. We conclude that verifying answers matters more than optimizing retrieval, and that factorial designs with diverse query types are necessary to evaluate RAG features.

</details>


### [337] [Structured Interaction, Visual Localization, and Robust Execution for Complex Web Tasks: A Technical Report on the WebRetriever Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35904)
*Ziqi Zhang,Shaohui Li,Bing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了用于 WebRetriever 挑战赛的 Web Agent 系统，采用"交互优先"策略，结合语义网页信息与视知觉，在正式比赛中以 59% 通过率获得第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web 检索与操作任务中，如何高效利用网页语义信息并减少对视知觉的依赖，从而提升多浏览器并发处理的稳定性与准确率，是本文要解决的问题。

Method: 系统采用结构化"交互优先"策略：常规浏览器操作基于语义网页信息，仅当结构化表示不足时启用视觉感知。包含三项设计：网格辅助视觉定位（处理难访问控件）、层次化上下文管理（减少冗余页面与交互历史）、故障感知执行机制（稳定多浏览器任务处理）。

Result: Protocol 1 本地评测通过率最高达 79%；官方 Protocol 3 竞赛中，在八个并发浏览器工作器下取得 59% 通过率，总体排名第一。

Conclusion: 该 Web Agent 系统通过交互优先与三项关键设计，在 WebRetriever 挑战赛中综合排名第一并赢得冠军，验证了语义优先、视觉补充策略的有效性。

Abstract: This report presents the web agent system developed for the WebRetriever Challenge. The system follows a structuredinteraction- first strategy, using semantic webpage information for routine browser operations and invoking visual perception only when structured representations are insufficient. Three key designs are introduced: grid-assisted visual localization for difficult-to-access controls, hierarchical context management for reducing redundant page and interaction history, and fault-aware execution mechanisms for stable multi-browser task processing. The system achieved a pass rate of up to 79% in local evaluation on Protocol 1. In the official Protocol 3 competition, it achieved a 59% pass rate with eight concurrent browser workers and ranked first overall, winning the WebRetriever Challenge.

</details>


### [338] [TSG Suggester: Tree-Structured Knowledge-Graph Retrieval for Troubleshooting Guide Recommendation in Cloud Incident Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35780)
*Shawn Pan,PavanUttej Ravva,Walt Williams,CJ Barberan,Nutan Sahoo,Ziran Min,David Gross,Irene Shaffer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 TSG Suggester 检索系统，可直接依据事件描述推荐故障排查指南（TSG），其中 Tree+KG 方法在生产数据上取得 54.78% Top-1 与 82.48% Top-5 精度，全面领先各基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模云服务的值班工程师面临巨大时间压力，但目前查找正确 TSG 仍主要依赖人工关键词搜索，且已有实证研究表明指南检索占据了总体故障缓解时间的相当大比例。因此需要一种能直接从事件描述中推荐相关 TSG 的自动化检索方案。

Method: 在 314 起真实事件、112 个唯一 TSG、来自 18 个服务团队的生产事件管理系统上评估五种检索策略：纯文本 RAG、图像增强 RAG、RAPTOR、树结构检索，以及提出的 Tree+Knowledge Graph（Tree+KG）。Tree+KG 将每份指南转为保留原章节层级的树，在内部节点上附加 LLM 生成的问题抽象以弥合指南“面向解决方案”与事件“面向问题”的语言鸿沟，并抽取每份指南的实体知识图谱，在查询时融合嵌入相似度与实体级匹配。

Result: Tree+KG 达到 54.78% Top-1 与 82.48% Top-5 精度，在所有截断点均领先全部基线，相较纯文本 RAG 的 Top-1 提升 8.58 个百分点。另有两个独立发现：一是结构对齐起主导作用，保留或重建文档结构的方法在需要精确区分时优于扁平分块；二是与初始假设相反，多模态增强反而有害——为指南截图生成并注入描述会使 Top-5 相较纯文本基线下降 22.64 个百分点，原因是泛化描述稀释而非强化了嵌入表示。

Conclusion: 结构对齐对检索精度至关重要，而泛化的多模态增强会损害效果。论文给出了两项结果的错误分析及具体部署指导。

Abstract: On call engineers in large scale cloud services work under intense time pressure, yet locating the correct Troubleshooting Guide (TSG) for an incident remains a largely manual, keyword driven process, and prior empirical work finds that guide search consumes a substantial fraction of total mitigation time.
  We present TSG Suggester, a retrieval system that recommends relevant TSGs directly from an incident description. We evaluate five retrieval strategies: Text Only RAG, Image Augmented RAG, RAPTOR, Tree Structured Retrieval, and our proposed Tree + Knowledge Graph (Tree+KG), on 314 real world incidents spanning 112 unique TSGs drawn from 18 service teams on a production incident management system.
  Tree+KG converts each guide into a tree that preserves its native section hierarchy, attaches LLM generated problem abstractions to internal nodes to bridge the solution oriented language of guides and the problem oriented language of incidents, extracts a per guide entity knowledge graph, and fuses embedding similarity with entity level matching at query time. Tree+KG attains 54.78% Top 1 and 82.48% Top 5 accuracy, leading every baseline at every cutoff, with an 8.58 point Top 1 gain over text only RAG.
  Two findings are of independent interest. First, structural alignment dominates: methods that preserve or rebuild document structure outperform flat chunking where precise discrimination matters. Second, and contrary to our initial hypothesis, multimodal enrichment actively hurts. Captioning guide screenshots and injecting the captions costs 22.64 Top 5 points relative to the text only baseline because generic captions dilute embeddings rather than sharpen them. We report error analyses for both results and provide concrete deployment guidance.

</details>


### [339] [Financial Evidence Crowding: Diagnosing and Mitigating Constraint-Induced Displacement in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35782)
*Yixi Zhou,Jiayi Yin,Fan Zhang,Xiangyi He,Haipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出"金融证据拥挤"（financial evidence crowding）这一集合级排序失败问题，并设计数据集与校正方法加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融问答中，检索到的段落可能匹配查询主题，却在时间、分部、指标口径或表格范围上相冲突。这类冲突候选会挤占有限上下文槽位，挤掉真正支持答案的证据。

Method: 1. 构建 FinDeCrowd-Stress 受控压力测试：固定查询、相关证据、排序模型、候选数量与检索预算，仅对比"兼容"与"不兼容"候选池；2. 提出 FinDeCrowd-RAG：将固定相关性得分与类型化兼容性、局部词汇竞争结合的学习式分数校正；3. 引入查询级身份门控（identity gate），仅在预测排序更优时应用校正，否则保留原始排序。

Result: 在含训练未见公司的 FinDER 测试集上，不兼容候选池使 Recall@10 相比同等难度的兼容池下降 0.147；受控条件下 FinDeCrowd-RAG 将 top-10 证据覆盖从 0.757 提升至 0.902；在无查询特定候选插入的 FinDER 索引上，门控重排使该覆盖率从 0.420 提升至 0.492，同时 top-100 覆盖保持 0.743；固定生成器时，该排序改进提升了 FinanceBench 与 FinQA 的答案准确率和引用召回。

Conclusion: 约束导致的证据位移是可度量的 RAG 评测目标；身份门控重排能恢复第一阶段检索已覆盖、但被挤出 top-k 的证据。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) retrieves candidate evidence and sends only a limited top-ranked subset, the top-k context, to a generator. In financial question answering, passages can match a query's topic while conflicting with its period, segment, metric scope, or table scope. We study the resulting set-level ordering failure, which we call financial evidence crowding. FinDeCrowd-Stress isolates this failure through matched compatible and incompatible candidate pools while fixing the query, relevant evidence, ranking model, candidate count, and retrieval budget. On a FinDER test split containing companies unseen during training, incompatible pools reduce top-10 evidence inclusion (Recall@10) by 0.147 relative to equally difficult compatible pools. This gap shows that conflicting candidates consume limited context slots and displace answer-supporting evidence. We then introduce FinDeCrowd-RAG, a learned score correction that combines a fixed relevance score with typed compatibility and local lexical competition. A query-level identity gate applies the correction only when it predicts a better order; otherwise, it preserves the original ranking. On identical controlled candidates, FinDeCrowd-RAG raises top-10 evidence inclusion from 0.757 to 0.902 by recovering evidence already present in the candidate set. On a FinDER index built without query-specific candidate insertion, gated reranking raises this inclusion rate from 0.420 to 0.492, while top-100 retrieval coverage remains 0.743 by design. With a fixed generator, the same ordering change improves answer accuracy and citation recall on FinanceBench and FinQA. These results identify constraint-induced displacement as a measurable RAG evaluation target and show that identity-gated reranking can recover evidence already covered by first-stage retrieval.

</details>


### [340] [Soft Curriculum Learning for Optimizing Fresh and Generalized Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35783)
*Arnab Bhadury,Siyan Zheng,Anlan Yu,Palaksh Rungta,Jiawei Li,Changping Meng,Dapeng Hong,Chuan He,Onkar Dalal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的软课程学习（Soft CL）框架，用于工业级检索与排序模型的持续训练，通过损失退火与图内权重调整替代刚性数据过滤，打破流行度反馈循环，在不牺牲吞吐的前提下提升用户满意度与新鲜内容消费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐系统（尤其短视频平台）受困于流行度反馈循环：模型推荐热门（head）物品，进而产生大量偏斜训练数据，形成自我强化循环，使检索与排序模型过度记忆头部模式、忽视长尾泛化。课程学习（CL）理论上可通过逐步暴露更难、更稀疏样本来打破该循环，但工业落地受限于硬件利用率低——动态数据过滤算法多为CPU瓶颈，使TPU/GPU加速器"饥饿"，同时需要复杂预处理。

Method: 提出可扩展的软课程学习框架，专为工业级检索与排序模型的持续训练设计。核心是用损失退火（loss annealing）与图内权重调整（in-graph weight adjustments）取代刚性数据过滤，从而在打破流行度反馈的同时实现动态课程节奏控制，且不损失系统吞吐。框架应用于基于序列的检索模型（如SASRec）、双塔检索模型以及大规模持续排序模型。

Result: 在上述多类模型上取得实证效果；在短视频平台的在线A/B测试显示，整体用户满意度与新鲜内容消费均获得显著提升，且模型吞吐未出现下降。

Conclusion: 软课程学习框架可在工业规模下有效缓解流行度反馈循环，兼顾训练效率与业务指标，适用于检索与排序的持续训练场景。

Abstract: Large-scale recommender systems, particularly short-form video platforms, are often bottlenecked by massive popularity feedback loops. In such environments, as models recommend popular items, they generate an overwhelming amount of skewed training data for "head" items. This creates a self-reinforcing cycle where retrieval and ranking models memorize "head" item patterns at the expense of generalizing across the vast "tail" of the catalogue. While Curriculum Learning (CL) offers a powerful mechanism to break this feedback loop by systematically exposing models to progressively more difficult and less frequent examples, its adoption in industrial recommendation has been hampered by hardware utilization inefficiencies or the needs for complicated pre-processing techniques because dynamic data rejection algorithms tend to starve hardware accelearators (TPUs/GPUs) by becoming largely CPU-bound. In this work, we introduce a scalable Soft Curriculum Learning framework designed specifically for continuous training setups within industry-scale retrieval and ranking models. By utilizing loss annealing and in-graph weight adjustments rather than rigid data filtering, we break the popularity feedback and enable dynamic curriculum pacing without sacrificing system throughput. We demonstrate empirical evidence through applications across sequence-based retrieval models (such as SASRec), two-tower retrieval models, and large-scale continuous ranking models. Online A/B tests on our short-video platform demonstrate substantial lifts in both overall user satisfaction and fresh content consumption, all without degrading model throughput.

</details>


### [341] [GRP v0.1 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36688)
*Wenfeng Zhuo,Vincent Xue,Charles Wei,Cong Ni,Ruiming Lu,Jiwen Ren,Mo Li,Peng Yang,Xufei Wang,Dongheng Li,Jiacong He,Yi Song,Yufei Fan,Mikhail Obukhov,Yiwen Chen,Yvette Liu,Yin Ye,Chengjie Wu,Mingtao Zhang,Jinchao Ye,Lili Zhang,Chunhui Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 GRP，一个将检索、排序与奖励建模统一到单一编码器-解码器中的生成式推荐框架，并提出 mGRPO 强化学习后训练方法。离线实验验证了各组件设计，serving 优化将端到端检索延迟降低 69%；在线实验显示检索场景下观看时长提升 0.46%、分享提升 0.77%，结合旁路与来源替换后分别提升 0.82% 和 2.56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统通常采用多阶段级联结构，其检索、排序与服务组件难以联合替换，导致难以实现端到端优化。作者希望探索一条渐进式走向端到端推荐的路径，用统一模型替代割裂的多阶段流程。

Method: 提出 GRP 生成式推荐框架，在单一编码器-解码器中整合检索、排序与奖励建模：模型生成多模态 Semantic IDs，并用联合训练的排序模块为候选打分；冻结的排序模块为强化学习后训练提供奖励。提出 mGRPO，在奖励优化中加入参考锚定的 margin，以保留已记录目标的似然。离线实验考察历史编码、模型容量分配、事件选择、tokenization 与奖励判别等设计。

Result: Serving 优化使端到端检索延迟降低 69%。在线实验分别评估模型作为检索来源、早期排序旁路以及替换较弱来源的效果：仅检索对比中，观看时长相对生产系统提升 0.46%，分享提升 0.77%；结合旁路与来源替换的对比中，观看时长提升 0.82%，分享提升 2.56%，且平台级护栏指标保持中性。

Conclusion: 结果支持渐进式部署路径，同时指出在排序质量与各类推荐指标表现上仍存在待改进的差距。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation systems rely on multi-stage cascades whose retrieval, ranking, and serving components are difficult to replace jointly. We present GRP, a generative recommendation framework that combines retrieval, ranking, and reward modeling in a single encoder-decoder model, and evaluate a progressive path toward end-to-end recommendation. The model generates multimodal Semantic IDs and scores candidates with a jointly trained ranking module. The frozen ranking module then supplies rewards for reinforcement-learning post-training. We introduce mGRPO, which adds a reference-anchored margin to reward optimization to preserve the likelihood of logged targets. Offline experiments examine history encoding, model capacity allocation, event selection, tokenization, and reward discrimination. Serving optimizations reduce end-to-end retrieval latency by 69%. Online experiments evaluate the model as a retrieval source, with early-ranking bypass, and with replacement of weaker sources. In a retrieval-only comparison, view time increases by 0.46% and shares by 0.77% relative to production. A separate comparison combining bypass and source replacement yields increases of 0.82% in view time and 2.56% in shares, with neutral platform-level guardrails. These results support progressive deployment while identifying remaining gaps in ranking quality and performance across recommendation metrics.

</details>


### [342] [Optimizing VLP-aligned Multimodal Intent Representation with Correct Visual Instantiation for Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36946)
*Xuri Ge,Chunhao Wang,Junchen Fu,Haokun Wen,Zhiwei Xu,Ying Zhou,Zhumin Chen,Pengjie Ren,Zhaochun Ren,Xin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一零样本组合图像检索框架VMIR-CVI，从文本意图推理与视觉实例解耦两个互补视角重构查询表示，在CIRR、CIRCO和FashionIQ上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本组合图像检索（ZS-CIR）需在无配对监督下，依据参考图像和修改文本检索目标图像。现有方法将组合查询编码为文本主导表示，但视觉伪词学习和基于MLLM的目标推理所重构的查询常偏离原生VLP表示空间：前者受参考噪声与粗糙文本融合影响，后者描述冗长且视觉接地弱，导致检索兼容性下降。

Method: 提出统一框架VMIR-CVI，包含三个模块：（1）VLP对齐的多模态意图推理（VMIR），将少样本VLP风格示例注入思维链提示，引导MLLM生成目标一致的意图查询，转化为统一文本描述以对齐VLP原生文本空间；（2）免训练视觉实例解耦（TVID），无需额外优化即可从全局参考特征中解耦细粒度视觉实例，降低参考噪声并保留目标相关内容；（3）轻量混合模态意图对齐与融合（HIAF），将推理文本意图与解耦视觉线索融合为统一混合模态表示。

Result: 在CIRR、CIRCO和FashionIQ三个CIR基准上，VMIR-CVI显著优于现有基线，达到新的最优性能。代码与训练模型将公开。

Conclusion: 从文本意图推理与视觉实例解耦两个互补视角重构多模态组合查询，可有效优化VLP兼容的多模态意图表示，提升ZS-CIR鲁棒性。

Abstract: ZS-CIR aims to retrieve a target image from a reference image and a modification text without paired supervision, typically by encoding composed queries as text-dominant representations within the image-text matching space of VLPs. However, queries reconstructed by visual pseudo-word learning or MLLM-based target reasoning often deviate from the native VLP representation space due to reference noise and coarse text fusion in the former, and verbose, weakly visually grounded descriptions in the latter. In this paper, we propose a unified ZS-CIR framework (named VMIR-CVI) to reconstruct multimodal composite queries from two complementary perspectives for optimizing VLP-compatible multimodal intent representation. First, it reasons and converts the multimodal intent into a unified textual description, aligning with the native text space of the VLP backbones to produce more retrieval-compatible textual queries. Second, it reconstructs the query representation with correctly decoupled visual instance cues, reducing reference noise while preserving target-relevant content. Specifically, a VLP-aligned Multimodal Intent Reasoning (VMIR) module injects few-shot VLP-style exemplars into chain-of-thought prompts, guiding the MLLM to generate target-consistent intent queries. A Training-free Visual Instance Disentanglement (TVID) module decouples fine-grained visual instances from global reference features without additional optimization. Finally, a lightweight Hybrid-modal Intent Alignment and Fusion (HIAF) module integrates the reasoned textual intent and disentangled visual cues into a unified hybrid-modal representation for robust ZS-CIR. Extensive experiments on three CIR benchmarks, namely CIRR, CIRCO and FashionIQ, show that VMIR-CVI significantly outperforms existing baselines and achieves new state-of-the-art performance. Code and trained models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [343] [HELIX: Purified and Unified - Rethinking Feature Interaction and Sequence Modeling for Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37183)
*Yuntao Zheng,Miao Zhang,Yadong Ding,Yanchuan Tang,Lixiyu Chen,Hao Wang,Quan Li,Shiying Cai,Yue Lin,Jiayu Li,Yu Feng,Wentao Yang,Rongkun Xing,Jiekai Wang,Mingge Zhang,Feiling Gong,Xiang Gao,Jinyu Dong,Yajing Zhang,Pengfei Ren,Yinzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HELIX，一种用于大规模推荐系统的统一架构，通过交错序列检索与特征交互并强制单向信息流，实现序列建模与特征交互的联合可扩展性，在TikTok电商推荐中显著提升离线与在线指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐排序模型沿特征交互和序列建模两个轴扩展，但单独扩展其中任一方都受限于规模上限和次优的scaling-law斜率。作者推测要获得更优的scaling-law斜率，需联合扩展这两个轴。

Method: 提出HELIX架构，交错序列检索与特征交互，并强制从可复用序列状态到候选条件mix-tokens的单向信息流。该设计在保持两个建模轴跨深度通信的同时，使用户侧序列计算可摊销，支持序列建模与特征交互的灵活、非对称扩展。

Result: 在TikTok电商推荐系统部署后，HELIX持续提升离线CTR AUC、CVR AUC及其他排序指标；在线A/B测试中，每用户电商视频GMV提升约6%。

Conclusion: 联合扩展序列建模与特征交互两轴可突破单独扩展的瓶颈，HELIX为大规模推荐提供统一且高效的架构方案，并在实际系统中验证了显著收益。
