Abstract: Industrial recommendation ranking models typically scale along two modeling axes: feature interaction over heterogeneous user, item, context, and cross features, and sequence modeling over long, informative, and multi-type user behavior histories. We find that scaling either capability in isolation is insufficient, as each exhibits a limited scaling ceiling and a suboptimal scaling-law slope. We conjecture that achieving a more favorable scaling-law slope requires jointly scaling both axes. To support this, we present HELIX, a purified and unified architecture for large-scale recommendation. HELIX interleaves sequence retrieval and feature interaction while enforcing one-way information flow from reusable sequence states to candidate-conditioned mix-tokens. This design preserves cross-depth communication between the two modeling axes while keeping user-side sequence computation amortizable, enabling flexible and asymmetric scaling of sequence modeling and feature interaction. Deployed in TikTok's e-commerce recommendation system, HELIX consistently improves offline CTR AUC, CVR AUC, and other ranking metrics. In online A/B tests, it achieves an approximately 6% increase in e-commerce video GMV per user.

</details>


### [344] [Do Evidence-Reading Diagnostics Improve Interface Selection in Small LLM Recommenders?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37472)
*Han Chen,Yingrui Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文检验语言模型的行为测试（证据阅读能力测量）是否能帮助选择推荐界面。在6个小型指令微调模型、4个推荐领域、3,426名用户的时序评估下，加入6个"证据阅读"提示特征的增强选择器并未显著优于基线选择器，NDCG@5仅变化-0.0019（95%区间[-0.0046, 0.0004]），未达到0.005的改进目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为测试用于衡量语言模型如何"阅读证据"，但尚不清楚这类测量结果能否用于指导推荐界面的选择。作者希望验证：诊断性测量是否能在已有特征和固定界面的基础上真正改善推荐决策。

Method: 评估6个小型指令微调模型、4个推荐领域，采用时序评估和3,426名评估用户，每次请求对8个候选项排序。基线选择器在"仅历史提示""带协同证据提示""分数融合"之间选择，使用可观测特征和6个稳定性提示（改变措辞与候选顺序）。增强选择器额外加入6个"证据阅读"提示的特征（要求模型比较支持计数）。每个领域和模型在开发（验证）数据上选择一次界面。

Result: 固定界面（每领域每模型在验证集上选一次）得0.5524 NDCG@5，基线选择器0.5447，增强选择器0.5428。加入诊断特征使NDCG@5变化-0.0019（95%区间[-0.0046, 0.0004]），区间包含零且上界低于分析计划设定的0.005改进目标。匹配超参数后上界仍低于目标。从检索到的相似用户获取证据比随机匹配活跃度的对照组提升0.0999 NDCG@5。证据阅读测试还揭示了答案位置偏差和并列回答偏差。

Conclusion: 这些结果针对所测试的选择器和候选集。研究说明：诊断性测量应以"是否能在已有特征和固定界面之外改善推荐选择"为标准来评估，而非仅凭其本身的表现。

Abstract: Behavioral tests measure how a language model reads evidence. We ask whether those measurements help choose a recommendation interface. We evaluate six small instruction-tuned checkpoints across four recommendation domains with chronological evaluation and 3,426 evaluation users. Each request ranks eight candidates. A baseline selector chooses among history-only prompting, prompting with collaborative evidence, and score fusion. It uses observable features and six stability prompts that vary wording and candidate order. An augmented selector adds features from six evidence-reading prompts that ask the model to compare support counts. An interface chosen once on development (validation) data for each domain and checkpoint scores 0.5524 NDCG@5, compared with 0.5447 for the baseline selector and 0.5428 for the augmented selector. Adding the diagnostic features changes NDCG@5 by -0.0019 (95% interval [-0.0046, 0.0004]). The interval includes zero, and its upper bound is below the analysis plan's 0.005 improvement target. Matching the selectors' hyperparameters also leaves the interval upper bound below that target. Evidence from retrieved similar users improves prompting by 0.0999 NDCG@5 over a control using randomly selected users matched for activity. The evidence-reading tests also reveal answer-position and tie-response biases. These results concern the tested selectors and candidate sets. They illustrate why diagnostic measurements should be evaluated by whether they improve recommendation choices beyond existing features and a fixed interface.

</details>


### [345] [MERGE: Multi-LLM Ensemble for Retrieval via Generative Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37574)
*Tzu-I Ho,Yung-Yu Shih,Shang-Yu Su,Dongzhe Wang,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MERGE框架，用多LLM集成加任务导向自动提示优化来做查询扩展，仅需一次BM25检索即可显著提升nDCG@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一LLM受训练数据和架构偏置限制，查询扩展效果有限；且其扩展行为依赖手工提示，每换一个模型都需重新设计提示，成本高且难以扩展。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage-1由三个异构7-8B开源LLM独立生成候选扩展；Stage-2由更大LLM生成式合成为单一查询。两阶段均嵌入任务导向的自动提示优化（APO）循环，以候选的下游检索性能为评分标准，在现任冠军提示与优化器草稿间进行小型锦标赛，冠军连续两轮胜出即终止；并设历史增强变体将近期锦标赛轨迹反馈给优化器。方法retriever无关，仅执行一次BM25检索，无需排序融合、监督式文档扩展或重建索引。

Result: 在五个BEIR基准（NQ、SciFact、FiQA、Touche-2020、DBPedia）上，相对原始查询将BM25的nDCG@10提升+2.1至+14.9点，且仅用紧凑开源模型即与强LLM查询扩展基线持平或更优。消融显示Stage-2集成优于任一Stage-1单一LLM，任务导向APO可将种子提示的大幅回退转化为稳定增益。

Conclusion: 多LLM集成结合任务导向自动提示优化，可在不手工调参、不重索引的前提下稳定提升BM25检索效果，兼具可扩展性与retriever无关性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to enrich user queries in information retrieval (IR) so that a standard retriever such as BM25 can bridge vocabulary gaps with the target corpus. Any single LLM, however, is limited by its training data and architectural biases, and its enrichment behavior depends on hand-crafted prompts that must be re-engineered for each new model -- an expensive and poorly scalable process. We present MERGE (Multi-LLM Ensemble for Retrieval via Generative Enrichment), a two-stage framework: three heterogeneous 7-8B open-source LLMs independently produce candidate expansions, and a larger LLM generatively synthesizes them into a single query. To make prompt engineering scalable across the ensemble, we integrate a task-grounded Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) loop into both stages. Unlike APO methods that judge candidates with an LLM evaluator, our loop scores each candidate by its downstream retrieval performance and runs a small tournament between the current champion prompt and optimizer-proposed drafts, terminating once the champion survives two consecutive rounds; a history-augmented variant additionally feeds the recent tournament trajectory back to the optimizer. MERGE is retriever-agnostic and issues a single BM25 pass with no rank fusion, no supervised document expansion, and no re-indexing. On five BEIR benchmarks (NQ, SciFact, FiQA, Touche-2020, DBPedia), MERGE improves BM25 nDCG@10 over the original queries by +2.1 to +14.9 points and matches or outperforms strong LLM-based query-expansion baselines despite using only compact open-source models. Ablations confirm that the Stage-2 ensemble beats any single Stage-1 LLM, and that task-grounded APO converts large seed-prompt regressions into consistent gains without hand-tuning.

</details>


### [346] [Towards Semi-Automatically Comparing Keyword-Based and Semantic Search Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37749)
*Mohamed Ben Salha,Fiete Lüer,Maik Betka,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个定量评估框架，用于比较关键词式与语义聊天式（如RAG）检索系统的准确率，并通过工业案例验证语义方法显著优于关键词方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关键词搜索在管理大规模数据集时存在明显局限，而基于RAG等上下文聊天式搜索的评估多依赖主观用户反馈，缺乏严谨的定量比较。

Method: 提出一个初步评估框架，针对不同输出格式（列表与消息），分别考察关键词系统的排序准确率与语义聊天系统的信息完整性；采用可替换的等价类实现半自动比较，并适配特定领域语境。

Result: 通过工业案例研究验证框架，结果表明上下文感知搜索相较关键词方法有统计学显著提升，并采用Mann-Whitney U检验等方法支撑分析。

Conclusion: 该框架设计灵活，为客观评估关键词式与语义聊天式检索方法奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: The increasing importance of Information Retrieval (IR) in managing large datasets has highlighted significant limitations in traditional keyword-based search systems. Context-aware chat-based search methods, such as Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), have recently emerged, but their evaluation compared to keyword-based systems often relies on subjective user feedback. A rigorous, quantitative comparison between these paradigms remains lacking. This work introduces a novel, preliminary framework to quantitatively assess IR accuracy of search systems that produce different output formats, such as lists and messages. It focuses on two key aspects: the ranking accuracy for keyword-based systems and the completeness of retrieved information for semantic chat-based systems. Our approach enables semi-automatic comparisons of semantic and keyword-based methods using interchangeable equivalence classes tailored to domain-specific contexts (e.g., companies or problems). We validate the framework through an industrial case study, demonstrating statistically significant improvements in context-aware search over keyword-based methods, supported by analyses including the Mann-Whitney U-Test. With its adaptable design, the proposed framework provides a strong foundation for objectively assessing keyword-based and semantic chat-based search methods.

</details>


### [347] [Generated Query Expansion Still Helps Strong Sparse Retrieval: A Controlled Study with SPLADE-v3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37911)
*Ryan C. Barron,Cade W. Trotter,Maksim E. Eren,Kim Ø. Rasmussen,Liz D. Miller,Benjamin J. Migliori*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在强学习式稀疏检索器SPLADE-v3上，四种生成式查询扩展格式均能提升检索效果，且增益在多数设置下稳健；而语料诱导的概念图无一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学查询通常简短，而相关论文使用专业词汇，二者存在语义鸿沟。生成式查询扩展可弥合该差距，但已有工作提示当底层检索器变强时其价值会下降，因此需要在强检索器上系统验证其是否仍有增益及其稳健性。

Method: 在NFCorpus、TREC-COVID、SciDocs三个数据集上，将四种生成格式（术语列表、伪文档、多伪参考、语料引导文本）与SPLADE-v3结合。所有条件共享同一冻结文档索引、相同的查询侧融合规则与256维预算，以隔离新增内容的影响。同时设置打乱文本、非上下文词袋、语料诱导类型概念图及其关系/深度/验证/随机/门控控制，并做114种插值设置与Holm校正检验。

Result: 全部12组方法-数据集比较均提升聚合nDCG@10，最佳相对增益分别为4.81%、8.92%、9.47%，其中11组经Holm校正后显著。增益在114种插值设置中的103种下持续存在，包括所有给原查询至少30%混合权重的设置。打乱文本与非上下文词袋控制在全部24组聚合比较中仍高于基线，说明增益主要来自新增词汇。语料诱导类型概念图无一致增益，其各类控制亦无法挽救。

Conclusion: 生成式词汇扩展能与强学习式稀疏检索器互补，前提是原查询在混合中保持较强权重（约30%以上）。

Abstract: Scientific queries are often brief, while relevant papers use specialized vocabulary. Generated query expansion can bridge this mismatch, but earlier work suggests that its value shrinks as the underlying retriever becomes stronger. We test the four generated formats of term lists, a pseudo-document, multiple pseudo-references, and corpus-steered text all together with SPLADE-v3 on NFCorpus, TREC-COVID, and SciDocs. Every condition searches the same frozen document index and follows the same query-side integration rule and 256-dimension budget, isolating the effect of the added content. All twelve method-collection comparisons improve aggregate nDCG@10, with best relative gains of 4.81%, 8.92%, and 9.47%. Eleven remain significant after Holm correction. The gain persists in 103 of 114 interpolation settings, including every setting that assigns at least 30% of the mixture weight to the original query. Shuffled-text and non-contextual lexical-bag controls also remain above baseline in all 24 aggregate comparisons, showing that the added vocabulary carries most of the benefit. A corpus-induced typed concept graph, by contrast, produces no consistent gain, and its relation, depth, validation, random, and gating controls do not rescue it. Generated vocabulary can therefore complement a strong learned sparse retriever, provided that the original query remains strongly represented.

</details>


### [348] [Effective Dense Retrieval using Only In-Context Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38099)
*Nour Jedidi,Abdul Basit Ali,Hang Li,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RICE方法，通过少样本上下文示例让纯解码器大语言模型无需训练即可生成高质量稠密检索表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将纯解码器大语言模型转化为强稠密检索器通常需要某种形式的检索器训练。本文探究能否仅通过少样本上下文示例提示LLM，从而在不训练的情况下生成有效表示。

Method: 提出RICE（Representations from In-Context Examples），一种简单的“免训练”方法，从LLM中提取高质量稠密表示。RICE以提供查询与文档共享上下文的示例作为LLM的条件输入，用于编码。

Result: 实验结果表明，RICE嵌入能够大幅提升基于提示的LLM嵌入的准确率，证明其是一种无需训练即可构建LLM稠密检索器的简单方法。代码已开源于 https://github.com/nourj98/RICE。

Conclusion: RICE证明LLM可仅借助上下文示例生成有效检索表示，为免训练的稠密检索器构建提供了简单可行的方案。

Abstract: Turning decoder-only large language models (LLMs) into strong dense retrievers typically requires some form of retriever training. In this paper, we ask whether LLMs can instead be prompted to produce effective representations for dense retrieval given only a few in-context examples. To answer this, we introduce RICE (Representations from In-Context Examples), a simple "training-free" approach that extracts high-quality dense representations from LLMs. To do so, RICE conditions the LLM on examples that provide a shared context for query and document encoding. Our results demonstrate that RICE embeddings can substantially improve the accuracy of prompt-based LLM embeddings, establishing it as a simple method to build LLM-based dense retrievers that do not require training. We release our code at https://github.com/nourj98/RICE.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [349] [SAIVE: Selecting AI Valuable Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36512)
*Inna Voloshchuk,Hayden Jananthan,Jeremy Kepner*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文为SAIVE启发式方法提供理论基础，通过假设底层数据服从Zipf-Mandelbrot幂律分布，将指数alpha约束在合理范围内，得到一种实用、低成本、无需专家的过滤器，用于从大型数据湖中筛选对AI分析有价值的字段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型企业数据湖存储了来自网络传感器、主机和应用的大量遥测日志，每个事件可能包含数百个字段，导致数据湖无法被AI高效分析。数据湖中许多字段和列缺乏足够的多样性或集中度，对AI分析无用。SAIVE原本只是一个简单的启发式方法（通过检查大表中的少量行并应用直方图的直方图过滤准则来筛选字段），但缺乏理论支撑。

Method: 假设底层数据服从Zipf-Mandelbrot幂律分布，为SAIVE启发式方法建立有原则的理论基础；通过将Zipf-Mandelbrot指数alpha约束在合理范围内，构造出一个筛选准则。

Result: 将alpha约束在合理范围可作为一种实用、低成本、无需专家的过滤器，用于在大型数据集中筛选对AI有价值的实体（字段）。

Conclusion: 该研究为SAIVE启发式方法提供了理论依据，证明了基于Zipf-Mandelbrot幂律分布约束指数alpha的筛选方式是有效的，可用于大规模数据湖中AI分析前的字段选择。

Abstract: Data lakes store large amounts of telemetry, with logs from network sensors, hosts, and applications containing possibly hundreds of fields for every event. Large enterprises are then left with data lakes that cannot be analyzed efficiently with AI. Aggregate analysis looks at persistent shifts in behavior over time. Many of the fields and columns in data lakes are not useful as they do not contain information that is sufficiently diverse or concentrated to support AI analysis. SAIVE is a simple method for examining a few rows in a large table and applies a histogram of histograms filtering criterion to select the fields that for AI analysis is more likely to yield useful results. This paper provides a principled foundation for the SAIVE heuristics by assuming of a Zipf-Mandelbrot power-law distribution of the underlying data. Constraining the Zipf-Mandelbrot exponent alpha to a reasonable range provides a a practical, cheap, expert-free filter for selecting AI valuable entities in large data sets.

</details>


### [350] [Epistemic Typing as a PostgreSQL Table Access Method: Adversarial Conflict Resolution Under Confidence Forgery and Sybil Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36795)
*Venkata Maguluri,Swapna Kommuri,Kritica Sinha,Ashish Sood*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: KNDB 是一个 PostgreSQL 18 表访问方法（TAM），为每行赋予引擎指定的认知类型（MEASURED/INFERRED/DERIVED），并在写时解决槽位冲突；在伪造置信度攻击下显著优于纯置信度基线，并在高密度饱和区优于四类真值发现基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是应对"置信度伪造"这一对抗性威胁：攻击者可声称高置信度的 INFERRED 写入，从而欺骗仅依赖置信度的冲突消解机制。作者希望通过引入由引擎分配的、写时不可伪造的"认知类型"这一独立轴，来增强数据可信度判定的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 以 PostgreSQL 18 表访问方法（TAM）形式实现 KNDB，行仍以普通堆元组存储；2) 覆写 44 个 TAM 回调中的 7 个（tuple_insert、multi_insert、tuple_update、tuple_delete、tuple_insert_speculative、tuple_complete_speculative、relation_toast_am），其余 37 个委托给 heap，并对接口完整性给出论证；3) 每个写入回调内按槽位解决冲突；4) 通过源重建的禁用-测试（中和类型格）验证该轴的关键作用；5) 从原始公式重实现 TruthFinder、CRH、CATD、ACCU 四个基线进行对比；6) 用 k* ~ rho_alg * h_top 形式化密度饱和阈值并做预测验证。

Result: 在伪造置信度工作负载下（攻击者以 [0.95,1.0] 置信度声称 INFERRED，对抗诚实 MEASURED 的 [0.5,0.9]），KNDB 在 Book-Author 融合数据集上比纯置信度基线高 63 个百分点，在 Zheng 众包数据集上高 92.7 个百分点；禁用类型轴后优势消失，证明其作用关键。对四个真值发现基线（重实现误差在已发表数值 0.3 个百分点内），KNDB 在每数据集密度饱和单元之下具有竞争力、达到或超过该单元后占优。饱和阈值 k* ~ rho_alg * h_top 的预测在 Book-Author 上误差 ±20%、Zheng 上 ±30%。由于类型轴由引擎从独立元数据分配、写时不可伪造，KNDB 的 k* 无上界。劣势：在 Zheng 未饱和区，CRH 与 ACCU 优于 KNDB；在三个以最后写入者为准（last-writer-wins）为真值的时间知识编辑基准上，KNDB 得分为零。

Conclusion: KNDB 通过在写时回调中引入引擎分配的、不可伪造的认知类型轴，有效抵御置信度伪造攻击，并在密度饱和区优于主流真值发现方法，其饱和阈值可由 k* 公式预测且因类型轴不可伪造而无上界；但方法并非普适，在低密度场景及 last-writer-wins 语义的时间知识编辑任务上存在明确失败情形，作者对此如实报告。

Abstract: We describe KNDB, a PostgreSQL 18 table access method (TAM) that types every row with an engine-assigned epistemic kind (MEASURED, INFERRED, or DERIVED) and resolves per-slot conflicts inside every write-time heapam callback. Rows land as ordinary heap tuples; seven of the 44 TAM callbacks are overridden (tuple_insert, multi_insert, tuple_update, tuple_delete, tuple_insert_speculative, tuple_complete_speculative, relation_toast_am), the other 37 delegate to heap; we provide a completeness argument over the interface as a paper artefact. This paper reports the engineering behind that decision and the adversarial evaluation that motivated it. On a confidence-forgery workload where an attacker asserts INFERRED writes with confidence in [0.95,1.0] against honest MEASURED writes with confidence in [0.5,0.9], KNDB beats a confidence-only baseline by 63 percentage points on the Book-Author fusion dataset and 92.7 points on the Zheng crowdsourcing dataset. Both wins are proven load-bearing on the kind axis by a source-rebuild disable-and-test in which the lattice is neutralised and the win vanishes. Against four truth-discovery baselines (TruthFinder, CRH, CATD, ACCU) reimplemented from the original equations and validated to within 0.3 percentage points of the published numbers, KNDB is competitive below a per-dataset density-saturation cell and dominant at or above it. We formalise the cell as k* ~ rho_alg * h_top, where h_top is per-slot top honest surface-form support, and validate the prediction within +/-20% on Book-Author and +/-30% on Zheng. Because the kind axis is assigned by the engine from independent metadata and cannot be forged at write time, KNDB's k* is unbounded. The paper is honest about where KNDB loses: CRH and ACCU outperform KNDB below saturation on Zheng, and KNDB scores zero on three temporal knowledge-editing benchmarks whose ground truth is last-writer-wins.

</details>


### [351] [VADER: Filtered Vector Search with Declarative Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38053)
*Manos Chatzakis,Duo Lu,Helena Caminal,Yannis Chronis,Fatma Özcan,Yannis Papakonstantinou,Timofey Asyrkin,Sebastian Infante Murguia,Itai Rosenblatt,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: VADER 提出声明式召回率的近似过滤向量搜索方法，消除超参数调优，实现近乎最优的提前终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似过滤向量搜索（FVS）中，过滤谓词的选择性和相关性会显著影响搜索难度，即使用户给出相同查询向量，也往往需要大量人工调参才能高效执行，这成为结构化数据与向量嵌入结合的瓶颈。

Method: VADER 引入声明式召回率，用户只需指定目标召回率；系统采用过滤感知的召回率预测器，能在不同选择性和相关性下泛化而无需显式调参，并在预测召回率达到目标时提前终止搜索。

Result: 大量实验表明，VADER 实现了近乎最优的提前终止，相比最佳基线提速最高达 53%，结果质量提升 28%。

Conclusion: VADER 是首个通过声明式召回率消除超参数调优的近似过滤向量搜索方案，兼顾效率与结果质量。

Abstract: Approximate filtered vector search (FVS), a core operation in many data management tasks that combine structured data with vector embeddings, exhibits increased complexity due to the characteristics of filtering predicates. Each predicate is defined by selectivity (i.e., the fraction of vectors that satisfy the predicate) and correlation (i.e., the relationship between the filter and the vector space), which can significantly affect search difficulty even for the same query vector. This poses a key challenge for users aiming to integrate vector search with structured data, as efficient execution often requires extensive manual tuning of algorithm parameters. In this paper, we present VADER, the first approach that eliminates hyperparameter tuning by introducing declarative recall for approximate filtered vector search. With declarative recall, users specify a desired recall target, and VADER executes FVS queries to meet this target without requiring manual configuration. VADER achieves this by employing a filter-aware recall predictor that generalizes across varying selectivities and correlations without explicit tuning, and by performing early termination once the predicted recall reaches the user-defined target. Through extensive experimental evaluation, we show that VADER achieves near-optimal early termination, while providing significant speedups of up to 53% faster and improved result quality of 28% compared to the best-performing baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [352] [Mixture-of-Kittens: MoE Megakernel for NVL72s](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36070)
*Stuart H. Sul,Nash Brown,Henry Wildermuth,William Lin,Federico Cassano,Christopher Ré*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Mixture-of-Kittens (MoK)，一种针对Nvidia NVL72规模扩展架构设计的MoE训练系统，通过三项关键优化将Moe层计算融合为单一确定性训练megakernel，实现最高2.37倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器系统正快速向规模扩展架构（scale-up）整合，数十至数千GPU通过高带宽单跳网络通信。现有MoE训练系统针对传统scale-out网络优化，迁移到该硬件条件下表现不佳，甚至比PyTorch+NCCL朴素基线还慢。随着行业路线图指向更大规模扩展域，理解该硬件范式的性能权衡日益重要。

Method: MoK基于三项洞察：(1)按算子选择push或pull通信方式；(2)重构计算-通信重叠方式；(3)完全消除CPU-GPU同步。这些洞察被凝练为单一确定性训练megakernel，融合了token dispatch、共享与路由专家FFN、token combine。

Result: 在来自四个广泛使用的开源权重模型的MoE层形状上，MoK达到最强公开基线最高2.37倍吞吐。在跨多个GB300 NVL72机架的512 GPU生产运行中，MoK将端到端训练吞吐提升1.41倍。

Conclusion: MoK证明了针对scale-up域专门设计的MoE训练系统能显著优于面向传统scale-out网络的方案，为未来更大规模扩展域下的训练系统设计提供了方向。

Abstract: AI accelerator systems are rapidly consolidating into scale-up architectures, where tens to thousands of GPUs communicate over high-bandwidth, single-hop fabrics. We find that existing Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training systems, optimized for conventional scale-out networks, transfer poorly to this setting, often running slower than a naive baseline built with PyTorch and NCCL. With industry roadmaps pointing toward even larger scale-up domains, understanding the performance tradeoffs of this hardware regime is increasingly important. We present Mixture-of-Kittens (MoK), an MoE training system designed for Nvidia NVL72. MoK builds on three insights that unlock performance on scale-up domains: (1) choosing push- or pull-based communication per operator, (2) restructuring the computation-communication overlap, and (3) fully eliminating CPU-GPU synchronization. MoK distills these insights into a single deterministic training megakernel that fuses token dispatch, shared and routed expert FFNs, and token combine. Across MoE layer shapes from four widely used open-weight models, MoK delivers up to $2.37\times$ the throughput of the strongest publicly available baseline. In a production run on 512 GPUs spanning multiple GB300 NVL72 racks, MoK improves end-to-end training throughput by $1.41\times$.

</details>


### [353] [ParaAnya: Accelerating Parallel Diffusion Sampling with Plug-and-Play Output Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36522)
*Chee-En Yu,Xiao-Xi Tan,Yi-Cheng Lin,Yun-Shao Tsai,Chee-An Yu,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出ParaAnya输出缓存机制，消除并行扩散采样中重叠时间步的冗余计算，显著降低NFE并加速生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型顺序采样存在严重计算瓶颈。现有并行时间（PinT）求解器通过滑动窗口并行生成，但重叠窗口机制迫使网络重复评估相同时间步；当迭代间输入变化很小时，这些冗余评估造成大量计算浪费。

Method: 提出ParaAnya，一种与并行采样算法无关的输出缓存机制。它缓存扩散模型的输入-输出对，在重叠时间步复用缓存输出，仅将缓存未命中的时间步分派给GPU工作节点，从而消除冗余计算，同时保持底层算法更新规则的结构。将其集成到四种代表性并行采样算法中。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.5上，用DDIM在八块GPU上评估四种并行采样器：相比无缓存版本获得1.30–2.43×加速，NFE最多降低70.1%，相比单GPU串行采样最高达5.62×加速，同时保持相当的CLIP分数。

Conclusion: ParaAnya通过输出缓存有效消除并行扩散采样中的冗余函数评估，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升效率，且对并行采样算法具有通用性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generative tasks, but their inherently sequential sampling process introduces a severe computational bottleneck. Recent Parallel-in-Time (PinT) solvers attempt to mitigate this by parallelizing generation across a sliding window of timesteps, advancing the window only when step-wise changes stabilize. However, this overlapping window mechanism forces the network to repeatedly evaluate the same timesteps. When the input variations between iterations are minimal, these redundant evaluations lead to significant computational waste. To address this inefficiency, we propose ParaAnya, an output cache mechanism agnostic to the parallel sampling algorithm that can reduce the number of function evaluations (NFE). ParaAnya caches input-output pairs of diffusion models and reuses the cached output at overlapping timesteps. By dispatching only cache-miss timesteps to GPU workers, our approach eliminates redundant computation while preserving the structure of the underlying algorithms' update rules. We integrate ParaAnya into four representative parallel sampling algorithms and evaluate its performance on Stable Diffusion v1.5. Across four parallel samplers evaluated with DDIM on eight GPUs, ParaAnya provides $1.30$--$2.43\times$ speedups over their uncached counterparts and reduces NFE by up to 70.1\%, reaching up to a $5.62\times$ speedup over single-GPU serial sampling while maintaining comparable CLIP scores.

</details>


### [354] [Reshaping Rollout Workloads for Asynchronous RL Post-Training on Heterogeneous Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36899)
*Jiahui Li,Hao Nie,Yibo Zhu,Pengjin Xie,Yu Zhou,Xiaolong Zheng,Liang Liu,Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CadenceRL 通过结构化工作负载重塑（Pacing 与 Concentration）解决异构集群中强化学习长时程多轮 rollout 的解码吞吐与轨迹完成时间之间的调度冲突，吞吐提升最高 48%，P95 延迟降低最高 64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练日益依赖长时程、多轮 rollout，任务规模超出单集群后需跨集群组建 rollout 池，引入硬件异构性。调度需同时满足两个目标：硬件侧期望高聚合解码吞吐，轨迹侧期望快速完成。由于自回归解码受内存带宽限制，大活跃批次虽能摊薄权重读取以提升吞吐，但会降低单条轨迹的带宽份额、延长完成时间，二者存在内在张力。因此调度目标转为"专业化分工"，而异构硬件恰好为分工提供了条件：高带宽加速器适合长上下文任务，高性价比加速器适合维持大批次。但工作负载演化使这种分工难以持续，动态重分配还面临循环依赖——某次迁移的收益取决于后续放置决策。

Method: CadenceRL 通过结构化工作负载重塑来绕开循环依赖，而非逐次估计迁移收益。具体包含三项机制：Pacing——用较短轨迹替代长上下文轨迹，提供结构性正向变换，维持大批次以实现高吞吐；Concentration——当累积的陈旧度要求更快完成时，将残余的长上下文尾部集中调度到高亲和性 worker 上；Late-bound KV 准备——在目标节点选定之前，先分阶段准备已累积的前缀。

Result: 在异构 rollout 池上，CadenceRL 将解码吞吐最高提升 48%，并将 P95 轨迹延迟最高降低 64%。增加高带宽加速器可降低尾部延迟，增加高性价比加速器可提升吞吐，且无需手动配置路由。

Conclusion: CadenceRL 表明，与其对动态迁移收益进行逐次估计，不如通过结构性重塑工作负载形态，即可在异构 rollout 池上同时改善吞吐与尾部延迟，并让硬件扩展自然映射到不同性能目标，减少人工调优。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training increasingly relies on long-horizon, multi-turn rollouts. As post-training jobs outgrow a single cluster, rollout pools assembled across clusters introduce hardware heterogeneity. Rollout scheduling must serve two stakeholders: the hardware needs high aggregate decode throughput, while each trajectory needs to finish quickly. The tension arises from the memory-bandwidth-bound nature of autoregressive decoding. A large active batch amortizes weight reads for high throughput but leaves each trajectory a smaller bandwidth share and a longer completion time. The scheduling objective is therefore specialization, letting different workers serve different roles. Heterogeneous hardware further enables this specialization. High-bandwidth accelerators favor long-context work, while cost-efficient accelerators sustain large batches. Workload evolution makes this specialization difficult to sustain, and dynamic reassignment faces a circular dependency because a move's benefit depends on subsequent placement decisions.
  We present CadenceRL, which bypasses this dependency through structural workload reshaping rather than per-move benefit estimation. Pacing replaces long-context trajectories with shorter ones, providing a structurally positive transformation that sustains large active batches for high throughput. When accumulated staleness demands faster completion, concentration directs the residual long-context tail onto high-affinity workers. Late-bound KV preparation stages accumulated prefixes before a destination is selected. On heterogeneous rollout pools, CadenceRL improves decode throughput by up to 48% and reduces P95 trajectory latency by up to 64%. Adding high-bandwidth accelerators reduces tail latency, while adding cost-efficient accelerators increases throughput, without manual routing configuration.

</details>


### [355] [Efficient Agentic LLM Serving over SSD-based Sparse KV Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36938)
*Wenhao He,Ping Zhang,Xiaohe Hu,Chutian Wang,Jinlong Hou,Yuan Cheng,Peng Sun,Fangcheng Fu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Janus 是一个面向稀疏注意力 LLM 的智能体服务框架，采用以 SSD 为中心的 KV 存储，通过预测 KV 需求将 SSD 读取移出关键路径并优化 SSD 效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体会话在推理与工具使用间交替，累积长历史。稀疏注意力可减少计算但 KV 选择依赖推理中间值，迫使 SSD 读取处于推理关键路径；同时 SSD 碎片化访问与读写干扰进一步拖慢读取。

Method: Janus 聚焦 append prefill，利用模型自身 KV 选择模块在较早中间值上预测 KV 需求，无需额外训练；预测读取与模型计算重叠，未命中项在注意力执行前补取。为提升 SSD 效率，合并相邻读取、将散乱 KV 页打包为 CPU 上的顺序写，并在读取期间限制后台写。

Result: 在三种模型和三种智能体轨迹上，Janus 的首 token 延迟相比现有工作提升最多 1.57–3.69 倍（平均 1.22–1.85 倍），同时保持解码效率。

Conclusion: Janus 通过预测式 KV 预取与 SSD 友好的 I/O 优化，有效将 SSD 读取移出关键路径，显著降低首 token 延迟且不损失解码效率。

Abstract: Agentic sessions driven by Large language models (LLMs) often alternate between model inference and tool use, accumulating long histories across successive rounds. Serving these sessions efficiently requires reducing attention computation and retaining history key-value (KV) caches to avoid recomputation. Recently, frontier open-source LLMs adopt sparse attention to reduce computation by selecting only part of the history, while SSDs provide a cheaper alternative to CPU DRAM for storing KV caches. However, sparse KV selection depends on the ad hoc intermediate values during model inference, so it forces SSD reads to lie on the inference critical path. These reads are further slowed by fragmented accesses and read-write interference in SSDs.
  To address these challenges, we present Janus, an agentic serving framework for sparse attention LLMs with SSD-centric KV storage. Janus focuses on append prefill, which processes each round's newly added inputs and accounts for most history KV loading. To move SSD reads out of the critical path, Janus runs the model's own KV selection module on earlier intermediate values, predicting KV demand without additional training. The predicted reads overlap with model computation, and any prediction misses are fetched before attention executes to preserve model outputs. To improve SSD efficiency, Janus coalesces adjacent reads, packs scattered KV pages into sequential writes on the CPU, and limits background writes while reads are active. Across three models and three agentic traces, Janus outperforms existing works by up to 1.57-3.69 times (1.22-1.85 times on average) in terms of the time to first token latency, while maintaining decode efficiency.

</details>


### [356] [Purlin: Separating Orchestration from the Datapath of Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36954)
*Osayamen Jonathan Aimuyo,Swapnil Gandhi,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Purlin是一个scale-up通信框架，通过将集合通信的语义、编排和数据通路解耦，实现对新硬件与定制工作负载的高效适配，在多种GPU和大模型服务场景中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集合通信实现将语义、编排（数据移动的位置与时机）和数据通路（数据如何移动）耦合在一起，导致难以采用新的硬件机制以及为应用定制通信。分布式推理依赖GPU集合通信，且必须跟上硬件和专用工作负载的演进，因此需要解耦这些关注点。

Method: 提出Purlin框架，分三层：顶层将集合通信定义为输入输出布局的命名加复制或归约操作；中间层引入共享编排协议SNAC（Stage, Notify, And Consume），从规格推导协调；底层为硬件专用数据通路Atom，实现copy和reduce两个关键数据移动原语。该分离使得在复用SNAC编排的同时可定制集合通信并采纳新硬件机制。

Result: 在A100、H200、B200 GPU上评估，7种集合通信的延迟最高加速5.14x，带宽最高提升4.50x。集成到SGLang后，离线LLM服务吞吐与交互性平均提升1.13x、最高1.37x；在线LLM推理交互性平均提升1.26x、最高2.85x，过载时增益最大；扩散图像生成端到端延迟最高降低1.13x。

Conclusion: 通过分离语义、编排与数据通路，Purlin在多种硬件和LLM/扩散服务场景中取得一致且显著的性能收益，验证了解耦设计的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Distributed inference depends on GPU collective communication that must keep pace with evolving hardware and specialized workloads. However, existing collective implementations often couple semantics, orchestration (where and when data moves), and the datapath (how data moves). This coupling makes it costly to adopt new hardware mechanisms and customize communication for applications. We present Purlin, a scale-up communication framework that separates these concerns. At the top of Purlin, we specify collectives as a naming of an input and output layout and a copy or reduction operation. In the middle, we introduce a shared orchestration protocol, Stage, Notify, And Consume (SNAC), which derives coordination from these specifications. Below SNAC sits a hardware-specific datapath we call Atom, which implements two key data movement primitives for collectives: copy and reduce. This separation lets us customize collectives and adopt new hardware mechanisms while reusing orchestration via SNAC. We evaluate Purlin on A100, H200, and B200 GPUs. Across seven collectives, Purlin achieves latency speedups of up to 5.14x and bandwidth improvements of up to 4.50x over baselines. Integrated into SGLang, Purlin improves offline LLM serving throughput and interactivity by 1.13x on average and up to 1.37x over baselines. For online LLM inference, Purlin improves interactivity by 1.26x on average and up to 2.85x, with the largest gain occurring under overload. For diffusion image generation, Purlin reduces end-to-end latency by up to 1.13x.

</details>


### [357] [Cobalt: Leveraging Expert Co-activation for Efficient Distributed MoE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36959)
*Junkang Zhou,Xinyi Liu,Fangcheng Fu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cobalt是一个利用专家共激活特性的高效MoE训练框架，通过两阶段专家布局规划与通信感知任务分配，减少跨节点通信与负载不均衡，在32块B200上实现最高2.41倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MoE训练依赖专家并行（EP），但其效率受两大系统瓶颈制约：跨节点令牌传输受限于节点间带宽，专家负载倾斜导致GPU间计算不均衡。已有工作基于单专家负载统计进行缓解，却忽视了专家之间可能共享通信这一事实。

Method: 作者实证发现许多专家对常被同一token共同激活。据此提出Cobalt框架：采用两阶段专家布局规划器，依据演化的专家共激活与负载状况自适应调整布局，周期性将高频共激活专家共置于同一节点以减少跨节点通信，并按步进行节点内调整以重平衡负载；进而设计通信感知的任务分配方法，基于当前布局将令牌路由至更少的远程节点。

Result: 在32块B200 GPU上的实验表明，相比现有MoE训练框架，Cobalt取得最高1.53-2.41倍（平均1.28-1.89倍）的加速，同时将跨节点令牌流量降低75.74%-99.26%。

Conclusion: 利用专家共激活特性可有效缓解EP中的跨节点通信与负载不均衡瓶颈，Cobalt在保持计算开销近似恒定的同时显著提升大规模MoE训练效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has increasingly become a mainstream approach for scaling large language models, as it expands model capacity while keeping computation cost nearly constant. Training large-scale MoE models relies on Expert Parallelism (EP), which distributes expert replicas across GPUs and exchanges tokens through all-to-all communication. The efficiency of EP is often constrained by two system bottlenecks: cross-node token transfers are limited by inter-node bandwidth, while skewed expert workloads lead to imbalanced computation across GPUs. Prior work mitigates these bottlenecks based on per-expert workload statistics, but overlooks the fact that experts could share the communication.
  In this work, we empirically present the observation that many pairs of experts are frequently co-activated by individual tokens. Motivated by this, we present Cobalt, an efficient MoE training framework that leverages expert co-activation to reduce cross-node traffic and workload imbalance. Cobalt adopts a two-stage expert layout planner that adapts expert layout to the evolving expert co-activation and workload conditions. It periodically co-locates frequently co-activated experts on the same node to reduce the cross-node communication, and performs per-step intra-node adjustment to rebalance the workloads. Subsequently, we develop a communication-aware task assignment method that routes tokens to fewer remote nodes based on the current expert layout. Experiments on 32 B200 GPUs show that Cobalt achieves up to 1.53-2.41 times (1.28-1.89 times on average) of speedup compared to existing MoE training frameworks, while reducing cross-node token traffic by 75.74%-99.26%.

</details>


### [358] [vSkipper: Translating Dynamic Layer Skipping into LLM Serving Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37062)
*Wei Da,Yavuz Ferhatosmanoglu,Evangelia Kalyvianaki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: vSkipper 是一个虚拟化层，使动态层跳过器可插入现代 LLM 服务引擎，在保持连续批处理等机制的同时将跳过转化为实际服务延迟和吞吐收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态层跳过虽能减少 LLM 计算，但现有跳过器依赖专用生成循环，无法与现代服务引擎集成，导致少执行层数并不必然降低服务延迟（如 FlexiDepth 平均跳过 8/32 层却解码更慢 14.6–21.0%）。

Method: 提出 vSkipper 虚拟化层，支持连续批处理、固定形状批次、分页 KV 缓存和捕获解码图；在每个路由层按跳过决策对 token 分组，仅在预测有利时使用路由执行；在 SGLang 中实现并评估。

Result: 在负载曲线拐点处，端到端平均延迟在 GSM8K 降低 36.8%、BBH 降低 13.6%；饱和时请求吞吐分别提升 11.3% 和 7.4%；除检查点自身外无额外统计显著质量损失；在合成跳过策略、两个 Qwen3 跳过器和三种 GPU 上验证可复用性。

Conclusion: vSkipper 是首个在现代 LLM 服务引擎中实现逐 token 内部层跳过服务效率收益的系统，代码已开源为 SGLang 分支。

Abstract: Dynamic layer skipping reduces LLM computation by allowing each token to execute only a subset of the model's layers. However, existing skippers rely on specialized generation loops and do not integrate with modern serving engines. As a result, fewer executed layers do not necessarily translate into lower serving latency: FlexiDepth skips 8 of Llama-3-8B's 32 layers on average, yet its standard generation loop decodes 14.6--21.0% more slowly than the base model. We present vSkipper, a virtualization layer that makes dynamic layer skippers pluggable in serving engines while preserving continuous batching, fixed-shape batches, paged KV caching, and captured decode graphs. At each routed layer, vSkipper groups tokens by the skipper's decision and uses routed execution only when predicted to be profitable. We implement vSkipper in SGLang and evaluate the released FlexiDepth checkpoint against upstream SGLang under identical prompts, arrivals, output lengths, and launch settings. At the knee of upstream's load curve, vSkipper reduces mean end-to-end latency by 36.8% on GSM8K and 13.6% on BBH. Under saturation, it increases request throughput by 11.3% and 7.4%. Serving adds no statistically resolved quality loss beyond the checkpoint's own. Across synthetic skip policies, two Qwen3 skippers, and three GPUs, we demonstrate reuse without workload-specific tuning. To our knowledge, vSkipper is the first system to realize serving-efficiency gains from per-token interior layer skipping within a modern LLM serving engine. The code is open-sourced as an SGLang fork at https://github.com/AKafakA/sglang-vskipper/tree/vskipper-ref

</details>


### [359] [Windowed and Quantized Group-Based ADMM for Distributed Optimization in Heterogeneous Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37069)
*Gaiguo Wei,Qingying Zhang,Heqiang Wang,Yu Zhang,Xiaoxiong Zhong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出WQ-GADMM方法，在边缘网络分布式优化中通过窗口化分组更新、上下行量化通信、允许有界陈旧性和非精确近端更新，降低通信开销并保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘网络中客户端计算能力异构且通信资源有限，现有分布式优化难以在有限激活容量下高效协调更新并降低通信成本。因此需要一种能适应异构计算、减少通信量且保持收敛性的方法。

Method: 提出Windowed and Quantized Group-Based ADMM（WQ-GADMM）。按估计计算时间对客户端分组；每轮窗口内只激活有限数量的组；当所有组均更新一次后云端更新全局模型；对下行和上行模型交换均进行量化以降低通信成本；同时允许有界模型陈旧性和非精确近端本地更新。

Result: 针对光滑非凸目标，在给定假设与参数条件下建立了平均平方KKT残差界，该界由随迭代次数下降的项和依赖量化的误差项组成。在MNIST和CIFAR-10上，12-bit通信降低了通信量和模拟墙钟时间，测试精度与全精度相当；12-bit配置还保持完整组覆盖，并实现比基线更短的平均组间完成间隔。

Conclusion: WQ-GADMM能够在有限激活容量和量化通信条件下有效协调边缘网络分布式优化，在保持与全精度相当的测试精度的同时降低通信开销和时间，并具备理论收敛性保证。

Abstract: Distributed optimization in edge networks is constrained by heterogeneous client computing capabilities and limited communication resources. We propose the Windowed and Quantized Group-Based Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (WQ-GADMM) to coordinate group updates under limited activation capacity and reduce communication costs. Clients are grouped by estimated computation time. Each window activates a limited number of groups per round, and the cloud updates the global model after all groups have updated once. The method quantizes both downlink and uplink model exchanges to reduce communication costs and allows bounded model staleness and inexact proximal local updates. For smooth nonconvex objectives, we establish an average squared Karush-Kuhn-Tucker residual bound under the stated assumptions and parameter conditions. The bound consists of a term that decreases with the iteration count and a quantization-dependent error term. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that 12-bit communication reduces communication volume and simulated wall-clock time while maintaining test accuracy comparable to full precision. The 12-bit configuration also maintains complete group coverage and achieves shorter mean group inter-completion gaps than the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [360] [ARGOS: Reinforcement Learning-Driven Multidimensional Elasticity for Service Orchestration in the Computing Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37085)
*Javier Mateos-Bravo,Sergio Laso,Juan Luis Herrera,Ilir Murturi,Pantelis Frangoudis,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 ARGOS，一种基于深度强化学习的端到端控制器，将多维弹性问题建模为每个请求的马尔可夫决策过程，同时调节分析质量与集群压力，并配合容量感知准入控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算连续体中的数据密集型服务需在异构、容量有限且不均衡的节点上平衡分析质量、资源使用与成本。当资源扩展达到容量上限时，需求变化与集群压力必须在客户端定义的质量范围内被吸收。现有编排器主要调整资源、放置或副本，而覆盖率、采样率、新鲜度等分析需求保持固定，难以应对这一挑战。

Method: ARGOS 将多维弹性建模为针对每个请求的马尔可夫决策过程，状态涉及分析质量与集群压力，并辅以容量感知的准入控制；采用深度强化学习策略进行决策。

Result: 在异构集群上以受控负载和时变多租户到达进行评测：受控场景中 DRL 策略持续优于非学习基线，并接近独立调优的最佳固定参考；实时评测中相对静态中点方案有改进，未记录 CPU 或内存违规，但仍存在覆盖率违规。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当仅靠资源扩展不足时，深度强化学习可作为多维弹性的有效自适应机制。

Abstract: Data-intensive services in the Computing Continuum must balance analytics quality, resource usage, and cost across heterogeneous nodes with limited and uneven capacity. This balance becomes especially difficult when resource scaling reaches capacity limits, because changes in demand and cluster pressure must then be absorbed without violating client-defined quality ranges. Existing orchestrators mainly adapt resources, placements, or replicas, while analytics requirements such as coverage, sample, and freshness remain fixed. This article presents ARGOS, the Adaptive Reinforcement Learning-Driven Governance for Orchestrated Services, an end-to-end controller that formulates multidimensional elasticity as a per-request Markov decision process over analytics quality and cluster pressure, supported by capacity-aware admission. ARGOS is evaluated under controlled workloads and time-varying multi-tenant arrivals on a heterogeneous cluster. Across the controlled scenarios, the deep reinforcement learning policies consistently outperform the non-learning baselines and approach the independently tuned best-fixed reference. A separate live evaluation reports improvements over the static midpoint under realistic and saturated arrivals, with no recorded CPU or memory violations but remaining coverage violations. These results support deep reinforcement learning as an adaptive mechanism for multidimensional elasticity when resource scaling alone is insufficient.

</details>


### [361] [DScale: Scaling Block-Diffusion Speculative Decoding with Adaptive Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37532)
*Rongjian Chen,Minxian Xu,Zhengxin Fang,Kejiang Ye,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DScale，一种面向大语言模型高并发推理的块扩散投机解码方法，通过路径感知分块、动态验证长度分配与固定地址工作空间，在不改动草稿模型架构、权重与草稿长度的前提下提升吞吐并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型应用对高效推理需求日益增长。高并发下块扩散投机解码存在验证填充、候选被拒、以及可变前缀与固定形状计算图不兼容等问题；统一截断则会牺牲可接受token，导致效率与质量受损。

Method: DScale保留草稿器架构、权重和完整草稿长度；引入独立的11.2万参数预测器，无需置信度校准或硬件速度曲线准备。采用路径感知分块减少填充；动态验证长度（DVL）分配将已打分前缀打包进原生验证容量的一半；固定地址工作空间在验证与接受过程中传递变化的边界，同时复用已捕获的计算图。

Result: 在A100-40GB、张量并行度1下，Qwen3-8B与Qwen3-4B覆盖四个数据集、并发8-32，复用各自目标模型冻结的预测器。几何平均吞吐相比DFlash分别提升43.9%和48.8%，相比DSpark提升22.2%和37.7%，相比Domino提升24.4%和32.0%，且请求延迟更低。累积消融显示三种机制依次加入可逐步提高几何平均吞吐，预算调整改善可接受token保留率。GPU剖析显示GSM8K上完整解码步时间相比DFlash降低30.8-52.5%。

Conclusion: DScale通过路径感知分块、DVL分配与固定地址工作空间三项机制，在保持草稿模型完整性的情况下有效缓解投机解码的填充与兼容性问题，显著提升高并发推理吞吐并降低延迟，消融与剖析结果验证了各机制的有效性。

Abstract: Growing large language model applications demand efficient inference. At high concurrency, block-diffusion speculative decoding suffers from verification padding, rejected candidates, and incompatibility between variable prefixes and fixed-shape graphs. Uniform truncation sacrifices acceptable tokens. We present DScale, preserving drafter architecture, weights, and full draft length. A separate 112K-parameter predictor requires neither confidence calibration nor hardware speed-curve preparation. Path-aware tiles reduce padding. Dynamic verify-length (DVL) allocation packs scored prefixes into half the native verification capacity. Fixed-address workspaces propagate changing boundaries through verification and acceptance while reusing captured graphs. On A100-40GB with tensor parallelism 1, Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B cover four datasets and concurrency 8-32, reusing each target's frozen predictor. Geometric-mean throughput gains across these configurations are respectively 43.9% and 48.8% over DFlash, 22.2% and 37.7% over DSpark, and 24.4% and 32.0% over Domino, with lower request latency. Cumulative ablations show that adding the three mechanisms successively increases geometric-mean throughput, while budget adjustment improves accepted-token retention. GPU profiling shows that complete decode-step time on GSM8K decreases by 30.8-52.5% relative to DFlash

</details>


### [362] [SPLASH: Switching Parallel Layouts of Attention with Seamless Handoff for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37626)
*Chuan Liu,Shuoming Zhang,Zhicheng Li,Qianqi Sun,Ruiyuan Xu,Qiuchu Yu,Xiyu Shi,Huimin Cui,Jiacheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SPLASH 是一个能在请求运行期间动态切换注意力并行布局的推理服务系统，以近零开销的切换机制和新增的 DOP 布局，把端到端吞吐提升 1.3-1.73 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 没有任何单一注意力并行方式能在所有负载下都表现良好：低并发偏好张量并行，多独立请求偏好数据并行注意力，长提示偏好上下文并行。而推理类、智能体类和 RL-rollout 类负载会让同一批请求从"多个短请求"演变为"少数超长请求"，最优布局随之改变。但现有服务引擎在启动时就固定一种布局，因为切换布局意味着排空请求并重启 worker。

Method: 基于一个关键观察：现代注意力（KV 头很少甚至没有）将请求 KV 缓存的存放位置与注意力权重的分片方式解耦。由此：其一，各布局差异仅在于谁拥有权重和缓存，且大部分状态已位于下一布局所需位置，SPLASH 复用这些状态，在推理后台迁移其余部分，并在 batch 边界完成交接；其二，该解耦暴露出既有引擎缺失的布局——解耦所有权并行（DOP），像张量并行那样分片注意力权重，同时像数据并行注意力那样将每个请求的缓存保留在单一拥有者上。配合感知切换的调度器，在四种布局中随负载选择最优者。

Result: 切换开销极低，中位开销不到所在步骤的 0.51%。DOP 不复制权重也不复制缓存，比数据并行注意力多提供 27-60% 的 KV 容量，并在 KV 内存限制准入时给调度器提供选择。在 B200 上服务 GLM-5.3，端到端服务吞吐相比固定布局部署提升 1.3-1.73 倍；在 H200 上的 DeepSeek-V3.2 和 DCU 上的 GLM-5.3-Flash 也出现相同的布局机制。

Conclusion: 通过在请求运行中切换注意力并行布局，SPLASH 突破了服务引擎"一次固定布局"的限制，并以低成本切换、DOP 新布局与切换感知调度，在多种硬件与模型上取得显著吞吐收益。

Abstract: No single way of parallelizing attention serves large language models well under all loads. Low concurrency favors tensor parallelism, many independent requests favor data-parallel attention, and long prompts favor context parallelism. Reasoning, agentic, and RL-rollout workloads make a fixed choice untenable: a batch that begins as many short requests ends as a few very long ones, so the best layout changes while the same requests run. Serving engines nevertheless fix one layout at launch, because changing it has meant draining requests and restarting workers. We present SPLASH, a serving system that switches the parallel layout of attention while requests are running. It builds on one observation: modern attention, with few or no KV heads, decouples where a request's KV cache lives from how attention weights are sharded. This has two consequences. First, layouts differ only in who owns the weights and the cache, and most of that state already sits where the next layout needs it; SPLASH reuses it, moves the rest in the background of ongoing inference, and hands off at a batch boundary, making a switch nearly free: its median overhead is under 0.51% of the step it runs in. Second, the decoupling exposes a layout that existing engines lack: Decoupled Ownership Parallelism (DOP) shards attention weights as tensor parallelism does while keeping each request's cache on a single owner as data-parallel attention does. DOP replicates neither, offers 27-60% more KV capacity than data-parallel attention, and gives the scheduler a choice when KV memory limits admission. A transition-aware scheduler follows the best of the four layouts as load changes. On B200 GPUs serving GLM-5.3, SPLASH improves end-to-end serving throughput by 1.3-1.73x over fixed-layout deployments, and the same layout regimes appear with DeepSeek-V3.2 on H200 and GLM-5.3-Flash on DCU.

</details>


### [363] [FP64 Is All You Want, INT8 Is All You Need, FP4/6/8 Is All You Have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37693)
*Pratyai Mazumder,Alexandru Calotoiu,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 将Ozaki方案族统一建模为最小化低精度GEMM次数的组合优化问题，给出GEMM次数下界，并据此导出FP6、FP8、FP4新方案，在Blackwell GPU上显著快于原生FP64。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ozaki方案II用INT8 GEMM通过余数模互素模数模拟FP64矩阵乘法，后续出现了FP8、FP4变体，但这些方案分散、缺乏统一视角，且关于"给定格式/累加器/内维时最少需要多少次低精度GEMM"缺乏理论指导与最优选择依据。

Method: 把Ozaki方案族视为一个统一族，将方案选择表述为组合优化程序：对每个模数给定一组用低精度GEMM计算该模数下乘积的有限方式，目标是在任意格式、累加器和内维下选出模数与方式的组合使GEMM次数最少；同时推导整个方案族GEMM次数的下界。

Result: 应用于当前GPU格式，方法给出首个FP6方案、比以往任何方案GEMM次数更少的FP8方案，以及一个FP4方案（下界证明其在模数位于给定范围内时是该族中GEMM次数最少的）；在三种Blackwell GPU上实现，INT8、FP8、FP4方案均快于原生FP64，在B300上最高加速83倍。

Conclusion: 该统一组合优化框架可为Ozaki式低精度模拟高精度矩阵乘法提供最优方案选择与理论下界，并在现代GPU上实现远超原生FP64的加速，验证了低精度GEMM模拟的实用性与最优性。

Abstract: Ozaki scheme II emulates FP64 matrix products with INT8 ones through residues modulo pairwise coprime moduli, and variants for FP8 and FP4 have followed. We treat these schemes as one family and pose the choice of a scheme as a combinatorial program that minimizes the number of low-precision GEMMs. Given, for each modulus, a finite set of ways to compute products modulo it from low-precision GEMMs, we find the choice of moduli and ways with the fewest GEMMs, for any format, accumulator and inner dimension, and derive lower bounds on the GEMM count over the whole family. Applied to the formats of current GPUs, the method gives the first FP6 schemes, an FP8 scheme with fewer GEMMs than any previous one, and an FP4 scheme that the bounds show needs the fewest GEMMs of any scheme in the family whose moduli lie in a stated range. Implemented on three Blackwell GPUs, the INT8, FP8 and FP4 schemes run faster than native FP64, up to 83x on B300.

</details>


### [364] [Joint Effects of GPU Server Topology, Parallelism, and Congestion Control on MoE Inference: A Controlled Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37828)
*Kaikai Yuan,Rui Xi,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文用ASTRA-sim+NS-3对32 GPU的MoE推理进行768次确定性仿真，发现通信暴露占完成时间89.9%–95.8%，且服务器拓扑、并行方案、集合算法与拥塞控制共同决定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型通过稀疏激活扩展容量，但跨GPU推理引入TP集合通信与EP分发/合并操作；完成时间不仅取决于通信量，还取决于逻辑分组如何映射到服务器内互联、GPU-NIC连接和节点间网络，因此需要系统性地量化这些因素的联合影响。

Method: 使用ASTRA-sim配合NS-3离散事件后端，构建32 GPU rank的受控矩阵（数据/流水并行固定为1）。负载为固定长度4096 token的prefill类合成Chakra trace，来自四种MoE配置。实验覆盖6种服务器拓扑、4种TP/EP划分、2种TP集合算法、4种网络/拥塞控制模式，共768次确定性仿真。

Result: 在每种模型144个启用反馈的配置子集中，暴露通信占平均完成时间的89.9%–95.8%；TP16EP2的平均完成时间是TP2EP16的3.68–4.35倍；固定rank映射下DBT比Ring多耗时28.3%–83.2%；IB类HPCC比RoCE上的HPCC低约0.9%，而RoCE+DCQCN比RoCE HPCC慢23.8%–35.7%；拓扑效应有条件：Topology~6在低TP度下领先但高TP度失去优势，增加GPU/NIC仅在rank映射能均衡注入路径流量时有效；均匀32路分片下最大检查点权重分片约48.75 GB/rank，所有配置满足64 GB每加速器权重驻留标准。

Conclusion: 在所评估的工作负载与仿真语义下，服务器拓扑、并行策略、集合实现和拥塞控制共同决定暴露通信量与完成时间，性能优化需联合考虑这些因素而非单一维度。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models expand capacity via sparse activation, but inference across GPUs introduces tensor-parallel (TP) collectives and expert-parallel (EP) dispatch and combine operations. Completion time depends not just on communication volume but on how logical groups map onto intra-server interconnects, GPU--NIC connections, and the inter-node network. Using ASTRA-sim with the NS-3 discrete-event backend, we build a controlled matrix of 32 GPU ranks with data and pipeline parallelism fixed at one. Workloads are fixed-length 4096-token prefill-like synthetic Chakra traces from four MoE configurations. Experiments cover six server topologies, four TP/EP partitions, two TP collective algorithms, and four network/congestion-control modes, yielding 768 deterministic simulations. In the 144-configuration feedback-enabled subset per model, exposed communication accounts for 89.9%--95.8% of mean completion time. TP16EP2 requires 3.68--4.35x the mean completion time of TP2EP16. With fixed rank mapping, ASTRA-sim Double Binary Tree (DBT) incurs 28.3%--83.2% more time than Ring. InfiniBand-like High Precision Congestion Control (HPCC) is ~0.9% lower than HPCC over RDMA over Converged Ethernet (RoCE), whereas RoCE with Data Center Quantized Congestion Notification (DCQCN) is 23.8%--35.7% slower than RoCE HPCC. Topology effects are conditional: Topology~6 leads at low TP degrees but loses its advantage at high TP degrees, and additional GPUs or NICs help only when rank mapping balances traffic across injection paths. Under uniform 32-way sharding, the largest checkpoint-weight shard is ~48.75 GB per rank, so all configurations meet a 64 GB per-accelerator weight-residency criterion. Within the evaluated workload and simulator semantics, server topology, parallelism, collective implementation, and congestion control jointly determine exposed communication and completion time.

</details>


### [365] [Byzantine Causal Reliable Broadcast with Constant Metadata Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37913)
*Purv Patel,Ajay D. Kshemkalyani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出首个具最优消息元数据开销的安全 Byzantine 因果可靠广播（BCRB）算法，通过改造 Bracha 的 BRB 算法实现常数级 O(1) 元数据开销和 O(n²) 通信词复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步 Byzantine 可靠广播（BRB）保证一致性与有效性，但不提供顺序保证。因果消息顺序对区块链、社交网络等应用很重要。现有 Byzantine 因果可靠广播（BCRB）方案通常将向量时钟或依赖屏障附加到应用消息上，导致元数据开销随进程数 n 线性增长，且通信词复杂度达 O(n³)。

Method: 对 Bracha 的 BRB 算法进行小幅但关键的改造，重新设计以消除随 n 线性增长的元数据开销，在保证安全性的前提下实现因果广播，并给出正确性证明。算法采用四阶段，容忍 f < n/3 的 Byzantine 进程。

Result: 实现常数级 O(1) 消息元数据开销、O(n²) 消息数量，通信词复杂度为 O(n²)，相比现有协议的 O(n³) 大幅降低；容错达到最优界限 f < n/3。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了首个具最优消息元数据开销的安全 BCRB 算法，在保持最优容错的同时显著降低通信复杂度，为区块链等需因果顺序的分布式应用提供了高效基础原语。

Abstract: Asynchronous Byzantine Reliable Broadcast (BRB) is a fundamental primitive that guarantees agreement and validity in distributed systems subject to Byzantine faults, but it lacks ordering guarantees. Causal message ordering is important for many applications such as blockchain and social networking. Existing solutions for Byzantine Causal Reliable Broadcast (BCRB) have several drawbacks. Such protocols typically append vector clocks or dependency barriers to application messages, resulting in a metadata overhead that scales linearly with $n$, the number of processes in the system. In this paper, we propose the first optimal message overhead BCRB algorithm that guarantees safety. We do this by re-engineering Bracha's BRB algorithm with relatively small but critical modifications, and prove that our algorithm solves BCRB with optimal message metadata. The algorithm achieves constant-size $\mathcal{O}(1)$ message metadata overhead and $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ messages, resulting in $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ communication word complexity. This is as against $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ communication word complexity of existing protocols. The algorithm tolerates $f < n/3$ Byzantine processes, which is the well-known optimal resilience bound, and uses four phases.

</details>


### [366] [RLX: A Unified Multi-Backend Tensor Compiler and Distributed Runtime in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37916)
*Eugene Hauptmann,Nataliya Kosmyna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RLX 是一个统一的 Rust 编译器+运行时框架，围绕单一三层 IR 与透明调度契约，支持 14 种运行时设备、两种专用代码生成路径及多种模型/量化格式，并在多项基准测试中超越 PyTorch、JAX、TensorFlow 等主流框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级机器学习栈通常将图编译与内核执行拆分到不同层次和语言中，导致后端行为、部署保证与性能回退难以端到端推理。RLX 旨在用一个统一代码库弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 采用单一 Rust 代码库，结合编译器与运行时角色；核心为原始级别三层 IR，并配透明调度契约，将每个算子解析为原生、通用 IR 或重写降级，无法合法化时编译失败。同一 IR 覆盖 14 种运行时设备与两种专用代码生成路径，支持多种模型格式、混合精度与量化流程、张量/流水线并行集合通信，并扩展到稀疏/稠密线性代数及 3D 高斯泼溅算子。

Result: 在相同输入生成与 p50 测量方法下与 PyTorch、TensorFlow、JAX、candle、MLX、TensorRT、tinygrad 等框架对比：all-MiniLM-L6-v2 上 RLX-Metal 在各批次均最快（batch 32 为 16.6 ms，对比 PyTorch-MPS 26.7 ms）；MNIST 训练中图融合 MLP 达 946,487 img/s，高于 NumPy+BLAS 的 787,349 img/s，并在 Qwen3 等参考检查中保持 100% top-1 一致性。

Conclusion: RLX 通过统一 IR 与调度契约，整合编译与运行时，覆盖广泛硬件与格式，在性能与精度一致性上优于现有主流框架，并具备向科学计算领域扩展的能力。

Abstract: Production machine learning (ML) stacks often split graph compilation and kernel execution across different layers and languages, making backend behavior, deployment guarantees, and performance fallbacks hard to reason about end-to-end. RLX addresses this gap with a single Rust codebase that combines compiler and runtime roles around one primitive-level, three-level intermediate representation (IR), plus a transparent dispatch contract that resolves each operator to native, common-IR, or rewritten lowering and fails compilation when legalization is not possible. The same IR targets fourteen runtime devices (cpu, metal, mlx, ane, cuda, rocm, oneapi, tpu, hexagon, gpu, vulkan, opengl, directx, webgpu) and two specialty codegen paths (Cortex-M INT8 and FPGA), ingests safetensors, GGUF, ONNX, and rten formats, supports F16/BF16/F64/C64 and quantized INT4/INT8 flows with AMP/PTQ/QAT, and scales via tensor-/pipeline-parallel collectives over TCP and RDMA transports. Beyond neural workloads, RLX also extends to scientific/physics-style domains through sparse and dense linear algebra extensions (e.g., CSR LU/CG/matvec and LAPACK- backed factorizations) and 3D Gaussian splatting operators. We evaluate RLX against PyTorch, TensorFlow, JAX, candle, burn, tch, rten, MLX, CoreML, IREE, Glow, TensorRT, and tinygrad under identical input generation and p50 measurement methodology on one host. On all-MiniLM-L6-v2, RLX-Metal is fastest at every batch (e.g., 16.6 ms at batch 32 vs. PyTorch-MPS 26.7 ms). In the MNIST training table, RLX also has the top-throughput entry (graph-fused MLP: 946,487 img/s), above NumPy+BLAS (787,349 img/s), while retaining 100% top-1 parity on reference checks (e.g., Qwen3).

</details>
